## ⛔ ART-TOOLING HAZARD LAWS (2026-08-15) — namespace **`TL-§N`**

Added 2026-08-15 from TASK-566's two runs (the WAR-ROOM import leg) + TASK-582/TASK-584; ⭐ **extended the same day with `TL-§4` (the UMG design-time tree), from TASK-568 cross-read against TASK-355.** ⚖️ **Filed under the MECHANISM, ⛔ NOT under the feature that found them** — `WR-§` is a feature namespace and these four bind every future import and every future widget, in every batch. (The `(c)`-extension lesson: *a law indexed by one of its symptoms fails to cover the next one.*) Cite as `TL-§1`, never as a bare `§1`.

### TL-§1 ✅ THE ASCII-ONLY `pipeline_manifest.json` HOLD IS **RETIRED**, GLOBALLY — AND THE HAZARD IT PROXIED WAS RECORDED WRONG

- ✅ **RETIRED 2026-08-15 BY MANAGER RULING, ⛔ and only after it was EXERCISED, not merely fixed.** **TASK-582 landed `encoding="utf-8"` on both text reads in `Tools/reimport_meshes.py` (`:148` sidecar, `:162` manifest); `qa/TASK-584.md` criterion (6) PASSED it; and TASK-566 run 2 then READ A MANIFEST CONTAINING 251 NON-ASCII BYTES through `:162` and authored the castle's 25 collision boxes from it.** ⇒ ⚖️ **The retirement is discharged by a green run, ⛔ not by a diff.** ⭐ **`pipeline_manifest.json` was ALREADY non-ASCII (em-dash ×81, `±` ×2, `§` ×2 — all in prose fields, ⛔ none in `ucx.boxes`): the constraint had been silently violated before it was lifted, and the crash had simply not been rolled yet.**
- ⛔ **THE RETIREMENT IS GLOBAL, ⛔ NOT PER-SCRIPT, AND THAT WAS *SWEPT*, NOT ASSUMED:** every other manifest reader in the tree was ALREADY encoding-explicit (`Tools/ArtPipeline/refine_trellis_glb.py:213`, `Tools/ArtPipeline/rescale_refined_fbx.py:634`); `Tools/ArtPipeline/build_warroom_props.py` and `Castle.cpp:64` mention the manifest in COMMENTS ONLY. ⭐ **And a fact the sweep added that nobody had claimed: there is NO manifest WRITER anywhere in the tree — it is hand-authored.**
- ⚠️⛔ **THE CORRECTED HAZARD, BECAUSE THE ONE WE WROTE DOWN WAS WRONG AND WRONG IN THE DANGEROUS DIRECTION.** **cp1252 has exactly FIVE UNDEFINED BYTES — `0x81 0x8D 0x8F 0x90 0x9D` — and ONLY a character whose UTF-8 encoding contains one of them raises `UnicodeDecodeError`. Everything else MOJIBAKES SILENTLY.**
  - ⛔ **`⭐` (U+2B50 → `0x90`) CRASHES. `🔍` (U+1F50D → `0x8D`) CRASHES.** ✅ **`⛔` does ⛔ NOT crash. Neither does `—`, `±` or `§` — they corrupt quietly.**
  - ⚖️ **Of the twenty glyphs this pipeline uses in specs and handoffs, TWO crash and EIGHTEEN mojibake.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE RULE WE HAD WAS A PROXY FOR A FIVE-BYTE TRAPDOOR THAT TWO OF OUR MOST-USED SPEC GLYPHS FALL STRAIGHT THROUGH — and it named the wrong trigger character, so anyone testing the rule with `⛔` would have "disproved" a real hazard.**
- ⛔⭐ **THE STANDING LAW THAT REPLACES THE HOLD — IT IS AN OBLIGATION ON THE *CODE*, ⛔ NOT A CONSTRAINT ON THE *DATA*, WHICH IS WHY IT CANNOT ROT:** **every text-mode `open()` in `Tools/**` carries an explicit `encoding=` (`"utf-8"` for reads of repo data).** ⚠️ **UE's embedded Python on Windows defaults to `cp1252`; a bare `open()` is a latent decode crash on the first `⭐` anyone pastes.** ⛔ **A new `Tools/` script with a bare text `open()` is a QA finding.** ✅ **Writes are ⛔ NOT retro-fitted without a ruling — changing an output encoding changes bytes another tool consumes, which is a behaviour change wearing a bug-fix costume** (there are currently ZERO write-mode `open(` in `reimport_meshes.py`).
- ⚠️ **KNOWN, OPEN, ⛔ CANNOT FIRE TODAY (`qa/TASK-584.md` WARN-3, manager follow-up TASK-587): the fix flips the FAILURE DIRECTION for a non-UTF-8 manifest and the two call sites handle it ASYMMETRICALLY** — `_resolve_card_ids()` has no `try/except` (a decode error kills the batch before card #1) while `_load_manifest()` degrades to `{}` and continues. ⚖️ **⛔ The follow-up is NOT "make them symmetric": see `TL-§2`, which rules that `_load_manifest()`'s graceful degradation is the DANGEROUS one.**

### TL-§2 ⚠️ **HEADLINE PARTLY FALSIFIED 2026-08-15 (`W6-R3`)** — `UCX_` HULLS **DO** SURVIVE UE 5.8 IMPORT **WHEN THE NODE NAMES BIND**; THE MANIFEST STAYS THE COLLISION AUTHORITY **BY CHOICE, NOT BY NECESSITY**; AND A ZERO-COLLISION BUILDING IS **STILL A STOP**

- ⛔⛔ **READ THIS BEFORE THE CLAUSES BELOW — THE ORIGINAL HEADING WAS WRONG AND IS PRESERVED ONLY AS THE THING BEING CORRECTED (`W6-R3`, from TASK-567's throwaway probe).** ⭐ **MEASURED: `Castle.fbx`'s `UCX_SM_Castle_00..24` imported as **25 CONVEX ELEMS**, with **ZERO** manifest authoring.** ⇒ ⛔ **"UCX does not survive UE 5.8 import" is FALSE as a general claim, and TASK-566's WarTable observation ⛔ DOES NOT GENERALISE.**
- ⭐ **THE ACTUAL MECHANISM, WHICH IS AN *ASSET-AUTHORING* FAULT AND ⛔ NOT AN ENGINE LIMITATION: UE binds a hull to its render node BY NAME — `UCX_<render-node-name>_NN`.** ⚠️ **The WarTable FBX carries BOTH a `WarTable` node AND an `SM_WarTable` node, so `UCX_SM_WarTable_00`'s binding never resolved and the hull was dropped. `Castle.fbx` has ONE render node, `SM_Castle`, and its hulls bind exactly.** ⇒ ⛔ **The rule is NAME DISCIPLINE AT EXPORT: one render node per mesh, and the `UCX_` prefix matches it byte for byte.**
- ⚖️ **WHAT THIS CHANGES AND WHAT IT DOES NOT — stated so the correction is not over-read:**
  - ⛔ **UNCHANGED, AND STRENGTHENED: the SILENT-PATH STOP below.** ⭐ **The correction ADDS A SECOND WAY TO REACH ZERO HULLS BEHIND A GREEN RUN** (a name-mismatched `UCX_` node) **on top of the decode-failure path.** ⇒ **the readback-and-STOP rule is now covering two mechanisms, not one.**
  - ⛔ **UNCHANGED: `pipeline_manifest.json` remains the authoring authority for building-class collision, in-editor hand-editing stays BANNED, and `_apply_box_collision()` stays the sanctioned mechanism.** ⚠️⛔ **BUT THE JUSTIFICATION IS RE-WRITTEN: it is a DELIBERATE DETERMINISM CHOICE — boxes are reviewable, diffable and identical run to run — ⛔ NOT a workaround for an engine that eats hulls.** ⚖️ **A law kept for a reason that turned out to be false is a law the next batch will discard for the wrong reason.**
  - ⛔ **CHANGED: TASK-588's premise.** ⛔ **A manifest WRITER is no longer the obvious fix; an EXPORT-TIME NAME ASSERTION is the cheaper candidate. See the amended pointer at the end of this section.**

- ⚠️ **MEASURED TWICE, YEARS APART IN PIPELINE TIME: `UCX_SM_WarTable_00` was authored in Blender, exported in the FBX, and imported with **0 hulls under BOTH `combine_meshes` settings**, with ⛔ NO stray asset and ⛔ NO error.** ⭐ **PRE-EXISTING AND ALREADY KNOWN — `Tools/reimport_meshes.py:309-311` documents working around exactly this for the castle** (*"Bootstrap a body_setup deterministically (guarantees one exists even if the FBX's UCX did not import), then overwrite its geometry with boxes"*). **TASK-566 repaired the war table the same proven way: 1 box, centre `(0,0,55)`, `380×250×110`.**
- ⛔ **THE LAW (it restates `WR-§1`'s clause and generalises it past the castle): `pipeline_manifest.json`'s `ucx.boxes` is the SOLE collision authority for building-class and prop-class meshes. ⛔ The raw FBX is NEVER trusted for collision. ⛔ In-editor hand-editing of collision primitives stays BANNED.** ✅ **The sanctioned mechanism is `_apply_box_collision()`, and it is deterministic.**
- ⛔⭐ **THE PART THAT WAS MISSING AND IS THE REASON THIS CLAUSE EXISTS: THE SILENT PATH.** ⚠️ **`_apply_box_collision()` with no manifest boxes appends a `WARN` and returns `False` — the import still reports DONE.** ⇒ ⛔ **A building can ship with NO authored collision and the batch summary still reads green.** ⚖️ **Combined with `TL-§1`'s open WARN-3, the two compose into the worst available outcome: a manifest that fails to decode returns `{}`, `{}` means "no boxes", "no boxes" means a WARN, and a WARN means a castle you can walk through, with a successful-looking run behind it.**
- ⛔ **THE ACCEPTANCE RULE, BINDING ON EVERY IMPORT FROM NOW ON: a mesh's authored hull/box count is READ BACK AND REPORTED, and a count of ZERO is a STOP — ⛔ UNLESS the manifest DECLARES the mesh collisionless on purpose** (`ucx: null`; the unit path, and `SM_Torch`, which is *required* to carry zero collision). ⚖️ **"Zero" and "zero on purpose" are different facts and only the manifest can tell them apart** — ⛔ do not infer it from the asset.
- 📌 **The authoring-side repair — deriving the manifest box FROM the authored `UCX_` geometry at export time, so no future prop needs a hand-typed box — is boarded as TASK-588, DIAGNOSE-FIRST.** ⛔ **It is an ergonomics fix; the STOP above is the safety fix and does not wait for it.**
  - ⚠️⛔ **AMENDED 2026-08-15 (`W6-R3`): TASK-588's PREMISE IS PARTLY FALSIFIED AND ITS PRIORITY DROPS.** ⛔ **The clause it was justified by — _"after UE 5.8 silently drops the imported hull"_ — is FALSE for a correctly-named FBX.** ⇒ ⭐ **THE LEADING CANDIDATE IS NOW THE CHEAPER ONE: assert the NAME DISCIPLINE at export (exactly one render node; `UCX_<node>_NN` matching it), which fixes the WarTable class at its source and ⛔ adds NO writer to a hand-authored file.** ⚖️ **The manifest-writer question stays OPEN and stays REFUSABLE — ⛔ and "the engine ate my hull" may no longer be used as its argument.**

### TL-§2a ⛔⛔ `auto_generate_collision=False` GATES WHETHER COLLISION IS PROCESSED **AT ALL** — THE COMMENT AT `reimport_meshes.py:275-278` IS **FALSE**, AND THE SCRIPT'S OWN `agg_geom` OVERWRITE IS **WHY NOBODY CAUGHT IT** (added 2026-09-01, TASK-742 integration)

> ### ⛔ **THE SHIPPED CLAIM, VERBATIM AT `Tools/reimport_meshes.py:275-278`: *"UCX_ collision nodes in the FBX are still recognized by the importer **regardless of** this flag; we overwrite the agg_geom afterwards."* — ⛔ FALSE.**

- ⭐ **MEASURED BY ISOLATING ONE VARIABLE AT A TIME** (same FBX, same options object, nothing else changed — `WatchTower.fbx`, 8 authored `UCX_SM_WatchTower_00..07`):

  | `auto_generate_collision` | hulls imported | `bIsGenerated` |
  |---|---|---|
  | **`False`** (the shipped value, `:278`) | ⛔ **0** | — |
  | **`True`** | ✅ **8** | ⛔ **false** on all 8 |

- ⭐⭐ **`bIsGenerated == false` IS THE DECISIVE READING, AND IT IS WHAT MAKES THIS A MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN A GUESS: the flag did ⛔ NOT *fabricate* 8 hulls — it PERMITTED THE AUTHORED ONES TO BE PROCESSED.** ⇒ **the flag gates whether collision is processed AT ALL, ⛔ not merely whether it is auto-generated.** ⚠️ **Its name is the trap: it reads as *"do not invent collision"* and behaves as *"do not import collision."*** Cross-check: `qa`-grade evidence in `handoffs/TASK-737-artist.md` §8 (`8 / 0`, `bIsGenerated` false ×8, read back out of a real import).
- ⭐⭐ **WHY IT SURVIVED EVERY PRIOR RUN — AND THIS IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING: THE SCRIPT ALWAYS OVERWRITES `agg_geom` AFTERWARDS.** Both branches do it (`_apply_unit_hulls` → `remove_collisions()` + convex decomposition; `_apply_box_collision` → bootstrap a `body_setup` then write manifest boxes) ⇒ **the import's 0 hulls were replaced by authored geometry on every path that HAD authored geometry, so the false comment never produced a visible symptom.** ⛔⛔ **THE SAME OVERWRITE IS EXACTLY WHAT WOULD STRIP HAND-AUTHORED COLLISION AND REPORT `DONE`** — ⚖️ *the defect and its camouflage are the same line of code.*
- ⭐ **IT CORRECTS `TL-§2`'s MECHANISM WITHOUT OVERTURNING IT, AND THE DISTINCTION MATTERS:** name discipline (`UCX_<render-node>_NN` matching exactly one render node) is **NECESSARY and ⛔ NOT SUFFICIENT** — a byte-perfect name still yields **0 hulls** through this options object. ✅ **TASK-567's 25-convex-elem `Castle.fbx` probe STANDS**; it simply did not run through `_make_import_options()`. ⚖️ *Two true findings about the same path, taken from different entry points — ⛔ neither refutes the other.*
- ⚠️⛔ **A SECOND, INDEPENDENT MEASUREMENT THAT CHANGES A REIMPORT'S SAFETY MODEL: UE ***PRESERVES CUSTOMIZED COLLISION*** ACROSS A SAME-PATH REIMPORT.** The old ramp's **14** hulls **survived the first pass of the new FBX** and had to be **explicitly cleared** before the FBX could be the source of truth. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A GREEN REIMPORT DOES ⛔ NOT PROVE THE SHIPPED HULLS CAME FROM THE FBX YOU JUST IMPORTED.** ✅ **Read back the COUNT *and* `bIsGenerated`, and CLEAR FIRST whenever the authored geometry changed** — otherwise the mesh silently keeps the previous design's collision under the new render mesh, which is the `TL-§2` walk-through-castle failure wearing the opposite costume.
- ⚖️ **THE PROCESS SIGNAL, STATED AS A SIGNAL AND ⛔ NOT AS BLAME: this is the SECOND defect in this ONE file's collision path** (the first is `TL-§2b`, still open). ⭐ **Two defects in one path means the path is UNDER-TESTED, ⛔ not that two authors were careless** — and both were found by someone **reading the code against a measurement**, never by a run going red. ⇒ **boarded as `TASK-765` + `TASK-766`, gated by `TASK-767`; the durable repair is a READBACK ASSERTION, ⛔ not a corrected comment.**

### TL-§2b ⛔ `_category_of()` DEFAULTS AN **ABSENT** CardID TO `"unit"` — WHICH **STRIPS AUTHORED HULLS AND REPORTS SUCCESS**. ⚠️ **STILL OPEN FOR THE ABSENT CASE**

- **At source, `Tools/reimport_meshes.py:180-186`:** `entry = manifest.get(card_id, {})` → `cat = entry.get("category", "unit")`. ⇒ a mesh with **no manifest entry** — a typo, a new asset, or a manifest that failed to load and degraded to `{}` (`TL-§1`'s open WARN-3) — takes the **unit** branch, which runs `remove_collisions()` + convex decomposition. ⛔ **A building's authored `UCX_`/box collision is DELETED and the batch summary still reads green.**
- ✅ **TASK-737 PROVED THE TRAP IS CLOSED FOR `WatchTower` BY SIMULATING THE SHIPPED FUNCTION — ⛔ not by reading it:**
  ```
  WatchTower                  -> category=building  boxes=[]  -> _apply_box_collision
                                 -> WARN + LEAVES imported collision      [TRAP CLOSED]
  CONTROL (no manifest entry) -> category=unit -> AUTO-DECOMPOSITION      [the trap]
  ```
  ⇒ ⭐ **the trap is REAL and its control is MEASURED; only the absent case is unfixed.**
- ⛔ **THE LAW UNTIL IT IS FIXED: AN ABSENT MANIFEST ENTRY IS A STOP, ⛔ NEVER A DEFAULT.** ⚖️ *`"unit"` is a guess wearing a default's clothes* — and `TL-§2`'s readback-and-STOP rule is the only thing standing between it and a castle you can walk through.

### TL-§3 ⛔ THE HEADLESS `-run=pythonscript` COMMANDLET CANNOT REACH `StaticMeshEditorSubsystem` — SO THE EXPLICIT LOD CHAIN IS AN **EDITOR-ONLY** STEP

- ⚠️ **MEASURED (TASK-566 run 2): `LOD_STEP_FAILED` on `SM_Castle` — the subsystem is unavailable in a headless commandlet, so the explicit `CASTLE_LOD_CHAIN` (`reimport_meshes.py:119-122`: LOD1 50 % @ 0.4 · LOD2 25 % @ 0.15 · ⛔ NO LOD3 — the landmark-silhouette law) was ⛔ NOT applied, and the mesh kept the chain it already had.** ✅ **Fixable over MCP in the RUNNING editor; ⛔ never from the commandlet.**
- ✅⭐ **THE FAILURE PATH IS *STATE-NEUTRAL*, AND THIS IS MANAGER-VERIFIED BY READING THE ARTIFACT, ⛔ NOT RELAYED: the `subsys is None` raise is at `:378-379`, ⛔ BEFORE the `lod_group = "None"` write at `:384`** — and the code says so in its own comment (*"only now that the options built — keeps the failure path state-neutral"*). ⇒ ⛔ **There is NO half-applied state: no orphaned `lod_group`, no truncated chain. The mesh is in a CONSISTENT PRIOR state, which is what makes the repair a measure-then-apply rather than a rescue.**
- ⛔ **THE LAW: `_apply_lods()` is NON-FATAL by design (the collision-helper precedent) — so a `LOD_STEP_FAILED` token in a headless run is EXPECTED, ⛔ NOT a build failure, and ⛔ MUST NOT be triaged as one.** ⛔ **But it may not be swallowed either: it is a DEBT that must be discharged in the editor before the batch's commit, and the discharge is a MEASURED readback per LOD (`lod_count`, `lod_group`, per-LOD `screen_size` + triangle count), ⛔ never a claim.**
- ⛔⭐ **THE INHERITANCE RULE, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THIS EXPENSIVE IF IT IS LEFT LATE: A DUPLICATE INHERITS THE SOURCE MESH'S LOD CHAIN.** ⚠️ **The crumble trio is duplicated FROM `SM_Castle`** ⇒ **a stale chain on the source becomes FOUR stale chains the moment the trio is derived.** ⛔ **And divergence is worse than staleness: if `SM_Castle` carries chain A and `SM_Castle_Crumble0N` carries chain B, the damage-state swap changes the LOD switch distance — a visible pop at a distance that has nothing to do with damage, on a mesh the whole `WR-§1` crumble law exists to keep visually stable.** ⇒ ⛔ **All four castle-class meshes carry the SAME chain, and the readback proves it mesh by mesh.**
- ⚖️ **PRIORITY, STATED SO NOBODY OVER-WEIGHTS IT: this is PERF/VISUAL POLISH. The castle ships a working 3-LOD chain today.** ⛔ **It may NOT block a commit, a nav measurement, or a playtest** — if the subsystem cannot be reached live either, ⛔ REPORT and board it, do not improvise a substitute.
- ✅⭐ **AMENDED 2026-08-16 (TASK-586) — `LOD_STEP_FAILED` IS **MORE** BENIGN THAN THE CLAUSE ABOVE STATES: ⭐ THE CHAIN SURVIVES THE FAILURE, AND THE MECHANISM IS WORTH KNOWING RATHER THAN RE-DISCOVERING.** **`replace_existing_settings=True` PRESERVES A PACKAGE'S REDUCTION SETTINGS ACROSS AN IN-PLACE OVERWRITE** ⇒ **an FBX re-derivation RE-COMPUTES the 50 % / 25 % reductions against the NEW LOD0 and KEEPS the authored screen sizes.** ⭐ **MEASURED ON ALL FOUR CASTLE-CLASS MESHES AFTER A HEADLESS `LOD_STEP_FAILED`: `lod_count 3`, thresholds `[1, 0.4, 0.15]`, per-LOD triangles `[28698, 14348, 7174]` — IDENTICAL across `SM_Castle` and the three crumbles.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE CORRECT DISCHARGE IS *MEASURE-THEN-APPLY*, AND *"THE READBACK ALREADY MATCHES, SO NOTHING WAS WRITTEN"* IS A COMPLETE RESULT** — ⛔ **do NOT manufacture a `set_lod_thresholds` / `generate_lods` call to prove the task did work; that dirties four packages and re-lands them in a commit for nothing.**
- ⚠️⛔ **AND ONE ROW THIS PROJECT'S LIVE LANE ⛔ CANNOT DELIVER, NAMED SO NO FUTURE SPEC ASKS FOR IT: `auto_compute_lod_screen_size` IS ⛔ NOT REFLECTED ON THE MCP SURFACE.** ⚠️ **The commandlet route is the other option and `TL-§3`'s own headline takes the subsystem out there** ⇒ **the only remaining instrument is a human in the Static Mesh Editor.** ⛔ **A spec that lists it as an ACCEPTANCE row is asking for something no agent can produce.** ✅ **What IS available, and is the right substitute: the thresholds read back EXACTLY `0.4` / `0.15` — auto-computed sizes are geometry-derived and do not land on authored round numbers — ⛔ but that is an INFERENCE and is reported as one, ⛔ never as a passed row.**
- 📌 **A DIVERGENCE THAT IS *INTENDED*, RECORDED SO IT IS NOT READ AS ROT (TASK-586): `SM_Castle` and the crumble trio now differ in EXACTLY ONE respect — the MATERIAL INSTANCES** (`MI_TeamColor_Blue` / `MI_Castle_PBR` on the pristine mesh vs `MI_Castle_Crumble0N` on both slots of each stage). ✅ **Geometry, LOD chain, bounds, min-Z and collision are IDENTICAL across all four, which is precisely what `WR-§1` requires.** ⛔ **Do not "repair" the material difference into uniformity — it is the damage state.**

### TL-§4 ⛔⭐ THE UMG DESIGN-TIME TREE — **THE ROOT IS THE WALL, ⛔ NOT THE CHILDREN**; AND A COMMANDLET IS ⛔ **NOT** A CAPABILITY UPGRADE OVER IN-EDITOR PYTHON

Added 2026-08-15 (`W7-R4`). ⚖️ **Written because this wall has now blocked THREE tasks across two months (TASK-355, TASK-438, TASK-568) and each one re-derived it from scratch, and because the last two proposed routes were costed WRONG in the same cell.**

- ⛔⛔ **THE LAW, IN ONE LINE: `UWidgetTree::RootWidget` IS UNWRITABLE FROM EVERY SCRIPTED LANE THIS PROJECT HAS. ✅ CHILDREN OF AN EXISTING ROOT ARE FULLY MACHINE-AUTHORABLE.** ⇒ **A from-scratch `WBP_` needs exactly ONE human act, and everything after it is automatable.**
- **THE MEASURED REFUSAL TABLE (TASK-355 §4, live, with `bRemoteExecution` ON — ⛔ these are not inferences from a failure):**

  | lane | result on `RootWidget` |
  |---|---|
  | in-editor Python `wt.set_editor_property("RootWidget", panel)` | ⛔ *"Property 'RootWidget' … is protected and cannot be set"* |
  | in-editor Python `get_editor_property` | ⛔ refused (unreadable) |
  | MCP `ObjectTools.set_properties` on the WidgetTree | ⛔ *"could not be set: RootWidget"* |
  | MCP `ObjectTools.get_properties` on the WidgetTree | ⛔ *"could not be read: RootWidget, AllWidgets"* |
  | `UWidgetBlueprintFactory.RootWidgetClass` (instance) | ⛔ **`private` + `Transient`, no edit specifier** — ✅ manager-verified `WidgetBlueprintFactory.h:33-34` |
  | `unreal.WidgetTree` exposed methods | ⛔ **none beyond `UObject`** — `ConstructWidget` is a C++ **template**, so it is unreflected BY CONSTRUCTION |

  ⛔ **THE SPECIFIER IS `UPROPERTY(Instanced)` WITH NO EDIT FLAG (`WidgetTree.h:149-150`) — the property is `public` in C++ and still unreachable, because the wall is the REFLECTION layer, ⛔ not the access specifier.** ⚠️ **Python's error says *"protected"*; that word is misleading and has misled twice. ⛔ Do not go looking for a subclass or a friend — there is nothing to subclass around.**
- ⛔⭐ **THE COROLLARY THAT COSTS A SESSION IF IT IS MISSED: `-run=pythonscript` DOES NOT HELP HERE.** ⚖️ **A commandlet is the SAME `unreal` reflection surface with no UI. It is an upgrade over MCP (it can call `UFUNCTION`s — `AddChild`, `MapKey`) and it is ⛔ NO upgrade at all over in-editor Python for a reflection-blocked PROPERTY.** ⇒ ⛔ **"Python can call UFUNCTIONs, therefore the tree is reachable" is FALSE: `UPanelWidget::AddChild` (`PanelWidget.h:58-59`, `UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable)`) is genuinely callable and needs a panel THAT IS ALREADY THE ROOT.**
- ⚠️⛔ **AND THE PRECEDENT THAT IS ROUTINELY MIS-CITED: TASK-355's "REWORK-PASS lane" (`new_object` + `add_child`, 5 design-time widgets, laid out and shipped) IS REAL AND IS PROVEN — but it ran INTO `CanvasPanel_52`, A ROOT JONATHAN HAD CREATED BY HAND.** ⇒ ⭐ **THE PROVEN LANE PRESUPPOSES THE HUMAN STEP; it never replaced it. ⛔ Citing it as a route to a from-scratch tree is citing half a measurement.**
- ⭐⭐ **THE ONE LEVER THAT IS NOT REFLECTION-BLOCKED, AND IT IS UNTESTED ON THIS PROJECT — ✅ MANAGER-READ AT THE INSTALLED 5.8 ENGINE:** the root is written by **ENGINE C++**, which no property specifier constrains:
  ```cpp
  // WidgetBlueprintFactory.cpp:181-186
  if (!GetDefault<UUMGEditorProjectSettings>()->bUseWidgetTemplateSelector)
  {   RootWidgetClass = GetDefault<UUMGEditorProjectSettings>()->DefaultRootWidget;   }
  return FWidgetBlueprintOperationUtils::CreateWidgetBlueprint(InParent, Name, BlueprintType, CurrentParentClass, RootWidgetClass, CallingContext, /*bRegisterAndCompile=*/false);

  // WidgetBlueprintOperationUtils.cpp:245-252
  if (BP->WidgetTree->RootWidget == nullptr)
  {   if (TSubclassOf<UPanelWidget> RootWidgetPanel = static_cast<UClass*>(RootWidgetClass))
      {   UWidget* Root = BP->WidgetTree->ConstructWidget<UWidget>(RootWidgetPanel);
          BP->WidgetTree->RootWidget = Root;              // ⭐ assigned in C++ — no reflection involved
          BP->OnVariableAdded(Root->GetFName());   }   }
  ```
  ⇒ **`UMGEditorProjectSettings.DefaultRootWidget` (ships `None`, `UMGEditorProjectSettings.cpp:48`; MCP-settable and MCP-restorable — TASK-568 proved both) IS the root class, PROVIDED the creation call actually routes through `UWidgetBlueprintFactory`.**
  ✅ **AND THE PARENT CLASS RIDES THE SAME FACTORY: `UWidgetBlueprintFactory::ParentClass` is `UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere)` and PUBLIC (`WidgetBlueprintFactory.h:22-23`)** ⇒ ⭐ **a rooted AND correctly-parented-at-creation WBP in ONE call, ⛔ with no duplicate and ⛔ no reparent — the corruption law honoured BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by discipline.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHY IT IS STILL *UNTESTED* AND NOT *DISPROVEN* — AND THIS IS THE STANDING LESSON OF THE WHOLE SECTION: TWO NEGATIVE RESULTS EXIST AND EACH HELD A DIFFERENT VARIABLE WRONG.**
  - **TASK-355** ran the REAL factory (`AssetToolsHelpers.create_asset`) — with `DefaultRootWidget = None` ⇒ a null root class ⇒ no root. ✅ **Correct for the state measured; ⛔ it is not evidence about the flipped state.**
  - **TASK-568** flipped `DefaultRootWidget = CanvasPanel` — and drove **MCP `BlueprintTools.create`**, which it then PROVED does **not** route through the factory ⇒ the setting could not reach the code above.
  ⇒ ⭐ **NOBODY HAS EVER RUN THE FACTORY WITH THE SETTING SET. ⛔ Two negatives with different variables held wrong do not compose into an impossibility** — the identical *"a single case generalised"* error `W6-R1` and `W6-R3` each caught, ⛔ **both of them mine.**
- ⛔ **THE PROBE PROTOCOL, IF ANY BATCH TESTS IT: flip the setting → create a **THROWAWAY** name → save → scan the package name table for the panel class → ⛔ **RESTORE the setting with an explicit `set_properties` and READ IT BACK** (⚠️ TASK-568 measured `reset_properties` returning `true` while restoring NOTHING on a CDO) → ⛔ **DELETE the probe.** ⛔ **Never probe on the real asset.** ⛔ **Run any commandlet `-unattended`: `ConfigureProperties()` raises a class picker, and a modal in an unattended editor is TASK-355's 5-hour wedge** (`Process.Responding = True` is the Win32 pump, ⛔ NOT proof of life — check the log's `][NNN]` frame counter).
- ✅ **ONCE A ROOT EXISTS, EVERYTHING ELSE IS ROUTINE AND PROVEN:** `unreal.new_object(unreal.Button, widget_tree, "RevealButton")` + `panel.add_child(...)` author real design-time, `BindWidget*`-bindable children. ⚠️ **Expect one `Widget [X] was added but did not get a GUID` `ensureAlways` per new widget on the FIRST compile — SELF-HEALING at `WidgetBlueprintCompiler.cpp:781`.** ⛔ **THE RULE: compile + save, then compile AGAIN; the second compile MUST be silent. An ensure that survives compile #2 is a real defect.**
- ⛔ **AND THE STANDING VERIFICATION LAW IS UNCHANGED AND OVERRIDES ALL OF THE ABOVE: UMG render/interaction correctness closes on a PIXEL OR HUMAN CHECK, ⛔ never on a tree/property readback.** ⚠️ **A tree that reads perfect has shipped visually broken on this project more than once.**

### SC-§50 ⛔⛔⛔ **A GATE THAT ⛔ NAMES AN ⛔ ABSENT DELIVERABLE HAS CREATED A ⛔ BOARD OBLIGATION, ⛔ NOT A FOOTNOTE — ⛔ A ⛔ DECLARED GAP WITH ⛔ NO ROW IS AN ⛔ ORPHAN, AND IT ⛔ SHIPS** (added 2026-09-03, from `qa/TASK-849.md` `N-5` → `1aa0fee`; ⛔ **the ⛔ THIRD species of `TL-§5e`'s orphan shape this batch — ⛔ applied to a ⛔ DECLARED GAP rather than to a ⛔ FILE**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE GATE WAS ⛔ RIGHT. ⛔ IT SAID SO ⛔ IN WRITING. ⛔ IT SHIPPED ANYWAY.**
> `qa/TASK-849.md` `N-5`, ⛔ verbatim: *"`BP_Unit_Witch` and `T_CardArt_Witch` do ⛔ not exist yet (`TASK-835` / `TASK-834`; the card face degrades to text-only, ⛔ never a crash)."*
> ⇒ ⛔ **`T_CardArt_Witch` was subsequently authored. ⛔ `BP_Unit_Witch` ⛔ WAS NOT — and 🧑 Jonathan found it by ⛔ clicking the card ⛔ twice and getting ⛔ nothing.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE ⛔ SHAPE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ SAME ARITHMETIC AS `TL-§5e` cl. 2.** ⛔ **A ⛔ NIT/NOTE that names an ⛔ absent deliverable is ⛔ INFORMATION WITH NO OWNER.** ⛔ **The gate cannot board (⛔ QA writes verdicts, ⛔ not rows) · the programmer's task is ⛔ already closed · the build-master commits ⛔ what is staged.** ⇒ ⛔ **`N` declared gaps and ⛔ ZERO rows produces ⛔ ZERO assets, ⛔ however many gates passed.**
2. ⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ WHY IT ⛔ SURVIVES REVIEW, AND ⛔ THIS IS THE PART THAT MATTERS: ⛔ IT IS ⛔ FILED AS A ⛔ NIT ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ CODE DEGRADES ⛔ GRACEFULLY — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ GRACEFUL DEGRADE IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT ⛔ HIDES IT.** ⛔ `ResolveCardActorClass` returning `nullptr` ⇒ ⛔ refusal, ⛔ no gold spent, ⛔ no crash. ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ code is ⛔ CORRECT and the ⛔ severity looks ⛔ LOW — ⛔ while the ⛔ user-visible outcome is a ⛔ CARD THAT CANNOT BE PLAYED.** ⚖️ ***⛔ SEVERITY IS ⛔ NOT A PROPERTY OF THE ⛔ CODE PATH. ⛔ It is a property of ⛔ WHAT THE PLAYER CANNOT DO.***
3. ✅⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE, ⛔ MANAGER-OWNED, ⛔ RUN AT THE ⛔ SAME BOARDING INSTANT AS `TL-§5e` cl. 5's COUNT: ⛔ EVERY QA REPORT IS READ FOR ⛔ *"DOES NOT EXIST"* / *"NOT YET AUTHORED"* / *"MISSING"* / *"TODO IN TASK-###"*, AND ⛔ EACH ONE EITHER BECOMES A ⛔ ROW OR IS ⛔ EXPLICITLY RULED ⛔ NOT-NEEDED ⛔ IN WRITING.** ⛔ **A named `TASK-###` inside a NIT is a ⛔ CITATION, ⛔ NOT A GUARANTEE THAT THE ROW EXISTS OR IS ⛔ STILL OPEN** (`SC-§40` cl. 16: ⛔ a ⛔ stated intention is a ⛔ PLAN, ⛔ never a ⛔ measurement).
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ PROSE CANNOT CLOSE THIS CLASS; ⛔ ONLY A ⛔ MECHANISM CAN.** ⛔ **For ⛔ ASSETS the mechanism is ⛔ CHEAP and it is the ⛔ census the diagnosis already ran, ⛔ automated: ⛔ walk every spawnable `DT_Cards` row and ⛔ assert its ⛔ composed CONVENTIONS path ⛔ RESOLVES.** ⭐ **It would have gone ⛔ RED at `1aa0fee`.** ⇒ ⛔ **`SHIP-§9` binds it: ⛔ the gate is validated against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects, ⛔ never merely against success.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ SCOPE. ⛔ This does ⛔ NOT make every NIT a task** (⛔ that would drown the board and ⛔ `SC-§29`'s one-gate-one-subject discipline exists for a reason). ⛔ **It binds ⛔ exactly one predicate: ⛔ a NIT asserting that a ⛔ DELIVERABLE THE FEATURE NEEDS IN ORDER TO FUNCTION ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST.** ⛔ **Style, wording, and *"could be tidier"* are ⛔ untouched by this section.**
6. ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE ⛔ SELF-INDICTMENT, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-03 BY THIS SECTION'S ⛔ OWN FIRST APPLICATION (`qa/TASK-950.md`): ⛔ CLAUSE 3'S ⛔ PRESCRIBED NEEDLE ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE CAUGHT THE ⛔ CASE CLAUSE 3 WAS ⛔ WRITTEN FOR.**
   > ⛔⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT. ⛔ `TASK-950` was handed `qa/TASK-849.md` `N-5` as its ⛔ REQUIRED positive control — ⛔ the ⛔ one known instance, ⛔ the case that ⛔ bought this whole section. ⛔ CLAUSE 3 PRESCRIBES THE SHAPES *"does not exist"* / *"not yet authored"* / *"missing"* / *"TODO in TASK-###"*. ⛔ `N-5` SAYS: *"do ⛔ **not exist yet**"*.**
   > ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE PRESCRIBED NEEDLE ⛔ MISSES IT. ⛔ A COMPLIANT CENSUS WOULD HAVE RETURNED A ⛔ CONFIDENT LOW NUMBER ⛔ AND PASSED ITS OWN POSITIVE CONTROL** — ⛔ because the control was ⛔ named by ⛔ report-and-section rather than ⛔ found by the predicate.
   - ✅ **WHAT THE CENSUS DID INSTEAD, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ BEHAVIOUR THIS CLAUSE MAKES LAW: ⛔ IT ⛔ WIDENED THE NEEDLE ⛔ BEFORE COUNTING, ⛔ rather than counting and publishing a ⛔ clean-looking zero.** ⛔ **Result, ⛔ with its scope per `SC-§49`: ⛔ `898` raw occurrences / ⛔ `799` files ⇒ ⛔ **1** live orphan (`Tools/ArtPipeline/cardart_render.py`, ⛔ medium), ⛔ **0** of Witch severity.**
   - ⛔⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ BINDING ON ⛔ EVERY CENSUS ROW THIS BOARD ISSUES: ⛔ THE ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL IS RUN ⛔ THROUGH THE PREDICATE, ⛔ NEVER ⛔ ASSERTED ALONGSIDE IT.** ⛔ **If the ⛔ named control does ⛔ not fall out of the ⛔ prescribed method, ⛔ THE METHOD IS ⛔ WRONG — ⛔ widen it, ⛔ say you widened it, ⛔ and ⛔ publish ⛔ both the prescribed and the widened counts.** ⛔ **A control that is ⛔ hand-injected into the hit set ⛔ tests nothing** (`SC-§39`: ⛔ a check that ⛔ passes for the ⛔ wrong reason is ⛔ not a check).
   - ⛔ **AND IT BINDS THE ⛔ MANAGER FIRST: ⛔ a spec that ⛔ names a control ⛔ AND ⛔ prescribes a method must have ⛔ CHECKED THE METHOD ⛔ AGAINST THE CONTROL ⛔ BEFORE DISPATCH.** ⛔ **This one did not, and ⛔ the agent caught it.**
   - ⚖️⚖️ ***⛔ THE GENERAL LAW, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WORTH MORE THAN THE CENSUS THAT BOUGHT IT: ⛔ A RULE WHOSE ⛔ PRESCRIBED METHOD ⛔ CANNOT CATCH THE ⛔ CASE THE RULE WAS ⛔ WRITTEN FOR IS ⛔ WORSE THAN ⛔ NO RULE — ⛔ because it ⛔ manufactures a ⛔ DEFENSIBLE ZERO. ⛔ No rule leaves the question ⛔ open; ⛔ a broken rule ⛔ closes it ⛔ WRONG, ⛔ in writing, ⛔ with a control cited.***
   - ⛔ **SPECIES: ⛔ this is `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects) applied to a ⛔ LAW'S OWN PREDICATE, and ⛔ `TL-§5b` 2c (⛔ a ⛔ wrong explanation of a ⛔ real hazard is ⛔ more dangerous than none) applied to a ⛔ LAW'S OWN METHOD.** ⛔ **Both were ⛔ already on the books; ⛔ neither was ⛔ pointed at ⛔ CONVENTIONS.md itself until now.**

### SC-§51 ⛔⛔⛔ **COUNT THE ⛔ TELLS BY WHAT EACH ONE CAN ⛔ SEE, ⛔ NOT BY HOW MANY ASSERTIONS YOU WROTE — ⭐ AND THE ⛔ NEGATIVE CONTROL IS ⛔ NOT A VACUITY TELL AT ALL** (added 2026-09-03; ⛔ **the author claimed THREE independent tells, `qa/TASK-948.md` `W-1` measured TWO — and the ⛔ load-bearing one was ⛔ neither of the three**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **`TotalRows > 0` AND `SpawnableRows > 0` ARE ⛔ ONE TELL, ⛔ NOT TWO — THE FIRST IS ⛔ STRICTLY SUBSUMED BY THE SECOND AND CAN ⛔ NEVER FIRE ALONE. AND ⛔ THREE OF THE FIVE COUNT ASSERTIONS ⛔ PASS AT `0 == 0`.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ AN ASSERTION THAT ⛔ PASSES AT `0 == 0` IS ⛔ NOT A VACUITY TELL.** ⛔ **A partition check (`A + B == Total`), a balance check, a *"no row was double-counted"* check — ⛔ all of them are ⛔ TRUE on an ⛔ EMPTY SET.** ⇒ ⛔ **they are tells for ⛔ OTHER properties (⛔ and correctly so), ⛔ but they contribute ⛔ ZERO to *"did this walk actually walk anything?"*** ⛔ **Counting them into the vacuity total is how a gate with ⛔ one live tell gets published as a gate with ⛔ five.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ SUBSUMPTION IS ⛔ NOT INDEPENDENCE.** ⛔ **If `X` can never be false while `Y` is true, ⛔ `X` and `Y` are ⛔ ONE tell.** ⇒ ⛔ **before publishing a count of *"independent tells"*, ⛔ state for each one the ⛔ WORLD IN WHICH IT ⛔ ALONE FIRES. ⛔ A tell with no such world is ⛔ decoration.** ⛔ **Do ⛔ NOT delete the subsumed assertion — it is free, it documents intent, and deleting it is how the live one gets deleted next.** ⛔ **Just ⛔ stop counting it.**
3. ⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE SHARPEST PART, AND IT IS THE ⛔ EASIEST TO LOSE IN A TIDY-UP: ⛔ THE ⛔ NEGATIVE CONTROL IS THE ⛔ LOAD-BEARING ASSERTION, AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A VACUITY TELL.** ⛔ **Every count-based tell answers *"did the walk see rows?"* ⛔ The negative control answers a ⛔ DIFFERENT AND ⛔ STRICTLY HARDER QUESTION: *"is the ⛔ PROBE still ⛔ able to say NO?"*** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **it is the ⛔ ONLY assertion that catches a probe which has gone ⛔ BLIND and answers ⛔ PRESENT for ⛔ everything — ⛔ a mode in which ⛔ every count is ⛔ healthy, ⛔ every partition balances, and ⛔ every row passes.** ⚖️ ***⛔ A FULL ROW SET AND A BLIND PROBE PRODUCE THE ⛔ SAME GREEN. ⛔ ONLY THE NEGATIVE CONTROL DISTINGUISHES THEM.***
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THEREFORE, IN A GATE'S OWN HEADER AND IN ITS HANDOFF, ⛔ TWO SEPARATE LEDGERS, ⛔ NEVER ONE NUMBER:** ⛔ **(a) the ⛔ VACUITY tells (⛔ *"the walk was non-empty"*), each with the world in which it alone fires; ⛔ (b) the ⛔ INSTRUMENT controls (`SC-§39`'s positive ⛔ and negative), which are ⛔ about the probe, ⛔ not about the data.** ⛔ **Merging (a) and (b) into a single count of *"tells"* ⛔ overstates the first and ⛔ hides the second.**
5. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ A MISSING `default:` IN A `switch` OVER AN ENUM CAN BE ⛔ LOAD-BEARING, AND ⛔ TIDYING IT IS A ⛔ SILENT DISARM.** ⛔ **Measured instance (`SiegeCardRosterTest.cpp`, `qa/TASK-948.md`): adding `default: Category = NotSpawnable;` would ⛔ swallow every unrouted card type into a ⛔ legitimate-looking bucket — ⛔ the partition would ⛔ still balance and the ⛔ walk would ⛔ still be non-empty ⇒ ⛔ BOTH halves of the gate disarmed by ⛔ ONE "cleanup".** ⇒ ⛔ **where a `switch`'s ⛔ ABSENT `default:` is the mechanism by which a new enumerator becomes a ⛔ RED, ⛔ SAY SO IN A COMMENT AT THE `switch`, ⛔ and ⛔ carry it on the board row so a future tidy-up cannot undo it.** ⚠️ **⛔ Do ⛔ NOT rest that mechanism on a ⛔ compile-time warning: this project sets ⛔ no `bWarningsAsErrors`/`4062` configuration, so the ⛔ compile-time half is ⛔ toolchain-conditional. ⛔ The ⛔ RUN-TIME red must stand on its own.**
6. ⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ A GATE THAT IS ⛔ BORN GREEN HAS ⛔ NO FREE RED, AND `SHIP-§9` IS ⛔ NOT WAIVED BY THAT.** ⛔ **`TASK-947` was ⛔ lucky: `BP_Unit_Witch` was ⛔ genuinely absent, so its ⛔ RED was ⛔ real and ⛔ unsynthesised.** ⛔ **A gate boarded over a surface that is ⛔ already 100 % complete (e.g. ⛔ 32/32 `T_CardArt_` assets present) ⛔ CANNOT be shown a real red** ⇒ ⛔ **the row must ⛔ SYNTHESISE the failure — ⛔ break it, ⛔ SHOW THE RED, ⛔ REVERT, ⛔ paste the transcript — ⛔ or it ships ⛔ UNVALIDATED and is a ⛔ status line wearing a gate's clothes** (`SHIP-§9c` cl. 1). ⛔ **The manager states which of the two applies ⛔ at boarding; ⛔ the author may ⛔ not decide it afterwards.**
7. ⚖️ **KINSHIP:** ⛔ this is `SC-§44`'s *"a green test that asserts a degrade path returns empty"* generalised from ⛔ one test to a ⛔ COUNT OF TESTS, and `SC-§39` read from the other end — ⛔ `§39` says ⛔ carry the controls; ⛔ this says ⛔ do not let the controls be ⛔ absorbed into a headline number that ⛔ obscures what only they can see.

### SC-§52 ⛔⛔⛔ **BOARD THE ⛔ QUESTION, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ ANSWER — ⭐ A ROW THAT ⛔ ASSUMES ITS DEFECT WILL ⛔ FIND ONE, AND WILL ⛔ AUTHOR A FIX FOR IT** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; bought by `TASK-952` + `TASK-953`, ⛔ **the first pair of rows in this project to close with ⛔ ZERO defects, ⛔ ZERO diffs and ⛔ ZERO churn — and to be ⛔ WORTH IT**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **BOTH ROWS WERE BOARDED AS *"IS THIS A DEFECT?"* ⛔ BOTH ANSWERED ⛔ NO. ⭐ HAD EITHER BEEN BOARDED AS *"FIX THE DEFECT"*, ⛔ BOTH WOULD HAVE SHIPPED A DIFF — AND ⛔ ONE OF THEM WOULD HAVE ADDED A ⛔ THIRD REDUNDANT LAYER TO A PROPERTY THAT WAS ⛔ ALREADY CORRECT TWICE OVER.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED INSTANCE, BECAUSE IT IS SHARPER THAN THE PRINCIPLE.** `TASK-946` §3.3 reported *"`BP_Unit_Wizard` is the only unit of 14 with no authored slot-0 team colour ⇒ it renders untinted in the editor viewport."* ⛔ **The FINDING was correct. The INFERRED CONSEQUENCE was FALSE** — `SM_<CardID>` slot 0 is named `TeamRegion` and is assigned `MI_TeamColor_Blue` **on the mesh asset, 14/14** ⇒ ⛔ **the other 13 Blueprint overrides are NO-OPS, and the Wizard is not the unit MISSING a team colour — it is the only one NOT CARRYING THE REDUNDANT RESTATEMENT.** ⇒ ⭐ **the row boarded as a question cost a document; the same row boarded as *"author the missing override"* would have cost a `Content/` write, an LFS blob, a QA gate, a commit — and would have made the tree ⛔ WORSE.**
2. ⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ⛔ REVERSE IS ALSO A DIFF, AND IT MUST BE REFUSED WITH THE SAME DISCIPLINE.** ⛔ **On finding 13 redundant overrides, the tempting "cleanup" is to STRIP them.** `TASK-953` refused, and its reason is the law: ⛔ **13 assets of LFS churn for zero pixels**, and ⛔ **if either state is correct it is the WIZARD'S — the 13 would silently MASK a future `TeamRegion` re-point, while the Wizard would FOLLOW it.** ⇒ ⛔ **"I found redundancy" is not on its own a licence to edit. State what PIXEL changes. If the answer is *none*, the correct deliverable is a ⛔ RECORD, ⛔ not an edit.**
3. ⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE BOARDING RULE THIS BUYS, BINDING ON THE MANAGER.** When an upstream handoff reports an ODDITY rather than a FAILURE, the row's title, its spec and its verdict shape are all **INTERROGATIVE**, and the spec says in as many words: ⛔ ***"if the subject turns out to be CORRECT AS SHIPPED, SAY SO PLAINLY AND LEAVE IT ALONE — a diff with no defect behind it is worse than no diff."*** ⛔ **The verdict vocabulary is fixed in advance and includes a NO-DEFECT member** (`DESIGN` / `INERT` / `STRUCTURALLY COVERED` / `LIVE DEFECT`) — ⛔ **a row whose only permitted outcomes are failure states will manufacture one.**
4. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 4 — WHY THE ODDITIES WERE FOUND AT ALL, AND IT NAMES A CLASS THIS PIPELINE STILL DOES NOT COVER: ⛔ BOTH WERE FOUND BY AN AGENT CHECKING WHETHER IT WAS ⛔ SAFE TO COPY A SHIPPED ASSET.** ⛔ **No gate, no test and no census in this project reads a shipped asset AS A DONOR.** ⭐ **That is why `TASK-946`'s "I could not justify this parent for a Witch" was worth three rows: the DONOR-READ is a detector we get for free every time an author is careful, and ⛔ nothing catches it when they are not.**
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — AND THE WARNING THAT POINTS AT THE WRONG PROPERTY IS ⛔ WORSE THAN NO WARNING.** `TASK-946` was told to avoid the Wizard as a template **for a defect the Wizard does not have** (the struck *"yaw 0 / Z 0"* claim) — while the Wizard's genuinely unusual property (`OverrideMaterials` `[]`) was **exactly the one it was about to copy onto 12 units.** ⇒ ⛔ **a template warning names the PROPERTY and cites the MEASUREMENT, never just the asset.**

### SC-§53 ⛔⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ REPAIR LANDS AND THE ⛔ PARAGRAPH DESCRIBING THE ⛔ PRE-REPAIR STATE ⛔ DOES NOT — ⭐ THIS IS THE ⛔ ORIGIN OF CITATION ROT, AND IT IS ⛔ TRACEABLE TO A ⛔ SINGLE COMMIT** (added 2026-09-03; traced by `TASK-953`; ⛔ **it explains THREE separate corrections made to one CONVENTIONS bullet in one day**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **BORN `0d717c0` (2026-07-26) WITH THE DEFECT. FIXED `390aff3` (2026-07-27, `TASK-334`) — ⛔ WHICH CORRECTED YAW AND Z AND ⛔ CORRECTLY LEFT THE REST ALONE. ⛔ THE LAW PARAGRAPH DESCRIBING THE ⛔ BROKEN STATE WAS ⛔ NEVER TOUCHED, AND IT HAS READ AS ⛔ PRESENT TENSE FOR ⛔ FIVE WEEKS.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MECHANISM, AND NOTE THAT ⛔ NOBODY ERRED.** A defect is measured; the measurement is written into law **in the present tense**; the repair ships in a **different file** under a **different task**. ⇒ ⛔ **the code is now correct and the law now LIES — and the lie is in the voice of a measurement, which is exactly the voice a reader trusts.** ⛔ **The repairing task did nothing wrong; the describing paragraph was simply not in its pathspec.**
2. ⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE.** ⛔ **A task that repairs a condition CONVENTIONS describes must amend or strike that description IN THE SAME BATCH, or the manager must board the amendment as a named row.** ⛔ **"The code is fixed" is not "the law is fixed."** ⭐ **Prefer STRIKING (`~~…~~`) with a dated correction over deleting** — a reader who remembers the old sentence must find its resolution rather than re-derive it (the `SLACK.md` v2 precedent).
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — WRITE THE MEASUREMENT IN THE ⛔ PAST TENSE WITH ITS ⛔ DATE AND ⛔ HASH.** ⛔ *"the Wizard's `SkeletalVisualMesh` sits at `(0,0,0)`"* rots silently. ✅ *"as measured at `0d717c0` (2026-07-26) the Wizard's `SkeletalVisualMesh` sat at `(0,0,0)`"* **cannot** rot — it stays true forever and it tells the reader to go re-measure. ⚠️ **A past-tense, correctly-dated sentence is NOT stale and must NOT be "corrected"** — `TASK-953` found one such site (`SummonedUnit.cpp`'s pre-fix narration) and explicitly declined to call it stale. ⛔ **Reporting a correct historical statement as a defect is its own error.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — A ⛔ NEGATIVE GREP HAS A ⛔ SHELF LIFE, AND UNDER CONCURRENT EDIT IT IS MEASURED IN ⛔ MINUTES.** ⛔ *"I grepped and it is not there"* is an instant-fact exactly as `TL-§5b`'s suite total is, and `SC-§40`'s *"an absence is a measurement"* inherits the expiry. ⛔ **Measured today, twice, in a single relay: a section cited at `CONVENTIONS:8056` read at `:8136`, and a claim cited at `:4386` read at `:4400` — ⛔ both because the SAME manager's OWN amendment had moved them between the citation and the read.** ⇒ ⛔ **NOBODY was wrong and BOTH citations were false.** ✅ **THEREFORE: cite by `SC-§38`'s SYMBOL — the section tag, the quoted sentence — and treat a line number as a dated annotation. ⛔ RE-GREP AT THE INSTANT OF USE, never on the strength of a relayed line.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP:** `SC-§38` says ⛔ the symbol is the key; **this section says WHY the key is needed** — ⛔ it is not merely that lines move, it is that **repairs and their descriptions live in different files and ship on different days.** `SC-§40` supplies the discipline (⛔ re-measure a relayed premise); ⛔ **this supplies the expectation that the premise WILL have expired.**

### SC-§54 ⛔⛔⛔ **A ⛔ PERMISSION REFUSAL IS A ⛔ MEASUREMENT, ⛔ NOT AN OBSTACLE — ⭐ AND A ⛔ NEGATIVE CONTROL IS ⛔ NEVER SYNTHESISED BY ⛔ DISTURBING A ⛔ REAL DELIVERABLE** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; bought by `TASK-949`, which hit the refusal, ⛔ **did NOT route around it**, and ⛔ **declared the resulting shortfall ⛔ ABOVE its claim**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **IT NEEDED A ⛔ COMPILED RED. THE ⛔ ONLY METHOD IT HAD IN HAND WAS *"MOVE THE ⛔ STAGED `BP_Unit_Witch.uasset` ASIDE."* ⛔ THE PERMISSION SYSTEM ⛔ REFUSED. ⭐ IT ⛔ STOPPED, ⛔ VERIFIED NOTHING HAD BEEN TOUCHED, AND ⛔ SAID THE RED WAS ⛔ STILL OWED — RATHER THAN FINDING A ⛔ SECOND PATH TO THE ⛔ SAME MOVE.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE CONDUCT, AND IT IS THE LAW.** An agent that hits a permission denial ⛔ **STOPS**, ⛔ **verifies the blocked operation was ⛔ ATOMIC** (⛔ nothing partially applied — ⛔ digest ⛔ and index unchanged), and ⛔ **reports the denial ⛔ AS A RESULT.** ⛔ **It does ⛔ NOT retry through a different tool, a different shell, a temp copy, or a rename.** ⭐ **A refusal that is ⛔ worked around is ⛔ WORSE than no permission system at all: ⛔ the next reader cannot tell which of the two happened.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ⛔ BOARDING DUTY IT CREATES, ⛔ BINDING ON THE MANAGER.** ⛔ **An evidence obligation that a refusal blocked does ⛔ NOT evaporate with the row that owed it** (`SC-§50`: ⛔ a declared gap with no row is an orphan, ⛔ and it ships). ⇒ ⛔ **it becomes a ⛔ NAMED ROW, ⛔ and that row ⛔ SPECIFIES A METHOD THAT ⛔ DOES NOT REQUIRE THE ⛔ REFUSED MOVE.** ⛔ **Re-boarding the ⛔ same method and hoping for a ⛔ different permission answer is asking the agent to route around it ⛔ on the manager's behalf.**
3. ⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ METHOD LAW, AND IT ⛔ GENERALISES PAST PERMISSIONS: ⛔ A NEGATIVE CONTROL IS SYNTHESISED AGAINST A ⛔ MEASURED-ABSENT ⛔ SYNTHETIC INPUT — ⛔ NEVER BY ⛔ MOVING, ⛔ RENAMING, ⛔ DELETING OR ⛔ EDITING A ⛔ REAL DELIVERABLE.** ⛔ **Three named reasons, ⛔ all measured on this project:** ⛔ **(a)** ⛔ the editor's revision-control provider ⛔ **AUTO-ADDS ON SAVE** (`§25b` cl. R — ⛔ measured this very batch on `BP_Unit_Witch.uasset`) ⇒ ⛔ **the "restore" you plan may not restore the ⛔ INDEX**; ⛔ **(b)** ⛔ the restore's success is ⛔ ASSERTED, ⛔ not observed — ⛔ verifying it costs the ⛔ same oid-vs-sha256 read the move was meant to save; ⛔ **(c)** ⭐ a ⛔ synthetic absent input yields a ⛔ **PERMANENT** test that goes red ⛔ every run, while a ⛔ moved asset yields ⛔ ONE transcript that ⛔ decays into a screenshot.
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — WHAT MAY ⛔ SUBSTITUTE IN THE MEANTIME, AND ⛔ HOW IT MUST BE ⛔ LABELLED.** `TASK-949` shipped with the red ⛔ still owed and was ⛔ **RIGHT** to: its green printed ⛔ `32 row(s) read; 22 SPAWNABLE probed (13 Unit, 2 Economy, 7 Building); 10 EXCLUDED` ⇒ ⛔ **it cannot be the ⛔ vacuous green `SHIP-§9` fears.** ⛔ **But a ⛔ PROBE COUNT is an ⛔ ANTI-VACUITY tell, ⛔ NOT a ⛔ RED.** ⇒ ⛔ **the substitute is declared ⛔ AS a substitute, ⛔ in the same breath as the claim and ⛔ ABOVE it — ⛔ never folded into it.** ⭐ **`TASK-949` did exactly that, and ⛔ that is the ⛔ only reason the debt is ⛔ collectable.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP.** `SHIP-§9` supplies the ⛔ REQUIREMENT (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects); `SC-§51` cl. 6 supplies the ⛔ COUNT; `TL-§5c` cl. 5(a) supplies the ⛔ SHAPE OF THE DECLARATION; ⛔ **this section supplies the ⛔ METHOD and the ⛔ REFUSAL ETIQUETTE.**

### SC-§55 ⛔⛔⛔ **THE CONTEXT'S `gitStatus` SNAPSHOT IS A ⛔ CITATION WITH A ⛔ SHELF LIFE — ⛔ NEVER A MEASUREMENT. ⭐ AND ⛔ EVERY AGENT IN THIS PIPELINE RECEIVES ⛔ THE SAME STALE ONE.** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; caught and declared by `TASK-966`, which ⛔ **ignored its own snapshot entirely** and said ⛔ **why**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **ITS SESSION-START SNAPSHOT SHOWED ⛔ CASTLE-ERA FILES FROM AN ⛔ OLDER SESSION — ⛔ COMPLETE WITH `A ` ⛔ STAGED ENTRIES. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ WELL-FORMED, ⛔ AUTHORITATIVE-LOOKING, AND ⛔ WRONG. ⛔ A ROW THAT BUILT A PATHSPEC FROM IT WOULD HAVE ⛔ STAGED THE WRONG FILES AND ⛔ NOTHING WOULD HAVE GONE RED.**

#### 🚨🚨⛔⛔⛔ **SC-§55a — A ⛔ LIVE HAZARD RIGHT NOW, ⛔ 2026-09-04: ⛔ EVERY AGENT SPAWNED ⛔ BEFORE COMMIT `1a457df` CARRIES A SNAPSHOT THAT DESCRIBES A ⛔ SHIPPED BATCH AS ⛔ PENDING.**

- ⛔ **MEASURED by ⭐ `TASK-965` ⛔ ON ITSELF: its session `gitStatus` read ⛔ HEAD `84eec02` and listed the ⛔ ENTIRE FOG BATCH as ⛔ UNCOMMITTED.** ⛔⛔ **THAT BATCH ⛔ IS `1a457df`.** ⛔ **It used ⛔ NOT ONE BYTE of it and ⛔ said why.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE FAILURE THIS INVITES IS ⛔ NOT *"a stale list"* — ⛔ IT IS AN AGENT CONCLUDING THAT ⛔ COMMITTED, ⛔ REVIEWED, ⛔ SHIPPED WORK IS ⛔ STILL SITTING UNCOMMITTED IN THE TREE, AND ⛔ ACTING TO *"RESCUE"* IT.** ⛔ **The ⛔ instinctive rescue is a ⛔ stage, a ⛔ commit, or — ⛔ worst — a ⛔ `git checkout` to *"clean up"* what looks like leftovers** (⭐ `SC-§71`, ⛔ which ⛔ already cost this project a `qa-passed` diff).
- ⛔ **AND IT ⛔ COMPOUNDS WITH THE ⛔ SEVENTH LIE (⭐ `SC-§68`): ⛔ a `git show HEAD:<path>` check ⛔ ALSO reports false divergence right after a commit** ⇒ ⛔ **two independent instruments ⛔ both saying *"the commit didn't happen"*, ⛔ to an agent whose snapshot ⛔ agrees.** ⛔ **⛔ THREE CONFIRMATIONS, ⛔ ALL WRONG.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§55b — THE ⛔ COMPANION RULE: ⛔ ON A ⛔ LIVE TREE, A ⛔ COUNT IS A MEASUREMENT WITH A ⛔ SHELF LIFE OF ⛔ MINUTES. ⛔ CITE THE ⛔ METHOD, ⛔ NEVER THE FIGURE.** (added 2026-09-04)
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: the orchestrator handed ⭐ `TASK-1034` *"a handful vs 45"* for the ⛔ untracked-collapse check. ⛔ The host measured ⛔ `27` collapsed vs ⛔ `59` expanded** ⇒ ⛔ **a drift of ⛔ 14 ⛔ WITHIN THE HOUR.**
  - ✅ **⛔ THE MECHANISM IT WAS GIVEN WAS ⛔ REAL AND ⛔ CORRECT** (⛔ `git status` ⛔ COLLAPSES an untracked directory to ⛔ one entry ⇒ ⛔ use ⛔ `--untracked-files=all`). ⛔ **⛔ ONLY THE FIGURES WERE STALE.** ⛔ **The host ⛔ RE-DERIVED every number itself and ⛔ said so.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A METHOD IS ⛔ DURABLE; ⛔ A COUNT TAKEN FROM A ⛔ LIVE TREE IS ⛔ PERISHABLE ON THE ⛔ ORDER OF MINUTES. ⛔ HANDING SOMEBODY A FIGURE INVITES THEM TO ⛔ RECONCILE AGAINST IT; ⛔ HANDING THEM THE METHOD MAKES THEM ⛔ MEASURE.***
  - ✅ **WHEN RELAYING A TREE OBSERVATION: ⛔ give the ⛔ COMMAND and the ⛔ HAZARD, and ⛔ mark any number as ⛔ *"my instant, ⛔ re-derive yours"*.** ⛔ **⛔ A mismatch against a relayed figure is ⛔ EXPECTED, ⛔ not a finding.**
- ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ UNCHANGED BUT ⛔ NOW URGENT: ⛔ `gitStatus` in your context is a ⛔ CITATION. ⛔ MEASURE the tree yourself (⛔ `git status --porcelain`, ⛔ `git log -1`) ⛔ BEFORE acting on ⛔ any belief about what is committed.** ⛔ **If you hold ⛔ no `Bash` (⭐ `SC-§78`), you ⛔ CANNOT measure it ⇒ ⛔ you ⛔ MUST NOT assert it — ⛔ route the question to a role that can.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SNAPSHOT IS ⛔ MOST DANGEROUS ⛔ IMMEDIATELY AFTER A LARGE COMMIT, ⛔ BECAUSE THAT IS EXACTLY WHEN IT IS ⛔ MOST WRONG AND ⛔ MOST PLAUSIBLE — ⛔ IT DESCRIBES A STATE THAT ⛔ GENUINELY EXISTED, ⛔ MINUTES AGO, ⛔ IN DETAIL.***

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE RULE.** The `gitStatus` block in an agent's environment preamble is a **dated citation**, governed by `SC-§40` cl. 1 exactly like a relayed QA verdict or a manager's *"EXPECTED"* list. ⇒ ⛔ **ANY row that stages, commits, counts, or reasons about ⛔ dirty state ⛔ MUST derive it from a ⛔ LIVE `git status` at its ⛔ OWN instant.** ⛔ The snapshot may be used **only** as a hint to be confirmed, ⛔ and ⛔ never cited as the measurement.
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — WHY IT IS ⛔ MORE DANGEROUS THAN AN ORDINARY STALE CITATION, AND THIS IS THE ⛔ LOAD-BEARING HALF: ⛔ IT CARRIES `A ` ⛔ STAGED ENTRIES.** ⛔ A stale *file list* merely misleads. ⛔ **A stale ⛔ INDEX STATE is the ⛔ exact input a pathspec and a `§25b` cl. S reconciliation are ⛔ built from** ⇒ ⛔ **an agent that trusts it will "reconcile" against an index that ⛔ has not existed for weeks, ⛔ find agreement, ⛔ and report a clean sweep.** ⛔ The failure is ⛔ silent, ⛔ confident, and ⛔ shaped exactly like success.
3. ⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — IT IS ⛔ NOT ONE AGENT'S PROBLEM.** ⛔ **The preamble is issued to ⛔ EVERY agent in this pipeline, ⛔ every dispatch** ⇒ ⛔ this is a **standing, pipeline-wide** hazard, ⛔ not an incident. ⛔ Its ⛔ staleness is ⛔ silent: ⛔ nothing marks it, ⛔ nothing expires it, and ⛔ it appears in the same block as facts that ⛔ are current (⛔ cwd, ⛔ platform, ⛔ branch name).
4. ⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE CONDUCT THAT ⛔ EARNS THE CITATION, ⛔ AND IT IS CHEAP.** `TASK-966` ⛔ **noticed the divergence, ⛔ named it in its handoff, ⛔ said what a trusting reader would have got wrong, ⛔ and proceeded from its own live status.** ⇒ ⛔ **when the snapshot and your live measurement ⛔ DISAGREE, ⛔ RECORD THE DISAGREEMENT** — ⛔ do ⛔ not silently prefer the right one. ⛔ **The next reader needs to know the snapshot lies ⛔ here, ⛔ in this repo, ⛔ routinely.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP.** `SC-§40` cl. 1 supplies the ⛔ PRINCIPLE (⛔ a citation is not a fact); `SC-§38` supplies the ⛔ DECAY MODEL; `SC-§53` supplies the ⛔ ORIGIN (⛔ the description and the state ship at different times); ⛔ **this section names the ⛔ ONE citation that arrives ⛔ UNREQUESTED, ⛔ pre-installed, in ⛔ every agent's context — ⛔ and is therefore the ⛔ least likely to be questioned.** ⛔ **Sibling: `SC-§56`, ⛔ same batch, ⛔ same disease — ⛔ a well-formed answer that is ⛔ not a measurement.**

### SC-§56 ⛔⛔⛔ **`<commit>:<path>` IS A ⛔ TREE-ENTRY LOOKUP ⇒ A MISS IS AN ⛔ ERROR (⛔ LOUD). `-- <pathspec>` IS A ⛔ FILTER ⇒ A MISS IS AN ⛔ EMPTY RESULT (⛔ EXIT `0`, ⛔ EMPTY `stderr`, ⛔ SILENT). ⭐⭐ THE ⛔ SAME WRONG PATH IS ⛔ FATAL IN ONE FAMILY AND ⛔ INVISIBLE IN THE OTHER — AND THE ⛔ DOCUMENTED FIX FOR THE FIRST ⛔ MANUFACTURES THE SECOND.** (⛔⛔ **REWRITTEN 2026-09-03 by the manager — the original section's ⛔ OWN REMEDY was ⛔ INERT over ⛔ HALF its subject matter, and its cl. 2 ⛔ reassured the reader ⛔ FALSELY.** ⛔ **FIVE occurrences, ⛔ FOUR agents, ⛔ ONE day** — `TASK-967` (⛔ caught it), `TASK-966` (⛔ pre-empted it), ⛔ the manager itself, `TASK-964` (⛔ which added the `--full-name` "fix"), and ⛔⛔ `TASK-957` — ⛔ **THE FIRST OCCURRENCE ⛔ INVISIBLE TO THIS SECTION'S ⛔ OWN PRESCRIBED DETECTOR**, confirmed and mechanised at `qa/TASK-958.md` §6)

> ### ⛔⛔ **A HISTORY PROBE PRINTED ⛔ THREE CONFIDENT EMPTY RESULTS AND THE ROW READ THEM AS *"THE FIELD WAS NEVER THERE."* ⛔ THE FIELD WAS ALWAYS THERE; ⛔ THE PATH WAS WRONG BY ⛔ ONE DIRECTORY.**
> ### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THEN THE ⛔ DOCUMENTED FIX WAS APPLIED — AND HANDED THE NEXT AUTHOR A ⛔ CLEAN-LOOKING `433 → 0` WITH ⛔ EXIT `0`, ⛔ NOTHING ON `stderr`, AND ⛔ NOTHING FOR cl. 3 TO CATCH.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE LAYOUT FACT.** The repository root is **one level ABOVE** the project directory ⇒ ⛔ **repo-relative paths carry a `GitClaudeUnrealTest/` prefix.** ⛔ A file the shell sees as `Docs/Data/cards.csv` is `GitClaudeUnrealTest/Docs/Data/cards.csv` ⛔ to git's object database.
2. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE CLEAVAGE LINE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE SECTION. ⛔ THE GENERALISATION IS ⛔ NOT *"`git ls-tree` is special"* AND ⛔ NOT *"git is inconsistent"* — IT IS THE ⛔ FORM OF THE ARGUMENT: ⛔ TWO ⛔ DIFFERENT OPERATIONS THAT ⛔ LOOK IDENTICAL IN A SCRIPT.**
   > ⛔ **`<commit>:<path>` is a ⛔ TREE-ENTRY LOOKUP** ⇒ resolved against the ⛔ **REPO ROOT**, and a miss is an ⛔ **ERROR**: `fatal:`, ⛔ non-zero exit, ⛔ a line on `stderr`. ⛔ **LOUD.** *(family: `git show <c>:<p>` · `git cat-file`)*
   > ⛔ **`-- <pathspec>` is a ⛔ FILTER** ⇒ resolved against the ⛔ **CWD**, and a miss is an ⛔ **EMPTY RESULT**: ⛔ exit `0`, ⛔ `stderr` ⛔ EMPTY, ⛔ the answer ⛔ confidently wrong. ⛔ **SILENT.** *(family: `git log --` · `git ls-tree --` · `git grep --` · `git diff --` · `git rev-list --`)*
   ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ SAME STRING IS ⛔ FATAL IN ONE COMMAND AND ⛔ INVISIBLE IN THE NEXT LINE OF THE SAME SCRIPT**, ⛔ so the usual sanity check (*"but `git log` found it"*) ⛔ **actively CONFIRMS the wrong path.**
   ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ CORRECTION THIS REWRITE EXISTS TO MAKE, ⛔ STATED PLAINLY BECAUSE A READER MAY REMEMBER THE OLD TEXT: ⛔ THE FORMER cl. 2 SAID `git log -- <relpath>` *"WORKS"*. ⛔ THAT SENTENCE WAS A ⛔ FALSE REASSURANCE.** ⛔ It works for a ⛔ **CWD-relative** path. ⛔ Hand it a ⛔ **ROOT-relative** path — ⛔ **the `GitClaudeUnrealTest/` prefix ⛔ THIS VERY SECTION TEACHES YOU TO ADD** — and it returns a ⛔ **silent, confident, wrong zero.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE PREFIX THAT ⛔ FIXES THE LOOKUP FAMILY ⛔ BREAKS THE FILTER FAMILY. ⛔ Know which family you are in ⛔ BEFORE you touch the path.**
3. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE `stderr` REMEDY, ⛔ NOW ⛔ SCOPED — ⛔ BECAUSE IT WAS PUBLISHED ⛔ UNSCOPED AND IS ⛔ INERT OVER HALF ITS SUBJECT.**
   ⛔ **FOR THE ⛔ LOUD FAMILY ⛔ ONLY, the mechanism of the silence is that ⛔ THE ERROR GOES TO `stderr`.** ⛔ git ⛔ DOES complain — `fatal: path 'GitClaudeUnrealTest/Docs/Data/cards.csv' exists, but not 'Docs/Data/cards.csv'` ⇒ ⛔ **but a pipeline that reads only ⛔ `stdout` (⛔ `git show … | grep …`, ⛔ a `$(…)` capture, ⛔ a loop over output lines) ⛔ CONVERTS A FATAL INTO AN ⛔ EMPTY RESULT.** ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE LAW ⛔ FOR THAT FAMILY: a probe whose ⛔ empty output is ⛔ load-bearing must ⛔ capture `stderr` ⛔ or ⛔ check the exit status. ⛔ An unexamined `stderr` is ⛔ where measurements go to ⛔ become zeros.**
   ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ LIMIT, ⛔ IN THE SAME BREATH AND ⛔ NEVER AGAIN IN A SEPARATE ONE: ⛔ THIS REMEDY IS ⛔ INERT FOR THE ⛔ ENTIRE `--`-PATHSPEC FAMILY, ⛔ `git log -- <root-relative-path>` ⛔ INCLUDED.** ⛔ There, ⛔ **git is NOT FAILING**: a pathspec that matches nothing is a ⛔ **LEGITIMATE EMPTY RESULT** ⇒ ⛔ **there is ⛔ NOTHING to capture and ⛔ NOTHING to check.** ⛔⛔ **An agent that "hardened" a `git log --` / `git ls-tree --` probe by adding `2>&1` and testing `$?` has hardened ⛔ NOTHING — ⛔ and now believes it is safe.** ⛔ **That belief is worse than the original defect.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE FIX, ⛔ THREE FORMS, ⛔ AND FORM (c) IS ⛔ NOT INTERCHANGEABLE WITH THE FLAG THAT ⛔ LOOKS LIKE IT.**
   ⛔ **(a) ⛔ LOUD family** — `git show <commit>:./<path>` — ⛔ the `./` prefix forces ⛔ CWD-relative resolution and is the ⛔ cheapest fix.
   ⛔ **(b) ⛔ EITHER family** — `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` ⛔ once, then ⛔ prefix every repo-relative path ⛔ explicitly (⛔ what `TASK-966` did, ⛔ before being bitten) — ⛔ **and ⛔ know which family you are prefixing FOR** (⛔ cl. 2).
   ⛔⛔ **(c) ⛔ SILENT family — `git ls-tree -r <commit> --full-tree -- <root-relative-path>`.** ⛔ **`--full-tree` ⛔ OPERATES AS IF RUN FROM THE REPO ROOT ⇒ it changes the ⛔ PATHSPEC, ⛔ and it ⛔ IMPLIES `--full-name`.**
   ⚠️⛔⛔ **IN THE SAME SENTENCE, ⛔ NEVER A FOOTNOTE: ⛔ `--full-name` IS ⛔ NOT A SUBSTITUTE. ⛔ IT CHANGES THE ⛔ OUTPUT FORMAT ⛔ AND NOTHING ELSE. ⛔ `--full-name` + a ⛔ ROOT-RELATIVE PATHSPEC ⛔ IS THE SILENT VARIANT ITSELF** — the pathspec is ⛔ still resolved against the ⛔ **cwd**, so `GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/…` is sought at `GitClaudeUnrealTest/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/…`, ⛔ matches nothing, ⛔ exit `0`, ⛔ `stderr` empty. ⛔ **Measured, three variants, one subject: bare `-- <cwd-relative>` = `32` ✅ · `--full-name -- <root-relative>` = ⛔ `0` · `--full-tree -- <root-relative>` = `32` ✅.**
5. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE POSITIVE CONTROL IS ⛔ PROMOTED: it was a ⛔ DETECTION DUTY; it is now ⛔ THE PRIMARY REMEDY FOR THE `--` FAMILY, ⛔ BECAUSE FOR THAT FAMILY IT IS ⛔ THE ONLY ONE** (`SC-§39`). ⛔ **A probe that returns ⛔ EMPTY is ⛔ NOT a finding until the ⛔ SAME COMMAND SHAPE has been run against a ⛔ commit and a ⛔ path you ⛔ KNOW carry the value.** ⛔ **`TASK-967` did exactly this, ⛔ caught its own three zeros, and ⛔ reported the trap ⛔ instead of the false finding. ⛔⛔ `TASK-957` did exactly this and ⛔ caught a `433 → 0` that had ⛔ exit `0`, ⛔ empty `stderr`, ⛔ and would have passed ⛔ every check cl. 3 prescribes.** ⇒ 📌 ⛔⛔ **NO EMPTY GIT RESULT IS ⛔ PUBLISHABLE WITHOUT ITS CONTROL — ⛔ not as a finding, ⛔ not as a census, ⛔ not as a reassurance, ⛔ not as a zero in a handoff table.**
6. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — THE STING, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ REUSABLE PRIZE OF THIS SECTION: ⛔ THE ⛔ DOCUMENTED FIX ⛔ PRODUCED A ⛔ WORSE VARIANT OF THE TRAP — ⛔ ONE THAT IS ⛔ SILENT TO THE ⛔ PRESCRIBED DETECTOR.** ⛔ `--full-name` is ⛔ **precisely what `TASK-964` added to fix the `git show` half of ⛔ THIS VERY SECTION.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **FIXING THE ⛔ OUTPUT HALF ⛔ RE-ARMED THE ⛔ PATHSPEC HALF — ⛔ IN THE ⛔ SILENT FORM — ⛔ AND cl. 3, ⛔ THIS SECTION'S OWN REMEDY, ⛔ CANNOT SEE IT.** ⇒ 📌 ⛔⛔ **THE GENERAL LAW: ⛔ A REMEDY IS VALIDATED AGAINST THE ⛔ FAILURE IT DETECTS, ⛔ NEVER AGAINST THE ⛔ SUCCESS IT PRODUCES** — ⛔ **`SHIP-§9` ⛔ BINDS THIS DOCUMENT'S ⛔ OWN CLAUSES, ⛔ not only the code they govern.** ⛔ **A fix that has ⛔ only ever been seen ⛔ WORKING is ⛔ indistinguishable from a fix that ⛔ moved the failure somewhere quieter.**
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, ⛔ AND THE PATTERN IS NOW ⛔ FIVE DEEP.** `SC-§39` (⛔ a probe needs a positive control) · `FR-§5`'s *"⛔ **absent** outranks ⛔ **empty**"* — ⛔ **cl. 2 is that principle's ⛔ MECHANISM, stated for git: ⛔ the lookup family ⛔ IS "absent", the filter family ⛔ IS "empty"** · `SC-§55` (⛔ the stale snapshot) · ⚠️ `SC-§57` cl. 4's ⛔ **DATING CHECK — ⛔ which is run with `git log --` and is therefore ⛔ EXPOSED TO THIS EXACT TRAP; ⛔ that clause already says so and ⛔ means it** · ⛔ **this section.** ⛔ **All of them are the ⛔ SAME DISEASE: ⛔ an instrument returning a ⛔ well-formed, ⛔ confident, ⛔ WRONG answer with ⛔ NO ERROR SURFACE.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **TREAT ⛔ EVERY ZERO AS ⛔ GUILTY UNTIL CONTROLLED. ⛔ That is this section's ⛔ load-bearing sentence, and the `--full-name` variant is what ⛔ PROVES it: ⛔ every other clause here can be obeyed ⛔ to the letter and ⛔ still return the wrong number.**

### SC-§57 ⛔⛔⛔ **A COUNT MISMATCH IS A FACT ABOUT ⛔ TWO SETS ⛔ AND THE RULE THAT ⛔ JOINS THEM — ⭐ AND WHEN THE ⛔ SAME DISCREPANCY APPEARS ⛔ TWICE WITH THE ⛔ SAME EXPLANATION, THE ⛔ COMPARISON IS WRONG, ⛔ NOT THE ASSETS** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; bought by `TASK-967`, which was ⛔ **dispatched to find an orphan and instead ⛔ refuted its own row's premise**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **`14` UNIT BLUEPRINTS vs `13` `CardType Unit` ROWS. ⛔ I BOARDED IT AS A ⛔ FINDING. ⛔ IT WAS A ⛔ CATEGORY ERROR IN THE ⛔ DENOMINATOR — THE REAL CENSUS IS AN ⛔ EXACT `22 ↔ 22` BIJECTION WITH ⛔ ZERO ORPHANS IN ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE RULE.** ⛔ A mismatch between ⛔ *"assets on disk"* and ⛔ *"rows in a table"* has **THREE** candidate causes, ⛔ and *"an asset is orphaned"* is only the ⛔ **THIRD**: ⛔ **(i)** the ⛔ JOIN RULE is wrong · ⛔ **(ii)** an ⛔ INSTRUMENT is blind on one side (`SC-§39`) · ⛔ **(iii)** a ⛔ genuine orphan. ⇒ ⛔ **INTERROGATE (i) AND (ii) BEFORE REPORTING (iii)**, ⛔ because ⛔ only (iii) invites an edit ⛔ and an edit made on (i) ⛔ damages a correct file.
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — HOW THE JOIN WENT WRONG HERE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ COMMON SHAPE: THE DENOMINATOR WAS A ⛔ DECLARED FIELD WHERE THE CODE USES A ⛔ DERIVED CATEGORY.** ⛔ The naive denominator was *"rows with `CardType == Unit`"*; ⛔ **the shipped code branches on `Unit || Economy` minus `BuildingEconomyCardIDs`** ⇒ ⛔ the true denominator is *"rows whose ⛔ SPAWN CATEGORY is `UnitActor`"* = ⛔ `13 Unit + 1 Economy = 14`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **JOIN ON THE CATEGORY THE ⛔ CODE COMPUTES, ⛔ NOT THE FIELD THE ⛔ DATA DECLARES.** ⛔ Wherever a classifier exists, ⛔ **the census must call it** (⛔ cf. `SC-§37`: ⛔ a code-shape fact ⛔ outranks a coincidence of today's data).
3. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ CORROBORATION TEST, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ REUSABLE PRIZE OF THIS SECTION: ⛔ TWO INDEPENDENT INSTANCES OF THE ⛔ SAME DISCREPANCY WITH THE ⛔ SAME EXPLANATION MEANS THE ⛔ COMPARISON IS WRONG, ⛔ NOT THE ASSETS.** ⛔ What settled `TASK-967` was ⛔ **not** proving Miner innocent one asset at a time — ⛔ it was noticing the ⛔ **Buildings side showed an ⛔ IDENTICAL `8`-vs-`7` mismatch from an ⛔ IDENTICAL cause (`BP_Building_DeepMine`).** ⇒ ⛔ **a defect hypothesis must explain ⛔ WHY THE SAME ACCIDENT HAPPENED TWICE IN TWO UNRELATED FAMILIES; ⛔ a wrong-join hypothesis explains it ⛔ for free.** ⭐ **Coincidence is ⛔ evidence — ⛔ against the theory that requires it.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ DATING CHECK, ⛔ CHEAP AND ⛔ DECISIVE.** ⛔ Before calling a mismatch a ⛔ regression, ⛔ **ask git when it started.** ⛔ `BP_Unit_Miner` and its `Economy` row were born in the ⛔ **SAME COMMIT** (`5bb9507`, 2026-07-04) ⛔ and the row has ⛔ never been anything else ⇒ ⛔ **there is ⛔ no drift event, ⛔ nothing ever moved, ⛔ and the tree has looked like this ⛔ since the day it shipped.** ⛔ **A discrepancy that is ⛔ AS OLD AS THE FEATURE is ⛔ overwhelmingly a ⛔ misreading, ⛔ not a break.** ⚠️ **Run that check under `SC-§56` ⛔ or your three zeros will ⛔ "prove" the opposite.**
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ MANAGER'S SHARE, ⛔ NAMED.** ⛔ **I wrote `14`-vs-`13` into the row ⛔ TITLE, in bold, as a ⛔ premise.** ⛔ A row whose ⛔ headline asserts the discrepancy ⛔ pushes its assignee toward (iii). ⭐ **`SC-§52`'s question-framing (⛔ *"⛔ WHICH ONE, AND ⛔ IS THAT CORRECT?"*) ⛔ plus a ⛔ verdict vocabulary containing a ⛔ NO-DEFECT MEMBER is ⛔ what saved it** ⇒ ⛔ **when boarding a count mismatch, ⛔ the `SC-§52` treatment is ⛔ MANDATORY, ⛔ not optional.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** `TL-§5` (⛔ a count is a fact about a ⛔ SCOPE) supplies the ⛔ numerator half; ⛔ **this supplies the ⛔ DENOMINATOR half — ⛔ a count is also a fact about the ⛔ JOIN.** `SC-§37` supplies ⛔ code-shape-over-data-coincidence; `SC-§52` supplies the ⛔ boarding discipline; `SC-§39` supplies the ⛔ blind-instrument branch.

### SC-§58 ⛔⛔ **WHEN THE ⛔ DISPATCH IS ⛔ LOOSER THAN THE ⛔ ROW, THE AGENT ⛔ REPORTS THE CONTRADICTION — ⛔ IT DOES ⛔ NOT RESOLVE IT. ⭐ THE ⛔ ROW WINS; THE ⛔ MANAGER RECONCILES.** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; bought by `TASK-967`, which ⛔ **refused to flip its own board status** because its `names:` clause was ⛔ stricter than the dispatch that sent it)

> ### ⛔ **MY DISPATCH SAID IT COULD UPDATE *"YOUR OWN STATUS."* ⛔ ITS ROW SAID *"WRITES ⛔ EXACTLY ONE FILE."* ⛔ IT DID THE ⛔ NARROWER THING AND ⛔ TOLD ME THE TWO DISAGREED.**

1. ⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE ⛔ PRECEDENCE.** `TASKBOARD.md` is the ⛔ **contract** (`CLAUDE.md`: ⛔ files are the contract). ⛔ A dispatch prompt is a ⛔ **delivery mechanism** for it. ⇒ ⛔ **where they conflict, the ⛔ ROW BINDS, and the ⛔ NARROWER of the two is obeyed.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ⛔ DUTY, ⛔ AND IT IS THE POINT: ⛔ OBEYING THE NARROWER SCOPE IS ⛔ NOT ENOUGH. ⛔ THE CONTRADICTION ITSELF IS A ⛔ DELIVERABLE.** ⛔ **Report it ⛔ explicitly, ⛔ in the handoff and ⛔ in Slack.** ⛔ **A silently-resolved contradiction leaves the manager believing its instruction was ⛔ followed as written**, ⛔ so the ⛔ same defective dispatch ⛔ ships again ⛔ next batch. ⛔ **The cost of reporting is ⛔ one sentence; ⛔ the cost of silence ⛔ recurs.**
3. ⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — WHICH WAY IT ⛔ RUNS.** ⛔ This governs **dispatch ⛔ LOOSER than row** ⇒ ⛔ take the row, ⛔ report. ⛔ **The reverse — a dispatch ⛔ STRICTER than the row — is ⛔ also obeyed ⛔ narrowly and ⛔ also reported** (⛔ a mid-task course correction is ⛔ legitimate and is ⛔ often exactly that). ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **IN BOTH DIRECTIONS: ⛔ DO THE NARROWER THING, ⛔ THEN SAY THE TWO DISAGREED.** ⛔ **⛔ NEVER take the wider grant on the strength of the looser source.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ SPECIFIC HAZARD THIS CAUGHT, ⛔ WORTH NAMING: ⛔ SELF-FLIPPING A BOARD ROW.** ⛔ `TASKBOARD.md` is under a ⛔ standing concurrent-writer hazard ⛔ and is ⛔ excluded from nearly every row's pathspec. ⇒ ⛔ **an agent inferring a board-write grant from a ⛔ loosely-worded dispatch is ⛔ exactly the race the exclusion exists to prevent.** ⛔ **Board flips are the ⛔ manager's, ⛔ by default, ⛔ always.**
4a. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4a — THE ⛔ THIRD AXIS, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-03 (bought by `TASK-957`, ⛔ ruled at `qa/TASK-958.md` §5): ⛔ WHEN THE ROW'S ⛔ OWN CITED LAW POINTS ⛔ ONE WAY AND THE ROW'S ⛔ HINT POINTS ⛔ ANOTHER, ⛔ THE ⛔ LAW WINS — ⛔ AND THE DIFFERENCE IS ⛔ DECLARED.**
   ⛔ **The case, ⛔ recorded as the ⛔ RIGHT CONDUCT so it is ⛔ copied rather than ⛔ re-derived:** the row's spec hinted the roster be read off ⛔ `ASiegePlayerController`'s CDO; the row's ⛔ **own top-cited law**, `SHIP-§9c` cl. 2, requires ⛔ **the predicate the CONSUMER itself uses** — ⛔ and the ⛔ card-art consumer is ⛔ `UCardHandWidget`. ⛔ **The author read the roster off the ⛔ CONSUMER's CDO, read the controller's ⛔ TOO, ⛔ ASSERTED THE TWO EQUAL, and ⛔ DECLARED the deviation.**
   ⇒ ⛔ **RULED ⛔ IN SCOPE, ⛔ and ⛔ not merely tolerated: ⛔ obeying the controller hint would have walked a table the ⛔ card-face path does not necessarily use, ⛔ in the one row whose ⛔ entire thesis is *"use the consumer's own predicate."*** ⭐ **The widening also ⛔ PINS SOMETHING REAL — if the two table paths ⛔ diverged, the card FACE would read art from a ⛔ different table than the SPAWN path resolves against, and ⛔ every existing gate would stay ⛔ GREEN.**
   📌 ⛔ **THE TEST FOR THE AGENT, ⛔ IN ONE LINE: a ⛔ HINT is a ⛔ delivery detail; a ⛔ CITED LAW is the ⛔ contract. ⛔ Take the LAW, ⛔ keep the hint's target ⛔ as a corroborating assertion where it is cheap, ⛔ and ⛔ SAY THE TWO DISAGREED.** ⛔ **What ⛔ FAILS here is ⛔ SILENCE — ⛔ never the deviation.** ⛔ **And the ⛔ MANAGER's share: ⛔ a hint that contradicts the row's own cited law is a ⛔ DEFECTIVE ROW, ⛔ and I reconcile it, ⛔ not the agent** (cl. 1).
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP.** `SC-§40` cl. 1 (⛔ a relay is a citation) · `SC-§54` cl. 1 (⛔ a refusal is ⛔ reported, ⛔ never routed around) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9c` cl. 2 (⛔ the ⛔ CONSUMER's predicate — ⛔ the law cl. 4a exists to protect) · ⛔ **this section extends the same etiquette from ⛔ TOOL refusals to ⛔ INSTRUCTION conflicts, ⛔ and cl. 4a extends it again to ⛔ LAW-vs-HINT conflicts ⛔ inside a single row: ⛔ in all three, the agent's job is to ⛔ SURFACE the constraint, ⛔ not to ⛔ dissolve it.**

### SC-§59 ⛔⛔⛔ **UNDER PARALLEL LANES, ⛔ ATTRIBUTE A DIRTY FILE BY WHAT IT ⛔ CONTAINS — ⛔ NEVER BY WHO ⛔ SAYS THEY TOUCHED IT, AND ⛔ NEVER BY A MARKER ALONE. ⭐⭐ A ⛔ CONTENT CENSUS IS A ⛔ STRUCTURAL ARGUMENT; A ⛔ MARKER CENSUS IS ⛔ ONLY CORROBORATION.** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; bought by `TASK-958`, whose spec made a named file's dirtiness a ⛔ **BLOCKER** — ⛔ the file ⛔ **WAS** dirty, ⛔ **and it belonged to another lane**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **MY OWN GATE ITEM SAID *"A `SiegePlayerController.cpp` WRITE ⇒ BLOCKER."* ⛔ THE FILE WAS `M` IN THE TREE. ⛔ THE REVIEWER DID ⛔ NOT TAKE THE HANDOFF'S WORD THAT IT BELONGED TO SOMEONE ELSE — ⛔ IT ⛔ MEASURED, AND A ⛔ WRONG FAIL AGAINST A ⛔ CORRECT ROW WAS ⛔ AVOIDED.**

> ### ⭐⭐ **THE SENTENCE THAT SETTLED IT, ⛔ RECORDED VERBATIM BECAUSE THE ⛔ ARGUMENT IS BETTER THAN ANY MARKER CHECK:**
> ### ⛔ ***"`CardArt` appears 0 times in `SiegePlayerController.cpp` … ⛔ A CARD-ART ROW CANNOT HAVE EDITED A FILE CONTAINING ⛔ NO CARD-ART CODE."***

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — WHEN IT BINDS.** ⛔ This pipeline runs ⛔ **concurrent lanes over one worktree** ⇒ ⛔ **a reviewer's tree is ⛔ routinely dirty with files ⛔ NO ROW UNDER REVIEW TOUCHED** (⛔ five `M` paths, here). ⇒ ⛔ **ANY gate item, pathspec, or handoff claim that turns on *"which row wrote this file"* ⛔ MUST MEASURE THE ATTRIBUTION.** ⛔ **Inferring it from ⛔ adjacency in time, ⛔ from a dispatch's file list, or ⛔ from the author's assurance is ⛔ NOT a measurement** (`SC-§40` cl. 1).
2. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE METHOD, ⛔ RANKED ⛔ WEAKEST TO STRONGEST. ⛔ USE THE STRONGEST THAT APPLIES, ⛔ AND SAY WHICH YOU USED.**
   ⛔ **(i) THE HANDOFF'S WORD — ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE.** ⛔ It is a ⛔ citation, and a ⛔ row cannot certify its own diff.
   ⛔ **(ii) A ⛔ TASK-MARKER CENSUS (`grep -rn "TASK-###" Source/`) — ⛔ CORROBORATION ⛔ ONLY.** ⛔ **A shipped file can be edited ⛔ WITHOUT leaving a marker** ⇒ ⛔ a marker zero is ⛔ consistent with innocence, ⛔ never proof of it. ⛔ **And it is ⛔ worthless uncontrolled: ⛔ grep a marker you have ⛔ MEASURED PRESENT in a shipped header ⛔ before trusting the zero** (`SC-§39`).
   ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(iii) THE ⛔ CONTENT CENSUS — ⛔ THE STRUCTURAL ARGUMENT, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ STRONGEST INSTRUMENT A ⛔ GIT-LESS REVIEWER HAS.** ⛔ **Ask whether the file contains ⛔ ANY code of the ⛔ KIND this row writes.** ⛔ A row about ⛔ X ⛔ cannot have edited a file with ⛔ ZERO ⛔ X in it. ⇒ ⛔ **this is a fact about the ⛔ FILE, ⛔ not about anyone's ⛔ testimony, and it ⛔ survives a missing marker, a ⛔ stale snapshot and a ⛔ lying handoff alike.**
   ⛔ **(iv) THE ⛔ POSITIVE ATTRIBUTION — ⛔ NAME THE ROW THAT ⛔ DOES OWN IT.** ⛔ *"Not mine"* is half an answer; ⛔ **find the other lane's marker ⛔ in the file and the ⛔ board row whose `names:` block ⛔ claims it.** ⛔ **An unattributed dirty file is ⛔ still an open question.**
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ DOUBLE CONTROL IS ⛔ MANDATORY, ⛔ AND ⛔ ONE OF THEM IS ⛔ NOT ENOUGH** (`SC-§39`). ⛔ For every published zero: ⛔ **(a)** show the ⛔ NEEDLE works — the same needle returns ⛔ NON-ZERO ⛔ somewhere you measured it present (⛔ `CardArt` = **119** across the module) ⛔ **AND (b)** show the ⛔ FILE is not blind — a ⛔ DIFFERENT needle returns ⛔ NON-ZERO in ⛔ THAT SAME FILE (⛔ `TEXT("/Game` = **32** in it). ⛔ **(a) alone leaves an unreadable file indistinguishable from a clean one; ⛔ (b) alone leaves a broken needle indistinguishable from a true zero.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ LEDGER IS A ⛔ DELIVERABLE, ⛔ AND SO IS ITS ⛔ LIMIT.** ⛔ Publish a ⛔ table: ⛔ **path · attributed to · ⛔ HOW attributed** — ⛔ one row per dirty path, ⛔ including the ones you are ⛔ clearing. ⛔⛔ **AND STATE THE HONEST LIMIT: a content census proves ⛔ NOT-THIS-ROW; ⛔ it does ⛔ NOT prove a ⛔ ZERO DIFF.** ⛔ **The binding positive check is a ⛔ LIVE `git status` at the integrating row's own instant ⇒ ⛔ NAME THAT ROW as owing it** (⛔ and see `SC-§55`: ⛔ the context snapshot is ⛔ NOT that status).
5. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — WHY THIS OUTRANKS A MISSED WARN, ⛔ STATED SO NOBODY TRADES IT AWAY: ⛔ A ⛔ WRONG FAIL IS THE ⛔ EXPENSIVE ERROR.** ⛔ It sends a ⛔ CORRECT row back through a QA loop, ⛔ burns one of the ⛔ THREE, ⛔ delays a lane that was ⛔ ready, ⛔ and ⛔ teaches the next author that ⛔ another lane's dirt is ⛔ their defect. ⇒ ⛔ **when a gate item names a FILE as a blocker, ⛔ the reviewer's ⛔ FIRST duty is ⛔ attribution, ⛔ not verdict.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** `SC-§39` (⛔ the control) · `SC-§40` cl. 1 (⛔ a relay is a citation) · `SC-§55` (⛔ the stale `gitStatus` — ⛔ **the exact instrument that would have produced the wrong attribution**) · `TL-§5b` cl. 3 (⛔ concurrent lanes make every census ⛔ stale before the ink dries — ⛔ **the `+4` declarations here were ⛔ another lane's and looked like this row's**) · `SC-§57` cl. 1 (⛔ interrogate the ⛔ JOIN and the ⛔ INSTRUMENT before reporting a ⛔ defect). ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ ATTRIBUTE BY ⛔ CONTENT, ⛔ CORROBORATE BY ⛔ MARKER, ⛔ CONFIRM BY ⛔ LIVE STATUS — ⛔ IN THAT ORDER, ⛔ AND ⛔ NEVER BY TESTIMONY.**

### SC-§70 ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A MEASUREMENT QUOTED ⛔ WITHOUT ITS ⛔ CAUSE READS AS A PROPERTY OF THE ⛔ CODE WHEN IT IS A PROPERTY OF A ⛔ GUARD.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought on `TASK-980-A`, and the manager ⛔ AND coordinator ⛔ BOTH relayed the defective sentence — the manager ⛔ once in a board row, the coordinator ⛔ twice in a dispatch**)

- ⛔ **THE SENTENCE: `qa/TASK-979.md` §3a measured *"`SummonedUnit.cpp` contains ⛔ ZERO fog symbols"* — ⛔ TRUE, ⛔ and quoted onward as a ⛔ LUCKY STRUCTURAL FACT proving a collapse was ⛔ *"structurally unrepresentable"*.**
- ⛔⛔ **IT IS ⛔ NOT LUCK. ⛔ IT IS AN ⛔ ENFORCED INVARIANT WITH A ⛔ GUARD BEHIND IT: `Tests/SiegeAcquisitionFunnelTest.cpp` test 9's `DecisionTokens` pins `FSiegeFogStatics` and `EffectiveVisionRadius` at ⛔ `0` in that file, calling itself *"⛔ THE PERMANENT LAW … an ⛔ automatic QA FAIL — ⛔ do NOT fix a red here by relaxing this row."***
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE BOARD THEN ⛔ QUOTED THE MEASUREMENT AS ⛔ REASSURANCE AND ⛔ ASKED AN AGENT TO ⛔ BE THE CHANGE THAT ⛔ BREAKS THE GUARD.** ⛔ **The agent could ⛔ not have known from the quoted sentence — ⛔ the cause was ⛔ stripped in the relay.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ A MEASUREMENT QUOTED ⛔ WITHOUT ITS ⛔ CAUSE READS AS A ⛔ PROPERTY OF THE CODE WHEN IT IS A ⛔ PROPERTY OF A GUARD — ⛔ and the two have ⛔ OPPOSITE implications for whether you may ⛔ change it.*** ⛔ **A ⛔ property invites you to rely on it; ⛔ a ⛔ guard forbids you to break it.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ when quoting a ⛔ ZERO or an ⛔ ABSENCE as a fact, ⛔ STATE WHETHER ANYTHING ⛔ ENFORCES IT, and ⛔ NAME the enforcer.** ⛔ **`"X is 0"` is ⛔ incomplete; ⛔ `"X is 0, pinned by <file>:<test>"` or ⛔ `"X is 0, unenforced"` is ⛔ the finding.**
- ⛔ **AND IT ⛔ COMPOUNDS THROUGH RELAYS: this one travelled ⛔ QA → coordinator → board → dispatch, ⛔ gaining authority at every hop while the ⛔ cause stayed behind at hop one** (⛔ the `SC-§66` failure mode, ⛔ on a different payload).
- ⭐ **SIBLING OF `SC-§63`** (⛔ a pin written against today's call graph becomes a trap for the ⛔ first legitimate second consumer). ⛔ **`§63` is about the ⛔ PIN; ⛔ this is about the ⛔ CITATION OF WHAT THE PIN PRODUCES.**

### SC-§140 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A TREND IS A PROPERTY OF THE ⛔ POPULATION, ⛔ NEVER OF THE ⛔ SAMPLES YOU HAPPENED TO HAVE OPEN. ⛔ THREE READINGS PULLED FROM ⛔ TEN, ⛔ PUT IN ORDER, AND PUBLISHED AS *"ESCALATING"* IS A ⛔ SUB-SELECTION WEARING A ⛔ TREND — ⛔ AND THE AUTHOR WAS THE ⛔ MANAGER, ⛔ ONE DAY AFTER MINTING ⭐ `SC-§138`.** (added 2026-09-21 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1364` §2.5 — ⛔ a census that was asked whether ⛔ THREE readings were real and ⛔ censused the ⛔ WHOLE POPULATION ⛔ UNASKED, ⛔ refuting its own row's ⛔ headline premise** · marker `SC-140-A-TREND-IS-A-PROPERTY-OF-THE-POPULATION`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⛔ The manager boarded ⭐ `TASK-1364` under the headline *"⛔ THREE ⛔ ESCALATING readings"* — ⛔ `8.242 → 172.082 → 382.789 MB` of the D3D12 `Video memory has been exhausted (N MB over budget)` banner. ⛔ **Each figure is ⛔ REAL and each was ⛔ re-verified at source.** ⛔ The census then did the thing nobody asked for: it ⛔ **grepped the identical string across the ⛔ WHOLE of `.claude/pipeline/`** and found ⛔ **TEN** recorded readings — ⛔ `0.922` · `8.242` · `15x.xxx` · `23.391` · `168.906` · `172.082` · `382.789` · `450.516` · `828.144` · `2798.546 MB`. ⇒ ⛔ **the trio ranks ⛔ 2nd, ⛔ 6th and ⛔ 7th SMALLEST of the ten**, with a reading ⛔ FAR BELOW it (`0.922`) and one ⛔ **~7.3× ABOVE its maximum** (`2798.546`), ⛔ **both from EARLIER dates** (`828.144` carries an explicit `2026-09-06` at source; the rest are labelled *"earlier wave"*). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ MONOTONE WITHIN THE THREE. ⛔ NOT MONOTONE OVER THE RECORD. ⛔ "ESCALATING" WAS TRUE OF MY SAMPLE AND ⛔ FALSE OF THE THING MY SAMPLE WAS STANDING IN FOR.***
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, AND IT IS ⛔ ONE COMMAND: ⛔ BEFORE THE WORDS *"ESCALATING" · "RISING" · "GROWING" · "WORSENING" · "DEGRADING" · "TREND"* ARE WRITTEN ABOUT ⛔ N READINGS, ⛔ CENSUS THE ⛔ SEARCH SPACE FOR THE ⛔ SAME STRING AND ⛔ PUBLISH THE ⛔ DENOMINATOR.** ⛔ *"N readings, rising"* is ⛔ INCOMPLETE. ⛔ *"N of M readings found in `<space>`, and here is the ⛔ whole M ordered by ⛔ DATE"* is ⛔ the finding. ⇒ ⛔ **THIS IS ⭐ `SC-§70`'s DUTY (⛔ a zero without its search space is ⛔ half a fact) ⛔ APPLIED TO AN ⛔ ORDER INSTEAD OF TO A ⛔ ZERO** — ⛔ and it is ⛔ cheaper than the zero case, because the ⛔ needle is ⛔ already in your hand: ⛔ you ⛔ quoted it.
3. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — WHY IT IS ⛔ SEDUCTIVE, ⛔ STATED AS ARITHMETIC SO IT CANNOT BE ARGUED WITH: ⛔ ANY THREE DISTINCT NUMBERS CAN ⛔ ALWAYS BE ARRANGED INTO A MONOTONE SEQUENCE. ⛔ SORTING ⛔ NEVER FAILS.** ⇒ ⛔ **a monotone triple carries ⛔ information ⛔ ONLY IF the ⛔ ORDER YOU MET THEM IN was ⛔ NOT CHOSEN BY YOU.** ⛔ Here it ⛔ WAS: the three were ⛔ *"the reports of the current wave"*, and I presented ⛔ **what I had open** as ⛔ **what happened over time.** 🚨 ⛔ **RECENCY IS A ⛔ SELECTION CRITERION. ⛔ It correlates with ⛔ WHAT I WAS READING and with ⛔ NOTHING WHATEVER about the quantity being read.**
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ SPECIES, AND IT IS ⭐ `SC-§137`'s ⛔ FAMILY MOVED ONE LEVEL UP: ⭐ `SC-§137` is an ⛔ INSTRUMENT that answers ⛔ confidently in the ⛔ WRONG DIALECT; ⛔ THIS is a ⛔ SAMPLE that answers ⛔ confidently about the ⛔ WRONG POPULATION.** ⛔ **Neither ERRORS. ⛔ Both return a number that is ⛔ ARITHMETICALLY CORRECT ABOUT THE ⛔ WRONG OBJECT** — ⛔ and ⛔ `8.242 < 172.082 < 382.789` is ⛔ as true as a sum can be. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE DEFECT WAS ⛔ NEVER IN THE THREE NUMBERS. ⛔ IT WAS IN THE ⛔ QUANTIFIER I WRAPPED AROUND THEM.***
5. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — WHAT ⛔ SURVIVES, ⛔ BECAUSE AN OVER-CORRECTION HERE WOULD ⛔ DESTROY GOOD EVIDENCE.** ⛔ Refuting the ⛔ TREND does ⛔ **NOT** refute the ⛔ READINGS: ⛔ all three figures ⛔ STAND, ⛔ the instrument ⛔ IS one instrument (⛔ same emitter, ⛔ same string, ⛔ same units), ⛔ all three are ⛔ PIXEL-ONLY against a ⛔ MEASURED log zero with a ⛔ firing control, and ⛔ no row that recorded the banner is ⛔ invalidated or ⛔ re-opened. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ KILL THE CLAIM, ⛔ KEEP THE MEASUREMENTS.**
