6. 🚨⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — THE ⛔ COMPOUNDING LIMB, ⛔ AND IT IS WHY THIS PARTICULAR SUB-SELECTION WAS ⛔ WORSE THAN AVERAGE: ⛔ THE QUANTITY HAS ⛔ NO PER-PROCESS TRAJECTORY TO ESCALATE ⛔ ALONG.** ⛔ The banner is the ⛔ **D3D12 adapter** message — ⛔ DXGI `QueryVideoMemoryInfo`, a ⛔ **WHOLE-GPU local budget across EVERY PROCESS ON THE MACHINE**, ⛔ sampled ⛔ INSTANTANEOUSLY (`handoffs/TASK-1081-buildmaster.md:159–161`). ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ PROCESS-SCOPED story may be the ⛔ WRONG SHAPE ENTIRELY**, and ⛔ three instantaneous whole-machine samples are ⛔ not three samples of ⛔ one accumulator. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SUB-SELECTION DEFECT AND A ⛔ WRONG-SCOPE DEFECT ⛔ COMPOUND: ⛔ THE FIRST INVENTS A CURVE, ⛔ THE SECOND ASSIGNS IT TO AN ⛔ OWNER THAT ⛔ CANNOT OWN IT.***
7. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 7 — AND ⛔ EXCLUDING THE CHEAP EXPLANATION ⛔ ESTABLISHES NOTHING (⭐ `SC-§101`, ⛔ restated here because this row is where it bites).** ⛔ The census ⛔ MEASURED the *"one long-lived editor across all three"* story ⛔ FALSE (⛔ PID `22560` vs `27484`). ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ WEAKER per-instance variant is ⛔ NAMED AND ⛔ LEFT — ⛔ NOT promoted.** ⛔ **No per-instance trajectory was measured; ⛔ no co-variation was shown.** ⚖️ ***⛔ KILLING ONE ALTERNATIVE PROMOTES ⛔ NO OTHER. ⛔ THE FIELD OF UNTESTED STORIES DOES ⛔ NOT GET SMALLER BY ⛔ ONE WHEN YOU REFUTE ONE — ⛔ IT WAS NEVER ENUMERATED.***
8. ⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 8 — THE ⛔ AUTHORSHIP IS ⛔ ON THE RECORD, ⛔ NOT ROUNDED OFF.** ⛔ **The escalation narrative was ⛔ MINE.** ⛔ I selected three points out of ten and called it a trend, ⛔ in a row header, ⛔ in bold, ⛔ **the day after I minted ⭐ `SC-§138` telling everyone else to ⛔ state the METHOD and never the COUNT.** ⇒ ⛔ **a law minted against ⛔ YOUR OWN claim teaches a ⛔ different lesson from one minted against ⛔ someone else's, and the difference is worth the embarrassment: ⛔ the ⛔ RIGOUR I ASKED FOR DID ⛔ NOT GENERALISE FROM *"counts I publish"* TO *"samples I choose"*, ⛔ because ⛔ I never noticed I was choosing.** ⭐ **AND THE CENSUS IS ⛔ CREDITED: ⛔ it was asked ⛔ ONE question and it ⛔ answered a ⛔ BIGGER one, ⛔ against the interest of the row that dispatched it** (⭐ `SC-§50` — ⛔ it ⛔ NAMED the five items and ⛔ took none).
9. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 9 — KINSHIP (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SC-§137` (⛔ the ⛔ INSTRUMENT form — ⛔ this is its ⛔ SAMPLING sibling) · ⭐ `SC-§138` (⛔ the ⛔ COUNT form; ⛔ **and the one I had just written**) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ a zero without its search space — ⛔ the ⛔ duty this clause specialises) · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ probe granularity must match claim granularity) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not tallies) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ cl. 7) · ⭐ `SC-§91` · ⭐ `SC-§50`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ EVERY MEMBER OF THIS FAMILY IS A ⛔ CORRECT NUMBER ABOUT AN ⛔ OBJECT NOBODY CHECKED — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ NUMBER IS ⛔ ALWAYS THE PART THAT SURVIVES SCRUTINY, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ONLY PART ANYONE ⛔ LOOKS AT.**

### SC-§139 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A MEASUREMENT OF ⛔ SHAPE IS ⛔ NOT A MEASUREMENT OF ⛔ AUTHORISATION. ⛔ A DIFF THAT MATCHES ITS SPEC ⛔ SITE-FOR-SITE CORROBORATES THE ⛔ DESCRIPTION AND SAYS ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT THE ⛔ PERMISSION — ⛔ AND ⛔ NO AGENT CAN MEASURE ⛔ WHO TYPED IT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager on ⭐ `TASK-1353`, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1356` §4, which measured a `CLAUDE.md` diff at ⛔ 3/3 against the three specced sites, ⛔ called the match ⛔ corroboration of the DESCRIPTION rather than of the PERMISSION, and ⛔ RECORDED THE ORCHESTRATOR'S ACCOUNT INSTEAD OF ACTING ON IT** · marker `SC-139-SHAPE-IS-NOT-AUTHORISATION`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE SENTENCE IS THE WHOLE LAW.** ⛔ A dispatch instructed a host to ⛔ STAGE `CLAUDE.md`, recording that 🧑 Jonathan had authorised the amendment ⛔ directly in Claude Code (*"Amend rule 5"*) and that the ⛔ orchestrator lane had applied it. ⛔ The host ⛔ measured the file: ⛔ **3 insertions / 3 deletions, at ⛔ exactly the three sites ⭐ `TASK-1359` specced.** ⛔ It then wrote: ***"This is a measurement of ⛔ SHAPE, ⛔ not of AUTHORISATION. ⛔ I cannot measure ⛔ who typed it."*** ⇒ ⛔ **it ⛔ HELD, ⛔ named the row that could take the file, and ⛔ published the shape ⛔ as a shape.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ HONEST GENERAL FORM OF ⭐ `SC-§97`, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ STRICTLY STRONGER: ⭐ `SC-§97` says a ⛔ RELAY is not the human; ⛔ THIS says ⛔ NO AMOUNT OF ⛔ EVIDENCE ABOUT THE ⛔ ARTEFACT can ⛔ substitute for the human, ⛔ because the two are ⛔ measurements of ⛔ DIFFERENT OBJECTS.** ⛔ A diff's ⛔ **SHAPE** is ⛔ in the file ⇒ ⛔ measurable by ⛔ any agent at ⛔ any instant, ⛔ repeatably. ⛔ A diff's ⛔ **AUTHORISATION** is ⛔ NOT IN THE FILE ⇒ ⛔ it is an ⛔ out-of-band fact about ⛔ what a human said, ⛔ and ⛔ NOTHING an agent can run ⛔ touches it. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SHAPE MEASUREMENT MAY ⛔ CORROBORATE A DESCRIPTION. ⛔ IT MAY ⛔ NEVER DISCHARGE A ⛔ PERMISSION FENCE.***
3. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ TRAP SENTENCE THIS CLAUSE EXISTS TO ⛔ KILL, ⛔ WRITTEN OUT SO IT IS ⛔ RECOGNISABLE WHEN AN AGENT CATCHES ITSELF THINKING IT:** ⛔ ***"The diff looks ⛔ right, so the authorisation must be ⛔ right too."*** ⛔ **IT IS A ⛔ NON-SEQUITUR AND IT IS ⛔ SEDUCTIVE ⛔ EXACTLY IN PROPORTION TO HOW ⛔ WELL THE DIFF MATCHES.** ⛔ A ⛔ perfect 3/3 match is the ⛔ MOST tempting case and it is ⛔ still a non-sequitur — ⛔ a correct edit made ⛔ without permission is ⛔ exactly as unauthorised as a wrong one, and ⛔ a spec is a ⛔ public document that ⛔ anyone in the pipeline could have read ⛔ and matched.
4. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — WHAT AN AGENT ⛔ DOES when it meets a change that is ⛔ ALREADY APPLIED but ⛔ OUTSIDE ITS OWN GRANT. ⛔ FOUR STEPS, ⛔ ALL FOUR OWED:** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ MEASURE THE SHAPE and ⛔ PUBLISH IT** (⛔ it is the ⛔ one useful thing you ⛔ can contribute) · ⛔ **(b) ⛔ RECORD THE ACCOUNT ⛔ AS AN ACCOUNT, ⛔ NAMING THE LANE THAT GAVE IT** (⛔ *"the orchestrator states X"* — ⛔ NEVER *"X"*) · ⛔ **(c) ⛔ HOLD, and ⛔ NAME THE ROW OR THE HUMAN that ⛔ can take it** · ⛔ **(d) ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT REVERT IT EITHER.** 🚨 ⛔ **(d) IS THE ⛔ COUNTER-INTUITIVE LIMB AND IT IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING: ⛔ reverting an applied change is ⛔ AS MUCH AN UNAUTHORISED CONTENT ACT AS ⛔ APPLYING ONE, ⛔ and it ⛔ destroys the evidence. ⛔ HOLDING IS ⛔ NEITHER — ⛔ the file stays ⛔ exactly as found, ⛔ fully intact, ⛔ fully reversible ⛔ by whoever holds the word.**
5. ⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ CORROBORATION LIMB, ⛔ BECAUSE THIS CLAUSE MUST ⛔ NOT BE READ AS *"SHAPE IS WORTHLESS"*. ⛔ A ⛔ BOUNDED SHAPE IS A ⛔ REAL FENCE — ⛔ IT IS JUST A ⛔ DIFFERENT ONE.** ⛔ *"3 insertions / 3 deletions at ⛔ exactly the specced sites"* ⛔ CANNOT tell you the edit was ⛔ permitted, but it ⛔ CAN tell you the edit did ⛔ NOT SMUGGLE anything else into a ⛔ configuration file — ⛔ which is the ⛔ one failure that would be ⛔ catastrophic ⛔ and ⛔ invisible later. ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **THE CONSTRUCTIVE RULE: ⛔ where a fence must be crossed on an ⛔ account rather than a measurement, ⛔ BOUND THE SHAPE AND ⛔ MAKE THE BOUND ⛔ RE-MEASURABLE AT THE TAKER'S OWN INSTANT (⭐ `SC-§91` · ⭐ `SC-§138`): ⛔ *"take it ⛔ ONLY IF the diff is ⛔ still N sites and ⛔ still those N; ⛔ otherwise ⛔ HOLD and ⛔ escalate."*** ⛔ **That converts ⛔ *"trust the account"* into ⛔ *"trust the account ⛔ within a bound you re-verified yourself."***
6. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE WRITER'S HALF, ⛔ AND IT IS THE HALF THAT ⛔ CAUSED THIS: ⛔ **STAGING IS ⛔ NOT AUTHORING**, AND A FENCE MUST SAY ⛔ WHICH VERB IT FORBIDS.** ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1356`'s ⛔ FIRST and ⛔ heaviest reason for holding was ⛔ the manager's ⛔ OWN DRAFTING: its `names:` read ⛔ `NEVER CLAUDE.md` ⛔ ONE CLAUSE AFTER granting the ⛔ identical ⛔ STAGE carve-out for `CONVENTIONS.md` (*"you ⛔ STAGE it under `TL-§5e` cl. 7b and ⛔ say that you did; ⛔ that is all"*). ⛔ The host read the asymmetry as ⛔ **deliberate drafting** — ⛔ and was ⛔ RIGHT TO, because ⛔ that is what an asymmetry ⛔ means. ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE ON THE ⛔ MANAGER: ⛔ any `names:` fence over a file a host may ⛔ encounter dirty states ⛔ BOTH VERBS ⛔ EXPLICITLY — ⛔ `NEVER AUTHOR` ⛔ PLUS either ⛔ `STAGE PERMITTED` or ⛔ `NEVER STAGE`. ⛔ A bare `NEVER <file>` is read — ⛔ correctly — as forbidding ⛔ BOTH, and a ⛔ git act then ⛔ inherits a ⛔ content fence it was ⛔ never meant to carry.** ⛔ **⛔ A STAGE GRANT IS ⛔ NOT A CONTENT RATIFICATION, and the row that grants it ⛔ SAYS SO ⛔ ON ITS OWN FACE.**
7. 📌⛔ **CLAUSE 7 — INSTANCE RECORD, ⛔ AND THE COUNT IS THE POINT: ⛔ **FOUR agents held this same fence across ⛔ TWO WAVES and ⛔ EVERY ONE WAS RIGHT** — ⛔ two before the ruling, ⛔ the ⛔ MANAGER on ⭐ `TASK-1353` (⛔ refusing the relay outright), and ⭐ `TASK-1356` §4 (⛔ refusing to ⛔ STAGE, ⛔ on drafting + ⛔ sweep-scope + ⛔ a live boarded host).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ FOUR CORRECT REFUSALS IN A ROW IS ⛔ EVIDENCE THE ⛔ LANE IS WRONG, ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE OF FOUR TIMID AGENTS** — ⛔ the same reading ⭐ `TASK-1359`'s `blocked-by` already made at ⛔ three.
8. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 8 — KINSHIP (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ a relayed instruction is not a ruling — ⛔ **this is its general form: ⭐ `§97` fences the ⛔ CHANNEL, ⛔ this fences the ⛔ INFERENCE**) · ⭐ `SC-§121` cl. 3/4 (⛔ a decision smuggled past him ⛔ acquires his authority ⛔ without his attention — ⛔ **the harm this prevents**) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim) · ⭐ `SC-§119` (⛔ an instrument is itself a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§91` · ⭐ `SC-§138` (⛔ the re-measurement cl. 5 leans on) · `SC-§104`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ EVERY OTHER LAW IN THIS REGISTER TELLS YOU TO ⛔ MEASURE INSTEAD OF INHERITING. ⛔ THIS ONE NAMES THE ⛔ ONE OBJECT THAT ⛔ CANNOT BE MEASURED AT ALL — ⛔ AND THEREFORE THE ⛔ ONE PLACE WHERE ⛔ HOLDING, ⛔ NOT MEASURING, IS THE ⛔ RIGOROUS ACT.**

### SC-§138 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A PUBLISHED COLLISION COUNT IS A ⛔ MOVING TARGET. ⛔ A DISPATCH STATES THE ⛔ ANCHORING *METHOD*, ⛔ NEVER THE *COUNT* — AND THE SAME MANAGER HAS NOW MADE THIS ERROR ⛔ THREE WAYS: A ⛔ LINE NUMBER, A ⛔ TALLY, AND A ⛔ COUNT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager on ⭐ `TASK-1353`, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1351`'s host, which ⛔ RE-MEASURED its anchor rather than obeying the number its dispatch handed it and ⛔ PUBLISHED THE DISCREPANCY** · marker `SC-138-STATE-THE-METHOD-NEVER-THE-COUNT`)

- 🚨⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ MINUTES APART, ⛔ NOT DAYS: a dispatch handed the bare `^- status:` collision count as ⛔ **1344**; ⭐ `TASK-1351`'s host measured ⛔ **1348** at its own instant and ⛔ said so; a later row measured ⛔ **1352**. ⇒ ⛔ **THE BOARD IS ⛔ WRITTEN BY THE VERY LANE THAT READS IT, so ⛔ ANY COUNT OVER IT IS ⛔ STALE THE MOMENT IT IS ⛔ TYPED.** ⛔ **Every one of those three readings was ⛔ CORRECT ⛔ WHEN TAKEN. ⛔ The defect is in ⛔ PUBLISHING one, ⛔ not in taking it.**
- ⛔ **THE SPECIES, ⛔ NAMED SO THE FAMILY IS VISIBLE: this is ⭐ `SC-§126`'s ⛔ LINE-NUMBER ROT ⛔ wearing a ⛔ TALLY, and ⭐ `SC-§133`'s ⛔ PREDICTED-FILENAME defect ⛔ wearing a ⛔ NUMBER.** ⛔ **All three share ⛔ ONE SHAPE: ⛔ a ⛔ DERIVED FACT ⛔ frozen into a ⛔ document that outlives the ⛔ state it was derived from.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ADDRESS, A FILENAME AND A COUNT ARE ⛔ ALL COORDINATES. ⛔ COORDINATES GO STALE; ⛔ THE INSTRUMENT THAT ⛔ PRODUCES THEM DOES ⛔ NOT.***
- ✅⛔⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ TWO LIMBS: ⛔ **(a)** a dispatch, a spec or a law that needs an anchor names the ⛔ **METHOD** — *"anchor on the ⛔ unique `#### TASK-####` header"* · *"measure the collision count at ⛔ YOUR OWN instant with `rg -c '<pattern>'`"* · *"derive the pathspec at ⛔ your own dispatch instant"* — ⛔ and ⛔ NEVER the resulting number as a ⛔ fact to rely on. ⛔ **(b)** ⛔ **A NUMBER MAY STILL BE GIVEN, ⛔ BUT ⛔ ONLY LABELLED AS A ⛔ SIGHTING: *"measured `N` at `<time>`; ⛔ re-measure — ⛔ it drifts."*** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A LABELLED SIGHTING is ⛔ USEFUL (⛔ it tells a reader the ⛔ ORDER OF MAGNITUDE and ⛔ flags a ⛔ wildly different reading as worth investigating). ⛔ AN ⛔ UNLABELLED COUNT is a ⛔ TRAP.**
- ⛔ **WHAT THE ⛔ READER OWES, ⛔ AND IT IS THE HALF THAT ⛔ ALREADY WORKED: ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1351`'s host ⛔ RE-MEASURED, found `1348` against a boarded `1344`, and ⛔ PUBLISHED BOTH ⛔ with the sentence *"it has ⛔ INCREMENTED since, ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly why the count is ⛔ re-measured."* ⇒ ⛔ **THAT IS THE ⛔ CORRECT BEHAVIOUR AND IT IS ⛔ ALREADY IN THE CULTURE. ⛔ This clause fixes the ⛔ WRITER'S half, ⛔ which is the half that ⛔ keeps re-offending.**
- 🚨⛔ **THE ⛔ ANTI-ABUSE LIMB, ⛔ BECAUSE *"I'LL MEASURE IT MYSELF"* CAN BECOME *"I NEED NO ANCHOR"*: ⛔ this clause does ⛔ NOT relax ⭐ `SC-§120` (⛔ `Edit`-only) or ⭐ `SC-§127` (⛔ clone rows carry a ⛔ discriminator). ⛔ **AN AGENT STILL OWES A ⛔ UNIQUE ANCHOR ⛔ BEFORE IT WRITES** — ⛔ this clause governs ⛔ WHERE THE UNIQUENESS COMES FROM (⛔ a ⛔ re-measurement), ⛔ not ⛔ WHETHER IT IS OWED.**
- ⚖️ **KINSHIP (`SC-§116` cl. 3):** ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 10/11 (⛔ the ⛔ LINE-NUMBER form — ⛔ this is its ⛔ TALLY sibling, ⛔ not a restatement: ⛔ a line number rots because ⛔ text moves, ⛔ a count rots because ⛔ text is ⛔ ADDED) · ⭐ `SC-§133` (⛔ the ⛔ FILENAME form) · ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ measure, ⛔ do not inherit — ⛔ the ⛔ general duty this clause ⛔ specialises) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not tallies — ⛔ **the closest relative, and the difference is the ⛔ AUDIENCE: `SC-§104` binds the agent ⛔ REPORTING, this binds the manager ⛔ SPECIFYING**) · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ probe granularity must match claim granularity).

### SC-§137 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A PATTERN WHOSE MEANING DEPENDS ON THE ⛔ TOOL'S DIALECT IS AN ⛔ UNCONTROLLED INSTRUMENT. ⛔ `[ \t]` IN POSIX `grep -E` IS ⛔ *SPACE, BACKSLASH, OR THE LETTER `t`*, AND `.` MATCHES ⛔ ONE BYTE WHILE AN EM DASH IS ⛔ THREE — ⛔ ONE ROW FIRED ⛔ BOTH, IN ⛔ OPPOSITE DIRECTIONS, AND ⛔ BOTH ANSWERS WERE ⛔ BLOCKER-SHAPED.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1343`'s ⛔ OWN two malformed probes — ⛔ caught, ⛔ re-run and ⛔ published by their ⛔ author, which is the only reason we have the pair** · marker `SC-137-A-PATTERN-DIALECT-IS-A-CLAIM`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE TWO MEASURED EVENTS, ⛔ ON ONE ROW, ⛔ ON ONE DIFF.**
   - ⛔ **THE FALSE TOTAL:** `grep -vcE '^\+[ \t]*//'` over a ⛔ comment-only diff returned ⛔ **29** ⇒ it read the diff as ⛔ **100% CODE**. ⛔ POSIX `grep -E` does ⛔ NOT expand `\t` ⛔ inside a bracket expression — ⛔ `[ \t]` is the set *{space, backslash, `t`}* — and ⛔ every one of those lines is ⛔ TAB-indented. ⛔ With `-P` the answer is ⛔ **0**.
   - ⛔ **THE FALSE ZERO:** `grep -n 'match ended . winner'` over the shipped source returned ⛔ **0 emit sites.** ⛔ `.` matches ⛔ ONE BYTE; the em dash in that literal is ⛔ THREE (UTF-8). ⛔ With `-F` the answer is ⛔ **2**.
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE DIRECTION IS ⛔ NOT THE POINT; THE ⛔ CONFIDENCE IS.** ⛔ One probe over-counted to a ⛔ maximum and one under-counted to a ⛔ zero, and ⛔ NEITHER announced a problem: a mis-dialected pattern does ⛔ not error, it ⛔ ANSWERS. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE FAILURE MODE OF A WRONG DIALECT IS A ⛔ CONFIDENT NUMBER, AND A HOST THAT PUBLISHES IT HAS ⛔ FAILED A ROW ON A ⛔ FICTION*** (⛔ here: *"comment-only is false, 29 code lines"* ⇒ a ⛔ BLOCKER against a diff with ⛔ zero code lines; and *"the literal does not exist"* ⇒ a ⛔ BLOCKER against a row whose ⛔ subject is that literal).
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE: ⭐ `SC-§39`'s DOUBLE CONTROL APPLIES TO THE ⛔ PATTERN, ⛔ NOT ONLY TO THE CORPUS.** ⛔ Before a count from a pattern containing `\t` · `\s` · `\d` · `.` · a bracket class · or ⛔ any non-ASCII byte is ⛔ published as a finding: ⛔ **(a)** the needle must return ⛔ NON-ZERO somewhere you have ⛔ measured it present, ⛔ **AND (b)** a ⛔ DIFFERENT needle must return ⛔ NON-ZERO in the ⛔ SAME corpus. ⛔ **(a) alone leaves a dead pattern indistinguishable from a true zero; ⛔ (b) alone leaves an unreadable corpus indistinguishable from a clean one.** 🚨 ⛔ **AND FOR A ⛔ TOTAL (⛔ not a zero), the control is the ⛔ COMPLEMENT: ⛔ the two halves must ⛔ SUM to the corpus** (⛔ here, `-c` and `-vc` on the ⛔ same pattern would have summed to ⛔ 29+29 ≠ 29 and the lie would have ⛔ shown itself in ⛔ one extra command).
4. 📌⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE DIALECT CRIB, ⛔ MEASURED ON THIS MACHINE, ⛔ NOT REMEMBERED FROM A MANUAL.**
   - ⛔ **POSIX `grep -E` / `grep`:** ⛔ `\t`, `\d`, `\s` are ⛔ NOT expanded inside `[...]`. ⇒ use ⛔ `-P`, or a ⛔ literal tab, or ⛔ `[[:space:]]`/`[[:digit:]]`.
   - ⛔ **`.` IS BYTE-ORIENTED** on a POSIX grep over UTF-8. ⇒ ⛔ any pattern that must cross a ⛔ non-ASCII character (⛔ this project's em dash, arrows, ⛔ every ⭐/⛔ marker in these files) uses ⛔ `-F` (literal) or ⛔ names the character explicitly. ⛔ **NEVER paper over a character you cannot type with `.`.**
   - ⛔ **PowerShell `-match` IS CASE-INSENSITIVE.** ⇒ ⛔ `-cmatch` whenever the needle also occurs ⛔ inside a name in the corpus (⭐ `TASK-1331`: `-match 'no'` hits the ⛔ `no` inside a case's own title).
   - ⛔ **A FIXED-COLUMN READ IS ⛔ NOT AN INSTRUMENT** when the output has ⛔ more than one section layout: ⭐ `TASK-1338` read ⛔ **35**, then ⛔ **50**, of an actual ⛔ **55**, because five case names are long enough that the padding collapses to ⛔ one space. ⇒ ⛔ a ⛔ DELIMITED, ⛔ case-SENSITIVE token.
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP, AND THIS IS THE ⛔ THIRD MEMBER OF ITS FAMILY, ⛔ NOT THE FIRST.** ⭐ `SC-§119` (⛔ an instrument is itself a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§131` (⛔ the bare-digit `401` grep — ⛔ BANNED after ⛔ ten consecutive wrong readings; ⛔ **same family, ⛔ same shape, ⛔ different metacharacter**) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ the double control) · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ the probe's granularity is part of its claim) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ a zero without its space is half a fact) · `SC-§104`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ THE CORPUS IS NOT THE ONLY THING UNDER TEST — ⛔ THE PATTERN IS TOO, AND IT IS THE ONE THAT ⛔ NEVER REPORTS ITS OWN FAILURE.**
6. 📌 **CLAUSE 6 — INSTANCE RECORD.** ⛔ `handoffs/TASK-1343-buildmaster.md` (⛔ both probes, ⛔ self-caught) · ⭐ `SC-§131`'s ten (⛔ `TASK-1309`..`TASK-1337`) · `handoffs/TASK-1338-programmer.md` §0b.7 (⛔ the column read) · `handoffs/TASK-1331-*` (⛔ the `-match`/`-cmatch` case).

### SC-§136 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A SENTENCE IS ENUMERATED ⛔ CONJUNCT BY CONJUNCT, AND ⛔ EVERY CONJUNCT NAMES THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT THAT DECIDES ⛔ IT — ⛔ A GREP FOR A ⛔ TEXT CLAIM, THE ⛔ CODE PATH FOR A ⛔ BEHAVIOURAL ONE. ⛔ A TEXT CENSUS IS ⛔ STRUCTURALLY INCAPABLE OF DECIDING A CLAIM ABOUT ⛔ RUNTIME. ⛔ AND A CLAUSE YOU ⛔ RE-WORDED IS ⛔ YOUR CLAUSE.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1339` BLOCKER-1 over ⭐ `TASK-1338` — ⛔ the FOURTH conjunct of a ⛔ four-conjunct sentence, ⛔ falsified by the ⛔ row's own behavioural byte, ⛔ after TWO earlier gates passed the paragraph** · marker `SC-136-ONE-INSTRUMENT-PER-CONJUNCT`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, AND IT COST ⛔ THREE GATES OVER ⛔ ONE PARAGRAPH.** ⭐ `TASK-1338` shipped: *"…THE DERIVED BOUNDARY IS NOWHERE WRITTEN DOWN HERE, AND NO ADDRESS INTO THIS FILE SURVIVES … the only ones pointing into this file are quoted FROM THE DIFFS THAT DELETED THEM, ⛔ **and the boundary itself is printed in the ledger line on EVERY run, pass or fail.**"* ⛔ Its handoff then declared *"my sentence makes exactly ⛔ **THREE** checkable claims"* and enumerated ⛔ A, B, C to ⛔ exhaustion — ⛔ **against the sentence it had quoted ⛔ verbatim four lines above, which makes ⛔ FOUR.** ⛔ The census was ⛔ rigorous, ⛔ correct, and ⛔ confirmed token-for-token by the gate with a ⛔ different instrument. ⛔ **It was also, for conjunct D, the ⛔ WRONG INSTRUMENT.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ENFORCEABLE PART OF THIS SECTION.** ⛔ **SPLIT THE ⛔ SHIPPED STRING on its own seams (`and` · `:` · `,`), ⛔ NOT a paraphrase of it, and ⛔ NOT a model of it. ⛔ THEN NAME ONE INSTRUMENT PER CONJUNCT ⛔ BEFORE measuring:**
   - ⛔ a claim about ⛔ **TEXT** (⛔ *"no address appears here"*) ⇒ a ⛔ **census of that text**, with its ⛔ search space named (⭐ `SC-§70` · ⭐ `SC-§130`);
   - ⛔ a claim about ⛔ **BEHAVIOUR** (⛔ *"it is printed on every run"*) ⇒ the ⛔ **CODE PATH**, ⛔ every return shape driven, ⛔ each against an ⛔ independent oracle.
   🚨 ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CENSUS OF A COMMENT'S TEXT CANNOT DECIDE A CLAIM ABOUT WHAT THE PROGRAM ⛔ DOES, AND IT DOES NOT ⛔ FAIL WHEN ASKED — ⛔ IT SIMPLY NEVER REACHES THE CONJUNCT.*** ⛔ **An unreached conjunct scores as ⛔ silence, and silence reads as ⛔ pass.**
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ A CLAUSE YOU RE-WORDED IS ⛔ YOUR CLAUSE. ⛔ PROVENANCE IS ⛔ NOT A DEFENCE, AND THE ⛔ MISFILING IS THE ⛔ CAUSE — ⛔ NOT A DETAIL.** ⛔ The row filed the trailing clause as *"pre-existing, retained verbatim … ⛔ **not in my diff**"* when it had in fact ⛔ **re-authored it** (*"the derived value is printed"* → *"⛔ the boundary itself is printed"*). ⇒ ⛔ **having classified it as inherited, its enumeration ⛔ never asked whether it was ⛔ TRUE.** ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE: *"retained verbatim"* is a ⛔ MEASUREMENT AGAINST THE DIFF, ⛔ never an assertion** — ⛔ and a clause you ⛔ touched at all re-enters ⛔ your enumeration ⛔ whole, ⛔ including the words you ⛔ did not change.
4. ⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — A UNIVERSAL OVER A ⛔ DISJUNCTION OWES A ⛔ WITNESS ON ⛔ EACH SIDE OF IT.** ⛔ The fixed sentence reads *"on ⛔ EVERY RUN THAT DERIVES ONE, ⛔ pass or fail"* ⇒ ⛔ the words *"pass or fail"* are ⛔ DECORATION unless a ⛔ FAIL that still prints is ⛔ exhibited. ⭐ **It exists and the fix names it: the ⛔ anchor-missing return is a ⛔ FAIL that ⛔ DERIVES a boundary and ⛔ STILL PRINTS IT.** ⇒ ⛔ **name the witness for ⛔ each disjunct, or ⛔ delete the disjunction from the sentence.**
5. ⚖️⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ SPECIES BEHIND IT IS RECORDED AS ⛔ INSTANCE ONE AND IS ⛔ DELIBERATELY ⛔ NOT DESIGNED AGAINST (⭐ `SC-§39`).** ⛔ The underlying shape is ⛔ **a PROSE claim whose truth value is owned by CODE hundreds of lines away**: ⭐ `TASK-1336` ⛔ verified conjunct D against the code and ⛔ passed it (⛔ true at its instant), ⛔ then ⛔ THREE FINDINGS LATER prescribed the change that ⛔ FALSIFIED it; ⭐ `TASK-1338` implemented ⛔ both halves and enumerated ⛔ only the half its instrument could reach. ⛔ **No census on either side sees this** — it is ⭐ `SC-§135`'s shape (⛔ a consumer no census sees) applied to ⛔ DOCUMENTATION. 🚨 ⛔ **THE RESTRAINT IS ON THE RECORD SO IT IS NOT READ AS AN OVERSIGHT: ⛔ ONE instance is ⛔ not a pattern, so ⛔ NO mechanism is built (⛔ no pinned-claim registry, ⛔ no doc-to-code link check). ⇒ ⛔ THE TRIGGER: the ⛔ SECOND instance of a ⛔ prose claim falsified by a code change ⛔ in its own diff carries a ⛔ FRESH MANAGER RULING on a mechanism.** ⛔ Nobody proposes one without a second instance ⛔ to name (⭐ `SC-§100`). ⛔ **CLAUSE 2 binds ⛔ NOW regardless — ⛔ the METHOD is law even though the MECHANISM is not.**
6. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 6 — THE ⛔ RETRACTION SHAPE, ⛔ CREDITED AND ⛔ REQUIRED.** ⭐ `TASK-1338` retracted the misfiling ⛔ IN PLACE, ⛔ struck through, ⛔ at the top of its own handoff, ⛔ naming it as the ⛔ cause rather than quietly correcting the text. ⇒ ⛔ **that is the required shape: a corrected claim is ⛔ STRUCK, ⛔ NOT DELETED, and its ⛔ replacement says ⛔ WHY the first one was reachable** (⭐ `SC-§53` cl. 3). ⛔ A silent correction destroys the ⛔ only evidence that the defect ⛔ had a mechanism.
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim until measured — ⛔ **this is that move turned ⛔ INWARD, onto one's own sentence**) · ⭐ `SC-§135` (⛔ the consumer no census sees) · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ probe granularity must match the claim's) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ a zero without its space) · ⭐ `SC-§132` (⛔ an unreached conjunct fails ⛔ SILENT) · ⭐ `SC-§39` · `SC-§104`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A QUANTIFIER IS ONLY AS GOOD AS THE CENSUS THAT CAN ⛔ REFUTE IT IN ONE COMMAND — ⛔ AND A CONJUNCT IS ONLY AS GOOD AS THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT YOU ⛔ NAMED FOR IT ⛔ BEFORE YOU LOOKED.**

### SC-§135 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A CONSUMER ⛔ OUTSIDE `Source/` IS ⛔ STILL A CONSUMER. ⛔ THE VERIFY LANE READS LOG STRINGS THAT ⛔ NO CODE READS — *"NOTHING READS IT"* MEASURED OVER `Source/`, `Tools/` AND THE SUITE IS ⛔ TRUE AND ⛔ INSUFFICIENT, AND ⛔ THREE `*-verify.md` REPORTS QUOTE `match ended — winner %s.` AS ⛔ RUNTIME EVIDENCE.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1320`'s WARN over ⭐ `TASK-1319` §7 — ⛔ the measurement was RIGHT and the conclusion drawn from it was ⛔ WRONG** · marker `SC-135-THE-EVIDENCE-LANE-IS-A-CONSUMER`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE HALF THAT WAS ⛔ CORRECT MATTERS.** `handoffs/TASK-1319-programmer.md` §7 measured *"no consumer anywhere in `Source/`, `Tools/` or the suite"* — ⛔ **LITERALLY TRUE, and ⭐ `TASK-1320` ⛔ re-ran it and ⛔ confirmed it.** ⛔ The ⛔ CONCLUSION drawn from it — *"nothing reads it that could drift"* — is ⛔ **FALSE:** `qa/TASK-1314-verify.md`, `qa/TASK-1311-verify.md` and `qa/TASK-1068-verify.md` ⛔ all quote the literal `match ended — winner` as the ⛔ runtime evidence behind a `VERIFIED` verdict. ⇒ ⛔ **this project's `playtest-verifier` lane ⛔ demonstrably reads that exact line.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE SPECIES, ⛔ AND IT POINTS THE ⛔ OPPOSITE WAY FROM ⭐ `SC-§50`.** ⭐ `SC-§50`'s orphan is a ⛔ BUILT THING WITH ⛔ NO CALLER. ⛔ **This is a thing with a caller ⛔ THAT NO CENSUS CAN SEE**, because the caller is ⛔ not code: it is a ⛔ documented evidence lane whose reader is an ⛔ AGENT following `VER-§`. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CENSUS OVER `Source/` ⛔ CANNOT FIND IT, AND THE ⛔ ABSENCE IT REPORTS IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM SAFETY.***
3. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — WHY IT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN AN ORDINARY COUPLING, AND IT IS ⭐ `SC-§132`'s SHAPE.** ⛔ If the literal is ⛔ reworded, the verifier's grep returns ⛔ ZERO and the lane reports ⛔ **`UNOBSERVABLE`**, ⛔ NOT `VERIFY-FAILED`. ⇒ ⛔ the breakage announces itself as ⛔ *"could not observe"* — which `VER-§5` ⛔ correctly refuses to read as a pass, but which reads ⛔ AT A GLANCE like an ⛔ ENVIRONMENT PROBLEM rather than a ⛔ CODE CHANGE. ⇒ ⛔ **A ⛔ FAIL-SILENT, in the ⛔ ONE LANE whose ⛔ entire job is to be the ⛔ runtime witness.**
4. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE RULE, ⛔ THREE PARTS.**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ *"NOTHING READS IT"* IS ONLY A ⛔ COMPLETE FINDING WHEN THE ⛔ SEARCH SPACE IS ⛔ NAMED** — and for any ⛔ user-facing or ⛔ log-facing literal that space ⛔ MUST include `.claude/pipeline/qa/**` and `.claude/pipeline/footage/**` (⭐ `SC-§70`: ⛔ *"X is 0"* is incomplete; ⛔ *"X is 0 over THIS space"* is the finding).
   - ⛔ **(b) A LOG LITERAL THAT A `*-verify.md` REPORT QUOTES IS A ⛔ PUBLISHED INTERFACE.** ⛔ The ⛔ SITE carries a ⛔ one-line comment naming the lane that reads it, so the ⛔ next person to edit the line ⛔ sees the consumer ⛔ at the line — ⛔ not in a handoff they will never open (⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 5).
   - ⛔ **(c) WHERE THE LITERAL EXISTS AT ⛔ TWO SITES, the comment says they ⛔ MUST STAY IN SYNC ⛔ and why** (⭐ `TASK-1320` TRADE 2: ⛔ the two sites are ⛔ mutually exclusive and ⛔ textually identical, ⛔ so a scraper gets ⛔ exactly one hit either way — ⛔ and that property is ⛔ only preserved by ⛔ keeping them identical).
5. ⚖️⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE SECOND-INSTANCE TRIGGER, ⛔ DECLARED RATHER THAN BUILT, ⛔ AND THE REASON IS ⛔ ON THE RECORD SO NOBODY READS THE RESTRAINT AS AN OVERSIGHT.** ⛔ Whether this coupling deserves a ⛔ MECHANISM (⛔ a pinned literal, ⛔ a registry of verify-lane strings) is ⛔ **NOT DECIDED HERE:** ⛔ today ⛔ EXACTLY ONE literal is known to be coupled this way, and ⭐ `SC-§39` refuses to design against ⛔ one observation. ⇒ ⛔ **WHEN A ⛔ SECOND IS FOUND, that finding carries a ⛔ FRESH MANAGER RULING on a mechanism** — the `TL-§6` third-instance pattern, ⛔ set at ⛔ TWO here ⛔ because the failure is ⛔ SILENT. ⛔ Nobody builds the registry before then; ⛔ nobody re-proposes it ⛔ without a second instance ⛔ to name.
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§50` (⛔ the orphan, ⛔ INVERTED) · ⭐ `SC-§132` (⛔ fail-silent outranks fail-loud) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ half a fact) · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ the probe's scope is part of its claim) · `VER-§1` / `VER-§5` (⛔ the evidence lane) · `SC-§104`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A REFACTOR CENSUS SEES ⛔ CALLERS. ⛔ THIS PROJECT HAS ⛔ READERS THAT ARE ⛔ NOT CALLERS, AND THEY ARE ⛔ THE ONES THAT DECIDE WHETHER A ROW SHIPS.**

### SC-§134 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A MULTI-LEG HOST ROW THAT SITS AT `backlog` WHILE ITS ⛔ OWN 5a LEG HAS ⛔ ALREADY RUN IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO A RESUMPTION CENSUS — AND THE `## Resume` PATH IS THE ⛔ ONE READER THAT ⛔ CANNOT ASK ANYBODY. ⛔ A HOST FLIPS ON ITS ⛔ FIRST LEG, ⛔ NOT ITS LAST.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1316`, ⛔ which compiled and relaunched an editor while its own row read *"BOARDED, NOT DISPATCHED"*** · marker `SC-134-A-HOST-FLIPS-ON-ITS-FIRST-LEG`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⭐ `TASK-1316` ran its ⛔ 5a leg (⛔ compile + ⛔ editor relaunch on new binaries) and wrote ⛔ NO HANDOFF UNTIL 5c. ⛔ Between those two instants the board read ⛔ `backlog — BOARDED, ⛔ NOT DISPATCHED` for a row whose work was ⛔ PARTLY DONE — ⛔ with a relaunched editor as ⛔ physical evidence that ⛔ no reader of the board could see.
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE HAZARD, ⛔ PRECISELY, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT TIDINESS.** `CLAUDE.md`'s ⛔ `## Resume` path says: ⛔ *if a session starts and `TASKBOARD.md` has unfinished tasks, ⛔ continue from the board.* ⛔ **A `backlog` row is dispatched ⛔ FROM THE TOP.** ⇒ ⛔ a resumed session would have ⛔ RE-RUN A COMPILE THAT HAD ALREADY HAPPENED — and, ⛔ worse, would have done so ⛔ believing nothing had been built. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE BOARD IS THE ⛔ ONLY WITNESS A RESUMED SESSION HAS. ⛔ A ROW THAT DOES NOT FLIP HAS ⛔ LIED TO IT.***
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE.** ⛔ **THE MOMENT A HOST EXECUTES ANYTHING ⛔ IRREVERSIBLE OR ⛔ OBSERVABLE OUTSIDE ITS OWN PROCESS — ⛔ a compile, ⛔ an editor lifecycle action, ⛔ a suite run, ⛔ a `git add` — ⛔ its `status:` ⛔ LEAVES `backlog` ⛔ IN THE SAME SITTING, ⛔ carrying ⛔ WHICH LEG RAN.** ⛔ The status ⛔ `built` ⛔ ALREADY EXISTS for ⛔ exactly this (⛔ 5a compile, ⛔ no commit; `VER-§2` cl. 3) and is ⛔ **UNDER-USED.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE COROLLARY, ⛔ AND IT COSTS NOTHING.** A host with ⛔ more than one leg ⛔ writes its handoff ⛔ INCREMENTALLY, ⛔ or ⛔ NAMES ON THE ROW the leg at which the handoff ⛔ first appears. ⇒ ⛔ **a handoff that exists ⛔ only at 5c is a record that is ⛔ ABSENT for the ⛔ whole window in which it would be ⛔ most useful.**
5. ⚖️⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — HOW IT IS ⛔ FALSIFIED, ⛔ AND WHO OWNS THE BREACH.** ⛔ If a row's `status:` reads `backlog` while ⛔ ANY artefact of that row exists ⛔ on disk or ⛔ in a process table, ⛔ this section has been ⛔ BREACHED. 🚨 ⛔ **AND IT IS A ⛔ BOARD DEFECT — ⛔ THE MANAGER'S — ⛔ when the row ⛔ never said to flip**, ⛔ the same attribution ⭐ `SC-§129` cl. 3(d) makes for the `-SelfTest` duty: ⛔ *a duty that depends on someone ⛔ remembering is a ⛔ HABIT, ⛔ not a rule.*
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§50` (⛔ the fence is a row) · ⭐ `SC-§103` (⛔ a batch commit owes N flips — ⛔ **this is its ⛔ FRONT HALF: a ⛔ multi-leg row owes a flip per ⛔ OBSERVABLE LEG**) · ⭐ `SC-§120` cl. 4 · `VER-§2` cl. 3 (⛔ the `built` status) · ⭐ `SC-§132` (⛔ an unflipped row ⛔ fails SILENT) · `SC-§104`.
7. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — THE ⛔ BOARDING REMEDY, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-20 BECAUSE cl. 5's BREACH SHAPE FIRED ⛔ TWICE IN ⛔ ONE WAVE. ⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ BOARDING DEFECT, ⛔ NOT BAD LUCK.** ⛔ **MEASURED:** ⭐ `TASK-1338` and ⭐ `TASK-1341` were ⛔ BOTH fenced from `TASKBOARD.md` by their ⛔ `names:` blocks while their ⛔ `status:` lines carried a cl. 3 flip duty. ⛔ **Both left their status lines ⛔ STALE while their edits sat ⛔ on disk; ⛔ both named it ⛔ loudly and ⛔ on purpose and ⛔ cited `TL-§5e` cl. 7c ⛔ correctly; ⛔ both were ⛔ RIGHT** (⛔ the narrower instrument wins, and ⛔ board + law outrank a dispatch). ⇒ ⛔ **cl. 5 charges ⛔ both to the ⛔ BOARD — and a law that is ⛔ breached twice in one wave by ⛔ compliant agents is ⛔ mis-sited, ⛔ not disobeyed.**
   ✅⛔ **THE RULE, AT ⛔ BOARDING, ⛔ NOT AT DISPATCH: ⛔ THE FENCE AND THE FLIP DUTY ARE WRITTEN BY THE ⛔ SAME HAND AT THE ⛔ SAME INSTANT.** ⛔ Whenever a row's `names:` fences it from `TASKBOARD.md`, the ⛔ manager writes ⛔ ONE of these ⛔ on the row, ⛔ never neither:
   - ⛔ **(a) THE DEFAULT — ⛔ THE FENCE ⛔ CARVES OUT THE ROW'S OWN `status:` LINE ⛔ BY NAME:** ⛔ *"`TASKBOARD.md` — ⛔ THIS ROW'S OWN `status:` LINE ⛔ ONLY, ⛔ `Edit` only (⭐ `SC-§120`), ⛔ never `replace_all` (⭐ `SC-§127`), ⛔ anchored on the `TASK-####:` discriminator whose collision count you ⛔ measure at your own instant."* ⇒ ⛔ **the agent that ⛔ takes the observable action is the agent that ⛔ records it, and ⛔ no relay exists to ⛔ drop.**
   - ⛔ **(b) IF THE FENCE MUST BE ⛔ TOTAL — ⛔ THE FLIPPER IS ⛔ NAMED ON THE ROW, and the ⛔ `status:` line ⛔ SAYS SO IN THE SAME SENTENCE:** ⛔ *"⛔ FENCED FROM THIS BOARD — ⛔ FLIPPER: ⭐ `TASK-####`. ⛔ Until that row lands, this line is ⛔ KNOWN-STALE ⛔ BY DESIGN; ⛔ a `## Resume` census must read it as ⛔ such."*
   🚨 ⇒ ⛔ **ROUTING IT BY HAND EACH TIME IS THE ⛔ HABIT THIS LAW EXISTS TO ⛔ REPLACE** (⛔ cl. 5's own words: ⛔ *a duty that depends on someone ⛔ remembering is a ⛔ HABIT, ⛔ not a rule*). ⛔ A row boarded with ⛔ neither (a) nor (b) is ⛔ defective ⛔ at boarding, and the defect is ⛔ the manager's ⛔ before it is anyone's.
8. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — THE ⛔ FIRST REAL DISCHARGE, ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE A LAW WITH ⛔ ONLY BREACHES ON ITS RECORD TEACHES ⛔ NOTHING.** ⭐ `TASK-1343` (2026-09-20) discharged this section ⛔ exactly as written, and ⛔ four details are the reusable part:
   - ⛔ it wrote ⛔ **`in-progress — 5a COMPILE RUNNING`** ⛔ **BEFORE** `Build.bat` was started, ⛔ not after it returned;
   - ⛔ it ⛔ **VERIFIED THE BOARD ACTUALLY CHANGED** (⛔ the flip is a ⛔ state to read back, ⛔ not an edit to issue — ⭐ `SC-§104`);
   - 🚨 ⛔ it ⛔ **DECLINED to write the literal `built` while the compile was still running**, on the grounds that ⛔ asserting `built` ⛔ before a `Result:` line exists is a ⛔ FALSE STATE — ⇒ ⛔ **cl. 3's *"carrying WHICH LEG RAN"* means the ⛔ leg's TRUE tense, ⛔ not the leg's ⛔ hoped-for outcome;**
   - ⭐ ⛔ it ⛔ **KEPT the `in-progress` wording ⛔ VERBATIM in its later lines** rather than overwriting it, ⛔ *because deleting it would delete the ⛔ proof that the flip was ⛔ EARLY.* ⇒ ⛔ **the early line is ⛔ EVIDENCE. ⛔ It is ⛔ retained beside the final status, ⛔ never replaced by it** (⛔ the same reasoning as ⭐ `SC-§136` cl. 6's struck-not-deleted retraction).

### SC-§133 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A BOARDING-TIME CLAUSE THAT ⛔ HARD-CODES AN ARTEFACT ⛔ BY FILENAME IS THE ⭐ `SC-§126` ROT DEFECT ⛔ WEARING A PATH INSTEAD OF A LINE NUMBER. ⛔ THE HOST PROMOTES ⛔ EVERY PAIR THE REPORT STILL MARKS `OWED` — ⛔ NEVER THE PAIR THE CLAUSE ⛔ PREDICTED.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1316` (5-bis) — ⛔ the manager's OWN clause, ⛔ and a host had to ⛔ override it to be right** · marker `SC-133-PROMOTE-WHAT-THE-REPORT-MARKS-OWED`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⭐ `TASK-1316` clause (5-bis) named ⛔ LIMB A's evidence PNG ⛔ BY FILENAME as the artefact to promote. ⛔ **LIMB B then amended `qa/TASK-1314-verify.md` ⛔ IN PLACE and produced ⛔ its own frame.** ⇒ ⛔ a host following the clause ⛔ LITERALLY would have promoted the ⛔ named file and ⛔ ORPHANED THE OTHER. ✅ ⛔ **The host took ⛔ BOTH and ⛔ cited the BOARD ROW over the DISPATCH — ⛔ correct, ⛔ credited, and ⛔ recorded here so the ⛔ next host does not have to be ⛔ that careful to be right.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — WHY IT IS THE ⛔ SAME DEFECT AS A LINE ADDRESS, ⛔ AND ⛔ WORSE.** ⛔ Both are a value the board ⛔ WROTE DOWN about a world that ⛔ had not happened yet. ⭐ `SC-§126`'s subject is ⛔ `file:NNN`; ⛔ this section's is ⛔ `file`. ⛔ The ⛔ SHAPE is identical (⛔ a boarding-time snapshot quoted as a ⛔ present fact) and the ⛔ CONSEQUENCE is worse: ⛔ **a rotted line address usually fails ⛔ LOUDLY (⭐ `SC-§132`); ⛔ a wrong filename ⛔ SILENTLY PROMOTES THE WRONG ARTEFACT and ⛔ leaves the right one behind.**
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE.** ⛔ **A PROMOTION / COLLECTION CLAUSE NAMES A ⛔ PREDICATE, ⛔ NEVER A PATH:** ⛔ *"every artefact the report still marks `OWED`"* · ⛔ *"every PNG referenced by a `Verdict:` line in this report"*. ⛔ **IF A SPECIFIC FILENAME IS WRITTEN DOWN, IT IS LABELLED ⛔ `EXPECTED, NOT EXHAUSTIVE` ⛔ IN THE SAME SENTENCE**, and the clause says ⛔ what to do when reality differs (⛔ take both; ⛔ report the divergence).
4. ⚖️⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE GENERAL FORM, ⛔ AND IT IS A LAW I ⛔ ALREADY WROTE FOR THE OTHER HALF.** ⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT PREDICT AN ARTEFACT SET AT BOARDING. ⛔ DESCRIBE HOW TO ⛔ DERIVE IT AT EXECUTION.** ⛔ That is ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 7a's rule for ⛔ PATHSPECS, verbatim — ⛔ *the derivation ⛔ IS the pathspec; a list written earlier is ⛔ EVIDENCE OF INTENT, ⛔ NEVER THE SET* — ⛔ applied to ⛔ EVIDENCE rather than to git paths. 🚨 ⛔ **THE MANAGER WHO WROTE THE DERIVED-PATHSPEC RULE IS THE ⛔ SAME PERSON WHO THEN HARD-CODED AN EVIDENCE FILENAME. ⛔ A LAW LEARNED IN ⛔ ONE DOMAIN DOES ⛔ NOT TRANSFER ⛔ BY ITSELF.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§126` (⛔ the rot family) · ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 7a (⛔ derive, ⛔ do not list) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§132` (⛔ this one fails silent) · ⭐ `SC-§50`.

### SC-§132 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A GUARD THAT ANSWERS ⛔ GREEN WHEN IT ⛔ COULD NOT LOOK ⛔ OUTRANKS ONE THAT ANSWERS ⛔ RED ABOUT THE ⛔ WRONG THING. ⛔ A FAIL-SILENT IS ⛔ ALWAYS THE HIGHER GRADE — AND THE GATE THAT GRADED THEM THE OTHER WAY ROUND WAS ⛔ CORRECTED BY THE NEXT GATE, ⛔ ON ITS OWN SUBJECT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1336` §2 re-grading ⭐ `TASK-1332` — ⛔ a gate correcting a gate, ⛔ unprompted, ⛔ in writing** · marker `SC-132-FAIL-SILENT-OUTRANKS-FAIL-LOUD`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⭐ `TASK-1332` graded ⛔ two defects in the ⛔ same artefact: the exhibit exclusion's ⛔ FAIL-OPEN (⛔ a RED naming the ⛔ wrong addresses) as ⛔ **WARN-2**, and a ⛔ DISCARDED `$errors` on the parse path (⛔ a GREEN about a file it ⛔ could not parse) as ⛔ **NIT-4**. ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1336` re-derived it ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION: ⛔ a best-effort `[Parser]::ParseFile` ⛔ STILL YIELDS A USABLE AST, so the header token ⛔ came back and the ledger line printed ⛔ GREEN ⛔ about a file with ⛔ one parse error. ⇒ ⛔ **THE NIT WAS THE WORSE DEFECT.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, ⛔ AND ⛔ WHY IT IS ASYMMETRIC.** ⛔ A ⛔ LOUD wrong answer is ⛔ SELF-ANNOUNCING: ⛔ somebody reads the red, ⛔ discovers it names the wrong thing, and the defect is ⛔ found in the same sitting. ⛔ **A ⛔ SILENT wrong answer ⛔ PRODUCES NO READER.** ⛔ It is ⛔ indistinguishable from a correct pass, and the ⛔ only thing that ever finds it is ⛔ somebody deliberately going to look — ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly what a green result ⛔ persuades nobody to do. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ GRADE BY ⛔ WHO WILL FIND IT, ⛔ NOT BY ⛔ HOW WRONG IT IS.***
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE OPERATIONAL FORM.** ⛔ When grading two defects in one artefact, ask of each: 🚨 ⛔ **IF THIS FIRES IN PRODUCTION, ⛔ DOES ANYTHING CHANGE ON A SCREEN?** ⛔ If ⛔ NO ⇒ ⛔ it outranks ⛔ every defect for which the answer is ⛔ YES, ⛔ whatever their relative *"wrongness"*. ⇒ ⛔ a fail-open exclusion that ⛔ reds too much is a ⛔ WARN; ⛔ a fail-open exclusion that ⛔ greens too much is a ⛔ BLOCKER CANDIDATE.
4. ⚖️⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE PRECEDENT, ⛔ WORTH NAMING SEPARATELY: ⛔ A GATE ⛔ CORRECTED A PRIOR GATE, ⛔ ON THE PRIOR GATE'S OWN SUBJECT, AND THE CORRECTION IS ⛔ ACCEPTED.** ⭐ `TASK-1336` re-graded ⭐ `TASK-1332`'s finding ⛔ without being asked to re-open it, ⛔ said so explicitly in its report (*"it revises my predecessor's grading"*), and the ⛔ manager ⛔ ENDORSES IT. ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **A GATE'S GRADES ARE ⛔ NOT FINAL AT `PASS`. ⛔ The next gate over the same file ⛔ MAY re-rank them and ⛔ MUST say so in words.** ⛔ **This is ⛔ NOT a QA loop and it does ⛔ NOT re-open the committed row** (⭐ `SC-§129` cl. 5's ⛔ non-retroactivity applies) — ⛔ it is the ⛔ record being corrected, ⛔ not the work.
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 9 (⛔ a green that ⛔ cannot fail) · ⭐ `SC-§119` (⛔ an instrument is ⛔ itself a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ half a fact) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate against the ⛔ FAILURE) · ⭐ `SC-§39`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⭐ `SHIP-§9` says a gate must be ⛔ SHOWN ITS FAILURE. ⛔ THIS SECTION SAYS ⛔ WHICH FAILURE TO SHOW IT ⛔ FIRST — ⛔ THE SILENT ONE.**

### SC-§131 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE BARE-DIGIT `401` GREP IS A ⛔ BANNED IDIOM, ⛔ NOT A CAUTION. ⛔ TEN CONSECUTIVE HOSTS RAN IT, ⛔ TEN GOT A NON-ZERO ANSWER, AND THE `HTTP 401` / `Unauthorized` ⛔ SIGNATURE WAS ⛔ 0 ALL TEN TIMES. ⛔ IT HAS ⛔ NEVER ONCE BEEN RIGHT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager ⛔ **on the nomination of ⛔ TWO independent hosts, ⛔ the tenth reading having landed at ⭐ `TASK-1337`** · marker `SC-131-THE-BARE-DIGIT-401-GREP-IS-BANNED`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE LEDGER, ⛔ AND ITS PROVENANCE IS ⛔ STATED RATHER THAN ASSUMED (⭐ `SC-§101`).** ⛔ Ten logs; the ⛔ bare-digit count relayed to the manager as ⛔ **6 · 8 · 9 · 13 · 14 · 15 · 7 · 4 · 1 · 8**. ⛔ **The individual values are ⛔ EACH HOST'S OWN MEASUREMENT in its own handoff and are ⛔ NOT re-derived here.** ⛔ The ⛔ LOAD-BEARING fact — ⛔ which ⛔ every one of the ten states ⛔ in its own words — is that the ⛔ **SIGNATURE count was ⛔ 0 in ⛔ ALL TEN.** ⇒ ⛔ **the idiom's ⛔ measured accuracy on this project is ⛔ 0/10.**
2. ⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE MECHANISM, ⛔ NAMED BY THE HOSTS THEMSELVES.** ⛔ The digits are the ⛔ `[401]` FRAME COUNTER in UE's log prefix — and they are ⛔ also reachable as a ⛔ line number, a ⛔ byte offset, a ⛔ tick, a ⛔ millisecond figure. ⇒ ⛔ **`401` is a ⛔ THREE-DIGIT NUMBER, and an ⛔ engine log is ⛔ made of three-digit numbers.**
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE BAN.** ⛔ A `401` probe is expressed as a ⛔ **SIGNATURE, ⛔ NEVER AS DIGITS:** ⛔ `HTTP 401` · ⛔ `401 Unauthorized` · ⛔ `Unauthorized` (⛔ `-i`). ⇒ ⛔ **a bare-digit count ⛔ MAY NOT be reported as a finding, ⛔ MAY NOT appear in an acceptance criterion, and ⛔ MAY NOT be cited as corroboration.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ ONE PERMITTED USE, ⛔ AND IT IS WHY THE LEDGER EXISTS.** ⛔ A host may run it ⛔ deliberately to ⛔ EXTEND THIS LEDGER — ⛔ reported ⛔ ONLY as the ⛔ FALSIFIED reading, ⛔ adjacent to the signature counts, ⛔ labelled as such (⛔ the shape ⭐ `TASK-1331` / `1333` / `1337` all used). ⛔ **That use is ⛔ permitted precisely because the ⛔ ten-row ledger is ⛔ what made this ban ⛔ DERIVABLE rather than ⛔ asserted.**
5. ⚖️⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE GENERAL FORM, ⛔ WHICH IS WHY THIS IS A ⛔ SECTION AND ⛔ NOT A FOOTNOTE: ⛔ A ⛔ SHORT NUMERIC LITERAL IS ⛔ NOT A SEARCH TERM.** ⛔ Any probe whose ⛔ pattern is a ⛔ bare 1–4 digit number over a ⛔ machine-generated log is measuring the ⛔ LOG'S ARITHMETIC, ⛔ not its ⛔ CONTENT. ⛔ **The same trap is ⛔ already on this board ⛔ twice wearing other hats:** ⛔ the ⛔ CLOCK TIMES (`23:42`, `04:55:38`) that a ⛔ line-address census picked up as addresses (⭐ `TASK-1331` (0)(b)) · ⛔ ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 11's ⛔ ledgered number ⛔ re-presented as provenance. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ NAME THE THING. ⛔ NEVER THE NUMBER THAT ⛔ HAPPENS TO SPELL IT.***
6. ⚖️🚨 **CLAUSE 6 — THIS ⛔ SUPERSEDES THE EARLIER ⛔ CAUTION (⭐ `SC-§102` cl. 6), ⛔ AND THE ⛔ REASON IT HAD TO BE PROMOTED IS THE ⛔ INTERESTING PART.** ⛔ The caution said ⛔ *"report by SIGNATURE, ⛔ never the digits."* ⛔ **Ten hosts complied with its ⛔ POSITIVE HALF (⛔ they all reported the signature) and ⛔ ignored its ⛔ NEGATIVE HALF (⛔ they all ran the digits anyway).** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CAUTION THAT IS ⛔ OBEYED IN ITS POSITIVE HALF AND ⛔ IGNORED IN ITS NEGATIVE HALF IS ⛔ NOT WORKING. ⛔ PROMOTE IT TO A ⛔ BAN.***
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§119` · ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 9(b-2) / cl. 11 · ⭐ `SC-§130` (⛔ the probe's ⛔ granularity; ⛔ this is the probe's ⛔ SPECIFICITY) · ⭐ `SC-§104` · ⭐ `SC-§132` (⛔ a ⛔ non-zero digit count is a ⛔ LOUD wrong answer — ⛔ which is ⛔ why ten hosts ⛔ caught it, ⛔ and why it ⛔ cost only ink).

### SC-§130 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ GRANULARITY OF YOUR PROBE MUST ⛔ MATCH THE GRANULARITY OF THE THING YOU ARE CLAIMING IS ⛔ ABSENT. ⛔ A ⛔ NEW CLAUSE INSIDE AN ⛔ EXISTING SECTION IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO A ⛔ SECTION-NAME GREP — AND THE COUNT READS ⛔ IDENTICAL ON ⛔ BOTH SIDES.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1322` RUN 5, which ⛔ caught its own probe ⛔ mid-claim and ⛔ re-measured** · marker `SC-130-PROBE-GRANULARITY-MUST-MATCH-CLAIM-GRANULARITY`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⛔ A host probing whether ⭐ `VER-§8` was ⛔ already in git counted the ⛔ TOKEN `VER-§8` on ⛔ disk and at ⛔ HEAD: ⛔ **9 and 9.** ⇒ ⛔ a token-count probe ⛔ concludes *"already in git."* 🚨 ⛔ **IT WAS ⛔ WRONG: the disk copy carried a ⛔ NEW CLAUSE 9 ⛔ INSIDE THAT SAME SECTION, and ⛔ HEAD did not.** ⛔ Re-measured against ⛔ distinctive text from the ⛔ CLAUSE BODY (`NOT EVERY CEILING IS A`) ⇒ ⛔ disk 1 / ⛔ HEAD **0**. ⛔ The clause landed in `36af9b6`.
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE COUNT WAS ⛔ NOT WRONG, AND ⛔ THAT IS THE WHOLE LESSON.** ⛔ **9 on both sides is the ⛔ TRUTH about the token.** ⛔ The defect is ⛔ NOT in the measurement — it is in the ⛔ **INFERENCE:** ⛔ a section-name token answers ⛔ *"does this section ⛔ EXIST"*, ⛔ never ⛔ *"does this section's ⛔ CONTENT match."* ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE PROBE MEASURED A ⛔ REAL THING AND WAS ASKED A ⛔ DIFFERENT QUESTION. ⛔ NO AMOUNT OF CARE IN ⛔ RUNNING IT WOULD HAVE HELPED.***
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE.** ⛔ When claiming a thing is ⛔ ABSENT from a ref, the ⛔ PROBE'S UNIT must be the ⛔ SMALLEST UNIT YOU ARE CLAIMING ABOUT. ⛔ **Section absent ⇒ grep the ⛔ SECTION HEADER. ⛔ Clause absent ⇒ grep ⛔ distinctive text from the ⛔ CLAUSE BODY. ⛔ Sentence absent ⇒ grep the ⛔ SENTENCE. ⛔ Word absent ⇒ grep the ⛔ WORD.** ⇒ ⛔ **going ⛔ COARSER than your claim is a ⛔ FALSE-NEGATIVE GENERATOR, ⛔ and it answers ⛔ REASSURINGLY.**
4. 🚨⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE TELL, ⛔ AND IT IS COUNTER-INTUITIVE: ⛔ AN ⛔ EQUAL COUNT ON BOTH SIDES IS THE READING THAT ⛔ MOST NEEDS THE GRANULARITY CHECK.** ⛔ An ⛔ UNEQUAL count is ⛔ SELF-CORRECTING — ⛔ you go and look. ⛔ **An ⛔ EQUAL count ⛔ CLOSES THE INVESTIGATION**, and it is ⛔ exactly the shape a ⛔ COARSE probe produces when a ⛔ FINE change has occurred. ⇒ ⛔ **`disk == HEAD` is ⛔ not a result; it is a ⛔ prompt to ask ⛔ at what scale.** (⭐ `SC-§132`: ⛔ this is a ⛔ FAIL-SILENT.)
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — CHOOSING THE ⛔ DISTINCTIVE TEXT.** Pick text that is ⛔ (a) ⛔ INSIDE the new unit · ⛔ (b) ⛔ unlikely to be ⛔ paraphrased elsewhere in the file · ⛔ (c) ⛔ **NOT a line address (⭐ `SC-§126`) and ⛔ NOT a bare digit (⭐ `SC-§131`)** — ⛔ both of those are ⛔ probes that ⛔ already have their own bans. ⇒ ⛔ **QUOTE THE TEXT YOU CHOSE ⛔ AND BOTH COUNTS.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 10 (⛔ read the ⛔ TOKEN, ⛔ not the ADDRESS) · ⭐ `SC-§119` (⛔ an instrument is a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not tallies) · ⭐ `SC-§131` · ⭐ `SC-§132` · ⭐ `SC-§39`. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 10 moved the probe from the ⛔ ADDRESS to the ⛔ TOKEN. ⛔ THIS SECTION MOVES IT FROM THE ⛔ TOKEN TO THE ⛔ TOKEN AT THE ⛔ RIGHT SCALE. ⛔ Both corrections were bought by the ⛔ same file and the ⛔ same habit, ⛔ two days apart.**

### SC-§129 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A SELF-TEST LANE IS A ⛔ MECHANISM, ⛔ NOT AN ⛔ ENFORCEMENT — AND ITS TRIGGER IS KEYED ON *"ANY ROW WHOSE DIFF ⛔ TOUCHES THIS FILE"*, ⛔ NEVER ON *"ANY ROW THAT CHANGES THIS FILE'S ⛔ CODE."* ⛔ ALL THREE ROT EVENTS THE GUARD EXISTS TO CATCH WERE ⛔ PROSE-ONLY EDITS ⇒ ⛔ A CODE-KEYED TRIGGER WOULD HAVE CAUGHT ⛔ NONE OF THEM ⛔ WHILE FEELING CORRECT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1331` clause (7) — ⛔ the manager's ⛔ OWN provisional rule, ⛔ REFUTED BY THE ROW WITH EVIDENCE and ⛔ CONFIRMED INDEPENDENTLY BY ⭐ `TASK-1332`'s own census** · marker `SC-129-KEY-THE-TRIGGER-ON-TOUCHES-NOT-ON-CODE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE GUARD WAS BUILT, ⛔ CONTROLLED SEVEN WAYS, ⛔ GATED PASS — AND `SelfTest` APPEARS ⛔ ZERO TIMES ACROSS ⭐ `TASK-1310` · `1317` · `1318` · `1324` · `1325` · `1326` · `1327` · `1328` · `1329`. ⇒ ⛔ AS PRACTICE STOOD, IT WOULD HAVE BEEN RUN BY ⛔ ZERO OF ITS ⛔ THREE INTENDED SUBJECTS.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ TAKEN TWICE BY TWO READERS.** ⭐ `TASK-1331` was ordered to ⛔ MEASURE current practice and ⛔ forbidden to invent the rule (⭐ `SC-§100`). It grepped the pipeline and reported: `-SelfTest` was run by ⛔ **1 of the 4 rows that have ever touched `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1`**, and that one was a ⛔ **CODE** row. ⭐ `TASK-1332` re-took the census at its own instant and got the ⛔ same five files and the ⛔ same nine zeroes. ⇒ ⛔ **the row ⛔ REFUTED the manager's provisional rule ⛔ with evidence, ⛔ declined to replace it, and ⛔ said so in plain words. ⛔ That is `SC-§100` ⛔ honoured, ⛔ not breached.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ⛔ INVERSION, AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE DIVIDEND OF HAVING ASKED: ⛔ THE PRACTICE IS ⛔ EXACTLY BACKWARDS FROM THE OBVIOUS RULE.** ⛔ Rows that changed the runner's ⛔ **CODE** ran `-SelfTest`. ⛔ **ALL THREE rows that changed only its ⛔ HEADER PROSE did not — ⛔ nor did their gates, ⛔ nor their hosts.** ⇒ ⛔ **and the ⛔ rot lives in the ⛔ PROSE.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A TRIGGER KEYED ON *"CHANGES THE CODE"* WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ WRITTEN CONFIDENTLY, ⛔ REVIEWED APPROVINGLY, AND ⛔ FIRED ON NONE OF THE ⛔ THREE EVENTS THAT ⛔ CREATED THE DEFECT.***
3. 🚨✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE. ⛔ FOUR DUTIES, ⛔ ALL BINDING.**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ SUBJECT — ⛔ ANY ROW WHOSE DIFF ⛔ TOUCHES `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1`. ⛔ ONE BYTE IS TOUCHING:** ⛔ a comment, ⛔ a header sentence, ⛔ a parameter default, ⛔ a new case. ⛔ **A row that only ⛔ READS the file is ⛔ NOT a subject.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ RUNNER — the row runs `-SelfTest` and ⛔ QUOTES THE WHOLE LEDGER, ⛔ RECONCILED ⛔ BY NAME (⭐ `SC-§104`), ⛔ NEVER BY TOTAL.** ⛔ If the row ⛔ cannot execute (⛔ a gate with no shell), the duty ⛔ FALLS TO ITS HOST ⛔ AND THE ROW ⛔ NAMES THE HOST. ⇒ ⛔ **an undelegated duty is a ⛔ dropped one.**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ GATE — the gate ⛔ GREPS THE HANDOFF for the ledger and checks the ⛔ case NAMES are present and green.** ⛔ A ⛔ missing ledger is a ⛔ BLOCKER ⛔ ON THE HANDOFF, ⛔ not on the code — ⛔ say which, so the row is not sent back to re-fix a correct diff.
   - ⛔ **(d) ⛔ MANAGER — ⛔ THE DUTY IS WRITTEN ⛔ ONTO THE ROW ⛔ AT BOARDING, ⛔ NOT REMEMBERED AT DISPATCH.** ⛔ If a row's `names:` WRITES list contains this file, its spec ⛔ cites this section and its gate ⛔ carries (c). ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 7a: ⛔ **a rule that depends on someone remembering to name the file is a ⛔ HABIT, ⛔ NOT A RULE.**
4. ⚖️⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — WHY THE GUARD WAS ALLOWED TO ⛔ COMMIT BEFORE THIS RULE EXISTED, ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE THE DISTINCTION IS ⛔ STRUCTURAL AND WILL BE NEEDED AGAIN.** ⭐ `TASK-1332` ruled ⛔ COMMIT, and the manager ⛔ accepted it. ⛔ **This is ⛔ NOT the ⭐ `TASK-1286` shape: there the entry path was ⛔ UNREACHABLE — the inertness was a property of the ⛔ CODE, curable by ⛔ no convention. ⛔ Here the lane ⛔ exists, ⛔ fires on demand, and is ⛔ invoked by ⭐ `TASK-1333`'s own spec (2) ⇒ the inertness is a property of ⛔ PRACTICE.** ⇒ ⛔ **AND AN ⛔ UNCOMMITTED GUARD IS ⛔ STRICTLY MORE INERT THAN A COMMITTED ONE — ⛔ there is ⛔ no state in which withholding the commit ⛔ increases the number of rows that run it.** 🚨 ⛔ **BUT THE CHAIN IS ⛔ NOT CLOSED AT THAT COMMIT: it ships a ⛔ PROVEN MECHANISM, ⛔ NOT AN ENFORCEMENT — ⛔ which is ⛔ why this section exists and ⛔ why ⭐ `TASK-1334` was boarded ⛔ IN PARALLEL rather than ⛔ afterwards (⭐ `SC-§50`: ⛔ the fence is a ⛔ ROW, ⛔ not a memory).**
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — SCOPE, ⛔ AND WHAT THIS DOES ⛔ NOT LICENSE.** ⛔ `-SelfTest` is ⛔ NOT the suite: it ⛔ launches no engine and ⛔ no process, so it is ⛔ cheap and ⛔ `SC-§118`-safe — ⛔ **but that purity is ⛔ RE-CONFIRMED AT THE RUNNER'S OWN INSTANT (⭐ `SC-§91`), ⛔ never inherited from this sentence.** ⛔ The rule is ⛔ NOT retroactive: ⛔ rows already committed are ⛔ not reopened. ⛔ And it ⛔ does not make `-SelfTest` a ⛔ substitute for the row's own controls — ⛔ a green ledger says the ⛔ existing cases still pass, ⛔ never that a ⛔ new case can fail (⭐ `SC-§39`).
6. ⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 6 — THE GENERALISATION, SO IT TRAVELS: ⛔ THE KEY IS THE ⛔ PROPERTY, ⛔ NOT THE FILENAME — ⛔ ANY FILE THAT CARRIES ⛔ ITS OWN SELF-CHECK LANE.** ⛔ Today ⛔ exactly one does (`Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1`, lane `-SelfTest`). ⛔ **When a second appears, it ⛔ INHERITS THIS SECTION with its own lane's name, and the ⛔ row that adds the lane ⛔ names it here (⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 5: ⛔ a measurement that lives only in a handoff is evidence nobody will find).**
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§50` (⛔ the fence is a row) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ half a fact, ⛔ and ⛔ BOTH halves are named here: ⛔ *six hand-corrections and no enforcer* ⛔ AND ⛔ *an enforcer nobody runs* — ⛔ **the remedy for half a fact is to ⛔ SUPPLY THE OTHER HALF, ⛔ not to discard the half you hold**) · ⭐ `SC-§123` (⛔ reachability is not correctness — ⛔ cl. 4 is its ⛔ boundary) · ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 7a (⛔ habit vs rule) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ BY NAME, ⛔ never a total) · ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 9 (⛔ a green that cannot fail).

### SC-§128 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **`git diff --stat` AND `git diff --numstat` ⛔ ANSWER DIFFERENT QUESTIONS, AND THE ⛔ PHANTOM A `--stat` RECONCILE MANUFACTURES IS ⛔ EXACTLY EQUAL TO THE ⛔ DELETION COUNT. ⛔ `--stat`'s NUMBER IS THE ⛔ CHANGED-LINE TOTAL (INSERTIONS ⛔ + DELETIONS), ⛔ NOT INSERTIONS.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by the ORCHESTRATOR'S OWN ERROR on ⭐ `TASK-1314` — it read `+70/−6` off `--stat` against a handoff's `+64/−6`, boarded a whole gate check to reconcile the gap, and ⛔ THE HANDOFF WAS RIGHT** · marker `SC-128-STAT-IS-NOT-NUMSTAT`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **`64 + 6 = 70`. ⛔ THE "EXTRA" LINES WERE THE ⛔ DELETIONS, ⛔ COUNTED TWICE — ⛔ ONCE IN THE TOTAL AND ⛔ ONCE AGAIN BESIDE IT.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE ATTRIBUTION IS ⛔ THE ORCHESTRATOR'S, ⛔ NOT THE PROGRAMMER'S.** ⭐ `TASK-1314`'s handoff declared ⛔ `+64/−6`. The orchestrator reported ⛔ `+70/−6` from `git diff --stat` and boarded ⭐ `TASK-1315` ⛔ check (11) to reconcile the ⛔ 6-line phantom. ⛔ **The gate reconciled it ⛔ three ways and ⭐ `TASK-1326`'s host ⛔ re-measured `+64/−6` ⛔ independently. ⛔ There was ⛔ nothing to find.** ⇒ ⛔ **the row is ⛔ recorded as a ⛔ MEASUREMENT ERROR BY THE READER, ⛔ and the programmer's number was ⛔ correct at every instant.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — WHAT EACH ONE ACTUALLY SAYS.** `git diff --stat` prints ⛔ `<file> | <N> ++++----` where ⛔ **`N` = INSERTIONS + DELETIONS**, with the `+`/`−` glyphs a ⛔ SCALED BAR, ⛔ not a count. `git diff --numstat` prints ⛔ **`<insertions>\t<deletions>\t<file>`** — ⛔ two separate integers, ⛔ unscaled, ⛔ machine-readable. ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **WHEN A HANDOFF OR A GATE ASSERTS `+X/−Y`, THE INSTRUMENT IS ⛔ `--numstat`. ⛔ ALWAYS. ⛔ `--stat` IS FOR EYES, ⛔ NOT FOR ASSERTIONS.**
3. 🚨⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ SIGNATURE, SO THE NEXT READER CATCHES IT IN ⛔ ONE SUBTRACTION INSTEAD OF A BOARD ROW: ⛔ IF YOUR *"EXTRA"* INSERTIONS EQUAL THE ⛔ DELETION COUNT ⛔ EXACTLY, YOU ARE ⛔ NOT LOOKING AT A DISCREPANCY — ⛔ YOU ARE LOOKING AT ⛔ `--stat`.** ⛔ `70 − 64 = 6 = the deletions`. ⇒ ⛔ **check the ⛔ INSTRUMENT before you board the ⛔ INVESTIGATION.**
4. ⚖️⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE COST, STATED PLAINLY, BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ REASON THIS IS A LAW AND NOT A NOTE: ⛔ A GATE CHECK WAS ⛔ BOARDED, ⛔ DISPATCHED, ⛔ PERFORMED THREE WAYS AND ⛔ RE-MEASURED BY A HOST — ⛔ FOUR AGENT-ACTIONS — TO CHASE A NUMBER THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER WRONG.** ⛔ **And the ⛔ worse counterfactual is the one to remember: had the programmer *"corrected"* its handoff to match the orchestrator's reading, ⛔ the FALSE number would have entered the record ⛔ with a gate's blessing.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not tallies — ⛔ this is that law's ⛔ instrument half) · `SC-§39` (⛔ a reading is a fact about your ⛔ instrument until you show the instrument fires) · ⭐ `SC-§117` (⛔ a proxy that ⛔ correlates is still not the claim — ⛔ `--stat` ⛔ correlates ⛔ perfectly with insertions on a ⛔ pure-addition diff, ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly why it survived) · `SC-§91` (⛔ re-measure at your own instant — ⛔ and ⛔ with the ⛔ right tool).

### SC-§127 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ROW TEMPLATE THAT MAKES ⛔ TWO ROWS BYTE-IDENTICAL ON ⛔ ANY LINE MAKES THAT LINE AN ⛔ UNSAFE `Edit` ANCHOR — AND A `replace_all` ACROSS IT WOULD ⛔ SILENTLY FLIP AN ⛔ UNDISPATCHED ROW. ⛔ CLONE ROWS CARRY A ⛔ DISCRIMINATOR.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **measured LIVE on the `TASK-1326`/`TASK-1329` host pair — ⛔ TWO intended anchors were ⛔ non-unique and ⛔ BOTH hosts had to work around it** · marker `SC-127-A-CLONE-ROW-NEEDS-A-DISCRIMINATOR`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE BOARD'S ⛔ CONSISTENCY IS ITS ⛔ VIRTUE. ⛔ IT IS ALSO THE ⛔ MECHANISM: ⛔ TWO COMMIT HOSTS WRITTEN FROM THE SAME TEMPLATE ⛔ CONVERGE ON THE SAME BYTES, AND THE ⛔ BETTER THE TEMPLATE, THE ⛔ MORE LINES COLLIDE.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⭐ `TASK-1329` is a ⛔ near-clone host row of ⭐ `TASK-1326`: it shares its ⛔ `parallel-safe:` line ⛔ AND its ⛔ `spec:` opener ⛔ VERBATIM. ⛔ **Two `Edit` calls aimed at `TASK-1326` ⛔ failed on non-uniqueness; ⛔ both were re-anchored on `blocked-by:`, which ⛔ differs.** 🚨 ⛔ **A `replace_all: true` would have ⛔ SUCCEEDED and ⛔ SILENTLY FLIPPED AN ⛔ UNDISPATCHED ROW** — ⛔ **and the ⛔ failure would have surfaced ⛔ only when that row was dispatched against a status it never earned.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, TWO HALVES, ⛔ BOTH BINDING.**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ `replace_all: true` IS ⛔ BANNED ON `TASKBOARD.md` AND `CONVENTIONS.md`.** ⛔ Both files carry ⛔ hundreds of templated rows and ⛔ dozens of repeated clause openers; ⛔ **an author cannot know a string is unique in a 36,000-line file by reading the row in front of them.** ⛔ If a string genuinely needs replacing everywhere, ⛔ grep the count ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ quote it, and do ⛔ N single edits.
   - ✅ ⛔ **(b) ⛔ ANCHOR ON A LINE THAT ⛔ CARRIES THE TASK ID, OR ON A LINE THAT IS ⛔ STRUCTURALLY UNIQUE.** ⛔ The `blocked-by:` line is ⛔ usually unique (⛔ it names ⛔ other rows); the `#### TASK-###` header and `- status:` are ⛔ unique by construction ⛔ only if the surrounding text is. ⛔ **`parallel-safe:`, the `spec:` opener, `- assignee:` and the `Slack:` line are ⛔ PRESUMED COLLIDING and are ⛔ never anchors on their own.**
3. ⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ AUTHORING DUTY THAT PREVENTS IT, AND IT IS ⛔ MINE: ⛔ A CLONE ROW GETS A ⛔ DISCRIMINATOR AT ⛔ BOARDING.** ⛔ When two rows are written from the same template, the manager ⛔ puts the ⛔ TASK ID into ⛔ at least one line that an editor is ⛔ likely to target — ⛔ the `parallel-safe:` line and the `spec:` opener ⛔ both name their own row. ⇒ ⛔ **cost: ⛔ four characters. ⛔ Benefit: the ⛔ wrong edit ⛔ fails loudly instead of ⛔ succeeding quietly.**
4. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE GENERALISATION, WHICH IS THE PART THAT TRAVELS: ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ SAME DEFECT FAMILY AS A ⛔ ROTTING LINE NUMBER, ⛔ ONE STEP OVER.** ⛔ `SC-§126` cl. 7/8 says ⛔ *find it by its text, not by its address*, because ⛔ addresses move. ⛔ **This clause says the ⛔ TEXT can be ⛔ AMBIGUOUS, because ⛔ templates repeat it.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ANCHOR MUST BE BOTH ⛔ STABLE AND ⛔ UNIQUE, AND THE ⛔ TWO PROPERTIES HAVE ⛔ OPPOSITE FAILURE MODES: ⛔ AN ADDRESS ROTS ⛔ LOUDLY (⛔ you land on the wrong thing and see it); ⛔ A NON-UNIQUE STRING FAILS ⛔ SILENTLY ⛔ UNDER `replace_all` (⛔ you land on ⛔ every wrong thing and see ⛔ none).***
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SC-§120` (⛔ `Edit` only, ⛔ never a truncating whole-file write — ⛔ this is the ⛔ next hazard down the ⛔ same lane, and ⛔ `SC-§120`'s remedy is ⛔ what exposes it) · ⭐ `SC-§126` cl. 7/8 (⛔ the address-vs-text half) · `SC-§102` (⛔ a mis-anchored ⛔ pathspec answers with ⛔ SILENCE — ⛔ the ⛔ git-side twin of exactly this) · `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE: ⛔ after a board edit, ⛔ verify the ⛔ row you meant, ⛔ by ID).

### SC-§126 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ DELIBERATE PRESERVATION IS ⛔ ITSELF A CLAIM, AND IT MUST BE ⛔ RE-MEASURED AT THE ⛔ INSTANT OF THE EDIT — ⛔ *"SURVIVING ON PURPOSE"* RECORDS AN ⛔ INTENTION, ⛔ NEVER A MEASUREMENT.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1310`: a row ⛔ ORDERED BY ITS OWN ACCEPTANCE to preserve a sentence ⛔ MEASURED IT INSTEAD, and the sentence had been ⛔ FALSE FOR NINE DAYS ⛔ INSIDE A CLAUSE THAT SAID IT WAS BEING KEPT ⛔ ON PURPOSE** · marker `SC-126-A-PRESERVATION-IS-A-CLAIM`)

> 📌⛔ **POINTER (added 2026-09-24, ⭐ `TASK-1444` (i)): this section gained ⛔ `SC-§126` **cl. 12** — ⛔ *"a fence over a line of code is cited by its ⛔ TEXT, ⛔ never by its ⛔ LINE NUMBER"* — ⛔ written at the ⛔ END of this file under the ⛔ `TASK-1444` amendment block (marker `SC-126-12-CITE-BY-TEXT-NEVER-BY-NUMBER`). ⛔ It is ⛔ there and ⛔ not here ⛔ on purpose: ⛔ inserting it inline would have ⛔ moved every line below it, ⛔ which is ⛔ the exact failure cl. 12 exists to stop.**

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE 09-18 EDITOR DID ⛔ EVERYTHING RIGHT. ⛔ IT SPLIT THE BULLET INTO TWO CLAIMS, ⛔ STRUCK THE FALSE ONE, ⛔ KEPT THE OTHER, ⛔ AND WROTE DOWN THAT THE KEEPING WAS ⛔ DELIBERATE. ⛔ THE SENTENCE IT KEPT WAS ⛔ ALSO FALSE, AND ⛔ NOTHING IN THE PROCEDURE REQUIRED ANYONE TO ⛔ LOOK.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** On 2026-09-18 a bullet at `CONVENTIONS.md:6174` carried ⛔ TWO claims. The command-lane half was ⛔ measured false and ⛔ struck. The bound half — ⛔ *"NO BOUND HAS EVER KILLED A REAL PROCESS"* — was ⛔ KEPT, with the edit stating ⛔ verbatim that it was ⛔ *"SURVIVING ON PURPOSE, NOT BY OVERSIGHT."* ⛔ **It had been FALSE since 2026-09-09** (`handoffs/TASK-1183-buildmaster.md:178` — the `OVERALL` bound killed PID 13816, ⛔ corpse still on disk). ⇒ ⛔ **The word *"purpose"* did ⛔ all the work of a measurement and ⛔ none of the work of one.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, AND IT COSTS THE ⛔ SAME GREP THE STRIKE ALREADY COSTS: ⛔ WHEN AN EDIT ⛔ SPLITS A CLAIM AND ⛔ KEEPS A HALF, THE ⛔ KEPT HALF IS ⛔ RE-MEASURED ⛔ IN THAT EDIT, ⛔ AND THE EDIT ⛔ SAYS SO.** ⛔ Not *"still true as far as I know"*, ⛔ not *"deliberately preserved"*, ⛔ not *"unchanged"* — ⛔ **the ⛔ instrument and the ⛔ reading, ⛔ at the ⛔ date of the edit.** ⇒ ⛔ **An edit that ⛔ dates the strike but ⛔ not the survivor has ⛔ dated only half of itself.**
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — WHY THE OBVIOUS DEFENCE FAILS: ⛔ THE GOVERNING MAXIM WAS ⛔ ALREADY ON THE SAME LINE AND WAS ⛔ OBEYED.** That bullet ⛔ ends with ⛔ *"CORRECTING A FALSE SENTENCE BY SWEEPING THE TRUE ONE BESIDE IT INTO THE STRIKE IS HOW A CORRECTION BECOMES THE NEXT FALSE CLAIM. TWO CLAIMS, TWO VERDICTS, ONE STRIKE."* ⇒ ⛔ **The maxim polices ⛔ SWEEPING A TRUE SENTENCE ⛔ INTO a strike. ⛔ It is ⛔ SILENT on whether the sentence you ⛔ keep is ⛔ still true.** ⛔ **The maxim is ⛔ NOT struck and is ⛔ still correct — ⛔ it is ⛔ INCOMPLETE, and ⛔ this section is its ⛔ missing half.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ *"TWO CLAIMS, TWO VERDICTS, ONE STRIKE"* ⛔ NEEDS ⛔ *"AND BOTH VERDICTS ARE ⛔ MEASURED TODAY."***
4. ⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE FAMILY, SO THE SHAPE IS RECOGNISABLE NEXT TIME IT WEARS DIFFERENT CLOTHES. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ FOURTH APPEARANCE OF ⛔ *AN INTENTION IS NOT A MECHANISM*.** ⭐ `SC-§119` (⛔ measure what the instrument ⛔ COVERS before arguing about what it ⛔ EXCLUDES — ⛔ this one arrives from the ⛔ OPPOSITE direction: ⛔ measure what your ⛔ OWN EDIT KEEPS) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ CLAIM until measured — ⛔ here a ⛔ preserved sentence is) · ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ re-measure at ⛔ YOUR instant, ⛔ never inherit a number) · ⭐ `SC-§39.1` (⛔ a success return is not evidence). ⇒ ⛔ **DISTINCT FROM ALL FOUR: ⛔ those govern what you ⛔ MEASURE. ⛔ THIS ONE GOVERNS WHAT YOU ⛔ DECLINE TO RE-MEASURE ⛔ BECAUSE YOU ⛔ ALREADY DECIDED TO KEEP IT.**
5. 🚨⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ SECOND FAILURE THE SAME EVENT EXPOSED, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ `SC-§50`'s SHAPE ARRIVING FROM THE ⛔ EVIDENCE SIDE: ⛔ A MEASUREMENT CAN BE ⛔ BOUGHT AND ⛔ NEVER HOMED.** `qa/TASK-1182-report.md` check ⛔ **#19** had ⛔ named the wanted evidence ⛔ exactly — ⛔ *"MAKE A BOUND KILL A REAL EDITOR — the one thing 52 cases and 16 mutants cannot buy."* ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1183` ⛔ BOUGHT IT ⛔ THE NEXT DAY, ⛔ wrote it in its handoff, ⛔ and ⛔ NO LAW EVER LEARNED IT.** ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ a row that ⛔ BUYS a measurement a law is ⛔ waiting for ⛔ names the law ⛔ in its handoff, and the ⛔ manager homes it (`SC-§82`). ⛔ A measurement that exists ⛔ only in a handoff is ⛔ evidence nobody will find — ⛔ which is the ⛔ same as evidence nobody took.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — WHAT THIS DOES ⛔ NOT LICENSE.** ⛔ It is ⛔ NOT an instruction to re-verify ⛔ every sentence in an 11 k-line file on ⛔ every edit — ⛔ that is unpayable and would ⛔ stop all law maintenance. ⛔ **The duty is ⛔ SCOPED and ⛔ CHEAP: it attaches ⛔ ONLY to a claim the edit ⛔ TOUCHES — ⛔ the one you ⛔ split, ⛔ the one you ⛔ argue about, ⛔ the one you ⛔ write *"still"* or *"on purpose"* or *"unchanged"* next to.** ⇒ ⛔ **If you were confident enough to ⛔ defend it in writing, you were ⛔ close enough to ⛔ measure it.**
7. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — THE ⛔ CROSS-FILE COROLLARY, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-19 ⛔ HOURS AFTER THIS SECTION WAS MINTED, ⛔ BECAUSE THE SECTION ⛔ IMMEDIATELY CAUSED THE FAILURE IT DESCRIBES: ⛔ A CLAIM ABOUT ⛔ ANOTHER FILE'S ⛔ CURRENT STATE HAS A ⛔ HALF-LIFE MEASURED IN ⛔ HOURS IN A CONCURRENT PIPELINE. ⛔ ANCHOR TO ⛔ QUOTED TEXT — ⛔ NEVER TO A LINE NUMBER, AND ⛔ NEVER TO *"X STILL READS Y."*** (⛔ bought by ⭐ `TASK-1317` ⛔ BLOCKER-2, ⛔ and the ⛔ offending edit was ⛔ THE MANAGER'S OWN STRIKE)
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT, AND THE ⛔ RECURSION IS THE POINT.** ⭐ `TASK-1310` wrote, ⛔ correctly and ⛔ carefully, a comment at `run_suite_bounded.ps1:127-134` declaring that the ⛔ code and the ⛔ law ⛔ DIVERGED — asserting the law *"still reads, unstruck."* ⛔ **It was ⛔ TRUE when written.** ⛔ Then the manager ⛔ STRUCK both sentences at `CONVENTIONS.md`, ⛔ hours later, ⛔ while the row sat in the ⛔ QA queue. ⇒ ⛔ **the divergence was ⛔ RESOLVED, so the paragraph declaring it became ⛔ THE NEXT STALE SENTENCE — ⛔ inside the comment block whose ⛔ entire purpose is not shipping stale sentences.** ⛔ **⛔ NOT the row's error at its instant, and ⛔ no gate and no test could ever have gone red for it.**
   - ⛔ **THREE INSTANCES IN ⛔ ONE SESSION, ⛔ WHICH IS WHY IT IS A CLAUSE AND NOT A NOTE — ⛔ ONE SPECIES, ⛔ THREE CLOCKS:** ⛔ (i) ⛔ **THIS ONE** — falsified by a ⛔ CONCURRENT agent editing the described file · ⛔ (ii) ⭐ `TASK-1311`'s in-code comment calling Play Again *"a product question ⛔ owed to Jonathan"* — falsified ⛔ MID-FLIGHT by 🧑 **his answer arriving while the row was being written** · ⛔ (iii) the ⛔ *"surviving on purpose"* preservation (⛔ cl. 1) — ⛔ already ⛔ NINE DAYS false when it was ⛔ deliberately kept. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ CLAUSES 1–6 POLICE THE ⛔ PAST (⛔ re-measure what you keep). ⛔ CLAUSE 7 POLICES THE ⛔ FUTURE (⛔ your sentence will be read after somebody else has moved the thing it describes).***
   - ✅⛔ **THE REMEDY, AND IT IS ⛔ CHEAP AND ⛔ MECHANICAL: ⛔ QUOTE THE TEXT YOU ARE CLAIMING ABOUT.** ⛔ A ⛔ QUOTE either ⛔ matches the other file or it ⛔ does not, and ⛔ anyone can check it in ⛔ one grep ⛔ at ⛔ any later instant. ⛔ **A ⛔ LINE NUMBER goes stale ⛔ SILENTLY (`DECK-§9` cl. 9: ⛔ eleven citations moved `+54` and ⛔ nothing they described changed). ⛔ A ⛔ *"still reads Y"* goes stale ⛔ SILENTLY ⛔ AND ⛔ INVERTS — ⛔ it does not merely point at the wrong place, it ⛔ ASSERTS A FALSEHOOD.**
   - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ STRUCTURAL REASON THIS CLASS IS ⛔ UNCATCHABLE BY ⛔ EVERY GATE THIS PROJECT HAS: ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ ONE KIND OF CLAIM WHOSE TRUTH ⛔ ANOTHER AGENT CAN CHANGE ⛔ WITHOUT TOUCHING YOUR FILE.** ⛔ Your diff is ⛔ clean, your compile is ⛔ green, your suite is ⛔ green, your gate ⛔ passes — ⛔ and the sentence is ⛔ false. ⇒ ⛔ **`HELP-§2`'s shape: ⛔ prose asserting something the project does not do, with ⛔ NO test that can ever go red for it.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE ONLY DEFENCE IS ⛔ AT AUTHORING TIME, WHICH IS ⛔ WHY THIS IS A ⛔ WRITING RULE AND ⛔ NOT A REVIEW RULE.**
   - ⚖️ **KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ re-measure at ⛔ YOUR instant) is the ⛔ READER'S duty; ⛔ **cl. 7 is the ⛔ WRITER'S** — ⛔ write the sentence so the ⛔ reader's re-measurement is ⛔ possible ⛔ and ⛔ cheap. ⭐ `DECK-§9` cl. 9 (⛔ cite the ⛔ FUNCTION, ⛔ line as corroboration) is ⛔ this clause ⛔ already discovered ⛔ in the narrow case of ⛔ addresses; ⛔ cl. 7 ⛔ generalises it to ⛔ every cross-file assertion.
   - ✅🚨⭐⭐ **FOURTH INSTANCE — ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ FIRST ONE ⛔ MEASURED BY AN ⛔ INDEPENDENT READER ⛔ RATHER THAN DISCOVERED AFTER THE FACT, ⛔ WHICH IS WHAT PROMOTES THIS CLAUSE FROM ⛔ ARGUED TO ⛔ EVIDENCED** (recorded 2026-09-19 by the manager, from ⭐ `TASK-1325` §0a; ⛔ the orchestrator confirmed it at a ⛔ fifth instant).
     - ⛔ **THE LEDGER FOR ⛔ ONE SENTENCE — `THE FILE EXISTS` — ⛔ IN ⛔ ONE WORKING DAY:** ⛔ `:5122-5123` (⛔ the board / ⛔ my own text) → ⛔ `:5122` (⭐ `TASK-1323`, ⛔ the gate, ⛔ that morning) → ⛔ `:5140` (⭐ `TASK-1324`, ⛔ the fix, ⛔ that afternoon) → ⛔ `:5140` (⭐ `TASK-1325`, ⛔ the gate, ⛔ later still, ⛔ re-measured ⛔ independently). ⇒ ⛔ **`+18` LINES IN ⛔ ONE DAY, ⛔ INSIDE THE VERY SECTION THAT ⛔ MINTS THIS LAW.** ⛔ **Every reader was ⛔ RIGHT at their own instant and ⛔ no two agreed (⭐ `SC-§91`).**
     - 🚨 ⛔ **THE COUNTERFACTUAL IS THE POINT, AND IT IS ⛔ NOT HYPOTHETICAL: the ⛔ obvious, ⛔ careful, ⛔ gate-prescribed-looking remedy was `:5122`, and it would have shipped ⛔ FALSE ⛔ WITHIN HOURS OF BEING WRITTEN.** ⛔ The row ⛔ declined it and wrote the ⛔ SUBSTRING instead. ⇒ ⛔ **that is ⛔ cl. 8's trap ⛔ caught ⛔ prospectively for the ⛔ first time, ⛔ by the row rather than by the gate ⛔ again.**
     - ⭐ ⛔ **THE SCOREBOARD THIS CHAIN PRODUCED, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE ARGUMENT IN ⛔ FOUR NUMBERS: ⛔ FOUR successive ADDRESSES for this one sentence (⛔ `:5072` → `:5093` → `:5122` → `:5140`) were ⛔ each true and ⛔ each then false. ⛔ ONE SUBSTRING has needed ⛔ ZERO corrections.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY ARTEFACT IN THE CHAIN THAT ⛔ NEVER NEEDED FIXING IS THE ⛔ ONE THAT ⛔ QUOTED THE TEXT.***
     - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ TRANSFERABLE HALF, ⛔ SO THIS IS NOT MERELY A WAR STORY: ⛔ THE ROT WAS MEASURED BY A READER WITH ⛔ NO STAKE IN THE CLAIM.** ⛔ The fix's own *"it moved"* is ⛔ testimony; ⛔ the gate's ⛔ independent re-measurement at a ⛔ later instant is ⛔ evidence. ⇒ ⛔ **when a row's ⛔ central thesis is *"addresses rot"*, its ⛔ gate can ⛔ corroborate the thesis ⛔ for free by ⛔ simply taking the same reading again — ⛔ and it should, ⛔ because that costs ⛔ one grep and ⛔ converts the row's argument into the ⛔ project's data.**
8. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — THE ⛔ REMEDY-SIDE COROLLARY, AND IT IS THE ONE THAT BITES ⛔ GATES: ⛔ A GATE THAT PRESCRIBES A ⛔ LINE NUMBER AS A ⛔ REMEDY CAN ⛔ PRESCRIBE A DEFECT.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1310` REV-2, which ⛔ caught it ⛔ before shipping and ⛔ iterated out of it**)
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** ⭐ `TASK-1317` blocked ⭐ `TASK-1310` for citing `:1646`/`:1643` where ⛔ measured was `:1647`/`:1644`, and ⛔ **prescribed the measured pair as the fix.** 🚨 ⛔ **BUT THE FIX ITSELF — ⛔ rewriting the comment at `:127-134` — ⛔ INSERTS LINES ⛔ ABOVE THE LAUNCH SITE.** ⇒ ⛔ **shipping the ⛔ PRESCRIBED `:1647`/`:1644` would have put a number in the artefact that was ⛔ TRUE WHEN THE GATE MEASURED IT AND ⛔ FALSE WHEN THE ROW COMMITTED IT — ⛔ the identical defect, ⛔ one revision later, ⛔ AND CARRYING THE ⛔ GATE'S OWN AUTHORITY.** ⛔ Shipping values were ⛔ `:1680`/`:1677`.
   - ⛔ **THE TRAP IS ⛔ STRUCTURAL, ⛔ NOT CARELESS, ⛔ WHICH IS WHY IT NEEDS A CLAUSE AND NOT A REMINDER: ⛔ a gate measures the ⛔ PRE-FIX file; ⛔ the remedy ⛔ CHANGES that file; ⇒ ⛔ any address in the remedy is measured against a file that ⛔ WILL NOT EXIST when the remedy lands.**
   - ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ TWO PARTS, ⛔ ONE FOR EACH SIDE.** ⛔ **(a) A GATE PRESCRIBING AN ADDRESS ⛔ SAYS SO ⛔ IN THE PRESCRIPTION:** *"this pair is measured against the ⛔ PRE-FIX file and ⛔ MUST be re-derived after your edit."* · ⛔ **(b) THE ROW ⛔ ITERATES TO A ⛔ FIXED POINT:** ⛔ edit → ⛔ re-grep → ⛔ patch → ⛔ re-grep, ⛔ until the numbers ⛔ stop moving. ⭐ **`TASK-1310` REV-2 did exactly that, and used a ⛔ WIDTH-NEUTRAL digit patch so the ⛔ final re-grep ⛔ could not shift again — ⛔ that detail is the ⛔ craft, and it is ⛔ worth copying.**
   - ⭐⛔ **BETTER STILL, AND IT IS ⛔ cl. 7's REMEDY ARRIVING FROM THE ⛔ OTHER END: ⛔ LEAD WITH A ⛔ GREP ANCHOR AND ⛔ DEMOTE THE NUMBER TO A ⛔ DATED ASIDE.** ⇒ ⛔ **a number that is ⛔ DECORATION cannot rot ⛔ LOAD-BEARINGLY.** ⛔ That is what the shipped clause (a) does, and it is why the ⛔ same fix survived the law moving ⛔ FOUR TIMES in one day.
   - ⚖️ ⛔ **THE SHAPE TO RECOGNISE, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS ⛔ NOT cl. 7: ⛔ cl. 7 is a claim about ⛔ ANOTHER file going stale — ⛔ it needs a ⛔ CONCURRENT AGENT and it happens ⛔ SOMETIMES. ⛔ cl. 8 is a claim about ⛔ THIS file going stale ⛔ BECAUSE OF THE VERY EDIT THAT CARRIES IT — ⛔ it is ⛔ SELF-INFLICTED and ⛔ DETERMINISTIC, and it happens ⛔ EVERY TIME.**
   - ⚖️ ⛔ **KINSHIP, AND IT IS THE ⛔ UNCOMFORTABLE ONE: `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE IT DETECTS) is the ⛔ same session's sibling and the ⛔ same root — ⛔ *A GATE IS AN ARTEFACT AND IS SUBJECT TO ⛔ EVERY LAW ITS SUBJECTS ARE.*** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **TWO OF THE MANAGER'S ⛔ OWN GATES FAILED THAT TEST ⛔ IN ONE DAY: ⛔ this one (⛔ a remedy that prescribed a defect) and ⭐ `TASK-1318`'s ⛔ positive control (⛔ break the ⛔ HEADER's `#>` ⇒ it ⛔ RE-PAIRS against the next of ⛔ 13 block comments ⇒ ⛔ `0` errors ⇒ ⛔ A CONTROL THAT ⛔ CANNOT FIRE). ⛔ BOTH WERE FOUND BY THE ⛔ ROW BEING GATED, ⛔ NOT BY THE GATE.**
9. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 9 — ⛔ A PROBE THAT REPORTS ⛔ CATASTROPHE IS ⛔ ITSELF A CLAIM, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONE CLAIM NOBODY RE-MEASURES, ⛔ BECAUSE IT ⛔ DEMANDS ACTION.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⛔ FOUR measured instrument failures in ⛔ ONE PASS — ⛔ THREE of them the ⛔ ORCHESTRATOR's or the ⛔ HOST's, ⛔ not an agent under gate**)
   - 🚨⛔⛔ **THE ASYMMETRY THAT MAKES THIS ITS OWN CLAUSE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE WHOLE REASON: ⛔ A ⛔ FALSE ALL-CLEAR GETS CAUGHT BY THE ⛔ NEXT GATE. ⛔ A ⛔ FALSE CATASTROPHE GETS ⛔ ACTED ON ⛔ IMMEDIATELY** — ⛔ it triggers a ⛔ stop, a ⛔ rollback, a ⛔ recovery, an ⛔ escalation to 🧑 him — ⛔ **and the action ⛔ DESTROYS THE EVIDENCE that would have refuted it.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ ALARMING READING IS THE ONE THAT ⛔ MOST NEEDS A CONTROL AND THE ONE ⛔ LEAST LIKELY TO GET ONE.***
   - ⛔ **THE FOUR, ⛔ EACH WITH ITS ⛔ CORRECT FORM, ⛔ BECAUSE A LAW THAT NAMES A BROKEN IDIOM WITHOUT ITS REPLACEMENT IS ⛔ HALF A LAW:**
     - ⛔ **(a) 🚨 `grep -c $'\0' <file>` ⛔ DEGENERATES TO AN ⛔ EMPTY PATTERN AND MATCHES ⛔ EVERY LINE.** ⛔ It returned ⛔ **11,964** and ⛔ **36,953** — ⛔ i.e. *"every line of the law and the board is corrupt"* — ⛔ **MINUTES AFTER A REAL POWER EVENT, ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly when it would have been ⛔ BELIEVED.** ⛔ Binary-safe re-run: ⛔ **0 and 0.** ✅ ⛔ **CORRECT FORM: `perl -0777 -ne 'print tr/\0//'`.** ⭐ ⛔ The host ⛔ caught and reported this ⛔ ITSELF rather than passing the reading up.
     - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ A BARE LITERAL `grep` RETURNS A ⛔ FALSE ZERO ON ⛔ THIS PROJECT'S OWN PROSE.** `grep -c "prescribes a line number"` returned ⛔ **0** while ⛔ cl. 8 ⛔ IS PRESENT — ⛔ the text is ⛔ **MARKER-INTERPOLATED** (`⛔ A GATE THA…`). ✅ ⛔ **SEARCH ⛔ CLAUSE HEADERS or a ⛔ MARKER-FREE FRAGMENT, ⛔ with a positive control.** 🚨 ⇒ ⛔ **THIS FILE'S ⛔ OWN STYLE DEFEATS THE ⛔ OBVIOUS SEARCH — ⛔ a fact ⛔ every agent quoting this law needs, and ⛔ a standing hazard of the emoji-marker convention.**
     - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(b-2) ⛔ THE SECOND MEASURED INSTANCE OF (b)'s SPECIES, ⛔ ARRIVING FROM A DIFFERENT DIRECTION AND ⛔ WORTH ITS OWN LABEL: ⛔ A PATTERN THAT ⛔ CANNOT MATCH THE FORM ⛔ ACTUALLY PRESENT RETURNS A ⛔ ZERO THAT IS A FACT ABOUT ⛔ YOUR PATTERN, ⛔ NOT ABOUT THE FILE.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ bought by ⭐ `TASK-1325`'s ⛔ WARN over ⭐ `TASK-1324`)
       - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** The handoff claimed ⛔ *"no address survived anywhere in the file, not merely in my new text"* on the strength of ⛔ `CONVENTIONS\.md:[0-9]` (and three other needles) all returning ⛔ 0. ⛔ **The form actually present in the file is `CONVENTIONS.md at :5966-5969` — ⛔ with ` at ` between — which that pattern ⛔ CANNOT MATCH.** ⛔ A broader sweep (`:[0-9]{3,4}`) finds ⛔ **NINE** surviving sites. ⇒ ⛔ **the zero was ⛔ real, the ⛔ instrument was ⛔ blind, and the ⛔ sentence built on it was ⛔ FALSE AS WRITTEN.**
       - 🚨 ⛔ **AND THE PART THAT MAKES IT A LAW RATHER THAN A NIT: ⛔ THE ⛔ GATE HIT THE ⛔ IDENTICAL BLIND SPOT ⛔ FIRST, AND CAUGHT IT ⛔ ONLY BY ⛔ READING THE HEADER — ⛔ NOT BY SEARCHING IT.** ⇒ ⛔ **two independent agents, ⛔ same file, ⛔ same hour, ⛔ same wrong pattern. ⛔ That is not carelessness; ⛔ it is the ⛔ obvious pattern being ⛔ obviously wrong.**
       - ✅ ⛔ **THE CORRECT FORM, AND IT IS THE ⛔ CONTROL PEOPLE SKIP: ⛔ AN ABSENCE SWEEP OWES A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL — ⛔ A ⛔ KNOWN-PRESENT INSTANCE OF THE THING YOU CLAIM IS ⛔ ABSENT MUST ⛔ MATCH YOUR PATTERN ⛔ BEFORE ITS ZERO MEANS ANYTHING** (⭐ `SC-§39` ⛔ pointed at a ⛔ NEGATIVE claim). ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **A ⛔ NEGATIVE CONTROL (⛔ a deliberately wrong needle returning 0) proves your needle is ⛔ DISCRIMINATING. ⛔ IT PROVES ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER YOUR PATTERN ⛔ COVERS THE TARGET'S SHAPES.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE TWO CONTROLS ANSWER ⛔ DIFFERENT QUESTIONS, ⛔ AND A SWEEP CLAIMING ⛔ ABSENCE NEEDS THE ⛔ ONE EVERYBODY SKIPS.***
       - ⛔ **HOW IT DIFFERS FROM (b), ⛔ SO BOTH ARE RECOGNISABLE: ⛔ (b) is a ⛔ MARKER INTERPOLATION defeating a ⛔ LITERAL. ⛔ (b-2) is a ⛔ SHAPE VARIANT defeating a ⛔ REGEX written against ⛔ ONE SPELLING of the thing.** ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **THE COMBINED RULE: ⛔ ENUMERATE THE ⛔ SHAPES BEFORE YOU SWEEP FOR THE ⛔ ABSENCE OF A ⛔ CLASS** — ⛔ here the class *"an address"* had ⛔ at least two spellings (`file.md:NNN` and `file.md at :NNN`) and the sweep knew ⛔ one.
       - ⭐ ⛔ **WHY IT WAS RECORDED THOUGH THE ⛔ VERDICT DID NOT MOVE (⛔ all nine sites were ⛔ pre-existing, ⛔ outside both hunks, and the ⛔ artefact was ⛔ correct): ⛔ A ⛔ FALSE CLAIM THAT HAPPENS TO ⛔ ACCOMPANY A ⛔ CORRECT ARTEFACT IS THE ⛔ ONE NOBODY HAS AN ⛔ INCENTIVE TO CATCH.** ⛔ Nothing goes red, ⛔ nothing ships broken, ⛔ and the ⛔ next row inherits the sentence as ⛔ established fact. ⇒ ⛔ **that is ⛔ exactly how `:5072` became `:5093`.**
       - 🚨⛔ **THE SIBLING HAZARD, ⛔ NAMED HERE BECAUSE A READER OF THIS BULLET IS ⛔ ABOUT TO GO SWEEPING: ⛔ A ⛔ CITATION AND AN ⛔ EXHIBIT LOOK ⛔ IDENTICAL TO A SWEEP.** ⛔ `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1`'s ` at :5966-5969` / `:6172-6175` are quoted ⛔ BECAUSE THEY ROTTED — ⛔ the sentence carrying them ⛔ says *"and BOTH rotted"*. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ADDRESS BEING ⛔ WRONG MAY BE THE ⛔ POINT. ⛔ READ THE SENTENCE THAT CARRIES AN ADDRESS ⛔ BEFORE YOU DELETE OR REFRESH IT — ⛔ *"FIXING"* AN EXHIBIT ⛔ DESTROYS THE EVIDENCE THE SENTENCE IS ABOUT.*** ⛔ **Fenced on the board at ⭐ `TASK-1327` (3) so the ⛔ next sweep of that file cannot ⛔ tidy them away** (⭐ `SC-§50`: ⛔ the fence is a ⛔ ROW, ⛔ not a memory).
     - ⛔ **(c)** `git rev-parse --short HEAD HEAD^ HEAD^^` ⛔ **FAILS** — *"Needed a single revision"*. ✅ ⛔ **`git rev-list --max-count=N --pretty="%h <- parent %p"`.**
     - ⛔ **(d) ⭐ `SC-§102` cl. 6 ⛔ CAUGHT THE ⛔ ORCHESTRATOR** — a ⛔ relative pipeline path run from the ⛔ GIT ROOT silently matches ⛔ nothing, because the pipeline lives ⛔ one level down. 🚨 ⛔ **HOURS AFTER IT RELAYED cl. 6 TO THE MANAGER.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ KNOWING A LAW IS ⛔ NOT THE SAME AS BEING ⛔ IMMUNE TO IT.***
   - 🚨⭐⭐ **THE STRUCTURAL POINT, ⛔ AND IT IS WHY THREE OF FOUR CAME FROM THE ⛔ TOP OF THE PIPELINE: ⛔ EVERY ARTEFACT HERE HAS A REVIEWER ⛔ EXCEPT THE ⛔ INSTRUMENTS THE REVIEWERS USE.** ⛔ A programmer's diff has a gate; a gate's verdict has a host; a host's commit has a hash. ⛔ **The ⛔ `grep` in the orchestrator's hand has ⛔ nothing.** ⇒ ⛔ **all four were caught by ⛔ RE-MEASURING, ⛔ not by review — ⛔ because ⛔ review was never available.**
   - ✅⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ BEFORE YOU REPORT A CATASTROPHE, ⛔ RE-MEASURE IT WITH A ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENT — ⛔ NOT the same command again, ⛔ a DIFFERENT MECHANISM** (⛔ `perl` vs `grep` · ⛔ `rev-list` vs `rev-parse` · ⛔ an ⛔ ABSOLUTE path vs a relative one) — ⛔ **AND ⛔ STATE BOTH READINGS.** ⛔ **A stop-the-line report with ⛔ ONE instrument behind it is an ⛔ UNCONTROLLED CLAIM, and ⭐ `SC-§39`'s positive control applies to it ⛔ EXACTLY as to any other measurement.**
   - ⚖️ **KINSHIP.** ⛔ cl. 7/8 police a ⛔ CLAIM ABOUT A FILE; ⛔ **cl. 9 polices ⛔ THE CLAIM YOUR INSTRUMENT MAKES ABOUT ⛔ REALITY.**
10. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 10 — ⛔ READ THE ⛔ TOKEN, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ ADDRESS. ⛔ *"VERDICT ON LINE 1"* IS ⛔ FALSE IN THE ⛔ MAJORITY OF THE REPORTS THAT ASSERT IT, AND IT IS ⛔ APPLIED ⛔ INCONSISTENTLY ACROSS THE THREE THAT EXIST.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1318`'s ⛔ OWN REWRITTEN CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ the clause written to ⛔ CURE an `SC-§124` failure contained a ⛔ FRESH `SC-§124` ⛔ ONE REWRITE LATER** · marker `SC-126-READ-THE-TOKEN`)
