    - ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT (⛔ orchestrator, ⛔ three reports, ⛔ 2026-09-19):** `qa/TASK-1323-report.md` line 1 = `# QA Report — TASK-1323` ⇒ ⛔ verdict on ⛔ **line 2** · `qa/TASK-1317-report.md` ⛔ same shape ⇒ ⛔ **line 2** · `qa/TASK-1312-report.md` ⇒ ⛔ verdict ⛔ **IS** on line 1 (⛔ embedded in the title). ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **THREE REPORTS, ⛔ THREE DIFFERENT SHAPES. ⛔ The convention is ⛔ not merely mis-stated — ⛔ it is ⛔ INCONSISTENTLY APPLIED, ⛔ so ⛔ no single address can ever be right.**
    - 🚨⛔⛔ **THE SELF-INDICTMENT, ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ STRONGEST EVIDENCE AND IT IS ⛔ MINE: ⭐ `TASK-1318`'s clause 1 — ⛔ WHICH I REWROTE ⛔ SPECIFICALLY TO CURE AN `SC-§124` FAILURE — ⛔ ASSERTS *"`qa/TASK-1323-report.md` ⛔ line 1 = PASS"*, AND THAT IS ⛔ LITERALLY FALSE.** ⛔ **It was satisfied ⛔ ONLY because the host ⛔ READ THE VERDICT RATHER THAN THE LINE.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A PREDICATE THAT SURVIVES ⛔ SOLELY ON ITS READERS' GOOD SENSE IS THE ⛔ DEFINITION OF A PREDICATE THAT IS ⛔ NOT DOING ITS JOB.***
    - ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ TWO HALVES, ⛔ BOTH BINDING.**
      - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ AUTHORING — ⛔ every QA / verify report carries a line matching the ⛔ TOKEN `Verdict:`. ⛔ ITS POSITION IS ⛔ UNCONSTRAINED.** ⛔ **A title, a front-matter block or a preamble ⛔ ABOVE it is ⛔ FINE and ⛔ always was — ⛔ fighting Markdown's natural first line is ⛔ HOW THIS BROKE.**
      - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ READING — ⛔ a predicate over a report ⛔ NAMES THE ⛔ TOKEN, ⛔ NEVER THE ⛔ ADDRESS:** ✅ *"`qa/X-report.md` ⛔ `Verdict:` = PASS"*, ⛔ satisfied by ⛔ GREPPING THE TOKEN. ⛔ **NEVER *"line 1"*.**
    - 🚨⛔ **SCOPE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THIS IS A ⛔ LAW AND ⛔ NOT A SWEEP: ⛔ `TASKBOARD.md` carries ⛔ 102 OCCURRENCES of *"line 1"* (⛔ measured 2026-09-19).** ⇒ ⛔ **THEY ARE ⛔ SUPERSEDED ⛔ WHOLESALE BY THIS CLAUSE AND ARE ⛔ NOT INDIVIDUALLY EDITED.** ⛔ **Editing 102 sites is ⛔ EXACTLY ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 7a's *"a rule that depends on someone remembering to name the file is a ⛔ HABIT, ⛔ NOT A RULE"* — ⛔ this law binds ⛔ WITHOUT being copied onto a row.** ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **FROM THIS DATE, A ROW SAYING *"verdict on line 1"* ⛔ READS AS *"verdict at the `Verdict:` token"*, ⛔ IN BOTH DIRECTIONS, ⛔ EVERYWHERE.**
    - ⚖️ **KINSHIP, AND IT COMPLETES THE SET.** ⛔ cl. 7 = ⛔ anchor to quoted text, ⛔ never a line number — ⛔ applied to ⛔ ANOTHER file. ⛔ cl. 8 = ⛔ applied to a ⛔ REMEDY. ⛔ **cl. 10 = ⛔ applied to a ⛔ ROW'S OWN ⛔ GATE PREDICATE — ⛔ THE LAST PLACE ANYBODY LOOKED, ⛔ because a `blocked-by` ⛔ FEELS LIKE STRUCTURE RATHER THAN PROSE.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ADDRESS IS A ⛔ LIABILITY ⛔ WHEREVER IT APPEARS, AND A `blocked-by` IS ⛔ NOT EXEMPT FOR BEING ⛔ SHORT.*** ⭐ `SC-§102` cl. 6 is the ⛔ SPECIAL CASE for git; ⛔ this is the ⛔ GENERAL law. ⭐ `SC-§96` / `SC-§39.1` (⛔ a success return is not evidence) is its ⛔ MIRROR: ⛔ **THERE the tool said ⛔ *fine* and was not asked; ⛔ HERE the tool says ⛔ *disaster* and is ⛔ not asked either.**

11. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 11 — THE ⛔ LEDGER OF WRONG NUMBERS IS ⛔ ITSELF A SOURCE OF WRONG NUMBERS: ⛔ A NUMBER QUOTED FROM A ⛔ ROT-LEDGER IS ⛔ NOT THEREBY MEASURED — AND ⛔ cl. 7's ⛔ OWN FOURTH-INSTANCE LEDGER PUT AN ADDRESS THAT ⛔ NEVER EXISTED INTO A ⛔ BOARD CLAUSE ⛔ AS FACT.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1331` clause (1) — ⛔ MY OWN BOARD TEXT — ⛔ caught by the ⛔ ROW and confirmed ⛔ FOUR independent ways by ⭐ `TASK-1332`** · marker `SC-126-A-LEDGERED-NUMBER-IS-NOT-A-MEASURED-ONE`)
    - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** The board's ⭐ `TASK-1331` clause (1) illustrated banned shape (a) with ⛔ **`CONVENTIONS.md:5122`**, presented as ⛔ the instance `f5697f3` deleted. ⛔ **The text `f5697f3` actually deleted was `CONVENTIONS.md:5093`.** ⛔ The row ⛔ measured it and ⛔ used its own value; the gate confirmed it ⛔ WITHOUT git — ⛔ the fix row's handoff ⛔ quotes the deleted text verbatim · ⭐ `TASK-1324`'s ⛔ own board spec says it · ⛔ two build-master handoffs name it · and ⛔ **the surviving wound in the script is the deleted parenthetical's ⛔ exact context ⛔ minus the parenthetical.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE WOUND MATCHES THE CORPSE.**
    - 🚨⛔⛔ **WHERE `:5122` CAME FROM, WHICH IS THE ⛔ ENTIRE POINT: ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ THIRD ENTRY OF ⛔ cl. 7's OWN LEDGER `:5072 → :5093 → :5122 → :5140` — ⛔ THE ⛔ FORBIDDEN FOURTH NUMBER ⭐ `TASK-1324` WAS ⛔ EXPLICITLY BANNED FROM WRITING, ⛔ LIFTED OUT OF THE BAN AND ⛔ RE-PRESENTED AS ⛔ PROVENANCE.** ⇒ ⛔ **The ledger is ⛔ genuine measured data — ⛔ about ⛔ WHEN each number was true. ⛔ Quoting one entry as *"the instance"* ⛔ SILENTLY DROPS THE INDEX: ⛔ four numbers exist for ⛔ one sentence and ⛔ at most one of them is ⛔ the one you mean.**
    - ⛔ **THE COUNTERFACTUAL, ⛔ AND IT IS THE COST: ⛔ HAD THE ROW ⛔ OBEYED THE BOARD, the shipped guard's ⛔ own documentation would have recorded, ⛔ AS BAN-LIST PROVENANCE, ⛔ A CITATION THAT NEVER EXISTED — ⛔ inside the comment whose ⛔ entire subject is ⛔ false provenance.** ⇒ ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§101` working ⛔ exactly as designed: ⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ CLAIM, the row ⛔ measured it, and ⛔ the board was ⛔ wrong.**
    - ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ WHEN YOU CITE AN INSTANCE OUT OF A ROT-LEDGER, ⛔ RE-MEASURE IT AGAINST THE ⛔ ARTEFACT — ⛔ the quote, the handoff, the diff.** ⛔ The ledger tells you a number ⛔ ROTTED; it ⛔ never tells you ⛔ which entry was ⛔ yours. ✅ ⛔ **PREFERRED FORM: ⛔ quote the ⛔ DELETED OR SURVIVING TEXT — ⛔ cl. 7's remedy applied to ⛔ the ledger itself.**
    - ⚖️ **KINSHIP, AND IT IS THE ⛔ UNCOMFORTABLE ONE AGAIN: cl. 8's *"a gate is an artefact and is subject to every law its subjects are"* ⛔ APPLIED TO THE ⛔ LAW'S OWN EVIDENCE SECTION.** ⛔ cl. 7 (⛔ anchor to quoted text) is ⛔ what this ledger exists to ⛔ teach; ⛔ cl. 8 (⛔ a remedy can prescribe a defect) is the ⛔ gate-side twin; ⭐ `SC-§128` cl. 1 (⛔ the reader's ⛔ instrument error) is the ⛔ same family one file over. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ONE PLACE NOBODY THOUGHT TO RE-MEASURE WAS THE ⛔ LIST OF NUMBERS THAT ⛔ PROVES NUMBERS ROT.***

### SC-§125 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A UE5 BLUEPRINT COMPILE DOES ⛔ NOT DIRTY THE PACKAGE ⇒ THE NAMED SAVE HAS ⛔ NOTHING TO WRITE AND ⛔ RETURNS `true`. AND THE ⛔ IN-MEMORY AUTO-RECOMPILE THAT HIDES IT REACHES THE ⛔ HEADLESS `-nullrhi` SUITE, ⛔ NOT JUST THE GUI EDITOR ⇒ ⛔ A SUITE RUN IS EVIDENCE ABOUT THE CLASS THE ⛔ PROCESS COMPILED, ⛔ NOT ABOUT THE CLASS ⛔ ON DISK.** (added 2026-09-18 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1298`'s 5a leg, which ran `TASK-1298` A2-1's four calls in the prescribed order and ⛔ STOPPED — correctly — because the predicate turned out ⛔ UNSATISFIABLE** · marker `SC-125-A-COMPILE-DOES-NOT-DIRTY-AND-A-GREEN-SUITE-IS-NOT-THE-DISK`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **I WROTE A STOP CONDITION WHOSE ⛔ MIDDLE LINK WAS ⛔ FALSE. `save_assets` RETURNED `true` OVER A ⛔ BYTE-IDENTICAL FILE WHOSE ⛔ MTIME WAS ⛔ NOT EVEN TOUCHED — AND THE ⛔ ONE INSTRUMENT THAT WOULD HAVE *"CONFIRMED"* THE FIX, A ⛔ GREEN SUITE, COULD ⛔ NOT HAVE SEEN THE DEFECT ⛔ IN EITHER PROCESS.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ IN THE ORDER THE CALLS WERE MADE, ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ MECHANISM IS IN THE ⛔ SEQUENCE.** Subject: `/Game/Blueprints/BP_MenuGameMode`, whose graph edit was ⛔ on disk while its ⛔ generated class had been compiled from the ⛔ old graphs.
   - ⛔ **(1)** `AssetTools.is_dirty` → ⛔ `false` (⛔ pre-state).
   - ⛔ **(2)** `BlueprintTools.compile_blueprint` → ⛔ clean; `UBlueprint::Status` moved ⛔ `BS_DIRTY` → ⛔ `BS_UP_TO_DATE` ⇒ ⭐ **the compile ⛔ GENUINELY LANDED ⛔ IN MEMORY. ⛔ Nothing failed.**
   - ⛔ **(3)** `is_dirty` ⛔ AFTER → ⛔ **still `false`**; ⛔ dirty content packages ⛔ **0**; ⛔ dirty map packages ⛔ **0**.
   - ⛔ **(4)** `AssetTools.save_assets(["/Game/Blueprints/BP_MenuGameMode"])` — ⛔ the ⛔ EXPLICIT single asset — returned ⛔ **`true`** and ⛔ **WROTE NOTHING**.
   - ⇒ 🚨 **sha256 `fd9817f0…66954962`, ⛔ `41634` bytes, mtime `2026-09-18 19:31:20` — ⛔ BEFORE ⛔ AND AFTER. ⛔ THE MTIME WAS ⛔ NOT EVEN TOUCHED.**
2. ⭐⭐⭐⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE MECHANISM, AND IT IS ⛔ ENGINE DESIGN RATHER THAN A BUG, WHICH IS ⛔ WHY IT WILL RECUR: ⛔ A UE5 BLUEPRINT COMPILE DOES ⛔ NOT MARK THE PACKAGE DIRTY.** ⛔ It must not — ⛔ **compile-on-load would otherwise dirty ⛔ every asset in the project at every startup.** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ chain `compile ⇒ dirty ⇒ save ⇒ hash change` has a ⛔ FALSE MIDDLE LINK, so a save gated on dirtiness has ⛔ nothing to write and ⛔ correctly reports success.** ⇒ ✅ **THE RULE FOR THE AUTHOR: ⛔ a predicate over `.uasset` BYTES may ⛔ not be routed through a ⛔ DIRTY FLAG. ⛔ Name the ⛔ writer that is ⛔ unconditional, ⛔ or name the ⛔ human.**
3. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE REMEDY, AND IT IS A 🧑 ⛔ HUMAN KEYSTROKE (🧑 Jonathan chose it 2026-09-18 from three options put to him): ⛔ THE BP EDITOR'S OWN SAVE PATH (⛔ BP editor → ⛔ Compile → ⛔ `Ctrl+S`) WRITES THE PACKAGE ⛔ UNCONDITIONALLY.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **THE BOARDING RULE THAT FALLS OUT OF IT, AND IT IS THE ⛔ TRANSFERABLE PART: ⛔ ANY ROW WHOSE DELIVERABLE IS A ⛔ COMPILED BLUEPRINT CARRIES A ⛔ HUMAN KEYSTROKE, AND THE ⛔ HONEST PLACE TO SAY SO IS THE ⛔ BOARD, ⛔ AT BOARDING** (⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 2 — ⛔ declare the ceiling at boarding, ⛔ never discover it at 5b). ⛔ First application: ⭐ `TASK-1311`, boarded with the hand-save named as a ⛔ declared cost.
   - ⛔ **AND THE INVARIANT IS ⛔ NOT RELAXED BY THE ROUTE CHANGE: ⛔ THE CLASS INSIDE THE ⛔ COMMITTED `.uasset` MUST BE THE CLASS ⛔ COMPILED FROM THE ⛔ FIXED GRAPH.** ⛔ **The reason is ⛔ shipping, not tidiness: ⛔ A COOKED BUILD DOES ⛔ NOT RECOMPILE ON LOAD, ⛔ SO A STALE SERIALIZED CLASS SHIPS AS A ⛔ REAL DEFECT.** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **"⛔ correct in memory + ⛔ compile-on-load at every consumer" is ⛔ REFUSED as a satisfying condition ⛔ wherever it is offered.**
4. 🚨🚨🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE HALF THAT ⛔ OUTRANKS THE ROW, AND IT IS A ⛔ TIMESTAMP ARGUMENT RATHER THAN AN INFERENCE: ⛔ THE FALSE GREEN REACHES THE ⛔ HEADLESS `-nullrhi` SUITE LANE, ⛔ NOT JUST THE LIVE GUI EDITOR.** The `.uasset` was written at ⛔ **19:31:20**; the editor process that read `BS_DIRTY` only ⛔ **STARTED at 21:07:07**. ⇒ ⛔ **that reading was taken in a ⛔ FRESH PROCESS that had loaded ⛔ exactly those disk bytes** ⇒ ⛔ **a fresh process arriving at ⛔ `BS_DIRTY` (⛔ NOT `BS_Unknown`) is ⛔ NOT excluded by `PlayLevel.cpp:1290`'s guard** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **THE HEADLESS SUITE PROCESS WOULD HAVE ⛔ SILENTLY AUTO-RECOMPILED THE CLASS ⛔ IN MEMORY TOO** (`AutoRecompileBlueprints=True`, `BaseEditorPerProjectUserSettings.ini:242`, ⛔ no project override ⇒ `bPromptForCompile = false`; sweep call `PlayLevel.cpp:2656`).
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐⭐ ***⛔ A SUITE RUN IS EVIDENCE ABOUT THE CLASS THE ⛔ PROCESS COMPILED, ⛔ NOT ABOUT THE CLASS ⛔ ON DISK.*** ⛔ **A green ⛔ ×3 could ⛔ not have discriminated a ⛔ stale on-disk class ⛔ from a fixed one.**
   - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ when a wave ships a `.uasset` whose ⛔ GENERATED CLASS is the payload, ⛔ the ⛔ HASH is the gate and the ⛔ SUITE is ⛔ corroboration ⛔ about a different object.** ⛔ **Say which claim each instrument covers ⛔ in the handoff; ⛔ do ⛔ not let ⛔ one green stand for ⛔ both.**
   - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ RELAUNCH ROUTE IS ⛔ STILL THE ⛔ HONEST FAILURE, ⛔ NOT A FIX:** `UBlueprint::Status` is `UPROPERTY(transient)` (`Blueprint.h:507`) ⇒ ⛔ it reloads `BS_Unknown`, the `:1290` guard ⛔ then excludes it, and the suite goes ⛔ legitimately RED. ⛔ **Do ⛔ not "fix" a red there by relaunching, and do ⛔ not read a pre-relaunch green as a pass.**
5. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — `save_assets` RETURNING `true` OVER A BYTE-IDENTICAL FILE IS ⭐ `SC-§39.1` (*"a success return is not evidence"*) ⛔ IN ITS ⛔ PUREST FORM — ⛔ THE ⛔ ONE SHAPE THAT SAILS PAST A HOST CHECKING ⛔ ONLY THE RETURN VALUE.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE CHEAP CONTROL, ⛔ THREE FIELDS, ⛔ ALWAYS: ⛔ sha256 ⛔ AND byte count ⛔ AND mtime, ⛔ before and after.** ⛔ **The ⛔ mtime is the ⛔ giveaway and it is the ⛔ one people omit: ⛔ a writer that ⛔ truly wrote ⛔ always moves it, ⛔ even when the bytes coincide.**
6. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — THE TWO REFUSALS ARE RECORDED AS ⛔ RIGHT CONDUCT, ⛔ SO THEY ARE ⛔ COPIED RATHER THAN RE-DERIVED — AND SO IS THE ⛔ WAY THE HOST ⛔ PROVED THE LANE EXHAUSTED ⛔ INSTEAD OF ASSUMING IT.** ⛔ **The proof (⛔ read-only, ⛔ one `describe_toolset`):** `compile_blueprint` has ⛔ exactly two properties (`blueprint`, `warnings_as_errors`) with ⛔ no save/dirty/flush/persist parameter · a regex sweep for `save|dirty|flush|persist|to disk` across ⛔ ALL 53 `BlueprintTools` entries returned ⛔ ZERO hits · `save_assets` has ⛔ no force flag. ⛔ **The refusals:** ⛔ (i) it ⛔ would not improvise a ⛔ metadata-tag write to force a dirty — ⛔ that injects an ⛔ unsanctioned tag into a ⛔ committed asset · ⛔ (ii) it ⛔ would not reach for `execute_unreal_python` as the ⛔ refused/unlisted tool. ⇒ ⛔ **BOTH CORRECT** (`SC-§54` cl. 1: ⛔ a refusal is ⛔ reported, ⛔ never routed around). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ "THE LANE IS EXHAUSTED" IS ITSELF A ⛔ CLAIM AND IS ⛔ MEASURED THE SAME WAY EVERYTHING ELSE IS — ⛔ ENUMERATE THE SURFACE, ⛔ QUOTE THE COUNT, ⛔ THEN STOP.*** ⛔ **A host that ⛔ hunts for a tool that slips through has ⛔ stopped being a host.**
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, ⛔ CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SC-§124` (⛔ a literal predicate over file state can become ⛔ UNSATISFIABLE — ⛔ **this is its ⛔ SECOND instance in ⛔ two days, and the ⛔ purer one: ⛔ there the predicate fired for the ⛔ wrong reason, ⛔ here it ⛔ cannot fire at all**) · ⭐ `SC-§39.1` (⛔ a success return is not evidence — ⛔ cl. 5) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ a pin that cannot fail) · ⭐ `SC-§122` cl. 4 (⛔ a gate that ⛔ PASSES for the wrong reason is worse than one that fails — ⛔ cl. 4's green ⛔ is exactly that) · ⭐ `SC-§123` cl. 2 (⛔ the instrument measured something ⛔ ADJACENT to the claim) · ⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 2 (⛔ declare the ceiling at ⛔ BOARDING — ⛔ cl. 3's boarding rule is its ⛔ `.uasset` twin) · `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE, ⛔ ONE MORE TIME: ⛔ THE CHECK MEASURED SOMETHING ⛔ ADJACENT TO THE CLAIM AND THE REPORT ⛔ SPENT THE CLAIM — ⛔ AND HERE THE ⛔ ADJACENT THING WAS A ⛔ RETURN VALUE AND A ⛔ PROCESS'S MEMORY, WHILE THE CLAIM WAS ⛔ THE BYTES ON DISK.***

### SC-§124 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A GATE CLAUSE WRITTEN AS A ⛔ LITERAL PREDICATE OVER FILE STATE CAN BECOME ⛔ UNSATISFIABLE — AND WHEN IT DOES IT FORCES THE HOST TO CHOOSE BETWEEN THE ⛔ LETTER AND THE ⛔ POINT. ⛔ EVERY STOP CONDITION SHIPS WITH THE ⛔ INVARIANT IT PROTECTS, ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ SO THE SUBSTANCE IS CHECKABLE WHEN THE LETTER IS NOT.** (added 2026-09-18 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1306` clause (4)'s pre-flight: it said ⛔ STOP if any of `TASK-1286`'s nine committed paths shows as ⛔ modified — and ⛔ THREE of them ⛔ NECESSARILY were, because `TASK-1304`'s gated diff sat ⛔ ON TOP of them and was ⛔ the very thing the row existed to commit** · marker `SC-124-NAME-THE-INVARIANT-NOT-JUST-THE-PREDICATE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **I WROTE A STOP CONDITION THAT THE ROW'S ⛔ OWN CORRECT EXECUTION WAS GUARANTEED TO TRIP. ⛔ OBEYED LITERALLY, THE ROW COULD ⛔ NOT BE EXECUTED AT ALL. ⛔ THE HOST DID THE RIGHT THING AND ⛔ SAID SO — ⛔ THAT IS THE ONLY REASON THIS IS A LAW AND NOT AN INCIDENT.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE TRAP IS ⛔ ORDINARY, WHICH IS WHY IT WILL RECUR.** The clause's ⛔ intent was *"⛔ do not sweep a ⛔ DIFFERENT `TASK-1286` diff into this commit."* Its ⛔ wording was *"if `git status` offers you any of those nine as ⛔ modified, ⛔ STOP."* ⛔ **Between the writing and the execution, a ⛔ LEGITIMATE, ⛔ GATED, ⛔ BOARD-ORDERED diff (`TASK-1304`) landed in three of those same nine files.** ⇒ ⛔ **the predicate fired on ⛔ exactly the payload the row was ⛔ created to ship.**
2. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ROOT CAUSE, ⛔ STATED GENERALLY BECAUSE THE SPECIFIC CASE IS THE LEAST INTERESTING PART: ⛔ A PATH-LEVEL PREDICATE WAS USED TO GUARD A ⛔ CONTENT-LEVEL INVARIANT.** *"⛔ file X is unmodified"* and *"⛔ the committed meaning of X has not been altered"* are ⛔ DIFFERENT CLAIMS, and ⛔ they diverge the instant ⛔ any later row is allowed to touch X. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A STOP CONDITION EXPRESSED OVER ⛔ PATHS IS ONLY VALID WHILE ⛔ NOTHING ELSE MAY WRITE THOSE PATHS — AND ⛔ ON THIS PROJECT, WITH CONCURRENT LANES OVER ⛔ ONE WORKTREE, THAT CONDITION IS ⛔ ALMOST NEVER TRUE*** (`SC-§59` cl. 1: a host's tree is ⛔ routinely dirty with files no row under review touched).
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE FOR THE ⛔ AUTHOR (⛔ me, and every future row author): ⛔ EVERY STOP CONDITION SHIPS IN ⛔ TWO PARTS — ⛔ THE ⛔ PREDICATE ⛔ AND THE ⛔ INVARIANT IT PROTECTS, ⛔ NAMED IN WORDS, ⛔ ON THE SAME LINE.** ⛔ The predicate is the ⛔ cheap check; ⛔ **the invariant is what the host falls back to when the predicate is ⛔ unsatisfiable, ⛔ ambiguous, or ⛔ obviously firing for the wrong reason.** ⛔ Worked form: ~~*"if any of the nine is modified, STOP"*~~ ⇒ ✅ *"⛔ STOP if the ⛔ committed `TASK-1286` content has changed — ⛔ i.e. if `<named commit>` does not carry all nine paths, or if `SetFocusedCardIndex`/`IsCardGridFocusLive` differ from their committed bytes. ⛔ A path showing `M` because a ⛔ LATER GATED ROW wrote it is ⛔ EXPECTED; ⛔ verify the diff is ⛔ additive and ⛔ proceed, ⛔ saying so."*
4. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE RULE FOR THE ⛔ HOST, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A LICENCE: ⛔ WHEN THE LETTER IS ⛔ IMPOSSIBLE, ⛔ CHECK THE SUBSTANCE, ⛔ AND ⛔ DECLARE THAT YOU DID.** ⛔ **THE EXHIBIT, recorded as ⛔ RIGHT CONDUCT so it is ⛔ copied rather than re-derived — `TASK-1306`'s host:** ⛔ named the conflict, ⛔ verified `6bd1aa5` carried ⛔ all nine paths, ⛔ verified `SetFocusedCardIndex`/`IsCardGridFocusLive` ⛔ unchanged, ⛔ verified the sitting diff was ⛔ ADDITIVE, ⛔ then proceeded ⛔ and wrote down every step. ⇒ ⛔ **THE THREE CONDITIONS, ⛔ ALL REQUIRED: ⛔ (i) the letter is ⛔ DEMONSTRABLY unsatisfiable — ⛔ show it, ⛔ do not assert it · ⛔ (ii) the ⛔ INVARIANT is identified and ⛔ measured by some other means · ⛔ (iii) the ⛔ substitution is ⛔ REPORTED, in the handoff ⛔ and in Slack** (`SC-§58` cl. 2 — ⛔ the contradiction ⛔ IS a deliverable). ⛔ **Fail any of the three and it is ⛔ not this clause, it is ⛔ a host routing around a gate.**
5. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ BOUNDARY, ⛔ SO THIS CLAUSE IS ⛔ NEVER QUOTED AS A ⛔ GENERAL OVERRIDE. ⛔ IT APPLIES ⛔ ONLY WHEN THE PREDICATE IS ⛔ IMPOSSIBLE TO SATISFY, ⛔ NEVER WHEN IT IS MERELY ⛔ INCONVENIENT, ⛔ UNEXPECTED, ⛔ OR DISAGREED WITH.** ⛔ A gate that fires because something is ⛔ actually wrong is ⛔ doing its job, and *"⛔ I checked the substance instead"* is then ⛔ exactly the failure `SC-§97` and `CONVENTIONS:2679` cl. 13 forbid (⛔ an agent AGREEING with a ruling is ⛔ precisely what does not authorise departing from it). ⇒ ⛔ **IF THERE IS ⛔ ANY READING UNDER WHICH THE LETTER COULD BE SATISFIED, ⛔ SATISFY IT. ⛔ THE ESCAPE HATCH IS FOR ⛔ CONTRADICTION, ⛔ NOT FOR ⛔ FRICTION.**
6. ⛔ **CLAUSE 6 — AND THE ⛔ RECONCILIATION IS ⛔ MINE, ⛔ NOT THE HOST'S** (`SC-§58` cl. 1: the ⛔ ROW binds, the ⛔ MANAGER reconciles). ⇒ ⛔ **a reported letter-vs-point conflict obliges the manager to ⛔ REWRITE THE CLAUSE, ⛔ not merely to thank the host** — ⛔ otherwise the ⛔ same defective clause ⛔ ships again next batch and the ⛔ next host, ⛔ with less nerve, ⛔ stops a correct row.
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, ⛔ CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** `SC-§58` (⛔ dispatch-vs-row conflict ⇒ ⛔ obey the narrower, ⛔ report the disagreement — ⛔ **this is the ⛔ SAME ETIQUETTE applied ⛔ INSIDE one clause, where there is ⛔ no narrower option to take**) · `SC-§59` (⛔ attribute a dirty file by ⛔ CONTENT, ⛔ never by testimony — ⛔ the instrument cl. 3 prescribes) · `SC-§122` cl. 4 (⛔ a gate that ⛔ passes for the wrong reason; ⛔ **this is its mirror — a gate that ⛔ FAILS for the wrong reason, ⛔ and the mirror is ⛔ cheaper only because somebody spoke up**) · `SC-§105` (⛔ a recipe can be ⛔ complete-looking and ⛔ incomplete) · `SC-§97` (⛔ cl. 5's fence). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A CHECK IS ⛔ ONLY AS GOOD AS THE ⛔ CLAIM IT IS WRITTEN OVER — ⛔ AND A CHECK WRITTEN OVER A ⛔ PROXY FAILS IN ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS.***

### SC-§123 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A STATE-ASSERTING TEST PROVES THE MECHANISM ⛔ WORKS ⛔ WHEN INVOKED — IT SAYS ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER ⛔ ANYTHING INVOKES IT. ⛔ AN ENTRY PATH NEEDS ⛔ ITS OWN EVIDENCE: ⛔ NAME THE CALLER, ⛔ OR NAME THE ENGINE MECHANISM THAT WILL CALL IT, ⛔ AND READ THAT MECHANISM ⛔ AT SOURCE.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1286`: ⛔ TWO gate PASSes — `TASK-1290` ⛔ and the `TASK-1299` ⛔ RE-GATE — ⛔ plus a compile `Result: Succeeded` and a ⛔ 561/561 green suite, on a feature that ⛔ CANNOT EXECUTE A ⛔ SINGLE LINE OF ITS ⛔ OWN ENTRY PATH** · marker `SC-123-REACHABILITY-IS-NOT-CORRECTNESS`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **EVERY INSTRUMENT WAS ⛔ WORKING. ⛔ THE GATES VERIFIED THE CODE'S ⛔ INTERNAL CONSISTENCY; ⛔ NOTHING VERIFIED ITS ⛔ REACHABILITY. ⛔ THE FEATURE WAS ⛔ INERT AND ⛔ EVERY GREEN LIGHT WAS ⛔ HONEST.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE COST, STATED FIRST SO THE CLAUSE IS ⛔ NEVER FILED AS PEDANTRY.** `UDeckBuilderWidget::NativeOnKeyDown` could ⛔ not receive an arrow key on ⛔ ANY route (⛔ keyboard, ⛔ mouse-then-keyboard, ⛔ gamepad): ⛔ nothing put the widget on Slate's ⛔ focus path, ⛔ and a focused descendant ⛔ converts an arrow to ⛔ navigation and returns ⛔ `Handled` ⛔ before any ancestor sees it (`SWidget.cpp:416-429`; the bubble starts at the ⛔ LEAF, `SlateApplication.cpp:388-406`). ⇒ ⛔ **the handler's `Down` branch held the ⛔ ONLY non-test callers of the functions that ⛔ ARM the feature ⇒ a ⛔ CLOSED LOOP WITH ⛔ NO DOOR.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — WHY THE TESTS WERE ⛔ RIGHT AND ⛔ STILL PROVED NOTHING. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ SUBTLE PART.** The suite passed because it calls the entry points ⛔ DIRECTLY — `ExitCardGridFocus()`, `SetFocusedCardIndex()` — ⛔ which is ⛔ EXACTLY what ⭐ `SC-§104` asks for and ⛔ remains correct practice. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A DIRECT-INVOCATION TEST MEASURES THE ⛔ MECHANISM. ⛔ REACHABILITY IS A ⛔ DIFFERENT CLAIM WITH A ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENT, AND ⛔ NO NUMBER OF THE FIRST EVER ⛔ ADDS UP TO THE SECOND.*** ⛔ `SC-§104` is ⛔ NOT weakened by this clause — it is ⛔ COMPLETED by it.
3. ✅ **CLAUSE 3 — THE OUTPUT SHAPE, ⛔ AND IT IS A ⛔ GATE ITEM, ⛔ NOT A COURTESY.** For ⛔ every handler, delegate, override or callback the ⛔ ENGINE invokes (`NativeOnKeyDown`, `NativeOnMouseDown`, `Tick`, a bound delegate, a timer): ⛔ the report names ⛔ **EITHER** a caller in ⛔ our code at `file:line`, ⛔ **OR** the ⛔ engine routine that dispatches it, ⛔ READ AT SOURCE with a ⛔ path and a ⛔ line — ⛔ **PLUS the ⛔ PRECONDITION that routine requires ⛔ and the line that ⛔ ESTABLISHES it.** ⛔ *"The engine calls this"* is ⛔ NOT evidence. ⛔ *"`ProcessKeyDownEvent` routes along `GetFocusPath()` (`SlateApplication.cpp:4961`) ⇒ this runs ⛔ iff the widget holds focus; focus is set at `<file:line>`"* ⛔ IS — ⛔ and if that second half is ⛔ missing, the finding is ⛔ *"UNREACHABLE"*, ⛔ not a WARN.
4. ⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ CIRCULARITY CHECK, ⛔ CHEAP AND ⛔ MECHANICAL. RUN IT ON EVERY NEW ENTRY-POINT FUNCTION.** ⛔ List its ⛔ non-test callers. ⛔ **If they ⛔ ALL live inside ⛔ ONE handler, then ⛔ THAT HANDLER'S REACHABILITY ⛔ IS THE WHOLE FEATURE'S REACHABILITY** — ⛔ and if the handler is unreachable the ⛔ entire dependent tree (⛔ here: Accept, Remove ⛔ and the exit gesture, ⛔ all behind an `IsCardGridFocusLive()` that ⛔ can never become true) is ⛔ dead ⛔ without a single test failing. ⛔ Two `grep`s answer it.
5. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ DISCLOSURE TRAP, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ PROCESS FINDING RATHER THAN ANY ONE AGENT'S ERROR.** The gap was ⛔ DECLARED: `TASK-1286`'s deliverable (0) left *"does Slate navigation reach the grid?"* ⛔ EXPLICITLY UNMEASURED — and ⛔ FOUR readers (⛔ QA twice, ⛔ the orchestrator, ⛔ the manager) ⛔ accepted the route argument ⛔ without spending the measurement. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A DECLARED-UNMEASURED PREMISE THAT THE FEATURE ⛔ DEPENDS ON IS A ⛔ BLOCKER, ⛔ NOT A CAVEAT — ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS ⛔ NOT A QUALIFIER ON THE CLAIM, ⛔ IT ⛔ IS THE CLAIM.*** ⛔ **Disclosure is ⛔ not discharge; ⛔ writing *"unmeasured"* next to the load-bearing sentence ⛔ transfers nothing to anybody.**
6. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — THE ⛔ LOOK-ALIKE CLAUSE: ⛔ WHEN THE ENGINE'S ⛔ OWN DEFAULT BEHAVIOUR ⛔ RESEMBLES THE FEATURE, A ⛔ HUMAN ACCEPTANCE QUESTION MUST ⛔ DISCRIMINATE — ⛔ OR IT ⛔ MANUFACTURES A ⛔ FALSE PASS.** Here Slate's ⛔ own navigation still walks focus into a card tile's button with a ⛔ visible focus rectangle, and `Enter` fires ⛔ that button's existing `OnClicked` (⛔ the details panel) through `SButton`'s Accept path. ⇒ ⛔ **the screen ⛔ FEELS navigable while ⛔ every line of the feature is ⛔ dead**, and *"⛔ does an outline appear when you press Down?"* returns a ⛔ YES that ⛔ means nothing. ✅ **THE WORKED EXAMPLE, ⛔ KEPT VERBATIM BECAUSE THE WORDING ⛔ IS THE INSTRUMENT:** ***"With a card outlined, does pressing `Enter` raise the deck total by ⛔ one — or does it ⛔ only open the card's details panel?"*** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ ASK FOR THE ⛔ EFFECT ⛔ ONLY YOUR CODE CAN PRODUCE, ⛔ NEVER FOR THE ⛔ APPEARANCE THE PLATFORM CAN PRODUCE ⛔ WITHOUT YOU.***
7. ⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — FAMILY. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ SEVENTH INSTANCE OF ⛔ ONE SHAPE IN ⛔ A SINGLE DAY, AND THE ⛔ MOST EXPENSIVE:** ⭐ `SC-§119` cl. 8 (⛔ measure what the instrument ⛔ COVERS) · the ⛔ absence-pin mirror · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ a pin that cannot fail) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert STATE, not tallies) · ⭐ `SC-§122` (⛔ existence is not status) · the ⛔ hunk-count proxy · ⛔ **this**. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ ALL SEVEN ARE ⛔ ONE SENTENCE: ⛔ THE CHECK MEASURED SOMETHING ⛔ ADJACENT TO THE CLAIM AND THE REPORT ⛔ SPENT THE CLAIM.*** ⛔ **And the ⛔ distinguishing cost of ⛔ this one: the adjacent thing was ⛔ the code's ⛔ CORRECTNESS, the claim was its ⛔ EXISTENCE AS A BEHAVIOUR — ⛔ and ⛔ nothing downstream of a gate can ⛔ recover a claim the gate ⛔ never held.**
8. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 8 — WHERE THE FAULT SITS, ⛔ SO THE LESSON LANDS ON THE ⛔ RIGHT DESK.** The ⛔ implementer built ⛔ the spec faithfully; ⛔ both gates did ⛔ the reading they claimed. ⛔ **The defect was in the ⛔ SPEC — it named an entry route and ⛔ never required anyone to ⛔ MEASURE it.** ⇒ ⛔ **a spec that describes ⛔ how a feature is ⛔ entered owes an ⛔ ACCEPTANCE LINE that ⛔ measures the entry, ⛔ in the same way it owes one for the ⛔ behaviour.** ⛔ Precedent and first application: ⭐ `TASK-1304` (5) and its gate ⭐ `TASK-1305` (1).

### SC-§122 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **EXISTENCE IS ⛔ NOT STATUS — ⛔ READING A SYMBOL AT ITS DECLARATION IS ⛔ NOT A DEPRECATION CHECK. ⛔ *"n SYMBOLS READ AT THE LINE, 0 DEPRECATED"* IS A TALLY PRODUCED BY A METHOD THAT ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE RETURNED ANY OTHER NUMBER.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1299` vs `TASK-1291`'s 5a compile: the gate read the ⛔ 6 delta symbols ⛔ at the line — `InputCoreTypes.h:518/747`, `GenericPlatformInput.h:32-35` — and reported *"0 deprecated, 0 removed"*; the compiler then found ⛔ TWO deprecated among ⛔ exactly those symbols** · marker `SC-122-EXISTENCE-IS-NOT-STATUS`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE DEFECT IS IN THE ⛔ METHOD, ⛔ NOT THE DILIGENCE. QA ⛔ DID THE READING IT CLAIMED, AT THE LINES IT NAMED, AND THE READING ⛔ CANNOT ANSWER THE QUESTION IT WAS ASKED.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE TWO QUESTIONS, STATED SO THEY STOP BEING ONE.** *"Does this symbol ⛔ EXIST in UE 5.8?"* is answered by ⛔ landing on its declaration. *"Is this symbol still ⛔ SANCTIONED in UE 5.8?"* is answered ⛔ ONLY by finding — or failing to find — the ⛔ DEPRECATION MARKER attached to it. ⛔ **A reader who lands on the declaration is looking at a ⛔ TRUE statement about the first question and ⛔ recording it as an answer to the second.**
2. ✅ **CLAUSE 2 — THE MARKER IS SEARCHED FOR ⛔ BY NAME, AND THE NAME GOES IN THE REPORT.** `UE_DEPRECATED(` · `UE_DEPRECATED_FORGAME(` · `[[deprecated` · `DEPRECATED_(`/`_DEPRECATED` · a `@deprecated` or `// Deprecated` doc line. ⛔ **Search at the declaration ⛔ AND at the symbol's DEFINITION / REGISTRATION SITE** — in UE the marker frequently sits on a ⛔ different line from the one a reader lands on (a block above, an adjacent overload, or the `.cpp` where the value is actually registered), and a `static const FKey` member reads ⛔ perfectly ordinary at its declaration. ⛔ **`EKeys::Virtual_Accept` is exactly that shape.**
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE OUTPUT SHAPE (⭐ `SC-§104`: assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not tallies).** ⛔ *"14 symbols read at the line, 0 deprecated"* is ⛔ NOT A FINDING. ✅ *"14 symbols; marker `UE_DEPRECATED` searched at each declaration ⛔ and at each definition site; ⛔ 2 hits: `<sym>` at `<file:line>`, `<sym>` at `<file:line>`"* ⛔ IS.
4. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE POSITIVE CONTROL IS ⛔ MANDATORY HERE, BECAUSE THE FAILURE MODE IS A ⛔ SILENT ZERO (⭐ `SC-§39`).** Before reporting ⛔ 0 deprecated, run the ⛔ SAME search against a symbol ⛔ KNOWN to carry the marker and ⛔ show it firing. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN INSTRUMENT THAT ⛔ CANNOT RETURN A NON-ZERO ANSWER HAS ⛔ NOT MEASURED ZERO.***
   - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **WORKED EXHIBIT, AND IT IS THE ⛔ AUTHOR'S OWN, ⛔ WITHIN THE HOUR OF WRITING THIS CLAUSE — ⛔ 2026-09-17, ⛔ SAME DAY, ⛔ DIFFERENT PAYLOAD.** Having just written *"a symbol read is not a deprecation check"*, the ⛔ manager pinned `TASK-1291`'s `CONVENTIONS.md` hunk fence at ⛔ **SEVEN** — a number reached by ⛔ COUNTING HER OWN TWO EDITS on top of the previous five, ⛔ NOT by running the command. ⛔ **Measured by the coordinator on the actual bytes: `git diff -U0 … | grep -c '^@@'` = ⛔ 5.** ⛔ Both inserts had landed ⛔ INSIDE contiguous already-added blocks and produced ⛔ ZERO new `@@`. ⇒ ⛔ **TWO EDITS, ⛔ ZERO HUNKS.**
   - ⛔⛔ **AND IT FAILED IN THE ⛔ ONE DIRECTION THAT LEAVES ⛔ NO TRACE:** the fence read *"if the total is ⛔ neither 5 nor 7, STOP"* — and the ⛔ TRUE value, ⛔ 5, was ⛔ INSIDE the allowed set. ⇒ ⛔ **the host would have ⛔ SAILED THROUGH while ⛔ concluding from that 5 that the ⛔ new laws never landed.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A GATE THAT PASSES FOR THE ⛔ WRONG REASON IS ⛔ WORSE THAN ONE THAT FAILS, BECAUSE ⛔ NOTHING ASKS AGAIN.***
   - ✅⭐⭐ **THE TWO TRANSFERABLE MOVES, ⛔ BOTH CHEAPER THAN THE ERROR:** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ A COUNT ⛔ NOBODY RAN IS ⛔ NOT A COUNT** — ⛔ a pin must describe what the ⛔ TOOL WILL ACTUALLY EMIT, ⛔ never what the author believes they ⛔ did (⛔ the ⛔ identical error was caught at the ⛔ `TASK-1293` stage the ⛔ same day, where ⛔ 3 was measured against an expected ⛔ 2 — ⛔ the remedy was ⛔ written and then ⛔ not applied by its own author). ⛔ **(b) ⭐⭐ ⛔ PIN THE ⛔ CLAIM, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ PROXY:** a ⛔ hunk count is a ⛔ PROXY for *"the right edits are present"* and is ⛔ SENSITIVE TO REPRESENTATION (⛔ `-U0` and ⛔ default context ⛔ coalesce differently — ⛔ measured 3 vs 2 on the ⛔ same bytes); a ⛔ GREP FOR THE EDIT'S OWN ⛔ MARKER is the ⛔ claim itself and is ⛔ INSENSITIVE to it. ⇒ ⛔ **when both are available, the ⛔ marker is the gate and the ⛔ count is corroboration — ⛔ and whichever you use, ⛔ NAME THE EXACT COMMAND.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐ ***⛔ THE POINT OF THIS EXHIBIT IS ⛔ NOT THAT THE AUTHOR ERRED. ⛔ IT IS THAT ⛔ KNOWING THE LAW, ⛔ HAVING JUST WRITTEN IT, AND ⛔ BEING ABLE TO RECITE IT ⛔ DID NOT PREVENT IT — ⛔ ONLY ⛔ RUNNING THE COMMAND DOES. ⛔ THAT IS WHY CLAUSES 2–4 PRESCRIBE A ⛔ PROCEDURE AND ⛔ NOT AN ATTITUDE.***
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — AND THE HONEST LIMIT: ⛔ THE INSTRUMENT THAT ACTUALLY ANSWERS THIS IS THE ⛔ COMPILER, AND ⛔ QA HOLDS NO COMPILER** (⭐ `SC-§71b`). ⇒ ⛔ **a text-level deprecation check is a ⛔ BEST EFFORT and the report ⛔ says so**, naming the 5a host as the row that measures it. ⛔ **That is ⛔ not an excuse to skip clauses 2–4 — it is ⛔ why they exist: the cheap check must be ⛔ shaped like the expensive one, or it adds ⛔ nothing but confidence.**
6. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ A COMPILE THAT SUCCEEDS ⛔ WITH WARNINGS IS ⛔ NOT A CLEAN COMPILE, AND THE 5a HOST ⛔ REPORTS THE WARNING ⛔ DELTA, ⛔ NOT JUST `Result: Succeeded`.** Every `C4996` ends *"otherwise your project will no longer compile"* ⇒ ⛔ **it is a ⛔ DEFERRED ERROR with a ⛔ delivery date: the next engine upgrade.** ⇒ ⛔ **NEW warnings on symbols the diff ⛔ INTRODUCED get ⛔ BOARDED AS THEIR OWN ROW** — ⛔ never folded as a quiet edit into the wave that produced them, because that wave's gate ⛔ already passed a diff that ⛔ did not contain the fix (⭐ `SC-§100`). ⛔ Precedent and first application: ⭐ `TASK-1301`. ⭐⛔ **CLARIFIED 2026-09-18 BY THE MANAGER, ⛔ ON THE FIRST CASE THIS CLAUSE ITSELF PRODUCED — ⛔ READ IT BEFORE YOU FLAG A FOLD: *"ITS OWN ROW"* IS A ⛔ BOARDING REQUIREMENT AND A ⛔ GATE REQUIREMENT. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A REQUIREMENT FOR ITS OWN ⛔ COMPILE WAVE.** The harm this clause names is ⛔ precise: *"that wave's gate ⛔ already passed a diff that ⛔ did not contain the fix."* ⇒ ✅ **LEGAL: the migration executes inside a ⛔ LATER wave whose ⛔ OWN gate reads the migrated lines** (`TASK-1301`'s two lines ride ⭐ `TASK-1304`, gated by ⭐ `TASK-1305`, hosted by ⭐ `TASK-1306`). ⛔ **ILLEGAL: the migration executes inside the ⛔ SAME wave that produced the warning, ⛔ after that wave's gate has passed** — that is the quiet edit, and it stays forbidden. ⇒ ⛔ **THE TEST IS ⛔ ONE QUESTION: ⛔ did a gate read the migrated bytes? ⛔ If yes, the fold is clean ⛔ however many rows it spans; ⛔ if no, it is the defect ⛔ regardless of how many rows it wears.**
7. ⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — FAMILY, so the shape is recognisable next time it wears different clothes.** ⭐ `SC-§119` cl. 8 (⛔ measure what the instrument ⛔ COVERS before arguing about what it ⛔ EXCLUDES) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ a pin that cannot fail proves nothing) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert STATE, not tallies). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ ALL FOUR ARE ONE SENTENCE: ⛔ THE CHECK MEASURED SOMETHING ⛔ ADJACENT TO THE CLAIM AND THE REPORT SPENT THE CLAIM.***

### SC-§121 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE CLOSED-RULING REGISTER LAW — ⛔ A KEY BINDING IS ⛔ NOT A UI CHOICE. ⛔ EVERY KEY ADDED TO A SPEC IS ⛔ CENSUSED AGAINST THE CLOSED-RULING REGISTER ⛔ BEFORE IT REACHES 🧑 HIM — AND ⛔ AN OPTION PUT IN FRONT OF HIM THAT ⛔ SILENTLY CONTAINS A ⛔ SECOND DECISION IS ⛔ NOT A CLEAN ASK.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1286`'s `Escape`: ⛔ QA proposed the key, the ⛔ ORCHESTRATOR put it in 🧑 his option table, the ⛔ MANAGER specced it — and the ⛔ GAMEPLAY-PROGRAMMER, the ⛔ FOURTH and ⛔ LAST hand, is the ⛔ ONLY one who cited `AS-§6 A-2`** · marker `SC-121-CENSUS-THE-KEY-AGAINST-THE-REGISTER`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE RULING WAS ⛔ WRITTEN, ⛔ CLOSED, ⛔ PERMANENT AND ⛔ CITED BY THE CLASS ⛔ NEXT DOOR IN THE SAME FEATURE (`DeckSlotEntryWidget.h:60`). ⛔ NOBODY LOOKED. ⛔ A REGISTER THAT IS NEVER CONSULTED IS THE ⛔ SAME AS NO REGISTER.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** `AS-§6 A-2` (2026-08-04, 🧑 Jonathan's, ⛔ CLOSED) says ⛔ verbatim that *"a Slate `FReply::Handled()` on `EKeys::Escape` … is overturning a Jonathan ruling and is an automatic QA FAIL."* `TASK-1286`'s 2026-09-17 amendment ordered ⛔ exactly that construct, ⛔ by name, and ⛔ cited A-2 ⛔ nowhere. It ⛔ reached implementation (`DeckBuilderWidget.cpp:1514`) before anyone noticed. ⛔ **It was caught by the ⛔ implementer, ⛔ not by any gate**, and dropped ⛔ before it compiled.
2. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, AND IT COSTS ⛔ ONE GREP: ⛔ BEFORE A KEY NAME ENTERS A SPEC, ⛔ CENSUS IT AGAINST THE REGISTER, AND ⛔ WRITE THE RESULT ON THE ROW.** Grep `CONVENTIONS.md` for the ⛔ key name ⛔ AND for the ⛔ shape of the absorb (`Handled`, `NativeOnKeyDown`, `NativeOnPreviewKeyDown`, `BindAction`, *"never absorbed"*, *"automatic QA FAIL"*). ⛔ **A hit ⇒ the row ⛔ CITES the ruling and says how it complies or ⛔ escalates to 🧑 him. ⛔ A miss ⇒ the row ⛔ SAYS *"censused against the register, no ruling"* — ⛔ the absence is ⛔ STATED, ⛔ never assumed** (`SC-§70`: a zero quoted without whether anything enforces it is ⛔ half a fact).
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ASK CLAUSE, AND IT IS THE HALF THAT ⛔ ESCALATES: ⛔ AN OPTION'S ⛔ LABEL MUST CONTAIN ⛔ EVERY DECISION INSIDE IT.** The option he selected read ***"Move remove to X, B exits grid"***. `Escape` rode along ⛔ inside the preview body, ⛔ not in the label. ⇒ ⛔ **he chose ⛔ ONE thing and ⛔ authorised ⛔ TWO.** ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ every binding, file, key or behaviour an option will produce is named ⛔ IN THE LABEL, or it is ⛔ NOT IN THE OPTION.** ⛔ If the label gets too long, that is the ⛔ signal it is ⛔ two asks.
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — WHY THIS IS WORSE THAN AN ORDINARY SPEC ERROR: ⛔ HIS SELECTION IS WHAT EVERYTHING DOWNSTREAM TREATS AS ⛔ AUTHORISATION (`SC-§97`).** An ordinary bad spec is ⛔ one agent's mistake and ⛔ any reviewer may refuse it. ⛔ **A decision smuggled inside an option he picked acquires ⛔ HIS AUTHORITY ⛔ without his attention** — and `SC-§97` then ⛔ correctly forbids every downstream agent from substituting it. ⇒ ⛔ **the smuggling ⛔ disarms the very law that protects his choices.** ⛔ That, ⛔ not the keystroke, is the harm.
5. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE POSITIVE EXHIBIT, AND IT IS THE BEHAVIOUR TO COPY: ⛔ ON A SPEC-VS-RULING COLLISION, ⛔ BUILD IT AND ⛔ FLAG IT — ⛔ NEVER SILENT DEVIATION, ⛔ NEVER SILENT COMPLIANCE.** The gameplay-programmer implemented the spec (⛔ `SC-§97` forbids substituting 🧑 his key choice), ⛔ AND raised it at ⛔ both code sites ⛔ AND in the handoff, ⛔ AND named the exact reversal (⛔ one line) ⛔ AND measured the mitigation (A-2's protected routes are in `ASiegePlayerController`, gated on in-match state, ⛔ unreachable from `L_MainMenu`). ⇒ ⛔ **that is the whole remedy: the row cost ⛔ one line and ⛔ zero compiles. A silent deviation would have cost a ⛔ QA loop; silent compliance would have cost ⛔ a shipped overturn of his ruling.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — THE REVERSIBLE-PATH RULE, FOR WHEN HE IS ⛔ NOT AVAILABLE.** A collision with a closed ruling is ⛔ never resolved by an agent's reading, ⛔ however good (`CONVENTIONS:2679` cl. 13: ⛔ *a reviewer AGREEING with it is exactly what does not authorise it*). ⇒ ✅ **take the ⛔ SUBTRACTION that keeps 🧑 his headline ruling ⛔ intact, ⛔ record the question with the ⛔ measured evidence and the ⛔ restore cost, and ⛔ let him answer.** ⛔ **Prefer the path whose ⛔ undo is one line ⛔ over the path that needs his ⛔ authority to exist.** ⛔ And the row that ⛔ later adds the thing ⛔ CITES the ruling ⛔ explicitly, so the register shows the exception was ⛔ GRANTED rather than ⛔ MISSED. ✅⭐⭐ **EXERCISED END-TO-END AND ⛔ THE UNDO WAS SPENT — 2026-09-18.** The subtraction was taken 2026-09-17 while he was away; he ruled `TASK-1300` **scoped** on 2026-09-18; the ⛔ one line goes back at ⭐ `TASK-1304` block B ⛔ citing `AS-§6 A-2` and ⛔ his ruling date. ⇒ ⛔ **the reversible path cost ⛔ exactly what it promised — ⛔ one line out, ⛔ one line in, ⛔ zero rework, ⛔ zero overturned ruling — and the ⛔ register now shows a ⛔ GRANTED exception. ⛔ THAT IS THE WHOLE POINT OF THE CLAUSE: it is ⛔ not a way of stalling, it is a way of making his answer ⛔ cheap to honour ⛔ either way.**
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** `SC-§97` (🧑 his choice is ⛔ not substitutable) · `SC-§100` (a rescope is a ⛔ board edit) · `SC-§101` (a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim until measured) · `SC-§70` (an absence quoted ⛔ without its enforcer) · `CONVENTIONS:2679` cl. 13 (⛔ agreement ⛔ is not authorisation). ⇒ ⛔ **DISTINCT FROM ALL FIVE: those govern what an agent may ⛔ CHANGE. ⛔ THIS ONE GOVERNS WHAT AN AGENT MAY PUT ⛔ IN FRONT OF HIM** — and it is the ⛔ only failure in the family that gets ⛔ stronger, ⛔ not weaker, as it travels downstream.

### SC-§120 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE BOARD-EDIT LAW — ⛔ COMMIT ⛔ BEFORE YOU EDIT THE BOARD, AND WRITE IT ⛔ TEMP-FILE-THEN-REPLACE. ⛔ A TRUNCATING OPEN (`open(path,'w')`) DESTROYS THE FILE AT THE MOMENT IT OPENS, SO ⛔ ANY EXCEPTION AFTER THAT POINT LEAVES ⛔ NOTHING TO RECOVER FROM — AND THE ⛔ COMMIT IS THE ⛔ ONLY BACKUP THIS PROJECT HAS.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1293`'s host — its first board-flip script threw a `UnicodeEncodeError` ⛔ MID-WRITE and truncated `TASKBOARD.md` from ⛔ 36,237 LINES TO 3,349** · ⛔ **SECOND TIME THIS EXACT FAILURE MODE HAS HIT THIS PROJECT** · marker `SC-120-COMMIT-BEFORE-YOU-EDIT-THE-BOARD`)

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASURED EVENT.** `TASK-1293`'s host had two status flips to write (`SC-§103` owes N flips on a batch commit). Its take-1 script opened `TASKBOARD.md` with Python `open(…, 'w')` and threw `UnicodeEncodeError` **partway through the write** — ⛔ lone surrogates from emoji escapes meeting the encoder mid-stream. ⛔ **The truncation had already happened before the exception fired**: `'w'` truncates ⛔ at open, ⛔ not at close. ⛔ **91 % of the board was gone** — ⛔ 36,237 lines → ⛔ 3,349.
2. ⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — WHY IT WAS ⛔ RECOVERABLE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE REASON THIS LAW LEADS WITH "COMMIT FIRST".** `git checkout -- TASKBOARD.md` restored it ⛔ EXACTLY — ⛔ **because the host had committed the board minutes earlier.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE COMMIT WAS THE BACKUP.** ⛔ Nothing else on this project snapshots a 36 k-line file: ⛔ no autosave, ⛔ no `.bak`, ⛔ no editor history. ✅ **THE RULE: a host about to flip rows ⛔ COMMITS FIRST — or confirms a ⛔ clean tree at a ⛔ NAMED HEAD — ⛔ before the first board write.** ⛔ An uncommitted board edit that fails mid-write is ⛔ unrecoverable, and ⛔ the board is the pipeline's ⛔ single source of truth (`CLAUDE.md`).
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE WRITE SHAPE, ⛔ AND IT IS NOT NEGOTIABLE: ⛔ NEVER A TRUNCATING OPEN.** Take 2 is the law: **(a)** ⛔ ENCODE THE PAYLOAD TO BYTES ⛔ IN MEMORY FIRST (explicit `encoding='utf-8'`, `errors=` chosen ⛔ deliberately) — ⛔ so an encoding failure happens ⛔ BEFORE anything on disk is touched; **(b)** write to a ⛔ TEMP FILE; **(c)** ⛔ VERIFY ⛔ IN THE TEMP FILE — the ⛔ line count ⛔ and ⛔ every flip the edit owes (`SC-§103`); **(d)** ⛔ `os.replace` (⛔ atomic on Windows for same-volume paths). ⛔ **A verification performed ⛔ AFTER the replace is a verification of a file you can ⛔ no longer compare against.**
4. ⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — THE CHEAPEST FORM OF ALL, FOR THE AGENTS WHO DO MOST OF THE FLIPPING.** An agent holding only the `Edit` tool ⛔ CANNOT truncate the board: `Edit` is a ⛔ partial replacement that ⛔ fails ⛔ LOUDLY on an absent or non-unique anchor. ⇒ ✅ **prefer `Edit` for board flips; reach for a script ⛔ only when the edit count makes it impractical — and then obey clauses 2–3.** ⛔ Writing a whole 36 k-line file to change two status lines is the ⛔ risk, ⛔ not the convenience.
5. ⚠️⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — IT IS THE ⛔ SECOND TIME, WHICH IS WHY IT IS A LAW AND NOT A NOTE.** On **2026-09-08** an orchestrator truncated `MEMORY.md` with the ⛔ same Python `open(…,'w')` shape; ⛔ that one was ⛔ NOT recoverable from git and the file had to be ⛔ RECONSTRUCTED by hand (the reconstruction notice still stands at the top of `MEMORY.md`'s `m7-m8-pending-decisions` entry). ⇒ ⛔ **the failure mode is ⛔ not exotic, ⛔ not transient and ⛔ not somebody's bad day — it is the ⛔ DEFAULT BEHAVIOUR of the most obvious way to write a file.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP, CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** `SC-§103` (⛔ a batch commit owes N flips) is ⛔ what makes board edits big enough to script — ⛔ this law governs ⛔ HOW they are written. `SC-§102` (⛔ a mis-anchored pathspec answers with SILENCE) is ⛔ adjacent but ⛔ distinct: ⛔ there the tool did ⛔ NOTHING; ⛔ here the tool did ⛔ MOST of a thing and ⛔ stopped. `SC-§39.1` (⛔ a success return is not evidence) is the ⛔ family: ⛔ this one did ⛔ not even return.

### SC-§119 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE SCOPE LAW — ⛔ MEASURE WHAT AN INSTRUMENT ⛔ COVERS BEFORE ARGUING ABOUT WHAT IT ⛔ EXCLUDES; AND ⛔ A NEGATIVE ANSWER IS WORTH ONLY THE ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY CHECKABLE ACCOUNT ATTACHED TO IT — ⛔ AN ABSTENTION YOU ⛔ ASKED FOR IS ⛔ NEVER ITSELF EVIDENCE.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by `TASK-1283` — ⛔ FOUR ROWS AND THREE RULINGS (`TASK-1258` · `1259` · `1265` · `1283`; R12 · R15 · R16) SPENT ARGUING WHETHER AN IGNORE PATTERN EXCLUDED A FILE FROM AN INDEX THAT ⛔ NEVER CONTAINED `Config/` AT ALL** · marker `SC-119-MEASURE-THE-SCOPE-NOT-THE-EXCLUSION`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **EVERY ROW IN THAT LANE INHERITED ONE ⛔ UNMEASURED PREMISE: *"the index is a crawl of the project tree."* ⛔ IT IS NOT. IT IS A ⛔ CURATED MEMORY DOCUMENT — `Saved/.Aura/project_memory.txt`, ⛔ 61 LINES, ⛔ COPIED VERBATIM by `Tools/aura_sync.ps1` FROM THE ⛔ GIT-TRACKED, ⛔ HAND-AUTHORED `Docs/AuraProjectMemory.md`. ⛔ THE SUBJECT OF THE ARGUMENT WAS ⛔ NEVER IN THE INSTRUMENT'S FIELD OF VIEW.**
> ### ⚠️⛔ **AND THE FIRST DRAFT OF THIS VERY SECTION SAID *"GENERATED BY `aura_sync.ps1` FROM OUR OWN TWO `Docs/` CANONICALS"* — ⛔ A PROVENANCE THE MANAGER ⛔ NEVER MEASURED, WRITTEN INTO THE LAW FILE ⛔ IN THE ACT OF WRITING THE LAW AGAINST DOING THAT. ⛔ SEE CLAUSE 8.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MEASUREMENT (2026-09-17, orchestrator, ⛔ AT SOURCE, ⛔ not relayed).** `Saved/.Aura/project_memory.txt` = **61 lines**; case-insensitive grep for `config/|SiegeCloudDev|AnonKey|ProjectUrl|DefaultInput` = **ZERO matches**. Its headings: the team (`.claude/agents/`) · asset prefixes (from `CONVENTIONS.md`) · texture suffixes (from `CONVENTIONS.md`) · the build command · *"Laws Aura must never break"*. ⇒ ⛔ **THE `AnonKey` VALUE IS NOT IN THE INDEX, AND `Config/` WAS NEVER IN ITS SCOPE.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE RULE, AND IT IS THE WHOLE LAW: ⛔ BEFORE YOU BOARD A ROW ABOUT WHAT AN INSTRUMENT ⛔ EXCLUDES, MEASURE WHAT IT ⛔ INCLUDES — ⛔ ENUMERATE ITS SCOPE FROM THE ⛔ ARTEFACT IT PRODUCES, ⛔ NEVER FROM THE EXISTENCE OF ITS CONFIGURATION FILE.** ⛔ A file named `INDEX_IGNORE.txt` is evidence that ⛔ *something* can be ignored. ⛔ It is ⛔ NEVER evidence of ⛔ what is covered. ⭐ **The cheap instrument was on disk the whole time** — the generated memory document is one `Read` away and answers the scope question in ten seconds; ⛔ four rows asked a model about the edges of a set nobody had listed.
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ABSTENTION CLAUSE, ⛔ MINTED AND CORRECTED IN THE SAME ACTION.** *"Answer from your project index only, ⛔ without calling any tools"* is a ⛔ REQUEST, ⛔ not a fence — and it was measured ⛔ BOTH WAYS on ⛔ one day, ⛔ same model, ⛔ same seat: `TASK-1265`'s control ⛔ IGNORED it (Aura self-reported a ⛔ failed `grep` **and** a ⛔ successful file read under the instruction that forbade both), `TASK-1283`'s probe ⛔ HONOURED it. ⇒ ⛔ **IT IS UNRELIABLE, ⛔ NOT USELESS — and the ⛔ practical consequence is the same: ⛔ NEVER BUILD A PROBE WHOSE CONCLUSION RESTS ON THE ⛔ ABSTENTION ALONE.** ⚠️ ⛔ A rendered *"Called N tools"* badge is ⛔ the same shape whether the answer came from the index or the disk (R15) — ⛔ so the badge does not rescue it either.
4. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — WHAT ⛔ DOES MAKE A NEGATIVE COUNT: ⛔ THE CHECKABLE ACCOUNT.** `TASK-1283`'s *"I don't know"* became ⛔ EVIDENCE only because the answer ⛔ volunteered an enumeration of what the index ⛔ does hold, and that enumeration was ⛔ VERIFIED against `project_memory.txt` ⛔ on disk, ⛔ heading for heading. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A NEGATIVE RESULT IS ONLY AS GOOD AS THE ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY VERIFIABLE ACCOUNT ATTACHED TO IT. ⛔ A BARE "I CANNOT SEE IT" PROVES ⛔ NOTHING — ⛔ it is indistinguishable from a model declining, guessing, or being asked the wrong question.*** ⛔ Write the corroboration ⛔ into the row's acceptance, ⛔ or do not board the probe.
5. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ THE GOOD NEWS MUST NOT EAT THE FINDING: THE INDEX IS CLEAN; THE DISK IS NOT.** A clean index is ⛔ NOT the exposure `R15` adopted. `R15`'s finding is that Aura's ⛔ FILE TOOLS read the disk on demand — ⛔ re-proven ⛔ TWICE on 2026-09-17: the control read `DefaultInput.ini` ⛔ off disk under an instruction forbidding it, and the value probe ⛔ volunteered *"If you want it, I can open the file directly the way I did for `DefaultInput.ini`."* ⇒ ⛔ **`R15`'s ACCEPTED-EXPOSURE CONDITIONS STAND ⛔ UNRELAXED: (a) `ACC-§11`'s contents rule (`ProjectUrl` + `AnonKey` and nothing else) · (b) the `TASK-1266` on-disk credential census · (c) `TASK-1267`'s doc correction — ⛔ which gets ⛔ SHARPER, not softer: the ignore list is ⛔ not a fence ⛔ AND the index was ⛔ never the exposure route.**
6. 📌 **CLAUSE 6 — CAPABILITY FACT, RECORDED SO THE NEXT PROBE IS NOT WRITTEN AROUND IT.** Aura's `grep` ⛔ CANNOT TARGET `.ini`: it fails with `unrecognized file type: ini` (self-reported, `TASK-1265` control, 2026-09-17) — ⛔ that is why its `Config/` enumeration failed. ⇒ ⛔ any fence, census or probe built on an ini-targeted grep behaves ⛔ unexpectedly, and ⛔ **an enumeration that silently failed is ⛔ not an absence** (`SC-§39`'s control, in a second shape).
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (`SC-§116` cl. 3).** `SC-§117` (⛔ a proxy that correlates is still not the claim) · `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a claim until measured) · `SC-§70` (⛔ an absence quoted without its cause) · `SC-§39` (⛔ the positive control). ⛔ **Distinct from all four:** there the instrument ⛔ pointed at the subject and mis-described it. ⛔ **Here the instrument was ⛔ never pointed at the subject at all**, and ⛔ nothing in four rows of argument could have revealed that, ⛔ because every one of them asked the ⛔ instrument about ⛔ itself.
8. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — ⛔ A PRESENCE PIN CANNOT DETECT A FALSE STATEMENT THAT ⛔ CONTAINS THE PINNED TOKEN. ⛔ MINTED 2026-09-17, ⛔ ON THIS SECTION'S OWN FIRST DRAFT, ⛔ HOURS AFTER CLAUSE 2 WAS WRITTEN.** ⛔ **THE EVENT, stated without softening because the author is the manager and the file is the law:** while writing this very section, the manager asserted that `Saved/.Aura/project_memory.txt` was *"generated by `Tools/aura_sync.ps1` from our own two `Docs/` canonicals"* — and, at `ACC-§11` and in `TASK-1292`'s PRESCRIBED VERBATIM doc text, *"from this document and `CONVENTIONS.md`"*. ⛔ **MEASURED FALSE:** the script's only writes are `New-Item` (`:116`) and `Copy-Item` (`:147`); `project_memory.txt` is a ⛔ byte-for-byte copy of ⛔ ONE git-tracked, hand-authored file, `Docs/AuraProjectMemory.md` (both 61 lines, sha256 `4bb799687cbc5c8c…f514297`); ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` and `Docs/setupdirections.md` are ⛔ NOT sources. ⛔ **THE TRUTH WAS ALREADY WRITTEN CORRECTLY IN FOUR PLACES** — `CONVENTIONS.md:43–45` · `Docs/setupdirections.md:864–869` (⛔ **THIRTEEN LINES BELOW the false sentence, in the SAME SECTION**) · `Docs/AuraProjectMemory.md:3` · the script's own `.SYNOPSIS`. ⛔ Nothing had to be discovered; ⛔ something had to be READ.
   - ⛔⛔ **WHY NO GATE CAUGHT IT — the clause's actual content:** `TASK-1292`'s acceptance pinned `grep -c 'project_memory.txt'` = 1 per file. ⛔ **The false sentence CONTAINS `project_memory.txt`.** ⇒ ⛔ all twelve pins PASSED on text whose central claim was untrue. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A PIN THAT ASKS "IS THE TOKEN PRESENT?" CANNOT ASK "IS THE SENTENCE AROUND IT TRUE?" — and the more distinctive the token, the more ⛔ CONFIDENT the false pass looks.***
   - ✅ **THE RULE: when prescribing VERBATIM text that makes a factual claim, pin the ⛔ CLAIM, not the ⛔ NOUN.** Pin the ⛔ verb (`copied verbatim by` = 1 **and** `generated by` = **0** — ⛔ a negative pin on the wrong word is what actually catches it), pin the ⛔ named source, and add ⛔ ONE mechanical re-measure the executor performs ⛔ itself (here: read the script's writes and hash the pair). ⛔ A pin the author derived from their own prose is ⛔ not an instrument — ⛔ it is the same sentence asked twice.
   - ⚠️ **AND IT COMPOUNDS THROUGH PRESCRIPTION.** Ordinary drift is one author's sentence. ⛔ **PRESCRIBED-VERBATIM text is a falsehood with a delivery mechanism**: it was copied, unexamined and by design, into ⛔ two shipped documents, ⛔ a vault twin, ⛔ two law sections and ⛔ two board rows before anyone read the script. ⇒ ⛔ **prescribed text carries a HIGHER evidentiary duty than ordinary prose, not a lower one** — the whole point of prescribing it is that ⛔ nobody downstream will check it.
   - ⭐⭐⭐ **THE MIRROR — ⛔ MEASURED THE SAME DAY, ⛔ HOURS LATER, ON `TASK-1293`'s HOST, AND IT BELONGS ⛔ HERE RATHER THAN IN A SECTION OF ITS OWN.** This clause says a ⛔ **PRESENCE** pin ⛔ cannot detect a ⛔ false statement that ⛔ contains the pinned token. ⛔ **The mirror is that an ⛔ ABSENCE pin ⛔ CANNOT RETURN ZERO WHEN THE CORPUS ⛔ LEGITIMATELY CONTAINS THE TOKEN** — the house secret pin, a ⛔ bare 3-character JWT prefix, read **8** on `CONVENTIONS.md` and **27** on `TASKBOARD.md`, ⛔ zero of them credentials: **12** were the prefix landing ⛔ by chance inside `WasInputKeyJustPressed`, and **25** were ⛔ the pin's own name quoted in ⛔ the law and board prose that ⛔ prescribes it. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE GENERALISATION THAT COVERS BOTH: ⛔ A PIN MEASURES THE ⛔ TOKEN, ⛔ NOT THE CLAIM.*** ⛔ Presence-shape: the token is there and the ⛔ SENTENCE is false. ⛔ Absence-shape: the token is there and ⛔ NO SECRET is. ⇒ ✅ **pin the CLAIM: for a presence pin, the ⛔ VERB and the ⛔ NAMED SOURCE (this clause); for an absence pin, the ⛔ VALUE SHAPE plus the ⛔ HEAD→STAGED DELTA.** Full measurement, the firing positive control and the three-part remedy live at ⭐ **`SC-§39`'s amendment of 2026-09-17** — ⛔ read it before writing any commit-time secret census.
   - 📜 **THE RECORD, kept because it is the argument for clause 2 and not against it:** clause 2 says measure what an instrument COVERS. ⛔ The manager measured Aura's index correctly and ⛔ then invented, in the same paragraph, the provenance of the file that ⛔ defines that coverage. ⛔ Caught by a recovery agent on its way out of the session, ⛔ not by any gate. ⭐ The corrected fact is ⛔ STRONGER than the invented one (a small hand-authored tracked file ⛔ bounds the index by construction) — ⚖️ ***⛔ which is the sharpest lesson available: the measurement you skip is not always one that would have cost you. ⛔ This one would have PAID.***

### SC-§118 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE `-game` SESSION LAW — A `-game` SESSION IS THE ⛔ USER'S, ⛔ NEVER THE PIPELINE'S. `UnrealEditor.exe` IS ⛔ ONE BINARY IN ⛔ THREE ROLES, IDENTIFIED BY ⛔ COMMAND LINE, ⛔ NEVER BY NAME OR WINDOW TITLE; THE STANDING CLOSE GRANT COVERS THE ⛔ EDITOR ONLY; WHEN A PLAY SESSION HOLDS THE MUTEX THE REMEDY IS ⛔ ASK AND WAIT, ⛔ NEVER KILL.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1193` invocation 2 under the ⭐ `TASK-1197` host — the build-master force-stopped ⛔ TWO of 🧑 Jonathan's play sessions believing each to be the reopened editor, then identified the third correctly and left it alone** · marker `SC-118-A-GAME-SESSION-IS-THE-USERS`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **A `-game` INSTANCE OF `UnrealEditor.exe` HAS THE ⛔ SAME PROCESS NAME AND THE ⛔ SAME WINDOW TITLE (`Siegebound (64-bit Development PCD3D_SM6)`) AS THE GUI EDITOR. ⛔ NOTHING THAT LOOKS AT EITHER CAN TELL HIS GAME FROM OUR EDITOR. THE COMMAND LINE CAN, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONLY THING THAT CAN.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — IDENTITY.** Enumerate with `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name='UnrealEditor.exe'"` and read `CommandLine`, `ProcessId`, `CreationDate`, `ParentProcessId`. Classify each instance: a `-game` token ⇒ **`game`** (a play session) · otherwise ⇒ **`editor`** (the GUI editor, PIE included) · `UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe` is a ⛔ different process name (the suite/commandlet lane) and is classified by its own name. ⛔ `Get-Process -Name UnrealEditor` returns a ⛔ COUNT and nothing else — it is a census, never an identity. ⛔ A window title is never an identity (measured identical across roles). ⭐ **Corroborate ownership by parent:** an explorer-parented, argument-less `powershell.exe` started hours earlier is a person's console; a `-game` child of it is 🧑 his.
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — THE GRANT'S SCOPE, STATED SO IT CANNOT BE STRETCHED.** The standing editor close/reopen grant (🧑 2026-08-30; "Editor-close discipline at a build gate" + its rider) covers the GUI **`editor`** instance, for a compile/import gate, under the never-save law — ⛔ and nothing else. It does ⛔ NOT cover a **`game`** instance, whatever launched it; it does not cover a session he has said he is mid-edit in. ⛔ **A `-game` process is his play session and is never ours to close.**
3. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE REMEDY WHEN A PLAY SESSION BLOCKS A GATE (the Live Coding mutex, ⭐ `SHIP-§10`, or anything else): STOP, NAME IT, WAIT.** Name it — class, PID, start time, parent, command line — in the handoff and in 🚨 Blockers, and wait for 🧑 his word. ⛔ Never `Stop-Process`. ⛔ Never `Ctrl+Alt+F11` (that is HIS session's Live Coding console, not ours). ⛔ Never *"schedule around it"* by racing his relaunch — on 2026-09-09 B1 passed by ⛔ 19 seconds of timing, and ⚖️ **timing is not a remedy.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — KILL BY PID AFTER CLASSIFICATION, OR NOT AT ALL.** `Stop-Process -Name UnrealEditor` and `taskkill /IM UnrealEditor.exe` are ⛔ BANNED: a kill by name kills every role at once. When the grant applies (an `editor` instance and a gate that needs it down), the kill is ⛔ by PID, ⛔ after the classification is printed, and the classification sits in the handoff beside the `L_Arena` hash.
5. 📌 **CLAUSE 5 — THE INSTANCE RECORD (⭐ `TASK-1193` inv. 2, 2026-09-09 PDT).** 13:56:32 PID 29712 appeared (title = the editor's; gone on its own by ~14:02) · 14:01:40 PID 7256 — ⛔ force-stopped ~14:02 under the grant, believed to be the reopened editor · 14:03:10 PID 8760 — ⛔ force-stopped 14:05:07 by the launch pre-check · 14:05:49 PID 12500 `-game -windowed -ResX=3200 -ResY=1800`, parent PID 2000 = argument-less `powershell.exe` started 08:38:14 under `explorer.exe` — ✅ identified by command line and LEFT RUNNING; it held the mutex at ~14:07:00 and the cook STOPped at `C2-UAT-LOG`. Apology posted in 🚨. `L_Arena.umap` sha256 `1f78419d…8f15622` unchanged throughout; `PackageRestoreData.json` = `RestoreEnabled:false, Packages:[]`; `Content/` porcelain empty. ⚖️ **Why it happened, without softening it:** the dispatch brief called the process *"the reopened editor"*, the window title agreed, and the kill was by name — ⛔ none of the three instruments in use could have said otherwise, which is exactly why cl. 1 exists. Record: `qa/TASK-1193-cook.md` INVOCATION 2 §3–§5 · `handoffs/TASK-1193-buildmaster.md` PART 2 §5.
6. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 6 — THE NEXT `/ship` INVOCATION IS GATED ON 🧑 JONATHAN'S WORD.** ⭐ `TASK-1193`'s re-run (invocation 1 verbatim: Phases A–C, STOP at C3) does ⛔ not start until he says no game and no editor is running — ⛔ his sentence in Claude Code, ⛔ not a channel read, ⛔ not a process census. **On that run the host is ⛔ FORBIDDEN from closing anything, of any class:** a `UnrealEditor.exe` found alive is a STOP and a question, never a kill. The standing grant is suspended for that one run and resumes after it. The `ship.ps1` row in `SHIP-§10` cl. 4 is ⛔ not a precondition of the re-run — the host performs the check by hand (`SHIP-§10` cl. 3).
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP, CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 3).** ⭐ `SHIP-§9a` (gates written against a name that only coincidentally matches) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9b` (probes that answer correctly in the normal case and lie in the case that matters) · `C3-BOOT-TITLE`'s *"resolved by PATH, never by NAME"*. This is that family's fourth member — **identify by the INVARIANT (command line, path), never by the COINCIDENT (name, title)** — and it is distinct because the cost was not a false gate verdict but 🧑 the user's own session, twice.
8. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — ⛔ THE FIELD REFILLS ITSELF. ⛔ CLOSING THE GUI EDITOR DOES ⛔ NOT LEAVE THE FIELD EMPTY: THE MCP BRIDGE ⛔ AUTO-LAUNCHES A ⛔ HEADLESS EDITOR THE MOMENT THE GUI EDITOR GOES DOWN. ⛔ A CENSUS IS A MEASUREMENT AT AN ⛔ INSTANT; A QUIET FIELD IS A CONDITION ACROSS A ⛔ WINDOW.** (added 2026-09-17 by the manager, ⛔ measured by ⭐ `TASK-1291`'s 5a compile host · marker `SC-118-THE-FIELD-REFILLS-ITSELF`)
   - ⛔⛔ **MEASURED, ⛔ NOT INFERRED:** graceful remote-exec quit of GUI editor ⛔ PID 8972 → ⛔ ~3.5 minutes later, ⛔ MID-SUITE, an `UnrealEditor.exe … -RenderOffScreen -AuraHeadless -unattended …` appeared ⛔ UNPROMPTED as ⛔ PID 4460 at ⛔ 21:21:46. ⛔ **Nobody in the pipeline launched it.** ⛔ It has ⛔ no window, so ⛔ every instrument that looks at a window sees ⛔ an empty field.
   - ✅⛔⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ RE-CENSUS IMMEDIATELY BEFORE ⛔ EVERY STEP THAT ⛔ ASSUMES A QUIET FIELD — ⛔ before the compile, ⛔ before the relaunch, ⛔ before a cook/package — ⛔ NEVER ONCE AT PRE-FLIGHT.** ⛔ `SHIP-§10`'s *"no `UnrealEditor.exe` of any kind alive"* is a ⛔ point-in-time gate over a ⛔ long window and ⛔ inherits this clause in full.
   - ⛔⛔ **THE COST IF YOU DO NOT, AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE REASON THIS IS A CLAUSE: ⛔ A HOST THAT RELAUNCHES WITHOUT A FRESH CENSUS ENDS WITH ⛔ TWO EDITORS ON ⛔ ONE PROJECT** — ⛔ competing for the Live Coding mutex, the MCP port, the DDC and `Saved/`, ⛔ with the second one ⛔ invisible.
   - ⛔ **CLASSIFICATION ADDENDUM TO CL. 1 — ⛔ A THIRD ROLE:** `-RenderOffScreen` / `-AuraHeadless` / `-unattended` with ⛔ NO `-game` ⇒ class **`headless`**. ⛔ It is ⛔ not the GUI editor and ⛔ not 🧑 his session; it is the ⛔ pipeline's own, so the standing close grant ⛔ does reach it — ⛔ but it is ⛔ closed on its ⛔ OWN evidence, ⛔ never counted as the editor's PID and ⛔ never used to conclude "the editor is still up".
   - ✅ **THE HANDLING, ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE IT WAS DONE ⛔ RIGHT (this clause has a ⛔ success exhibit, ⛔ not an incident — write-discipline rule 4's point):** classify by ⛔ command line first (⛔ no `-game` ⇒ ⛔ not his, cl. 1) → ⛔ corroborate with the bridge's own `get_headless_status` (`headless_running, pid 4460`) ⛔ BEFORE touching it → ⛔ then force-close. ⚠️ `shutdown_headless` ⛔ REFUSED with `dirty_check_failed` because that instance's bridge on `:41250` ⛔ never came up ⇒ ⛔ **the graceful lane is ⛔ not always available for this class**; the force was covered by the ⛔ never-save law ⛔ only because the instance had ⛔ authored nothing. ⛔ **A `headless` instance that ⛔ HAS authored something is a ⛔ STOP, ⛔ not a force.**
   - 📌 **AND THE ⛔ HONEST LIMIT, so this is ⛔ not quoted as more than it is: ⛔ NO CORRUPTION WAS MEASURED.** Post-compile suite run 2 ran ⛔ alongside PID 4460 and was ⛔ 561/561 green. ⇒ ⛔ **this clause is about ⛔ CENSUS DISCIPLINE, ⛔ not about a measured harm** — ⛔ do ⛔ not cite it as *"the headless editor breaks the suite."*
9. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 9 — ⛔ THE DUTY RESTATED FOR ⛔ SHELL-LESS AGENTS. ⛔ CL. 1's INSTRUMENT IS A ⛔ POWERSHELL CMDLET AND THE `playtest-verifier` HOLDS ⛔ NO SHELL — SO CL. 1 AS WRITTEN WAS AN ⛔ UNWRITTEN EXPECTATION THE LAW ⛔ DEMANDED AND THE GRANT ⛔ FORBADE. ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ CLOSED HERE.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **the verifier hit it on ⭐ `TASK-1314`'s LIMB A, ⛔ named its substitute as weaker ⛔ rather than dressing it as a census, and ⛔ escalated the conflict instead of resolving it (`SC-§82`)** · marker `SC-118-9-THE-CENSUS-DUTY-FOR-A-SHELL-LESS-AGENT`)
   - ⛔ **THE CONFLICT, MEASURED:** the verifier's read-only Python lane rejects ⛔ `subprocess` ⛔ **and** `ctypes`/`windll` ⇒ ⛔ `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process` is ⛔ unreachable. ⛔ **No amount of care closes a gap that is a ⛔ GRANT, not a skill** (`VER-§8` cl. 8).
   - ⚖️⛔ **THE RULING — ⛔ NO NEW TOOL GRANT. ⛔ THE DUTY IS ⛔ RESTATED, ⛔ NOT WEAKENED.** ⛔ A process-list tool is a ⛔ new surface on the ⛔ one agent whose whole safety case is that it ⛔ cannot mutate anything, ⛔ to serve a duty whose ⛔ actual purpose — 🧑 *"is one of these his?"* — is ⛔ already answerable from ⛔ inside the editor it is driving. ⇒ ⛔ **the grant stays as it is** (`VER-§7` cl. 4: ⛔ what the verifier is ⛔ NOT, ⛔ enumerated).
   - ✅⛔ **THE ⛔ SUFFICIENT SUBSTITUTE, ⛔ ENUMERATED SO IT IS ⛔ PERFORMED THE SAME WAY EVERY TIME AND ⛔ NEVER IMPROVISED. ⛔ ALL FOUR, ⛔ OR IT IS NOT DISCHARGED:** ⛔ **(i)** the driven process's ⛔ OWN `os.getpid()` read from ⛔ inside it, ⛔ matched against the dispatch's PID · ⛔ **(ii)** the ⛔ LIVE-LOG-WRITER census: ⛔ count ⛔ every file in `Saved/Logs`, name ⛔ how many are being written ⛔ now and the ⛔ staleness of the next-freshest (⛔ *"1 of 73 at age 0.0 s, next-freshest 387 s stale"* ⇒ ⛔ exactly one live UE instance) · ⛔ **(iii)** that instance's ⛔ OWN `LogInit: Command Line:` line, ⛔ quoted — ⛔ **an EMPTY value is a ⛔ POSITIVE finding: ⛔ no `-game`, ⛔ no `-RenderOffScreen`, ⛔ no switches at all ⇒ ⛔ a GUI launch, ⛔ class `editor`** · ⛔ **(iv)** `is_pie_active`'s `num_instances`, ⛔ as corroboration ⛔ only.
   - 🚨⛔ **AND IT IS ⛔ LABELLED WHAT IT IS, ⛔ EVERY TIME: ⛔ *"`SC-§118` census — ⛔ PARTIAL, by cl. 9 (shell-less lane); ⛔ substitute performed in full."*** ⛔ **A verifier that performs all four and ⛔ says so has ⛔ DISCHARGED the duty. ⛔ No gate, no host and no manager may fail it for the missing `Win32_Process` read** — ⛔ and ⛔ equally, ⛔ **a report that presents the substitute as a ⛔ full census is ⛔ defective** (`SC-§39`: ⛔ *"absent"* and *"empty"* are different measurements; ⛔ so are *"censused"* and *"inferred"*).
   - ⛔ **WHERE THE FULL DUTY ⛔ STILL LIVES, ⛔ UNRELAXED: ⛔ THE BUILD-MASTER.** ⛔ Cl. 1/4/8 are ⛔ untouched for ⛔ every agent that holds `Bash` — ⛔ and ⛔ every step that ⛔ KILLS, ⛔ LAUNCHES or ⛔ ASSUMES A QUIET FIELD is ⛔ that agent's, ⛔ never the verifier's (`VER-§7` cl. 4: ⛔ process lifecycle is ⛔ not the verifier's lane). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE CENSUS EXISTS TO PROTECT 🧑 HIS SESSION FROM A ⛔ KILL. ⛔ AN AGENT THAT CANNOT KILL ANYTHING OWES THE ⛔ IDENTIFICATION, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ ENUMERATION.***
   - ⛔ **BOARDING COROLLARY (`VER-§8` cl. 2's shape): ⛔ a row that genuinely needs a ⛔ full command-line census ⛔ puts that step on the ⛔ build-master, ⛔ at boarding. ⛔ Writing it into a verifier's spec is a ⛔ MANAGER defect, ⛔ not a verifier shortfall.**
10. ✅⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 10 — THE INSTRUMENT IS NOW A TRACKED SCRIPT, AND THE SCRIPT IS THE ONLY SANCTIONED PATH FOR A CLOSE OR A RELAUNCH.** (added 2026-10-04 by the manager on Jonathan's rule-change instruction, orchestrator-implemented · marker `SC-118-10-THE-SCRIPT-IS-THE-PATH`)
   - **The census of cl. 1 is `powershell -NoProfile -File Tools\stop_editor.ps1 -WhatIf`:** every `UnrealEditor.exe` classified by COMMAND LINE into PLAY (`-game`) / HEADLESS (`-run=` · `-AuraHeadless` · `-RenderOffScreen` · `-unattended`, cl. 8's third role) / EDITOR (names THIS project's `.uproject`, none of the above) / OTHER (another project), with PID, start time and parent — printed before and after any action. `-WhatIf` acts on nothing and is the re-census instrument cl. 8 demands before the compile, the relaunch and the cook.
   - **The kill of cl. 4 is the same script without `-WhatIf`:** it terminates THIS project's single EDITOR instance by PID (or exactly `-TargetPid N`, after classifying it; several EDITOR instances without `-TargetPid` is a refusal, exit 4), and the never-save proof rides inside it — `Content/Maps/L_Arena.umap` sha256 / size / mtime read before and after, a change is exit 6. A `-game` instance exercises the script's REFUSAL PATH: it is reported and never touched; `-TargetPid` on it is exit 4; with `-RequireAllDown` (the cook pre-flight, `SHIP-§10`) a surviving PLAY instance is exit 3 and the remedy is cl. 3's — name it in the handoff and 🚨 Blockers, wait for 🧑 his word. HEADLESS instances are left alone unless `-IncludeHeadless`, and a HEADLESS instance that has authored something is still a STOP, not a force (cl. 8).
   - **The relaunch is `powershell -NoProfile -File Tools\launch_editor.ps1`:** it censuses first and refuses a second GUI editor of this project (exit 2), starts the editor detached on the `.uproject`, and returns only when the MCP server ANSWERS an HTTP request on :8000 (`SHIP-§9e`: a listening port is not a live server), printing `LAUNCH_EDITOR: PID=… SECONDS=…`. The PID it prints is the one every downstream row identifies by command line at its own instant. `LAUNCH_EDITOR` fires when MCP ANSWERS, not when the editor is idle — the plugin's HTTP server comes up early in boot (17 s on `TASK-1602`'s run, against the 45–140 s boots measured before), so a downstream row that needs the editor itself ready (level loaded, asset registry scanned) reads `LAUNCH_EDITOR` as "MCP answers" and establishes readiness by its own instrument.
   - **What is no longer legal, stated so the allow-list and the law agree:** raw `Stop-Process` in ANY form is not allow-listed (`.claude/settings.local.json` carries the two scripts and `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process` instead); `Stop-Process -Name UnrealEditor` and `taskkill /IM UnrealEditor.exe` stay banned by cl. 4; a hand-typed `Stop-Process -Id` is not the sanctioned path either, because it carries none of the proof. The evidence a handoff quotes is the script's exit code and its before/after census output verbatim — a re-typed cmdlet is not evidence of a census.
   - **Ownership is unchanged by this clause.** The build-master owns the full census and every kill or launch (cl. 9's last bullet; the orchestrator may run the two scripts under the standing grant, cl. 2). The verifier's four-part PARTIAL census (cl. 9) is unchanged and is not replaced: it holds no shell, cannot run the script, and labels its substitute as partial exactly as before. The grant's scope (cl. 2) is unchanged: the GUI editor only, for a compile / import gate, under the never-save law the script now proves by hash.
   _Changelog: 2026-10-04 — clause added; cl. 1 / 3 / 4 / 8 are discharged by `Tools/stop_editor.ps1` and `Tools/launch_editor.ps1` rather than by hand-run cmdlets, and raw `Stop-Process` left the allow-list, Jonathan's rule-change instruction, orchestrator-implemented._
   _Changelog: 2026-10-04 (later the same day) — the relaunch bullet gained the sentence that `LAUNCH_EDITOR` means "MCP answers", not "editor idle": the build-master measured a 17 s answer against 45–140 s boots on the first live run (`handoffs/TASK-1602-buildmaster.md` §9 / §11 item 2), so downstream rows establish editor readiness by their own instrument, manager, `TASK-1602` finding (b)._

### SC-§117 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CORRELATED-PROXY LAW — ⛔ A PROXY THAT ⛔ CORRELATES WITH THE TRUTH IS ⛔ STILL NOT THE CLAIM, AND ⛔ THE BETTER IT CORRELATES THE ⛔ LONGER IT SURVIVES. ⛔ `r = −0.921` IS ⛔ NOT A REASON TO TRUST IT — ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ REASON NOBODY CAUGHT IT.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1177` — ⛔ the deepest thing measured that night, and the ⛔ art-director measured it ⛔ against its own row's convenience** · marker `SC-117-A-PROXY-THAT-CORRELATES-IS-NOT-THE-CLAIM`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ ASK (B) IS A SENTENCE ABOUT ⛔ HOW FAR THE PLAYER CAN SEE. ⛔ EVERY NUMBER THAT CLOSED IT WAS A SENTENCE ABOUT ⛔ WHAT COLOUR THE FAR FIELD IS.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE NUMBER IS THE WHOLE LAW: ⭐ `TASK-1177` computed the correlation between the ⛔ PROXY (`B/G`, a ⛔ dimensionless colour ratio) and the ⛔ CLAIM (⛔ Koschmieder ⛔ VISIBILITY DISTANCE ⛔ in `uu`) over its ⛔ 101-frame series ⇒ ⛔ **`r = −0.921`.**
   - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE PROXY AGREED WITH THE TRUTH ON ⛔ ESSENTIALLY EVERY FRAME ⛔ ANYONE EVER LOOKED AT.** ⛔ **⛔ IT WAS ⛔ NEVER WRONG ABOUT THE ⛔ DIRECTION. ⛔ IT WAS ⛔ ONLY EVER WRONG ABOUT ⛔ WHAT IT ⛔ ENTITLED US TO SAY.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ AND THAT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHY IT COST ⛔ FOUR ROWS: ⛔ every spot-check of the proxy ⛔ against reality ⛔ PASSED. ⛔ There was ⛔ no frame on which a careful reader could have ⛔ noticed.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ INVERSION, ⛔ AND IT IS THE SENTENCE TO REMEMBER: ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ HIGH CORRELATION IS ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE THAT A PROXY IS ⛔ SAFE. ⛔ IT IS EVIDENCE THAT THE SUBSTITUTION WILL ⛔ NOT BE DETECTED.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ A ⛔ BADLY-CORRELATED PROXY IS ⛔ HARMLESS — ⛔ IT ⛔ DISAGREES WITH REALITY AND SOMEBODY ⛔ NOTICES WITHIN A DAY. ⛔ A ⛔ WELL-CORRELATED PROXY IS THE ⛔ DANGEROUS ONE, BECAUSE THE ⛔ ONLY THING IT GETS WRONG IS THE ⛔ QUESTION.***
   - ⛔ **⛔ THE FAILURE IS ⛔ NEVER IN THE ⛔ DATA. ⛔ IT IS IN THE ⛔ VERDICT SENTENCE: ⛔ *"the far field is uniform"* was ⛔ TRUE and was ⛔ read as ⛔ *"the player can see far enough"*, which is a ⛔ DIFFERENT CLAIM ⛔ NOBODY HAD MEASURED.**
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTY, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ TWO SECONDS: ⛔ **⛔ THE UNITS TEST.** ⛔ WRITE THE ASK AS A ⛔ SENTENCE, THEN ⛔ READ THE ⛔ UNITS OF THE NUMBER THAT WILL CLOSE IT. ⛔ IF THE ⛔ SENTENCE SAYS *"HOW FAR"*, THE NUMBER IS IN ⛔ `uu` — ⛔ NOT A ⛔ RATIO, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ σ, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ CHANNEL.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ WORKED, ⛔ FROM THE ROW THAT BOUGHT IT: ask (B) = *"⛔ how far can the player ⛔ SEE"* ⇒ ⛔ units `uu`. ⛔ `B/G` is ⛔ dimensionless ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IT CANNOT ANSWER, ⛔ AT ⛔ ANY VALUE, ⛔ WITH ⛔ ANY CORRELATION.** ⛔ That check was ⛔ available on day one and costs ⛔ nothing.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WHY ⭐ `TASK-1177` cl. (2) SAID ⛔ *"THE METRIC MUST BE A ⛔ DISTANCE"* ⛔ IN THE SPEC ⛔ RATHER THAN LEAVING IT TO THE ROW: ⛔ **⛔ THE UNITS BELONG IN THE ⛔ ASK, ⛔ NOT IN THE ⛔ ANSWER.** ⛔ A row handed a ⛔ question without units will ⛔ reach for whatever it ⛔ already has an instrument for.**
4. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ WHAT A PROXY IS ⛔ STILL GOOD FOR, ⛔ BECAUSE THIS LAW MUST ⛔ NOT BAN THEM: ⛔ a correlated proxy is a ⛔ FINE ⛔ SCREEN, a ⛔ FINE ⛔ TRIPWIRE and a ⛔ FINE ⛔ CORROBORATOR** (⭐ `SC-§113` cl. 6's second source). ⛔ **⛔ IT IS ⛔ NEVER THE ⛔ CLOSING NUMBER, AND IT ⛔ NEVER APPEARS IN A ⛔ VERDICT SENTENCE ⛔ WITHOUT THE WORD ⛔ *"PROXY"* ⛔ NEXT TO IT.** ✅ **⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT: ⭐ `TASK-1177` ⛔ REPORTED the `r` ⛔ RATHER THAN SUPPRESSING IT — ⛔ the correlation is what makes the ⛔ old rows' readings ⛔ INTELLIGIBLE instead of ⛔ incompetent.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ KINSHIP, ⛔ CHECKED BEFORE MINTING (⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 3's discipline): ⭐ `SC-§94` is an instrument that ⛔ ECHOES THE REQUEST (⛔ it never measured ⛔ anything) · ⭐ `SC-§114` governs what a ⛔ NULL may close (⛔ the instrument said ⛔ nothing) · ⭐ `SC-§104` says assert ⛔ STATE, ⛔ not a tally (⛔ the wrong ⛔ KIND of quantity).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THIS ONE IS ⛔ DISTINCT AND IS THE ⛔ HARDEST OF THE FAMILY: ⛔ the instrument ⛔ WORKED, the number was ⛔ REAL, the reading was ⛔ REPRODUCIBLE — ⛔ and it was an ⛔ ANSWER TO A ⛔ NEIGHBOURING QUESTION.** ⛔ **⛔ NONE OF THE THREE WOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT; ⛔ ONLY THE ⛔ UNITS TEST DOES.**

### SC-§116 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ SILENT-FAILURE INVOCATION RECIPE — ⛔ A ⛔ DOCUMENTED COMMAND LINE THAT IS ⛔ SYNTACTICALLY WRONG IN A WAY THE TOOL ⛔ ACCEPTS WITHOUT COMPLAINT. ⛔ IT RUNS ⛔ NOTHING, PRINTS ⛔ NO ERROR, AND ⛔ NEVER RETURNS — AND IT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN A ⛔ BROKEN ONE, BECAUSE A ⛔ BROKEN RECIPE ⛔ TELLS YOU.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1175`'s code case (⭐ W9) — ⛔ the build-master ran the ⛔ documented lane, got ⛔ nothing, and ⛔ went to the engine source instead of ⛔ concluding the feature did not work** · marker `SC-116-SILENT-INVOCATION`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ CODE WAS ⛔ CORRECT. ⛔ THE ⛔ PROSE WAS ⛔ WRONG. ⛔ AND THE ⛔ PROSE IS THE PART ⛔ EVERY FUTURE ROW ⛔ COPIES.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE ⛔ MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND IT ⛔ CROSSED THREE DOCUMENTS ⛔ BEFORE ANYONE RAN IT.** ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1174-report.md` §0, `handoffs/TASK-1173-programmer.md` §1(b)/§4 and the board all carried:**
   ```
   -ExecCmds="Siege.Fog.Raise;Siege.Fog.Status;Quit"
   ```
   ⛔ **⛔ MEASURED: the engine took the ⛔ WHOLE STRING as ⛔ ONE COMMAND NAME — `Cmd: Siege.Fog.Raise;Siege.Fog.Status;Quit` — and then ⛔ NOTHING. ⛔ No fog line. ⛔ **⛔ NO `Command not recognized`.** ⛔ No warning. ⛔ No exit; the process was ⛔ killed at ~10 minutes.**
   - ⛔ **ROOT CAUSE, ⛔ FROM ENGINE SOURCE, ⛔ NOT FROM A GUESS: `-ExecCmds` splits on ⛔ **COMMA**, ⛔ never on `;` — `Engine/Source/Runtime/Engine/Private/ParseExecCommands.cpp:29`, `else if (CurrentChar == ',' && !bInQuotes)`.**
   - ⛔ **SECOND DEFECT, ⛔ INDEPENDENT OF THE FIRST: ⛔ `Quit` does ⛔ NOT quit an ⛔ EDITOR commandlet. `QUIT`/`EXIT` live in `UGameEngine::Exec` (`GameEngine.cpp:1527`); a commandlet runs `UUnrealEdEngine`, which handles ⛔ **`QUIT_EDITOR`** (`EditorServer.cpp:5993`). ⛔ With commas + `Quit`, ⛔ all three commands ran ⛔ correctly and the process ⛔ STILL HUNG.**
2. ✅🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ PINNED RECIPE. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ WHOLE REASON THE LAW HAS A NUMBER; ⛔ COPY FROM ⛔ HERE, ⛔ NEVER FROM A HANDOFF.**

   | lane | invocation | why |
   |---|---|---|
   | **the automation suite** | `-ExecCmds="Automation RunTests Siegebound;Quit"` | ✅ ⛔ **CORRECT AS WRITTEN — ⛔ DO NOT "FIX" IT.** ⛔ This is ⛔ ONE command (`Automation`) whose ⛔ OWN handler splits its ⛔ OWN arguments on `;`. ⛔ The `;` is ⛔ NOT an `-ExecCmds` feature here — ⛔ it never reaches `-ExecCmds`' parser. |
   | **any other command list** | `-ExecCmds="CmdA,CmdB,CmdC,QUIT_EDITOR"` | ✅ ⛔ **COMMAS. ⛔ AND `QUIT_EDITOR`, ⛔ NOT `Quit`.** ⛔ Measured on `Siege.Fog.Raise,Siege.Fog.Clear,Siege.Fog.Status,QUIT_EDITOR`: ⛔ all four dispatched, ⛔ **self-terminated in 12 s, ⛔ exit 0.** |
   | **the in-game / PIE console** | type `Siege.Fog.Raise` and press Enter | ✅ ⛔ **UNAFFECTED.** ⛔ This defect is ⛔ SPECIFIC to the `-ExecCmds` command line; ⛔ a human typing at the console has ⛔ no separator problem. |

   ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ THIRD RULE THAT ALREADY COST THIS PROJECT A RUN (⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 1): ⛔ `-ExecCmds` must be ⛔ **ONE** PowerShell argument. ⛔ PowerShell splitting it on ⛔ SPACES produced `Cmd: Automation` and a ⛔ zero-started, ⛔ zero-failed, ⛔ perfectly green-looking run.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THREE SEPARATE WAYS TO ⛔ SILENTLY RUN NOTHING ⛔ ON ONE FLAG.**
   - 🚨 **⛔ THE ⛔ WARNING THAT MATTERS MORE THAN THE FIX: ⛔ A READER WHO LEARNS *"COMMAS, NOT SEMICOLONS"* WILL GO AND ⛔ "CORRECT" THE ⛔ ~20 BOARD AND HANDOFF SITES CARRYING `Automation RunTests Siegebound;Quit` — ⛔ **AND BREAK THE ⛔ ONE LANE THAT WORKS.** ⛔ THE RULE IS ⛔ NOT *"commas"*; ⛔ IT IS ⛔ *"⛔ ASK ⛔ WHOSE PARSER SEES THE SEPARATOR"*.
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ WHY THIS IS ⛔ ITS OWN LAW AND ⛔ NOT AN INSTANCE OF ⭐ `SC-§105`. ⛔ THE DISTINCTION IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING AND I CHECKED IT ⛔ BEFORE MINTING A NUMBER:** ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§105` is a recipe with a ⛔ MISSING STEP — ⛔ every step present is ⛔ CORRECT, and the ⛔ defect is ⛔ DOWNSTREAM (a value evaporates at the ⛔ next launch). ⛔ **THIS is a recipe whose steps are all ⛔ PRESENT and one of them is ⛔ FALSE, and the defect is ⛔ IMMEDIATE and ⛔ TOTAL: ⛔ nothing runs ⛔ at all.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ `SC-§105` MANUFACTURES A ⛔ SILENT DEFECT IN THE PRODUCT. ⛔ THIS ONE MANUFACTURES A ⛔ SILENT NULL IN THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT — ⛔ AND A NULL FROM AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT THAT NEVER SPOKE IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT ⭐ `SC-§114` FORBIDS ANYONE FROM ⛔ CONCLUDING ON.***
   - 🚨 **⛔ THE ⛔ NEAR-MISS, ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ REAL COST: ⛔ THE ⛔ AVAILABLE WRONG CONCLUSION WAS ⛔ *"THE CONSOLE LANE ⛔ DOES NOT WORK"* — ⛔ i.e. ⛔ `TASK-1173`'s ⛔ DELIVERABLE (b) ⛔ DECLARED ⛔ DEAD ⛔ ON THE STRENGTH OF A ⛔ TYPO IN ITS OWN INSTRUCTIONS.** ⛔ **⛔ THAT WOULD HAVE ⛔ RE-OPENED ⭐ `SC-§113` AND ⛔ SENT A GOOD DIFF BACK THROUGH A QA LOOP.**
4. ✅ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ DUTIES, ⛔ CHEAP ON PURPOSE (⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c — ⛔ an ignored law protects nothing):**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ **AN INVOCATION RECIPE IS ⛔ PUBLISHED ⛔ ONLY BY SOMEONE WHO ⛔ RAN IT**, ⛔ or it is published with the words ⛔ *"⛔ NOT EXECUTED"* ⛔ beside it. ⛔ A command line ⛔ derived by ⛔ substitution from a working one is a ⛔ CLAIM (⭐ `SC-§101`), ⛔ not a recipe.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ **THE ⛔ SELF-TERMINATION IS ⛔ PART OF THE RECIPE**, ⛔ not an afterthought: ⛔ publish the ⛔ MEASURED wall time (⛔ *"exits itself in 12 s"*). ⛔ A recipe that ⛔ hangs is ⛔ indistinguishable from a ⛔ slow one until someone ⛔ decides to kill it — ⛔ which is a ⛔ judgement call made by the ⛔ least-informed reader (⭐ `SC-§87`).**
   - 🚨 **(c) ⛔ **THE ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL FOR AN INVOCATION IS THE ⛔ DISPATCH ECHO, ⛔ NOT THE OUTPUT** — ⛔ and it is a ⛔ **CONTENT MATCH**, ⛔ NEVER A ⛔ LINE COUNT. 🚨 **⛔ CORRECTED 2026-09-09 (⛔ see cl. 6): the ⛔ ORIGINAL WORDING OF THIS CLAUSE — ⛔ *"grep `Cmd: ` and confirm ⛔ ONE LINE PER COMMAND"* — ⛔ IS ⛔ FALSE, ⛔ MEASURED AGAINST THE ⛔ REAL 554-TEST LOG BY ⭐ `TASK-1181`. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ STRUCK, ⛔ NOT DELETED, ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ WRONG LITERAL IS ⛔ ALREADY QUOTED ELSEWHERE.**
     - ⛔ **⛔ WHY THE COUNT IS FALSE, ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS, ⛔ BOTH MEASURED:** ⛔ **(i)** the ⛔ ENGINE EMITS ITS ⛔ OWN `Cmd:` LINES — ⛔ **3 engine lines for 1 of ours** (`MAP LOAD`, `MAP CHECKDEP`, ⛔ then ours) ⇒ ⛔ **a line-counting guard ⛔ FAILS A GOOD RUN.** ⛔ **(ii)** the engine ⛔ **STRIPS `;Quit` BEFORE ECHOING** ⇒ ⛔ **`grep -i quit` over that ⛔ ENTIRE log returns ⛔ ZERO hits** — ⛔ so a per-command echo may ⛔ NEVER be demanded for the ⛔ TERMINATOR (`Quit` / `QUIT_EDITOR`). ⛔ **Its evidence is ⛔ SELF-TERMINATION + ⛔ EXIT CODE (⛔ duty (b)), ⛔ not an echo.**
     - ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ CORRECT DUTY, ⛔ AS ⭐ `TASK-1181`'s RUNNER ⛔ ACTUALLY IMPLEMENTS IT: ⛔ FOR ⛔ EACH ⛔ NON-TERMINATOR COMMAND YOU DISPATCHED, ⛔ ASSERT THE LOG CONTAINS A `Cmd:` LINE WHOSE ⛔ PAYLOAD ⛔ EQUALS THAT COMMAND** (⛔ trimmed, ⛔ case-insensitive). ⛔ **⛔ EXTRA `Cmd:` LINES ARE ⛔ EXPECTED AND ⛔ IRRELEVANT; ⛔ A ⛔ MISSING ONE IS THE ⛔ FAILURE.**
