       - ✅🚨⭐⭐ **⛔ `B-1` ⛔ DISCHARGED 2026-09-09 ⛔ BY ⛔ CODE, ⛔ NOT BY ⛔ PROSE — ⛔ AND THE DISTINCTION IS THE ⛔ RECORD: ⛔ WHEN THIS BULLET WAS WRITTEN, *"⛔ AS THE RUNNER ⛔ ACTUALLY IMPLEMENTS IT"* WAS A ⛔ CLAIM (⭐ `SC-§101`). ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1182` ⛔ VERIFIED IT AND FOUND ⛔ TWO ⛔ INDEPENDENT BARRIERS: ⛔ (i) `TerminatorEcho` ⛔ NEVER ENTERS THE MATCHING LOOP, and ⛔ (ii) `Get-CleanCommandList:248` ⛔ DROPS `quit|exit|quit_editor` ⛔ BEFORE THE LOOP IS REACHED.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE TERMINATOR ⛔ CANNOT BE DEMANDED OF THE ECHO ⛔ EVEN IF ONE BARRIER IS ⛔ LATER REMOVED. ⛔ **⛔ THIS BULLET IS ⛔ NOW TRUE OF ITS REFERENCE IMPLEMENTATION AND IS ⛔ NOT AN OPEN ITEM — ⛔ DO NOT RE-ROUTE IT TO THE MANAGER AS ONE.**
       - ⚠️🚨⛔⛔ **⛔ READER'S RIDER, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-09 (⛔ LATE) — ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ REFERENCE IMPLEMENTATION THIS BULLET CITES IS ⛔ ON DISK AND ⛔ NOT IN THE TREE ⛔ AS OF `ea7b4d2`** (⭐ `TASK-1183` withheld it under ⛔ routing rule 6). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IF YOU CLONED THIS REPO AND `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1` IS ⛔ ABSENT, ⛔ THE CLAUSE IS ⛔ STILL CORRECT AND YOU ARE ⛔ NOT MISSING A FILE YOU SHOULD HAVE. ⛔ IT LANDS WITH ⭐ `TASK-1187`.** ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ DUTY IN THIS BULLET (⛔ content match, ⛔ never a line count) ⛔ BINDS ⛔ REGARDLESS — ⛔ it is ⛔ TRUE OF THE ENGINE'S LOGS, ⛔ not merely of one script.** ⛔ **⛔ FULL FOUR-STATE READING AT ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 3.** ✅🧑 **⛔ SUPERSEDED 2026-09-09 (LATE): ⛔ IN THE TREE at rev-2 since 🧑 `8c444ca` (Jonathan's own commit, ⛔ pushed) — a fresh clone ⛔ HAS the file, ⛔ carrying the `:1567` census defect until ⭐ `TASK-1187` lands rev-3. ⛔ The clause's duty is unchanged; only the tree-membership sentence moved (measured at `TASK-1193` Phase A, `TL-§6` table cell).**
     - ⛔ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ SIGNATURE IS ⛔ UNCHANGED AND ⛔ STILL THE POINT: ⛔ **⛔ A `Cmd:` LINE WHOSE PAYLOAD ⛔ CONTAINS YOUR SEPARATORS** (`Cmd: Siege.Fog.Raise;Siege.Fog.Status;Quit`) ⛔ IS THE ⛔ SIGNATURE OF THIS DEFECT** — ⛔ under the content match it ⛔ ALSO goes red ⛔ automatically, ⛔ because ⛔ NO individual command matches it. ⛔ Visible in ⛔ two seconds, ⛔ before you read a single result.
     - ⛔⛔ **⛔ SCOPE FENCE, ⛔ SAME SPIRIT AS cl. 2's WARNING: ⛔ **⛔ THIS CORRECTION LICENSES ⛔ NO SWEEP.** ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-09: ⛔ **51 `;Quit` OCCURRENCES ACROSS 32 FILES ARE ⛔ CORRECT AND ⛔ UNTOUCHED** — ⛔ they are cl. 2's ⛔ suite lane, ⛔ where `Automation` splits its ⛔ own arguments. ⛔ **⛔ NOBODY MAY READ *"the engine strips `;Quit`"* AS *"⛔ `;Quit` IS WRONG"*. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ RIGHT, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ MERELY ⛔ INVISIBLE IN THE ECHO.**
       - 📌 **⛔ CENSUS ⛔ RECONCILED 2026-09-09 (⛔ ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2's ⛔ PROPAGATION DUTY, ⛔ ANNOTATED ⛔ NOT REWRITTEN): ⛔ the ⛔ `51 / 32` above was the ⛔ COUNT AT ⛔ MINTING TIME. ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ MEASURED FIGURE IS NOW ⛔ `58` OCCURRENCES ACROSS ⛔ `34` FILES** (⭐ `TASK-1182`, ⛔ EXECUTED — ⛔ it reconciles the ⛔ earlier `53 / 33` ⛔ + `3` ⛔ + `2` ⛔ EXACTLY). ⛔ ⚠️ A ⛔ handoff figure of ⛔ `55 / 34` is ⛔ **⛔ NOT MEASURED** — ⛔ it was ⛔ COMPUTED FROM AN ⛔ OLDER BASELINE; ⛔ do not quote it.**
         - 🚨⛔⛔ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ ONLY THING THE NEW NUMBER ⛔ CHANGES IS THE ⛔ NUMBER: ⛔ **⛔ ZERO PROSE SITES HAVE BEEN ⛔ SWEPT AND ⛔ NONE MAY BE.** ⛔ THE SUITE `;` IS ⛔ CORRECT (⛔ cl. 2's ⛔ suite lane). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ⭐ `SC-§116` MUST ⛔ NEVER BE READABLE AS ⛔ LICENSING A SWEEP — ⛔ a ⛔ RISING count is a ⛔ HEALTHY lane ⛔ growing, ⛔ not a ⛔ backlog accruing.**
   - ⛔ **(d) ⛔ **THE FINDER ⛔ ROUTES, ⛔ THE MANAGER ⛔ CORRECTS ⛔ EVERY SITE** (⭐ `SC-§82`, ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2's propagation duty). ✅ **⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT: ⭐ `TASK-1175` did ⛔ NOT edit the programmer's handoff (⭐ `SC-§53` cl. 3) and ⛔ routed it instead — ⛔ that is the ⛔ correct shape.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ KINSHIP, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ FAMILY IS ⛔ ONE FLAG WIDE: ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 1 (⛔ **THE SAME FLAG**, ⛔ split on ⛔ SPACES by ⛔ PowerShell ⇒ ⛔ zero tests, ⛔ `0 failed`) · ⭐ `SC-§87` (⛔ a hang lies by ⛔ OMISSION — ⛔ **this recipe's ⛔ SECOND defect ⛔ IS a hang**) · ⛔ the `Build.bat` exit-code law (⛔ a lie by ⛔ COMMISSION) · ⭐ `SC-§113` (⛔ a path that ⛔ CANNOT run — ⛔ **this is a path that ⛔ CAN run and was ⛔ ASKED WRONG**) · ⭐ `SC-§105` (⛔ cl. 3 above) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate against the ⛔ FAILURE).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ WE HAVE NOW BEEN ⛔ SILENTLY BETRAYED BY ⛔ `-ExecCmds` ⛔ TWICE, IN ⛔ TWO DIFFERENT WAYS, ⛔ THREE DAYS APART. ⛔ THE FLAG IS ⛔ NOT THE PROBLEM — ⛔ THE PROBLEM IS THAT ITS ⛔ CORRECT FORM LIVES IN ⛔ PROSE THAT ⛔ EVERY SESSION ⛔ RE-TYPES (⭐ `TL-§6`).***

6. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL IS ⛔ ITSELF AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT, AND A CONTROL THAT HAS ⛔ NEVER BEEN RUN AGAINST A ⛔ REAL ARTEFACT IS ⛔ `NOT EXECUTED` — ⛔ EXACTLY LIKE THE ⛔ RECIPE IT GUARDS.** ⛔ **cl. 4(a) BINDS THE ⛔ RECIPE. ⛔ NOTHING BOUND THE ⛔ CONTROL — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ FIRST THING THAT RAN cl. 4(c) AGAINST A ⛔ REAL LOG ⛔ FOUND IT FALSE.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **on ⛔ MY OWN LAW, ⛔ minted ⛔ EARLIER THE SAME SESSION; ⛔ bought by ⭐ `TASK-1181`** · marker `SC-116-A-CONTROL-IS-AN-INSTRUMENT`)
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT: cl. 4(c) was minted with a ⛔ *"two-second"* ⛔ confidence and ⛔ ZERO executions. ⭐ `TASK-1181` ran it against the ⛔ real 554-test log: the engine's ⛔ OWN `Cmd:` lines (⛔ 3 for 1 of ours) make the ⛔ count false, and the stripped `;Quit` makes a ⛔ terminator echo ⛔ unobtainable.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE GUARD MINTED TO CATCH A ⛔ SILENT FAILURE WOULD ITSELF HAVE PRODUCED A ⛔ FALSE FAILURE** — ⛔ i.e. `SC-§68` cl. 5's ⛔ EXPENSIVE ERROR, ⛔ manufactured ⛔ by law.
   - ✅ **THE DUTY, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ ONE RUN: ⛔ **⛔ BEFORE A CONTROL IS ⛔ WRITTEN INTO A LAW, ⛔ EXECUTE IT ONCE AGAINST ⛔ ONE REAL ARTEFACT OF THE CLASS IT WILL JUDGE, ⛔ AND SAY SO.** ⛔ If you cannot, ⛔ publish it with ⛔ *"⛔ NOT EXECUTED"* ⛔ beside it (⛔ cl. 4(a)'s ⛔ own words, ⛔ now pointed at the ⛔ guard instead of the ⛔ command line). ⛔ **⛔ A CONTROL DERIVED BY ⛔ REASONING FROM A ⛔ DEFECT YOU JUST DIAGNOSED IS A ⛔ CLAIM (⭐ `SC-§101`), ⛔ NOT A CONTROL.**
   - ⚖️⭐ **⛔ WHY A ⛔ CLAUSE HERE AND ⛔ NOT A NEW SECTION — ⛔ CHECKED ⛔ BEFORE MINTING, ⛔ AS cl. 3 REQUIRES:** ⛔ **⭐ `SHIP-§9`/`§9c` ⛔ presuppose a gate that ⛔ COULD be shown its failure and ⛔ never was ⇒ ⛔ it guards against a ⛔ FALSE PASS. ⛔ THIS PRODUCED A ⛔ FALSE FAIL — ⛔ the ⛔ opposite direction.** ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§112`/`§112a` are the ⛔ ACQUIRER taxonomy (⛔ *is my apparatus ⛔ where I think it is*); ⛔ ⭐ `SC-§114` governs what a ⛔ NULL may close; ⛔ ⭐ `SC-§113` cl. 6 governs a ⛔ positive reading with a ⛔ SECOND SOURCE — ⛔ **⛔ THE NEAREST RELATIVE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ SAME PHYSICS** (⛔ the engine's `Cmd:` lines ⛔ ARE the second source) — ⛔ but `§113` cl. 6 is about that source ⛔ FAKING A PASS, ⛔ whereas here it ⛔ BREAKS A COUNT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ NONE OF THE FOUR COVERS IT, ⛔ AND ⛔ ALL FOUR ARE ⛔ ADJACENT ⇒ ⛔ a ⛔ FIFTH SECTION WOULD BE ⛔ NOISE. ⛔ THE DEFECT IS IN ⛔ THIS SECTION'S ⛔ OWN cl. 4(c); ⛔ IT IS HOMED ⛔ WHERE THE READER OF THAT CLAUSE WILL ⛔ ACTUALLY SEE IT.**
     - 🚨⭐⭐ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ SAME QUESTION CAME BACK THE ⛔ SAME DAY WITH A ⛔ FOURTH INSTANCE — ⭐ `TASK-1182`'s ⛔ W-13, ⛔ a `-notlike '*seen*'` ⛔ SATISFIED BY ⛔ `NOT SEEN`. ⇒ ⛔ CONSOLIDATED AT ⭐ `SC-§114` cl. 8, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ FIFTH SECTION ⛔ THIS BULLET REFUSED, ⛔ WRITTEN AS A ⛔ CLAUSE INSTEAD.** ⛔ **⛔ THIS CLAUSE'S DEFECT IS A ⛔ CONTROL THAT ⛔ NEVER RAN; ⛔ cl. 8's IS A ⛔ BRANCH THAT ⛔ CANNOT GO RED. ⛔ SIBLINGS, ⛔ NOT DUPLICATES.**
   - ⭐⭐ **⛔ TWO CORROBORATING INSTRUMENT DEFECTS FROM ⭐ `TASK-1181`'s ⛔ SELF-TESTING — ⛔ THE RUNNER FOUND ⛔ THREE IN ⛔ ITSELF, AND ⛔ TWO GENERALISE:**
     - 🚨 **(i) ⛔ **⛔ 8/8 FIXTURES PASSED WHILE THE SCRIPT ⛔ COULD NOT READ A REAL LOG AT ALL*** — ⛔ real logs carry ⛔ BLANK LINES, and a `Mandatory [string[]]` ⛔ rejects them. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A ⛔ SYNTHETIC CORPUS TESTS THE ⛔ LOGIC, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ INPUT. ⛔ THE FIXTURES WERE ⛔ TOO CLEAN TO BE ⛔ EVIDENCE OF ANYTHING BUT ⛔ THEMSELVES*** (⛔ same family as this clause: ⛔ **⛔ green against ⛔ what you built, ⛔ untried against ⛔ what exists**).
     - 🚨 **(ii) ⛔ PowerShell's `-f` binds ⛔ TIGHTER than `+` ⇒ ⛔ **5 verdict messages printed a ⛔ LITERAL `{0}`** while ⛔ EVERY EXIT CODE STAYED ⛔ CORRECT.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SELF-TEST THAT ASSERTS ⛔ ONLY THE CODE AND ⛔ NEVER THE MESSAGE IS ⛔ BLIND TO THE HALF OF THE TOOL A ⛔ HUMAN ACTUALLY READS*** — ⛔ and a verdict nobody can read is ⛔ how a red gets ⛔ ignored.
   - ⚖️ **⛔ HONEST NEGATIVE, ⛔ RECORDED SO IT CANNOT BE ⛔ QUOTED AS A WIN: ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1181`'s author's ⛔ OWN ⛔ W9 GUARD WAS PROVEN ⛔ REDUNDANT ⛔ FOR DETECTION — ⛔ mutant ⛔ M2 ⛔ SURVIVED it because the ⛔ generic `k/n` echo check ⛔ ALREADY CATCHES THAT CASE. ⛔ The W9 branch supplies the ⛔ NAMED CAUSE, ⛔ NOT the detection.** ✅ **⛔ THE AUTHOR ⛔ DECLINED TO CLAIM OTHERWISE, ⛔ AND THAT REFUSAL IS THE ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT** (⭐ `SC-§101` — ⛔ a ⛔ prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim ⛔ until measured; ⛔ here it ⛔ was measured and it ⛔ came back ⛔ smaller than hoped).

7. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ WRAPPER IS PART OF THE INVOCATION, AND ⛔ `powershell.exe` ITSELF ⛔ LIES IN ⛔ TWO DIFFERENT WAYS. ⛔ PUT THIS WHERE A ⛔ HOST WILL REACH FOR IT, BECAUSE ⛔ TWO OF ⭐ `TASK-1183`'s EARLY *"MISMATCHES"* WERE ⛔ ITS OWN READER AND ⛔ NOT THE SCRIPT.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **measured by the build-master ⛔ against its own instrument, ⛔ mid-run** · marker `SC-116-THE-WRAPPER-LIES`)
   - 🚨 **⛔ (a) ⛔ `powershell -Command "& script"` ⛔ MASHES ⛔ EVERY NON-ZERO EXIT TO ⛔ `1`. ⛔ MEASURED: a script exiting ⛔ `42` was reported as ⛔ `1`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ EVERY DISTINCT FAILURE CODE A GUARD WAS BUILT TO EMIT ⛔ COLLAPSES INTO ONE VALUE, AND THE ⛔ DIAGNOSIS THE CODE CARRIED IS ⛔ GONE.** ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY CORRECT AGENT INVOCATION: `powershell -Command "& <script> <args> ; exit $LASTEXITCODE"` — ⛔ the trailing `exit` is ⛔ NOT decoration, it is the ⛔ CODE'S ONLY WAY OUT.**
   - 🚨 **⛔ (b) ⛔ `powershell -File <script> -Param a,b,c` ⛔ SILENTLY TRUNCATES AN ⛔ ARRAY PARAMETER TO ITS ⛔ FIRST ELEMENT and ⛔ SLIDES THE REST ONTO POSITIONAL PARAMETERS. ⛔ MEASURED: a 2-element list produced ⛔ `Expected = 1`, ⛔ with ⛔ no error.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ `-File` ⛔ CANNOT CARRY A LIST. ⛔ IT IS FAITHFUL ON ⛔ EXIT CODES AND ⛔ UNFAITHFUL ON ⛔ ARGUMENTS — ⛔ the ⛔ exact opposite of (a), which is why ⛔ neither wrapper is ⛔ safe by default.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE ⛔ TWO WRAPPERS FAIL ON ⛔ OPPOSITE HALVES OF THE SAME CALL, SO A HOST THAT ⛔ SWITCHES WRAPPERS TO ESCAPE ONE DEFECT ⛔ WALKS INTO THE OTHER — ⛔ AND ⛔ BOTH FAILURES PRESENT AS ⛔ *"THE SCRIPT IS WRONG."*** ⛔ **⛔ THAT IS ⛔ `SC-§95`'s ⛔ SILENT NULL, ⛔ ONE LAYER OUTSIDE THE PROGRAM — ⛔ and it means an ⛔ undocumented exit `1` is ⛔ REACHABLE FROM THE WRAPPER, ⛔ not only from the script.**
   - ✅ **⛔ THE DUTY, ⛔ CHEAP: ⛔ **⛔ BEFORE BLAMING A SCRIPT FOR AN EXIT-CODE OR ARGUMENT MISMATCH, ⛔ RUN THE ⛔ WRAPPER CONTROL** — ⛔ a two-line throwaway that ⛔ `exit 42` and one that ⛔ echoes its array parameter's ⛔ `.Count`. ⛔ **⛔ IF THE CONTROL LIES, ⛔ THE SCRIPT WAS NEVER THE SUBJECT.** ⭐ **⛔ THE BUILD-MASTER ⛔ RAN EXACTLY THIS AND ⛔ CAUGHT BOTH — ⛔ recorded as the ⛔ positive exhibit, ⛔ since this section is otherwise a list of instruments that fooled someone.**

### SC-§114 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ NULL-RESULT LAW — ⛔ A MEASUREMENT THAT SOMETHING DOES ⛔ NOTHING IS THE ⛔ WEAKEST RESULT AN EXPERIMENT CAN PRODUCE, ⛔ BECAUSE A ⛔ NULL IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT A ⛔ BROKEN, ⛔ MIS-ANCHORED OR ⛔ WRONGLY-POINTED INSTRUMENT RETURNS. ⛔ A NULL MAY ⛔ NEVER CLOSE A ROUTE WITHOUT A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL ON THE ⛔ SAME INSTRUMENT IN THE ⛔ SAME SESSION — AND ⛔ UNTIL IT HAS ONE IT IS ⛔ `NOT MEASURED`, ⛔ NOT *"REFUTED"*.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on my own file's most expensive defect of the day; ⛔ bought by ⭐ `TASK-1172` §1a/§14, which ⛔ re-ran a flip three rows had already treated as settled** · marker `SC-114-A-NULL-IS-NOT-A-FINDING`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THREE ROWS, A ⛔ QA LOOP, A ⛔ COMPILE, A ⛔ 556-TEST SUITE AND ⛔ THIRTEEN MUTATIONS WERE SPENT ⛔ REFUTING THE ⛔ RIGHT ANSWER — ⛔ BECAUSE ⛔ ONE UNREPLICATED MEASUREMENT SAID A ⛔ WORKING LEVER WAS ⛔ DEAD, AND ⛔ EVERY ROW DOWNSTREAM ⛔ INHERITED IT AS ⛔ SETTLED.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE INSTANCE, ⛔ MEASURED ⛔ BOTH WAYS, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ COST IS THE POINT.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1151` answered ⛔ *"the colour goes in at `general Data.base Color`"*. ⛔ CORRECT.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1154` ran the ⛔ decisive test — ⛔ set it to ⛔ PURE RED — and reported ⛔ *"no change at all; renders ⛔ EXACTLY the same beige"*, ⛔ B/G `0.890` vs baseline `0.891`. ⛔ THE LANE RECORDED THE FIELD AS ⛔ REFUTED ON PIXELS.**
   - 🚨 **⭐ `TASK-1172` ran the ⛔ IDENTICAL SINGLE-VARIABLE FLIP: ⛔ `(202.9, 80.6, 55.7)`, ⛔ B/G ⛔ `0.6908`, ⛔ sat ⛔ `72.56 %` against `0.8461` / `19.64 %`. ⛔ NOT A SUBTLE DISAGREEMENT — ⛔ A ⛔ VIOLENT CHANGE THAT THE ⛔ FIRST ROW SCORED AS ⛔ ZERO.**
   - ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ WHAT THE NULL BOUGHT: ⭐ `TASK-1154` ⇒ ⭐ `1165` ⇒ ⭐ `1166` ⇒ ⭐ `1167` — ⛔ a ⛔ MATERIAL BUILT, a ⛔ C++ CHANGE WRITTEN, ⛔ COMPILED, ⛔ QA'd, ⛔ GATED and ⛔ REVERTED UNSHIPPED — ⛔ ALL to route around a field that ⛔ WORKED THE WHOLE TIME.** ⛔ **⛔ AND IT SPAWNED A ⛔ LAW-LEVEL FICTION: ⛔ I INVENTED A ⛔ CONSTRUCTION-TIMING MECHANISM (⭐ `FOG-§12.4b`, ⛔ now struck) ⛔ WHOSE ⛔ ONLY PURPOSE WAS TO MAKE THE ⛔ FALSE NULL ⛔ CONSISTENT WITH REALITY.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ ASYMMETRY, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ WHOLE TRANSFERABLE IDEA. ⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE RESULT AND A ⛔ NULL RESULT ARE ⛔ NOT TWO OUTCOMES OF EQUAL STANDING.**
   - ✅ **⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE RESULT IS ⛔ NEARLY SELF-AUTHENTICATING: ⛔ if the picture ⛔ MOVED when you pulled the lever, then the ⛔ DRIVER reached the object, the ⛔ ACQUIRER was live, and the ⛔ ANALYSER was sensitive — ⛔ ALL THREE, ⛔ FOR FREE. ⛔ A broken apparatus ⛔ struggles to fake a ⛔ correlated change.**
   - 🚨 **⛔ A ⛔ NULL RESULT IS ⛔ CONSISTENT WITH ⛔ EVERY FAILURE MODE THE APPARATUS HAS. ⛔ *"NOTHING HAPPENED"* IS THE ⛔ DEFAULT OUTPUT OF: ⛔ a write that landed on the ⛔ WRONG OBJECT · ⛔ a write to the ⛔ WRONG WORLD (`L_Arena` vs `UEDPIE_0_L_Arena`) · ⛔ a ⛔ silent no-op API · ⛔ a ⛔ stale or cached frame · ⛔ an acquirer pointed at ⛔ nothing · ⛔ a metric ⛔ insensitive to the axis · ⛔ a ⛔ precondition never set up · ⛔ and ⛔ ALSO of a genuinely dead lever.** ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE NULL IS THE ⛔ ONE READING THAT ⛔ EVERY BROKEN CONFIGURATION AND THE ⛔ TRUE ANSWER ⛔ ALL PRODUCE ⛔ IDENTICALLY.***
   - 🚨🚨 **⛔ AND THE ⛔ AGGRAVATING PROPERTY, ⛔ WHICH IS WHY THIS IS ⛔ EXPENSIVE RATHER THAN MERELY WRONG: ⛔ A NULL ⛔ ENDS AN INVESTIGATION. ⛔ A positive result ⛔ invites the next question; ⛔ a null ⛔ CLOSES A DOOR AND ⛔ SENDS EVERYONE ELSEWHERE.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ ONE RESULT WHOSE ⛔ CONSEQUENCE IS THAT ⛔ NOBODY EVER LOOKS AGAIN — ⛔ so its errors are ⛔ NOT SELF-CORRECTING. ⛔ EVERY OTHER MISTAKE IN THIS PIPELINE GETS ⛔ RE-EXAMINED BY THE ⛔ NEXT ROW THAT ⛔ TRIPS OVER IT. ⛔ A FALSE NULL GETS ⛔ CITED.**
3. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTIES. ⛔ ONE EXTRA WRITE AND ⛔ ONE EXTRA CAPTURE, ⛔ DELIBERATELY CHEAP (⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c — ⛔ an ignored law protects nothing).**
   - 🚨 **(a) ⛔ THE ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL, ⛔ AND ITS ⛔ THREE QUALIFIERS ARE ⛔ ALL LOAD-BEARING: ⛔ BEFORE a null may be reported as a ⛔ FINDING, the ⛔ SAME INSTRUMENT, in the ⛔ SAME SESSION, must be shown to ⛔ PRODUCE A NON-NULL ON ⛔ SOMETHING.**
     - ⛔ **⛔ *SAME INSTRUMENT* — ⛔ a control run through a ⛔ different driver/acquirer/analyser proves ⛔ nothing about the one that returned the null.**
     - ⛔ **⛔ *SAME SESSION* — ⛔ the defect is ⛔ often the ⛔ SESSION (⛔ wrong world, ⛔ stale actor, ⛔ dead handle, ⛔ contaminated cvar). ⛔ A control from ⛔ yesterday's run is a ⛔ DIFFERENT APPARATUS.**
     - ⛔ **⛔ *ON SOMETHING* — ⛔ ideally on the ⛔ SAME LEVER driven to an ⛔ ABSURD value, ⛔ which is the ⛔ strongest and ⛔ cheapest form: ⛔ if ⛔ PURE RED does ⛔ nothing, your ⛔ APPARATUS is broken, ⛔ full stop — ⛔ no material in this engine is ⛔ invariant to its own base colour.**
   - 🚨 **(b) ⛔ THE ⛔ LABEL, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT OPTIONAL: ⛔ A NULL ⛔ WITHOUT (a) IS WRITTEN ⛔ `NOT MEASURED — ⛔ NULL WITH NO POSITIVE CONTROL`. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NEVER *"REFUTED"*, ⛔ NEVER *"INERT"*, ⛔ NEVER *"NOT WIRED"*, ⛔ NEVER *"DEAD"* — ⛔ those words assert a ⛔ PROPERTY OF THE ENGINE, and ⛔ you have only observed a ⛔ PROPERTY OF YOUR SETUP.**
   - 🚨 **(c) ⛔ THE ⛔ ROUTE-CLOSING BAR, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ TEETH: ⛔ A NULL MAY ⛔ NOT CLOSE A ROUTE, ⛔ KILL AN ANSWER, ⛔ RETIRE A CANDIDATE OR ⛔ REDIRECT A LANE ⛔ UNLESS IT CARRIES (a). ⛔ THE MANAGER ⛔ REFUSES TO BOARD A ⛔ REDIRECTION WHOSE ⛔ ONLY EVIDENCE IS AN ⛔ UNCONTROLLED NULL** — ⛔ **⛔ THAT IS THE ⛔ EXACT DECISION I ⛔ FAILED TO MAKE ⛔ FOUR ROWS RUNNING.**
   - ⛔ **(d) ⛔ AND IF THE NULL ⛔ SURVIVES ITS CONTROL — ⛔ i.e. the apparatus ⛔ demonstrably works and the lever ⛔ still does nothing — ⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ REAL FINDING, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WORTH A GREAT DEAL. ⛔ THIS LAW DOES ⛔ NOT DISTRUST NULLS; ⛔ IT ⛔ PRICES THEM.**

   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐ ***⛔ THAT IS THE ⛔ CORRECT SHAPE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ EXACT OPPOSITE OF WHAT ⛔ I DID WITH THE ⛔ SAME CONTRADICTION NINE HOURS EARLIER. ⛔ I HAD THE ⛔ SAME TWO INCOMPATIBLE FACTS ON MY DESK AND I ⛔ WROTE A MECHANISM TO ⛔ JOIN THEM. ⛔ THE ROW BELOW ME ⛔ LEFT THE HOLE ⛔ OPEN AND ⛔ LABELLED IT.*** ⛔ **⛔ AN ⛔ UNEXPLAINED CONTRADICTION, ⛔ HONESTLY FLAGGED, IS ⛔ WORTH MORE THAN A ⛔ TIDY THEORY — ⛔ because the ⛔ hole gets ⛔ re-tested and the ⛔ theory gets ⛔ cited.**
7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SC-§39`/`§39.1` (⛔ the parent, on searches) · ⭐ `SC-§112` + cl. 6 (⛔ the instrument chain, ⛔ incl. the ⛔ DRIVER) · ⭐ `SC-§113` cl. 3(c) (⛔ absence-of-errors on an unreachable path) · ⭐ `SC-§107` cl. 4 (⛔ the converse) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ verify the ⛔ CONSUMER — ⛔ **⛔ the pixels are what ⛔ finally settled it, ⛔ both times**) · ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ inheritance) · ⭐ `SC-§110` (⛔ a law that cites a fact ⛔ rots silently — ⛔ **⛔ THIS FALSE NULL ⛔ BECAME LAW TEXT, ⛔ WHICH IS HOW IT ⛔ REACHED THREE ROWS**) · ⭐ `SC-§111` · ⭐ `SC-§104` · ⭐ `SC-§101` · ⭐ `SHIP-§9`.** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ THE CHEAPEST EXPERIMENT IN THIS PROJECT IS ⛔ SETTING A PARAMETER TO SOMETHING ⛔ ABSURD AND ⛔ LOOKING. ⛔ WE DECLINED TO SPEND ⛔ ONE FRAME ON IT, AND ⛔ PAID THREE ROWS, A ⛔ COMPILE, A ⛔ SUITE AND ⛔ THIRTEEN MUTATIONS ⛔ INSTEAD.***

8. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — ⛔ THE ⛔ NEGATIVE-READING RULE. ⛔ CLAUSE 2's ASYMMETRY ⛔ DOES NOT STOP AT THE ⛔ RESULT: ⛔ IT ⛔ REAPPEARS ⛔ INSIDE THE ⛔ PREDICATE — AND A CHECK'S ⛔ ABSENT/NEGATIVE BRANCH IS THE ⛔ ONE BRANCH THAT ⛔ NO SUCCESSFUL RUN ⛔ EVER EXERCISES.** (added 2026-09-09 by the manager, ⛔ **on ⛔ FOUR instances ⛔ MEASURED IN ⛔ ONE SESSION — ⭐ `TASK-1182`'s ⛔ W-13 plus its ⛔ three siblings** · marker `SC-114-CL8-NOBODY-CONTROLS-THE-NULL`)

   > ⛔⛔ **⛔ A POSITIVE READING GETS ⛔ SCRUTINISED BECAUSE IT ⛔ ASSERTS SOMETHING. ⛔ A NULL SLIPS THROUGH BECAUSE IT ⛔ APPEARS TO ASSERT ⛔ NOTHING.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ NEGATIVE/ABSENT READING IS THE ONE ⛔ NOBODY CONTROLS — ⛔ AT ⛔ EVERY LAYER OF THE STACK ⛔ AT ONCE.**

   - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(a) ⛔ THE ⛔ NEW MECHANISM, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THIS CLAUSE EXISTS RATHER THAN A ⛔ CITATION: ⛔ **⛔ AN ASSERTION THAT ⛔ CANNOT FAIL IN THE ⛔ DIRECTION IT WAS ⛔ WRITTEN TO FAIL.** ⛔ MEASURED: `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1:1264` asserted ⛔ `-notlike '*seen*'` — ⛔ and `-like` is ⛔ CASE-INSENSITIVE, so the guard ⛔ ALSO MATCHES ⛔ `NOT SEEN`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ EXACT STRING MEANING *"THE THING IS ⛔ ABSENT"* ⛔ SATISFIES THE GUARD WRITTEN TO ⛔ CATCH ITS ABSENCE — ⛔ and it sat ⛔ INSIDE THE CASE TESTING ⛔ B-1, ⛔ the ⛔ one behaviour the runner exists to ⛔ prove.** ⛔ **⛔ THE NEGATION WAS ON THE ⛔ WRONG SIDE OF THE ⛔ MEANING, ⛔ not on the wrong side of an ⛔ operator — ⛔ which is why it ⛔ reads correct.**
   - 🚨⛔ **(b) ⛔ SIBLING ⛔ ONE — ⛔ AN ⛔ ABSENCE DETECTOR THAT ⛔ REPORTS PRESENCE: ⛔ `Get-Process -Id` ⛔ RESOLVES A ⛔ DEAD PID ⛔ while `Start-Process -PassThru` still holds a ⛔ handle ⇒ ⛔ the kill-confirmation printed ⛔ `SURVIVED the kill` ⛔ for a process ⛔ ALREADY CONFIRMED DEAD.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ HAD IT SHIPPED, THE ⛔ FIRST BOUND RUN WOULD HAVE REPORTED A ⛔ FAILED KILL ⛔ ON A SUCCESSFUL ONE** — ⛔ the ⛔ FALSE-FAIL direction (⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 6's shape) ⛔ from the ⛔ same root as (a)'s ⛔ FALSE-PASS one.
   - 🚨⛔ **(c) ⛔ SIBLING ⛔ TWO — ⛔ THE ⛔ NULL INPUT THAT ⛔ SCORES A PASS: ⛔ an ⛔ ALL-BLACK FRAME scores ⛔ `sat 0.0 %`, ⛔ INSIDE the pass band (⭐ `SC-§112a` cl. 7 — ⛔ saved ⛔ only by `nan >= 0.945` evaluating ⛔ false).**
   - 🚨⛔ **(d) ⛔ SIBLING ⛔ THREE — ⛔ THE ⛔ NULL RESULT THAT ⛔ CLOSED A ROUTE: ⛔ THIS SECTION'S ⛔ OWN cl. 1 — ⛔ an ⛔ instrument failure that ⛔ shut the ⛔ correct route for ⛔ FOUR ROWS.**
   - ✅🚨⭐⭐⭐ **THE DUTY, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ ONE QUESTION ASKED OF ⛔ EVERY GUARD YOU ⛔ WRITE OR ⛔ REVIEW: ⛔ **⛔ *"WHAT ⛔ EXACT INPUT MAKES THIS GO ⛔ RED — AND HAVE I ⛔ FED IT ONE?"*** ⛔ **⛔ IF THE ANSWER IS *"the ⛔ run will produce it"*, ⛔ IT IS ⛔ `NOT EXECUTED`: ⛔ A ⛔ GREEN RUN EXERCISES ⛔ ONLY THE POSITIVE BRANCH, ⛔ EVERY TIME, ⛔ FOR FREE — ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ PRECISELY WHY THE ⛔ NEGATIVE BRANCH ⛔ ACCUMULATES DEFECTS ⛔ UNDISTURBED FOR AS LONG AS THE TOOL ⛔ KEEPS WORKING.**
   - ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ MECHANICAL FORMS, ⛔ CHEAP ON PURPOSE (⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c):**
     1. ⛔ **⛔ SPELL THE NEGATIVE ⛔ OUT — ⛔ ⛔ NEVER a ⛔ SUBSTRING NEGATION (`-notlike '*x*'`, `not in`, `grep -v x`) where the ⛔ VOCABULARY CONTAINS ⛔ `NOT x` / ⛔ `NO x` / ⛔ `UN-x`. ⛔ MATCH THE ⛔ WHOLE TOKEN OR ⛔ ANCHOR IT.** ⛔ **⛔ THE TELL: ⛔ your ⛔ negative pattern is a ⛔ SUBSTRING of the ⛔ phrase you are trying to ⛔ reject.**
     2. ⛔ **⛔ FEED THE GUARD ITS ⛔ OWN NULL — ⛔ the ⛔ EMPTY STRING, the ⛔ MISSING FILE, the ⛔ BLACK FRAME, the ⛔ DEAD PID, the ⛔ ZERO-LINE LOG — ⛔ and ⛔ REQUIRE A RED** (⭐ `SC-§112a` cl. 7's ⛔ arithmetic, ⛔ generalised ⛔ off pixels onto ⛔ any predicate).
     3. ⛔ **⛔ AN ⛔ ABSENCE DETECTOR IS ⛔ CONTROLLED ON A ⛔ KNOWN-ABSENT SUBJECT, ⛔ NEVER ONLY ON A ⛔ KNOWN-PRESENT ONE — ⛔ presence and absence are ⛔ TWO BRANCHES of the API and it may ⛔ lie on ⛔ exactly one of them** (⛔ (b) is that, ⛔ measured).
   - ⚖️⭐⭐⭐ **⛔ WHY A ⛔ CLAUSE HERE AND ⛔ NOT A ⛔ FIFTH SECTION — ⛔ CHECKED ⛔ BEFORE MINTING, ⛔ AS ⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 3 REQUIRES, ⛔ AND THE ANSWER WAS ⛔ NOT THE FLATTERING ONE:**
     - ⛔ **⛔ **⛔ THIS SECTION ⛔ ALREADY OWNS THE IDEA.** ⛔ cl. 2 IS ⛔ VERBATIM *"a ⛔ POSITIVE result is ⛔ nearly self-authenticating … a ⛔ NULL is ⛔ consistent with ⛔ EVERY failure mode the apparatus has"*. ⛔ cl. 8 CHANGES ⛔ EXACTLY ONE THING — ⛔ **⛔ WHERE THE NULL LIVES**: ⛔ cl. 2 puts it in the ⛔ EXPERIMENT'S OUTCOME, ⛔ cl. 8 puts it in the ⛔ CHECK'S OWN BRANCH.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ SAME ASYMMETRY, ⛔ ONE LAYER IN ⇒ ⛔ A ⛔ CLAUSE, ⛔ NOT A NUMBER.**
     - ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ assert ⛔ STATE, not ⛔ TALLIES) is the ⛔ NEAREST BY ⛔ DISCOVERY METHOD — ⛔ both are found by ⛔ READING the assertion, ⛔ not by running a control. ⛔ BUT ITS ⛔ REMEDY DOES ⛔ NOTHING HERE: ⛔ `-notlike '*seen*'` ⛔ **IS** a ⛔ STATE assertion ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ STILL FALSE.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A HOME WHOSE ⛔ CURE DOES NOT ⛔ CURE IT IS ⛔ NOT A HOME.***
     - ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§112a` cl. 7 is the ⛔ SAME DEFECT ⛔ FENCED TO ⛔ PIXELS (⛔ its duty is ⛔ arithmetic on a ⛔ synthesised black image) · ⭐ `SC-§113` cl. 6 is the ⛔ OPPOSITE DIRECTION (⛔ a ⛔ POSITIVE reading with a ⛔ SECOND SOURCE) · ⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 6 is a ⛔ CONTROL THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER RUN ⛔ AT ALL.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THREE ⛔ ADJACENT SECTIONS, ⛔ NONE COVERING IT, ⛔ ALL TOUCHING IT — ⛔ which is ⛔ EXACTLY the configuration in which ⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 6 ⛔ ruled that a ⛔ *"FIFTH SECTION WOULD BE ⛔ NOISE"*. ⛔ I ⛔ APPLY MY OWN RULING ⛔ TO MYSELF.**
     - ⛔ **⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects) is the ⛔ PARENT REMEDY, ⛔ ACKNOWLEDGED ⛔ RATHER THAN RE-MINTED. ⛔ WHAT cl. 8 ⛔ ADDS IS THE ⛔ SELECTION RULE ⭐ `SHIP-§9` ⛔ LACKS: ⛔ **⛔ OF ALL A GATE'S BRANCHES, ⛔ SPEND YOUR ⛔ ONE VALIDATION ON THE ⛔ NEGATIVE ONE** — ⛔ the ⛔ positive branch is validated by ⛔ every green run you will ⛔ ever do; ⛔ the negative one is validated ⛔ ONLY if you ⛔ deliberately break something.**
   - 🚨⛔ **⛔ RIDERS ⛔ PLACED THIS PASS, ⛔ SO THE CLAUSE IS ⛔ REACHABLE FROM THE ⛔ SCENE OF THE CRIME AND ⛔ NOT ONLY FROM HERE: ⭐ `SC-§112a` cl. 7 · ⭐ `SC-§113` cl. 6 · ⭐ `SC-§116` cl. 6 ⛔ EACH NOW ⛔ POINT AT ⭐ `SC-§114` cl. 8 AS THE ⛔ CONSOLIDATED FORM.** ⛔ **⛔ A CLAUSE NOBODY CAN ⛔ REACH FROM WHERE THE DEFECT IS ⛔ WRITTEN IS ⛔ PROSE** (⭐ `SC-§110`).

### SC-§113 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ UNREACHABLE-SUBSYSTEM LAW — ⛔ IF ⛔ NO AGENT IN THIS PIPELINE CAN ⛔ TRIGGER A CODE PATH, THEN ⛔ NOTHING ON THAT PATH IS ⛔ EVER TESTED, ITS ⛔ ERROR LOGS ARE ⛔ SILENT FOR THE ⛔ WRONG REASON, AND ⛔ EVERY CLAIM ABOUT ITS RUNTIME BEHAVIOUR IS ⛔ `NOT MEASURED` ⛔ BY DEFAULT. ⛔ REACHABILITY IS A ⛔ DESIGN REQUIREMENT, ⛔ NOT A TESTING CONVENIENCE.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1167` §5 — ⛔ the build-master mapped the dead end ⛔ exhaustively and ⛔ declared it rather than working around it** · marker `SC-113-NOBODY-CAN-RUN-IT`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ WE SPENT ⛔ THREE ROWS, A ⛔ QA LOOP, A ⛔ COMPILE AND ⛔ THIRTEEN MUTATIONS ON A FUNCTION THAT ⛔ NO ONE IN THIS PIPELINE HAS ⛔ EVER EXECUTED ⛔ ONCE.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE INSTANCE, ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ AND IT IS A ⛔ FOUR-WAY DEAD END, ⛔ NOT A MISSING SKILL.** ⛔ **`AFogVolume::RaiseFog()` cannot be triggered from ⛔ any agent seat:**
   - ⛔ **`AFogVolume` exposes ⛔ NO `UFUNCTION` ⛔ AT ALL** ⇒ ⛔ nothing on it is ⛔ reflection-callable.
   - ⛔ **Its ⛔ only runtime caller, `USpellLibrary::ResolveSpell`, is a ⛔ PLAIN STATIC**, ⛔ likewise unreachable.
   - ⛔ **MCP's `ObjectTools` has ⛔ NO FUNCTION-INVOCATION TOOL ⛔ WHATSOEVER** — ⛔ get / set / list ⛔ properties only ⇒ ⛔ even a `BlueprintCallable` (`SiegePlayerController::PlayHandSlot`) is ⛔ out of reach.
   - ⛔ **`FogActiveUntilTimeSeconds` ⛔ IS a writable `UPROPERTY`, but ⛔ NOTHING POLLS IT** — ⛔ `RefreshFogVisual()` is reached ⛔ only from `RaiseFog` / `ApplyBrightSun` / `ResetFog`, with ⛔ no `BeginPlay` and ⛔ no `Tick` hook ⇒ ⛔ the ⛔ one writable door opens onto ⛔ no corridor.
   - ⛔ **And `add_to_scene_from_*` is ⛔ REFUSED while PIE is active** ⇒ ⛔ the visual must be staged in the editor world and ⛔ carried in.
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ CONSEQUENCE THAT ⛔ ACTUALLY BIT, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN *"UNTESTED"*: ⛔ THE PATH'S ⛔ SILENCE READS AS ⛔ SUCCESS.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1165` added a ⛔ SEVENTH `Error` site whose ⛔ whole job is to ⛔ shout if the material write fails. ⛔ IT NEVER FIRED — ⛔ and ⛔ NOT ONE PERSON COULD SAY WHETHER THAT WAS ⛔ GOOD NEWS, ⛔ because ⛔ `grep` over the whole editor log returns ⛔ ZERO lines matching `AFogVolume|ApplyFogVisualMaterial|VerifyFogVisualMaterial`.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ERROR THAT ⛔ CANNOT FIRE AND AN ERROR THAT ⛔ CHOSE NOT TO FIRE ⛔ PRODUCE BYTE-IDENTICAL LOGS. ⛔ THE DEFAULT READING OF AN EMPTY LOG IS ⛔ "FINE", AND IT IS ⛔ THE WRONG ONE.*** ⛔ **⛔ THIS PROJECT HAS ⛔ ALREADY PAID FOR THIS EXACT SENTENCE ONCE — ⛔ the climb instrument that printed ⛔ nothing without `Log LogGitClaudeUnrealTest Verbose`, where an ⛔ EMPTY LOG READ AS A ⛔ ZERO OFFSET (⛔ a ⛔ false pass).**
3. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTIES. ⛔ DELIBERATELY CHEAP (⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c — ⛔ an ignored law protects nothing):**
   - 🚨 **(a) ⛔ THE ⛔ STANDING DISCLOSURE. ⛔ A subsystem with ⛔ no agent-reachable trigger carries ⛔ ONE LINE at its law head naming ⛔ WHICH CHANNEL IS MISSING** — ⛔ *"`<Class>`: ⛔ NO AGENT-REACHABLE TRIGGER — ⛔ no `UFUNCTION`, ⛔ no console command, ⛔ no automation-test entry"*. ⛔ **⛔ ONE LINE. ⛔ It is ⛔ not an audit and ⛔ not a plan; ⛔ it is a ⛔ warning label.**
   - 🚨 **(b) ⛔ THE ⛔ ROW DUTY, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⭐ `SC-§111` cl. 3(d) SHAPE ⛔ VERBATIM: ⛔ ANY row asserting that subsystem's ⛔ RUNTIME behaviour writes ⛔ *"PATH NEVER EXECUTED"* ⛔ IN THOSE WORDS.** ⛔ **⛔ An unrun instrument reports ⛔ `NOT MEASURED`, ⛔ never `PASS`.** ✅ **⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT: ⭐ `TASK-1167` §5 did ⛔ exactly this ⛔ unprompted, ⛔ mapped all four dead ends, and ⛔ declared ask (B) ⛔ NOT re-measured ⛔ rather than quietly skipping it.**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ ABSENCE-OF-ERRORS ON AN ⛔ UNREACHABLE PATH IS ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE (⭐ `SC-§39`'s positive-control rule, ⛔ applied to ⛔ EXECUTION rather than to ⛔ SEARCH). ⛔ The control that discharges it is ⛔ ONE LOG LINE PROVING THE FUNCTION ⛔ RAN.**
   - 🚨 **(d) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ FORWARD-LOOKING ONE, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ ONLY ONE THAT ⛔ ENDS THE PROBLEM: ⛔ A ⛔ NEW GAMEPLAY SUBSYSTEM SHIPS WITH ⛔ ONE REACHABLE TRIGGER — ⛔ an `Exec` / console command, a ⛔ `CallInEditor` button, or an ⛔ automation test that drives the ⛔ real entry point. ⛔ IF YOU CANNOT ⛔ TRIGGER IT, YOU CANNOT ⛔ GATE IT, AND ⛔ EVERY GATE YOU RUN ON IT IS ⛔ CEREMONY.**
4. ⚠️⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ TRAP IN THE ⛔ OBVIOUS FIX, ⛔ NAMED SO THE REMEDY ROW DOES NOT ⛔ SHIP A NO-OP: ⛔ ADDING A `UFUNCTION` ⛔ ALONE DOES ⛔ NOT MAKE A PATH AGENT-REACHABLE ⛔ HERE, ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ TOOL THAT WOULD CALL IT ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST** (cl. 1). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE TRIGGER MUST LAND ON A CHANNEL WE HAVE ⛔ MEASURED OURSELVES ⛔ USING: ⛔ a ⛔ console command (⛔ the `r.VolumetricFog` lane in ⭐ `TASK-1160` proves console ⛔ reaches us), ⛔ a ⛔ `CallInEditor` button (⛔ 🧑 he can press it even if we cannot), ⛔ or the ⛔ automation suite (⛔ 556 tests already execute). ⛔ CHOOSE BY ⛔ MEASUREMENT, ⛔ NOT BY WHICH MACRO LOOKS RIGHT.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SC-§111` (⛔ the ⛔ TARGET we never compile — ⛔ **⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ CODE PATH WE NEVER RUN; ⛔ same hole, ⛔ different axis**) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ a probe needs a positive control) · ⭐ `SC-§36.1` (⛔ a built-and-tested mechanism with ⛔ ZERO callers shipped and ⛔ nothing failed — ⛔ **⛔ THAT IS THIS LAW'S ⛔ OUTCOME, ⛔ recorded before we had its ⛔ cause**) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ the log printed the ⛔ REQUEST) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9`.** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ A GATE CAN ONLY SEE WHAT ⛔ RUNS. ⛔ WE BUILT A FOG WHOSE ⛔ ONLY WITNESS IS 🧑 THE MAN PLAYING THE GAME.***
6. ✅🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE ⛔ INSTANCE IS ⛔ DISCHARGED ⛔ ON EVIDENCE 2026-09-09 (⭐ `TASK-1173` → `TASK-1174` PASS → ⭐ `TASK-1175` code case, ⛔ commit `4a3da63`) — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ DISCHARGE ⛔ ALMOST WENT THROUGH ON A ⛔ CONFOUNDED INSTRUMENT (⭐ W8). ⛔ READ THIS ⛔ BEFORE YOU DISCHARGE ⛔ ANY FUTURE INSTANCE OF THIS LAW.**
   - ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ LINE THAT CLOSED IT, ⛔ QUOTED, ⛔ FROM ⛔ PRODUCTION CODE (`FogVolume.cpp:338`, ⛔ inside the ⛔ SHIPPED `FindOrSpawn` — ⛔ not scaffolding, ⛔ not a test fixture):**
     ```
     LogGitClaudeUnrealTest: [FogVolume_0] AFogVolume spawned — the fog-state actor now exists for this match (FOG-§10.1: one state object)
     ```
     ⛔ **Reached in ⛔ BOTH lanes: the ⛔ automation suite (555 / 555, ⛔ 0 `Fail`) ⛔ AND the ⛔ console commandlet, ⛔ where `Siege.Fog.Status` ⛔ READ THE STATE BACK after `Siege.Fog.Clear` — ⛔ a ⛔ resolved-state assertion, ⛔ not a call tally (⭐ `SC-§104`).**
   - 🚨🚨⛔⛔⛔ **⛔ W8 — ⛔ THE ⛔ DISCHARGE GREP ⛔ AS PRESCRIBED IS A ⛔ CONFOUNDED INSTRUMENT, ⛔ AND ⛔ CL. 2's `0` IS ⛔ NOT COMPARABLE TO IT.** ⛔ **`grep -cE "AFogVolume|Siege\.Fog\." <log>` returned ⛔ **8** on the suite log — ⛔ and ⛔ **7 OF THOSE 8** are a ⛔ PRE-EXISTING TEST NAME, `TheFogStateIsReadInExactlyOnePlaceAndTheSeamConsultsAFogVolume`, which ⛔ CONTAINS THE SUBSTRING `AFogVolume` and was ⛔ MEASURED PRESENT AT `20c1bea` — ⛔ i.e. ⛔ BEFORE THE REMEDY EXISTED.**
     - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ BARE COUNT WOULD HAVE READ ⛔ NON-ZERO WITH THE FOG PATH ⛔ NEVER EXECUTING. ⛔ *"8, ⛔ non-zero, ⛔ discharged"* would have been a ⛔ FALSE GREEN ⛔ OF EXACTLY THE CLASS THIS LAW WAS WRITTEN ABOUT — ⛔ closed by an instrument that ⛔ measures the ⛔ NAMES OF TESTS, ⛔ not the ⛔ EXECUTION OF CODE.**
     - ✅ **⛔ WHAT ⛔ SAVED IT WAS ⛔ QA's ⛔ FULLER CRITERION, ⛔ WHICH WAS ⛔ CORRECTLY SPECIFIED AND IS ⛔ NOW THE ⛔ ONLY SANCTIONED FORM: ⛔ *"a ⛔ NON-ZERO count ⛔ **WITH AT LEAST ONE QUOTED `LogGitClaudeUnrealTest:` LINE**"*.**
       ```
       grep "LogGitClaudeUnrealTest:" <log> | grep -cE "AFogVolume|Siege\.Fog\."     # = 1
       ```
       ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ FILTER IS ⛔ NOT A REFINEMENT. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ THE DISCRIMINATOR: it separates ⛔ *"the string appears in this log"* from ⛔ *"this code emitted a line"*, ⛔ and ⛔ ONLY THE SECOND IS EVIDENCE.**
     - ⚠️ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ OPPOSITE ERROR IS ⛔ ALSO LIVE, ⛔ SO THE ⛔ FILTERED COUNT IS ⛔ NOT A TALLY EITHER: ⛔ `*GetNameSafe(this)` yields ⛔ `FogVolume_0`, so ⛔ `Fog INTEGRITY FLOOR engaged` and ⛔ `Fog VISUAL spawned` match ⛔ NEITHER alternative in the pattern. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ LOW filtered count is ⛔ NOT *"the floor did not run"*. ⛔ READ THE LINES; ⛔ DO NOT SCORE THEM.**
   - ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ TRANSFERABLE PIECE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⭐ `SC-§39`/⭐ `SC-§114`'s FAMILY ⛔ WEARING NEW CLOTHES: ⛔ **AN INSTRUMENT WHOSE ⛔ POSITIVE READING HAS A ⛔ SECOND SOURCE ⛔ CANNOT DISCHARGE ANYTHING.** ⛔ THE ⛔ QUESTION TO ASK OF ⛔ EVERY GREP OFFERED AS ⛔ PROOF OF EXECUTION: ⛔ *"⛔ WHAT ELSE IN THIS FILE COULD MATCH — ⛔ AND WAS IT ⛔ ALREADY THERE BEFORE MY CHANGE?"* ⛔ THE ⛔ CHEAP ANSWER IS A ⛔ `git grep` AT THE ⛔ BASE COMMIT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT FOUND IT.***
     - ⭐ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ MIRROR IMAGE OF THIS CLAUSE, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-09: ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ POSITIVE reading with a ⛔ SECOND SOURCE. ⛔ THE ⛔ NEGATIVE reading has its ⛔ OWN, ⛔ WORSE PATHOLOGY — ⛔ **⛔ NOBODY CONTROLS IT AT ALL** ⇒ ⭐ `SC-§114` cl. 8.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ STANDING CORRECTION TO ⛔ CL. 2: its `0` was measured with the ⛔ BARE pattern over the ⛔ editor log. ⛔ **⛔ DO NOT COMPARE A ⛔ FILTERED COUNT AGAINST IT AS IF THEY WERE THE SAME INSTRUMENT.** ⛔ The ⛔ before/after pair that ⛔ actually holds is ⛔ `0` bare @ `20c1bea`-era editor log ⇒ ⛔ `1` FILTERED, ⛔ production-emitted, @ `4a3da63`.**

### SC-§112 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ ACQUISITION-STAGE LAW — ⛔ A CAPTURE INSTRUMENT MUST BE SHOWN A ⛔ KNOWN-NONBLANK FRAME ⛔ BEFORE ITS OUTPUT IS EVIDENCE. ⛔ A POSITIVE CONTROL ON THE ⛔ ANALYSER SAYS ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT THE ⛔ ACQUIRER — AND A ⛔ BLACK FRAME DOES ⛔ NOT PRODUCE ⛔ GARBAGE, IT PRODUCES THE ⛔ PASSING ANSWER.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1167` §4.1 — ⛔ GDI screen capture of the editor window returned ⛔ `mean (0,0,0)`, ⛔ `max 0`, and it was ⛔ CAUGHT AND DISCARDED** · marker `SC-112-SHOW-THE-CAMERA-SOMETHING-FIRST`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ HAD THE HOST TRUSTED THAT FRAME, ⛔ EVERY NUMBER IN THAT TABLE WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ FABRICATED — ⛔ AND ⛔ EVERY ONE OF THEM WOULD HAVE READ ⛔ PLAUSIBLE.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE ⛔ SHARP EDGE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT *"BAD DATA IN, BAD DATA OUT"*. ⛔ THE METRIC IN USE WAS ⛔ FAR-FIELD `B/G` + ⛔ HSV SATURATION, and the ⛔ target was ⛔ *"NEUTRAL GREY"* — ⛔ i.e. ⛔ `B/G → 1.000`, ⛔ `saturation → 0 %`.** ⇒ 🚨 **⛔ AN ⛔ ALL-BLACK FRAME SCORES ⛔ SATURATION `0 %` AND ⛔ PERFECTLY NEUTRAL CHANNELS. ⛔ IT IS NOT ⛔ NOISE — ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ BEST POSSIBLE SCORE THE ROW COULD HAVE REPORTED.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE INSTRUMENT'S ⛔ NULL OUTPUT AND THE ROW'S ⛔ SUCCESS CONDITION WERE THE ⛔ SAME PIXEL VALUES. ⛔ THE FAILURE MODE POINTED ⛔ DIRECTLY AT `PASS`.***
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ TWO-STAGE INSIGHT, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ TRANSFERABLE PART: ⛔ EVERY PIXEL CLAIM HAS ⛔ TWO INSTRUMENTS IN SERIES — ⛔ AN ⛔ ACQUIRER (⛔ take the frame) AND AN ⛔ ANALYSER (⛔ reduce it to numbers) — AND ⛔ THEY FAIL ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1167` had a ⛔ GOOD analyser control (🧑 Jonathan's own beige frame ⇒ `B/G 0.891`, ⛔ no-fog grass ⇒ `0.548` — ⛔ it does ⛔ not answer *grey* to everything) ⛔ AND its acquirer was ⛔ RETURNING BLACK ⛔ AT THE SAME TIME.** ⇒ 🚨 **⛔ THE ANALYSER CONTROL WOULD ⛔ NOT HAVE CAUGHT IT, ⛔ BECAUSE THE ANALYSER CONTROL RUNS ON AN ⛔ ARCHIVED FILE, ⛔ NOT ON A ⛔ LIVE CAPTURE. ⛔ TWO CONTROLS, ⛔ TWO LEDGERS, ⛔ NEVER ONE SENTENCE SAYING *"THE PROBE IS CONTROLLED"* (⛔ the ⭐ `SC-§39` cl. 4 ⛔ two-ledger shape, ⛔ one stage earlier).**
3. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTIES. ⛔ ONE LINE OF ARITHMETIC EACH:**
   - 🚨 **(a) ⛔ BEFORE any capture is used as evidence, ⛔ POINT THE ACQUIRER AT SOMETHING ⛔ KNOWN NON-BLANK and ⛔ REPORT A SCALAR FROM IT — ⛔ `mean`, ⛔ `max`, ⛔ distinct-colour count. ⛔ A CAPTURE WHOSE `max` IS ⛔ `0` IS A ⛔ FAILED CAPTURE, ⛔ NEVER A ⛔ DARK SCENE. ⛔ *"The tool returned an image"* is ⛔ not the check; ⛔ *"the image contains ⛔ more than one value"* ⛔ is.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ STATE WHAT THE ⛔ NULL FRAME ⛔ WOULD HAVE SCORED under your metric, ⛔ in the handoff. ⛔ IF THAT SCORE FALLS IN YOUR ⛔ PASS BAND (⛔ as it did here), ⛔ SAY SO ⛔ OUT LOUD — ⛔ that metric is ⛔ UNSAFE without (a), and ⛔ the reader deserves to know ⛔ which way its blindness points (⭐ `SC-§44`'s ⛔ reassuring-direction clause).**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ WHEN AN ACQUIRER FAILS ITS OWN CONTROL, ⛔ SWITCH INSTRUMENTS AND ⛔ SAY WHICH ONE SHIPPED. ⛔ Here: ⛔ GDI window capture ⛔ DISCARDED ⇒ ⛔ MCP `CaptureViewport`, ⛔ with the camera echoed back in ⛔ every result — ⛔ a ⛔ second, independent coordinate on ⛔ every frame (⭐ `SC-§110` cl. 3(c)'s ⛔ carry-the-landmark rule, ⛔ applied to ⛔ evidence instead of to ⛔ code).**
4. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT A DUPLICATE. ⛔ CHECKED AGAINST ⛔ FOUR NEIGHBOURS ⛔ BEFORE MINTING:**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§39.1` family member ⛔ #4 is ⛔ ALSO a black frame — ⛔ `concept_generate.py` ⛔ WRITING one and reporting `SUCCESS`. ⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ GENERATOR WHOSE ⛔ OUTPUT was wrong. ⛔ THIS IS AN ⛔ ACQUIRER WHOSE OUTPUT BECOMES THE ⛔ INPUT TO A ⛔ PERFECTLY HONEST ANALYSER ⛔ THAT THEN REPORTS ⛔ CONFIDENT, ⛔ WELL-CONTROLLED, ⛔ COMPLETELY FICTIONAL NUMBERS.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ `§39` distrusts the ⛔ RETURN VALUE; ⛔ this distrusts the ⛔ PAYLOAD ⛔ THAT EVERY LATER STAGE TREATS AS ⛔ GROUND TRUTH. ⛔ ADDED TO THAT FAMILY AS THE ⛔ NINTH ⛔ AND ⛔ GIVEN ITS OWN SECTION ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ REMEDY IS DIFFERENT: ⛔ `§39` says ⛔ *"read the number beside the word SUCCESS"*; ⛔ this says ⛔ *"⛔ take a control frame ⛔ FIRST"*.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `SHIP-§9` — ⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects. ⛔ THAT IS ABOUT A ⛔ GATE'S PREDICATE. ⛔ THIS IS ABOUT THE ⛔ EVIDENCE THE PREDICATE ⛔ CONSUMES, ⛔ one stage upstream of ⛔ any predicate at all.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§107` — ⛔ a red ⛔ DESTROYED BY OUR OWN FIX. ⛔ HERE ⛔ NOTHING DESTROYED ANYTHING; ⛔ THE INSTRUMENT WAS ⛔ BLIND FROM THE FIRST FRAME AND WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ BLIND WITH OR WITHOUT A FIX.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§111` — ⛔ a red the instrument was ⛔ NEVER CAPABLE of producing. ⛔ HERE IT ⛔ WAS capable; ⛔ it was ⛔ pointed at ⛔ nothing.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP + ⛔ THE ⛔ CREDIT, ⛔ WHICH BELONGS IN THE LAW: ⭐ `SC-§39`/`§39.1` · ⭐ `SC-§44` · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` · ⭐ `SC-§107` · ⭐ `SC-§111` · ⭐ `SC-§113` (⛔ its ⛔ sibling from the ⛔ same handoff — ⛔ **⛔ ONE ROW BOUGHT ⛔ BOTH: ⛔ the ⛔ instrument that could not ⛔ SEE, and the ⛔ code path that could not ⛔ RUN**).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY REASON THIS PROJECT KNOWS THE FOG IS ⛔ STILL BEIGE IS THAT ⛔ SOMEBODY LOOKED AT THE ⛔ CAMERA BEFORE LOOKING AT THE ⛔ PICTURE.***

🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **`SC-§112a` — THE ⛔ AMENDMENT. ⛔ THIS LAW SHIPPED ⛔ THE SAME DAY IT WAS WRITTEN AND THE ⛔ VERY NEXT ROW TO ⛔ OBEY IT ⛔ EARNED IT ⛔ SHARPER TEETH** (amended 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on ⭐ `TASK-1172` §2b/§2c — ⛔ the row ⛔ ran the duty, ⛔ then reported that ⛔ HALF ITS OWN PASS CONDITION WAS SATISFIABLE BY A ⛔ FAILED CAPTURE. ⛔ THAT IS A ROW ⛔ AUDITING ITS OWN GATE AND IT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT CLAUSE 3(b) ASKS FOR**)

6. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE ⛔ THIRD INSTRUMENT, AND IT IS ⛔ UPSTREAM OF BOTH THE OTHERS: ⛔ THE ⛔ DRIVER. ⛔ CLAUSE 2 NAMED ⛔ TWO INSTRUMENTS IN SERIES (⛔ ACQUIRER ⇒ ⛔ ANALYSER). ⛔ THERE ARE ⛔ THREE: ⛔ DRIVER ⇒ ⛔ ACQUIRER ⇒ ⛔ ANALYSER — ⛔ AND THEY FAIL ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ DRIVER'S QUESTION IS ⛔ NOT *"did my write succeed"* — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ *"⛔ DID MY WRITE LAND ON THE ⛔ OBJECT THAT IS ⛔ ACTUALLY BEING RENDERED?"* ⛔ A write can ⛔ return success, ⛔ read back perfectly, ⛔ and be on the ⛔ WRONG OBJECT** — ⛔ **⛔ MEASURED HAZARD IN THIS ENGINE: ⛔ `/Game/Maps/L_Arena…` and ⛔ `/Game/Maps/UEDPIE_0_L_Arena…` are ⛔ SEPARATE ACTORS WITH ⛔ SEPARATE MIDs, ⛔ addressed by ⛔ different paths, ⛔ and only ⛔ ONE of them is on screen.**
   - 🚨 **⛔ AND THE ⛔ SYMPTOM IS THE ⛔ NASTIEST ONE AVAILABLE: ⛔ A ⛔ BROKEN DRIVER PRODUCES A ⛔ PERFECTLY ACQUIRED, ⛔ PERFECTLY ANALYSED, ⛔ PERFECTLY CONFIDENT ⛔ NULL. ⛔ BOTH downstream controls ⛔ PASS. ⛔ The frame is ⛔ non-blank, the analyser is ⛔ calibrated, the numbers are ⛔ real — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ LEVER WAS NEVER CONNECTED.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ A NULL READS AS ⛔ "LEVER REFUTED", ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ ONE CONCLUSION NOBODY RE-TESTS. ⛔ THE ⛔ FULL LAW ON THAT IS ⭐ `SC-§114`; ⛔ THIS CLAUSE IS ⛔ ONLY THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT TAXONOMY IT RESTS ON.*** ⛔ **⛔ CREDIT: offered as a ⛔ candidate law by ⭐ `TASK-1172` §1a, ⛔ which found it ⛔ by re-running a flip somebody else had ⛔ already closed.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ DRIVER'S CONTROL, ⛔ ONE LINE: ⛔ DRIVE THE SUBJECT TO A ⛔ STATE YOU CAN ⛔ SEE FROM ORBIT (⛔ pure red, ⛔ pure blue, ⛔ zero, ⛔ absurd) AND ⛔ CONFIRM THE PICTURE MOVED. ⛔ IF AN ⛔ ABSURD VALUE CHANGES ⛔ NOTHING, YOUR ⛔ DRIVER IS BROKEN — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A FINDING ABOUT THE LEVER.**
7. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — ⛔ CLAUSE 3(b) GETS ⛔ TEETH, ⛔ BECAUSE *"SAY SO OUT LOUD"* WAS ⛔ NOT ENOUGH. ⛔ MEASURED ON ⭐ `TASK-1172` §2b: ⛔ AN ⛔ ALL-BLACK FRAME SCORES ⛔ `sat 0.0 %`, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ INSIDE THAT ROW'S PASS BAND (`sat ≤ 8.0 %`) — ⛔ THE ⛔ BEST SCORE THE ROW COULD PRODUCE.**
   - 🚨 **⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY THING THAT ⛔ REFUSED IT WAS THE ⛔ OTHER HALF, ⛔ `B/G ≥ 0.945`, ⛔ AND IT REFUSED ⛔ INCIDENTALLY: ⛔ `B/G` ON BLACK IS ⛔ `nan`, AND ⛔ `nan >= 0.945` EVALUATES ⛔ FALSE.** ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE GATE WAS SAVED BY AN ⛔ ACCIDENT OF ⛔ IEEE SEMANTICS, ⛔ NOT BY DESIGN. ⛔ HAD THE METRIC BEEN ⛔ SATURATION ALONE — ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ OBVIOUS METRIC FOR *"MAKE IT GREY"* — ⛔ THE FAILED CAPTURE WOULD HAVE SCORED A ⛔ PERFECT PASS.***
   - ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ RESTATED DUTY, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ WORDING CHANGE IS THE ⛔ POINT: ⛔ THE CHECK IS ⛔ NOT *"IS THE FRAME NON-BLANK"*. ⛔ THE CHECK IS ⛔ *"⛔ DOES MY INSTRUMENT'S ⛔ NULL OUTPUT ⛔ SCORE AS A ⛔ FAILURE UNDER MY ⛔ PASS CONDITION?"* ⛔ COMPUTE IT. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ ARITHMETIC ON A ⛔ SYNTHESISED BLACK IMAGE AND IT COSTS ⛔ NOTHING.** ⇒ 🚨⭐ **⛔ GENERALISED ⛔ OFF PIXELS 2026-09-09: ⭐ `SC-§114` cl. 8 — ⛔ THIS DUTY IS ⛔ NOT ABOUT ⛔ CAMERAS. ⛔ IT IS ABOUT ⛔ ANY PREDICATE, AND ⛔ THREE MORE INSTANCES OF IT WERE ⛔ MEASURED IN ⛔ ONE SESSION (⛔ incl. a ⛔ PowerShell `-notlike '*seen*'` ⛔ satisfied by ⛔ `NOT SEEN`).**
   - 🚨 **⛔ AND IF THE ANSWER IS ⛔ *"IT SCORES A PASS"* — ⛔ EVEN ⛔ PARTIALLY, ⛔ EVEN ON ⛔ ONE OF TWO AXES — ⛔ THE ⛔ ACQUISITION GATE IS ⛔ NOT AN ⛔ OPTIONAL PRELIMINARY. ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ ONLY THING PROTECTING THE ROW, AND IT MUST ⛔ RAISE, ⛔ NOT RETURN.** ✅ **⛔ THE SHIPPED SHAPE: ⛔ every capture computes ⛔ `max`, ⛔ whole-frame `mean` and ⛔ DISTINCT-COLOUR COUNT; ⛔ a frame failing ⛔ `max > 0 AND ncolours > 16` is ⛔ REFUSED, ⛔ NEVER SCORED. ⛔ MEASURED ACROSS 20 CAPTURES: ⛔ `max` 237–255, ⛔ ncolours 21,288–339,780.**
   - ⚖️ **⛔ THE ⛔ GENERAL FORM, ⛔ BEYOND PIXELS: ⛔ ANY GATE WHOSE ⛔ *"GOOD"* DIRECTION IS ⛔ *"TOWARD ZERO"*, ⛔ *"TOWARD SILENCE"*, ⛔ *"TOWARD EMPTY"* OR ⛔ *"TOWARD NEUTRAL"* HAS ITS ⛔ PASS CONDITION ⛔ POINTING AT ITS OWN ⛔ FAILURE MODE.** ⛔ **⛔ THIS PROJECT HAS PAID FOR IT ⛔ THREE TIMES NOW: ⛔ zero grep hits (⭐ `SC-§39`) · ⛔ an ⛔ EMPTY VERBOSE LOG READ AS A ⛔ ZERO OFFSET · ⛔ and now a ⛔ BLACK FRAME READ AS ⛔ PERFECT GREY.**
8. ⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — THE ⛔ TECHNIQUE THAT ⛔ ACTUALLY DISCHARGES THIS, ⛔ PROMOTED INTO THE LAW BECAUSE IT IS ⛔ STRICTLY STRONGER THAN THE DUTY I WROTE. ⛔ CLAUSE 3(a) ASKS FOR ⛔ ONE NON-BLANK FRAME. ⛔ ONE FRAME CANNOT DISTINGUISH A ⛔ LIVE ACQUIRER FROM A ⛔ FROZEN, ⛔ CACHED OR ⛔ STALE ONE.**
   - ✅ **⛔ WHAT ⭐ `TASK-1172` DID INSTEAD: ⛔ DROVE THE SUBJECT TO ⛔ FOUR ⛔ MUTUALLY DISTINGUISHABLE, ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY PREDICTED STATES AND ⛔ CONFIRMED THE CAPTURES MOVED ⛔ THE PREDICTED WAY ⛔ EACH TIME** (⛔ emissive red ⇒ `0.8656`/`28.67 %` · ⛔ emissive blue ⇒ `1.1376`/`12.10 %` · ⛔ base red ⇒ `0.6908`/`72.56 %` · ⛔ shipped ⇒ `0.8461`/`19.64 %`).
   - ⇒ 🚨 **⛔ THE ⛔ REASON THIS IS THE ⛔ RIGHT SHAPE, ⛔ IN ONE SENTENCE: ⛔ A ⛔ FROZEN FRAME RETURNS ⛔ IDENTICAL NUMBERS ⛔ FOUR TIMES. ⛔ IT DID NOT.** ⛔ **⛔ AND ⛔ *"PREDICTED"* IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING — ⛔ the direction is ⛔ WRITTEN DOWN ⛔ BEFORE the capture, ⛔ or you are ⛔ narrating whatever arrived (⭐ `SC-§83`).**
   - ✅ **⛔ THIS ⛔ ALSO DISCHARGES ⛔ CLAUSE 6 IN THE ⛔ SAME PASS — ⛔ four states ⛔ tracking means the ⛔ DRIVER reached the rendered object ⛔ AND the ⛔ ACQUIRER is live. ⛔ ONE EXPERIMENT, ⛔ TWO CONTROLS ⇒ ⛔ CHEAP ENOUGH TO ⛔ ACTUALLY GET RUN (⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c).**
   - ⚠️ **⛔ THE ⛔ HONEST RESIDUAL LIMIT, ⛔ STATED BY THE ROW ITSELF AND ⛔ KEPT HERE: ⛔ four-state tracking proves the acquirer ⛔ LIVE, ⛔ NOT ⛔ CORRECT. ⛔ IT WAS SHOWN A ⛔ KNOWN-DIFFERENT SCENE, ⛔ NEVER A ⛔ KNOWN REFERENCE IMAGE.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THAT IS ⛔ WHY THE ⛔ ANALYSER CONTROL ON ⛔ ARCHIVED FILES ⛔ REMAINS SEPARATELY OWED. ⛔ TWO CONTROLS, ⛔ TWO LEDGERS (⛔ cl. 2) — ⛔ NOW ⛔ THREE.**
9. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 9 — ⛔ THE ⛔ RESIDUAL LIMIT IN CLAUSE 8 ⛔ STOPPED BEING THEORETICAL ⛔ THE VERY NEXT ROW, AND IT COST A ⛔ WHOLE MEASUREMENT SET. ⛔ A ⛔ LIVE CONTROL PROVES THE APPARATUS ⛔ RESPONDS. ⛔ ONLY A ⛔ CALIBRATED CONTROL — ⛔ ONE WITH A ⛔ PUBLISHED EXPECTED VALUE — PROVES IT IS ⛔ POINTED AT THE RIGHT THING.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on ⭐ `TASK-1167` rev-2's ⛔ TWO SELF-REPORTED INSTRUMENT FAILURES — ⛔ BOTH CAUGHT BY ⛔ CONTROLS, ⛔ NEITHER BY REVIEW**)
   - 🚨 **⛔ FAILURE 1 — ⛔ THE ⛔ DRIVER POINTED SOMEWHERE ELSE: ⛔ `unreal.Rotator`'s ⛔ POSITIONAL order is ⛔ `(roll, pitch, yaw)`. ⛔ `unreal.Rotator(-11.5, 0, 0)` — ⛔ the obvious transcription of a vantage pinned as ⛔ *"pitch `-11.5°`"* — ⛔ PUT THE TILT IN ⛔ ROLL AND LEFT PITCH AT ⛔ `0`.** ⛔ **⛔ THE FRAME WAS ⛔ PERFECTLY ACQUIRED, ⛔ PERFECTLY ANALYSED, ⛔ NON-BLANK, ⛔ FULL OF STRUCTURE, ⛔ AND ⛔ OF THE WRONG VIEW. ⛔ CLAUSE 8's FOUR-STATE TRACKING WOULD HAVE ⛔ PASSED IT — ⛔ four wrong views ⛔ still differ from each other.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ CAUGHT ⛔ ONLY because the ⛔ NEGATIVE CONTROL (⛔ no-fog grass) ⛔ DISAGREED WITH ITS ⛔ PUBLISHED VALUE. ⛔ THE ⛔ NUMBER ON FILE IS WHAT ⛔ SAVED IT.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL ANSWERS ⛔ *"IS MY APPARATUS ALIVE?"*. ⛔ A ⛔ CALIBRATED CONTROL ANSWERS ⛔ *"IS MY APPARATUS ⛔ WHERE I THINK IT IS?"* — ⛔ AND ⛔ ONLY THE SECOND CATCHES A ⛔ MIS-POINTED RIG, ⛔ BECAUSE A MIS-POINTED RIG PRODUCES A ⛔ HEALTHY, ⛔ VARYING, ⛔ ENTIRELY FICTIONAL DATA SET.*** ⛔ **⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ FAILURE MODE ⭐ `SC-§114` CANNOT SEE: ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A NULL. ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ CONFIDENT NON-NULL.**
   - ✅ **⛔ THE DUTY: ⛔ AT LEAST ⛔ ONE CONTROL PER SESSION MUST HAVE A ⛔ PUBLISHED PRIOR NUMBER, AND THE ⛔ RUN REPORTS ⛔ MEASURED-vs-PUBLISHED. ⛔ FOR `FOG-§12` THOSE ARE ⛔ 🧑 HIS BEIGE `0.891`/`15.5` AND ⛔ NO-FOG GRASS `0.548`/`45.2` (⭐ `FOG-§12.5`). ⛔ A RUN THAT ⛔ CANNOT REPRODUCE ⛔ BOTH IS ⛔ NOT CALIBRATED AND ITS NUMBERS ⛔ DO NOT ENTER THE RECORD.**
   - 📌 **⛔ AND ⛔ YES, IT BELONGS IN THE ⛔ PINNED NAMES — ⛔ RULED, ⛔ NOT DEBATED: ⛔ the vantage pin ⛔ LITERALLY SAYS *"pitch `-11.5°`"*, ⛔ so ⛔ EVERY future art or engine row reproducing it will type ⛔ EXACTLY THE CALL THAT FAILED. ⛔ THE ⛔ KEYWORD FORM IS NOW ⛔ IN THE PIN ITSELF (⭐ `FOG-§12.5`, ⛔ actuation row): ⛔ `unreal.Rotator(pitch=-11.5, yaw=0.0, roll=0.0)`.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A LAW NOBODY READS AT THE MOMENT OF THE MISTAKE IS ⛔ DECORATION. ⛔ THE FIX GOES WHERE THE ⛔ HAND IS, ⛔ NOT WHERE THE ⛔ LESSON IS.***
   - 🚨 **⛔ FAILURE 2 — ⛔ THE ⛔ CENSUS TOOL LIED, ⛔ AND IT LIED IN THE ⛔ REASSURING DIRECTION: ⛔ `grep -c $'\r$'` REPORTED ⛔ **1,305 CR LINES ON A BLOB CONTAINING ⛔ ZERO CR BYTES**. ⛔ THE REPO STORES ⛔ LF; ⛔ CHECKOUT MATERIALISES ⛔ CRLF; ⛔ THE TOOL WAS REPORTING THE ⛔ WORKING FILE'S LINE ENDINGS AS THE ⛔ BLOB'S.** ⛔ **⛔ CAUGHT BY A ⛔ RAW BYTE CENSUS (`tr -cd`) ⛔ RUN AS A CONTROL.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ TEXT-MODE TOOL'S VIEW OF LINE ENDINGS IS ⛔ NOT A BYTE FACT. ⛔ A ⛔ REVERT IS PROVEN BY THE ⛔ BLOB OID, ⛔ NEVER BY A FILE HASH — ⛔ the ⛔ same shape as the ⛔ LFS oid-vs-size law (⭐ `SC-§68`), ⛔ one layer down.**
   - ⚖️ **⛔ WHY BOTH BELONG IN ⛔ ONE CLAUSE: ⛔ NEITHER WAS FOUND BY ⛔ READING THE SCRIPT. ⛔ BOTH WERE FOUND BY A ⛔ CONTROL DISAGREEING WITH A ⛔ KNOWN ANSWER.** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ REVIEW FINDS DEFECTS IN ⛔ LOGIC. ⛔ ONLY CONTROLS FIND DEFECTS IN ⛔ INSTRUMENTS — ⛔ AND AN AGENT'S ⛔ OWN TOOLING IS ⛔ THE LEAST-REVIEWED CODE IN THIS PROJECT.***

### SC-§111 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ BLIND-TARGET LAW — ⛔ WE COMPILE ⛔ EXACTLY ONE TARGET (`GitClaudeUnrealTestEditor`), SO ⛔ ANY DEFECT THAT EXISTS ⛔ ONLY IN THE ⛔ COOKED TARGET IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO ⛔ EVERY GATE WE RUN. ⛔ NOT *"UNDER-TESTED"* — ⛔ STRUCTURALLY ⛔ UNREACHABLE. ⛔ A `#if WITH_EDITOR` API CALLED FROM A `"Type": "Runtime"` MODULE COMPILES ⛔ GREEN, PASSES ⛔ QA, PASSES THE ⛔ SUITE, AND ⛔ FAILS THE COOK.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1166` BLOCKER-1 over ⭐ `TASK-1165`** · marker `SC-111-THE-TARGET-WE-NEVER-COMPILE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ HONEST WAY TO SAY THIS: ⛔ OUR PIPELINE'S ⛔ COMPILE GATE IS ⛔ NOT *"THE COMPILER"*. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ ONE CONFIGURATION OF IT, AND THERE IS AN ⛔ ENTIRE CLASS OF DEFECT ON THE OTHER SIDE OF THAT CHOICE THAT ⛔ NO AMOUNT OF CARE INSIDE THE GATE CAN SEE.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE INSTANCE, ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ NOT INFERRED.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1165` wrote `Visual->RerunConstructionScripts()` into `FogVolume.cpp`. ⛔ `AActor::RerunConstructionScripts()` is ⛔ `#if WITH_EDITOR`-ONLY in UE 5.8 (⛔ declaration `Actor.h:3415-3418` · ⛔ definition `ActorConstruction.cpp:253`), and `GitClaudeUnrealTest` is a ⛔ `"Type": "Runtime"` module (`.uproject:9`) with ⛔ ZERO `WITH_EDITOR` guards in that file.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ `Build.bat … GitClaudeUnrealTestEditor` — ⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY BUILD COMMAND THIS PROJECT HAS EVER RUN, ⛔ written into `CLAUDE.md` as ⛔ *"the build command"* — ⛔ COMPILES IT ⛔ GREEN. ⛔ THE COOK DOES NOT.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ SHIPPED SYMPTOM WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ WORSE THAN A BUILD BREAK: with a `#if WITH_EDITOR` guard added (⛔ the *"obvious"* fix), ⛔ the vendor rebuild — ⛔ **the whole mechanism** — ⛔ vanishes in the packaged game ⇒ 🧑 **HE WOULD SEE ⛔ GREY FOG IN THE EDITOR AND ⛔ BEIGE FOG IN THE ZIP**, with an `Error` log ⛔ only in the build ⛔ nobody tails.**
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT ⭐ `SHIP-§9`, ⛔ NOT ⭐ `SHIP-§9a`, AND ⛔ NOT ⭐ `SC-§107`, ⛔ THOUGH IT SITS BETWEEN ALL THREE:**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `SHIP-§9` — *"validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects"* — ⛔ PRESUPPOSES THE GATE ⛔ COULD BE SHOWN THE FAILURE. ⛔ HERE IT ⛔ CANNOT BE, ⛔ EVER, ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION. ⛔ You cannot red a compile gate with a defect the ⛔ compilation unit does not contain.**
