### SC-§110 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A LAW THAT ASSERTS A ⛔ CODE FACT MUST ⛔ NAME THE SYMBOL IT WAS ⛔ MEASURED FROM — AND IT IS ⛔ VOID WHERE THAT SYMBOL ⛔ MOVED. ⛔ LAW TEXT IS THE ⛔ ONE ARTEFACT IN THIS PIPELINE WITH ⛔ NO COMPILER, ⛔ NO TEST AND ⛔ NO REVIEWER — ⛔ SO A CODE CLAIM WRITTEN INTO IT ⛔ ROTS ⛔ SILENTLY AND IS ⛔ INHERITED BY ⛔ EVERY DOWNSTREAM READER AS ⛔ SETTLED.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on my own defect**: ⭐ `FOG-§12.4a` named `RefreshFogVisual()` as the host of the scale force; ⛔ **the scale force is in `SpawnFogVisual()` — ⛔ flagged by ⭐ `TASK-1165` under ⭐ `SC-§82`, ⛔ CONFIRMED by first-hand measurement in ⭐ `TASK-1166`** · marker `SC-110-A-LAW-THAT-CITES-CODE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ CODE HAS A ⛔ COMPILER. ⛔ THE ⛔ TESTS HAVE A ⛔ SUITE. ⛔ THE ⛔ BOARD HAS A ⛔ GATE. ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` HAS ⛔ ME — AND ⛔ I WAS THE ONE WHO WROTE THE WRONG SENTENCE.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE INSTANCE, ⛔ AND ITS ⛔ PROPAGATION PATH IS THE WHOLE POINT.** ⛔ **⭐ `FOG-§12.4a` said *"`AFogVolume::RefreshFogVisual()` ⛔ already forces the fog's SCALE after spawn"*. ⛔ MEASURED: ⛔ `SpawnFogVisual()` does (`FogVolume.cpp:755`); ⛔ `RefreshFogVisual()` (`:581`) has ⛔ no `SpawnActor`, ⛔ no null-check, ⛔ no `SetActorScale3D` — ⛔ it ⛔ CALLS the function that has them.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE SENTENCE WENT ⛔ LAW ⇒ ⛔ BOARD ROW (⭐ `TASK-1165` cl. 1) ⇒ ⛔ DISPATCH PROMPT ⇒ ⛔ IMPLEMENTER, ⛔ GAINING AUTHORITY AT EVERY HOP (⭐ `SC-§97`) ⛔ WHILE THE MEASUREMENT IT CAME FROM ⛔ NEVER EXISTED.**
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ WHY LAW TEXT IS THE ⛔ WORST PLACE FOR THIS, ⛔ NOT MERELY ANOTHER PLACE. ⛔ THREE PROPERTIES, ⛔ AND THEY COMPOUND:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ NOTHING VALIDATES IT. ⛔ A wrong identifier in `.cpp` ⛔ fails to compile. ⛔ A wrong identifier in a ⛔ test ⛔ reddens. ⛔ A wrong identifier in `CONVENTIONS.md` ⛔ renders as bold text ⛔ forever.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ IT IS ⛔ CITED, ⛔ NOT RESTATED — ⛔ by our own design (⛔ rows say *"Law: `FOG-§12.4a` — ⛔ cited, ⛔ NOT restated"*) ⇒ ⛔ readers ⛔ DELIBERATELY do not re-derive it. ⛔ THE CITATION DISCIPLINE THAT KEEPS ROWS SHORT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT MAKES A ROTTEN LAW ⛔ UNCHALLENGED.**
   - 🚨 **(c) ⛔ IT OUTRANKS THE READER'S OWN EYE. ⛔ An implementer who opens `RefreshFogVisual()`, ⛔ sees no scale force, and ⛔ concludes *"I must be looking at the wrong build"* is ⛔ BEHAVING CORRECTLY GIVEN THE LAW'S STANDING. ⛔ THAT IS THE FAILURE MODE ⛔ WE ARE BUYING WITH EVERY UNMEASURED CODE CLAIM.**
3. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTIES. ⛔ ON ⛔ ME, ⛔ PRIMARILY, ⛔ BECAUSE I OWN THIS FILE:**
   - 🚨 **(a) ⛔ A LAW SENTENCE THAT ASSERTS ⛔ WHAT CODE DOES ⛔ MUST NAME THE ⛔ SYMBOL IT WAS MEASURED FROM — ⛔ `Class::Function()`, ⛔ a member, ⛔ a literal, ⛔ a macro. ⛔ *"the reconciler"*, ⛔ *"that function"*, ⛔ *"the spawn path"* are ⛔ NOT symbols and ⛔ do not discharge this.**
   - 🚨 **(b) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ TEETH: ⛔ WHERE THE ⛔ NAMED SYMBOL ⛔ DOES NOT CARRY THE ASSERTED BEHAVIOUR, ⛔ THE SENTENCE IS ⛔ VOID — ⛔ NOT *"approximately right"*, ⛔ not *"close enough"*. ⛔ ITS READER ⛔ MUST RE-DERIVE FROM THE CODE AND ⛔ ROUTE THE DIVERGENCE TO ME (⭐ `SC-§82`) ⛔ RATHER THAN OBEYING IT.** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT: ⭐ `TASK-1165` did ⛔ precisely this, ⛔ and ⭐ `TASK-1166` ⛔ re-measured it ⛔ independently ⛔ rather than relaying it (⭐ `SC-§40` cl. 1).**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ THE ⛔ CARRY-THE-LANDMARK RULE, ⛔ WHICH IS WHAT ⛔ ACTUALLY SAVED THIS ONE: ⛔ a code-citing law or row carries ⛔ BOTH the ⛔ SYMBOL ⛔ AND a ⛔ SECOND, INDEPENDENT LANDMARK — ⛔ an adjacent statement, a ⛔ log string, a ⛔ distinctive expression. ⛔ Two coordinates ⛔ disagreeing is ⛔ DETECTABLE; ⛔ one coordinate being wrong is ⛔ NOT.** ⛔ **⛔ Here `"beside `Spawned->SetActorScale3D`"` ⛔ contradicted `RefreshFogVisual()` and the ⛔ contradiction is ⛔ the entire reason the write landed in the right function.**
   - ⛔ **(d) ⛔ AND WHAT IS ⛔ BANNED, ⛔ BY THE FILE'S OWN PRECEDENT: ⛔ **LINE NUMBERS AS THE PIN.** ⭐ `FOG-§12.5` already ruled it — ⛔ *"a pin is a NAME; a line number in a pin is a SECOND, SILENTLY-ROTTING CLAIM"* — ⛔ after that very entry ⛔ rotted a line number ⛔ inside the sentence explaining why line numbers rot. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A LINE NUMBER MAY APPEAR ⛔ ONLY AS A ⛔ DATED FIND-AID BESIDE A NAME, ⛔ NEVER AS THE IDENTITY.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ SCOPE FENCE, ⛔ SO THIS DOES NOT BECOME AN ⛔ AUDIT OF THE WHOLE FILE (⛔ which would be skipped, ⭐ `SC-§109` cl. 4c): ⛔ THIS BINDS ⛔ NEW AND ⛔ AMENDED LAW TEXT, ⛔ AND ⛔ ANY EXISTING SENTENCE THE MOMENT SOMEONE ⛔ CITES IT INTO A ROW.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ RE-DERIVATION IS OWED AT ⛔ CITATION TIME, ⛔ BY THE CITER, ⛔ NOT ON A SWEEP. ⛔ A law nobody is currently acting on ⛔ cannot currently mislead anybody.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SC-§82` (⛔ the gate routes law findings to me and ⛔ does not edit the file — ⛔ **⛔ THAT ROUTE IS WHAT MADE THIS FIXABLE, and ⛔ this section is the ⛔ other half of it: ⛔ a route in ⛔ obliges a ⛔ correction out**) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim — ⛔ this is ⛔ a prescribed ⛔ LOCATION is a claim) · ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ authority gained per hop) · ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ the row is the stale thing — ⛔ **⛔ here the ⛔ LAW was**) · ⭐ `SC-§99` (⛔ a name census cannot catch a site that never mentions the name) · ⭐ `SC-§63` (⛔ a pin written against today's call graph) · ⭐ `FOG-§12.5` (⛔ the ⛔ line-number ruling) · ⭐ `SC-§106` (⛔ its ⛔ sibling on ⛔ PATHS: ⛔ a path that resolves to nothing ⛔ answers with silence — ⛔ **⛔ a SYMBOL that resolves to the wrong function does ⛔ the same, ⛔ with a person in the loop instead of `git`**).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ EVERY OTHER ARTEFACT HERE IS ⛔ CHECKED BY A MACHINE. ⛔ THE LAW IS CHECKED BY ⛔ WHOEVER READS IT — ⛔ AND WE HAVE SPENT MONTHS TEACHING THEM ⛔ NOT TO.***

### SC-§109 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A SHIPPED STRING LITERAL MAY TELL ITS READER ⛔ WHAT TO OBSERVE. ⛔ IT MAY NOT TELL HIM ⛔ WHAT TO CONCLUDE. ⛔ AN INSTRUCTION COMPILED INTO A BINARY IS A ⛔ VERDICT PRE-REGISTERED AT AUTHORING TIME, ⛔ DELIVERED IN THE SAME BREATH AS THE EVIDENCE, AND ⛔ UNFIXABLE WITHOUT A COMPILE.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on a ruling requested directly**; ⛔ **bought on `FogVolume.cpp`'s failure branch — *"⛔ Report this as a FLOOR failure, ⛔ never as 'no fog'"* — which shipped, ⛔ survived three gates, and was ⛔ MEASURED FALSE by ⭐ `TASK-1160` before ⭐ `TASK-1163` reversed it** · marker `SC-109-OBSERVE-NOT-CONCLUDE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ A FALSE ⛔ STATEMENT MISLEADS. ⛔ A FALSE ⛔ INSTRUCTION ⛔ GETS ⛔ ACTED ON — ⛔ AND THIS ONE FIRED ⛔ ONLY ON THE ⛔ FAILURE BRANCH, ⛔ i.e. ⛔ EXACTLY WHEN THE DEFECT WAS ⛔ LIVE.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE ⛔ LINE, ⛔ AND WHY IT IS A ⛔ CATEGORY ERROR ⛔ RATHER THAN A WORDING ONE.** ⛔ **The log said, on the branch that fires when the render floor ⛔ DID NOT TAKE: *"⛔ Report this as a FLOOR failure, ⛔ never as 'no fog'."* ⇒ ⛔ IT TOLD THE OPERATOR TO ⛔ MISCLASSIFY THE EXPLOIT ⛔ AT THE MOMENT OF DETECTION.** ⛔ **⛔ THE AUTHOR COULD ⛔ NOT HAVE KNOWN IT WAS WRONG — ⛔ the measurement that refuted it (⭐ `TASK-1160`) was taken ⛔ WEEKS LATER. ⛔ THAT IS THE POINT: ⛔ THE LITERAL FROZE A ⛔ BELIEF AND SHIPPED IT AS A ⛔ DIRECTIVE.**
2. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — THE ⛔ LINE THAT DIVIDES THE TWO, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ SHARP ENOUGH TO APPLY AT ⛔ REVIEW SPEED:**
   - ✅ **⛔ ALLOWED — ⛔ WHAT TO ⛔ OBSERVE: *"⛔ LOOK AT THE CARD'S WASH"* · *"⛔ if the `'%s' ACHIEVED %d` line above is not the floor's value, X is at risk too"* · *"⛔ this value was read back, this one was requested"* · ⛔ any ⛔ pointer to a ⛔ second observable.** ⛔ **⛔ That is an ⛔ INSTRUMENT ⛔ EXTENDING ITSELF, and it is ⛔ exactly what ⭐ `SC-§98` asks of instruments.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ FORBIDDEN — ⛔ WHAT TO ⛔ CONCLUDE: *"⛔ report this as X"* · *"⛔ never as Y"* · *"⛔ do not read this line as Z"* · *"⛔ this does NOT mean W"* ⛔ where `W` is a ⛔ DOMAIN CLAIM rather than a ⛔ statement about the ⛔ variable just printed.**
   - ⚖️ ***⛔ THE TEST IN ONE QUESTION: ⛔ COULD A ⛔ FUTURE MEASUREMENT MAKE THIS SENTENCE ⛔ FALSE? ⛔ IF YES, IT IS A ⛔ CONCLUSION AND IT DOES ⛔ NOT BELONG IN A COMPILED LITERAL. ⛔ AN ⛔ OBSERVATION INSTRUCTION SURVIVES ⛔ ANY MEASUREMENT, ⛔ BECAUSE IT ⛔ ASKS FOR ONE.***
3. ⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THREE PROPERTIES THAT MAKE THIS ⛔ WORSE THAN THE SAME SENTENCE IN A ⛔ COMMENT OR A ⛔ DOC, ⛔ AND THEY COMPOUND:** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ IT ARRIVES ⛔ SIMULTANEOUSLY WITH THE EVIDENCE ⇒ it ⛔ outranks the reader's own eye by ⛔ timing alone · ⛔ (b) ⛔ IT CANNOT BE CORRECTED WITHOUT A ⛔ COMPILE AND A ⛔ COMMIT ⇒ the ⛔ cheapest fix is ⛔ never cheap · ⛔ (c) ⛔ IT IS READ BY SOMEONE ⛔ MID-INCIDENT, ⛔ i.e. by the ⛔ least sceptical reader this project ever has.**
4. ✅ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ DUTIES, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ REMEDY IS ⛔ DELIBERATELY ⛔ CHEAP, ⛔ BECAUSE AN EXPENSIVE ONE WOULD JUSTIFY ⛔ SKIPPING IT:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ AUTHOR: ⛔ prefer ⛔ NAMING THE SECOND OBSERVABLE over ⛔ naming the verdict. ⛔ *"Check X before you rule Y out"* ⛔ does the same work and ⛔ cannot go stale.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ GATE: ⛔ read every ⛔ NEW compiled literal for the ⛔ conclusion shape. ⛔ An ⛔ UNDECLARED one is a ⛔ WARN, ⛔ never a blocker on its own.**
   - 🚨 **(c) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ PROPORTIONALITY RULE, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ HALF THIS SECTION: ⛔ A ⛔ CONCLUSION LITERAL THAT IS ⛔ CURRENTLY TRUE ⛔ AND ⛔ CONSERVATIVE IN DIRECTION (⛔ it ⛔ WIDENS what the reader considers rather than ⛔ narrowing it) IS ⛔ NOT A DEFECT AND ⛔ NEVER JUSTIFIES A COMPILE OF ITS OWN.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IT IS ⛔ RIDDEN ON THE ⛔ NEXT COMPILE THAT FILE WAS ⛔ ALREADY GOING TO TAKE.** ⚖️ *⛔ A LAW THAT DEMANDS A BUILD FOR A SENTENCE WILL BE ⛔ IGNORED, AND AN IGNORED LAW PROTECTS ⛔ NOTHING.*
   - ⚖️🚨⭐⭐ **(d) ⛔ RULED INSTANCE 2026-09-09 — ⭐ **W5**, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ FIRST TIME THIS LAW HAS BEEN APPLIED TO A LITERAL THAT IS ⛔ FALSE ABOUT ITS ⛔ OWN BEHAVIOUR RATHER THAN ABOUT THE ⛔ WORLD.** ⛔ **`FogVolume.cpp:99-104`'s shared warning says the state actor ⛔ *"is spawned into the map"*. ⛔ `ExecClearFog` (`:166`) uses ⛔ `Find` and ⛔ **CANNOT SPAWN** — and ⭐ `TASK-1175` ⛔ MEASURED it printing on exactly that path. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ UPGRADED FROM A ⛔ CODE READING TO AN ⛔ OBSERVATION; ⛔ it is ⛔ no longer arguable.**
     - ⚖️ **⛔ THE RULING: ⛔ **⛔ IT IS A ⛔ DEFECT, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A cl. 2 CONCLUSION LITERAL.** ⛔ It does ⛔ not tell the reader ⛔ what to conclude; ⛔ it ⛔ MISDESCRIBES WHAT THE PROGRAM JUST DID. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ FALSE STATEMENT, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ FALSE INSTRUCTION** — ⛔ the ⛔ lesser of this law's two evils, ⛔ and it is ⛔ still a defect ⛔ because a ⛔ literal about the program's ⛔ own behaviour is the ⛔ one class the ⛔ program could always have gotten right.**
     - ✅ **⛔ AND cl. 4(c)'s ⛔ PROPORTIONALITY ⛔ APPLIES ⛔ UNCHANGED: its ⛔ direction is ⛔ CONSERVATIVE (⛔ it ⛔ over-warns about dirtying the map — ⛔ it ⛔ WIDENS caution rather than ⛔ narrowing it) ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IT NEVER JUSTIFIES A COMPILE OF ITS OWN. ⛔ IT RIDES THE ⛔ NEXT COMPILE `FogVolume.cpp` WAS ⛔ ALREADY GOING TO TAKE** — ⛔ boarded as ⭐ `TASK-1178`, ⛔ NOT as an emergency.**
     - ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ REMEDY SHAPE, ⛔ SO THE FIXER DOES ⛔ NOT SUBSTITUTE ONE FALSE SENTENCE FOR ANOTHER: ⛔ the string is ⛔ SHARED across `Raise` / `Clear` / `Status`, and it is ⛔ TRUE on ⛔ exactly one of them. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ STATE THE ⛔ PREDICATE, ⛔ NOT THE EVENT** — ⛔ the warning names ⛔ what this command ⛔ MAY do, or the ⛔ spawn claim moves onto the ⛔ Raise path ⛔ alone. ⛔ ⛔ SAME REMEDY SHAPE AS ⭐ W3/W4 (⛔ *"SEVEN Error sites"* when there are ⛔ six; ⛔ *"Nine files"* when there are ⛔ ≥15): ⛔ **⛔ A COMMENT THAT STATES A ⛔ COUNT OR AN ⛔ EVENT GOES STALE; ⛔ A COMMENT THAT STATES A ⛔ PREDICATE DOES NOT.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ a citation gains authority at every hop — ⛔ **a compiled literal is the ⛔ LAST HOP, and it ⛔ gains the authority of the ⛔ MACHINE**) · ⭐ `SC-§98` (⛔ an instrument with an off switch — ⛔ this is an instrument with an ⛔ OPINION) · ⭐ `SC-§105` cl. 4(a) (⛔ `SHIP-§9` applied to an ⛔ INSTRUCTION instead of a gate — ⛔ same move, ⛔ different artefact) · ⭐ `FOG-§12.7` (⛔ the ⛔ compiled-literal carve-out that made these ⛔ boardable at all) · ⭐ `SC-§107` (⛔ below — ⛔ **⛔ the two met on the ⛔ SAME BRANCH: the line told you ⛔ how to classify a result the ⛔ repro could no longer produce**).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ AN INSTRUMENT'S JOB IS TO ⛔ NARROW THE POSSIBILITIES, ⛔ NOT TO ⛔ PICK ONE. ⛔ THE MOMENT IT PICKS, IT HAS ⛔ STOPPED MEASURING AND ⛔ STARTED ASSERTING — ⛔ IN A VOICE THE READER TRUSTS ⛔ MORE THAN HIS OWN.***

### SC-§108 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A DECLARED `.uasset` HASH IS A ⛔ TIMESTAMP, ⛔ NOT A PROPERTY — THE ⛔ SECOND ROW TO TOUCH AN ASSET ⛔ SILENTLY VOIDS THE FIRST ROW'S EVIDENCE, AND AN ⛔ EXPERIMENTAL WRITE THAT WAS ⛔ REVERTED IS ⛔ STILL A WRITE.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager, ⛔ **on a ruling requested by the build-master from ⭐ `TASK-1158`'s integration check**; ⛔ **measured: `handoffs/TASK-1152-artist.md` §4 records `dbea1466…` / 38,335 B and ⛔ DISK CARRIED `6cf85fa9…f342d5` / 38,225 B** · marker `SC-108-HASH-BELONGS-TO-THE-ASSET`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE FIRST ROW'S HASH WAS ⛔ HONEST WHEN IT WAS WRITTEN AND ⛔ WRONG BY THE TIME ANYONE READ IT — ⛔ AND ⛔ NOTHING IN EITHER ROW SAID SO.**

1. ⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE INSTANCE, ⛔ IN ORDER.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1152` edited `BP_SiegeFog.uasset`, ⛔ saved, ⛔ declared its hash. ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1154` then ran on the ⛔ SAME asset in the ⛔ SAME session (⛔ correctly — ⛔ their `parallel-safe:` fences ⛔ REQUIRED that serialisation and it ⛔ HELD), ⛔ set the CDO `material` slot as an ⛔ EXPERIMENT, ⛔ found it ⛔ ignored, ⛔ reverted it to `None` and ⛔ re-saved.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ END STATE IS ⛔ CORRECT — ⛔ confirmed by ⭐ `TASK-1158` reading the ⛔ CDO — ⛔ BUT THE ⛔ FIRST ROW'S HASH NO LONGER MATCHES ⛔ ANYTHING, AND THE ⛔ ONLY REASON WE KNOW THE ASSET IS CLEAN IS THAT SOMEONE ⛔ READ ITS STATE INSTEAD OF ⛔ TRUSTING THE REVERT.**
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE RULING, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ TWO SENTENCES: ⛔ THE HASH BELONGS TO THE ⛔ ASSET, ⛔ NOT TO THE ROW. ⛔ A FINISHED ROW ⛔ CANNOT RE-DERIVE IT — ⛔ SO THE DUTY FALLS ⛔ FORWARD, ⛔ NEVER BACKWARD.**
   - ✅ **(a) ⛔ THE ⛔ SECOND ROW OWES THE ⛔ RE-DERIVATION. ⛔ When you touch an asset a ⛔ prior row in the same batch already declared, you ⛔ MUST: ⛔ (i) ⛔ NAME that row and ⛔ quote its declared hash · ⛔ (ii) ⛔ PUBLISH the ⛔ NEW hash · ⛔ (iii) ⛔ STATE whether your ⛔ NET contribution to that asset is ⛔ ZERO or ⛔ NON-ZERO.** ⛔ **⛔ *"I reverted it"* is ⛔ (iii) ⛔ claimed; ⛔ (ii) is ⛔ (iii) ⛔ evidenced.**
   - ✅ **(b) ⛔ THE ⛔ HOST VERIFIES AGAINST THE ⛔ LAST DECLARED HASH, ⛔ NEVER THE FIRST — ⛔ and where the row and ⛔ DISK disagree, ⛔ DISK WINS and the ⛔ ROW IS STALE (⭐ `SC-§91`, ⭐ `SC-§106`).** ⛔ **⛔ A hash mismatch against a ⛔ first-row declaration is ⛔ NOT automatically a finding — ⛔ but an ⛔ UNEXPLAINED one is, ⛔ and the host must ⛔ NAME which row moved it.**
   - 🚨 **(c) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT THAT ACTUALLY SETTLES IT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ NOT THE HASH: ⛔ READ THE ⛔ STATE (⛔ the CDO, ⛔ `get_rows`, ⛔ the property that matters). ⭐ `SC-§68` ⛔ ALREADY SAYS `sha256` ⛔ PLUS a ⛔ read-back is the ⛔ ONLY pair that discriminates — ⛔ THIS IS ⛔ WHY: ⛔ a hash proves ⛔ SOMETHING CHANGED; ⛔ only a ⛔ state read proves ⛔ WHAT IT NOW IS.** ✅ **⭐ `TASK-1158` did ⛔ exactly this and it is ⛔ recorded as a ⛔ POSITIVE exhibit.**
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE ⛔ SECOND RULING, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONE WITH TEETH: ⛔ AN ⛔ EXPERIMENTAL WRITE ON A ⛔ LIVE SHARED ASSET IS A ⛔ WRITE. ⛔ *"I put it back"* IS A ⛔ CLAIM, AND IT IS ⛔ ONE FORGOTTEN REVERT AWAY FROM ⛔ SHIPPING AN EXPERIMENT.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ SO: ⛔ (i) ⛔ DECLARE the experiment ⛔ BEFORE you write it (⛔ in the handoff, ⛔ naming the field) · ⛔ (ii) ⛔ RESTORE and ⛔ RE-SAVE · ⛔ (iii) ⛔ RE-DERIVE and ⛔ PUBLISH the hash · ⛔ (iv) ⛔ READ BACK the restored field ⛔ BY NAME.** ⛔ **⛔ All four, ⛔ or ⛔ do the experiment on a ⛔ DUPLICATE.**
   - ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ NEAR-MISS IS THE WHOLE POINT: ⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY THING BETWEEN ⛔ THIS BATCH AND A ⛔ SHIPPED EXPERIMENT WAS THE ⛔ AGENT REMEMBERING. ⛔ A PROCESS THAT WORKS BECAUSE SOMEONE ⛔ REMEMBERED IS ⛔ NOT A PROCESS.***
4. ✅ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ AND WHAT THIS ⛔ DOES NOT CHANGE, ⛔ SO NOBODY OVER-CORRECTS: ⛔ SERIALISATION WAS ⛔ ALREADY REQUIRED AND IT ⛔ WORKED.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1152`/`1154`'s `parallel-safe:` fields already read *"⛔ NO vs each other · ⛔ ONE editor session · ⛔ uniformity FIRST"*, and ⛔ that is exactly what happened.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE DEFECT WAS ⛔ NEVER THE ORDERING. ⛔ IT WAS THAT ⛔ NOTHING IN THE ⛔ SECOND ROW'S DUTIES SAID *"⛔ AND NOW THE FIRST ROW'S EVIDENCE IS ⛔ STALE"*.** ⛔ **⛔ TWO ROWS ON ONE ASSET STAY ⛔ TWO ROWS AND ⛔ TWO HANDOFFS — ⛔ they are ⛔ two deliverables 🧑 he judges ⛔ separately — ⛔ but the ⛔ ASSET has ⛔ ONE current hash and it belongs to ⛔ whoever wrote last.**
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SC-§68` (⛔ hash ⛔ NEVER size — ⛔ and note the instance ⛔ moved by ⛔ 110 BYTES, ⛔ which a size check would have called a ⛔ real change and a ⛔ hash calls ⛔ exactly what it is) · ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ the row is the stale thing) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ the property echo — ⛔ cl. 2(c) is its remedy) · ⭐ `SC-§103` (⛔ what a ⛔ commit owes ⛔ N rows; ⛔ this is what ⛔ N rows owe ⛔ ONE artefact) · ⭐ `SC-§106` (⛔ disk wins over the plan).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ EVERY OTHER FIELD ON A ROW DESCRIBES ⛔ WHAT THAT ROW DID. ⛔ A HASH DESCRIBES ⛔ THE WORLD — AND THE ⛔ WORLD KEEPS MOVING AFTER THE ROW STOPS.***

### SC-§107 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A FIX CAN ⛔ DISARM THE TEST THAT ⛔ PROVED THE DEFECT. ⛔ RE-RUNNING THE ⛔ ORIGINAL REPRO ⛔ AFTER THE FIX READS ⛔ *"NO BUG"* ⛔ WHETHER OR NOT THE FIX WORKS — ⛔ SO AN ACCEPTANCE TEST THAT IS ⛔ ONLY THE OLD REPRO IS ⛔ NOT A TEST, IT IS A ⛔ CEREMONY.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager; ⛔ **caught ⛔ BEFORE it fired, ⛔ twice: ⭐ `TASK-1160` named it as the reason it had to run ⛔ FIRST, and it ⛔ still reached 🧑 Jonathan's own acceptance row ⭐ `TASK-1159` cl. (1) ⛔ needing a ⛔ third amendment** · marker `SC-107-THE-FIX-DISARMS-THE-REPRO`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ WE WOULD HAVE HANDED HIM AN EXPERIMENT WHOSE RESULT ⛔ OUR OWN FIX ⛔ PREDETERMINED — AND ⛔ NOTHING ON HIS SCREEN WOULD HAVE TOLD HIM.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MECHANISM, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ GENERAL, ⛔ NOT A FOG FACT.** ⛔ **The repro was *"⛔ set Shadows to Low ⇒ the fog disappears"*, which worked because `sg.ShadowQuality 0` drove `r.VolumetricFog` to `0`. ⭐ `TASK-1149` shipped a ⛔ FLOOR that ⛔ PINS that cvar while a fog card is active.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE REPRO'S ⛔ FIRST STEP ⛔ NO LONGER HAS ITS ⛔ EFFECT. ⛔ THE TEST NOW ⛔ CANNOT GO RED — ⛔ not because the bug is gone, but because the ⛔ LEVER IS GONE.** ⛔ **⛔ AND A TEST THAT CANNOT GO RED ⛔ REPORTS ⛔ PASS.**
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT ⭐ `SHIP-§9`, ⛔ THOUGH IT IS ITS ⛔ CLOSEST RELATIVE. ⭐ `SHIP-§9` SAYS ⛔ VALIDATE A GATE AGAINST THE ⛔ FAILURE IT DETECTS. ⛔ THIS SAYS THE ⛔ FAILURE ITSELF CAN BECOME ⛔ UNREACHABLE — ⛔ BY YOUR OWN HAND, ⛔ ON PURPOSE, ⛔ AS THE ⛔ DELIVERABLE.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ `SHIP-§9` is about an instrument that was ⛔ never shown a red. ⛔ THIS is about an instrument whose red was ⛔ DELIBERATELY DESTROYED — ⛔ and the destroyer is ⛔ the thing you are trying to validate.**
3. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE DUTIES. ⛔ AN ACCEPTANCE TEST FOR A FIX MUST HAVE ⛔ AT LEAST ONE OF THESE, ⛔ AND IT MUST SAY ⛔ WHICH:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ THE ⛔ BEFORE-PICTURE, ⛔ TAKEN ⛔ BEFORE THE FIX SHIPS. ⛔ This is the ⛔ cheapest and it is ⛔ perishable — ⛔ once the fix lands it can ⛔ never be taken again.** ✅ **⭐ `TASK-1160` is the ⛔ worked example: it was ⛔ boarded, ⛔ sequenced ahead of the fix ⛔ for this reason in writing, and it ⛔ ran in time.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL THAT ⛔ DEFEATS THE FIX ⛔ DELIBERATELY — ⛔ a console override, a ⛔ test-only seam, a ⛔ config that outranks the pin. ⛔ THE FIX MUST BE ⛔ DEFEATABLE ⛔ ON PURPOSE, ⛔ OR ITS ACCEPTANCE IS ⛔ UNFALSIFIABLE.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ DESIGN THE ⛔ OVERRIDE INTO THE FIX. ⛔ Here `ECVF_SetByConsole` ⛔ outranks `SetByCode` ⛔ by engine design and that is ⛔ luck, ⛔ not design — ⛔ do not count on luck twice.**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ A ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENT ENTIRELY — ⛔ one that reads the ⛔ MECHANISM (⛔ the cvar's ⛔ achieved value, the ⛔ pin's source) rather than the ⛔ SYMPTOM (⛔ the picture).**
4. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ REPORTING RULE, ⛔ BECAUSE THIS DEFECT'S ⛔ WHOLE DANGER IS THAT IT ⛔ PRODUCES A ⛔ CONFIDENT GREEN: ⛔ A RUN OF THE ⛔ OLD REPRO ⛔ POST-FIX, ⛔ WITHOUT (a), (b) OR (c), IS ⛔ *"NOT MEASURED"*. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NEVER A PASS. ⛔ WRITE THOSE WORDS.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ CONVERSE, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ FREE: ⛔ IF THE ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL ⛔ CHANGES NOTHING, ⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ FINDING, ⛔ NOT A PASS — ⛔ it means the ⛔ override did not take, the ⛔ precondition was not set up, ⛔ or the ⛔ DIAGNOSIS WAS WRONG AND THE FIX PROTECTS ⛔ NOTHING.**
5. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ THE ⛔ CONTAMINATION COROLLARY, ⛔ MEASURED ON THIS SAME LANE AND ⛔ NASTIER THAN THE MAIN LAW: ⛔ A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL CAN ⛔ POISON THE PROCESS IT RAN IN. ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1160`'s ⛔ RESTORE WRITE (⛔ typing `r.VolumetricFog 1` back) left the cvar at ⛔ `ECVF_SetByConsole`, which ⛔ OUTRANKS SCALABILITY ⛔ FOR THE LIFE OF THE PROCESS ⇒ ⛔ `sg.ShadowQuality 0` was ⛔ VERIFIED to leave it at `1` afterwards.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ANYONE RE-TESTING IN ⛔ THAT SESSION GETS A ⛔ FALSE ALL-CLEAR ⛔ FROM THE CLEANUP, ⛔ NOT FROM THE FIX.** ✅ **⛔ THE DUTY: ⛔ AFTER ANY DELIBERATE OVERRIDE, ⛔ RESTART THE PROCESS — ⛔ a ⛔ FRESH PROCESS, ⛔ NOT A RELOADED LEVEL — ⛔ AND ⛔ REPORT THE ⛔ PID. ⛔ *"I restarted"* is a ⛔ CLAIM; ⛔ the ⛔ PID is the ⛔ EVIDENCE.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP. ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ cl. 2 — ⛔ the parent) · ⭐ `SC-§99` (⛔ a parser that has ⛔ only ever seen green) · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ an assertion ⛔ nothing can redden) · ⭐ `SC-§96` (⛔ control the ⛔ READER before trusting a zero — ⛔ **this is ⛔ control the ⛔ WORLD before trusting a green**) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ the echo) · ⭐ `SC-§109` (⛔ above — ⛔ they met on the ⛔ same branch).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ THE MOMENT YOU ⛔ SHIP A FIX, THE ⛔ WORLD IN WHICH THE BUG WAS ⛔ OBSERVABLE ⛔ STOPS EXISTING. ⛔ IF YOU DID NOT ⛔ PHOTOGRAPH IT ⛔ FIRST, OR KEEP A ⛔ DOOR BACK INTO IT, ⛔ YOUR ACCEPTANCE TEST IS ⛔ ADMIRING THE FIX — ⛔ NOT ⛔ TESTING IT.***

### SC-§106 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ PATHSPEC COMMIT AGAINST A PATH THAT ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST STAGES ⛔ NOTHING — ⛔ SILENTLY, ⛔ AND `git show --stat` READS ⛔ GREEN FOR EVERY OTHER PATH IN THE LIST** (added 2026-09-08; ⛔ **flagged ⛔ TWICE on 2026-09-07 (⭐ `TASK-1116`/`1119`/`1123`), ⛔ left unrepaired on ⛔ four sibling sites, and it ⛔ NEARLY COST ⭐ `TASK-1149` ITS EVIDENCE**)

- ⛔ **THE MECHANISM, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT A TYPO CLASS: `git add <missing>` ⛔ errors, ⛔ but a ⛔ multi-path host command that ⛔ tolerates one miss — ⛔ or a glob, ⛔ or a list assembled from a ⛔ row rather than from ⛔ disk — ⛔ produces a commit that is ⛔ CORRECT IN EVERY VISIBLE RESPECT and ⛔ MISSING THE ONE ARTEFACT THE COMMIT EXISTS TO CARRY.** ⚖️ ***⛔ THE VERIFICATION STEP THAT CATCHES ⛔ EVERYTHING ELSE — ⛔ "VERIFY THE COMMIT, NOT THE INDEX" — ⛔ CANNOT SEE AN ⛔ ABSENCE IT WAS NEVER TOLD TO EXPECT.***
- ✅ **THE NAMING RULE THAT ⛔ PREVENTS THE COMMON CASE: ⛔ A GATE WRITES ⛔ ITS OWN ROW ID.** ⛔ **`qa/TASK-<gate row>.md`, ⛔ NEVER `qa/TASK-<reviewed row>.md`** (⛔ house precedent `qa/TASK-1103.md`). ⛔ **The reviewed row is named ⛔ INSIDE the report, ⛔ in its `subject:` line — ⛔ that is where the join lives.**
- 🚨 **THE ⛔ HOST'S DUTY, ⛔ NON-OPTIONAL: ⛔ RESOLVE EVERY PATHSPEC ⛔ AGAINST DISK ⛔ BEFORE COMMITTING, ⛔ and ⛔ NAME any that ⛔ do not resolve.** ⛔ **A `- names:` / `STAGES:` list is a ⛔ PLAN written before the work; ⛔ the work may have ⛔ correctly written somewhere else. ⛔ Where they disagree, ⛔ DISK WINS and the ⛔ ROW IS THE THING THAT IS STALE** (⭐ `SC-§91`).
- ⛔ **THE ⛔ MANAGER'S duty on the other side: ⛔ when a gate is observed writing a ⛔ different path from its row, ⛔ REPAIR ⛔ EVERY SIBLING ROW IN THE SAME PASS.** ⚖️ *⛔ A CORRECTION APPLIED TO THE ⛔ THREE ROWS SOMEONE HAPPENED TO READ, AND ⛔ NOT TO THE ⛔ FOUR NOBODY OPENED, IS A ⛔ TRAP WITH BETTER DOCUMENTATION.*
- 📌 **Instance record: ⭐ `TASK-1116`/`1119`/`1123` corrected 2026-09-07 · ⭐ `TASK-1114`/`1121` and ⭐ `TASK-1148` (`qa/TASK-1147.md` → `qa/TASK-1148.md`, ⛔ plus its ⛔ two downstream citations in ⭐ `TASK-1149`) corrected 2026-09-08.**
- 🚨📌 **INSTANCE RECORD, ⛔ SECOND PASS 2026-09-08 — ⛔ THE SWEEP THIS SECTION ⛔ DEMANDED WAS ⛔ NOT ACTUALLY COMPLETE WHEN I WROTE IT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ ITSELF THE FOURTH-BULLET FAILURE:**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1166` (⛔ gate over ⭐ `TASK-1165`) — ⛔ row said `qa/TASK-1165.md`; ⛔ the ⛔ dispatch said `qa/TASK-1166-report.md`; ⛔ the gate ⛔ WROTE the dispatched path and ⛔ said so ⛔ in its own status. ⛔ ONE verdict file exists, at ⛔ `qa/TASK-1166-report.md`. ⛔ ROW + ⭐ `TASK-1167`'s `STAGES:` ⛔ RECONCILED TO ⛔ DISK 2026-09-08.** ⚠️ **⛔ NOTE THE SUFFIX: ⛔ disk carries `-report`, which this section's naming rule does ⛔ NOT prescribe ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ DISK STILL WINS** (⛔ the bullet above), ⛔ and the ⛔ rule binds the ⛔ ID, ⛔ not the suffix. ⛔ DO NOT *"tidy"* a live file's name to match a law.**
  - 🚨 **⭐ `TASK-1144` (⛔ gate over ⭐ `TASK-1143`) + ⭐ `TASK-1145`'s `STAGES:` — ⛔ BOTH named `qa/TASK-1143.md`, ⛔ i.e. the ⛔ REVIEWED row's id, ⛔ the exact shape this section forbids. ⛔ CORRECTED TO `qa/TASK-1144.md` 2026-09-08 — ⛔ **⛔ BEFORE THE GATE RAN**, ⛔ so ⛔ no verdict was ever misfiled and ⛔ no commit was ever silent.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY CHEAP INSTANCE IN THIS WHOLE RECORD IS THE ONE CAUGHT ⛔ BEFORE THE ROW EXECUTED. ⛔ EVERY OTHER ONE COST A ⛔ NEAR-MISS.***
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1156`/`1157` carried the ⛔ same shape (`qa/TASK-1153.md` / `qa/TASK-1155.md`) and ⛔ NEVER FIRED — ⛔ both rows closed `unnecessary`/`superseded` ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ LEFT AS WRITTEN, ⛔ DELIBERATELY**: ⛔ a closed row's spec is ⛔ history, and ⛔ rewriting history to satisfy a later law ⛔ destroys the record of ⛔ why the law exists.**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1158` (⛔ done, `20c1bea`) staged `qa/TASK-115{3,5}.md` ⛔ *"(those that exist)"* — ⛔ MEASURED: ⛔ NEITHER exists ⇒ ⛔ the clause resolved to ⛔ ZERO paths and the ⛔ self-guard ⛔ HELD.** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ RECORDED AS THE ⛔ WORKED EXAMPLE OF THE ⛔ CHEAP DEFENCE: ⛔ *"those that exist"* on a ⛔ conditional path list turns a ⛔ SILENT MISS into a ⛔ NO-OP. ⛔ It is ⛔ not a substitute for cl. 3's ⛔ resolve-against-disk duty — ⛔ it is what that duty looks like ⛔ written into the row ⛔ in advance.**

### SC-§105 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ RECIPE CAN BE ⛔ COMPLETE-LOOKING AND ⛔ INCOMPLETE — AND WHEN THE ⛔ MISSING STEP IS THE ⛔ PERSISTENCE / REGISTRATION STEP THAT A ⛔ MANUAL, ONE-BY-ONE COPY SITE REQUIRES, ⛔ FOLLOWING THE INSTRUCTIONS ⛔ CORRECTLY ⛔ MANUFACTURES A SILENT DEFECT. ⛔ EVERY OBSERVABLE REPORTS SUCCESS, BECAUSE ⛔ EVERY OBSERVABLE IS READING THE ⛔ ONE COPY THAT WAS ⛔ UPDATED.** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager; ⛔ **bought by ⭐ `TASK-1120`, which was told to follow the *"HOW TO ADD SETTING"* recipe ⛔ EXACTLY — ⛔ DID — and ⛔ THEREBY FOUND THE HOLE** · marker `SC-105-RECIPE-OMITS-THE-COPY-SITE`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ FOUR STEPS WERE ⛔ EACH CORRECT. ⛔ THE ⛔ LIST WAS ⛔ WRONG. ⛔ AN AGENT WHO ⛔ DEVIATES GETS CAUGHT BY A REVIEW; AN AGENT WHO ⛔ COMPLIES GETS ⛔ SHIPPED.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — THE INSTANCE, ⛔ MEASURED.** ⛔ **`USiegeSettingsSubsystem::SaveSettingsToSlot()` copies the in-memory values onto a ⛔ FRESHLY CONSTRUCTED SaveGame object ⛔ FIELD BY FIELD ⇒ a field added by the recipe's ⛔ steps 1-4 alone is written to disk ⛔ AT ITS C++ DEFAULT, ⛔ every save, ⛔ forever.** ⛔ **In the running session ⛔ NOTHING is wrong: the ⛔ getter returns the new value, the ⛔ delegate fires, the ⛔ UI updates, the ⛔ save logs ⛔ SUCCESS. ⛔ The preference ⛔ evaporates at the ⛔ next launch, with ⛔ no error and ⛔ nothing in any log to point at** (⭐ `SC-§94` cl. A). ⛔ **Corrected at source — ⛔ `SiegeSettingsSubsystem.h:70-96`, ⛔ step 5 — and the ⛔ one quote of it in this file (`GFX-§3`) is corrected ⛔ in place.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE TELL, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ MECHANICAL, ⛔ SO IT IS ⛔ NOT KEPT BY MEMORY:** ⚖️ ***⛔ A *"HOW TO ADD X"* LIST WHOSE STEPS DO ⛔ NOT INCLUDE THE ⛔ PERSISTENCE / REGISTRATION STEP THAT A ⛔ MANUAL, ONE-BY-ONE COPY SITE REQUIRES.*** ⇒ ⛔ **THE QUESTION TO ASK ⛔ OF ANY RECIPE, ⛔ BEFORE FOLLOWING IT: ⛔ *"WHAT CODE ⛔ ENUMERATES THESE THINGS ⛔ BY HAND?"*** ⛔ **⛔ EVERY hand-enumerating funnel owes the list ⛔ ONE STEP: ⛔ a save/load ⛔ COPY PAIR (⛔ this instance — ⛔ note the recipe ⛔ DID have the ⛔ LOAD step and ⛔ NOT the ⛔ SAVE step, ⛔ which is exactly why it read complete) · ⛔ `GetLifetimeReplicatedProps` · ⛔ a factory / `switch` over an enum · ⛔ a name or CVar ⛔ REGISTRY array (`CanonicalNames`, `SettingName_*`) · ⛔ a cloud/jsonb ⛔ PROJECTION (`ACC-§13`) · ⛔ an ⛔ exclusion or pathspec ⛔ FLOOR (`TL-§5e`) · ⛔ a ⛔ manifest.** ⛔ **⛔ A generic `for`-over-`UPROPERTY` funnel owes ⛔ nothing; ⛔ a hand-written line per member owes ⛔ a step. ⛔ THE ⛔ HAND-WRITTEN LINE IS THE ⛔ WHOLE HAZARD.**
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ WHY IT IS ⛔ INVISIBLE, AND IT IS ⛔ EXACTLY ⭐ `SC-§104`'S COLLAPSE ⛔ MOVED FROM THE ⛔ ASSERTION TO THE ⛔ OBSERVATION.** ⛔ **The ⛔ getter, the ⛔ delegate, the ⛔ UI and the ⛔ success log look like ⛔ FOUR independent confirmations. ⛔ THEY ARE ⛔ ONE: ⛔ all four read the ⛔ IN-MEMORY member, which ⛔ WAS correctly updated.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ MANY OBSERVABLES, ⛔ ONE SOURCE ⇒ ⛔ agreement between them carries ⛔ ZERO information about the ⛔ ONE copy nobody read — ⛔ the ⛔ BYTES ON DISK.** ⚖️ ***⛔ CORROBORATION IS ⛔ ONLY EVIDENCE WHEN THE ⛔ WITNESSES ARE ⛔ INDEPENDENT. ⛔ FOUR ECHOES OF ONE VARIABLE ARE ⛔ ONE WITNESS, ⛔ REPEATED FOUR TIMES.***
4. ✅🚨⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — THE ⛔ DUTIES, ⛔ AND THEY ARE ⛔ CHEAP:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ A RECIPE IS ⛔ LAW WITH ⛔ NO GATE ON IT.** ⛔ Steps 1-4 were ⛔ never reviewed against the ⛔ FAILURE they must prevent — ⛔ only against the ⛔ success they describe (⭐ `SHIP-§9`, ⛔ applied to an ⛔ INSTRUCTION instead of a gate). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A RECIPE'S ⛔ ACCEPTANCE IS A ⛔ TEST THAT GOES ⛔ RED WHEN A ⛔ STEP IS ⛔ SKIPPED — ⛔ not a reading of the list.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ THE TEST MUST CROSS THE ⛔ BOUNDARY THE RECIPE CROSSES: ⛔ load into a ⛔ SECOND STORE. ⛔ Re-reading the object you just wrote is a ⛔ PROPERTY ECHO (`SC-§94`) and is ⛔ GREEN ON THE BUG.**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ THE FINDER ⛔ CORRECTS THE ⛔ SOURCE (⛔ the header / the file the recipe lives in) ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION, ⛔ and ⛔ ROUTES the ⛔ quotes upward — ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` is the ⛔ manager's (⭐ `SC-§82`).** ✅ **⭐ `TASK-1120` did ⛔ exactly this and ⛔ said so; ⛔ that is the ⛔ correct shape and it is ⛔ recorded as a ⛔ POSITIVE exhibit.**
   - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(d) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ ONE THAT COSTS THE MOST IF SKIPPED: ⛔ WHEN A RECIPE IS FOUND INCOMPLETE, THE ⛔ FIRST QUESTION IS ⛔ NOT *"fix the recipe"* — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ *"⛔ WHO ELSE ⛔ ALREADY FOLLOWED IT?"*** ⛔ **⛔ A DEFECTIVE RECIPE IS A ⛔ DEFECT FACTORY WITH ⛔ N UNITS ⛔ ALREADY SHIPPED, and ⛔ every one of them is ⛔ silent by construction.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ BOARD THE ⛔ CENSUS ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION AS THE CORRECTION** (⛔ here: ⭐ `TASK-1121` counts, ⭐ `TASK-1143` repairs).
5. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ KINSHIP, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ DISTINCTION IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING: ⭐ `SC-§97` is an ⛔ UNMEASURED CLAIM · ⭐ `SC-§101` is an ⛔ UNAUDITED REMEDY (⛔ a prescribed pin is a ⛔ claim — ⛔ **a recipe is a ⛔ PRESCRIBED REMEDY ⛔ ISSUED IN ADVANCE, ⛔ to an author who ⛔ cannot audit it because ⛔ they are following it for the ⛔ first time**) · ⛔ **THIS is an ⛔ INSTRUCTION THAT IS ⛔ COMPLETE-LOOKING AND ⛔ INCOMPLETE** · ⭐ `SC-§104` (⛔ the many-to-one collapse — ⛔ cl. 3 above) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ the echo) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate against the failure) · ⭐ `SC-§36.1` (⛔ the better the surface, the more complete the illusion).** ⇒ 📌 ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ AN INSTRUCTION YOU ⛔ OBEYED IS THE ⛔ LAST PLACE ANYONE LOOKS FOR THE ⛔ BUG — ⛔ SO A ⛔ RECIPE WITHOUT A ⛔ RED TEST BEHIND IT IS AN ⛔ UNGATED COMMIT THAT ⛔ EVERY FUTURE AUTHOR ⛔ RE-SHIPS.**

### SC-§104 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A COUNTER THAT ⛔ AGGREGATES TWO ⛔ SEMANTICALLY DIFFERENT EVENTS ⛔ CANNOT DISCRIMINATE A DEFECT THAT ⛔ SWAPS ONE FOR THE OTHER — THE ⛔ TALLY IS ⛔ EQUAL ON BOTH BRANCHES, SO THE MUTATION TEST GOES ⛔ GREEN ON THE BUG IT WAS ⛔ WRITTEN TO CATCH. ⛔ ASSERT ⛔ STATE, ⛔ NOT TALLIES, ⛔ WHEREVER THE CHOICE EXISTS.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager, ⛔ **on the general form the ⛔ QA gate proposed and ⛔ routed here** — ⛔ **THREE INDEPENDENT INSTANCES ⛔ IN ONE DAY: `qa/TASK-1119.md` § LOOP 1 `M21` · `qa/TASK-1114.md` § LOOP 1 (`SC-§96` cl. 3a) · `qa/TASK-1123.md` `M8`** · marker `SC-104-ASSERT-STATE-NOT-TALLIES`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ TEST WAS ⛔ GREEN ⛔ BEFORE AND ⛔ AFTER THE FIX. ⛔ NOT BECAUSE THE FIX DID NOTHING — ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT ⛔ ADDS UP THE ⛔ TWO THINGS THE DEFECT ⛔ TRADES BETWEEN.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE ⛔ ARITHMETIC-COINCIDENCE INSTANCE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ PUREST OF THE THREE** (⭐ `TASK-1118` / `qa/TASK-1119.md` § LOOP 1, ⛔ mutation `M21`).
   - ⛔ **THE SETUP: `M21` restores the ⛔ LATCHED predicate on the wrap-around branch — ⛔ i.e. it ⛔ RE-INTRODUCES the exact BLOCKER-1 defect (⛔ the branch goes ⛔ DEAD, the wrapping press ⛔ RE-APPLIES and ⛔ RESTARTS the ten seconds instead of ⛔ closing the window).**
   - ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ ARITHMETIC: ⛔ FIXED branch = `ModeCount−1` provisional applies, ⛔ then the wrap ⛔ closes the window ⇒ the ⛔ CLOSING REVERT ⛔ ITSELF APPLIES ONE. ⛔ BROKEN branch = `ModeCount−1` provisional applies ⛔ + ⛔ ONE MORE provisional apply. ⇒ ⛔ `ModeCount−1 + 1` ⛔ EITHER WAY.** ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ SAME TOTAL BY ⛔ TWO DIFFERENT ROUTES ⇒ ⛔ `test:1643-1644` — ⛔ the apply-count assertion — ⛔ IS GREEN ON ⛔ BOTH, AND ⛔ THEREFORE MEASURES ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT THE DEFECT IT ⛔ EXISTS FOR.**
   - ✅ **⛔ WHAT ⛔ DOES DISCRIMINATE: the ⛔ STATE ROWS — ⛔ is the confirm window ⛔ OPEN? ⛔ is the latch ⛔ DOWN? ⛔ is the staged mode ⛔ DISCARDED? ⛔ Those differ ⛔ categorically between the branches and ⛔ cannot be reached by ⛔ any total.**
   - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ TELL THAT SHOULD HAVE PROMPTED THE CHECK: the ⛔ two events being summed have ⛔ OPPOSITE MEANINGS — ⛔ *"the player asked for another provisional mode"* and ⛔ *"the system undid one"*. ⛔ A counter that ⛔ cannot tell those apart is ⛔ not an instrument, it is a ⛔ coin flip that always lands the same way.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE SIBLING WHERE THE AGGREGATION IS ⛔ ACROSS A ⛔ LAYER BOUNDARY** (⭐ `TASK-1113` / `qa/TASK-1114.md` § LOOP 1; ⛔ recorded in full at ⭐ `SC-§96` cl. 3b).
   - ⛔ **`SaveSettingsCallCount` counts the ⛔ FACADE's OWN saves. ⛔ `AutoDetectQuality()` reaches the engine's `ApplyHardwareBenchmarkResults`, whose ⛔ LAST LINE saves to disk ⇒ ⛔ the counter read ⛔ `0` on the ⛔ BROKEN build ⛔ AND on the ⛔ FIXED one.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ TWELVE mutation tests asserting on it would have ⛔ PASSED ⛔ ON THE DEFECT.**
   - ⛔ **SAME DISEASE, ⛔ DIFFERENT AXIS: ⛔ §1 aggregates ⛔ two events at ⛔ one layer; ⛔ this aggregates ⛔ *"saves I made"* with ⛔ *"saves that happened"* — ⛔ and the defect lives ⛔ EXACTLY in the gap.** ⛔ **The cure was ⛔ THE SAME SHAPE: a ⛔ SECOND instrument on the ⛔ STATE/DECISION (`RefusedSaveWhileVideoModePendingCount`), ⛔ not on the call.**
3. 🚨⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE THIRD INSTANCE, ⛔ WHERE THE PREDICTED RED WAS ⛔ ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE** (⭐ `TASK-1122` / `qa/TASK-1123.md`, mutation `M8`).
   - ⛔ **`M8` predicted a red that ⛔ `FMath::Clamp`'s `Max(Min(...))` ⛔ MAKES IMPOSSIBLE — ⛔ the mutated value is ⛔ squeezed back onto the ⛔ same output by the clamp ⇒ ⛔ the assertion ⛔ cannot see the change.**
   - ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ GENERALISE PAST *"counters"*: ⛔ ANY ⛔ MANY-TO-ONE FUNCTION between the mutation and the assertion — ⛔ a ⛔ SUM, a ⛔ CLAMP, a ⛔ MIN/MAX, an ⛔ `abs`, a ⛔ bucketing, a ⛔ `bool` cast — ⛔ CAN COLLAPSE THE TWO BRANCHES ONTO ⛔ ONE OBSERVABLE.** ⛔ **⛔ The counter is just the ⛔ commonest lossy funnel in this codebase.**
4. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE ⛔ GENERAL FORM, ⛔ IN ONE SENTENCE, AS THE GATE PROPOSED IT:**
   > ***⛔ A COUNTER THAT AGGREGATES TWO ⛔ SEMANTICALLY DIFFERENT EVENTS ⛔ CANNOT DISCRIMINATE A DEFECT THAT ⛔ SWAPS ONE FOR THE OTHER.***
   ⛔ **And its ⛔ REMEDY, ⛔ equally short: ⛔ ASSERT ⛔ STATE, ⛔ NOT TALLIES, ⛔ WHEREVER THE CHOICE EXISTS.**
5. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ THE ⛔ MECHANICAL DUTY, ⛔ BINDING ON ⛔ AUTHOR ⛔ AND ⛔ GATE ⛔ ALIKE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ CHEAP:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ BEFORE publishing ⛔ ANY *"`M<n>` reddens ≥ N rows, including …"*, ⛔ COMPUTE THE ASSERTED VALUE ⛔ ON BOTH BRANCHES ⛔ AND WRITE ⛔ BOTH NUMBERS.** ⛔ **⛔ If they are ⛔ EQUAL, that row is ⛔ NOT a red — ⛔ strike it from the prediction ⛔ and say why** (⛔ this is ⭐ `SC-§83`'s ⛔ named-rows discipline given an ⛔ arithmetic obligation).
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ FOR EVERY COUNTER YOU ASSERT ON: ⛔ NAME THE ⛔ SET OF EVENTS IT INCREMENTS ON. ⛔ If that set has ⛔ MORE THAN ONE MEMBER, ⛔ you must ⛔ show the members ⛔ CANNOT TRADE under the mutation — ⛔ or ⛔ pick a different assertion.**
   - ⛔ **(c) ⛔ PREFER, ⛔ IN ORDER: ⛔ (i) a ⛔ STATE PREDICATE (⛔ is the window open, ⛔ is the latch down, ⛔ what mode is staged) · ⛔ (ii) a ⛔ counter ⛔ SPLIT PER EVENT KIND · ⛔ (iii) a ⛔ counter on the ⛔ REFUSAL/DECISION rather than on the ⛔ ACTION · ⛔ (iv) ⛔ a bare total — ⛔ LAST, and ⛔ only with (b) discharged in writing.**
   - ⛔ **(d) ⛔ THE HONEST LIMIT, ⛔ STATED SO NOBODY OVERSELLS THIS: ⛔ these three were caught by ⛔ DERIVATION, ⛔ not execution — ⛔ NOBODY COMPILED ANYTHING (`SC-§78`). ⛔ A derivation that says ⛔ *"equal on both branches"* is ⛔ strong (it is ⛔ arithmetic); a derivation that says ⛔ *"this reddens"* is ⛔ still a ⛔ PREDICTION until a ⛔ WITNESSED red exists.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ KINSHIP, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ SAME DISEASE IN ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENTS: ⭐ `SC-§96` cl. 3b (⛔ the ⛔ FACADE COUNTER — ⛔ clause 2 above, and its ⛔ home) · ⭐ `SC-§99` (⛔ a ⛔ LOG PARSER THAT HAS ⛔ ONLY EVER SEEN GREEN — ⛔ **⛔ EXACTLY THIS DISEASE ⛔ ONE INSTRUMENT OVER: ⛔ an instrument that has ⛔ never been shown the ⛔ failure it exists to detect, ⛔ so its green is ⛔ uninformative**) · ⭐ `SC-§94` (⛔ an instrument that ⛔ ECHOES THE REQUEST) · ⭐ `SC-§83` (⛔ the ⛔ AUTHORED guard is the ⛔ unproven one, and its ⛔ named-rows rule) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects, ⛔ never merely against ⛔ success — ⛔ THIS LAW IS `SHIP-§9` ⛔ APPLIED TO AN ⛔ ASSERTION) · ⭐ `SC-§101` (⛔ a ⛔ prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim — ⛔ and `M21` ⛔ WAS a prescribed pin).** ⇒ 📌 ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A GREEN TEST IS ⛔ EVIDENCE ⛔ ONLY IF SOMETHING COULD HAVE MADE IT ⛔ RED — ⛔ AND A ⛔ LOSSY ASSERTION IS A ⛔ TEST THAT ⛔ NOTHING CAN.**

### SC-§103 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A COMMIT THAT CARRIES `N` TASK IDS ⛔ OWES `N` STATUS FLIPS — ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION AS THE COMMIT. ⛔ THE LEAD ID GETS FLIPPED; ⛔ THE PASSENGERS ⛔ NEVER DO — AND THE PASSENGERS ARE ⛔ EXACTLY THE ROWS OTHER ROWS ⛔ FENCE AGAINST.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager, from `handoffs/BOARD-STALENESS-audit.md` — ⛔ **build-master's git-resolved sweep of ⛔ ALL 1058 board ids against ⛔ 256 commits, the half the manager ⛔ structurally cannot run, `SC-§71b`** · marker `SC-103-BATCH-OWES-N-FLIPS`)

> ### ⛔⛔ **⛔ EVERY ONE OF THE ⛔ THIRTEEN STALE ROWS FOUND BY THAT SWEEP RODE A ⛔ BATCH COMMIT WHOSE SUBJECT ⛔ LEADS WITH ⛔ ONE ID.** ⛔ **`TASK-093/094/097-102` · `TASK-110..112` · `TASK-113..121` · `TASK-268..272` · `TASK-273..277` · `TASK-082/083/084` · `TASK-780 (…768/775-778/783-788)`.** ⛔ **⛔ NOT ⛔ ONE of them is a ⛔ single-id commit that was merely forgotten.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — THE MECHANISM, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE SECTION. ⛔ THE HOST REMEMBERS THE ⛔ ID IT TYPED IN THE SUBJECT ⛔ AND FLIPS THAT ROW. ⛔ THE OTHER `N-1` ROWS ⛔ EXIST, ⛔ SHIPPED, AND ⛔ STILL SAY `backlog` / `qa-passed` / `ready-for-integration`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THAT IS THE ⛔ MANUFACTURING PROCESS FOR ⛔ END 2 (⛔ a `status:` claiming to be running against a commit that names it) — ⛔ AND ⛔ END 2 ⛔ MANUFACTURES ⛔ END 1 (⛔ a fence naming a committed id), ⛔ because a passenger is ⛔ precisely the kind of row other rows ⛔ depend on.** ⛔ **⛔ MEASURED COST: ⛔ 9 of the 13 fenced ⛔ 16 STILL-OPEN ROWS. ⛔ Worst: ⭐ `TASK-447` — ⛔ `backlog` for ⛔ 35 DAYS, ⛔ THREE commits carrying its name, ⛔ FOUR open rows behind it; ⭐ `TASK-780` — ⛔ `backlog` with ⛔ FIVE behind it, ⛔ including 🧑 Jonathan's own sitting.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE SHARPEST EXHIBIT THIS PROJECT HAS EVER PRODUCED FOR *"⛔ THE BOARD CANNOT AUDIT THE BOARD"*: ⭐ `TASK-438` ⇄ ⭐ `TASK-447` ⛔ FENCE ⛔ EACH OTHER.** ⛔ **`438`'s `blocked-by` named `447`; ⛔ `447`'s named `438` (*"hosts TASK-438 in-session"*).** ⛔⛔ **⛔ BOTH SHIPPED ⛔ THE SAME DAY — `87c4784` and `cd5f4ed`, ⛔ 2026-08-03 — AND ⛔ NEITHER WAS FLIPPED.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ READ LITERALLY, THE BOARD DESCRIBED A ⛔ DEADLOCK FOR ⛔ 35 DAYS THAT ⛔ GIT HAD ⛔ ALREADY RESOLVED — AND ⛔ NO AMOUNT OF ⛔ READING THE BOARD COULD ⛔ EVER HAVE BROKEN IT, BECAUSE ⛔ EACH ROW'S ⛔ ONLY EVIDENCE WAS THE ⛔ OTHER ROW'S ⛔ LYING FIELD.*** ⛔ **⛔ AND `438`'s ⛔ OWN STATUS TEXT ALREADY SAID *"⛔ PLACED + WIRED, ⛔ done inside TASK-447's session"* — ⛔ THE TRUTH WAS ⛔ IN THE ROW, ⛔ ONE LINE ABOVE THE FENCE THAT ⛔ DENIED IT.**
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE, ⛔ AND IT ⛔ COMPOSES WITH THE ⛔ FLIP-IN-THE-SAME-ACTION RULE ⛔ RATHER THAN REPEATING IT.** ⛔ **`TASKBOARD.md` write-discipline rule 7 already says *"⛔ a commit host flips ⛔ ITS OWN row in the ⛔ same action as the commit."* ⛔⛔ THAT IS ⛔ INSUFFICIENT FOR A ⛔ BATCH HOST.** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ A COMMIT CARRYING `N` IDS ⛔ FLIPS ⛔ ALL `N`, ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION, ⛔ EACH WITH THE ⛔ SAME HASH.** ⛔ **⛔ *"The lead one is the real one"* is ⛔ a story about ⛔ how the subject was typed, ⛔ not about ⛔ what shipped.**
   - ✅⭐ **⛔ THE TELL, ⛔ MECHANICALLY CHECKABLE, ⛔ SO THIS IS ⛔ NOT KEPT BY MEMORY: ⛔ A COMMIT SUBJECT CONTAINING A ⛔ RANGE (`..`) OR A ⛔ LIST (`/`) OF IDS — ⛔ or a ⛔ trailing parenthetical of ids.** ⛔ **⛔ Extract ⛔ EVERY maximal `TASK-[0-9]+` token from the ⛔ WHOLE MESSAGE (⛔ subject ⛔ AND body bullets), and ⛔ enumerate them ⛔ before writing the commit.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IF YOU CANNOT NAME ⛔ WHY AN ID IN YOUR OWN SUBJECT IS ⛔ NOT GETTING A FLIP, ⛔ IT IS GETTING A FLIP.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ ONE LEGITIMATE EXCEPTION, ⛔ AND IT MUST BE ⛔ WRITTEN: an id named as a ⛔ GATE (*"gated by TASK-N"*), a ⛔ LICENCE (*"licensed by TASK-N"*) or a ⛔ FENCE is ⛔ NOT a passenger and ⛔ does ⛔ NOT get flipped** (⛔ a gate row ⛔ ships no code and ⛔ never owns a commit). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ SAY WHICH IDS IN YOUR SUBJECT ARE ⛔ PASSENGERS AND WHICH ARE ⛔ REFERENCES — ⛔ the distinction is ⛔ free at commit time and ⛔ unrecoverable 60 days later.**
   - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **⛔ AMENDED 2026-09-08 BY THE MANAGER — ⛔ A ⛔ THIRD CLASS, ⛔ RULED ON A ⛔ LIVE QUESTION THE HOST ⛔ CORRECTLY REFUSED TO DECIDE ALONE (⭐ `20c1bea`, ⭐ `TASK-1158`): ⛔ THE ⛔ EXCLUSION.** ⛔ **⛔ AN ID NAMED IN A MESSAGE ⛔ PRECISELY TO SAY THAT ITS WORK IS ⛔ **NOT** ⛔ IN THIS COMMIT IS ⛔ NOT A PASSENGER AND ⛔ DOES NOT GET FLIPPED.** ⛔ **⛔ THE THREE CLASSES ARE ⛔ PASSENGER (⛔ its work ⛔ shipped here ⇒ ⛔ FLIP) · ⛔ REFERENCE (⛔ a gate / licence / fence ⇒ ⛔ no flip) · ⛔ EXCLUSION (⛔ *"ask (C) is ⛔ NOT shipped — see `TASK-1154`"* ⇒ ⛔ NO FLIP).**
     - ⚖️ ***⛔ THE REASON IS ⛔ NOT SYMMETRY, IT IS ⛔ TRUTH: ⛔ FLIPPING AN EXCLUSION WOULD RECORD A ROW AS ⛔ SHIPPED BY A COMMIT WHOSE ⛔ OWN MESSAGE SAYS IT ⛔ WAS NOT. ⛔ `SC-§103` EXISTS TO STOP THE BOARD ⛔ UNDER-STATING WHAT GIT DID; ⛔ APPLIED HERE IT WOULD MAKE THE BOARD ⛔ OVER-STATE IT, WHICH IS THE ⛔ SAME DISEASE POINTING THE OTHER WAY — AND ⛔ WORSE, BECAUSE A ⛔ FALSE `done` ⛔ STOPS PEOPLE LOOKING.***
     - ✅ **⛔ THE ⛔ TEST, ⛔ MECHANICAL: ⛔ FOR EACH ID IN THE MESSAGE, ⛔ ASK *"⛔ DID THIS ROW'S ⛔ DELIVERABLE ⛔ ENTER THIS COMMIT'S ⛔ FILE LIST?"* ⛔ YES ⇒ ⛔ PASSENGER ⇒ ⛔ FLIP. ⛔ NO ⇒ ⛔ REFERENCE ⛔ OR ⛔ EXCLUSION ⇒ ⛔ NO FLIP, ⛔ AND ⛔ SAY WHICH.** ⛔ **⛔ Resolve it against the ⛔ COMMIT'S OWN `--stat`, ⛔ never against the board (⛔ the board cannot audit the board).**
     - 🚨 **⛔ AND THE ⛔ RECIPROCAL DUTY, ⛔ BECAUSE AN EXCLUSION IS ⛔ EXACTLY HOW A ROW BECOMES AN ⛔ ORPHAN: ⛔ AN EXCLUDED ID ⛔ OWES, ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION, ⛔ EITHER A ⛔ NAMED SUCCESSOR ROW ⛔ OR AN ⛔ EXPLICIT NON-TERMINAL STATUS ON ⛔ ITS OWN ROW.** ⛔ **⛔ Naming a row only to ⛔ disown it, and then ⛔ leaving it saying nothing, produces a row that ⛔ every future reader sees ⛔ mentioned in git and ⛔ assumes was handled.** ✅ **⛔ THE LIVE INSTANCE ⛔ DISCHARGED IT: ⭐ `TASK-1154` reads ⛔ `blocked-on-tooling` and ⛔ names its successor ⭐ `TASK-1165`.**
     - 📌 **Instance record: ⛔ `20c1bea` named ⛔ `TASK-1158` + `TASK-1152` (⛔ passengers, ⛔ both flipped) and ⛔ `TASK-1154` (⛔ EXCLUSION, ⛔ correctly ⛔ not flipped). ⛔ The host ⛔ flipped two and ⛔ routed the third to the manager ⛔ rather than guessing ⇒ ⛔ recorded as a ⛔ POSITIVE exhibit (⭐ `SC-§82`).**
4. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ FOUR READER HAZARDS THAT ⛔ ALL PRODUCE A ⛔ CONFIDENT WRONG ANSWER. ⛔ EVERY FUTURE SWEEP MEETS ⛔ ALL FOUR.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ (a) THE ⛔ PREFIX TRAP: `--grep="TASK-110"` returns ⛔ 5 commits, ⛔ 4 of them `TASK-1104`/`TASK-1107`/`TASK-1131`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ MAXIMAL-TOKEN EXTRACTION IS ⛔ MANDATORY; ⛔ substring `--grep` on a 3-digit id ⛔ silently harvests its ⛔ 4-digit descendants and ⛔ INVENTS stale rows.**

   - 🚨⛔⛔ **⛔ (c) THE ⛔ SECOND `- status:` LINE: a row ⛔ restored after the `BOARD-TRUNCATION-2026-09-05` incident can carry ⛔ MORE THAN ONE `- status:` line, and ⛔ THE FIRST IS ⛔ NOT NECESSARILY CURRENT.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1055`'s first reads `GATE ⛔ DISCHARGED`, its ⛔ second reads `done … COMMITTED c4955cb`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A FIRST-LINE READER REPORTS THAT ROW ⛔ STALE. ⛔ SCAN THE ⛔ WHOLE ROW BLOCK, ⛔ EVERY TIME.**

   - ⛔⛔ **⛔ (f) THE ⛔ TERMINAL-VOCABULARY GAP: the sweep's terminal set was `done/shipped/superseded/closed/void/integrated/adopted` — ⛔ and ⛔ `cancelled` IS ⛔ NOT IN IT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-075` reads ⛔ `cancelled`, its commit's body bullet reads ⛔ *"TASK-075: ⛔ CANCELLED — audit proved no editor-asset change is needed"*, ⛔ board and git ⛔ AGREE — ⛔ and it was filed as an ⛔ OPEN row worth the manager's eye.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A STATUS CLASSIFIER IS A ⛔ VOCABULARY, AND AN ⛔ UNLISTED WORD DOES ⛔ NOT ERROR — ⛔ IT SILENTLY BECOMES *"⛔ OPEN". ⛔ PUBLISH THE VOCABULARY WITH THE COUNT, ⛔ OR THE COUNT IS ⛔ UNCHECKABLE.***

7. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 7 — KINSHIP. ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ FOURTH APPLICATION OF *"⛔ AN INTENTION IS ⛔ NOT A MECHANISM"* — ⛔ after the `GATE: OWED` abolition, ⭐ `SC-§100` (⛔ a rescope lands ⛔ in the same action as the dispatch) and ⛔ write-discipline rule 7's ⛔ flip-in-the-same-action.** ⛔ **Also ⭐ `SC-§102` (⛔ the mis-anchored pathspec — ⛔ the ⛔ same false conclusion from the ⛔ opposite direction: ⛔ `SC-§102` says *"⛔ never landed"* about work that ⛔ did; ⛔ this says *"⛔ not done"* about work that ⛔ is) · ⭐ `SC-§96`/`SC-§99` (⛔ control the reader ⛔ before trusting a zero — ⛔ this sweep ran ⛔ TWO positive controls ⛔ INSIDE its derived table ⛔ plus a nonsense-id negative, which is ⛔ why its zeros are ⛔ usable) · ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ do ⛔ not upgrade an inference into an instruction).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A COMMIT SUBJECT IS A ⛔ SENTENCE WITH ⛔ ONE SUBJECT, AND ⛔ SHIPPED WORK HAS ⛔ AS MANY OWNERS AS IT HAS ⛔ ROWS. ⛔ THE ⛔ GRAMMAR OF THE MESSAGE IS ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ ARITHMETIC OF THE BOARD.***

### SC-§102 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THIS REPO'S GIT ROOT IS ⛔ ONE LEVEL ABOVE THE PROJECT DIRECTORY, SO ⛔ EVERY TRACKED PATH CARRIES A ⛔ `GitClaudeUnrealTest/` PREFIX — AND A ⛔ MIS-ANCHORED GIT PATHSPEC ANSWERS WITH ⛔ SILENCE, ⛔ NOT AN ERROR.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager, ⛔ **at the host's request**; ⛔ **it has bitten ⛔ FOUR TIMES — ⭐ `TASK-1110` (⛔ 8 false mismatches), ⭐ `TASK-1131` (⛔ a guard that could ⛔ never match), ⭐ `TASK-1117` (⛔ `cat-file` ⛔ AND `git log --`, ⛔ same run), ⛔ and ⭐ `TASK-1313` 2026-09-19 (⛔ `git diff --`, ⛔ and it is the one that ⛔ NAMED THE MECHANISM — ⛔ cl. 6) · marker `SC-102-GIT-ROOT-IS-ABOVE`**)

1. ⛔⛔ **THE MECHANICAL FACT, ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE SECTION: ⛔ the working directory is ⛔ `C:/GitProjects/GitHub/GitClaudeUnrealTesting/GitClaudeUnrealTest`. ⛔ THE ⛔ REPOSITORY ROOT IS ⛔ `C:/GitProjects/GitHub/GitClaudeUnrealTesting` — ⛔ ONE LEVEL UP.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ every path in the ⛔ INDEX, the ⛔ LOG, a ⛔ PATHSPEC and ⛔ every `--name-only` line reads ⛔ `GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/…`, ⛔ NOT `Source/…`.** ⛔ **⛔ MEET THIS ⛔ BEFORE YOU WRITE YOUR ⛔ FIRST PATHSPEC — ⛔ that is why it is a section and ⛔ not a note on a row.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ DANGEROUS HALF: ⛔ A PATH THAT IS ⛔ CORRECT FOR `Read`, `Grep`, `Glob` AND ⛔ EVERY OTHER TOOL IN THIS PIPELINE IS ⛔ WRONG FOR GIT — ⛔ AND ⛔ GIT DOES ⛔ NOT COMPLAIN.** ⛔ `git cat-file -p :<path>` returns ⛔ **EMPTY**. ⛔ `git log -- <path>` returns ⛔ **NO COMMITS**. ⛔ A `grep '^Source/'` over git output matches ⛔ **NOTHING**. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ANSWER IS A ⛔ ZERO — AND A ⛔ ZERO IS A ⛔ PERFECTLY PLAUSIBLE ANSWER TO ⛔ MOST OF THE QUESTIONS WE ASK IT.**
3. ⛔ **THE ⛔ THREE MEASURED INSTANCES, ⛔ AND ⛔ NOTE THAT THEY FAIL IN ⛔ THREE DIFFERENT DIRECTIONS:**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1110` — ⛔ a ⛔ LOUD FALSE ALARM: ⛔ 8 empty `cat-file` reads compared unequal to 8 real hashes ⇒ ⛔ *"all eight binaries are corrupt"*, ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly what a real disaster looks like** (⭐ `SC-§96` cl. 1).
   - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1131` — ⛔ THE ⛔ WORST ONE: a `'^Source/'` guard that ⛔ COULD NEVER MATCH returned ⛔ ZERO, ⛔ and zero was ⛔ ALSO the expected answer.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **a ⛔ TRUE RESULT FOR THE ⛔ WRONG REASON — ⛔ it agreed with the hypothesis, so ⛔ nothing prompted a second look, ⛔ and the guard was ⛔ dead in every future run too.**
   - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1117` — ⛔ BOTH READERS AT ONCE (`cat-file` ⛔ and `git log --`), ⛔ CAUGHT ⛔ ONLY because the host ran ⛔ PAIRED POSITIVE/NEGATIVE CONTROLS on each reader ⛔ before trusting either.**
4. ✅⛔ **THE REMEDY — ⛔ TWO FORMS, ⛔ EITHER IS SUFFICIENT, ⛔ ONE IS MANDATORY:**
   - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ ANCHOR FROM THE REPO ROOT: ⛔ derive the prefix from ⛔ `git rev-parse --show-prefix` (⛔ or `--show-toplevel`) and ⛔ prepend it — ⛔ never hand-type it, ⛔ and never assume the cwd is the root.**
   - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ CONTROL EVERY GIT READER ⛔ BEFORE TRUSTING A ZERO: ⛔ ask it for ⛔ ONE THING YOU KNOW EXISTS and ⛔ assert the answer is ⛔ NON-EMPTY, ⛔ then ask for ⛔ one you know does ⛔ NOT.** ⛔ **⛔ A reader that cannot produce a ⛔ known hit has ⛔ not measured anything** (⭐ `SC-§96` cl. 2, ⛔ generalised from `cat-file` to the ⛔ whole git surface).
5. ⛔ **⛔ SCOPE: this binds ⛔ `build-master` and ⛔ `gameplay-programmer` (⛔ the only roles holding `Bash`).** ⛔ **⛔ `qa-reviewer` and the ⛔ manager hold ⛔ none ⇒ ⛔ they can ⛔ neither commit this error nor ⛔ catch it, which is ⛔ precisely why the ⛔ control must be ⛔ inside the run** (⭐ `SC-§71b` / `SC-§78`).
6. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE FOURTH BITE ⛔ NAMED THE ⛔ MECHANISM, AND IT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN *"REMEMBER THE PREFIX"*: ⛔ `git status` AND `git diff` ⛔ DISAGREE ABOUT WHAT A PATH MEANS, SO YOU CAN COPY A PATH ⛔ OUT OF GIT'S OWN OUTPUT, FEED IT ⛔ BACK TO GIT, AND GET ⛔ SILENCE.** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, on ⭐ `TASK-1313`'s `built` report)
   - ⛔ **THE ASYMMETRY, ⛔ WHICH IS THE WHOLE CLAUSE:** ⛔ **`git status --porcelain` prints paths ⛔ REPO-ROOT-RELATIVE** (⛔ `GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/…`), while ⛔ **`git diff` / `git add` / `git commit` ⛔ PATHSPECS resolve against the ⛔ CURRENT WORKING DIRECTORY.** ⇒ ⛔ from inside the project dir, ⭐ `TASK-1313`'s `git diff -- GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/...` returned ⛔ **SILENCE** — ⛔ the pathspec was ⛔ *"correct"* in the sense that ⛔ git itself had just printed it.
   - 🚨 ⛔ **WHY THIS IS THE ⛔ MOST CONVINCING FORM OF THE BUG: ⛔ THE PATH CAME FROM GIT.** ⛔ Clauses 1–2 warn you against a path you ⛔ hand-typed from the ⛔ filesystem's point of view — ⛔ you can be suspicious of that. ⇒ ⛔ **a path ⛔ COPIED FROM `git status` carries ⛔ git's own authority, so ⛔ nothing prompts a second look, ⛔ and the empty result reads as ⛔ *"no changes"* rather than ⛔ *"wrong question."*** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1313` caught it and ran ⛔ everything git-side from the ⛔ REPO ROOT thereafter — ⛔ that is cl. 4(a) in practice and it is the ⛔ standing remedy.**
   - ⛔⭐ **AND A ⛔ SIBLING FROM THE ⛔ SAME RUN, RECORDED HERE BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ SAME FAILURE WEARING ⛔ DIFFERENT CLOTHES: ⛔ A MIS-ANCHORED ⛔ PATHSPEC ANSWERS WITH A ⛔ SILENCE; A MIS-SPECIFIED ⛔ NEEDLE ANSWERS WITH A ⛔ NUMBER.** ⛔ A bare `grep -c '401'` over a suite log ⛔ ALSO counts ⛔ millisecond timestamps (`:401]`) and ⛔ frame counters (`[401]`) — ⛔ it returned ⛔ **6 / 9 / 14** across three logs where the ⛔ real `HTTP 401` / `Unauthorized` signature was ⛔ **0**. ⇒ ⛔ **the host would have reported a ⛔ FLAKE THAT IS NOT THERE, ⛔ on an instrument nobody would think to control.** ✅ ⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ COUNT THE ⛔ SIGNATURE, ⛔ NEVER THE ⛔ DIGITS** (⛔ kin: ⭐ `TASK-1294`'s conflated needle).
   - ⚖️ ⇒ ***⛔ CL. 4(b)'s CONTROL REQUIREMENT ⛔ COVERS BOTH AND IS ⛔ HEREBY GENERALISED BEYOND GIT: ⛔ BEFORE YOU TRUST A COUNT — ⛔ ZERO ⛔ OR NON-ZERO — ⛔ ASK THE INSTRUMENT FOR ⛔ ONE THING YOU KNOW EXISTS AND ⛔ ONE YOU KNOW DOES NOT. ⛔ AN INSTRUMENT THAT CANNOT PRODUCE A ⛔ KNOWN HIT HAS NOT MEASURED ANYTHING, ⛔ AND ONE THAT CANNOT PRODUCE A ⛔ KNOWN MISS IS COUNTING ⛔ SOMETHING ELSE.***
6. ⚖️ **KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§96` (⛔ the ⛔ broken reader — ⛔ this is its cl. 1 ⛔ mechanical fact, ⛔ raised to a surface-wide rule) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ a binary probe needs a ⛔ positive control) · ⭐ `SC-§99` (⛔ prove the parser can say ⛔ NO) · ⭐ `SC-§87` + the ⛔ `Build.bat` exit-code law (⛔ instruments that lie by ⛔ OMISSION and by ⛔ COMMISSION).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ AN ⛔ ERROR YOU CAN ⛔ READ COSTS A MINUTE. ⛔ A ⛔ ZERO YOU CANNOT ⛔ DISTINGUISH FROM AN ⛔ ANSWER COSTS A ⛔ WHOLE CONCLUSION — ⛔ AND GIT RETURNS THE ⛔ SECOND ONE FOR A PATH THAT IS ⛔ MERELY IN THE WRONG DIRECTORY.***

### SC-§101 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ PRESCRIBED REMEDY IS A ⛔ CLAIM LIKE ANY OTHER, AND IT ⛔ INHERITS ⛔ NO AUTHORITY FROM THE ⛔ REPORT THAT PRESCRIBED IT. ⛔ A GATE THAT ⛔ CORRECTLY DIAGNOSES A DEFECT CAN ⛔ PRESCRIBE A FIX WITH THE ⛔ SAME DEFECT IN IT — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ FINDING'S CREDIBILITY IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT STOPS ANYONE CHECKING.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **bought on `qa/TASK-932.md` `W-2` — ⛔ the report ⛔ correctly found a ⛔ ONE-SIDED assertion and then ⛔ prescribed a fix that was ⛔ ONE-SIDED ⛔ IN EXACTLY THE SAME WAY · marker `SC-101-REMEDY-IS-A-CLAIM`**)

1. 🚨⛔⛔ **THE MEASURED CASE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NEARLY PERFECT AS A ⛔ TRAP:** ⛔ `W-2` found that `(c-ii)`'s `ConsultAt < FlashAt` bounds the veil guard's tells ⛔ **FROM ABOVE ONLY** — ⛔ **TRUE, and ⛔ CONFIRMED BY EXECUTION** (⭐ `TASK-937` §3 SET C: ⛔ the flash moved ⛔ below the guard, ⛔ suite ⛔ `517 / 0` ⛔ GREEN). ⛔ **It then prescribed the shape `NumberAt < CancelAt`, anchored on `CancelWitchCast(` as *"the first statement outside the branch."*** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE ANCHOR IS ⛔ 19 LINES BELOW THE CLOSING BRACE AND ⛔ EVERY LINE BETWEEN THEM IS ⛔ COMMENT — ⛔ WHICH `CodeLinesOnly` ⛔ STRIPS.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a tell moved ⛔ just below the brace ⛔ STILL satisfies `< CancelAt`** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE PRESCRIBED FIX WOULD HAVE SHIPPED ⛔ GREEN OVER THE ⛔ EXACT MUTATION IT WAS WRITTEN TO CATCH.**
2. ⛔⛔ **WHY IT WOULD HAVE SURVIVED: ⛔ the ⛔ DIAGNOSIS was ⛔ right, ⛔ measured and ⛔ dramatic** ⇒ ⛔ **every downstream reader arrives ⛔ already persuaded, and the ⛔ remedy rides in on the ⛔ finding's credibility.** ⛔ **The row would have been ⛔ written as *"implement `W-2`"*, the diff would have ⛔ matched the prescription ⛔ exactly, and ⛔ QA would have ⛔ PASSED it ⛔ against the prescription.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ EVERY GATE IN THE CHAIN WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ CHECKING CONFORMANCE TO THE REMEDY ⛔ INSTEAD OF ⛔ CLOSURE OF THE DEFECT.***
3. ✅⛔ **THE RULE — ⛔ THREE PARTIES, ⛔ THREE DUTIES:**
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ GATE/REPORT: ⛔ MARK A PRESCRIBED SHAPE AS A ⛔ HYPOTHESIS. ⛔ Write *"⛔ a candidate shape, ⛔ NOT derived against the mutation"* ⛔ unless you ⛔ actually derived it** — ⛔ **and ⛔ you usually cannot, because ⛔ you hold no compiler** (⭐ `SC-§78`).
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ MANAGER: ⛔ a row ⛔ NEVER says *"implement `<FINDING-ID>`"*. ⛔ It states the ⛔ DEFECT and the ⛔ PROOF OBLIGATION, and ⛔ relegates the prescribed shape to ⛔ *"recommended, ⛔ not mandated."*** ⛔ **⛔ If the manager ⛔ reads the source and finds the shape ⛔ broken, ⛔ SAY SO ⛔ ON THE ROW ⛔ BY NAME — ⛔ a silently-substituted shape teaches the next reader ⛔ nothing.**
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ IMPLEMENTER + ⛔ ITS GATE: ⛔ the acceptance test is ⛔ *"does it ⛔ RED under the ⛔ named mutation"*, ⛔ NEVER ⛔ *"does it ⛔ match the prescription"*.**
4. ⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CHEAPEST DETECTOR, ⛔ AND IT IS WHAT WORKED HERE: ⛔ A ⛔ SOURCE READ. ⛔ The falsification cost ⛔ ONE `Read` of the 75 lines around the site** — ⛔ **the prescription had travelled a ⛔ QA report, a ⛔ build handoff and a ⛔ dispatch ⛔ unexamined, because ⛔ each hop was ⛔ relaying a ⛔ correct finding.**
5. ⚖️ **KINSHIP: ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects — ⛔ this is `SHIP-§9` applied to the ⛔ REMEDY rather than the ⛔ GATE) · ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ a citation gains authority at ⛔ every hop while ⛔ nobody holds the evidence — ⛔ here the ⛔ payload is an ⛔ INSTRUCTION, which is ⛔ worse: ⛔ a bad fact ⛔ misinforms, a ⛔ bad instruction ⛔ gets built) · ⭐ `SC-§41` (⛔ call-shape pins) · ⭐ `SC-§83` (⛔ derived-not-witnessed reds) · ⭐ `SC-§99` (⛔ an instrument that has ⛔ never been shown a failure).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A CORRECT DIAGNOSIS IS ⛔ NOT A WARRANT FOR ITS OWN CURE — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ MORE CONVINCING THE DIAGNOSIS, THE ⛔ LESS ANYONE AUDITS THE CURE.***
6. ✅⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE ⛔ FIRST CLEAN EXHIBIT, ⛔ RECORDED ⛔ THE SAME DAY THIS LAW WAS WRITTEN, AND IT IS ⛔ THE OUTCOME THE LAW ASKS FOR** (added 2026-09-07; ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1118` / `qa/TASK-1119.md` § LOOP 1**).
   - ⛔ **THE PRESCRIPTION: at ⛔ loop 0 the gate suggested ⛔ STRENGTHENING `M16` by ⛔ driving ⛔ TEN TICKS after `Back`. ⛔ The author ⛔ DECLINED IT and ⛔ substituted his own.**
   - ⛔ **THE AUDIT, ⛔ RUN BY THE ⛔ PARTY WHO PRESCRIBED IT: the ten-tick shape ⛔ DERIVES ⛔ GREEN. ⛔ At zero the stranded tick reaches `DiscardStagedVideoMode`, whose ⛔ GUARD finds the window ⛔ ALREADY CLOSED by Back's own revert and ⛔ returns false ⇒ ⛔ the second revert is prevented ⛔ ONE LAYER DOWN ⇒ ⛔ NO TICK COUNT CAN MAKE THOSE ROWS LOAD-BEARING.** ⛔ **The author's substitute (⛔ the disarm ⛔ ZEROES the remainder ⇒ ⛔ exactly ⛔ TWO rows, ⛔ both immediately after `BackPressed()`, ⛔ at ⛔ zero ticks) is ⛔ STRICTLY STRONGER.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE GATE WROTE, ⛔ IN AS MANY WORDS, ⛔ "THE AUTHOR IS RIGHT AND I AM WITHDRAWN." ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ THIS LAW EXECUTING ⛔ CORRECTLY IN ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS: ⛔ the ⛔ IMPLEMENTER refused to ⛔ conform to a prescription, and the ⛔ GATE judged its ⛔ OWN prescription against the ⛔ MUTATION rather than against ⛔ its own authorship.***
   - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND EXHIBIT ⛔ IN THE SAME REPORT: the loop's ⛔ ONE WARN is against `SiegeGraphicsMenuWidget.cpp:1654-1655`, a sentence the ⛔ GATE ITSELF WROTE AT LOOP 0 and the author ⛔ adopted ⛔ VERBATIM (*"nothing the player can see moves"* — ⛔ FALSE: ⛔ the display ⛔ moves from the previous provisional mode to the confirmed one, ⛔ and it must).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A GATE WARNING AGAINST ⛔ ITS OWN PRESCRIPTION IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT CLAUSE 3 ASKS FOR ⇒ ⛔ RECORD IT, ⛔ NEVER SOFTEN IT.**

### SC-§100 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A DISPATCH THAT ⛔ NARROWS A ROW'S SCOPE HAS ⛔ CHANGED THE BOARD — AND ⛔ SAYING SO IS ⛔ PART OF THE DISPATCH, ⛔ NOT AN AFTERTHOUGHT. ⛔ AN UNRECORDED RESCOPE LEAVES THE BOARD ASSERTING A ⛔ DEPENDENCY THAT ⛔ NOBODY INTENDS AND ⛔ NOTHING WILL SATISFY.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager, ⛔ **on the orchestrator's ⛔ OWN confirmation — ⛔ measured on ⭐ `TASK-1117`, which was boarded as the host for ⭐ `TASK-1113` ⛔ AND ⭐ `TASK-1115` and ⛔ dispatched for `1113` ⛔ alone** · marker `SC-100-RESCOPE-IS-A-BOARD-EDIT`)

1. ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT: ⭐ `TASK-1117` carried `blocked-by: TASK-1114 AND TASK-1116`. ⛔ It was dispatched on ⭐ `TASK-1114`'s PASS ⛔ ALONE, for ⭐ `TASK-1113`'s files ⛔ only, ⛔ because ⭐ `TASK-1115` ⛔ had not been written.** ✅ **THE DECISION WAS ⛔ CORRECT** (⛔ *"holding a gated, tested layer hostage to an ⛔ unwritten menu ⛔ buys nothing"*). ⛔⛔ **⛔ IT WAS RECORDED ⛔ NOWHERE.**
2. ⛔⛔ **THE DAMAGE IS ⛔ NOT THE NARROWING — IT IS THE ⛔ RESIDUE: the board kept a blocker (⭐ `TASK-1116`) that had ⛔ never run and ⛔ could not run until its own upstream did ⇒ ⛔ the board briefly described a ⛔ CYCLE (`1115` → `1117` → `1116` → `1115`), and a ⛔ row's ⛔ HEADING went on claiming a ⛔ deliverable (*"the first playable checkpoint"*) that the ⛔ live run was ⛔ not producing.**
3. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A BOARD ROW IS A ⛔ CONTRACT AND A ⛔ DISPATCH IS ITS ⛔ EXECUTION. ⛔ WHEN THE EXECUTION IS ⛔ NARROWER THAN THE CONTRACT AND ⛔ ONLY THE EXECUTOR KNOWS, ⛔ EVERY OTHER READER IS REASONING FROM A ⛔ DOCUMENT THAT IS ⛔ NO LONGER TRUE — ⛔ AND THE BOARD IS THE ⛔ ONE ARTEFACT THIS PIPELINE TREATS AS ⛔ GROUND TRUTH.***
4. ✅⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ IF A DISPATCH ⛔ CHANGES A ROW'S ⛔ SCOPE, ⛔ BLOCKERS OR ⛔ DELIVERABLE, THE ⛔ BOARD EDIT LANDS ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION AS THE DISPATCH** — ⛔ **the ⛔ IDENTICAL mechanism the `GATE: OWED` abolition already imposes on gates, ⛔ for the ⛔ identical reason: ⛔ *"I will record it after"* is an ⛔ INTENTION, ⛔ NOT A MECHANISM.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ SCOPE FIELDS ARE THE ⛔ MANAGER'S** ⇒ ⛔ **the orchestrator ⛔ EITHER routes the rescope to the manager ⛔ IN THE SAME ACTION, ⛔ OR writes it into the row ⛔ LABELLED AS THE ORCHESTRATOR'S** (⛔ the ⛔ narrow standing licence already granted for ⛔ falsified pointers — ⛔ **⛔ a ⛔ labelled correction is ⛔ auditable; ⛔ a ⛔ faithfully-preserved falsehood is ⛔ not**).
   - ⛔ **⛔ NEVER ⛔ REWRITE A ⛔ LIVE ROW'S ⛔ HEADING to reflect a rescope: ⛔ agents ⛔ RE-LOCATE A MOVED ROW ⛔ BY ITS HEADING, and ⛔ that is the ⛔ ONLY recovery mechanism a concurrent writer has.** ✅ **Put the correction in a ⛔ CLAUSE that ⛔ says it ⛔ OUTRANKS THE HEADING, and ⛔ fix the heading ⛔ after the row closes.**
5. ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ HALF WORTH KEEPING, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ REASON THIS IS A ⛔ RECORD AND ⛔ NOT A GUESS: ⛔ the manager ⛔ SPOTTED the cycle, ⛔ DERIVED the likely rescope, and ⛔ REFUSED TO ACT ON IT — ⛔ flagging it ⛔ on the row and ⛔ asking instead.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ the orchestrator ⛔ confirmed it in ⛔ its own words, and the board now carries the ⛔ CAUSE rather than a ⛔ plausible reconstruction** (⭐ `SC-§97`). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ INFERRING A MISSING FACT AND ⛔ WRITING IT DOWN AS ⛔ ESTABLISHED IS HOW A ⛔ RUMOUR ENTERS THE RECORD. ⛔ INFERRING IT AND ⛔ ASKING IS HOW A ⛔ CAUSE DOES.***
6. ⚖️ **KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§97` (⛔ the ⛔ unmeasured inherited fact — ⛔ this is that problem ⛔ ONE LAYER UP: ⛔ the unrecorded ⛔ DECISION rather than the unmeasured ⛔ CLAIM) · the ⛔ `GATE: OWED` abolition (⛔ *an intention is not a mechanism*) · ⛔ WRITE-DISCIPLINE rule 6 (⛔ *a dispatch is ⛔ NOT a board entry* — ⛔ **⛔ rule 6 covers work ⛔ dispatched-but-unboarded; ⛔ THIS covers work ⛔ boarded-but-silently-narrowed, and ⛔ the second is ⛔ harder to see because the row ⛔ EXISTS and ⛔ looks fine**).**

### SC-§99 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A LOG PARSER THAT HAS ⛔ ONLY EVER SEEN GREEN IS ⛔ UNPROVEN — AND THE ⛔ CHEAPEST POSITIVE CONTROL THIS PROJECT HAS EVER FOUND IS A ⛔ BUILD YOU ⛔ DID NOT MEAN TO BREAK. ⛔ WHEN ONE HAPPENS, ⛔ RECORD IT AS EVIDENCE — ⛔ DO NOT DELETE IT AS AN EMBARRASSMENT.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **obtained ⛔ FREE on ⭐ `TASK-937` — a bad `sed` escape in its ⛔ FIRST `M5` attempt emitted a literal `tUpdateViewerSuppression();`, and the host's build-log parser returned ⛔ `Result: Failed (OtherCompilationError)` · marker `SC-99-FREE-POSITIVE-CONTROL`**)

1. ⛔⛔ **THE HOLE IT CLOSES IS THE ⛔ `Build.bat` EXIT-CODE LAW'S ⛔ OWN.** ⛔ That law says *"⛔ parse the log for `Result:`, ⛔ NEVER trust `$LASTEXITCODE`"* — ⛔ **and then ⛔ NOTHING anywhere asks whether the ⛔ parser can ⛔ SEE A RED.** ⛔ **A parser that greps for `Result: Succeeded` ⛔ correctly and a parser that ⛔ reports success ⛔ unconditionally are ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE ACROSS ⛔ ANY NUMBER OF ⛔ SUCCESSFUL BUILDS.**
2. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ EVERY GREEN A NEVER-RED PARSER PRODUCES IS ⛔ EQUALLY CONSISTENT WITH THE PARSER BEING ⛔ BROKEN. ⛔ THE `N`th GREEN ADDS ⛔ ZERO EVIDENCE THAT THE `N+1`th ⛔ RED WOULD BE ⛔ SEEN — AND ⛔ THE PROJECT'S ⛔ ENTIRE COMPILE-VERIFICATION CHAIN ⛔ HANGS OFF THAT ONE ⛔ UNTESTED BRANCH.***
3. ✅⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ WHEN A RUN PRODUCES AN ⛔ UNINTENDED FAILURE — ⛔ a typo'd mutation, a ⛔ bad `sed`, a ⛔ half-applied patch, a ⛔ stray brace — ⛔ QUOTE THE FAILURE LINE ⛔ VERBATIM IN THE HANDOFF AND ⛔ NAME IT AS A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL ON THE INSTRUMENT.** ⛔ **It costs ⛔ ONE SENTENCE, the evidence is ⛔ ALREADY PAID FOR, and for most lanes here it is the ⛔ ONLY red the parser will ⛔ ever be shown.**
4. ⛔ **AND ⛔ DO NOT MANUFACTURE ONE TO SATISFY THIS CLAUSE. ⛔ THIS IS A ⛔ HARVESTING RULE, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ BREAK-THE-BUILD RULE** — ⛔ the whole value is that the evidence is ⛔ FREE ⛔ when it arrives ⛔ on its own. ⛔ **A ⛔ deliberate breakage is a ⛔ mutation and owes a ⛔ byte-exact revert like any other.**
6. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 6 — ⛔ THE ⛔ COMPLIANT NEGATIVE, ⛔ RECORDED AS A ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT ⛔ BECAUSE THIS SECTION OTHERWISE ⛔ ONLY REWARDS A ⛔ LUCKY BREAKAGE.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **measured on ⭐ `TASK-1117` / `42734b7`**) ⛔ **⛔ ALL 15 MUTATION EDITS ⛔ APPLIED CLEANLY ⇒ ⛔ NO unintended failure arose ⇒ ⛔ NO harvest was ⛔ AVAILABLE. ⛔ The host ⛔ REFUSED to manufacture one (⛔ cl. 4), ⛔ controlled the ⛔ PARSE EXPRESSION against a ⛔ SYNTHETIC `Failed` log instead, and ⛔ NAMED THAT AS THE ⛔ WEAKER INSTRUMENT IT IS.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ *"I DID NOT GET ONE"* IS THE ⛔ COMPLIANT OUTCOME OF A ⛔ HARVESTING RULE, ⛔ NOT A GAP IN THE RUN. ⛔ A SYNTHETIC SUBSTITUTE MAY BE ⛔ REPORTED, ⛔ NEVER ⛔ EQUATED — ⛔ IT PROVES THE ⛔ EXPRESSION MATCHES A ⛔ STRING; ⛔ IT PROVES ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER THE ⛔ TOOLCHAIN WOULD ⛔ EMIT THAT STRING.*** ⛔ **⛔ A CONTROL IS ⛔ HARVESTED WHEN AVAILABLE, ⛔ DECLARED WHEN NOT, ⛔ AND ⛔ NEVER FABRICATED.**
7. ⚖️ **KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§96` (⛔ a positive control on the ⛔ READER) · ⭐ `SC-§102` (⛔ the ⛔ git surface's ⛔ silent zero — ⛔ the same control, ⛔ one instrument over) · ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ the positive control on a ⛔ NEEDLE) · ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 1 (⛔ a ⛔ NON-ZERO `N`) · ⭐ `SC-§98` (⛔ prove the ⛔ CHANNEL SPEAKS) · ⭐ `SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects, ⛔ never merely against success) · ⭐ `SC-§87` (⛔ a hang: ⛔ a lie by ⛔ OMISSION).** ⇒ 📌 ***⛔ `SC-§96` MAKES THE READER PROVE IT ⛔ READ. ⛔ `SC-§98` MAKES THE CHANNEL PROVE IT ⛔ SPOKE. ⛔ THIS ONE MAKES THE PARSER PROVE IT CAN ⛔ SAY NO.***

### SC-§98 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A LOG CHANNEL IS AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT WITH AN ⛔ OFF SWITCH. ⛔ SET THE VERBOSITY ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ READ IT BACK, AND ⛔ QUOTE THE LINE THAT PROVES THE CHANNEL ⛔ SPEAKS — ⛔ BEFORE YOU TRUST ITS ⛔ SILENCE.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **it has now fired ⛔ TWICE on this project — the ⛔ climb instrument 2026-09-02 and ⭐ `TASK-1106` 2026-09-07, where the channel was ⛔ MEASURED OFF ⛔ BEFORE the run · marker `SC-98-CHANNEL-SPEAKS`**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **PROMOTED FROM A ⛔ PER-ROW WARNING TO A ⛔ NAMED CLAUSE, ⛔ BECAUSE A WARNING THAT MUST BE ⛔ RE-TYPED INTO EVERY ROW IS ⛔ KEPT BY MEMORY — AND ⛔ MEMORY IS WHAT `GATE: OWED` DIED OF.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — THE RULE, ⛔ FOUR STEPS, ⛔ IN ORDER, ⛔ BEFORE THE RUN THAT MATTERS.** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ READ the verbosity ⛔ FIRST and ⛔ REPORT what you found** (⛔ *"it was already on"* and *"I turned it on"* are ⛔ different facts) · ⛔ **(b) ⛔ SET it** · ⛔ **(c) ⛔ READ IT BACK** (⭐ `SC-§94`: ⛔ the set is the ⛔ REQUEST, the read-back is the ⛔ RESULT) · ⛔ **(d) ⛔ TRIGGER A ⛔ KNOWN EMITTER AND ⛔ QUOTE THE LINE ⛔ VERBATIM IN THE HANDOFF.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ WITHOUT (d) you have a ⛔ configured channel, ⛔ not a ⛔ working one.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE TRAP IS ⛔ LIVE, ⛔ NOT THEORETICAL, AND BOTH FIRINGS SAY SO.** ⛔ **2026-09-02: the new climb probe printed ⛔ NOTHING without the verbosity set, and an ⛔ EMPTY LOG READS AS A ⛔ ZERO OFFSET — ⛔ a FALSE PASS.** ⛔ **2026-09-07: ⭐ `TASK-1106` read `GetVerbosity("LogGitClaudeUnrealTest")` = ⛔ `"Log"` ⛔ BEFORE its run** — ⛔ **and across ⛔ TWO full PIE sessions the `Verbose` channel emitted ⛔ EXACTLY ONE LINE, ⛔ WHICH WAS ITS OWN POSITIVE CONTROL.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A RUN THAT EMITTED ⛔ NOTHING WOULD HAVE LOOKED ⛔ IDENTICAL TO A ⛔ WORKING CHANNEL REPORTING A ⛔ CLEAN ZERO.***
3. ✅⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ BETTER MOVE, WHICH ⭐ `TASK-1106` ⛔ TOOK: ⛔ IF YOU CAN ⛔ READ THE STATE ⛔ LIVE, ⛔ DO NOT LOG IT AT ALL.** ⛔ **MCP + the editor's Python remote-execution channel read `GetActorRotation().Roll` and `GetControlRotation().Roll` ⛔ off the possessed instance across the ⛔ whole death timeline — ⛔ that is ⛔ strictly better than a probe (⭐ `SC-§94` cl. B: ⛔ it reads the ⛔ STATE, it does not ⛔ echo a request), and it ⛔ costs ⛔ no compile, ⛔ no diff and ⛔ no gate.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A ROW THAT ⛔ DECLINES TO WRITE THE INSTRUMENTATION IT WAS ⛔ OFFERED, ⛔ BECAUSE IT FOUND A ⛔ BETTER INSTRUMENT, IS ⛔ DOING THE JOB — ⛔ not skipping a clause.**
4. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 4 — KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§39` (⛔ the positive control, ⛔ here applied to a ⛔ LOG CHANNEL) · ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 1 (⛔ a run that started ⛔ ZERO tests) · ⭐ `SC-§96` (⛔ a comparator whose ⛔ reader is dead) · ⭐ `SC-§87` (⛔ a hang: ⛔ a lie by ⛔ OMISSION).** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ SAME FAMILY, ⛔ SAME CURE: ⛔ MAKE THE INSTRUMENT ⛔ PROVE IT SPOKE ⛔ BEFORE YOU BELIEVE ⛔ WHAT IT SAID — ⛔ and ⛔ SILENCE IS THE ⛔ HARDEST CASE, because it is the ⛔ one reading that ⛔ arrives looking like ⛔ good news.**

### SC-§97 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AN INHERITED *"FACT"* THAT ⛔ NO ROW EVER MEASURED IS A ⛔ CITATION, ⛔ NOT A MEASUREMENT — AND A CITATION ⛔ GAINS AUTHORITY AT ⛔ EVERY HOP WHILE ⛔ NOBODY ANYWHERE HOLDS THE ⛔ EVIDENCE.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **bought on the `RotationRate` claim — ⛔ FALSIFIED by ⭐ `TASK-1106` §5 with ⛔ TWO independent instruments ⛔ after it had travelled ⛔ FOUR FILES, ⛔ ONE BOARD ROW ⛔ TWICE, and ⛔ TWO verbal relays to 🧑 Jonathan · marker `SC-97-CITATION-NOT-MEASUREMENT`**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE CLAIM WAS ⛔ *"A ROLL THAT LANDS IS ⛔ HELD FOREVER."* ⛔ THE ⛔ MEASURED TRUTH IS THAT A ROLL ON A ⛔ LIVING HERO IS ⛔ GONE IN `0.119 s` — ⛔ THE ⛔ OPPOSITE, ⛔ NOT A REFINEMENT.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE CORRECTED LAW, ⛔ QUOTABLE, ⛔ SO NO ROW RESTATES IT FROM MEMORY AGAIN.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ FALSE (⛔ struck, ⛔ not deleted): ~~`RotationRate = (0, 500, 0)` ⇒ `PhysicsRotation` corrects ⛔ YAW ONLY ⇒ a roll that ⛔ LANDS is ⛔ HELD FOREVER; there is ⛔ no self-healing path.~~**
   - ✅ **⛔ MEASURED TRUTH, ⛔ BY ⭐ `TASK-1106` (gameplay-programmer, 2026-09-07), ⛔ TWO INSTRUMENTS: ⛔ `RotationRate.Roll = 0` does ⛔ NOT freeze roll — it ⛔ ARMS the engine's ⛔ SNAP-UPRIGHT OVERRIDE.** ⛔ **`CharacterMovementComponent.cpp:6698-6710`: when `bWantsToBeVertical`, a ⛔ `IsNearlyZero(DeltaRot.Roll)` is ⛔ OVERRIDDEN TO `360.0`, and `FMath::FixedTurn` with rate ⛔ `>= 360` returns the desired value ⛔ outright ⇒ ⛔ upright in ⛔ ONE TICK.** ⛔ **The CVar `p.PreventNonVerticalOrientationBlock` ⛔ DEFAULTS TO `1` (`:337`) and its ⛔ own help text says so.** ⛔ **⛔ LIVE CORROBORATION: an injected `89.9°` on a ⛔ LIVING, possessed, `MOVE_Walking` hero was ⛔ GONE IN `0.119 s` of world time; the ⛔ SAME injection on the ⛔ CORPSE ⛔ HELD, because `MOVE_None` + ⛔ unpossessed fails ⛔ BOTH `PhysicsRotation` guards (`:6636-6640` + `ShouldRemainVertical()`).**
   - ⇒ ⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CONSEQUENCE THAT ⛔ NARROWS EVERY FUTURE HUNT: ⛔ A ROLL ⛔ CANNOT SURVIVE ON A ⛔ LIVING HERO.** ⇒ ⛔ **any candidate mechanism that rolls the capsule ⛔ BEFORE `DisableMovement()` is ⛔ SELF-REFUTING; the ⛔ ONLY window in which a capsule roll can persist is ⛔ AFTER `DisableMovement()` and ⛔ BEFORE repossession.** ⛔ **The ⛔ reasoning that produced the false claim (⛔ *"rate 0 ⇒ `FixedTurn` holds current"*) is the ⛔ PRE-5.x behaviour, ⛔ overtaken by that CVar — ⛔ i.e. it was ⛔ once true, which is ⛔ exactly why it survived four hops.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE ROUTE, ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ BECAUSE THE ⛔ ROUTE IS THE LESSON.** ⛔ **ORIGIN: `handoffs/TASK-1102-programmer.md` §2b — a ⛔ HONEST, ⛔ PLAUSIBLE, ⛔ UNMEASURED ⛔ INFERENCE from a ctor value.** ⛔ **HOP 2: `qa/TASK-1103.md` ruling 5 + §*"what this does not mean"* — ⛔ quoted as ⛔ *"confirmed independently"*.** ⛔ **HOP 3: `handoffs/TASK-1104-buildmaster.md` §6(1) — ⛔ quoted as a ⛔ follow-up for the manager.** ⛔ **HOP 4: ⛔ the MANAGER wrote it into ⭐ `TASK-1106`'s ⛔ ROW HEADING and its ⛔ spec cl. (2)(c) as an ⛔ INHERITED FACT, ⛔ VERBATIM, ⛔ *"so you do not re-derive it"*.** ⛔ **HOP 5: ⛔ the ORCHESTRATOR relayed it to 🧑 Jonathan ⛔ TWICE as ⛔ established fact.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ AT ⛔ NO HOP DID ANYONE ⛔ LIE, AND AT ⛔ NO HOP DID ANYONE ⛔ MEASURE. ⛔ THE CLAIM ⛔ ACQUIRED ITS AUTHORITY ⛔ ENTIRELY FROM ⛔ BEING REPEATED BY ROLES THAT ⛔ EACH ASSUMED AN ⛔ EARLIER ROLE HAD CHECKED IT.***
   - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ AGGRAVATING FEATURE, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ MINE: ⛔ writing it into a spec as an ⛔ INHERITED FACT ⛔ *"so you do not re-derive it"* is a ⛔ DIRECT INSTRUCTION ⛔ NOT TO CHECK IT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A `(2)(c)`-style *"facts you inherit, verbatim"* block is ⛔ STILL CORRECT PRACTICE — ⛔ it stops five agents re-deriving the same thing — ⛔ BUT ⛔ EVERY ENTRY IN ONE NOW CARRIES ITS ⛔ VERB AND ITS ⛔ OWNER** (⛔ cl. 3).
3. ✅⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE RULE: ⛔ EVERY INHERITED FACT HANDED TO AN AGENT CARRIES ⛔ ONE OF THREE LABELS, ⛔ NO FOURTH OPTION.** ⛔ **`MEASURED BY TASK-###` (⛔ with the instrument) · ⛔ `DERIVED FROM <file:line>, READ AT <commit>` · ⛔ `INHERITED — ⛔ UNMEASURED, ⛔ CHECK IT IF YOUR CONCLUSION DEPENDS ON IT`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ AN UNLABELLED FACT IN A SPEC IS ⛔ ITSELF THE DEFECT — ⛔ exactly as an ⛔ ABSENT `GATE:` LINE is, ⛔ regardless of whether a gate was needed.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ SAME SPECIES AS ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2 (⛔ the `306` baseline that travelled ⛔ 190 TESTS) — ⛔ and ⛔ WORSE IN ⛔ ONE RESPECT: ⛔ `306` was a ⛔ NUMBER with the ⛔ WRONG VERB (⛔ *"censused"* read as *"executed"*); ⛔ this was a ⛔ MECHANISM with ⛔ NO VERB AT ALL.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a wrong number ⛔ corrupts one arithmetic; ⛔ a wrong mechanism ⛔ LICENSES REASONING — ⛔ it tells the next four rows ⛔ where to look and ⛔ where not to bother.**
4. ✅ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ THE PROPAGATION DUTY (⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2, ⛔ GENERALISED FROM ⛔ FIGURES TO ⛔ CLAIMS), ⛔ BINDING ON THE MANAGER ⛔ BY NAME: ⛔ when a claim is ⛔ FALSIFIED, the ⛔ SAME ACTION ⛔ GREPS EVERY FILE UNDER `.claude/pipeline/` FOR IT and ⛔ ANNOTATES ⛔ EVERY ASSERTION — ⛔ board rows, ⛔ QA reports and ⛔ handoffs alike.** ⛔ **⛔ STRIKE IN PLACE (`~~…~~`), ⛔ KEEP THE HISTORY, ⛔ STATE THE MEASURED TRUTH AND ⛔ WHO MEASURED IT (⭐ the `TASK-1083` style).** ⛔ **⛔ NEVER silently rewrite — ⛔ the author read that sentence and was ⛔ entitled to.**
   - ⛔ **⛔ AND ⛔ ANNOTATE THE ⛔ DOWNSTREAM ARTEFACTS ⛔ EVEN THOUGH THEY ARE ⛔ SOMEONE ELSE'S: a ⛔ QA report and a ⛔ handoff are ⛔ READ BY THE NEXT AGENT ⛔ as law. ⛔ A correction that lands ⛔ only on the board leaves ⛔ three uncorrected copies in the ⛔ exact files the ⛔ next diagnosis opens first** (⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2's ⛔ *"a correction recorded in ONE place does not correct the claim"*).
5. 🧑⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ THE ORCHESTRATOR'S ⛔ PUBLIC CORRECTION, ⛔ RECORDED AT ITS ⛔ OWN REQUEST, ⛔ BECAUSE A CORRECTION MADE ⛔ ONLY IN CHAT IS ⛔ THE SAME DEFECT AGAIN.** ⛔ **It relayed the false claim to 🧑 Jonathan ⛔ TWICE as ⛔ ESTABLISHED FACT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE FAILURE MODE IS ⛔ NAMED PRECISELY: ⛔ RELAYING AN ⛔ UNMEASURED INHERITED CLAIM — ⛔ it is ⛔ NOT a measurement error, ⛔ NOT carelessness, and ⛔ NOT a reason to distrust the ⛔ measurements in those same reports.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY DURABLE REMEDY IS ⛔ THE LABEL: ⛔ WHEN YOU TELL THE ⛔ HUMAN A MECHANISM, ⛔ SAY WHO MEASURED IT — AND IF THE ANSWER IS ⛔ "NOBODY", ⛔ SAY THAT INSTEAD.***
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2 (⛔ the propagating figure) · ⭐ `SC-§70` (⛔ a measurement quoted ⛔ WITHOUT ITS CAUSE) · ⭐ `SC-§40` cl. 1 (⛔ a relay is a ⛔ citation) · ⭐ `SC-§66` (⛔ authority ⛔ accumulating along a relay) · ⭐ `SC-§90` (⛔ a cause is a claim about ⛔ REACHABILITY, ⛔ settled by measurement).** ⇒ 📌 **⛔ THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A FACT WITHOUT AN ⛔ OWNER AND A ⛔ VERB IS A ⛔ RUMOUR WITH A `file:line` ATTACHED.**
7. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — THE ⛔ SIBLING DEFECT, ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-07 ON ⭐ `TASK-1114`'s ⛔ BLOCKER: ⛔ A TRACE MUST REACH A ⛔ TERMINATOR, ⛔ NOT MERELY A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE STOPPING POINT — BECAUSE A ⛔ PARTIAL TRACE READS ⛔ EXACTLY LIKE A ⛔ COMPLETE ONE.** (⛔ marker `SC-97-TRACE-TO-THE-TERMINATOR`)

### SC-§96 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A VERIFICATION LOOP MUST DISTINGUISH *"THE VALUES ⛔ DIFFER"* FROM *"I ⛔ FAILED TO READ A VALUE"* — AN ⛔ UNREAD VALUE COMPARES ⛔ UNEQUAL TO ⛔ EVERYTHING, SO A ⛔ BROKEN READER REPORTS ⛔ MAXIMUM ALARM AND LOOKS ⛔ EXACTLY LIKE A ⛔ CATASTROPHIC FAILURE.** (added 2026-09-06 by the manager; ⛔ **measured on ⭐ `TASK-1110`'s LFS pass — ⛔ `8 mismatches` that were ⛔ `8 × ""` · marker `SC-96-POSITIVE-CONTROL-ON-THE-READER`**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE READER WAS BROKEN AND THE ⛔ REPORT SAID *"EVERY SINGLE BINARY IS CORRUPT"*. ⛔ THE CORRECT RE-RUN SAID ⛔ `8/8 MATCH`.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE MEASURED EVENT, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ PORTABLE FACT INSIDE IT.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1110` verified its ⛔ eight LFS binaries by ⛔ index-oid-vs-`sha256`. ⛔ Its ⛔ FIRST pass used ⛔ cwd-relative paths with `git cat-file -p :<path>` ⇒ ⛔ **every read returned ⛔ EMPTY** ⇒ ⛔ the comparison reported ⛔ **8 MISMATCHES**, ⛔ which were ⛔ empty strings failing to equal a hash. ⛔ Re-run with ⛔ repo-root-relative paths: ⛔ **8/8 MATCH.**
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ MECHANICAL FACT, ⛔ WORTH ITS OWN LINE BECAUSE IT WILL ⛔ BITE AGAIN: ⛔ `git cat-file -p :<path>` RESOLVES ⛔ AGAINST THE ⛔ REPOSITORY ROOT, ⛔ NOT THE CWD.** ⛔ **This repo's root is ⛔ `C:/GitProjects/GitHub/GitClaudeUnrealTesting` ⇒ ⛔ every tracked path carries the ⛔ `GitClaudeUnrealTest/` PREFIX, ⛔ and the working directory is ⛔ one level inside it.** ⛔ **⛔ A path that is ⛔ correct for `Read`, `ls` and ⛔ every other tool is ⛔ WRONG for the index — ⛔ and the index ⛔ answers with ⛔ silence, ⛔ not an error.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE ⛔ DANGEROUS PROPERTY IS ⛔ NOT THAT IT FAILED. ⛔ IT IS THAT ⛔ FAILING TO READ AND ⛔ READING A WRONG VALUE PRODUCE THE ⛔ SAME VERDICT — AND THE ⛔ FAILURE MODE IS ⛔ LOUD, ⛔ TOTAL, AND ⛔ PERFECTLY PLAUSIBLE. ⛔ "ALL EIGHT ARE CORRUPT" IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT A REAL DISASTER LOOKS LIKE.***
   - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND-ORDER COST: a ⛔ maximum-alarm false positive ⛔ invites an ⛔ emergency response — ⛔ re-staging, ⛔ re-uploading, ⛔ reverting — ⛔ against files that were ⛔ FINE.** ⛔ **⛔ The broken reader does not merely ⛔ waste the check; ⛔ it ⛔ recruits the next action against ⛔ healthy state.**
2. ✅🚨 **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE RULE: ⛔ A COMPARISON LOOP CARRIES A ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL ⛔ ON ITS OWN READER, ⛔ RUN BEFORE THE ⛔ FIRST COMPARISON IS ⛔ TRUSTED.** ⛔ **Read ⛔ ONE value you ⛔ ALREADY KNOW and ⛔ ASSERT IT ⛔ NON-EMPTY (⛔ better: ⛔ assert it ⛔ EQUALS the known value).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IF the control is ⛔ empty, the run is ⛔ NOT MEASURED — ⛔ it is ⛔ NEVER a fail.**
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ CHEAP GENERAL FORM, ⛔ NO MATTER THE INSTRUMENT: ⛔ *"⛔ HOW MANY VALUES DID I ⛔ SUCCESSFULLY READ?"* ⛔ IS READ ⛔ BEFORE ⛔ *"⛔ HOW MANY MATCHED?"*** ⛔ **⛔ `reads = 0` and `matches = 0` is a ⛔ BROKEN READER; ⛔ `reads = N` and `matches = 0` is a ⛔ FINDING. ⛔ Quote ⛔ BOTH numbers or the verdict is ⛔ unreadable** (⛔ the ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 1 `N / M` discipline, ⛔ applied to a ⛔ comparator instead of a ⛔ suite).
   - ⛔ **⛔ AN ⛔ EMPTY STRING IS ⛔ NEVER A LEGITIMATE COMPARAND. ⛔ Any comparator that can be handed one ⛔ MUST refuse it ⛔ loudly — ⛔ a ⛔ silent `"" != "90526b41…"` is the ⛔ whole bug in one expression.**

A ⛔ exactly, ⛔ one instrument over). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A TEXTURE COMPRESSION CHANGE IS ⛔ VERIFIED BY ⛔ READING BACK THE ⛔ PIXEL FORMAT (`PF_BC…` vs `PF_B8G8R8A8`) ⛔ AND THE ⛔ RESOURCE SIZE — ⛔ NEVER by the ⛔ compression setting.**
