   - ⛔ **READER 2 — ⛔ TWO STRIKE READ-BACKS RETURNED ⛔ EMPTY because a ⛔ SINGLE-LINE regex was run against strikes that ⛔ SPAN LINES.** ⛔ **⛔ Not a missing strike; ⛔ a reader that ⛔ cannot see a multi-line one** (⛔ the `Grep` `multiline:` flag exists ⛔ precisely for this).
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ A GUARD THAT RETURNS THE ⛔ ANSWER YOU WANTED IS ⛔ NOT THEREBY A MEASUREMENT — ⛔ AND A ⛔ ZERO IS THE ⛔ ONE OUTPUT WHERE ⛔ *"NOTHING IS WRONG"* AND ⛔ *"I CANNOT SEE"* ARE ⛔ THE SAME CHARACTER.*** ⛔ **⛔ Reader 1 is the ⛔ mirror image of ⭐ `TASK-1110`'s: ⛔ there a dead reader screamed ⛔ MAXIMUM ALARM; ⛔ here a dead reader whispered ⛔ ALL CLEAR — ⛔ and ⛔ THIS direction is the ⛔ one nobody re-runs.**
   - ✅ **THE DUTY, ⛔ MECHANICAL: ⛔ ANY `grep`/filter over ⛔ GIT-DERIVED PATHS ⛔ anchors on the ⛔ REPO-ROOT-RELATIVE form (⛔ `GitClaudeUnrealTest/Source/`) or uses an ⛔ UNANCHORED match — ⛔ and it ⛔ PROVES ITSELF with a ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL (⛔ run the same pattern against a path you ⛔ KNOW is in the set and ⛔ assert ⛔ non-zero) ⛔ BEFORE the `0` is quoted as ⛔ evidence.**
   - ⚖️ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ IRONY IS THE ⛔ POINT, ⛔ NOT A JOKE: ⛔ `001b331` is the commit that ⛔ SHIPPED ⭐ `SC-§97` (*"⛔ an inherited fact nobody measured is a ⛔ CITATION"*) ⛔ and it ⛔ carried TWO unmeasured readers ⛔ inside its own verification.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ WRITING THE LAW IS ⛔ NOT OBEYING IT.**
3b. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3b — ⛔ THE ⛔ FACADE-COUNTER BLIND SPOT: ⛔ A COUNTER ON A ⛔ WRAPPER MEASURES THE ⛔ WRAPPER'S DECISION, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ WRAPPED BODY'S SIDE EFFECTS — SO A ⛔ WHOLE COUNTER SUITE CAN BE ⛔ GREEN ON A ⛔ REAL DISK WRITE.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager; ⛔ **measured by ⭐ `TASK-1114` § LOOP 1 on ⭐ `TASK-1113`'s `USiegeGraphicsSettingsSubsystem` · marker `SC-96-FACADE-COUNTER-BLIND-SPOT`**)
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT: the facade counts its ⛔ OWN `SaveSettings()` calls (`SaveSettingsCallCount`) and ⛔ twelve mutation tests assert on it. ⛔ `AutoDetectQuality()` reaches the engine's `ApplyHardwareBenchmarkResults`, whose ⛔ LAST LINE is `SaveSettings()` (`GameUserSettings.cpp:1142` ⛔ of `:1132-1143`) ⇒ ⛔ A ⛔ REAL `SaveConfig` TO DISK ⛔ AT COUNTER VALUE ⛔ `0`.** ⛔ **⛔ The counter read ⛔ `0` on the ⛔ BROKEN build ⛔ AND on the ⛔ FIXED one ⇒ ⛔ asserting it would have ⛔ PASSED ⛔ ON THE DEFECT.** ⛔ **The fix is a ⛔ SECOND instrument that counts the ⛔ REFUSAL (`RefusedSaveWhileVideoModePendingCount`), ⛔ not the call.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE SURVIVING GENERAL LAW: ⛔ A FACADE COUNTER MEASURES THE FACADE'S ⛔ DECISION, SO ⛔ ANY WRAPPED ENGINE BODY THAT ⛔ TERMINATES IN ITS OWN SAVE — ⛔ OR ITS OWN SIDE EFFECT — IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO THE ⛔ ENTIRE COUNTER SUITE.***
   - ✅⭐⭐ **HOW THE BLIND SPOT WAS ⛔ BOUNDED, AND ⛔ THIS IS THE PART TO ⛔ COPY: the gate answered *"⛔ does the counter suite have a ⛔ WIDER blind spot?"* ⛔ BY MEASUREMENT, ⛔ NOT BY ARGUMENT — it read ⛔ ALL ELEVEN engine calls the facade makes, ⛔ EACH TO ITS ⛔ CLOSING BRACE (⭐ `SC-§97` cl. 7 — ⛔ the trace-to-the-terminator law ⛔ bought by ⛔ THIS VERY BLOCKER — ⛔ on its ⛔ FIRST outing).** ⛔ **RESULT: the hole is ⛔ EXACTLY ⛔ ONE CALL WIDE — ⛔ only `ApplyHardwareBenchmarkResults` saves ⛔ inside itself; ⛔ `ApplySettings` has ⛔ ZERO call sites; ⛔ every other engine setter is a ⛔ PLAIN FIELD WRITE (`GameUserSettings.cpp:199-202`, `:956-969`, `:992-1005`) — ⛔ which ⛔ INCIDENTALLY CONFIRMS the suite's ⛔ HERMETICITY claim, since ⛔ none of them push a CVar.**

### SC-§95 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A RUN THAT STARTS ⛔ ZERO TESTS IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A ⛔ GREEN ONE — AND A ⛔ COUNT OF TEST MACROS ⛔ ON DISK IS ⛔ NOT A SUITE RESULT. ⛔ QUOTE `N / M` FROM AN ⛔ EXECUTION WITH A ⛔ NON-ZERO `N`, OR WRITE ⛔ "NOT MEASURED".** (added 2026-09-06 by the manager; ⛔ **bought TWICE on ⭐ `TASK-1104` — ⛔ once by its ⛔ first two runs and ⛔ once by the ⛔ number it inherited from ⛔ me · marker `SC-95-NONZERO-N`**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE FAIL COUNT READ `0` BOTH TIMES. ⛔ ONCE BECAUSE ⛔ 498 TESTS PASSED, AND ⛔ ONCE BECAUSE ⛔ NONE HAD BEEN ⛔ ASKED TO RUN.**

1. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ THE ⛔ ZERO-STARTED RUN. ⛔ A SUITE RUNNER WITH A ⛔ QUOTING BUG REPORTS ⛔ `0 started / 0 failed`, WHICH IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM GREEN TO ⛔ ANYTHING THAT READS ⛔ ONLY THE FAIL COUNT.**
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT: ⭐ `TASK-1104`'s ⛔ FIRST TWO ATTEMPTS ran ⛔ ZERO tests because ⛔ PowerShell ⛔ SPLIT THE `-ExecCmds` VALUE ⛔ ON ITS SPACES.** ⛔ **The engine then logged ⛔ `Cmd: Automation`, ⛔ `Ready to start automation`, and ⛔ IDLED TO THE BOOT BOUND — ⛔ no error, ⛔ no warning, ⛔ no failure.** ⛔ **⛔ `Result={Fail}` count: ⛔ `0`. ⛔ Correct, ⛔ honest, ⛔ and ⛔ meaningless.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE ⛔ FAILURE COUNT IS THE ⛔ WRONG NUMBER TO READ ⛔ FIRST. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ `0` ON A ⛔ PERFECT RUN AND ⛔ `0` ON A ⛔ RUN THAT ⛔ NEVER HAPPENED, AND ⛔ THOSE ARE THE ⛔ TWO CASES A GATE MOST NEEDS TO ⛔ TELL APART.***
   - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ `N` IS ⛔ READ FIRST AND IT MUST BE ⛔ NON-ZERO ⛔ AND ⛔ PLAUSIBLE.** ⛔ **Quote ⛔ BOTH halves — `498 / 0` — and ⛔ corroborate `N` against ⛔ `TEST_STARTED` == ⛔ `TEST_COMPLETE` == ⛔ `Result={Success}`+`Result={Fail}`.** ⛔ **⛔ A run whose `N` is `0`, or whose `N` ⛔ collapses versus the previous execution, is a ⛔ FINDING — ⛔ never a pass.**
   - ⭐ **AND THE ⛔ CHEAP CORROBORATION THAT COSTS NOTHING: ⛔ compare `N` against a ⛔ FRESH `^IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST` CENSUS (⭐ `TL-§5b`). ⛔ `N` ⛔ MATCHING the census is ⛔ strong; ⛔ `N` ⛔ far below it means ⛔ tests were ⛔ never reached** — ⛔ **⛔ but note the direction: ⛔ the census ⛔ VALIDATES an execution, ⛔ it ⛔ NEVER SUBSTITUTES for one (⛔ cl. 2).**
   - ⚖️ **KINSHIP: ⭐ `SC-§87` (⛔ a HANG lies by ⛔ OMISSION) · ⛔ the `Build.bat` law (⛔ an exit code lies by ⛔ COMMISSION) · ⛔ **THIS** (⛔ a runner lies by ⛔ ANSWERING A QUESTION ⛔ NOBODY ASKED).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ three instruments, ⛔ three lies, ⛔ ONE cure: ⛔ demand a ⛔ POSITIVE STATEMENT OF THE OUTCOME.**
   - 🚨⭐⭐ **⛔ SECOND INSTANCE ON THE ⛔ SAME FLAG, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-09 — ⛔ SEE ⭐ `SC-§116`, ⛔ WHICH HOLDS THE ⛔ PINNED RECIPE FOR ⛔ BOTH LANES.** ⛔ **⛔ THIS clause's defect is ⛔ PowerShell splitting `-ExecCmds` on ⛔ SPACES. ⛔ ⭐ `SC-§116`'s is the ⛔ ENGINE splitting it on ⛔ COMMA ⛔ AND NOT ON `;` (`ParseExecCommands.cpp:29`), ⛔ plus `Quit` ⛔ not quitting an ⛔ EDITOR commandlet (⛔ that is `QUIT_EDITOR`).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ SAME FLAG, ⛔ THREE INDEPENDENT WAYS TO ⛔ RUN NOTHING SILENTLY. ⛔ THE ⛔ TWO-SECOND CHECK THAT CATCHES ⛔ ALL THREE: ⛔ **⛔ GREP THE LOG FOR `Cmd: ` AND COUNT ⛔ ONE LINE PER COMMAND** ⛔ BEFORE YOU READ A SINGLE RESULT.**
2. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ A COUNT OF ⛔ TEST MACROS ⛔ ON DISK IS ⛔ NOT A SUITE RESULT. ⛔ ⭐ `TL-§5c` SAID THIS ⛔ FOUR DAYS EARLIER AND THE ⛔ WRONG NUMBER STILL TRAVELLED ⛔ 190 TESTS.**
   - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT: `306` originated at ⭐ `TASK-805` as a ⛔ static `^IMPLEMENT_` census, was written as ⛔ `306/306`, and was ⛔ STILL BEING QUOTED AS *"the shipped baseline"* in ⛔ TWO fresh 2026-09-06 specs (⭐ `TASK-1102` cl. 4, ⭐ `TASK-1104` cl. 3) — ⛔ both ⛔ written by the ⛔ MANAGER, ⛔ 190 tests after it stopped being true.**
   - ✅ **THE ⛔ CORRECTED LEDGER, ⛔ EXECUTIONS ⛔ ONLY: `301 / 301` at `8910c17` → ⛔ *(a two-commit gap where three records carried `306/306` as a pass)* → `496 / 0` at `c6bb376` (⭐ `TASK-1074`) → ⛔ **`498 / 0` at `eeb29c4`** (⭐ `TASK-1104`).** ⇒ ⛔ **`496 + 2 = 498`: ⛔ the ⛔ ARITHMETIC WAS RIGHT AND THE ⛔ BASE WAS WRONG — ⛔ which is ⛔ the most dangerous combination, because ⛔ the delta checks out and ⛔ the check feels done.**
   - ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE SECTION IN ONE LINE: ⛔ QUOTE `N / M` ⛔ FROM AN EXECUTION, ⛔ OR SAY ⛔ *"NOT MEASURED"*.** ⛔ **⛔ There is ⛔ no third form. ⛔ *"The shipped baseline is X"* in a ⛔ SPEC is ⛔ banned unless X is ⛔ labelled with the ⛔ commit whose ⛔ execution produced it** (⭐ `TL-§5b` cl. 1's *"stale anchor"* discipline, ⛔ tightened: ⛔ name the ⛔ COMMIT, ⛔ not just the staleness).
   - 🚨⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ ACTUAL FINDING, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ NOT ABOUT THE NUMBER: ⛔ THE CORRECTION ⛔ ALREADY EXISTED. ⛔ `handoffs/LADDER-REGRESSION-diagnosis.md` had established it, ⭐ `TL-§5c` had ⛔ codified it, and ⭐ `TASK-805`'s ⛔ own board row ⛔ carried it in full.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ ***⛔ A CORRECTION RECORDED IN ⛔ ONE PLACE ⛔ DOES NOT CORRECT THE NUMBER. ⛔ THE FALSE FIGURE LIVES IN ⛔ EVERY ROW THAT ⛔ COPIED IT, AND ⛔ NONE OF THEM READS THE PLACE WHERE IT WAS FIXED.***

### SC-§94 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AN INSTRUMENT THAT ⛔ ECHOES THE REQUEST INSTEAD OF ⛔ MEASURING THE RESULT IS ⛔ NOT A MEASUREMENT — AND A ⛔ LADDER OF GATES THAT EACH VERIFY ONE LAYER ⛔ DEEPER CAN ⛔ NEVER REACH THE ⛔ OUTCOME.** (added 2026-09-05 by the manager; ⛔ **bought at ⛔ THREE 🧑 *"no fog"* REPORTS · diagnosis `handoffs/TASK-1071-artist-diagnosis.md` §4 · marker `SC-94-READ-BACK`**)

⛔⛔ **THE ONE-LINE INCIDENT: `AFogVolume::SpawnFogVisual()` logged `Fog VISUAL spawned: … scale (640, 360, 260)`. ⛔ THAT NUMBER IS THE ⛔ ARGUMENT IT PASSED IN. ⛔ The engine substituted the vendor SCS template's ⛔ `(20,20,5)` (`SCS_Node.cpp:141`, ⛔ `bIsDefaultTransform = true` from `Actor.cpp:4358`), and the log ⛔ never read `Spawned->GetActorScale3D()` back.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ 2,000 × 2,000 × 500 uu slab ⛔ 6,750 uu above the field, ⛔ ~21,200 uu from the hero, ⛔ outside a `VolumetricFogDistance = 6000` froxel grid ⇒ ⛔ ⛔ RENDERS NOTHING, FOR ANYONE, ALWAYS — ⛔ while ⛔ every character in the log was ⛔ TRUE.**

⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE VALUE WAS ⛔ NEVER WRONG. ⛔ IT WAS ⛔ NEVER READ. ⛔ AND SILENCE ABOUT AN OUTCOME IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM THE OUTCOME BEING ⛔ RIGHT.***

#### ⛔ CL. A — THE INSTRUMENT HALF

- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **WHY THIS IS ⛔ ITS OWN SECTION AND ⛔ NOT AN INSTANCE UNDER AN EXISTING ONE: ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ FIRST CATALOGUED INSTRUMENT LIE THAT LIED ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION RATHER THAN ⛔ BY DEFECT.** ⛔ **`Build.bat`'s exit code is ⛔ BROKEN. The `QueueEmpty` probe is ⛔ BROKEN (⭐ `SHIP-§9f`). A single-shot volumetric capture is ⛔ UNCONVERGED (⭐ `SC-§88`). The PNG-byte metric is ⛔ CONFOUNDED (⭐ `SC-§85`).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ EVERY ONE OF THOSE HAS A ⛔ BUG YOU COULD FIX. ⛔ THIS LOG LINE HAD ⛔ NONE. ⛔ It was working ⛔ EXACTLY AS WRITTEN, and ⛔ what it was written to do was the ⛔ wrong thing.**
- ⚠️⛔ **THE TALLY IS ⛔ RELAYED AND I HOLD ⛔ NO INSTRUMENT TO CENSUS IT: it reached me as ⛔ *"eleven instrument lies"* ⇒ ⛔ ⛔ TREAT IT AS A ⛔ LOWER BOUND** (⭐ `SC-§91`) ⛔ **and ⛔ NEVER quote it as a total.** ⛔ **⛔ The ⛔ CLASSIFICATION — ⛔ *by construction* vs ⛔ *by defect* — is the ⛔ load-bearing claim here, ⛔ not the count.**
- ✅⛔⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ ANY log line, assertion, handoff sentence or QA claim about a value the ⛔ ENGINE APPLIES must quote a ⛔ READ-BACK obtained ⛔ AFTER the operation, ⛔ FROM THE ENGINE — ⛔ never the value passed in.** ⛔ **⛔ A ⛔ WRITE-THEN-LOG-THE-WRITE is a ⛔ DEFECT ON SIGHT, ⛔ in review, ⛔ with no further argument required.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ TELL, SO IT IS ⛔ FINDABLE BY ⛔ READING AND ⛔ NOT ONLY IN HINDSIGHT: ⛔ THE LOGGED EXPRESSION AND THE ⛔ ARGUMENT TO THE CALL ARE THE ⛔ SAME SYMBOL.** ⛔ `SpawnActor(Class, SpawnTransform, …)` ⛔ followed by `UE_LOG(… SpawnTransform …)`. ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IF THE ⛔ TWO NAMES MATCH, YOU ARE ⛔ PRINTING YOUR OWN VARIABLE.**
- ⇒ ⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ FALSIFIABLE TEST, WHICH IS A ⛔ MUTATION TEST POINTED AT AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT: ⛔ ⛔ DELETE THE OPERATION. ⛔ IF THE INSTRUMENT'S OUTPUT IS ⛔ UNCHANGED, THE INSTRUMENT WAS ⛔ NEVER MEASURING THE OPERATION.** ⛔ **Here: ⛔ delete the `SpawnActor` call and the log ⛔ still prints `(640, 360, 260)` ⇒ ⛔ it was ⛔ never about the spawn.** ⛔ **⛔ This generalises to ⛔ every probe, ⛔ counter and ⛔ status line in the pipeline and it costs ⛔ one thought.**
- ⛔ **WHERE IT BINDS, ⛔ ENUMERATED SO NOBODY SCOPES IT TO FOG: ⛔ spawn transforms (⛔ scale, ⛔ rotation, ⛔ location, ⛔ attachment, ⛔ SCS roots) · ⛔ ASSET IMPORTS AND REIMPORTS (⛔ *"reported `DONE`"* is ⛔ the same shape) · ⛔ MCP property writes · ⛔ material-parameter sets · ⛔ nav/collision rebuilds · ⛔ anything whose ⛔ APPLIED value the engine is ⛔ free to substitute, ⛔ clamp, ⛔ round or ⛔ ignore.**
- ⛔ **⛔ AND THE COROLLARY THAT COSTS ⛔ NOTHING: a read-back must come from the ⛔ ENGINE'S OWN QUERY, ⛔ never from a ⛔ mirror you also wrote.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ reading back your own cached copy is the ⛔ SAME DEFECT with ⛔ one more hop in it.**
- ⭐ **FAMILY, ⛔ AND THE DISTINCTIONS ARE THE ⛔ USEFUL PART: ⭐ `SC-§88` — ⛔ the number was ⛔ WRONG · ⭐ `SC-§92` — ⛔ the agreement was ⛔ CLAIMED · ⭐ `SC-§79` — ⛔ the ⛔ SCOPE was unstated · ⛔ the standing ⛔ *"a success return is not evidence"* — ⛔ the ⛔ CHANNEL was mistaken for the ⛔ RESULT.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ §94 IS THE ⛔ SHARPEST OF THEM: THE NUMBER WAS ⛔ RIGHT, ⛔ HONEST AND ⛔ ABOUT THE ⛔ WRONG THING — WHICH IS WHY IT ⛔ SURVIVED SPOT-CHECKING, ⛔ SURVIVED A GATE, AND ⛔ WAS QUOTED TO 🧑 JONATHAN AS REASSURANCE.***

#### ⛔ CL. B — THE GATE HALF: **⛔ THE LADDER THAT ⛔ NEVER REACHES THE TOP**

- ⛔⛔ **THE RECORD, ⛔ THREE GATES, ⛔ EACH ONE LAYER DEEPER, ⛔ EACH TRUE, ⛔ EACH INSUFFICIENT, ⛔ AND 🧑 JONATHAN PAID FOR EVERY RUNG WITH A ⛔ *"NO FOG"* REPORT:**
  - ⛔ **RUNG 1 — ⛔ PROPERTIES** (⭐ `TASK-1043`): ⛔ *"is the asset ⛔ CORRECT?"* ⇒ ⛔ **YES. ⛔ And it was ⛔ INERT** (⭐ `SC-§36.1` INSTANCE 2).
  - ⛔ **RUNG 2 — ⛔ REACHABILITY** (⭐ `TASK-1069`): ⛔ *"does a caller ⛔ EXIST, and is it ⛔ REACHED?"* ⇒ ⛔ **YES. ⛔ And it was ⛔ INVISIBLE.**
  - ⛔ **RUNG 3 — ⛔ ACHIEVED STATE** (⭐ `TASK-1073`): ⛔ *"did the engine ⛔ APPLY what we asked?"* ⇒ ⛔ **NECESSARY. ⛔ STILL NOT THE OUTCOME.**
  - ⛔⛔ **RUNG 4 — ⛔ THE ONE NOBODY ASKED: ⛔ ⛔ *DOES THE THING THE PLAYER SEES ⛔ CHANGE?*** ⇒ ⛔ **for a ⛔ VISUAL that means a ⛔ PIXEL MEASUREMENT AGAINST A ⛔ ZERO-CONTROL, ⛔ NEVER a code reading, ⛔ never a green suite, ⛔ never a log line.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ EACH RUNG WAS A ⛔ TRUE AND INSUFFICIENT ANSWER, AND A ⛔ TRUE ANSWER IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT STOPS THE SEARCH. ⛔ THREE CORRECT VERDICTS IN A ROW READ AS ⛔ CONVERGING ON THE PROBLEM WHEN THEY WERE ⛔ CLIMBING PAST IT.***
- ✅⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ EVERY gate ⛔ NAMES THE RUNG IT IS ON ⛔ AND THE RUNG ⛔ ABOVE IT THAT IT DOES ⛔ NOT REACH, ⛔ AND NAMES THE ⛔ ROW OR THE ⛔ PERSON WHO OWNS THAT RUNG.** ⛔ **A gate that does ⛔ not say what it ⛔ cannot see ⛔ reads as complete** — ⛔ **this is ⭐ `SC-§79` pointed at the ⛔ LADDER instead of at a ⛔ single check.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ ORDERING RULE, WHICH IS THE ⛔ ACTIONABLE HALF AND THE ⛔ EXPENSIVE ONE: ⛔ THE LADDER WAS CLIMBED ⛔ BOTTOM-UP, ⛔ ONE RUNG PER PLAYTEST.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ WHEN A ⛔ DIRECT OUTCOME MEASUREMENT ⛔ EXISTS, ⛔ TAKE IT ⛔ FIRST AND ⛔ DESCEND ⛔ ONLY IF IT FAILS.**
- ⛔ **⛔ THE COST COMPARISON IS ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ NOT RHETORICAL: ⛔ THREE GATES + ⛔ THREE 🧑 REPORTS ⛔ SETTLED NOTHING. ⛔ ⛔ ONE ARTIST SESSION WITH A ⛔ ZERO-CONTROL SETTLED IT ⛔ COMPLETELY — ⛔ control `0.261408` · ⛔ achieved `0.261480` (⛔ `+0.04%`, ⛔ inside the noise floor) · ⛔ intended `0.452790` (⛔ `+73%`).** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ the ⛔ TOP RUNG was ⛔ CHEAPER THAN ⛔ ANY of the three below it, and it was taken ⛔ LAST.**
- ⛔ **⛔ WHY THE TWO HALVES ARE ⛔ ONE SECTION AND ⛔ NOT TWO: ⛔ they are the ⛔ SAME ASYMMETRY AT ⛔ TWO ALTITUDES. ⛔ The log verified the ⛔ REQUEST and not the ⛔ RESULT; the gates verified the ⛔ MECHANISM and not the ⛔ OUTCOME.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ BOTH CHECKED THE SIDE ⛔ WE CONTROL INSTEAD OF THE SIDE ⛔ THE PLAYER SEES.*** ⛔ **⛔ SPLIT THEM AND A READER FIXES ⛔ ONE AND BELIEVES THEY ARE ⛔ DONE — ⛔ which is ⛔ precisely the failure cl. B ⛔ documents.**
- 🧑 ⛔ **⛔ AND THE RUNG ⛔ ABOVE RUNG 4, ⛔ WHICH NO AGENT OWNS: ⛔ *"does it look RIGHT?"* ⇒ ⛔ 🧑 Jonathan's eye is the ⛔ ONLY instrument for ⛔ LEGIBILITY** (⭐ `AS-§6 A(e)`). ⛔ **⛔ A `+73%` luma delta proves the fog is ⛔ THERE. ⛔ It does ⛔ NOT prove it ⛔ READS as fog ⛔ to him.**

#### ⛔ CL. D — **THE ⛔ WRITE HALF, AND THE ⛔ CONTAMINATED HALF** (added 2026-09-08 by the manager; ⛔ **both bought on ⭐ `TASK-1154`/`TASK-1152`, ⛔ same day, ⛔ same lane · marker `SC-94-D-SILENT-NOOP`**)

- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **cl. A is a ⛔ READ that echoes the request. ⛔ cl. D is the ⛔ SAME LIE WITH THE ⛔ WRITE ON THE OTHER SIDE: ⛔ A ⛔ SILENT NO-OP.** ⛔ **⛔ MEASURED: `set_properties` on `BP_SiegeFog`'s `Box Materials` `TMap` — ⛔ TWO payload shapes, ⛔ BOTH returned ⛔ WITHOUT error, ⛔ read-back ⛔ UNCHANGED. ⛔ The CDO `material` override slot ⛔ stored the value and the engine ⛔ ignored it.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SUCCESS RETURN IS ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE OF A WRITE. ⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY EVIDENCE OF A WRITE IS THE ⛔ VALUE, ⛔ READ BACK, ⛔ FROM THE ENGINE, ⛔ AFTER.***
- ✅⭐⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT, ⛔ RECORDED AS ONE BECAUSE THIS FILE IS ⛔ THICK WITH FAILURES AND ⛔ THIN WITH THE THING THAT ⛔ CAUGHT ONE: ⛔ THE ROW'S ⛔ POST-WRITE ASSERTION ⛔ CAUGHT THE NO-OP, AND THE AGENT ⛔ REFUSED TO SAVE A BLUEPRINT WHOSE COLOUR ROUTE ⛔ DID NOTHING.** ⛔ **⛔ THE ALTERNATIVE OUTCOME WAS A ⛔ SAVED ASSET, A ⛔ GREEN HANDOFF, A ⛔ COMMIT, AND 🧑 JONATHAN LOOKING AT ⛔ THE SAME BEIGE WHILE THE BOARD SAID ⛔ *"GREY SHIPPED"*.** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ THE RULE: ⛔ EVERY MCP/property WRITE IS ⛔ FOLLOWED BY A ⛔ READ-BACK ASSERTION, ⛔ AND A ⛔ FAILED ASSERTION ⛔ REFUSES THE SAVE ⛔ RATHER THAN REPORTING PARTIAL SUCCESS. ⛔ A row that ⛔ half-lands is ⛔ worse than one that ⛔ stops, because ⛔ only the second one tells you.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CONTAMINATED-INSTRUMENT COROLLARY, ⛔ CAUGHT AND ⛔ DISCARDED BY THE AGENT ITSELF: ⛔ TWO scripts drove the editor at once (⛔ `pkill` ⛔ SILENTLY FAILS on Windows `python.exe`) and the run was killed by a ⛔ MONOTONICITY VIOLATION — ⛔ NOT by an error. ⛔ The bad numbers ⛔ LOOKED CLEAN (⛔ D 482–501, ⛔ 1.04× spread).** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CONTAMINATED INSTRUMENT DOES ⛔ NOT REPORT AN ERROR; ⛔ IT REPORTS A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE NUMBER. ⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY TELL IS AN ⛔ INVARIANT THE DATA ⛔ SHOULD HAVE OBEYED AND ⛔ DIDN'T.*** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ ANY MEASUREMENT SERIES ⛔ CARRIES AT LEAST ⛔ ONE INVARIANT IT CAN ⛔ VIOLATE (⛔ monotonicity, ⛔ ordering, ⛔ a known-zero, ⛔ a positive control) — ⛔ a series with ⛔ NO falsifiable invariant ⛔ cannot detect its own contamination, ⛔ and *"the numbers looked reasonable"* is ⛔ NOT a check.** ⛔ **⛔ AND ON WINDOWS: ⛔ a process-kill is ⛔ VERIFIED BY A ⛔ SUBSEQUENT ENUMERATION, ⛔ never by the kill's return.**

### SC-§93 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLASSIFY BY WHAT THE ⛔ COMPILER SEES, ⛔ NEVER BY HOW THE ⛔ TEXT READS. ⛔ *"COMMENT-ONLY"* AND ⛔ *"BEHAVIOUR-NEUTRAL"* ARE ⛔ DIFFERENT PROPERTIES — AND THIS BOARD ⛔ CONFLATED THEM ⛔ THREE TIMES IN ⛔ ONE NIGHT.** (added 2026-09-05 by the manager; ⛔ **bought in `qa/TASK-1058.md` §2.2-2.3 + ⛔ F-1 — ⛔ AND THE DEFECT WAS IN A ROW ⛔ THE MANAGER WROTE · marker `SC-93-COMPILER-SEES`**)

> ### ⭐⭐ **THE ONE SENTENCE, ⛔ QUOTED FROM THE GATE THAT BOUGHT IT, SO THE NEXT AUTHOR CAN APPLY IT ⛔ WITHOUT ASKING ANYBODY:**
> ### ⛔ ***"Text inside `TEXT("…")` is ⛔ CODE, ⛔ never a comment — so editing it ⛔ ALWAYS owes a ⛔ COMPILE (and a suite run); whether it ⛔ ALSO owes an `SC-§83` decoy depends on whether the ⛔ PIN'S DECISION PROCEDURE changed, ⛔ never on whether the edited text happens to ⛔ read like prose."***

1. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 1 — ⛔ TWO QUESTIONS, ⛔ ASKED SEPARATELY, ⛔ IN THIS ORDER. ⛔ COLLAPSING THEM INTO ONE IS THE ⛔ WHOLE DEFECT.**
   ⛔ **(a) ⛔ CLASSIFICATION — ⛔ WHAT DOES THE ⛔ LEXER SEE?** ⛔ comment ⛔ vs ⛔ token. ⇒ ⛔ **this decides whether a ⛔ COMPILE and a ⛔ SUITE RUN are owed, and ⛔ nothing else.**
   ⛔ **(b) ⛔ BURDEN — ⛔ WHAT ⛔ CHANGED?** ⛔ an ⛔ assertion MESSAGE ⇒ ⛔ compile only · a ⛔ PREDICATE or a ⛔ pin's ⛔ DECISION PROCEDURE ⇒ ⛔ compile ⛔ + ⭐ `SC-§83` decoy ⛔ + ⭐ `SC-§39` control.
   ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ (a) AND (b) ARE ⛔ INDEPENDENT. A DIFF CAN BE ⛔ FULLY EXECUTABLE AND STILL OWE ⛔ NO DECOY — ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ NOT A CONTRADICTION, IT IS THE ⛔ NORMAL CASE FOR A MESSAGE STRING, AND `qa/TASK-1058.md` RULED ⛔ EXACTLY THAT COMBINATION (⛔ branch (a) ⛔ CORRECT, ⛔ 8 executable lines, ⛔ no decoy owed).***
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ THE THREE INSTANCES, ⛔ ALL 2026-09-05, ⛔ ALL THE ⛔ SAME SHAPE: A ⛔ CLASSIFICATION INFERRED FROM ⛔ HOW TEXT READS.**
   ⛔ **(I1) `Tests/SiegeBrightSunTest.cpp:300` — a `TEXT("…")` spanning three lines as the ⛔ FIRST ARGUMENT of `TestEqual(…)`, ⛔ batched into a ⛔ comment-only row because it ⛔ reads as prose.** ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1054.md:324-326` ruled it ⛔ EXECUTABLE — *"a ⛔ string literal that is a ⛔ function-call argument … ⛔ compiled into the binary"* — ⇒ ⛔ split out as its own row (⭐ `TASK-1064`), ⛔ which is the ⛔ correct cure.**
   ⛔ **(I2) ⭐ `TASK-1057` cl. 3(a) — ⛔ MINE.** ⛔ **The spec's own parenthetical read *"Comment text. ⛔ Keeps this row at ⛔ zero executable lines."* ⛔ It was ⛔ FALSE: the site is a `TEXT("…")` fragment in a ⛔ LIVE `TestTrue(FString::Printf(…))` argument.** ⇒ ⛔ **the author ⛔ INHERITED THE VOCABULARY IN GOOD FAITH and ⛔ *"zero executable lines"* shipped on ⛔ THREE ARTEFACTS (⛔ spec · ⛔ handoff §4/§6.5 · ⛔ board `status:`) while the ⛔ true figure was ⛔ 8 (⛔ whole-file `545 → 549`).**
   ⛔ **(I3) `Tests/SiegeFogRetentionWiringTest.cpp`, residual (iii)'s exposure sentence — ⛔ the ⛔ CLAIM is general (*"⛔ NOT ONE string literal"*) while the ⛔ REASON offered is a ⛔ `TEXT(`-ONLY census.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a ⛔ SPELLING standing in for a ⛔ LEXICAL CLASS: a plain `"…"` is ⛔ exposed identically and the stated reason ⛔ steps over it.** ⛔ **Boarded as ⭐ `TASK-1067` (⛔ from `qa/TASK-1058.md` ⛔ F-3).**
3. 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ AND IT CUTS ⛔ BOTH WAYS: A ⛔ `TEXT(` GREP HIT IS ⛔ NOT PROOF OF ⛔ CODE, EITHER.**
   ⛔ **MEASURED by the manager 2026-09-05, ⛔ before typing *"comment-only"* on ⭐ `TASK-1067`: the doc block above `CodeWithoutTrailingComments` in `Tests/SiegeFogRetentionWiringTest.cpp` contains the characters `TEXT(` as ⛔ BACKTICKED PROSE, ⛔ inside a `/** … */` block whose opener and closer were ⛔ both verified with ⛔ nothing between.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ needle census would have flagged a ⛔ COMMENT as ⛔ CODE.**
   ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE CLASSIFIER IS THE ⛔ ENCLOSING LEXICAL STATE — ⛔ comment · ⛔ literal · ⛔ token — ⛔ NEVER THE NEEDLE, AND ⛔ NEVER THE PROSE-LIKENESS.*** ⛔ **⛔ Both failure directions are ⛔ live in ⛔ ONE FILE, ⛔ 40 lines apart.**
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ WHAT A ROW, A HANDOFF OR A `status:` LINE MUST NOW CARRY BEFORE IT MAY SAY ⛔ *"COMMENT-ONLY"*.**
   ⛔ **Say ⛔ HOW it was established — ⛔ *"inside the `/** … */` block at `<symbol>`, ⛔ opener and closer verified"* — ⛔ not merely ⛔ THAT it is.** ⛔ **⛔ AND NEVER ON A ⛔ SPEC'S AUTHORITY: a spec's classification is a ⛔ CITATION (⭐ `SC-§78`), and ⭐ `TASK-1057` is the ⛔ proof that a spec can be ⛔ wrong.**
   🚨⛔ **⛔ THE MANAGER'S HALF, ⛔ WRITTEN AGAINST MYSELF: a row that ⛔ PRE-CLASSIFIES ITS OWN DELIVERABLE is making a claim the author ⛔ WILL INHERIT ⛔ AND WILL REPEAT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ classify by ⛔ OPENING THE FILE, or ⛔ do ⛔ NOT classify at all and ⛔ make the classification the ⛔ AUTHOR'S FIRST DELIVERABLE.**
5. ⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ THE COST, ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ SO NOBODY GRADES THIS AS PEDANTRY.** ⛔ **An ⛔ unexecuted `FString::Printf` format string is ⛔ evaluated ⛔ EAGERLY on the ⛔ HAPPY path, ⛔ every iteration** (`qa/TASK-1058.md` §6) ⇒ ⛔ **a stray `%` would have run on ⛔ every green run.** ⛔ **⛔ It was caught by a gate holding ⛔ NO COMPILER, ⛔ by READING — ⛔ which is ⛔ luck's shape, ⛔ not a process.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§83` (⛔ the ⛔ BURDEN half — ⛔ this section is what decides ⛔ whether §83 is even ⛔ the right question) · ⭐ `SC-§78` / ⭐ `SC-§79` (⛔ a relayed classification is a ⛔ citation; ⛔ *"comment-only"* asserted without a method is ⛔ indistinguishable from ⛔ *"I did not look"*) · ⭐ `SC-§90` (⛔ a ⛔ CLASSIFICATION is a claim about ⛔ what the compiler reaches ⇒ ⛔ settle it ⛔ structurally, ⛔ never by plausibility) · ⭐ `SC-§91` (⛔ *"zero executable lines"* is a ⛔ COUNT, and ⛔ every count in this incident came back ⛔ HIGHER) · ⭐ `SC-§92` (⛔ two instruments ⛔ agreeing — ⛔ the tokenizer and the reviewer's ⛔ reading ⛔ both said ⛔ CODE). ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ ASK THE ⛔ LEXER WHAT IT IS, ⛔ THEN ASK THE ⛔ DIFF WHAT CHANGED — ⛔ AND ⛔ NEVER LET THE ⛔ SECOND ANSWER DECIDE THE ⛔ FIRST.**

### SC-§92 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AN ⛔ UNCLAIMED INCIDENTAL AGREEMENT IS ⛔ STRONGER EVIDENCE OF AN INSTRUMENT'S FIDELITY THAN ⛔ ANY AGREEMENT ITS AUTHOR ⛔ ASSERTS — ⛔ BECAUSE IT ⛔ CANNOT HAVE BEEN TUNED.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **bought in `qa/TASK-1046.md` §6.2 — ⛔ the ⛔ STRONGEST VALIDATION IN THE WHOLE GATE WAS ONE ⛔ NOBODY CLAIMED · marker `SC-92-UNCLAIMED-AGREEMENT`**)

- ⛔ **THE INCIDENT: ⭐ `TASK-1045`'s instrument was an ⛔ OUT-OF-ENGINE RE-IMPLEMENTATION of the source-text scanners — ⛔ a ⛔ SIMULATION, ⛔ declared as one (⛔ handoff §2), ⛔ never compiled and ⛔ never run in the engine.** ⛔ **Its author reported ⛔ `335` body lines for `UpdateState()`.**
- ⛔⛔ **⛔ THAT NUMBER IS RIGHT ⛔ ONLY BECAUSE `SummonedUnit.cpp` IS ⛔ CRLF (⛔ `5,763` `\r`) AND `ExtractFunctionBody` LEAVES A ⛔ DANGLING `\r` THAT BECOMES A ⛔ 335th ⛔ EMPTY ELEMENT.** ⇒ ⛔ **a simulation splitting ⛔ naively on `\n` reports ⛔ `334`.**
- ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE SIMULATION MATCHED THE ENGINE ON AN ⛔ INCIDENTAL DETAIL ITS AUTHOR ⛔ NEVER NOTICED, ⛔ NEVER MENTIONED, AND ⛔ THEREFORE ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE FITTED TO.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE ASYMMETRY IS THE WHOLE LAW: AN ⛔ ASSERTED AGREEMENT IS EVIDENCE OF THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT ⛔ AND OF THE ⛔ AUTHOR'S ATTENTION AT ONCE, AND ⛔ YOU CANNOT SEPARATE THEM. AN ⛔ UNCLAIMED ONE HAS ⛔ NO SUCH CONFOUND — ⛔ NOBODY TUNES TOWARD A NUMBER NOBODY IS LOOKING AT. ⇒ A ⛔ CLAIMED MATCH IS ⛔ CONSISTENT WITH FITTING; AN ⛔ UNCLAIMED MATCH IS ⛔ NOT.***
- ✅ **THE RULE, FOR ⛔ ANYONE GRADING AN INSTRUMENT** (⛔ simulation · ⛔ harness · ⛔ probe · ⛔ re-implementation · ⛔ any out-of-engine stand-in): ⛔ **do ⛔ NOT grade it on the agreements it ⛔ ADVERTISES. ⛔ HUNT for one it did ⛔ NOT advertise — ⛔ an off-by-one, a ⛔ line ending, an ⛔ encoding, an ⛔ ordering, a ⛔ trailing element — and ⛔ weigh THAT.** ⇒ ⛔ **if you ⛔ cannot find one, write ⛔ *"fidelity ⛔ UNCORROBORATED"*, ⛔ never ⛔ *"fidelity confirmed"*.**
- ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ COROLLARY THAT COSTS THE AUTHOR ⛔ NOTHING: ⛔ DECLARE THE INSTRUMENT'S ⛔ CONSTRUCTION IN FULL AND ITS ⛔ NUMBERS ⛔ UNCURATED — ⛔ precisely so a reader can find the agreements you ⛔ did not claim.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1045` declared the simulation ⛔ in full, and ⛔ THAT IS WHY THE CHECK WAS ⛔ AVAILABLE AT ALL.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ curated instrument write-up ⛔ destroys the only evidence of its own fidelity that ⛔ cannot be faked.**
- ⭐⭐ **SECOND HALF, ⛔ SAME GATE, ⛔ SAME DISCIPLINE: `qa/TASK-1046.md` ⛔ ALSO stated that ⛔ NOTHING IN IT RESTS ON THE SIMULATION — ⛔ it ⛔ RE-DERIVED ⛔ ALL SIX NUMBERS ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY.** ⇒ ⛔ **the author's ⛔ PROGRAM and the reviewer's ⛔ READING are ⛔ TWO INSTRUMENTS ⛔ AGREEING, ⛔ not ⛔ ONE BEING TRUSTED.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN INSTRUMENT YOU HAVE ⛔ CORROBORATED IS A ⛔ DATUM; AN INSTRUMENT YOU HAVE ⛔ MERELY ACCEPTED IS A ⛔ CITATION*** (⭐ `SC-§78`).
- ⭐ **COMPANION CONDUCT, ⛔ RECORDED IN THE ⛔ SAME ACTION BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ SAME INSTINCT POINTED AT A ⛔ REPORT INSTEAD OF A ⛔ PROGRAM: `qa/TASK-1046.md` §5.3 ⛔ CORRECTED ITS ⛔ OWN EARLIER `qa/TASK-1042.md` ⛔ W-4** — ⛔ which had certified the trailing-comment blindness ⛔ **LOUD-ONLY** — ⛔ **and filed the correction as ⛔ ITS OWN ERROR, ⛔ not as a finding against the author.** ⛔ **It then checked ⛔ EXPLICITLY whether this was a ⛔ THIRD report-vs-file disagreement and ruled ⛔ NO (⛔ W-4 ⛔ prescribes no line ⇒ ⛔ there was ⛔ nothing to deviate from)** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE MANAGER'S ⛔ W-1 EXCEPTION WAS ⛔ NOT EXTENDED.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⭐ ***⛔ A NARROW EXCEPTION THAT ⛔ STAYS NARROW IS WORTH MORE THAN A WIDE ONE THAT WAS ⛔ RIGHT ONCE — AND AN INSTRUMENT THAT ⛔ REPORTS AGAINST ITS ⛔ OWN PRIOR VERDICT IS THE ⛔ ONLY KIND WHOSE VERDICTS ARE WORTH ⛔ RELAYING.***
- ⭐ **FAMILY: ⭐ `SC-§90`** (⛔ settle a ⛔ CAUSE and a ⛔ SEVERITY by ⛔ measurement, ⛔ never by plausibility) — ⛔ **⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ SAME INSTINCT POINTED AT THE ⛔ INSTRUMENT RATHER THAN AT THE ⛔ CAUSE: ⛔ *"my simulation matches the engine"* is ⛔ as much a claim about ⛔ reachability as ⛔ *"this defect is hypothetical"*, and it carries the ⛔ SAME BURDEN** · ⭐ **`SC-§79`** (⛔ a green is ⛔ not evidence a guard exists) · ⭐ **`SC-§78`** (⛔ ACCEPTED-AS-DECLARED) · ⭐ **`SC-§88`** (⛔ a plausible-looking number does ⛔ not announce that it is wrong — ⛔ **and `334` would have looked ⛔ EXACTLY as plausible as `335`**).
- ⭐⭐⭐ **CL. 5 — THE ⛔ COMPANION, ⛔ POINTED THE ⛔ OTHER WAY (added 2026-09-05 from ⭐ `TASK-1056`). ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ DIFFERENT DUTY, ⛔ NOT A RESTATEMENT: ⛔ §92 ABOVE IS ABOUT THE AGREEMENT AN INSTRUMENT DID ⛔ NOT *CLAIM*. ⛔ THIS IS ABOUT ⛔ REFUSING TO ⛔ INHERIT A PROOF YOU COULD ⛔ RE-RUN YOURSELF.**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1053` handed forward a ⛔ TOKENIZER PROOF that its two `FogVolume` diffs held ⛔ ZERO executable lines (⛔ `.cpp` 163/163, ⛔ `.h` 38/38). ⭐ `TASK-1056`'s compile was ⛔ MODULE-WIDE and therefore ⛔ INCLUDED that ⛔ UNGATED content ⇒ ⛔ its own `Result: Succeeded` ⛔ DEPENDED on that claim being true.**
  - ✅ **⛔ IT DID ⛔ NOT RELAY IT. ⛔ It filtered ⛔ every added ⛔ and removed line of ⛔ both diffs for ⛔ non-comment content — ⛔ EMPTY across ⛔ 173 insertions / ⛔ 27 deletions.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ TWO INSTRUMENTS THAT WERE ⛔ NOT TUNED TO EACH OTHER, ⛔ AGREEING. ⛔ A TOKENIZER OVER ⛔ FILE CONTENT AND A ⛔ LINE FILTER OVER A `git diff` SHARE ⛔ NO CODE, ⛔ NO AUTHOR AND ⛔ NO ASSUMPTION ⇒ ⛔ THEIR AGREEMENT IS ⛔ EVIDENCE, ⛔ NOT ECHO.*** ⛔ **Contrast ⭐ `SC-§88`'s ruling ⛔ deliberately: a ⛔ SECOND RUN OF THE ⛔ SAME instrument is the ⛔ same error twice, ⛔ now carrying two signatures and ⛔ reading as stronger.**
  - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ when a claim you are ⛔ RELYING ON is ⛔ CHEAP to re-derive with an instrument you ⛔ ALREADY HOLD, ⛔ RE-DERIVE IT — ⛔ hardest where your ⛔ own evidence depends on it.** ⛔ **⛔ CITE the original ⛔ as well, ⛔ never instead: ⛔ the reader must be able to see ⛔ TWO instruments.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ relay costs ⛔ zero and buys ⛔ zero; a ⛔ re-derivation costs ⛔ minutes and buys the ⛔ untuned agreement this section rates ⛔ highest.**

### SC-§91 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ RELAYED COUNT IS A ⛔ LOWER BOUND, ⛔ NEVER A COUNT. ⛔ RE-DERIVE IT AT YOUR ⛔ OWN INSTANT, OR ⛔ SCOPE THE WORK AS ⛔ UNBOUNDED.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **bought ⛔ SIX TIMES in ⛔ ONE NIGHT — ⛔ and ⛔ every correction ran ⛔ UPWARD**)

⚠️⛔ **HEADER COUNT ⛔ AMENDED ⛔ TWICE ON 2026-09-05 — ⛔ `THREE` ⇒ ⛔ `FOUR` ⇒ ⛔ `SIX`, ⛔ EACH TIME IN THE SAME ACTION AS THE INSTANCE THAT MOVED IT** (⭐ `SC-§82`'s ⛔ baseline clause — ⛔ **a law whose own tally is ⛔ stale is the ⛔ exact artefact it exists to forbid, and it would have been ⛔ RELAYED AS `THREE` by the next reader**). ⛔ **The ⛔ fourth is ⛔ NOT from the hang incident — ⛔ which is ⛔ why it matters: ⛔ the pattern ⛔ outlived the incident that bought it.**

- ⛔⛔ **THE FIRST THREE, ⛔ IN ORDER, ⛔ ALL INSIDE THE ⭐ `TASK-1041` HANG INCIDENT:**
  - ⛔ **`three` → ⛔ `FOUR` pins** — the `Clamp` census, ⛔ re-derived ⛔ comment-INCLUSIVE (`qa/TASK-1042.md` §3.4).
  - ⛔ **`one` → ⛔ `THREE` copies of `CodeLinesOnly`** — ⛔ presented as a ⛔ FRESH helper; the tree ⛔ already held two (`qa/TASK-1047.md` ⛔ W-2).
  - ⛔ **`"at least four"` → ⛔ `EIGHTEEN` live hang loops across ⛔ `FIFTEEN` files** (`qa/TASK-1047.md` §3.3 / ⛔ W-3).
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ FOURTH, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONE THAT PROMOTES THIS FROM AN ⛔ INCIDENT PATTERN TO A ⛔ STANDING LAW — ⛔ DIFFERENT LANE, ⛔ DIFFERENT ARTEFACT CLASS, ⛔ SAME SHAPE:**
  - ⛔ **`"the contradiction is in ⛔ TWO places"` → ⛔ `FOUR`** — the ⛔ **`BP_SiegeFog`-is-a-child-of-`AFogVolume`** falsehood, ⛔ relayed to the manager as ⛔ two sites. ⛔ **⛔ RE-DERIVED AT THE MANAGER'S OWN INSTANT (⛔ `Grep` for the ⛔ SYMBOL across the whole tree, ⛔ not for the ⛔ sentence): ⛔ `FogVolume.h` ⛔ ×2 (⛔ the `CoreRedirects` paragraph ⛔ AND a second sentence at the old `:95` that the row ⛔ never named — ⛔ both fixed under ⭐ `TASK-1050`) · ⛔ `FogVolume.cpp:42` (⛔ OUTSIDE that row's fence ⇒ ⭐ `TASK-1053`) · ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` `FOG-§6`'s ⛔ *"the actor"* cell (⛔ the ⛔ MANAGER'S OWN, ⭐ `SC-§82` WHO ⇒ ⛔ corrected in the ⛔ same action).**
  - ⭐ **AND THE ⛔ CENSUS DID ⛔ NOT STOP AT FOUR, ⛔ WHICH IS THE POINT: a ⛔ FIFTH mention exists (`SiegeFogStatics.h:73`) and was ⛔ READ AND ⛔ RULED ⛔ TRUE-AS-WRITTEN — ⛔ it names `BP_SiegeFog` as ⛔ *"the visual"*, ⛔ which it is.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a census ⛔ ends at a ⛔ PREDICATE, ⛔ never at a ⛔ NUMBER; ⛔ record the ⛔ ruled-out sites too, or the ⛔ next reader censuses them ⛔ again.**
  - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SEVERITY MOVED ⛔ WITH THE COUNT, ⛔ NOT MERELY THE SIZE** (⭐ `SC-§90`): ⛔ **the relay carried *"wrong parent"*; the ⛔ re-derivation found a ⛔ LIVE WIRE — ⛔ `Find` matches ⛔ SUBCLASSES and `FindOrSpawn` calls `Find` ⛔ BEFORE it spawns (`FogVolume.cpp:59-63`) ⇒ ⛔ a Blueprint child of `AFogVolume`, ⛔ if one were ever made, would be ⛔ RETURNED AS AUTHORITATIVE.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE UNDERCOUNT WAS ⛔ HIDING A ⛔ DIFFERENT KIND OF FACT, ⛔ not just more of the same one.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ FIFTH — ⛔ QA'S, ⭐ `TASK-1051`, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ MECHANISM ABOVE ⛔ CAUGHT IN THE ACT: ⛔ a ⛔ DELIBERATE, ⛔ COMPETENT SWEEP OF ⛔ ONE SMALL FILE ⛔ STILL LEFT A STALE SENTENCE (`FogVolume.h:18-21`), ⛔ because the sweep's predicate was the ⛔ CLAIM (*"wrong parent"*) and the sentence was stale on a ⛔ DIFFERENT AXIS (*"premise-blocked on `TASK-836`"* — ⛔ done 2026-09-03).**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ SIXTH, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ BEST ONE THIS PROJECT HAS PRODUCED — ⛔ A COUNT ⛔ INVALIDATED BY THE ⛔ ACT OF WRITING IT DOWN:**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1050` added a sentence to `FogVolume.h:155-156` ⛔ FOR THE EXPRESS PURPOSE of stopping a future counter tripping on a false positive:** ⛔ *"The ⛔ **single** textual `UFUNCTION` in this header is the comment on `BrightSunWindowSeconds` saying it is NOT one."*
  - ⛔⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1051`'s ⛔ OWN census returned ⛔ FOUR textual lines (`:153 :155 :160 :348`), ⛔ 0 declarations.** ⇒ ⛔ **the author's ⛔ `1` was their ⛔ PRE-EDIT number — ⛔ ⛔ THEIR OWN DIFF ADDED THREE OCCURRENCES, ⛔ ONE OF THEM ⛔ INSIDE THE SENTENCE ITSELF.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ A CENSUS TAKEN BEFORE YOUR OWN EDIT IS A ⛔ PRE-EDIT COUNT. ⛔ YOUR DIFF IS ⛔ PART OF THE SUBJECT — ⛔ AND A SENTENCE THAT ⛔ STATES A COUNT ⛔ CHANGES THE THING IT COUNTS. ⛔ RE-RUN THE CENSUS ⛔ AFTER THE WRITE, ⛔ OR STATE THE ⛔ PREDICATE INSTEAD OF THE ⛔ NUMBER.***
  - ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ SAVING GRACE, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ WHY QA ⛔ ARGUED IT ⛔ DOWN FROM BLOCKER TO ⛔ WARN AND ⛔ WHY THAT REASONING IS ⛔ WORTH KEEPING: ⛔ the ⛔ SUBSTANTIVE claim (⛔ `0` DECLARATIONS) is ⛔ TRUE and ⛔ REPRODUCED, and ⛔ unlike the two sentences it ⛔ REPLACED, ⛔ THIS ONE IS ⛔ FALSIFIED BY THE ⛔ VERY GREP IT ANTICIPATES** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ IT MISLEADS ⛔ TOWARD RE-CHECKING, ⛔ NOT TOWARD REASSURANCE — ⛔ and ⛔ the reassuring direction is the ⛔ one this lane exists to kill.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a ⛔ SEVERITY ARGUED ⛔ FROM THE ⛔ DIRECTION OF THE ERROR, ⛔ not from its ⛔ size** (⭐ `SC-§90`).
- ⚠️⛔⛔⭐ **AND THE ⛔ ORDINAL ITSELF WAS ⛔ RELAYED TO ME AS ⛔ *"THE FIFTH"* — ⛔ I ⛔ RE-DERIVED IT AND IT IS ⛔ SIX.** ⛔ **The ⛔ `UFUNCTION` `1` → `4` was reported ⛔ ALONGSIDE the mechanism rather than ⛔ AS AN INSTANCE OF IT, ⛔ so it was ⛔ not counted.** ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THIS LAW UNDERCOUNTED ITS ⛔ OWN INSTANCES, ⛔ IN THE ⛔ SAME DIRECTION, ⛔ ON THE ⛔ SAME NIGHT. ⛔ IF ANYTHING IN THIS DOCUMENT IS ⛔ LOAD-BEARING, IT IS ⛔ THIS SECTION.***
- ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE ⛔ DIRECTION IS THE FINDING. ⛔ NOT ONE RE-DERIVATION CAME BACK ⛔ SMALLER.*** ⛔ **That is ⛔ not chance: ⛔ a relayer counts ⛔ WHAT THEY SAW, and ⛔ what they saw is bounded by ⛔ WHERE THEY LOOKED.**
- 🚨⚖️⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ SHARPER STATEMENT, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05 ⛔ BECAUSE ⛔ *"VERIFY COUNTS"* IS ⛔ TOO WEAK TO ACT ON AND ⛔ FOUR CONSECUTIVE UNDERCOUNTS IN ⛔ ONE NIGHT HAVE ⛔ EARNED A BLUNTER ONE:**
  > ### ⛔⛔ ***THE COUNT IN A RELAY IS THE COUNT SOMEONE ⛔ STOPPED LOOKING AT.***
  ⛔ **It is ⛔ not an ⛔ estimate, ⛔ not a ⛔ sample and ⛔ not an ⛔ error — ⛔ it is a ⛔ FAITHFUL RECORD OF ⛔ WHERE A SEARCH ⛔ TERMINATED, ⛔ carried forward as though it were a ⛔ property of the ⛔ WORLD.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ask ⛔ *"what ⛔ STOPPED that search?"* — ⛔ a fence, a ⛔ file, a ⛔ symbol spelling, a ⛔ satisfied expectation — and ⛔ THAT is the ⛔ boundary you must ⛔ cross to re-derive it. ⛔ Re-running the ⛔ SAME search ⛔ reproduces the ⛔ SAME number and ⛔ feels like ⛔ confirmation.**
- ⛔ **WHY IT IS ⛔ SYSTEMATICALLY LOW — ⛔ a ⛔ MECHANISM, ⛔ not carelessness:**
  - ⛔ the relayer censused a ⛔ **NAME** while the defect lives in a ⛔ **SHAPE** (⛔ *a name census cannot catch a site that ⛔ RECOMPUTES a value*);
  - ⛔ the relayer censused the ⛔ **FILE THEY WERE IN**;
  - ⛔ **`"at least N"` is ⛔ HONEST and reads as ⛔ `"N"` ⛔ ONE HOP LATER** — ⛔ the ⛔ hedge is dropped in the relay while the ⛔ number survives.
  - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ FOURTH MECHANISM, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05 AND IT IS ⛔ QA'S (⭐ `TASK-1051`): ⛔ THE CENSUS PREDICATE WAS THE ⛔ CLAIM, ⛔ NOT THE ⛔ SUBJECT.** ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1050`'s author ⛔ DID sweep their file — ⛔ deliberately, ⛔ thoroughly — ⛔ for *"sentences asserting the ⛔ WRONG PARENT."* ⇒ ⛔ a ⛔ CLAIM-shaped predicate.** ⛔ **`FogVolume.h:18-21` was ⛔ stale for a ⛔ DIFFERENT REASON (⛔ *"still premise-blocked on `TASK-836`"* — ⛔ `TASK-836` is ⛔ DONE) ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE SWEEP ⛔ PASSED OVER IT ⛔ WHILE LOOKING DIRECTLY AT IT.**
    ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ A CLAIM-SHAPED CENSUS CANNOT CATCH A SENTENCE THAT IS WRONG FOR A REASON YOU ARE NOT HUNTING. ⛔ CENSUS THE ⛔ SUBJECT — ⛔ "every sentence in this file" — ⛔ AND ⛔ JUDGE EACH ONE; ⛔ DO NOT CENSUS THE ⛔ DEFECT.*** ⛔ **⛔ EXACT SIBLING of ⛔ *"a ⛔ NAME census cannot catch a site that ⛔ RECOMPUTES a value"* — ⛔ same law, ⛔ different field: ⛔ in ⛔ BOTH the predicate was ⛔ narrower than the subject and ⛔ the gap was ⛔ INVISIBLE FROM INSIDE IT.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ a count you did ⛔ NOT derive yourself is a ⛔ FLOOR. ⛔ WRITE IT AS ONE — ⛔ `"≥ 18"`, ⛔ never `"18"` — and ⛔ NEVER scope, schedule or size a ⛔ SWEEP against a ⛔ relayed figure.** ⛔ **A row scoped to ⛔ *"four copies"* against a real ⛔ eighteen is a ⛔ PARTIAL SWEEP, and a partial sweep of a ⛔ copied helper is ⛔ precisely the ⛔ DIVERGENCE the verbatim law exists to prevent.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ ASYMMETRY THAT MAKES IT ⛔ WORSE THAN AN ORDINARY ERROR: ⛔ A SWEEP SIZED FROM A ⛔ FLOOR ⛔ STILL FINISHES. ⛔ IT REPORTS ⛔ DONE. ⛔ NOTHING REDS.** ⛔ **The ⛔ fourteen unswept copies are ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE from copies that ⛔ never existed.**
- ⭐ **FAMILY: ⭐ `SC-§88`** (⛔ a plausible-looking number does ⛔ not announce that it is wrong) · ⭐ **the relay laws** (⛔ a fact ⛔ loses its cause at every hop while ⛔ gaining authority) · ⭐ **`SC-§78`** (⛔ ACCEPTED-AS-DECLARED). ⇒ ⛔ **`§88` is about a number that is ⛔ WRONG; ⛔ THIS is about a number that is ⛔ TRUE AND ⛔ INCOMPLETE — ⛔ which is ⛔ harder, because it ⛔ SURVIVES SPOT-CHECKING.**

### SC-§90 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ CAUSE AND A ⛔ SEVERITY ARE THE ⛔ SAME KIND OF CLAIM — ⛔ BOTH ARE ASSERTIONS ABOUT ⛔ REACHABILITY. ⛔ SETTLE BOTH BY ⛔ TIMELINE OR ⛔ MEASUREMENT, ⛔ NEVER BY PLAUSIBILITY.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **bought in the `TASK-1041` hang incident — ⛔ TWO false causes killed by measurement, ⛔ ONE severity set by assumption and ⛔ WRONG**)

- ⛔ **INSTANCE A — ⛔ A FALSE CAUSE, ⛔ KILLED BY A ⛔ CONTROL.** ⛔ **build-master's ⛔ audio-spam candidate for the ten-hour hang.** ⛔ It was ⛔ **not argued away — it was ⛔ REPRODUCED ALONE with `-nosound` (⛔ audio refuted) beside a ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL at ⛔ `19 ms` with ⛔ identical flags (⛔ harness exonerated).** ⇒ ⛔ **the candidate died on ⛔ evidence, ⛔ and the report then ⛔ DECLINED to name a replacement** (⭐ `SC-§87`'s closing clause).
- ⛔ **INSTANCE B — ⛔ A SECOND FALSE CAUSE, ⛔ KILLED BY A ⛔ MEASUREMENT AT `HEAD`.** ⛔ The ⛔ **`FString::Printf` growth path** was ⛔ attractive, ⛔ specific, and ⛔ wrong: ⛔ **measured at `HEAD` it was ⛔ ALREADY EXCEEDED — ⛔ `550`/`545` UTF-16 units ⛔ BEFORE the diff vs ⛔ `792`/`787` after.** ⇒ ⛔ **the condition ⛔ PREDATED the change ⇒ ⛔ it ⛔ CANNOT be what the change introduced.** ⛔ **⛔ ONE measurement, ⛔ at the ⛔ RIGHT INSTANT, ⛔ ended it.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ TWO PLAUSIBLE FIRST CAUSES IN ⛔ ONE INCIDENT, ⛔ BOTH FALSE, ⛔ BOTH REFUTED BY ⛔ TIMELINE OR ⛔ MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN BY ⛔ ARGUMENT. ⛔ THE ⛔ FIRST PLAUSIBLE CAUSE IS ⛔ NOT A ⛔ WEAK PRIOR — ⛔ ON THIS PROJECT IT HAS NOW BEEN THE ⛔ WRONG ONE ⛔ TWICE RUNNING, AND THE ⛔ TRUE CAUSE WAS AN ⛔ ENGINE DEFECT NOBODY WOULD HAVE GUESSED.***
- ✅ **THE RULE FOR CAUSES: ⛔ BEFORE adopting a candidate, ⛔ TRY TO KILL IT — and ⛔ the cheapest killer is a ⛔ MEASUREMENT AT `HEAD`.** ⛔ **⛔ If the condition ⛔ ALREADY HOLDS ⛔ BEFORE the diff, ⛔ the diff did not cause it. ⛔ Full stop.** ⇒ ⛔ **the asymmetry is the point: ⛔ refuting a candidate is ⛔ usually ONE measurement; ⛔ ACTING on a wrong one costs a ⛔ diff, a ⛔ gate, and a ⛔ QA loop.**
- 🚨⛔⛔ **INSTANCE C — ⛔ THE ⛔ MIRROR, AND THE ⛔ REASON THIS IS ⛔ LAW AND NOT AN ANECDOTE: ⛔ THE ⛔ SAME QUESTION, ⛔ ANSWERED BY ⛔ ASSUMPTION, ⛔ IN A ⛔ TRIAGE FIELD.** ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1042.md`'s ⛔ W-1 was filed ⛔ NON-BLOCKING partly on the belief that its false green was ⛔ HYPOTHETICAL.** ⛔ **⛔ IT WAS NOT.** ⛔ A ⛔ **fall-through leaving a `// return;` behind ⛔ ANCHORS a comment-BLIND `Find` on a line that ⛔ CANNOT EXECUTE, while the comment-AWARE count ⛔ SKIPS that line and ⛔ still reads `1`** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ LIVE false green, ⛔ reachable by an ⛔ ordinary edit, ⛔ wearing a comment as camouflage.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ "HYPOTHETICAL" IS ⛔ NOT A DESCRIPTION — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ CLAIM ABOUT REACHABILITY, ⛔ AND IT CARRIES THE ⛔ SAME BURDEN AS A CAUSE. ⛔ A SEVERITY SET BY PLAUSIBILITY IS AN ⛔ UNMEASURED SEVERITY, AND IT DOWNGRADES THE ⛔ EXACT FINDING NOBODY WILL RE-EXAMINE, ⛔ BECAUSE IT NOW HAS A ⛔ VERDICT ON IT.***
- ✅ **THE RULE FOR SEVERITIES: ⛔ when you downgrade a finding because it is ⛔ *"theoretical"*, ⛔ *"contrived"* or ⛔ *"hypothetical"*, ⛔ EITHER ⛔ EXHIBIT the reaching case ⛔ OR write ⛔ *"reachability ⛔ UNMEASURED"*.** ⛔ **⛔ Both are honest; ⛔ the bare adjective is ⛔ not.** ⇒ ⛔ **and ⛔ a downgrade on an ⛔ unmeasured belief must be ⛔ re-openable ⛔ without re-litigating the whole verdict.**
- ⭐ **THE ⛔ MITIGATION THAT ⛔ ACTUALLY SAVED IT HERE, ⛔ WORTH COPYING: the ⛔ REMEDIATION ROW (⭐ `TASK-1045`) was written to ⛔ DEMAND THE DECOY BE ⛔ SEEN GREEN-THEN-RED ⛔ REGARDLESS of the triage word.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a row that requires the ⛔ EXHIBIT does ⛔ not care whether the finding was filed ⛔ hypothetical — ⛔ which is ⛔ why the mis-triage cost ⛔ nothing this time. ⛔ Design for that: ⛔ put the ⛔ exhibit in the ⛔ ROW, not the ⛔ confidence in the ⛔ REPORT.**
- ⭐ **FAMILY: ⭐ `SC-§79`** (⛔ a green is ⛔ not evidence a guard exists) · ⭐ **`SC-§87`'s closing clause** (⛔ *"four facts, ⛔ no mechanism"* ⛔ beats a ⛔ confident wrong diagnosis) · ⭐ **`SC-§88`** (⛔ a plausible-looking number does ⛔ not announce that it is wrong). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ all four are the ⛔ same discipline pointed at ⛔ different fields: ⛔ ⛔ PLAUSIBILITY IS ⛔ NOT A MEASUREMENT, ⛔ WHEREVER IT IS WRITTEN DOWN.**
- ⭐ **⛔ FIFTH FIELD, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05 AS ITS ⛔ OWN SECTION RATHER THAN AS AN INSTANCE HERE (⛔ because the ⛔ RULE inverts): ⭐ `SC-§92` — ⛔ an ⛔ INSTRUMENT'S FIDELITY is the ⛔ same kind of claim, but the ⛔ cure is ⛔ not *"measure it"*, it is ⛔ *"weigh the agreement the author ⛔ NEVER CLAIMED, because ⛔ that one cannot have been ⛔ TUNED"*.**

### SC-§89 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A PRECONDITION WRITTEN AS A ⛔ LITERAL OR A ⛔ BARE COUNT WILL ⛔ CONVERT A ⛔ PERFECTLY CORRECT STATE INTO A ⛔ STOP. ⛔ WRITE IT AS A ⛔ RELATION OR A ⛔ NAMED EXPECTED SET.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **TWO instances on `TASK-1044` ⛔ IN ONE NIGHT, ⛔ both caught ⛔ BEFORE dispatch**)

- ⛔ **INSTANCE 1 — ⛔ THE LITERAL HASH.** ⛔ `TASK-1044` was drafted with ⛔ *"`HEAD` = `12b8707`"* as a precondition. ⇒ ⛔ **`TASK-1043` was then ordered to commit ⛔ ahead of it ⇒ ⛔ `HEAD` ⛔ MOVED, ⛔ CORRECTLY, and the ⛔ literal would have ⛔ STOPPED a run that was ⛔ entirely healthy.** ✅ **FIXED to a ⛔ RELATION: ⛔ *"`12b8707` is ⛔ REACHABLE FROM `HEAD`"* (`git merge-base --is-ancestor` ⇒ exit `0`) — ⛔ which ⛔ passes the degenerate case ⛔ AND every descendant, and ⛔ still stops ⛔ rewritten or unrelated history.**
- ⛔ **INSTANCE 2 — ⛔ THE BARE COUNT.** ⛔ The same row carries ⛔ *"a ⛔ FOURTH `Source/` path is a ⛔ STOP"*, counting ⛔ THREE `Tests/` paths as its complete expected set. ⇒ ⛔ **`TASK-1050`'s ⛔ comment-only correction to `FogVolume.h` — ⛔ a ⛔ correct, ⛔ boarded, ⛔ gated edit — ⛔ would have become that fourth path and ⛔ STOPPED the commit host.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ SAME DEFECT, ⛔ DIFFERENT FIELD: ⛔ THE PRECONDITION ENCODES ⛔ TODAY'S INCIDENTAL STATE AS THOUGH IT WERE THE ⛔ INVARIANT. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ CORRECT WHEN WRITTEN AND ⛔ FALSE THE MOMENT ANOTHER ⛔ CORRECT ROW MOVES — AND IT FAILS ⛔ LOUDLY AND ⛔ WRONGLY, WHICH IS ⛔ EXPENSIVE IN A DIFFERENT WAY THAN A MISSED CHECK: ⛔ IT TEACHES THE NEXT AGENT THAT THE ⛔ STOP IS NOISE.***
- ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ IN ORDER OF PREFERENCE:**
  1. ⛔ **A ⛔ RELATION over a ⛔ LITERAL** (⛔ *"is an ancestor of"*, ⛔ not ⛔ *"equals `abc123`"*).
  2. ⛔ **A ⛔ NAMED EXPECTED SET over a ⛔ BARE COUNT** (⛔ *"exactly these three paths, ⛔ by name; ⛔ anything else stops"*, ⛔ not ⛔ *"a fourth stops"*) ⇒ ⛔ **an ⛔ UNEXPECTED path ⛔ still stops, a ⛔ KNOWN one ⛔ does not, and the ⛔ STOP keeps ⛔ full strength.**
  3. ⛔ **⛔ ORDERING, when the set ⛔ cannot safely be widened.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ If the row holding the STOP is ⛔ gate-critical or ⛔ mid-incident, ⛔ do ⛔ NOT loosen it — ⛔ SEQUENCE the other work ⛔ behind it.** ⛔ **⛔ When ⛔ nothing depends on the delayed row, ⛔ ordering costs ⛔ ZERO and ⛔ touches ⛔ NOTHING.** ⛔ **⛔ This is what was chosen for `TASK-1050`.**
- ⛔⛔ **⛔ THE CURE IS ⛔ NEVER *"WEAKEN THE STOP"*.** ⛔ **`TASK-1044`'s fourth-path STOP is ⛔ load-bearing: ⛔ `qa/TASK-1042.md` §5.1 could refute residue ⛔ BY CONTENT ⛔ ONLY, ⛔ never by bytes ⇒ ⛔ a fourth path is ⛔ precisely the shape its instruments ⛔ could not see.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a STOP that is ⛔ tripping on a ⛔ correct state is a ⛔ SPECIFICATION defect, ⛔ not a ⛔ strictness defect — ⛔ fix the ⛔ wording, ⛔ not the ⛔ threshold.**
- ⭐⭐ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ DETECTION PATTERN IS THE ⛔ REAL FINDING: ⛔ BOTH instances were caught by a ⛔ READER, ⛔ NOT THE AUTHOR, and ⛔ BOTH before dispatch.** ⛔ Instance 1 by the ⛔ manager re-reading its own row; ⛔ instance 2 by the ⛔ COORDINATOR reading a ⛔ finished breakdown and ⛔ refusing to dispatch until the edge was ⛔ written down. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN AUTHOR CANNOT SEE THIS CLASS OF DEFECT, BECAUSE THE ⛔ INCIDENTAL STATE IT ENCODES IS THE ⛔ STATE THE AUTHOR WAS ⛔ LOOKING AT WHILE WRITING. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ VISIBLE ONLY TO SOMEONE HOLDING ⛔ TWO ROWS AT ONCE.*** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ that is ⛔ exactly what a coordinator holds, and ⛔ *"I would rather not manufacture it twice in one night"* is the ⛔ correct instinct ⇒ ⛔ ⛔ HOLD THE DISPATCH AND ⛔ ASK.**
- ⭐ **⛔ WRITE THE EDGE ON ⛔ BOTH ROWS. ⛔ AN ORDERING CONSTRAINT THAT LIVES IN ⛔ ONE ROW — ⛔ OR IN A ⛔ COORDINATOR'S MEMORY — IS THE ⛔ `GATE: OWED` FAILURE ⛔ WEARING A DIFFERENT HAT** (⛔ *"an ⛔ INTENTION is ⛔ NOT a MECHANISM"*). ⛔ **The ⛔ blocked row says ⛔ WHY it waits; the ⛔ blocking row says ⛔ WHAT IT SHOULD NOT SEE ⛔ AND WHAT TO DO IF IT DOES.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **INSTANCE 4 — 2026-09-05, AND IT LANDED ⛔ ONE ROW AFTER THIS LAW WAS WRITTEN, ⛔ AGAINST THE SIDE OF THE TABLE THAT WAS ⛔ ENFORCING IT.** *(⛔ Instance 3 is ⭐ `TASK-1053`'s ⛔ `ONE` ⇒ ⛔ `TWO` re-scope, ⛔ recorded on that row — ⛔ named here so the ⛔ numbering does not fork.)*
  - ⛔ **The ⭐ `TASK-1056` dispatch wrote ⛔ *"dirty tracked files — ⛔ FIVE"* and then ⛔ ENUMERATED ⛔ SIX. ⛔ Build-master ⛔ MEASURED ⛔ SIX, filed it as a ⛔ FINDING rather than a ⛔ STOP, and ruled the ⛔ NAMED SET ⛔ INTACT AND AUTHORITATIVE with ⛔ only the scalar wrong.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE BARE COUNT FAILED AND THE NAMED SET HELD — ⛔ IN THE SAME SENTENCE, ⛔ WRITTEN BY THE ROLE THAT OWNS THE RULE, ⛔ ONE ROW AFTER WRITING IT. ⇒ ⛔ THIS IS ⛔ NOT A LAW ABOUT CARELESSNESS. ⛔ A CAREFUL AUTHOR MISCOUNTS A LIST THEY ARE ⛔ LOOKING DIRECTLY AT; ⛔ THE NAMES SURVIVE BECAUSE ⛔ EACH ONE CARRIES ITS OWN CHECK AND A ⛔ TOTAL CARRIES NONE.***
  - ✅ **THE PRACTICAL CLAUSE, ⛔ SO THE NEXT DISAGREEMENT COSTS NOTHING: ⛔ WHERE A ROW STATES ⛔ BOTH A COUNT AND A LIST, THE ⛔ LIST GOVERNS AND THE COUNT IS ⛔ COMMENTARY — ⛔ and the row ⛔ SAYS SO.** ⇒ ⛔ **a reader who finds them disagreeing ⛔ FILES A FINDING AND ⛔ KEEPS WORKING, instead of ⛔ halting a healthy run on an ⛔ arithmetic slip** (⛔ which is this law's ⛔ own failure mode, ⛔ one level up).

### SC-§87 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A TEST THAT ⛔ HANGS IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO ⛔ EVERY INSTRUMENT WE OWN. ⛔ BOUND EVERY SUITE RUN, ⛔ OR A NON-TERMINATING TEST COSTS A ⛔ NIGHT AND REPORTS ⛔ NOTHING.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **bought at ⛔ TEN HOURS on `TASK-1043`'s executed suite**)

- ⛔ **THE INCIDENT: ⛔ `223/223 Success, 0 Fail`, then ⛔ test 224 — `Siegebound.Fog.TheUnitSideCeilingIsOneDoorAndAmbushIsExemptByConstruction` — ⛔ STOPPED RETURNING.** ⛔ **⛔ Not a fail. ⛔ Not a crash. ⛔ Over ⛔ TEN HOURS burned before it was cut, and ⛔ NO VERDICT AT THE END OF IT.** ⛔ **Board record: the ⛔ SUITE-TIMEOUT LAW section of `TASKBOARD.md`; ⛔ owner row ⭐ `TASK-1048`.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE ⛔ EXIT CODE HAS NOT BEEN WRITTEN YET. ⛔ THE ⛔ PASS COUNT IS ⛔ HONEST AND ⛔ CLIMBING. ⛔ THE ⛔ LOG TAIL IS ⛔ RECENT. ⛔ ALL THREE READ ⛔ "STILL WORKING" — ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM WHAT THEY WOULD READ IF IT ⛔ WERE.*** ⇒ ⛔ **a hang defeats ⛔ every instrument ⛔ SIMULTANEOUSLY, which is ⛔ why no amount of ⛔ watching the log catches it.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ EVERY suite execution ⛔ BOUNDS its runtime — ⛔ PER-TEST or ⛔ OVERALL. ⛔ On expiry: ⛔ report *"hung at test N: `<name>`"* and ⛔ STOP.** ⇒ ⛔ **minutes and a ⛔ NAME, ⛔ instead of a night and ⛔ nothing.**
- ⛔ **AN ⛔ UNBOUNDED RUN IS ⛔ ITSELF A DEFECT — ⛔ EVEN IF IT FINISHES GREEN.** ⛔ **The bound is ⛔ not a reaction to a ⛔ KNOWN hang; ⛔ it is the instrument that makes an ⛔ UNKNOWN one visible ⇒ ⛔ it ⛔ cannot be added when you need it, because ⛔ you find out you needed it ⛔ ten hours late.**
- ⛔⛔ **A TIMEOUT IS ⛔ NOT A SKIP. ⛔ NEVER close a hang by ⛔ disabling, ⛔ filtering, ⛔ `-ExcludeTags`-ing, ⛔ shortening or ⛔ deleting the test** ⇒ ⛔ **that turns an ⛔ invisible defect into an ⛔ invisible defect ⛔ WITH A PASSING NUMBER ON IT — ⛔ strictly worse, because ⛔ the hang at least ⛔ stopped the line.**
- ⛔ **A ⛔ TIMED-OUT RUN IS ⛔ NOT A PASS AND ⛔ NOT A WAIVER.** ⛔ It is a ⛔ **FINDING WITH A NAME IN IT** ⇒ ⛔ **route it, ⛔ do not round it.**
- ⭐⭐ **FAMILY — ⛔ IT SITS ⛔ BESIDE THE `Build.bat`-EXIT-CODE LAW, ⛔ NOT NEAR IT.** ⛔ `Build.bat` returns ⛔ **exit `0` on a FAILED build** ⇒ ⛔ an instrument that lies by ⛔ **COMMISSION.** ⛔ A hang ⇒ ⛔ an instrument that lies by ⛔ **OMISSION** — ⛔ it says ⛔ nothing, and ⛔ **silence is read as progress.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ BOTH DEFEAT ONE HABIT: ⛔ TRUSTING THE ⛔ CHANNEL A TOOL HAPPENS TO SPEAK ON ⛔ INSTEAD OF THE ⛔ RESULT YOU NEED. ⛔ SAME CURE BOTH TIMES: ⛔ DEMAND A ⛔ POSITIVE STATEMENT OF THE OUTCOME — ⛔ `Result:` IN THE LOG, ⛔ A REAL `N / M` FROM THE SUITE — ⛔ AND TREAT ANYTHING ELSE, ⛔ INCLUDING QUIET, AS ⛔ NO ANSWER.***
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND HALF OF WHAT THIS INCIDENT BOUGHT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ NOT ABOUT TIMEOUTS AT ALL: ⛔ THE HANG WAS ⛔ ONLY EVER FOUND BECAUSE A ⛔ DECLARED `489 / 0` WAS ⛔ REFUSED AND ⛔ EXECUTED INSTEAD.** ⛔ The manager's written grounds were ⛔ **new compile surface nobody had compiled** + ⛔ **five assertions nobody had executed.** ⇒ ⛔ ***⛔ HAD THE NUMBER BEEN CARRIED FORWARD AS DECLARED, A COMMIT WOULD HAVE SHIPPED CLAIMING `489 / 0` ON A SUITE THAT ⛔ CANNOT REACH TEST 224.*** ⇒ ⛔ **`SC-§79`'s family, ⛔ paid in full: ⛔ EXECUTING IS EXPENSIVE ⛔ BECAUSE IT WORKS, and ⛔ that is ⛔ not an argument for relaying.**
- ✅⭐ **CONDUCT WORTH COPYING: build-master ⛔ DECLINED TO NAME A MECHANISM.** ⛔ It held ⛔ four hard facts (⛔ reproduced alone with `-nosound` ⇒ ⛔ audio refuted · ⛔ positive control at ⛔ `19 ms` with identical flags ⇒ ⛔ harness exonerated · ⛔ passed at `12b8707` · ⛔ one-commit suspect window) and ⛔ **no root cause, and it refused to hand the next agent a ⛔ CONFIDENT WRONG DIAGNOSIS.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A NAMED MECHANISM IS ⛔ ACTED ON ⛔ BEFORE IT IS ⛔ CHECKED. ⛔ AN HONEST *"FOUR FACTS, NO MECHANISM"* IS ⛔ MORE USE THAN A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE STORY, AND ⛔ COSTS THE NEXT AGENT ⛔ NOTHING TO DISCARD.***
- 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **INSTANCE 2 — 2026-09-05, ⭐ `TASK-1056`. ⛔ THE BOUND WORKED; THE ⛔ TERMINAL-STATE PROBE DID ⛔ NOT — AND ⛔ BUILD-MASTER DECLARED IT ⛔ AGAINST ITSELF.** ⛔ **Its rebuilt runner probes ⛔ only for UE's `Automation Test Queue Empty` phrasing; ⛔ that run ended on `**** TEST COMPLETE. EXIT CODE: 0 ****`** ⇒ ⛔ **the runner's `QueueEmpty` field printed ⛔ `NO` on a ⛔ PERFECTLY COMPLETE RUN.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ A TERMINAL-STATE PROBE THAT READS `NO` ON A ⛔ CLEAN COMPLETION IS A PROBE THAT WOULD ALSO READ `NO` ON A ⛔ REAL HANG. ⛔ IT CANNOT ⛔ DISCRIMINATE — AND ⛔ DISCRIMINATION IS THIS SECTION'S ⛔ ENTIRE PURPOSE: A HANG MUST ⛔ ANNOUNCE ITSELF.*** ⇒ ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§79` pointed at the ⛔ TIMEOUT INSTRUMENT ITSELF. ⛔ Boarded as ⭐ `TASK-1060`; ⛔ validation law ⭐ `SHIP-§9f`; ⛔ tool law ⭐ `TL-§6`.**
  - ✅⭐ **CONDUCT WORTH COPYING: it ⛔ DECLARED the field rather than ⛔ quietly dropping it, and ⛔ named the decisive evidence as ⛔ INDEPENDENT of it (⛔ `489` started = ⛔ `489` completed = ⛔ `489` `Success`, ⛔ process ⛔ self-terminated).** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ VERDICT was never at risk; the ⛔ GUARD ON EVERY FUTURE RUN was.** ⚖️ *⛔ A broken field inside a ⛔ GREEN report is the ⛔ cheapest moment this project will ever get to find a broken guard — ⛔ and the ⛔ only one where nobody is under pressure to keep it.*
  - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ STRUCTURAL HALF, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ REUSABLE PART: the runner lived in the ⛔ SESSION SCRATCHPAD and ⛔ DIED WITH ITS SESSION. ⭐ `TASK-1044`'s copy was ⛔ GONE, so ⭐ `TASK-1056` ⛔ REBUILT IT FROM MEMORY.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ TWO DIFFERENT PROGRAMS HAVE BOTH BEEN CALLED *"the bounded runner"*, and ⛔ that is ⛔ exactly how the probe wording drifted between them.** ⚖️ *⛔ A GUARD THAT IS ⛔ RE-TYPED EVERY SESSION IS ⛔ NOT A GUARD, IT IS A ⛔ HABIT — AND A HABIT HAS ⛔ NO DIFF, ⛔ NO GATE AND ⛔ NO HISTORY.*

### SC-§88 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ SINGLE `CaptureViewport` OF UE ⛔ VOLUMETRIC FOG IS ⛔ NOT A CONVERGED MEASUREMENT — AND A ⛔ SINGLE-SHOT READING IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A ⛔ REAL ONE.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **measured by the artist under `TASK-841`; ⛔ acted on by build-master under `TASK-1043`**)

- ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT: ⛔ CONSECUTIVE captures at ⛔ IDENTICAL SETTINGS and the ⛔ SAME DISTANCE returned ⛔ `55.8%` and ⛔ `100.8%`, ⛔ converging ⛔ MONOTONICALLY FROM BELOW.** ⇒ ⛔ **volumetric fog ⛔ ACCUMULATES ACROSS FRAMES (⛔ temporal reprojection) ⇒ ⛔ an early frame is a ⛔ TRUE READING OF A ⛔ HALF-BUILT IMAGE.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE DANGEROUS PART IS ⛔ NOT THE ERROR, IT IS THE ⛔ PLAUSIBILITY: ⛔ `55.8%` IS A ⛔ REASONABLE-LOOKING FOG NUMBER. ⛔ NOTHING IN IT ⛔ ANNOUNCES THAT IT IS ⛔ UNCONVERGED. ⛔ AN UNCONVERGED READING DOES ⛔ NOT LOOK BROKEN — ⛔ IT LOOKS LIKE A ⛔ DIFFERENT ANSWER.***
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ a fog number requires a ⛔ CONVERGENCE SERIES — ⛔ capture repeatedly at ⛔ FIXED settings until ⛔ consecutive readings agree within a ⛔ STATED tolerance, and ⛔ PUBLISH THE SERIES, ⛔ not just the final value.** ⛔ **⛔ A single capture may be ⛔ reported as an ⛔ ILLUSTRATION; ⛔ it may ⛔ NEVER be reported as a ⛔ MEASUREMENT.**
- ⭐⭐ **THE DOWNSTREAM MOVE IS THE LESSON: ⛔ build-master, offered the chance to ⛔ CORROBORATE the artist's finding with a capture of its own, ⛔ DECLINED — ⛔ on the grounds that a single shot would be ⛔ *"a fresh instrument lie wearing the clothes of corroboration."*** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ CORROBORATION BY A ⛔ SECOND RUN OF THE ⛔ SAME DEFECTIVE INSTRUMENT IS ⛔ NOT CORROBORATION — ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ SAME ERROR, ⛔ TWICE, ⛔ NOW CARRYING TWO SIGNATURES AND ⛔ READING AS STRONGER.*** ⛔ **⛔ Declining to measure is ⛔ sometimes the ⛔ measurement.**
- ⚠️ **OPERATIVE ON ⛔ EVERY FOG ROW, ⛔ STARTING WITH ⭐ `TASK-1038`'s lighting-gate re-run.** ⛔ **SIBLING OF `SC-§85`** (⛔ a camera pose is a falsifiable requirement) ⛔ **and of the ⛔ NINTH INSTRUMENT LIE on `TASK-1036`** (⛔ a byte-level PNG metric is ⛔ not a pixel comparison — ⛔ two captures of ⛔ identical content differed at a ⛔ `0.98` byte ratio from ⛔ compression alone). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THREE separate ways a ⛔ screenshot has now lied on this project ⇒ ⛔ treat ⛔ any capture-derived number as ⛔ guilty until it has a ⛔ CONTROL and a ⛔ CONVERGENCE.**

### SC-§88a 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A PIE-POSED `CaptureViewport` RENDERS THE ⛔ EDITOR WORLD, ⛔ NOT THE MATCH. ⛔ POSED MATCH EVIDENCE IS TAKEN IN ⛔ SIMULATE-IN-EDITOR (`bSimulate=true`) — AND ⛔ EVERY POSED SCATTER FRAME PROMOTED BEFORE 2026-09-06 IS A PICTURE OF A WORLD THE SCATTER ⛔ DOES NOT EXIST IN.** (added 2026-09-06 by the manager; ⛔ **measured by build-master under `TASK-1084`, `handoffs/TASK-1084-buildmaster.md` §0a; proof image `playtest-evidence/2026-09-06/TASK-1084-INSTRUMENT-editorworld-vs-simulate-lane.png`; ⛔ the sibling of `SC-§88`, not a restatement — `SC-§88` says a single capture is ⛔ UNCONVERGED; ⛔ this says a PIE-posed capture is of the ⛔ WRONG WORLD, and ⛔ a converged reading of the wrong world is still the wrong world**)

- ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT, ⛔ NOT ARGUED:** the same pose `(-15000, 0, 260)` pitch −10 captured ⛔ (i) in PIE seed 159322881, ⛔ (ii) in PIE seed 1309252609, ⛔ (iii) with ⛔ NO PIE AT ALL → mean |ΔRGB| ⛔ **0.96 / 2.06 / 1.08 of 255** — ⛔ the burst-noise band (0.4–1.3). ⛔ **Seven captures aimed ⛔ BY NAME at live HISM instance positions** (a grass tuft at 3 m, a plant at 3 m, a scatter tree / rock / hill / slab at 30 m) — ⛔ **ALL BARE** — while the ⛔ same PIE session's census showed ⛔ 59 HISMs visible, `bHiddenInGame=false`, correct cull distances, ⛔ **12,244 grass instances with sane transforms at Z=0**. A capture aimed at `PlayerStart_0` from 5 m mid-match showed ⛔ NO HERO; `CaptureEditorImage` (⛔ the real screen) in the ⛔ same PIE showed the hero at the gate ⛔ with the HUD (`Gold: 17`, `Rally Ready`). The null-transform capture reported the ⛔ EDITOR camera pose. ⛔ **Same pose, editor world vs Simulate: ⛔ 20–46/255.**
- ⛔ **WHY:** `EditorAppToolset.CaptureViewport` with a `captureTransform` renders the ⛔ level-editor viewport client's world. ⛔ PIE-in-viewport runs the match in a ⛔ SEPARATE world; ⛔ the scatter is spawned at match start and ⛔ exists only there. ⛔ **Simulate-in-Editor (`StartPIE bSimulate=true`, `PlayMode_Simulate`) makes the editor viewport client ⛔ itself view the simulated world** — `GenerateScatter` runs there, the hero pawn is there, the runtime actors are there.
- ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE `SC-§94` cl. A SHAPE, ⛔ ONE INSTRUMENT DOWN: ⛔ THE CAPTURE ECHOED THE EDITOR, ⛔ NOT THE RESULT. ⛔ A BARE FIELD IN A PIE-POSED FRAME IS ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE THE SCATTER IS INVISIBLE — ⛔ IT IS EVIDENCE THE INSTRUMENT WAS POINTED AT A WORLD WITH ⛔ NO SCATTER IN IT.*** ⛔ **`TASK-1084`'s *"why can he not see it?"* came within one un-checked frame of a ⛔ FALSE CAUSE from exactly this.**
- ✅⛔⛔ **THE RULE:**
  1. ⛔ **Posed match evidence — ⛔ scatter, ⛔ hero, ⛔ runtime actors, ⛔ anything spawned at match start — is taken in ⛔ SIMULATE-IN-EDITOR, and the handoff ⛔ NAMES THE MODE (`bSimulate=true`) ⛔ beside the pose coordinates (⭐ `SC-§85`) and the convergence series (⭐ `SC-§88`).** ⛔ A promoted PNG whose caption does not state the instrument mode is an ⛔ UNLABELLED instrument.
  2. ⛔ **A PIE-posed `CaptureViewport` may be reported ⛔ ONLY as an ⛔ EDITOR-WORLD ILLUSTRATION — ⛔ never as match evidence, ⛔ never as a before/after of anything the match spawns.**
  3. ⛔ **`CaptureEditorImage` (⛔ the real screen, ⛔ the game camera) remains the ⛔ ONLY instrument of what 🧑 HE sees — and it ⛔ cannot be posed (⛔ no MCP input lane).** ⇒ ⛔ **Simulate is the ⛔ only POSED match instrument this pipeline has.**
  4. ⛔ **Writing `InstanceEndCullDistance` / `OverrideMaterials` / `bVisible` on ⛔ live PIE HISMs via `set_properties` ⛔ NEVER REACHES THE RENDER PROXY** (control: rock HISMs given `M_HillGrass` ⛔ stayed rock-textured after a visibility toggle). ⛔ **In-match property experiments on HISMs are ⛔ VOID, ⛔ not evidence — recorded so nobody repeats them.**
- ⛔⛔ **THE TWO CONSEQUENCES FOR TODAY'S TREE WORK, ⛔ NAMED SO THEY ARE NOT RE-DERIVED:**
  - ⛔ **(a) `TASK-1083-D-BEFORE / -AFTER / -SIDE-BY-SIDE.png` imaged ⛔ ONLY the 15 hand-placed ring trees** (`SM-Mobile_Tree_*` StaticMeshActors outside the playfield) — ⛔ the D1 host said so in its own caveat: *"no battlefield scatter tree is imaged at this vantage — every canopy in frame is a hand-placed ring tree on the same two materials."* ⇒ ✅ **the MATERIAL verdict ⛔ STANDS** (same two materials; the change map proves the edit is live in the render; tree line +0.082 CTI, canopies near-black at CTI ≈ 4 before and after ⇒ *"no measurable change"*, the valid outcome that hands Option A back to 🧑 him) — ⛔ **but the rostered `Layers[Trees]` scatter trees have ⛔ NEVER been photographed at a posed vantage.** They are first imaged in Simulate by ⭐ `TASK-1085` (`TASK-1085-SIMULATE-lane.png` / `-hero.png`, ⛔ post-D1 state only). ⛔ A true scatter-tree BEFORE would need a ⛔ material-REVERT capture row — 🧑 his option at `TASK-1086`, ⛔ not done.
  - ⛔ **(b) `TASK-1081` §2's dark-vs-gold one-frame attribution (`TASK-1081-lane-vantage.png`) was ⛔ ALSO of the ring trees.** ⛔ *"Same mesh, differing only by bearing-to-sun + distance at dusk"* is ⛔ TRUE OF THE RING; ⛔ its extension to `Layers[Trees]` is ⛔ INFERENCE (same meshes, same materials, same sun), ⛔ not image. ⛔ The ruling that rested on it (roster unchanged, D → D1) ⛔ stands on the material and mesh identity, ⛔ which are measured from the assets, ⛔ not from the frame.
- ⚠️⭐⭐⭐ **ADDENDUM 2026-09-06 — ⛔ THE CONVERGENCE TEST THAT `SC-§88` PRESCRIBES IS THE ⛔ WRONG TEST IN SIMULATE, AND IT WILL ⛔ NEVER PASS THERE.** (⛔ **measured by ⭐ `TASK-1085`, `handoffs/TASK-1085-buildmaster.md` §2; ⛔ binds ⭐ `SC-§88` and ⭐ `SC-§88a` ⛔ jointly — cite it as `SC-§88a` cl. 6**)
  - ⛔ **WHY: ⛔ `SC-§88`'s rule was written for ⛔ VOLUMETRIC FOG IN A STILL WORLD, where the residual between consecutive captures is ⛔ TEMPORAL ACCUMULATION and ⛔ genuinely decays to zero.** ⛔ **Simulate ⛔ TICKS THE WORLD: ⛔ wind moves the foliage, ⛔ units walk, ⛔ animations play.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ CONSECUTIVE `|Δ|` ⛔ NEVER REACHES ZERO, ⛔ no matter how long you wait — ⛔ and an agent applying `SC-§88` literally will either ⛔ burn the session waiting or ⛔ quietly relax the tolerance until something passes, ⛔ which is worse.**
  - ✅⛔ **THE RIGHT TEST — ⛔ TWO PARTS, ⛔ BOTH REPORTED: ⛔ (i) a ⛔ STATIONARY SERIES — the per-frame `|Δ|` series shows ⛔ NO MONOTONE TREND and ⛔ NO DRIFT (⛔ it wobbles about a floor); ⛔ and (ii) a ⛔ CONVERGED METRIC — the ⛔ number you are actually reporting is quoted with its ⛔ SPREAD ACROSS THE BURST.**
  - ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT BEHIND IT (⭐ `TASK-1085`, lane vantage, `bSimulate=true`): ⛔ series `2.27 / 2.21 / 2.16 / 2.33`, ⛔ median `|Δ|` ⛔ `1.0 / 255` — ⛔ stationary, ⛔ not shrinking — while the ⛔ reported metric, tree-line CTI, sat at ⛔ `5.459 ± 0.045`, a ⛔ metric spread of ⛔ `1.64 %`.** ⇒ ⛔ **the residual is ⛔ WORLD MOTION, ⛔ not a half-built image; ⛔ a series that ⛔ TRENDS in Simulate ⛔ still means unconverged and ⛔ still stops you.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CONVERGENCE CRITERION IS A ⛔ CLAIM ABOUT THE ⛔ NOISE SOURCE, ⛔ NOT A RITUAL. ⛔ WHEN THE NOISE IS ⛔ ACCUMULATION, ⛔ WAIT FOR IT TO ⛔ STOP; ⛔ WHEN THE NOISE IS ⛔ THE WORLD ITSELF, ⛔ WAITING IS ⛔ SUPERSTITION AND THE ONLY HONEST OUTPUT IS AN ⛔ ERROR BAR.***
  - ⛔ **STILL-WORLD CAPTURES (⛔ editor world, ⛔ no PIE, ⛔ no Simulate) keep ⛔ `SC-§88`'s original test ⛔ unchanged. ⛔ This clause does ⛔ NOT relax it — ⛔ it scopes it.**
- ⚠️ **INSTANCE TALLY (kept current on purpose):** ⛔ **INSTANCE 1 — 2026-09-06, `TASK-1084` §0a.** ⛔ Every posed scatter frame promoted before this date is ⛔ re-classified: ⛔ illustration of the editor world.

### SC-§86 ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ SELF-DOCUMENTING STEP ⛔ CANNOT DOCUMENT ITSELF ⛔ INSIDE ITS OWN ARTEFACT. ⛔ THE ⛔ ONE-CYCLE LAG IS ⛔ STRUCTURAL ⇒ ⛔ WRITE THE ⛔ EXPECTATION DOWN OR SOMEONE WILL *"FIX"* IT.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`handoffs/TASK-1040-buildmaster.md`, left untracked ⛔ deliberately**)

- ⛔ **THE SHAPE: a commit host writes `handoffs/TASK-<host>-buildmaster.md` ⛔ AFTER the commit it documents ⇒ ⛔ **it ⛔ CANNOT be inside that commit.** ⛔ `12b8707` swept `1034`/`1036`/`1037`'s; ⛔ the ⛔ NEXT host sweeps `1040`'s.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ EXACTLY ⛔ ONE untracked `handoffs/TASK-###-buildmaster.md` is the ⛔ EXPECTED STATE at every host's instant. ⛔ ⛔ STAGE IT AND SAY NOTHING. ⛔ ⛔ TWO OR MORE ⛔ IS a finding — ⛔ a host skipped its sweep.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ⛔ UNWRITTEN EXPECTATION IS ⛔ RE-DISCOVERED AS AN ⛔ ANOMALY BY EVERY AGENT THAT MEETS IT — AND ⛔ SOME AGENT WILL EVENTUALLY *"CORRECT"* IT, ⛔ WHICH HERE MEANS ⛔ AMENDING A SHIPPED COMMIT.*** ⛔ **The ⛔ danger is ⛔ not the lag; it is the ⛔ CONSCIENTIOUS RESPONSE to it.**
- ⭐ **SIBLING OF the STANDING EXCLUSION REGISTRY'S founding argument: ⛔ *"a file with ⛔ NO ROW is ⛔ indistinguishable from a file ⛔ nobody has got to yet."*** ⛔ **⛔ Same cure: ⛔ an ⛔ instruction the next agent ⛔ MEETS, ⛔ not an intention someone held.**

### SC-§85 ⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ RECORDED CAMERA POSITION IS A ⛔ FALSIFIABLE REQUIREMENT. ⛔ *"SAMPLE REPRESENTATIVE VANTAGES"* IS ⛔ NOT — ⛔ IT PASSES AGAINST ⛔ THREE SHOTS OF THE ⛔ SAME WALL.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`TASK-1036`'s evidence camera, ⛔ recovered from its own handoff**)

- ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT THAT BOUGHT IT: the volumetric-fog verdict rested on a ⛔ −0.24% global-luma delta captured at ⛔ `x −20607.8, y 0, z 98.15`, ⛔ yaw `180`, ⛔ FOV `90` — ⛔ a ⛔ GROUND-LEVEL CASTLE-GATE CLOSE-UP.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ VOLUMETRIC FOG SHOWS ⛔ OVER DISTANCE AND ⛔ AGAINST LIGHT SOURCES. ⛔ THAT FRAME CONTAINS ⛔ NEITHER.** ⇒ ⛔ **the number is ⛔ TRUE and ⛔ UNGENERALISABLE, which is the ⛔ most dangerous combination a measurement can have** (⭐ `SC-§70`'s family: ⛔ **a fact quoted out of the condition that produced it**).
- ✅ **BINDING, ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS: ⛔ (a) a visual gate ⛔ NAMES THE VANTAGE IT MUST ⛔ NOT REPRODUCE, ⛔ in coordinates · ⛔ (b) it ⛔ NAMES THE CONDITIONS IT ⛔ MUST SAMPLE, ⛔ and the handoff ⛔ PRINTS THE COORDINATES IT ⛔ ACTUALLY USED.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WITHOUT PRINTED COORDINATES, *"I SAMPLED VARIED VANTAGES"* IS ⛔ UNCHECKABLE TESTIMONY — ⛔ AND AN ⛔ UNCHECKABLE REQUIREMENT IS A ⛔ WISH, ⛔ NOT A GATE.***
- ⚠️ **AND THE ⛔ CONTROL STILL BINDS: ⛔ a ⛔ compressed-image ⛔ BYTE comparison is ⛔ NOT a pixel comparison — ⛔ two captures of ⛔ IDENTICAL content differ at ⛔ ~0.98 byte ratio from ⛔ PNG compression alone** (⭐ `SC-§68`, ⛔ ninth lie). ⛔ **⛔ Run the control ⛔ FIRST or the delta has ⛔ no unit.**

### SC-§84 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A FENCE THAT FORCES A ⛔ CORRECT AGENT TO ⛔ BREACH IT IS A ⛔ DEFECTIVE FENCE, ⛔ NOT A DEFECTIVE AGENT — ⛔ AND WHEN IT FORCES THE ⛔ SAME BREACH ⛔ TWICE, THE ⛔ EVIDENCE IS IN.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`TASK-1007` and `TASK-1008` ⛔ both corrected their own `blocked-by`, ⛔ QA ⛔ accepted both** — ⛔ **the manager's ruling, on the coordinator's referral**)

> ### ⚖️⭐⭐ **THE RULING, ⛔ BINDING: ⛔ YES — ⛔ AN ASSIGNEE ⛔ MAY CORRECT ITS ⛔ OWN ROW'S `blocked-by`, ⛔ AND ⛔ ONLY IN THE ⛔ ONE SHAPE BELOW. ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ NARROW LICENCE, ⛔ NOT A GENERAL ONE.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE ⛔ SHAPE THAT BOUGHT IT.** ⛔ A row is boarded `blocked-by: TASK-A`. ⛔ **`TASK-A` then ⛔ completes.** ⛔ The ⛔ dispatch itself ⛔ records it as ⛔ discharged. ⇒ ⛔ **the assignee now faces a row whose ⛔ own text ⛔ contradicts the ⛔ instruction that sent it, and ⛔ every path is a breach: ⛔ ⛔ (a) obey the stale row ⇒ ⛔ REFUSE WORK THE COORDINATOR ORDERED · ⛔ (b) obey the dispatch ⇒ ⛔ WORK A ROW MARKED BLOCKED · ⛔ (c) fix the row ⇒ ⛔ EDIT A BOARD FIELD THAT IS THE MANAGER'S.**
2. ⚖️⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ (c) IS ⛔ CORRECT, AND THE OTHER TWO ARE ⛔ WORSE.** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ stalls a green lane on a ⛔ known-false fact. ⛔ (b) leaves the ⛔ FALSEHOOD ON THE BOARD, where the ⛔ NEXT reader — ⛔ a gate audit, an integrator's pathspec, a future manager — ⛔ reads a ⛔ live blocker that is ⛔ not live.** ⇒ ⛔ **an ⛔ UNCORRECTED stale blocker is ⛔ EXACTLY the ⛔ `GATE: OWED` disease: a ⛔ board field that ⛔ lies while ⛔ looking procedural.**
3. ✅⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 3 — THE ⛔ FOUR CONDITIONS. ⛔ ALL FOUR, ⛔ OR IT IS A BREACH.**
   ✅ **(i) ⛔ THE BLOCKER IS ⛔ DISCHARGED ⛔ IN FACT — ⛔ and the assignee ⛔ NAMES THE EVIDENCE** (⛔ the gate verdict path, the commit hash, or ⛔ the dispatch sentence that records it). ⛔ **⛔ NOT *"it looks done"*.**
   ✅ **(ii) ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ ASSIGNEE'S ⛔ OWN ROW. ⛔ ⛔ NEVER ANOTHER ROW'S `blocked-by`, ⛔ never a ⛔ downstream gate's.**
   ✅ **(iii) ⛔ THE EDIT ⛔ STRIKES ⛔ NOTHING. ⛔ Mark it ⛔ `✅ TASK-A (discharged <evidence>)` — ⛔ the blocker stays ⛔ VISIBLE and ⛔ DATED.** ⛔⛔ **⛔ DELETING A BLOCKER DESTROYS THE ⛔ DEPENDENCY RECORD AND IS ⛔ FORBIDDEN OUTRIGHT.**
   ✅ **(iv) ⛔ IT IS ⛔ DECLARED in the handoff, ⛔ under its own heading, ⛔ as a ⛔ BOARD EDIT — ⛔ so the ⛔ manager and the ⛔ gate both see it.** ⛔ **An ⛔ undeclared board edit is ⛔ still a breach ⛔ even if the content is right.**
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — ⛔ WHAT IT DOES ⛔ NOT LICENSE, ⛔ STATED SO NOBODY WIDENS IT.** ⛔ **⛔ NOT `status:` · ⛔ NOT `spec:` · ⛔ NOT `names:` · ⛔ NOT `GATE:` · ⛔ NOT `parallel-safe:` · ⛔ NOT ⛔ ADDING a blocker · ⛔ NOT ⛔ another row's anything.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ONE FIELD, ⛔ ONE ROW, ⛔ ONE DIRECTION (⛔ discharged-only), ⛔ WITH EVIDENCE.** ⛔ **A row may ⛔ never mark ⛔ itself `qa-passed` or ⛔ its own gate waived — ⛔ that is ⛔ still the `SC-§64a` prohibition and it is ⛔ untouched.**
5. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ REAL DEFECT WAS ⛔ UPSTREAM, AND THE ⛔ MANAGER OWNS IT.** ⛔ **⛔ TWO agents hit ⛔ the same wall on ⛔ the same night ⇒ ⛔ the wall was ⛔ mine.** ⇒ ✅ **⛔ BINDING ON ME: ⛔ when a coordinator's dispatch records a blocker as ⛔ discharged, the ⛔ BOARD SHOULD ALREADY SAY SO. ⛔ Flipping a satisfied `blocked-by` is ⛔ MANAGER WORK, ⛔ done at the ⛔ same moment the gate verdict is boarded — ⛔ not left for the ⛔ assignee to trip over.** ⛔ **⛔ THIS LICENCE IS A ⛔ SAFETY NET FOR MY LATENCY, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ TRANSFER OF THE FIELD.**
5a. 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5a — THE ⛔ MECHANISM BEHIND MY LATENCY, ⛔ MEASURED THE SAME NIGHT ON THE ⛔ SHARPEST POSSIBLE ROW: ⛔ A ⛔ RE-SPEC PASS MUST ⛔ RE-VERIFY ⛔ EVERY FIELD, ⛔ NOT ONLY THE ONES IT ⛔ REWRITES.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`TASK-841`, ⛔ the FIFTH stale-status instance in one night**)
   - ⛔ **THE ROW THAT DELIVERS 🧑 HIS ⛔ SECOND ASK CARRIED: *"STILL ⛔ NOT DISPATCHABLE — ⛔ ONE HARD BLOCKER REMAINS: ⭐ `TASK-858`."* ⛔ `TASK-858` was ⛔ CLEARED BY HIS OWN RULING, ⛔ EXECUTED, AND ⛔ SHIPPED. ⛔ So was the ⛔ OTHER blocker.**
   - 🚨⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ DAMNING DETAIL: ⛔ THE RE-SPEC WAS DELIVERED ⛔ THAT SAME DAY, ⛔ IN THE PASS THAT ⛔ LEFT THE STALE LINE STANDING.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ NOT neglect. ⛔ NOT an old row nobody read. ⛔ A row someone had just ⛔ carefully improved.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐⭐ ***⛔ A ROW CAN BE ⛔ FRESHLY AND CAREFULLY EDITED AND ⛔ STILL SHIP A ⛔ STALE FIELD — ⛔ BECAUSE THE PASS THAT ⛔ IMPROVED IT ⛔ NEVER RE-READ THE FIELD IT DID ⛔ NOT INTEND TO TOUCH. ⛔ ATTENTION FOLLOWS ⛔ INTENT, AND ⛔ STALENESS LIVES ⛔ EXACTLY WHERE INTENT ⛔ ISN'T.***
   - ✅ **BINDING ON ME: ⛔ ANY pass that touches a row ⛔ RE-READS ⛔ `status:` ⛔ AND `blocked-by:` ⛔ AND `GATE:` ⛔ BEFORE SAVING — ⛔ even when the pass is about the `spec:`.** ⛔ **⛔ THREE FIELDS. ⛔ It is ⛔ cheap and it ⛔ would have caught ⛔ ALL FIVE instances tonight.**
   - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ DOWNSTREAM COST IS ⛔ NOT ABSTRACT: ⛔ the coordinator ⛔ REFUSED TO DISPATCH — ⛔ correctly.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ NO AGENT SHOULD BE PUT IN FRONT OF A ROW WHOSE ⛔ OWN STATUS CONTRADICTS THE ⛔ INSTRUCTION SENDING IT. ⛔ A STALE FIELD DOESN'T MERELY MISINFORM — ⛔ IT ⛔ STALLS A GREEN LANE AND ⛔ MANUFACTURES THE EXACT CONFLICT `SC-§84` EXISTS TO ADJUDICATE.***
   - ⭐ **THIS IS ⛔ WHY CLAUSE 5 IS WRITTEN AS ⛔ MANAGER DEBT AND ⛔ NOT AS AGENT LICENCE: ⛔ the ⛔ licence handles the ⛔ symptom; ⛔ THIS clause handles the ⛔ cause.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§58` (⛔ an instruction conflict is ⛔ SURFACED, ⛔ never dissolved — ⛔ **this is that law's ⛔ first case where surfacing ⛔ alone was ⛔ insufficient, because the ⛔ falsehood ⛔ persists in a ⛔ shared artefact**) · ⭐ `SC-§64a` (⛔ a row cannot certify ⛔ itself — ⛔ **the boundary this licence stops at**) · ⭐ the `GATE: OWED` abolition (⛔ **the ⛔ same disease in a ⛔ different field**) · ⭐ `SC-§54` cl. 1. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ AN AGENT MAY ⛔ RETIRE A FACT THAT ⛔ EVENTS HAVE RETIRED, ⛔ WITH EVIDENCE AND IN THE OPEN — ⛔ AND MAY ⛔ NEVER MANUFACTURE ONE.**

### SC-§83 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ROW THAT ⛔ RED-PROOFS AN ⛔ EXISTING PIN AND ⛔ NOT ITS ⛔ OWN NEW ONE HAS ⛔ PROVED THE ⛔ EASIER HALF. ⛔ THE ⛔ AUTHORED GUARD IS THE ⛔ UNPROVEN ONE ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1039.md` W-1 — ⛔ THREE mutations against the ⛔ INHERITED test, ⛔ ZERO against the ⛔ AUTHORED one, ⛔ and the authored one was ⛔ the defective one**)

- ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT: `TASK-1008` ⛔ extended an existing guard ⛔ AND authored a new one. ⛔ It red-proofed the ⛔ EXTENSION ⛔ three ways. ⛔ The ⛔ NEW pin got ⛔ zero mutations — ⛔ and it is the one that ⛔ does not guard what it names** (`Tests/SiegeFogRetentionWiringTest.cpp:698-718`: ⛔ **`Find(TEXT("return;"), …, DispatchIndex)` returns the ⛔ FIRST return ⛔ ANYWHERE after the dispatch, and `UpdateState` holds ⛔ THREE MORE before the retention line** ⇒ ⛔ **delete the dispatch's own `return;` and the pin ⛔ STILL PASSES**).
- ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ EFFORT FLOWS TO THE ⛔ FAMILIAR GUARD. ⛔ AN INHERITED PIN COMES WITH A ⛔ KNOWN FAILURE MODE — ⛔ YOU ALREADY KNOW WHAT TO BREAK. ⛔ A PIN YOU ⛔ JUST WROTE COMES WITH THE ⛔ AUTHOR'S OWN MODEL OF IT, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ EXACTLY THE MODEL THAT ⛔ PRODUCED THE BUG.***
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ GREEN IS ⛔ WORTHLESS AS EVIDENCE HERE: ⛔ a pin that matches ⛔ too much is ⛔ green on ⛔ correct code and ⛔ green on ⛔ broken code alike.** ⛔ **That is ⛔ `SC-§79` — ⛔ ⛔ but `§79` asks *"was it ⛔ seen red?"*; ⛔ this section says ⛔ WHICH pin to ask it about ⛔ FIRST.**
- ✅ **BINDING: ⛔ WHEN A ROW ⛔ TOUCHES BOTH AN ⛔ EXISTING GUARD AND A ⛔ NEW ONE, THE ⛔ MUTATION BUDGET GOES TO THE ⛔ NEW ONE ⛔ FIRST.** ⛔ **⛔ AT LEAST ONE observed RED per ⛔ AUTHORED pin, ⛔ named in the handoff with the ⛔ mutation applied.** ⛔ **⛔ Red-proofing only the inherited half is ⛔ a WARN at gate, ⛔ automatically.**
- ⚠️ **THE ⛔ SUBSTRING SIBLING, ⛔ SAME ROW, ⛔ SAME CAUSE: ⛔ a bare `FogVisionCeilingUU` needle matches ⛔ `FogVisionCeilingUU_Something` too.** ⛔ **CENSUS CORRECTION: ⛔ FOUR bare pins tree-wide, ⛔ NOT the relayed count — ⛔ the author's own sweep ⛔ MISSED `SiegeFogReachSeamTest.cpp:379`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A SWEEP THAT MISSES ONE FILE ⛔ RE-CREATES THE BLINDNESS ⛔ ONE FILE OVER; ⛔ fix ⛔ ALL or ⛔ NONE** (⛔ boarded as `TASK-1041`, ⛔ **not hardened in place — ⛔ it fails ⛔ RED, and a red guard is ⛔ noisy, ⛔ not unsafe**).
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **ADDENDUM (added 2026-09-06 by the manager; ⛔ bought in `qa/TASK-1103.md` §6 and ⛔ CONFIRMED BY EXECUTION in `handoffs/TASK-1104-buildmaster.md`) — ⛔ THE MUTATION SET MUST COVER THE ⛔ PRIMARY FIX'S ⛔ CALL SITE, ⛔ NOT ONLY ITS ⛔ HELPER.**
  - ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT: ⭐ `TASK-1102` shipped a ⛔ HELPER (`FSiegeDeathCameraStatics::MakeDeathViewRotation` / `LevelViewRoll`) ⛔ AND the ⛔ WIRING that calls it (`SiegeGameMode.cpp:868`). ⛔ Its three authored mutations ⛔ M1/M2/M3 ⛔ ALL target the ⛔ HELPER'S BODY or the ⛔ reset half.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ ALL THREE WOULD HAVE GONE ⛔ RED-THEN-GREEN ⛔ IDENTICALLY ON A BUILD WHERE ⛔ `:868` WAS ⛔ NEVER REWIRED — ⛔ i.e. on a build where half (a) was a ⛔ LANDED FILE rather than a ⛔ LANDED FIX.**
  - ✅ **THE GATE ADDED ⛔ M4 (⛔ revert `:868` to a raw `GetActorRotation()`), and ⭐ `TASK-1104`'s ⛔ EXECUTION ⛔ CONFIRMED IT: ⛔ M4 reds the ⛔ call-site census half `(a4)` ⛔ ALONE, ⛔ and ⛔ nothing else in the suite notices.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ M4 is the ⛔ ONLY mutation in the set that ⛔ distinguishes *"the function exists and is correct"* from *"the function is ⛔ CALLED"*.**
  - ⇒ 🚨⚖️⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE SHARP HALF, AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THIS IS AN ADDENDUM AND NOT A NOTE: ⛔ THE ROLE THAT COULD ⛔ EXECUTE NOTHING — ⛔ NO COMPILER, ⛔ NO ENGINE, ⛔ NO GIT — ⛔ CAUGHT THE GAP THE ⛔ AUTHOR WHO ⛔ WROTE THE MUTATIONS ⛔ COULD NOT. ⛔ MUTATION COVERAGE IS A ⛔ READING PROBLEM, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ RUNNING ONE, AND THE ⛔ AUTHOR IS THE ⛔ WORST-PLACED PARTY TO SEE IT — ⛔ FOR THE ⛔ EXACT REASON THIS SECTION ALREADY GIVES: ⛔ HE IS MUTATING AGAINST ⛔ HIS OWN MODEL OF HIS OWN CODE.***
  - ✅ **BINDING: ⛔ WHEN A FIX HAS A ⛔ HELPER ⛔ AND A ⛔ CALL SITE, ⛔ AT LEAST ONE MUTATION ⛔ TARGETS THE ⛔ CALL SITE.** ⛔ **⛔ Break the ⛔ WIRING, ⛔ not the ⛔ logic, and ⛔ name which assertion reds.** ⛔ **⛔ This is ⭐ `SC-§36.1` in mutation form: ⛔ this project has ⛔ already shipped a ⛔ built-and-tested trigger with ⛔ ZERO CALLERS while ⛔ nothing failed — ⛔ a mutation set that ⛔ only breaks the callee ⛔ cannot detect that state.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **ADDENDUM B — ⛔ THE ⛔ BREADTH CLAUSE. ⛔ A PREDICTION DERIVED FROM ⛔ NEEDLES ⛔ SYSTEMATICALLY ⛔ UNDER-COUNTS ⛔ BEHAVIOURAL REDS ⇒ ⛔ STATE THE ⛔ DIRECTION, OR A ⛔ CONFIRMATION ARRIVES LOOKING LIKE A ⛔ FAILURE.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager — ⛔ **PROMOTED from the `a521687` ledger's `L-4` after a ⛔ SECOND INSTANCE THE ⛔ SAME DAY. ⛔ Two instances in one day is a ⛔ pattern, ⛔ not an anecdote, and a ⛔ ledger line is ⛔ not read by the next author** · marker `SC-83B-PREDICT-THE-DIRECTION`)
  - ⛔ **INSTANCE 1 — ⭐ `TASK-937` / `a521687`: ⛔ `M2` ⛔ AND `M4` each reddened ⛔ THREE extra `HealthBarOcclusion` rows beyond prediction.**
  - ⛔ **INSTANCE 2 — ⭐ `TASK-1117` / `42734b7`: ⛔ `M2` reddened ⛔ FOUR tests where ⛔ ONE was predicted — ⛔ round-trip, ⛔ clamp, ⛔ the no-op law ⛔ AND the ⛔ fog predicate, ⛔ which reads the ⛔ very group the swap misdirects and ⛔ never names the mutated symbol.**
  - ⛔⛔ **WHY IT IS ⛔ STRUCTURAL AND ⛔ NOT SLOPPINESS: a ⛔ needle census answers *"⛔ which TEXT mentions this symbol"*. ⛔ A mutation changes ⛔ BEHAVIOUR ⇒ ⛔ it reaches ⛔ every test that ⛔ EXERCISES the value — ⛔ including every test that ⛔ never names it.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ the two questions have ⛔ different answer sets, and the ⛔ needle set is ⛔ always the ⛔ smaller one.**
  - ✅⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ ASYMMETRIC ⛔ ON PURPOSE: ⛔ a red set ⛔ WIDER than predicted that ⛔ CONTAINS every predicted row is ⛔ CONFIRMATION — ⛔ report the breadth, ⛔ do NOT treat it as a defect in the mutation or the diff. ⛔ A red set ⛔ NARROWER than predicted, or ⛔ MISSING any predicted row, ⛔ IS A FINDING and ⛔ stops the line.**
  - ✅ **BINDING ON ⛔ EVERY `SC-§83` PREDICTION: write ⛔ *"≥ N rows, ⛔ including `<named rows>`"* — ⛔ never a ⛔ bare `N`.** ⛔ **The ⛔ named rows are the ⛔ claim; ⛔ the count is a ⛔ floor.**
- ⚖️ **KINSHIP.** ⭐ `SC-§79` (⛔ a green is not evidence) · ⭐ `SC-§37` (⛔ the pin's ⛔ shape) · ⭐ `SC-§63` (⛔ a pin written against ⛔ today's call graph) · ⭐ `SC-§80` (⛔ comment-blindness). ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ THE GUARD YOU ⛔ TRUST LEAST SHOULD BE THE ONE YOU ⛔ WROTE MOST RECENTLY.**

### SC-§82 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **WHEN A RULING ⛔ AUTHORISES BREAKING A ⛔ STANDING CHECK, THE ⛔ BASELINE MOVES IN THE ⛔ SAME ACTION. ⛔ A ⛔ RED-BUT-EXPECTED CHECK IS THE ⛔ WORST ARTEFACT THIS PROJECT PRODUCES.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`L_Arena`'s SHA256 pin, moved on 🧑 his ruling**)

- ⛔ **THE SHAPE: a ⛔ STANDING CHECK exists precisely ⛔ BECAUSE an artefact should ⛔ not change** (⛔ here: ⛔ *"build-master verifies `L_Arena`'s SHA256 is ⛔ UNCHANGED at the gate"*, ⛔ backed by the whole ⛔ terminate-over-graceful editor discipline). ⇒ ⛔ **then a ⛔ LEGITIMATE RULING authorises ⛔ exactly the change it forbids.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE CHECK IS NOW ⛔ RED FOR A ⛔ GOOD REASON — WHICH IS THE ⛔ STALE-VERDICT SHAPE (⭐ `SC-§73`) WEARING A ⛔ GREEN-LOOKING PROCESS. ⛔ THE NEXT READER EITHER ⛔ IGNORES A REAL RED OR ⛔ SOFTENS THE CHECK, AND ⛔ BOTH ARE PERMANENT.***
- ⛔⛔ **AND IT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN A STALE ROW, BECAUSE A ⛔ STANDING CHECK HAS ⛔ NO OWNER AND ⛔ NO EXPIRY: ⛔ it is consulted by ⛔ every future gate, ⛔ forever, and ⛔ nothing in its own text says ⛔ *"this was deliberately invalidated on <date>"*.**
- ✅ **BINDING: ⛔ THE ⛔ RE-PIN IS ⛔ PART OF THE ⛔ SAME ACTION AS THE CHANGE. ⛔ NOT A FOLLOW-UP ROW, ⛔ NOT A NOTE, ⛔ NOT *"we'll update it when it settles"*.**
- ✅ **AND THE RE-PIN MUST CARRY ⛔ FOUR THINGS, ⛔ NOT ONE: ⛔ the ⛔ OLD value · ⛔ the ⛔ NEW value · ⛔ the ⛔ DATE · ⛔ 🧑 THE RULING THAT MOVED IT, ⛔ QUOTED.** ⇒ ⛔ **a future reader must find ⛔ WHY it moved, ⛔ not merely ⛔ that it did — ⛔ otherwise the ⛔ next person to see an unexplained baseline change treats it as a ⛔ defect.**
- ⛔ **WHO: ⛔ the ⛔ measuring role ⛔ REPORTS both hashes; ⛔ the ⛔ MANAGER lands the law edit** (⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` is the manager's) ⇒ ⛔ **but the ⛔ row is ⛔ not closeable until the pair is ⛔ in hand.**
- ⚠️ **AND ⛔ NARROW THE CHANGE: ⛔ if an artefact's ⛔ immutability is a gate, its ⛔ authorised diff must be ⛔ ONE LINE OF INTENT.** ⛔ **⛔ A second incidental edit in the same save makes the ⛔ new baseline unexplainable — ⛔ and the diff ⛔ IS the evidence.**
