### SC-§81 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ EMPTY-LOG FALSE PASS ⛔ ACTUALLY FIRED — ⛔ AND THE VERDICT SURVIVED BY ⛔ CATEGORY DEFAULTS, ⛔ NOT BY DESIGN.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **`TASK-1027`, ⛔ the ⛔ first time a boarded trap was ⛔ observed happening**)

- ⛔ **THE ROW WAS BOARDED WITH THE WARNING ⛔ IN THOSE WORDS: *"⛔ AN EMPTY LOG READS AS A ⛔ FALSE PASS."*** ⛔⛔ **AND `Verbose` GENUINELY ⛔ WAS OFF: ⛔ ZERO `Verbose` LINES.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **HAD THE ROW READ THAT ZERO AS ⛔ *"NO DEFECT"*, IT WOULD HAVE ⛔ CLEARED A CARD ON THE STRENGTH OF AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER SWITCHED ON.**
- ✅ **WHY IT SURVIVED — ⛔ AND ⛔ NEITHER REASON IS *"WE WERE CAREFUL":**
  - ⛔ **(a) ⛔ LUCK OF CATEGORY: the evidence it needed lives at ⛔ `Log` and ⛔ `Error`, ⛔ BOTH ON BY DEFAULT.** ⛔ **If the three `cast spell` lines had been `Verbose`, ⛔ there would have been ⛔ nothing to read.**
  - ✅ **(b) ⛔ DESIGN: it ran a ⛔ THREE-LEGGED POSITIVE CONTROL ⛔ BEFORE reading ⛔ any absence** (`SC-§39`) ⇒ ⛔ **it ⛔ KNEW the instrument was dark ⛔ before it interpreted a zero.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A BOARDED WARNING DID ⛔ NOT PREVENT THE CONDITION — ⛔ IT ⛔ PREPARED THE READER FOR IT. ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ ALL A WARNING CAN DO, AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THE ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL MUST BE ⛔ MANDATORY RATHER THAN ADVISORY: ⛔ THE WARNING TELLS YOU THE TRAP EXISTS; ⛔ ONLY THE CONTROL TELLS YOU WHETHER YOU ARE ⛔ IN IT.***
- ✅ **BINDING: ⛔ before any conclusion rests on an ⛔ ABSENCE in a log, ⛔ PRODUCE ⛔ ONE KNOWN LINE FIRST and ⛔ say which verbosity category it came from.** ⛔ **⛔ *"I saw nothing"* is ⛔ not a finding until ⛔ *"I can see something"* is ⛔ established.**
- ⚠️ **AND ⛔ RECORD THE CATEGORY IN THE VERDICT: ⛔ a conclusion resting on ⛔ `Log`/`Error` is ⛔ robust; ⛔ one resting on ⛔ `Verbose` is ⛔ hostage to a setting ⛔ nobody in this pipeline can prove was on.**

### SC-§80 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **`CountOccurrencesInCode` IS ⛔ COMMENT-BLIND ⛔ BY DESIGN — ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ CORRECT FOR A ⛔ CODE CENSUS AND ⛔ FATAL FOR A ⛔ PROSE ONE. ⛔ A PROJECT-WIDE INSTRUMENT LIMITATION, ⛔ NOT A ROW FINDING.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **measured on `qa/TASK-1019.md`**)

- ⛔ **MEASURED: it ⛔ SKIPS ANY LINE TRIMMING TO ⛔ `//`, ⛔ `* `, ⛔ `*/`, ⛔ `/*`.**
- ✅ **THAT IS ⛔ RIGHT FOR ITS ⛔ INTENDED JOB: ⛔ counting ⛔ CODE SITES. ⛔ A commented-out call ⛔ does not execute, and a pin that counted it would go red on ⛔ documentation.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **AND IT IS ⛔ FATAL THE MOMENT THE SAME FUNCTION IS ⛔ REPURPOSED TO CENSUS ⛔ PROSE — ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT THIS PROJECT SPENT 2026-09-04 DOING.** ⇒ ⛔ **every `CountOccurrencesInCode`-based sweep for a ⛔ STALE CLAIM was ⛔ STRUCTURALLY BLIND TO ⛔ THE COMMENTS IT WAS LOOKING FOR.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE TOOL WAS ⛔ RIGHT AND THE ⛔ APPLICATION WAS ⛔ WRONG — ⛔ THE ⛔ SAME SHAPE AS *"DERIVE FROM `SpellDelivery`"*: ⛔ AN INSTRUMENT ⛔ CORRECT FOR THE QUESTION IT WAS ⛔ BUILT FOR, ⛔ SILENTLY WRONG FOR THE ONE IT WAS ⛔ POINTED AT.***
- ⛔⛔ **THE CONSEQUENCE FOR ⛔ ALREADY-SHIPPED WORK, ⛔ STATED PLAINLY RATHER THAN BURIED: ⛔ any *"N occurrences"* figure in this batch that came from that helper is a ⛔ CODE-SITE COUNT, ⛔ NOT A PROSE COUNT.** ⛔ **It does ⛔ NOT invalidate those verdicts — ⛔ the pins were ⛔ about code sites — ⛔ but it ⛔ DOES mean ⛔ NO stale-comment census that used it was ⛔ ever complete.** ⇒ ⭐ **and it is ⛔ ANOTHER reason the counts kept rising (⭐ `SC-§65a`).**
- ✅ **THE REPAIR EXISTS AND IS ⛔ IN-REPO: ⛔ a ⛔ COMMENT-AWARE SCANNER now ships in ⛔ `Tests/SiegeSpellRoutingTest.cpp`.** ⛔ **⛔ REUSE IT. ⛔ Do ⛔ not write a third one, and ⛔ do ⛔ not reach for `CountOccurrencesInCode` when the ⛔ subject is a ⛔ SENTENCE.**
- ✅ **CHOOSING RULE: ⛔ *"is the thing I am counting ⛔ EXECUTABLE?"* ⇒ ⛔ YES ⇒ `CountOccurrencesInCode`. ⇒ ⛔ NO (⛔ a claim, a tense, a doc block, a tooltip) ⇒ ⛔ the ⛔ COMMENT-AWARE scanner, or a ⛔ FULL READ** (⭐ `SC-§65b`).

#### 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§80a — THE CENSUS LADDER'S ⛔ FOURTH FAILURE: ⛔ A SYMBOL WHOSE ⛔ NAME ASSERTS A RELATIONSHIP ITS ⛔ BODY DOES NOT HAVE. ⛔ NO SCANNER CATCHES IT — ⛔ ONLY READING THE BODY DOES.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **measured by ⭐ `TASK-1026`**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE EXHIBIT: ⛔ `UDeckBuilderWidget::GetCardMaxCopies` is ⛔ NAMED FOR THE COLUMN AND ⛔ DOES NOT READ IT.** ⛔ **It resolves the row as an ⛔ EXISTENCE CHECK and returns ⛔ `SiegeLegalDeckSize`.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **A ⛔ NAME CENSUS WOULD SCORE IT A ⛔ LIVE CONSUMER — ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT ONE.** ⛔ **The wrong answer here is ⛔ *"`MaxCopies` is read in the deck builder"*, which would have ⛔ INVERTED the row's verdict.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE LADDER NOW HAS ⛔ FOUR RUNGS AND ⛔ EACH IS BLIND TO THE NEXT: ⛔ (1) a ⛔ VALUE census misses claims that ⛔ CHARACTERISE · ⛔ (2) a ⛔ SHAPE census misses claims whose tell is a ⛔ TENSE · ⛔ (3) a ⛔ COMMENT-BLIND scan misses claims ⛔ IN PROSE · ⛔ (4) ⛔ EVERY TEXT SCAN MISSES A ⛔ NAME THAT LIES ABOUT ITS OWN BODY.***
- ⛔ **AND (4) IS THE ⛔ WORST OF THE FOUR FOR A ⛔ CONSUMER CENSUS, BECAUSE IT FAILS ⛔ TOWARD FALSE POSITIVES: ⛔ (1)–(3) make you ⛔ MISS a site; ⛔ (4) makes you ⛔ COUNT A SITE THAT ⛔ ISN'T THERE** ⇒ ⛔ **it can turn a ⛔ *"not a defect"* into a ⛔ *"defect"*, which is the ⛔ direction that ⛔ generates work.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ IN A ⛔ CONSUMER CENSUS, ⛔ `Read` EVERY CANDIDATE'S ⛔ BODY. ⛔ A HIT IS A ⛔ CANDIDATE, ⛔ NEVER A CONSUMER, ⛔ UNTIL ITS BODY IS READ** (⭐ `SC-§38a`, ⛔ extended: ⛔ read the ⛔ hit, ⛔ read ⛔ AROUND the hit, and ⛔ now ⛔ read ⛔ WHAT THE HIT DOES).
- ⭐ **⭐ `TASK-1026` did this ⛔ correctly and it is ⛔ why its verdict inverted from ⛔ *"defect"* to ⛔ *"working as ruled"*.**

### SC-§79 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ VERIFICATION PROCEDURE NEEDS ITS ⛔ OWN SCOPE STATEMENT. ⛔ *"VERIFIED"* MUST SAY ⛔ WHICH FAILURE CLASS THE CHECK CAN ⛔ DETECT — ⛔ BECAUSE A CHECK THAT CATCHES ⛔ ONE CLASS READS AS ⛔ *"VERIFIED"* FOR ⛔ ALL OF THEM.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **the orchestrator mandated the check, the manager showed it was blind, and ⛔ the orchestrator ⛔ accepted the argument ⛔ against its own condition**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE EXHIBIT: a ⛔ CONSOLIDATION PASS on ⭐ `TASK-987` was authorised under ⛔ five conditions, one of which mandated the verification: ⛔ COUNT THE CLAUSES ⛔ BEFORE AND AFTER, and ⛔ GREP EACH CLAUSE'S MARKER BACK OUT.**
- ⛔ **THAT CHECK DETECTS A ⛔ DROPPED CLAUSE. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ STRUCTURALLY BLIND TO A ⛔ SUBTLY REWORDED ONE.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ OPERATION IT WAS AUTHORISING — ⛔ a ⛔ VERBATIM RETYPE OF ~90 LINES OF DENSE TEXT — HAS ⛔ SILENT REWORDING AS ITS ⛔ PRIMARY FAILURE MODE.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE CHECK ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE DETECTED THE ⛔ ONE THING MOST LIKELY TO GO WRONG, ⛔ WHILE THE PASS WOULD HAVE READ AS ⛔ FULLY VERIFIED.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THIS IS ⭐ `SC-§40`'s DISTINCTION (⛔ A CITATION IS NOT A MEASUREMENT) ⛔ TURNED ON A ⛔ VERIFICATION PROCEDURE INSTEAD OF A ⛔ CLAIM. ⛔ THE DEFECT IS IN THE ⛔ CHECK, ⛔ NOT THE CLAIM — AND A ⛔ GREEN CHECK IS ⛔ EXACTLY AS PERSUASIVE WHETHER OR NOT IT WAS ⛔ CAPABLE OF GOING RED.***
- ✅ **THE RULE — ⛔ WHEN YOU MANDATE A VERIFICATION, ⛔ STATE ⛔ BOTH HALVES:** ⛔ **(a)** ⛔ what it ⛔ CAN detect · ⛔ **(b)** ⛔ what it ⛔ CANNOT. ⛔ **An unqualified *"verify afterwards"* will be ⛔ read as ⛔ TOTAL.**
- ✅⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SHARPER TEST, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ONE THAT ACTUALLY CATCHES THIS: ⛔ MATCH THE CHECK TO THE ⛔ OPERATION'S OWN FAILURE MODE, ⛔ NOT TO FAILURE MODES IN GENERAL.** ⛔ **Ask: *"⛔ what is the ⛔ MOST LIKELY WAY ⛔ THIS SPECIFIC OPERATION goes wrong, and ⛔ does my check see ⛔ THAT?"*** ⇒ ⛔ **a marker-grep is ⛔ excellent for a ⛔ MOVE and ⛔ useless for a ⛔ RETYPE.**
- ⭐⭐ **WHO IS POSITIONED TO CATCH IT: ⛔ THE ⛔ EXECUTOR, ⛔ NOT THE AUTHOR — ⛔ because the executor is the one who knows ⛔ HOW THE OPERATION WILL BE PERFORMED, and therefore ⛔ how it will fail.** ⇒ ⛔ **an agent handed a verification it believes is ⛔ blind to its own task's risk must ⛔ SAY SO ⛔ BEFORE ACTING** (⭐ `SC-§62`'s ⛔ declare-don't-deviate shape, ⛔ applied to a ⛔ CHECK rather than a ⛔ FENCE).
- ⭐⭐⭐ **THE PUREST FORM OF THIS LAW, ⛔ 2026-09-04, FROM ⭐ `qa/TASK-965.md`: ⛔ *"THE GREEN IS A ⛔ CONSEQUENCE, ⛔ NOT THE EVIDENCE."***
  - ⛔ **THE QUESTION: did the ⛔ 33rd card redden any ⛔ TRANSCRIBED COUNT?** ⛔ **The suite was ⛔ GREEN — and the reviewer ⛔ REFUSED to answer from that.**
  - ✅ **IT ANSWERED ⛔ BY REASON: ⛔ all five COUNT assertions are ⛔ RELATIONAL with ⛔ NO NUMERIC LITERAL** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the 33rd card ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE REDDENED THEM ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A PASSING SUITE IS ⛔ COMPATIBLE WITH ⛔ *"THE ASSERTION IS SOUND"* ⛔ AND WITH ⛔ *"THE ASSERTION CANNOT FAIL"* — ⛔ AND IT ⛔ CANNOT DISTINGUISH THEM. ⛔ THE GREEN IS WHAT YOU GET ⛔ EITHER WAY.*** ⇒ ⛔ **when asked ⛔ *"would X have been caught?"*, ⛔ a green is ⛔ NOT an answer; ⛔ the assertion's ⛔ SHAPE is.**
  - ⭐ **AND IT DID THIS WITH A ⛔ GENUINE PASS IN HAND — ⛔ the hardest moment to refuse the easy evidence.**
- ⭐⭐ **A SECOND, ⛔ UNPROMPTED APPLICATION OF ⭐ `SC-§78`(2b) IN THE SAME REPORT — ⛔ CORROBORATING A COMMIT IT ⛔ COULD NOT READ:**
  - ⛔ **an ⛔ ANCHORED census of `^IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST` across `Source/**` = ⛔ `475`, with ⛔ `0` complex/custom macros, against an ⛔ EXECUTED `475 / 0`** ⇒ ⛔ **cardinality matches ⇒ ⛔ EVERY DECLARED TEST ⛔ RAN and ⛔ PASSED.**
  - ⭐⭐ **AND IT ⛔ NAMED ITS OWN GRANULARITY: ⛔ corroboration at ⛔ TEST-DECLARATION level, ⛔ EXPLICITLY NOT byte equality.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CORROBORATION THAT ⛔ STATES WHAT IT DOES ⛔ NOT COVER IS ⛔ WORTH MORE THAN ONE THAT IMPLIES IT COVERS EVERYTHING — ⛔ AND IT IS THIS LAW APPLIED TO ITS OWN OUTPUT.***
- ⛔ **THE OUTCOME, ⛔ RECORDED: the ⛔ reorder was ⛔ CANCELLED OUTRIGHT — ⛔ not deferred — and the ⛔ purpose was bought another way (⛔ a ⛔ read-order MAP + ⛔ LIVE/HISTORY labelling, ⛔ zero text moved).** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WHEN THE ⛔ SAFE VERSION OF AN OPERATION DELIVERS THE ⛔ PURPOSE, THE ⛔ RISKY VERSION IS ⛔ NOT A FALLBACK — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ SIMPLY WRONG.***

### SC-§78 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **THE TOOL-CAPABILITY AUDIT — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ INVERSION IT FOUND: ⛔ THE TWO ROLES WHOSE ⛔ ENTIRE JOB IS VERIFICATION HOLD THE ⛔ FEWEST MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENTS.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **orchestrator-run audit of `.claude/agents/*.md`; ⛔ table ⛔ RE-VERIFIED at source by the manager rather than relayed**)

| agent | `Bash` | `Edit` | `Write` | Read/Grep/Glob | Unreal MCP |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| gameplay-programmer | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| art-director | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| build-master | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| footage-analyst | ✅ | ⛔ **NO** | ✅ | ✅ | ⛔ |
| ⛔ **manager** | ⛔ **NO** | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ⛔ |
| ⛔ **qa-reviewer** | ⛔ **NO** | ✅ *(granted 2026-09-04)* | ✅ | ✅ | ⛔ |

*(⛔ blank `tools:` line ⇒ ⛔ all tools. ⛔ Verified: `qa-reviewer.md:4` · `manager.md:4` · `footage-analyst.md:4` carry explicit lists; ⛔ the other three carry ⛔ none.)*

#### ⛔⛔⛔ **(1) THE INVERSION**

- ⛔ **`manager` and `qa-reviewer` hold ⛔ NO `Bash`** ⇒ ⛔ **no Git · ⛔ no hashing · ⛔ no compile · ⛔ no suite execution · ⛔ no binary inspection.** ⛔ **Their verification is ⛔ CONFINED TO READING TEXT.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ EVERY `PASS` THIS PIPELINE HAS EVER ISSUED IS A ⛔ TEXT-LEVEL VERIFICATION. ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ CORRECT AND ⛔ VALUABLE — AND IT IS ⛔ NOT WHAT *"VERIFIED"* READS LIKE TO SOMEONE DOWNSTREAM.***
- ⛔ **THE SCOPE STATEMENT BELONGS ⛔ WHERE VERDICTS ARE ⛔ READ, ⛔ NOT ONLY WHERE TOOLS ARE ⛔ LISTED** ⇒ ⛔ **boarded in `TASKBOARD.md`'s ⛔ *"Status flow"* section, ⛔ beside the definition of `qa-passed` itself.**

#### ⛔⛔ **(2) THE CONCRETE EXHIBIT — AN ⛔ UNEXERCISABLE MANDATE**

- ⛔ **⭐ `SC-§68` mandates ⛔ *"verify by `sha256`, ⛔ never by size"*. ⛔ NEITHER VERIFIER ROLE CAN ⛔ HASH ANYTHING.** ⇒ ⛔ **every asset claim in a QA report is ⛔ ACCEPTED-AS-DECLARED.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A LAW CAN BE ⛔ CORRECT, ⛔ NECESSARY AND ⛔ ADDRESSED TO SOMEBODY WHO ⛔ PHYSICALLY CANNOT OBEY IT — ⛔ AND ⛔ NOTHING IN THE LAW'S OWN TEXT REVEALS THAT.**
- ⛔ **IT TOUCHES A LIVE ROW: ⛔ `TASK-987`'s ⛔ ATOMIC-3-FILE obligation rests on ⛔ `HEAD`-vs-tree ⛔ AND hash reasoning that ⛔ NO GATE IN THIS PIPELINE CAN PERFORM.** ⇒ ⛔ **build-master ⛔ MEASURES it; ⛔ it may ⛔ NOT inherit it.**

#### ⭐⭐⭐ **(2a) THE ⛔ WORKED EXAMPLE — ⛔ AND IT IS A ⛔ BETTER EXHIBIT THAN THE AUDIT THAT PRODUCED THIS LAW, BECAUSE IT SHOWS THE CONSTRAINT ⛔ BITING THE ROLE THAT ⛔ WROTE IT DOWN, ⛔ MID-TASK, WITH ⛔ NO WAY AROUND IT.**

- ⛔ **Hours after boarding this law, the ⛔ MANAGER was asked to ⛔ VERIFY a consolidation of ⭐ `TASK-987` by ⛔ counting clauses before and after.** ⛔ **It reached for `Bash` to scope the count to the row.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`Error: No such tool available: Bash.`**
- ⇒ ⛔ **It completed the verification with ⛔ `Grep` and ⛔ `Read` — ⛔ re-locating the row ⛔ by heading, then confirming ⛔ all 20 markers by line number ⛔ inside the row's bounds, ⛔ in two batches.** ✅ **The verification ⛔ succeeded; ⛔ the instrument was ⛔ text, ⛔ because text is ⛔ all that role holds.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE LAW WAS ⛔ WRITTEN FROM AN AUDIT OF A ⛔ TABLE. ⛔ THIS IS THE LAW ⛔ ARRIVING AS A ⛔ WALL, ⛔ IN THE MIDDLE OF A TASK, FOR THE ⛔ AGENT THAT AUTHORED IT — ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ ONLY FORM THAT MAKES A CAPABILITY LIMIT ⛔ FEEL REAL RATHER THAN ⛔ ADMINISTRATIVE.***
- ⭐ **AND IT IS THE ⛔ POSITIVE READING TOO: ⛔ the constraint ⛔ DID NOT BLOCK THE WORK.** ⛔ **A text-level verification was ⛔ sufficient for a ⛔ text-level operation.** ⇒ ⛔ **the finding is ⛔ NOT *"the verifiers are crippled"* — ⛔ it is *"⛔ their instruments match ⛔ text and ⛔ nothing else, and ⛔ every claim they make ⛔ inherits that boundary."***

##### ⭐⭐⭐ **(2b) THE ⛔ CONSTRUCTIVE COUNTERPART — ⛔ THE BOUNDARY LIMITS WHAT YOU CAN ⛔ MEASURE, ⛔ NOT WHAT YOU CAN ⛔ ESTABLISH.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **⭐ `qa/TASK-1012.md` — ⛔ the best single piece of reviewer method in this batch**)

- ⛔ **THE PROBLEM: the gate was required to confirm a ⛔ `sha256` CHANGE on `DT_Cards.uasset`. ⛔ It holds ⛔ NO `Bash` ⇒ ⛔ IT CANNOT HASH.**
- ✅⭐⭐ **WHAT IT DID INSTEAD: ⛔ IT DISCHARGED THE REQUIREMENT ⛔ BY CONSEQUENCE. ⛔ It proved the `abe66ed2…` baseline held ⛔ ZERO `BrightSun` bytes and the current file holds ⛔ THREE** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A ⛔ BYTE-IDENTICAL SAVE WAS ⛔ IMPOSSIBLE.** ⛔ **The claim is ⛔ ESTABLISHED without ever being ⛔ MEASURED.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A ROLE REASONING ⛔ CORRECTLY TO A CONCLUSION ITS ⛔ OWN INSTRUMENTS CANNOT REACH. ⛔ *"I CANNOT MEASURE X"* IS ⛔ NOT THE SAME AS ⛔ *"X IS UNDISCHARGEABLE BY ME"* — AND ⛔ TREATING THEM AS EQUAL IS HOW A ⛔ CAPABILITY LIMIT BECOMES AN ⛔ EXCUSE.***
- ⭐ **IT ALSO ⛔ CORROBORATED THE ASSET ON AN INSTRUMENT THE ⛔ AUTHOR NEVER USED: a ⛔ byte probe returning ⛔ `BrightSun` 3 · `T_CardArt_BrightSun` 2 · `Bright Sun` 1 · `FogClear` 1 — ⛔ matching ⛔ EVERY declared count ⛔ EXACTLY**, and confirming `FogClear` ⛔ TWICE, the second time via an ⛔ FName ⛔ ONLY `BrightSun` CAN CARRY (⛔ `Fog` carries `FogCover`). ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ CORROBORATION BY A ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENT, ⛔ not repetition** (⭐ `SC-§66`).
- ⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ THIRD APPLICATION, 2026-09-04 (`qa/TASK-1019.md` §L2) — ⛔ AND IT IS A ⛔ NEW VERIFICATION SHAPE: ⛔ A REVIEWER'S ⛔ OWN PRIOR REPORT IS AN ⛔ INSTRUMENT FOR ITS ⛔ NEXT LOOP.**
  - ⛔ **THE PROBLEM: ⛔ it holds ⛔ no `Bash` ⇒ ⛔ it ⛔ cannot run a `diff` to see what the repair changed.**
  - ✅⭐ **WHAT IT DID: ⛔ ANCHOR ARITHMETIC over the ⛔ 18 LINE COORDINATES ⛔ ITS OWN LOOP-1 REPORT CITES** ⇒ ⛔ **shifts ⛔ PIECEWISE-CONSTANT, ⛔ exactly ⛔ THREE insertions and ⛔ ONE deletion** — ⛔ **and it ⛔ AGREED with the author's ⛔ normalise-then-diff ⛔ by a ⛔ COMPLETELY INDEPENDENT ROUTE.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A LOOP-1 REPORT IS ⛔ NOT ONLY A ⛔ RECORD — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ DENSE SET OF ⛔ MEASURED COORDINATES TAKEN AT A ⛔ KNOWN INSTANT. ⛔ THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THOSE AND ⛔ TODAY'S IS A ⛔ DIFF, ⛔ COMPUTED WITHOUT A DIFF TOOL.***
  - ⭐ **AND IT IS ⛔ CORROBORATION BY A ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENT** (⭐ `SC-§66`), ⛔ not repetition — ⛔ **the author normalised text; ⛔ the reviewer arithmetised positions.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE SHARPEST CATCH OF THE LOOP, ⛔ IN THE REVIEWER'S OWN WORDS — *"THE ONE I WOULD HAVE FAILED ON"*: ⛔ A REPAIR CAN ⛔ CHANGE THE ⛔ CHARACTER OF THE FAILURE IT LEAVES BEHIND.**
  - ⛔ **Deleting the directive converted a ⛔ MISSED QUALIFICATION from an ⛔ AMBIGUITY into an ⛔ UNDECLARED-IDENTIFIER ⛔ HARD ERROR — ⛔ at ⛔ 43 SITES.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ OLD FAILURE MODE WAS ⛔ *"IT MIGHT RESOLVE TO THE WRONG SYMBOL"*; ⛔ THE ⛔ NEW ONE IS ⛔ *"IT DOES NOT COMPILE"*.** ⛔ **A reviewer re-checking the ⛔ OLD hazard would find it ⛔ GONE and call the repair ⛔ complete.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ CHECKING THAT THE ⛔ OLD FAILURE IS GONE IS ⛔ NOT EVIDENCE THE REPAIR IS SOUND. ⛔ A REPAIR MOVES THE FAILURE; ⛔ ONLY CHECKING THE ⛔ NEW ONE IS EVIDENCE.*** ⇒ ⛔ **after any repair, ⛔ ask ⛔ *"what does this now fail as?"*, ⛔ not ⛔ *"is the old thing fixed?"*.**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ FOURTH APPLICATION, 2026-09-06 (`qa/TASK-1089.md`) — ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ FURTHEST THIS METHOD HAS BEEN CARRIED: ⛔⛔ AN ⛔ EXECUTION-FREE GATE REACHING ⛔ ESTABLISHED-BY-CONSEQUENCE ON THE ⛔ TEST RESULTS THEMSELVES — ⛔ THE ARCHETYPAL ⛔ ACCEPTED-AS-DECLARED CLAIM.** (⛔ **marker `SC-78-DERIVED-RED`**)
  - ⛔ **THE PROBLEM: ⛔ no `Bash`, ⛔ no interpreter, ⛔ no network, ⛔ no engine ⛔ AND no `git`** ⇒ ⛔ **it could ⛔ not run the suite, ⛔ not apply a mutation, ⛔ not witness a red.**
  - ✅⭐⭐ **WHAT IT DID INSTEAD: ⛔⛔ IT ⛔ DERIVED ⛔ BOTH DECLARED REDS ⛔ BY HAND ⛔ FROM SOURCE.** ⛔ **MUTATION A (`if missing:` → `if False:`) ⇒ ⛔ `51/55` ⛔ AND it ⛔ NAMED THE ⛔ FOUR FAILING LABELS — ⛔ matching the author's four. ⛔ MUTATION B (`sorted(resolved)`) ⇒ ⛔ `53/55` ⛔ WITH THE ⛔ EXACT DETAIL STRING `index 0 (Front), index 1 (Side), index 2 (Back)`, ⛔ derived from the sort order against the requested order.**
  - ✅ **AND IT ⛔ RECONCILED THE WHOLE LEDGER: ⛔ counting `check(...)` calls per group — ⛔ `6+7+10+9+5+3+2+6+3+4 = 55` — against a declared ⛔ 55 ⛔⛔ WITH ⛔ NO SLACK.** ⛔ **It also censused the ⛔ MUTATION ANCHOR as ⛔ 1 OF 1 IN 1909 LINES** ⇒ ⛔ **the instruction handed to the host is unambiguous ⛔ to apply ⛔ AND to find.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ A DECLARED TEST RESULT IS NORMALLY ⛔ RUN-IT-OR-TRUST-IT. ⛔ THIS GATE FOUND A ⛔ THIRD OPTION: ⛔ RE-DERIVE WHAT THE SUITE ⛔ WOULD PRINT AND ⛔ COMPARE. ⛔ AGREEMENT ON A ⛔ NUMBER ⛔ AND ON ⛔ FOUR LABELS ⛔ AND ON A ⛔ FORMATTED STRING IS NOT SOMETHING A ⛔ FABRICATED OR ⛔ MISREMEMBERED RESULT SURVIVES.***
  - ⭐ **THE ⛔ STRONGEST PART COST THE ⛔ LEAST: the reconciliation ⛔ HAD NO SLACK. ⛔ 55 counted, ⛔ 55 declared.** ⇒ ⛔ **a count that lands ⛔ EXACTLY is a ⛔ far narrower claim than one that lands ⛔ *"about right"* — ⛔ and the narrowness is ⛔ free.**
  - ✅ **THE COPYABLE RULE: ⛔ WHEN YOU CANNOT ⛔ RUN THE SUITE, ⛔ ASK WHAT THE SUITE ⛔ WOULD PRINT ⛔ AND COMPUTE IT.** ⛔ **⛔ A RED IS A ⛔ PREDICTION ABOUT ⛔ TEXT, AND ⛔ PREDICTIONS ABOUT TEXT ARE ⛔ CHECKABLE BY ⛔ READING.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ BEFORE DECLARING A REQUIREMENT ⛔ UNDISCHARGEABLE, ⛔ ASK WHETHER ITS ⛔ CONSEQUENCES ARE REACHABLE WITH THE TOOLS YOU ⛔ DO HOLD.** ⛔ **Then ⛔ SAY WHICH YOU DID** — ⛔ *"established by consequence"* is ⛔ honest ⛔ and useful; ⛔ *"verified"* would have been ⛔ neither.

#### ⛔⛔ **(3) THE SAME DEFECT, ⛔ STILL LIVE, IN ANOTHER ROLE**

- ⛔ **`footage-analyst` holds ⛔ `Write` but ⛔ NOT `Edit`** — ⛔ **`qa-reviewer`'s six-gate problem, ⛔ UNFIXED.**
- ⛔ **It has ⛔ not bitten ⛔ only because that role ⛔ writes `VID-###` reports and the ⛔ manager boards the fixes.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the day it is asked to ⛔ flip a status or ⛔ amend its own report ⛔ in place, it hits the ⛔ same wall — and ⛔⛔ THE FAILURE WILL LOOK LIKE ⛔ RELUCTANCE RATHER THAN A ⛔ MISSING TOOL**, which is ⛔ exactly how the `qa-reviewer` case presented for ⛔ six gates.

#### ⭐⭐⭐ **(4) THE RULE — ⛔ AND THE SYMMETRY THAT MAKES IT MEMORABLE**

> ⛔ **`footage-analyst` ⛔ CAN MEASURE BUT ⛔ CANNOT AMEND.** ⛔ **`qa-reviewer` (until 2026-09-04) ⛔ COULD AMEND BUT ⛔ CANNOT MEASURE.**

- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **⛔ NO ROLE MAY BE ASSIGNED A DUTY WHOSE ⛔ VERB IT LACKS THE ⛔ TOOL FOR.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE AUDIT IS THE ⛔ `tools:` LINE, ⛔ NEVER THE AGENT'S ⛔ PROSE — ⛔ in ⛔ BOTH cases the prose described duties the ⛔ GRANT DID NOT SUPPORT.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN AGENT DEFINITION IS ⛔ TWO DOCUMENTS IN ONE FILE: A ⛔ CAPABILITY GRANT AND A ⛔ JOB DESCRIPTION — AND ⛔ NOTHING CHECKS THEM AGAINST EACH OTHER.***
- ✅ **BEFORE WRITING A DUTY INTO AN AGENT FILE, A LAW, OR A ROW: ⛔ READ THAT ROLE'S `tools:` LINE AND ⛔ ASK WHETHER THE ⛔ VERB IS REACHABLE.** ⛔ **Before ⛔ CITING a report's factual claim: ⛔ ask whether its author held the ⛔ tool that could have produced it** (⭐ `SC-§71b`).

#### 🧑 **(5) THE GRANT DECISION IS ⛔ JONATHAN'S — ⛔ NOT AN AGENT'S, ⛔ NOT THE ORCHESTRATOR'S, ⛔ NOT MINE**

- ⛔ **`qa-reviewer`'s `Edit` was granted ⛔ because he asked for it ⛔ by name.** ⛔ **⛔ NOTHING ELSE MOVES WITHOUT HIM.**
- ⚠️⛔ **AND THERE IS A ⛔ REAL ARGUMENT AGAINST GRANTING THE MANAGER `Bash`, ⛔ RECORDED SO THE DECISION IS ⛔ NOT MADE BY DEFAULT: ⛔ A MANAGER THAT CAN ⛔ MEASURE MAY START DOING ⛔ SPECIALIST WORK** — ⛔ and this role's ⛔ first line is *"you never write code, never create art, never touch the engine."* ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ absence of `Bash` may be a ⛔ FEATURE, ⛔ not a gap.**
- ⛔ **THE SAME QUESTION IS ⛔ OPEN AND ⛔ DIFFERENT FOR `footage-analyst`'s missing `Edit`, ⛔ where the duty (⛔ amend its own report) is ⛔ squarely inside its job.**

### SC-§77 ⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CITE BY ⛔ KEY, ⛔ NEVER BY ⛔ COORDINATE. ⛔ THE GENERAL REPAIR FOR THE ⛔ WHOLE STALE-PROSE CLASS.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **ruled on `qa/TASK-1014.md` loop 2, ⛔ where the alternative would have gone stale ⛔ INSIDE THE SAME BATCH**)

- ⛔ **A ⛔ COORDINATE is a ⛔ POSITION: ⛔ `cards.csv:24` · ⛔ *"the other 31 rows"* · ⛔ `SummonedUnit.h:157` · ⛔ *"the two sites below"*.** ⛔ **A ⛔ KEY is an ⛔ IDENTITY: ⛔ a `CardID` · ⛔ a symbol name · ⛔ a function signature · ⛔ a `RowName`.**
- ⛔⛔ **THE DECIDING EVIDENCE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT HYPOTHETICAL: an author declined to write ⛔ *"31 other rows"* and ⛔ `cards.csv:24`/`:25`, citing ⛔ TWO ROW NAMES instead. ⭐ `Fog` then took the roster ⛔ 32 → 33 ⛔ IN THIS SAME BATCH** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`"31"` WOULD HAVE BEEN ⛔ FALSE ⛔ WITHIN HOURS OF BEING WRITTEN, and ⛔ every line number after the insertion point would have ⛔ shifted.**
- ✅ **THE KEYED VERSION WAS ⛔ BOTH MORE PRECISE ⛔ AND MORE DURABLE: ⛔ two `CardID`s are the CSV's ⛔ PRIMARY KEY, and the reviewer verified ⛔ BOTH sentences in ⛔ ONE lookup.** ⇒ ⛔ **this is ⛔ not a trade of convenience for safety — ⛔ the keyed form ⛔ wins on ⛔ both axes.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A COORDINATE IS A ⛔ CLAIM ABOUT THE ⛔ FILE'S CURRENT SHAPE; ⛔ A KEY IS A CLAIM ABOUT THE ⛔ THING. ⛔ THE FILE CHANGES CONSTANTLY AND THE THING ALMOST NEVER DOES — ⛔ SO A COORDINATE IS A ⛔ CLAIM WITH AN ⛔ EXPIRY DATE NOBODY WRITES DOWN.***
- ⛔⛔ **THIS SUBSUMES A LARGE FRACTION OF WHAT THIS BATCH FOUGHT: ⛔ counts that rose `3 → 8 → 15 → 23` · ⛔ *"the TWO pin re-derivations"* fences that missed a third region · ⛔ dated line numbers in ⛔ every `names:` line · ⛔ *"the other two non-starter spells"* when there were ⛔ three.** ⇒ ⛔ **all of them are ⛔ COORDINATE CITATIONS, and ⛔ all of them went stale the ⛔ same way.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ in ⛔ prose, ⛔ specs, ⛔ `names:` lines, ⛔ handoffs and ⛔ verdicts — ⛔ name the ⛔ THING.** ⛔ **If a coordinate is genuinely useful (⛔ it often is, ⛔ as a hint), ⛔ mark it ⛔ DATED and ⛔ pair it with the key** (⛔ `SC-§38`'s *"locate by SYMBOL"* is ⛔ this law applied to ⛔ code sites).
#### 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§77a — THE DERIVED PATHSPEC ⛔ ASSUMES AN ⛔ EMPTY INDEX, AND ⛔ NOBODY SAID SO. ⛔ A ⛔ PRE-POPULATED INDEX ⛔ BREAKS IT.** (added 2026-09-05; ⛔ **`TASK-1037` left ⛔ 27 paths staged and ⛔ uncommitted — a combination ⛔ no host tonight has faced**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE GAP: ⭐ `SC-§77` says ⛔ DERIVE the pathspec from `git status --porcelain` ⛔ rather than a hand list. ⛔ EVERY host tonight ran that against an ⛔ EMPTY INDEX, where ⛔ *"derive"* and ⛔ *"stage what I derived"* are ⛔ the same act.**
- ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-1037` staged ⛔ 27 paths and ⛔ committed nothing** (⛔ correctly — ⛔ it had no commit of its own). ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE NEXT HOST OPENS ON AN INDEX THAT IS ⛔ ALREADY PARTLY BUILT ⛔ BY SOMEONE ELSE.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE FAILURE: a host that ⛔ re-derives *"what is untracked?"* sees those 27 as ⛔ ALREADY-STAGED (⛔ `A `), ⛔ not untracked, and may ⛔ conclude they are ⛔ NOT ITS BUSINESS — ⛔ or worse, ⛔ `git reset` to *"start from a clean pathspec"* and ⛔ SILENTLY UNSTAGE ANOTHER ROW'S COMPLETED WORK.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE INDEX IS ⛔ SHARED MUTABLE STATE BETWEEN ROWS, ⛔ EXACTLY AS THE WORKING TREE IS — AND ⛔ THIS PIPELINE HAS SPENT A DAY LEARNING THAT LESSON ABOUT THE ⛔ TREE WHILE TREATING THE ⛔ INDEX AS PRIVATE.***
- ✅ **THE RULE FOR A COMMIT HOST: ⛔ READ THE INDEX ⛔ BEFORE DERIVING** (⛔ `git diff --cached --name-only`), ⛔ **and treat ⛔ ANYTHING ALREADY STAGED as ⛔ DELIBERATE UNTIL PROVEN OTHERWISE.** ⛔ **⛔ Your pathspec is ⛔ what you ADD to that, ⛔ not what you REPLACE it with.**
- ⛔⛔ **⛔ NEVER `git reset` TO NORMALISE A PRE-POPULATED INDEX.** ⛔ **If the staged set looks wrong, ⛔ STOP AND REPORT — ⛔ a `reset` here is the ⛔ index-side twin of the `git checkout --` that destroyed a `qa-passed` diff** (⭐ `SC-§71`).
- ✅ **AND THE ⛔ HANDING-OFF ROW MUST ⛔ SAY SO: ⛔ a row that stages without committing ⛔ MUST name the paths ⛔ and the inheriting host in its handoff.** ⭐ **⭐ `TASK-1037` did this ⛔ unprompted.**
- ⚠️ **ALSO RECORDED — ⛔ THE COLLAPSED-FORM HAZARD THAT MAKES THE DERIVATION ⛔ UNDERCOUNT: ⛔ plain `git status` reports an untracked ⛔ DIRECTORY as ⛔ ONE entry.** ⛔ **`Content/FogArea/` read as ⛔ 1 and ⛔ hid 27.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ALWAYS `--untracked-files=all`** (⭐ `SC-§55b`'s sibling: ⛔ the ⛔ method matters more than the count, and ⛔ this method has a ⛔ default that lies by ⛔ omission).

- ⚠️ **THE ⛔ ONE PLACE A COUNT IS STILL RIGHT: ⛔ when the count is the ⛔ SUBJECT (⛔ a pin that asserts *"exactly N chokepoints"*) — ⛔ and even then it must be ⛔ DERIVED, ⛔ never typed** (⭐ `SC-§65a`). ⛔ **A count as ⛔ EVIDENCE is a key; ⛔ a count as ⛔ DESCRIPTION is a coordinate.**

### SC-§76 ⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CONCENTRATE ⛔ PERISHABLE PROSE AT ⛔ ONE SITE, SO ITS ⛔ SUPERSESSION IS A ⛔ SINGLE EDIT. ⛔ THE CHEAPEST DEFECT-PREVENTION PATTERN THIS PROJECT HAS PRODUCED — AND IT WAS AUTHORED ⛔ BEFORE ANYONE KNEW IT WOULD BE NEEDED.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **measured on `TASK-839` by `qa/TASK-1015.md`**)

- ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT: of `TASK-839`'s ⛔ EIGHT gradable sentences, ⛔ SEVEN ARE ⛔ STILL TRUE after ⭐ `TASK-998` superseded its feature.** ⛔ **The reviewer ⛔ refused to manufacture findings to justify the gate — ⛔ and the ⛔ REASON for the low count ⛔ IS the finding.**
- ⭐⭐ **WHY: ⛔ `TASK-839` CONCENTRATED ⛔ EVERY PERISHABLE *"NOT LIVE YET"* CLAIM INTO ⛔ ONE PARAGRAPH AND ⛔ ONE `return false;`** (⛔ its own §5 seam list), ⛔ **deliberately, ⛔ so a supersession could kill them ⛔ in one place.** ⇒ ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-998` struck ⛔ exactly that paragraph and inverted ⛔ exactly that arm.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ CONTROL IS ⛔ INSIDE THE SAME MEASUREMENT: ⛔ THE ⛔ TWO SURVIVORS ARE ⛔ THE TWO IT DID ⛔ NOT CONCENTRATE.** ⛔ **That is ⛔ not a coincidence and ⛔ not a small sample — ⛔ it is a ⛔ 6-for-6 vs ⛔ 0-for-2 split on ⛔ one variable.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ PROSE ABOUT A ⛔ NOT-YET-LIVE FEATURE IS ⛔ GUARANTEED TO GO FALSE. ⛔ THE ONLY QUESTION IS ⛔ HOW MANY EDITS ITS DEATH WILL COST — ⛔ AND THAT NUMBER IS DECIDED ⛔ WHEN THE PROSE IS ⛔ WRITTEN, ⛔ NOT WHEN IT IS ⛔ SUPERSEDED.***
- ✅ **THE PATTERN: ⛔ when you write *"not live yet"* / *"until X lands"* / *"currently refuses"* — ⛔ put it in ⛔ ONE paragraph, beside ⛔ ONE code seam, and ⛔ SAY IN THE PARAGRAPH that it is the ⛔ single site to strike.** ⛔ **Do ⛔ NOT scatter the caveat across every function it touches, ⛔ however helpful that feels to a reader today.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ WAY THIS DEVICE FAILS, ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-04 ON ⭐ `qa/TASK-1028.md`: ⛔ THE CONCENTRATION WAS ⛔ REAL AND ⛔ NOT COMPLETE — ⛔ AND IT WAS ⛔ DEFEATED BY ⛔ ONE VERB.**
  - ⛔ **`Tests/SiegeSpellVFXRosterTest.cpp:39-41` states the ⛔ OLD FACT in the ⛔ PRESENT TENSE, ⛔ OUTSIDE the marked block — and the ⛔ marked block ⛔ CORRECTS IT ⛔ FIFTEEN LINES LATER.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A READER WHO STOPS AT `:41` HAS READ A ⛔ FALSEHOOD, ⛔ IN A FILE WHOSE AUTHOR ⛔ CORRECTLY APPLIED THIS LAW.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ CONCENTRATION FAILS ⛔ NOT BY SCATTERING THE CLAIM BUT BY LEAVING A ⛔ SINGLE PRESENT-TENSE SENTENCE ⛔ UPSTREAM OF THE MARKED BLOCK. ⛔ THE DEVICE IS ⛔ DEFEATED BY ⛔ ONE VERB — AND ⛔ `SC-§65b` ALREADY SAID ⛔ TENSE IS THE ⛔ TELL A CENSUS CANNOT SEE.***
  - ✅ **THEREFORE: ⛔ after concentrating, ⛔ RE-READ THE FILE ⛔ FROM THE TOP and ⛔ ask of every sentence ⛔ BEFORE the marked block: ⛔ *"does this state the ⛔ OLD fact in the ⛔ PRESENT tense?"*** ⛔ **The marked block ⛔ cannot protect what sits ⛔ above it.**
- ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ COROLLARY FOR REVIEWERS: ⛔ a ⛔ LOW stale-claim count after a supersession is ⛔ EVIDENCE OF GOOD AUTHORSHIP, ⛔ not evidence of a shallow review** — ⛔ **check ⛔ WHETHER THE PROSE WAS CONCENTRATED ⛔ before concluding the census was lazy.** ⛔ **`CardRow.h` went `3 → 23` because its claims were ⛔ scattered; ⛔ `TASK-839` held at ⛔ 1-of-8 because they were ⛔ not.**

#### ⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§76a — TWO ROWS WITH ⛔ NO SHARED CHANNEL CAN ⛔ STILL PARTITION A FILE, ⛔ BY EACH DECLARING ITS BOUNDARY ⛔ IN WRITING. ⛔ THIS IS WHAT HANDOFFS ARE ⛔ FOR.**

- ⛔ **MEASURED: `TASK-1000`'s handoff §7.4 ⛔ DELIBERATELY REFUSED to touch `CardRow.h:45-97` — *"so two rows do not fight over one paragraph"* — and ⭐ `TASK-1015` ⛔ MEASURED ⛔ ZERO OVERLAP as a result.**
- ⛔ **Agents ⛔ CANNOT talk to each other** (⛔ CLAUDE.md's own model). ⇒ ⛔ **these two coordinated ⛔ anyway, ⛔ correctly, ⛔ through the ⛔ FILES — which is ⛔ exactly the contract the pipeline claims to run on.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A FENCE THE ⛔ BOARD FORGOT TO DRAW CAN STILL BE DRAWN ⛔ BY THE FIRST ROW TO NOTICE IT — ⛔ IF IT ⛔ WRITES THE BOUNDARY DOWN ⛔ RATHER THAN MERELY ⛔ OBSERVING IT.*** ⛔ **An agent that ⛔ silently avoids a region has ⛔ protected today's diff; ⛔ one that ⛔ DECLARES the region has ⛔ protected every future one.**
- ✅ **ENCOURAGED, ⛔ NOT MERELY TOLERATED: ⛔ when you decline to touch a region ⛔ because another row plausibly owns it, ⛔ SAY SO ⛔ BY REGION in the handoff.** ⛔ **The manager then ⛔ boards it as a real fence** (⛔ this is how `SC-§74`'s prompt-only fence became a board entry).

### SC-§75 ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A DATA CELL NOTHING READS IS ⛔ WORSE THAN AN ABSENT ONE, AND A ⛔ BLACKLIST GUARD IS A ⛔ DEFECT GENERATOR. ⛔ BOTH FAIL BY ⛔ LOOKING CORRECT.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **both measured on `TASK-840`, ⛔ both in the ⛔ `Fog` card, ⛔ within one hour**)

#### ⛔⛔ **(A) THE ⛔ INERT CELL**

- ⛔ **MEASURED: `cards.csv`'s `Fog.EffectDuration = 300` is ⛔ READ BY NOTHING.** ⛔ The `FogCover` arm calls `RaiseFog()` with ⛔ no argument and the duration comes from the ⛔ CDO; ⛔ `Row.EffectDuration` is read by the ⛔ FREEZE arms and ⛔ never by the fog arm.
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE TWO `300`s AGREE ⛔ ONLY BECAUSE SOMEBODY TYPED THEM TO AGREE. ⛔ RETUNE ONE AND ⛔ NOTHING GOES RED.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ AN ⛔ ABSENT CELL ⛔ ASKS A QUESTION. ⛔ AN ⛔ INERT CELL ⛔ ANSWERS IT ⛔ WRONGLY AND ⛔ SURVIVES EVERY GATE — ⛔ IT READS AS ⛔ CONFIGURATION AND BEHAVES AS ⛔ A COMMENT.***
- ⛔ **IT IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO: ⛔ the compiler (⛔ the field exists) · ⛔ the suite (⛔ the value is ⛔ never asserted against ⛔ behaviour) · ⛔ a data review (⛔ the number is ⛔ RIGHT) · ⛔ a code review (⛔ the CDO is ⛔ RIGHT).**
- ✅ **THE ⛔ ONLY ASSERTION THAT CATCHES IT: ⛔ a ⛔ CHANGED ROW VALUE ⇒ a ⛔ CHANGED OBSERVED BEHAVIOUR.** ⛔ **`row = X ⇒ X` and `blank ⇒ default` ⛔ BOTH PASS against the ⛔ inert version.** ⇒ ⛔ **when a cell claims to drive something, ⛔ test the ⛔ DERIVATIVE, ⛔ not the value.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE SAME SHAPE, ⛔ ONE LEVEL UP — ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 FROM ⭐ `TASK-982`: ⛔ SOMETIMES ⛔ TWO IMPLEMENTATIONS ARE ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE BY ⛔ ANY BEHAVIOUR TEST, AND THE ⛔ STRUCTURAL ASSERTION IS THE ⛔ ONLY INSTRUMENT THAT CAN SEE THE RULING.**
  - ⛔ **🧑 `J-F18` ruled ⛔ CONDITIONAL: ⛔ a ⛔ LONGER window ⛔ RESETS, a ⛔ SHORTER one ⛔ REFUSES.** ⛔ **The ⛔ WRONG implementation is `FMath::Max(remaining, new)`.**
  - ⛔⛔ **AND ⛔ `max` AND ⛔ *"reset if longer"* ⛔ PRODUCE THE ⛔ IDENTICAL DURATION IN ⛔ EVERY CASE** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ NO duration test, at ⛔ any input, can ⛔ EVER discriminate them.**
  - ⇒ ⛔ **THE AUTHOR ASSERTED ⛔ `FMath::Max` AT ⛔ ZERO OCCURRENCES — ⛔ a ⛔ SOURCE-TEXT assertion about the ⛔ SHAPE of the code, ⛔ not its output.** ⇒ ⭐ **that is the ⛔ ONLY thing that can fail if someone *"simplifies"* the branch into a `max`.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WHEN A RULING DISTINGUISHES ⛔ TWO PATHS THAT ⛔ AGREE ON EVERY OUTPUT, THE RULING IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO BEHAVIOUR TESTING ⛔ ENTIRELY. ⛔ PIN THE ⛔ FORBIDDEN SPELLING, OR THE ⛔ RULING HAS ⛔ NO GUARD AT ALL — ⛔ AND THE SUITE WILL STAY ⛔ GREEN THROUGH ITS DELETION.***
  - ⚠️ **USE THIS ⛔ SPARINGLY: a source-text pin is ⛔ brittle and ⛔ breaks on innocent refactors.** ⛔ **It earns its place ⛔ only when the behaviours are ⛔ PROVABLY identical — ⛔ i.e. when there is ⛔ nothing else to assert.**
- ⛔ **PREFERRED REPAIR: ⛔ make the row ⛔ AUTHORITATIVE with the CDO as a ⛔ FALLBACK** (⛔ `ResolveNoticeRadiusUU`'s proven truth table: ⛔ row `> 0` ⇒ row · ⛔ else default). ⛔ **Zeroing or deleting the cell ⛔ documents the trap; ⛔ wiring it ⛔ removes it.**

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(A2) THE ⛔ COROLLARY, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 — AN ⛔ INERT VALUE ⛔ DEFENDED BY A ⛔ FILTERED ARGUMENT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT NOBODY RE-CHECKS**

- ⛔ **MEASURED (`qa/TASK-850.md`): `MaxCopies 2` on the `Fog` row was defended as *"it matches the other ⛔ TWO non-starter spells."* ⛔ THERE ARE ⛔ THREE. ⛔ The omitted one is `BattleCry`, which carries ⛔ `3`.** ⛔ **The five shipped Spell rows are `{3, 3, 2, 3, 2}` — ⛔ NOT unanimous.**
- ✅ **THE ⛔ VALUE SURVIVES** (⛔ `UNCAP-§2`: it is the ⛔ hero-upgrade stack cap and is ⛔ INERT on a Spell row ⇒ ⛔ behaviourally identical at any value). ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ METHOD DOES ⛔ NOT.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ARGUMENT ⛔ SELECTED ITS SUPPORTING CASES. ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ SAME SHAPE AS THE ⛔ STALE-CLAIM COUNTS THAT ⛔ ROSE FIVE TIMES: ⛔ A CENSUS RUN BY SOMEBODY WHO ⛔ ALREADY HAS AN ANSWER ⛔ FINDS THE CASES THAT ⛔ AGREE WITH IT.***
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ COMPOUNDING IS THE POINT OF FILING IT ⛔ HERE: ⛔ an ⛔ INERT value is ⛔ already invisible to every gate (§(A)). ⛔ Give it a ⛔ CONFIDENT-SOUNDING JUSTIFICATION and it becomes ⛔ invisible to ⛔ REVIEW as well** — ⛔ **the next reader sees a number, a reason, and ⛔ moves on.** ⇒ ⛔ **when a value is ⛔ inert, ⛔ scrutinise its ⛔ ARGUMENT ⛔ harder, ⛔ not less: ⛔ nothing downstream will ⛔ ever contradict it.**
- ✅ **CHECK: ⛔ when an argument says *"it matches the others"*, ⛔ COUNT THE OTHERS ⛔ YOURSELF and ⛔ report the ⛔ FULL SET, ⛔ including the ⛔ disagreeing members.** ⛔ **`{3,3,2,3,2}` is an ⛔ honest answer; ⛔ *"matches the other two"* is ⛔ not.**

#### ⛔⛔⛔ **(B) THE ⛔ BLACKLIST GUARD**

- ⛔ **MEASURED: the deck-builder printed *"Aimed at a spot on the ground: it goes off where you place the reticle"* for ⛔ `Fog` — a ⛔ NO-RETICLE, ⛔ MAP-WIDE spell — because the guard reads `if (Row.SpellEffect != ESpellEffect::GoldSteal)`.**
- ⛔ **A BLACKLIST is ⛔ CORRECT ONLY UNTIL THE ⛔ NEXT VALUE, and it ⛔ FAILS OPEN: ⛔ a new effect ⛔ silently claims a property it does ⛔ not have.** ⛔ **This one had ⛔ ALREADY FAILED TWICE — ⛔ once per new no-reticle spell (`Fog`, then `BrightSun`).**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A ⛔ BLANK LINE IS A ⛔ GAP; A ⛔ WRONG LINE IS A ⛔ LIE. ⛔ THE `default:` ARM THAT PRODUCES THE BLANK IS THE ⛔ MILDER BUG, AND IT IS THE ONE THAT GETS BOARDED, BECAUSE ⛔ AN ABSENCE IS ⛔ EASIER TO NOTICE THAN A ⛔ CONFIDENT FALSEHOOD.***
- ✅ **REPAIR ⛔ STRUCTURALLY: ⛔ DERIVE THE CLAIM FROM THE ⛔ DATA THAT DEFINES IT** ⇒ ⛔ **a new spell is ⛔ correct by ⛔ CONSTRUCTION.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AMENDED 2026-09-04 — ⛔ THE PARENTHETICAL THAT USED TO SIT HERE WAS ⛔ FALSE, AND FOLLOWING IT ⛔ LITERALLY WOULD HAVE ⛔ REPRODUCED THE BUG.**
  - ⛔ ~~*"(here: the `SpellDelivery` column, not the effect enum)"*~~ ⇒ ⛔⛔ **MEASURED BY ⭐ `TASK-999`: `USpellLibrary::GetEffectiveDelivery`'s ⛔ `Auto` arm returns ⛔ `GroundCircle` for ⛔ EVERY effect that is not `AoEDamage`/`Freeze` — ⛔ `GoldSteal`, ⛔ `FogCover` ⛔ AND `FogClear` ⛔ INCLUDED.** ⇒ ⛔ **a guard written `== GroundCircle` would have ⛔ REPRINTED THE LIE ON ⛔ THREE CARDS ⛔ WHILE LOOKING EXACTLY LIKE THE RULING ASKED.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE STRUCTURAL REASON, ⛔ AND IT IS THE KEEPER: ⛔ `ESpellDelivery` HAS ⛔ NO VALUE MEANING *"NO AIM AT ALL"*. ⛔ DELIVERY SAYS ⛔ WHICH AIMING SENTENCE, ⛔ NEVER ⛔ WHETHER THERE IS ONE.** ⇒ ⛔ **the field the ruling named ⛔ CANNOT ANSWER THE QUESTION ASKED OF IT.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE PRINCIPLE ⛔ SURVIVES AND THE ⛔ POINTER DID NOT. ⛔ *"DERIVE FROM THE DATA THAT DEFINES THE CLAIM"* WAS ⛔ RIGHT; ⛔ NAMING ⛔ WHICH DATA WAS A ⛔ SEPARATE ACT, AND ⛔ THE MANAGER GOT IT WRONG WITHOUT ⛔ CHECKING THAT THE FIELD COULD ⛔ EXPRESS THE DISTINCTION.***
  - ⛔⛔ **THE NEAR-MISS IS THE POINT: ⛔ A RULING THAT NAMES A ⛔ SPECIFIC FIELD IS ⛔ ITSELF A CLAIM ABOUT THAT FIELD'S ⛔ SEMANTICS — ⛔ and it is ⛔ EXACTLY THE KIND OF CLAIM THIS ENTIRE SECTION SAYS TO ⛔ VERIFY BEFORE TRUSTING.** ⛔ **The repair prescription was ⛔ a stale-prose defect ⛔ inside the law that repairs stale prose.**
  - ✅ **BEFORE NAMING A FIELD IN A *"DERIVE FROM THE DATA"* RULING: ⛔ CHECK THAT THE FIELD HAS A ⛔ VALUE FOR ⛔ EVERY ANSWER THE QUESTION NEEDS — ⛔ INCLUDING THE ⛔ NEGATIVE ONE.** ⛔ **A field with ⛔ no *"none"* value ⛔ cannot express ⛔ absence, and ⛔ every derivation from it will ⛔ silently pick a default.**
  - ✅⛔⛔ **CONFIRMED 2026-09-04 BY ⭐ `TASK-1013` (⛔ PASS, ⛔ 0 blockers, ⛔ verified ⛔ AT SOURCE): ⛔ `ESpellDelivery` has ⛔ EXACTLY THREE VALUES — ⛔ `Auto` · `GroundCircle` · `HeroLine` — and ⛔ NONE of them means *"no aim at all"*.** ⇒ ⛔ **this correction is now ⛔ MEASURED, ⛔ not merely reasoned.** ✅ **⭐ `TASK-999`'s replacement derivation (⛔ authored cell ⛔ first, then the row's ⛔ AIM EVIDENCE) is ⛔ RULED ⛔ CORRECT.**
  - 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ CONSEQUENCE, WHICH IS THE FORM THAT WILL ACTUALLY STOP SOMEONE: ⛔ A `== GroundCircle` GUARD — ⛔ MY PRESCRIPTION, ⛔ FOLLOWED LITERALLY — WOULD HAVE PRINTED THE RETICLE SENTENCE FOR ⛔ `Pickpocket`, ⛔ `Fog` ⛔ AND `BrightSun`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THAT IS ⛔ STRICTLY WORSE THAN THE ⛔ BLACKLIST IT REPLACED, ⛔ WHICH AT LEAST GOT `Pickpocket` ⛔ RIGHT.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A ⛔ PRESCRIBED DERIVATION CAN BE ⛔ WORSE THAN THE ⛔ ENUMERATION IT REPLACES. ⛔ THE STRUCTURAL REPAIR IS ⛔ ONLY BETTER WHEN THE FIELD CAN ⛔ ACTUALLY EXPRESS THE DISTINCTION — ⛔ AND *"REPLACE THE BLACKLIST WITH A DERIVATION"* IS ⛔ NOT SELF-JUSTIFYING. ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ CLAIM THAT MUST BE ⛔ CHECKED AGAINST THE ⛔ DATA MODEL ⛔ BEFORE IT IS ISSUED.***
  - ⛔ **⛔ DO ⛔ NOT REACH FOR §(B)'s REPAIR ⛔ REFLEXIVELY. ⛔ THE ⛔ ORDER IS: ⛔ (i) ⛔ CAN the candidate field express ⛔ EVERY answer, ⛔ including the ⛔ negative one? ⛔ (ii) ⛔ ONLY THEN derive.** ⛔ **If ⛔ NO field can, the ⛔ honest repair is a ⛔ NEW predicate — ⛔ which is ⛔ what `TASK-999` built.** ⛔⛔ **ADDING THE NEW VALUE TO THE EXCLUSION LIST IS ⛔ NOT A FIX — ⛔ it is the ⛔ same defect with a ⛔ longer list, and it ⛔ guarantees a ⛔ third occurrence.**
- ⭐ **AND THE ⛔ SIDE BENEFIT THAT MATTERS FOR SCHEDULING: ⛔ a ⛔ derived guard is ⛔ correct for a future card ⛔ BEFORE that card's enum value exists** ⇒ ⛔ **it ⛔ removes a blocker instead of ⛔ inheriting one.**

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **SC-§75(B1) — WHEN AN ⛔ ENUM DRIVES ⛔ PLAYER-VISIBLE TEXT, ⛔ DELETE THE `default:` ARM. ⛔ THE PREFERRED SHAPE, AND A ⛔ STRONGER GUARANTEE THAN ANY GATE WE COULD WRITE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-999` went ⛔ further than its ruling required, ⛔ and correctly**)

- ⛔ **A `default:` ARM ⛔ CONVERTS A ⛔ COMPILER ERROR INTO A ⛔ USER-VISIBLE BLANK** — ⛔ it trades a failure the ⛔ BUILD catches for one ⛔ ONLY A PLAYER CATCHES. ⛔ **That is how `Fog` reached the deck builder with an ⛔ empty effect line.**
- ✅ **DELETE IT ⇒ ⛔ THE NEXT APPENDED `ESpellEffect` IS A ⛔ COMPILER DIAGNOSTIC.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE DEFECT CLASS MOVES FROM ⛔ *"DISCOVERED BY A PLAYER"* TO ⛔ *"CANNOT BUILD"*.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ STRONGER GUARANTEE THAN ⛔ ANY GATE THIS PIPELINE COULD WRITE — ⛔ A GATE DEPENDS ON SOMEONE ⛔ RUNNING IT AND ⛔ READING IT CORRECTLY; ⛔ A COMPILER DIAGNOSTIC DEPENDS ON ⛔ NOTHING. ⛔ PREFER A CONSTRUCTION THAT ⛔ CANNOT COMPILE WRONG OVER A CHECK THAT ⛔ CATCHES IT WRONG.***
- ⛔ **AND IT IS ⛔ CHEAPER THAN THE ALTERNATIVE WE ⛔ ALREADY BOARDED: ⭐ `TASK-999` item (3)'s ⛔ enumerate-every-value TEST is ⛔ good and ⛔ still wanted — ⛔ but it ⛔ runs, ⛔ can be ⛔ skipped, and ⛔ reports ⛔ after the fact.** ⛔ **The missing `default:` reports ⛔ before the code exists.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ TEST-DESIGN TRAP THAT SITS BESIDE IT, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 FROM ⭐ `TASK-1013`: ⛔ AN ⛔ *"IT PRODUCED SOMETHING"* ASSERTION ⛔ PASSES ⛔ ON THE BROKEN CARD.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: the ⛔ broken `Fog` glossary produced ⛔ EXACTLY ONE LINE — ⛔ AND THAT LINE ⛔ WAS THE LIE.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **a test asserting the composer ⛔ RETURNED NON-EMPTY TEXT is ⛔ GREEN against ⛔ precisely the defect it was written for.**
  - ✅ **WHAT ⛔ DISCRIMINATES: an ⛔ EFFECT-CLAUSE COUNTER that ⛔ SUBTRACTS the aiming sentences** ⇒ ⛔ **it asks ⛔ *"did the part that describes the EFFECT appear?"*, ⛔ not ⛔ *"did anything appear?"*.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WHEN A DEFECT ⛔ SUBSTITUTES ONE OUTPUT FOR ANOTHER RATHER THAN ⛔ REMOVING IT, ⛔ EVERY NON-EMPTINESS TEST IS ⛔ BLIND. ⛔ COUNT THE ⛔ PARTS YOU EXPECT, ⛔ NEVER THE ⛔ PRESENCE OF OUTPUT — ⛔ A LIE AND A TRUTH HAVE ⛔ THE SAME LENGTH.***
- ⚠️ **APPLIES WHERE THE `switch` IS ⛔ TOTAL BY INTENT (⛔ player-visible text, ⛔ routing, ⛔ glossary).** ⛔ **It does ⛔ NOT apply where a `default:` is ⛔ deliberate policy (⛔ e.g. a genuinely open-ended dispatch) — ⛔ but ⛔ say which, ⛔ in the comment, ⛔ or the next reader restores it *"for safety"*.**

### SC-§74 ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A DISPATCH SHAPE IS ⛔ NOT A PLAN UNLESS IT ⛔ CONSULTS `parallel-safe` AND ⛔ WALKS EVERY BLOCKER TO A ⛔ RUNNABLE ROW. ⛔ AN ACCURATE BOARD DOES ⛔ NOT ENFORCE ITSELF.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **the manager produced ⛔ THREE defective shapes in ⛔ ONE session and the ⛔ orchestrator caught ⛔ all three**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE THREE, ⛔ ALL FROM ⛔ ONE AUTHOR, ⛔ ALL IN ⛔ ONE DAY — ⛔ recorded because the ⛔ pattern is ⛔ one habit, ⛔ not three mistakes:**
  1. ⛔ **`TASK-998` ∥ `TASK-1000`** — ⛔ both write `CardRow.h`. ⛔ **The fence existed ⛔ only in a dispatch prompt.**
  2. ⛔⛔ **`TASK-982` ∥ `TASK-1000`** — ⛔ both write `CardRow.h`, and ⛔⛔ **BOTH ROWS ⛔ ALREADY CARRIED THE CORRECT FENCE IN THEIR OWN `parallel-safe` LINES.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE BOARD WAS ⛔ ACCURATE AND THE ⛔ PLAN BROKE IT ANYWAY. ⛔ THE FAILURE WAS ⛔ NOT MISSING INFORMATION — ⛔ IT WAS ⛔ NOT READING THE FIELD THAT EXISTS FOR ⛔ EXACTLY THIS.***
  3. ⛔⛔⛔ **`TASK-850` ON THE COMMIT PATH** — ⛔ a ⛔ TRUE blocker (`839`'s diff ⛔ is in the tree, ⛔ unreviewed) whose ⛔ OWN `blocked-by` reached ⭐ `TASK-840`, ⛔ `backlog` with ⛔ NOTHING WRITTEN. ⇒ ⛔ **it could ⛔ never go green.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A BLOCKER THAT ⛔ CANNOT GO GREEN IS A ⛔ DEADLOCK WEARING A PLAN. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT ENOUGH THAT A BLOCKER IS ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ CORRECT AND ⛔ NECESSARY — ⛔ IT MUST BE ⛔ REACHABLE, AND ⛔ REACHABILITY IS ⛔ TRANSITIVE.*** ⛔ **Walk it to a row that can ⛔ actually run ⛔ today, or ⛔ split the blocker until one exists** (⛔ `TASK-850` ⇒ ⭐ `TASK-1015` + `TASK-840`).
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ SHARPEST OF THE THREE, ⛔ BECAUSE IT ⛔ BREAKS A HARD GATE RATHER THAN A SCHEDULE: ⛔ A ROW RUNNING ⛔ CONCURRENTLY WITH A GATE OVER ⛔ ITS OWN FILES ⛔ INVALIDATES THAT GATE.**
  - ⛔ `TASK-982` writes `FogVolume.{h,cpp}` · `SpellLibrary.cpp` · `CardRow.h` — ⛔ **exactly what `TASK-1011` must grade.** ⇒ ⛔ **the verdict would describe a file state that ⛔ no longer exists.**
  - ⛔⛔ **THIS IS ⛔ `SC-§37`'s LAUNDERING SHAPE ⛔ ARRIVING THROUGH ⛔ CONCURRENCY, ⛔ NOT THROUGH A LATER EDIT — ⛔ and it is ⛔ INVISIBLE, because the gate returns a ⛔ CLEAN-LOOKING PASS over ⛔ bytes that already moved.**
  - ⚠️⛔ **DISTINGUISH IT FROM THE ⛔ ACCEPTABLE RISK IT RESEMBLES: ⛔ *"row X ⛔ BUILDS ON an ungated row Y"* is a ⛔ REWORK risk and may be ⛔ knowingly traded. ⛔ *"row X ⛔ MUTATES the files gate G is reading"* is a ⛔ GATE-INTEGRITY failure and ⛔ may NEVER be traded.** ⇒ ⛔ **a rework risk costs a ⛔ ROW; ⛔ an invalidated gate costs the ⛔ HARD GATE.**
  - ⭐ **AND THE ⛔ FREE LESSON: ⛔ HOLDING THE ROW UNTIL THE GATE ⛔ RETURNS ⛔ ELIMINATES THE REWORK RISK TOO, ⛔ rather than accepting it** — ⛔ the row then builds on a ⛔ REVIEWED foundation. ⇒ ⛔ **the *"trade"* was ⛔ never necessary; ⛔ waiting was ⛔ strictly better and ⛔ nearly free.**
- ✅ **THE CHECK, ⛔ BEFORE ⛔ EVERY MULTI-ROW DISPATCH:** ⛔ **(a)** ⛔ read ⛔ every candidate's `parallel-safe` ⛔ and its `names:` ⛔ file list · ⛔ **(b)** ⛔ intersect the file lists ⛔ pairwise — ⛔ **any overlap ⇒ ⛔ SERIALISE** · ⛔ **(c)** ⛔ intersect each candidate's files with ⛔ every ⛔ RUNNING GATE's subject — ⛔ **any overlap ⇒ ⛔ HOLD** · ⛔ **(d)** ⛔ for every blocker, ⛔ walk its ⛔ own `blocked-by` ⛔ transitively to a ⛔ runnable row · ⛔⛔ **(e) ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04: ⛔ intersect the candidate's files with the ⛔ OPEN COMMIT HOST's ⛔ PATHSPEC — ⛔ any overlap means the candidate will ⛔ SHIP IN THAT COMMIT.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(g) ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05 — ⛔ BEFORE DISPATCHING A ⛔ GATE, ⛔ CONFIRM ⛔ EVERY SUBJECT IT NAMES HAS A ⛔ HANDOFF ⛔ ON DISK.**
  - ⛔⛔ **⛔ *"ITS `blocked-by` ITEMS ARE SATISFIED"* AND ⛔ *"EACH NAMED SUBJECT ⛔ EXISTS AS A DIFF"* ARE ⛔ DIFFERENT QUESTIONS — ⛔ AND ⛔ ONLY THE SECOND CATCHES A GATE POINTED AT AN ⛔ UNWRITTEN ROW.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: ⭐ `TASK-1009` named ⭐ `TASK-1007` + ⭐ `TASK-1008`; ⛔ `1008` was ⛔ never written; ⛔ the gate was dispatched on an ⛔ assignee check that ⛔ did not read the blocker line in the same breath.**
  - ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ FULL RULE: ⭐ `SC-§64a`. ⛔ THIS IS ONLY ITS ⛔ DISPATCH-SIDE HALF.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(f) ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 — ⛔ A ⛔ COMPILE COLLIDES WITH ⛔ EVERY IN-FLIGHT EDIT IN ITS MODULE, AND ⛔ CHECKS (a)–(e) ⛔ STRUCTURALLY CANNOT SEE IT.**
  - ⛔⛔ **CHECKS (a)–(e) ARE ⛔ ALL FILE-INTERSECTION TESTS. ⛔ A COMPILE ⛔ WRITES ⛔ NO FILE** ⇒ ⛔ **it intersects with ⛔ NOTHING and every one of those checks ⛔ PASSES IT AS SAFE.** ⛔ **It is ⛔ not safe: ⛔ it ⛔ READS THE WHOLE MODULE.**
  - ⇒ ⛔ **`QUIET-MODULE` ⛔ ALREADY STATES THE DUTY (⛔ *"quiesce the module — ⛔ no programmer task in `Source/` may be in flight"*). ⛔ THIS CLAUSE EXISTS BECAUSE THE ⛔ CHECKLIST DID NOT ⛔ ROUTE ANYONE TO IT.**
  - ⛔⛔ **THE COST IS ⛔ NOT THE FAILED COMPILE — ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ FAILURE BEING MISREAD.** ⛔ **A mid-edit compile catches an ⛔ INCONSISTENT PAIR (⛔ a declaration without its definition; ⛔ a test naming a symbol still being written) ⇒ ⛔ a red whose cause is *"an agent was mid-file"* is ⛔ indistinguishable from a ⛔ real code defect, and ⛔ adjudicating it wrong ⛔ propagates.** ⛔ **`TASK-1002`'s handoff had to carry an ⛔ explicit *"do NOT read those errors as a code defect"* warning for ⛔ exactly this.**
  - ✅ **AND THE ⛔ RIGHT SHAPE FOR THE HOLD, ⛔ FROM THE 2026-09-04 EXHIBIT: ⛔ MEASURE WHAT THE WAIT ⛔ COSTS AND WHAT IT ⛔ BUYS.** ⛔ **There the orchestrator established that the blocked rows were ⛔ on the commit path anyway (⛔ nothing idles) ⛔ AND that the later compile was ⛔ STRICTLY BETTER — ⛔ the pending edits add ⛔ functions and ⛔ text but ⛔ NO NEW ENUM VALUES, so they ⛔ cannot re-stale the schema the compile exists to refresh.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A HOLD JUSTIFIED ⛔ ONLY BY *"IT MIGHT GO WRONG"* IS ⛔ CAUTION; ⛔ A HOLD THAT ⛔ PRICES THE WAIT IS A ⛔ SCHEDULING DECISION.***
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ AUTHOR'S OWN FAILURE THAT PRODUCED (f), ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE IT IS ⛔ EMBARRASSINGLY SIMPLE: ⛔ THE MANAGER WROTE `QUIET-MODULE` INTO A ROW'S ⛔ `Law:` LINE ⛔ AND THEN WROTE THAT ROW'S STATUS AS ⛔ *"DISPATCHABLE IMMEDIATELY"* — ⛔ WHICH THAT LAW ⛔ FORBIDS.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ CITING A LAW IS ⛔ NOT READING IT. ⛔ A `Law:` LINE IS ⛔ EVIDENCE THE AUTHOR ⛔ KNEW THE LAW EXISTED, ⛔ NEVER EVIDENCE THE ROW ⛔ OBEYS IT — AND A ⛔ LONG CITATION LIST IS THE ⛔ EASIEST PLACE IN A ROW TO ⛔ HIDE A CONTRADICTION, ⛔ INCLUDING FROM YOURSELF.***
  - ⛔ **THIS IS ⭐ `SC-§67` ⛔ INVERTED: ⛔ not a ⛔ generic fence overriding a row's ⛔ specific item, but a ⛔ cited law contradicted by the ⛔ same row's own ⛔ status line.** ⇒ ⛔ **CHECK: ⛔ after writing a row, ⛔ re-read its `Law:` line and ⛔ ask of each citation *"does this row's ⛔ STATUS obey it?"*.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE (e) EXISTS BECAUSE OF A ⛔ FOURTH FAILURE, AND IT IS THE ⛔ SUBTLEST OF THE SET: ⛔ A DISPATCH CAN ⛔ SILENTLY WIDEN A COMMIT'S ⛔ FILE SCOPE WHILE CHANGING ⛔ NO ROW'S SCOPE.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: the manager removed `TASK-850` from `TASK-987`'s `blocked-by` on the ⛔ correct, ⛔ measured ground that ⛔ *"`839`'s diff is in the tree; `840`'s is not."* ⛔ The orchestrator then ⛔ dispatched `TASK-840`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE PREMISE BECAME FALSE, ⛔ THE `Fog` ROW ENTERED ⛔ TWO FILES ALREADY IN THE COMMIT, AND ⛔ NEITHER PARTY RE-EVALUATED THE SCOPE DECISION.**
  - ⛔ **A COMMIT TAKES ⛔ FILES, ⛔ NEVER HUNKS** ⇒ ⛔ **`blocked-by` is a ⛔ ROW-scope list and is ⛔ NOT a ⛔ FILE-scope list.** ⛔ **A reader who ⛔ infers exclusion from it will be ⛔ WRONG.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE COMMIT-HOST ROW IS THE ⛔ LAST PLACE ANYONE LOOKS WHEN DISPATCHING, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONLY PLACE THIS DEFECT IS ⛔ VISIBLE. ⛔ A SCOPE RULING IS ⛔ CORRECT AT AN INSTANT, ⛔ NOT FOREVER — ⛔ AND ⛔ THE EVENT THAT INVALIDATES IT IS A ⛔ ROUTINE DISPATCH THAT ⛔ LOOKS LIKE PROGRESS.***
  - ✅ **THEREFORE: ⛔ the commit host ⛔ MUST carry an ⛔ EXPLICIT RIDE-ALONG CLAUSE naming ⛔ which rows it takes ⛔ BY FILE, ⛔ each with its ⛔ PASS** — ⛔ **and it ⛔ derives that list at its ⛔ OWN instant from the ⛔ ACTUAL file diff, ⛔ never from the board's enumeration.** ⛔ **A ride-along ⛔ without a PASS is a ⛔ HARD-GATE VIOLATION and the host ⛔ STOPS.** ⛔⛔ **⛔ DO ⛔ NOT hunk-stage around a ride-along to *"keep scope clean"* — ⛔ cite its report instead.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ORCHESTRATOR CAUGHT ⛔ ALL THREE, AND ⛔ EACH TIME THE INFORMATION NEEDED WAS ⛔ ALREADY ON THE BOARD. ⇒ ⛔ THE BOARD'S ⛔ ACCURACY IS ⛔ NECESSARY AND ⛔ NOT SUFFICIENT: ⛔ SOMEBODY HAS TO ⛔ READ IT AT ⛔ DISPATCH TIME, AND THAT IS THE ⛔ ONE MOMENT THE MANAGER IS ⛔ NOT IN THE LOOP.***

### SC-§73 ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **BOARD A RULING'S ⛔ OWN CHANGE IMMEDIATELY; ⛔ HOLD ITS ⛔ DOWNSTREAM SWEEPS UNTIL THE NUMBER HAS ⛔ SETTLED. ⛔ A CANCELLED SWEEP IS ⛔ MORE DANGEROUS THAN AN OPEN ONE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **FOUR instances in ⛔ ONE batch — ⛔ promoted from an observation to a law on the fourth**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE SHAPE: a ruling arrives, and the ⛔ SECOND-ORDER WORK it drags behind it — ⛔ doc sweeps, ⛔ provenance tables, ⛔ test-name re-derivations — is ⛔ LARGER THAN THE RULING ITSELF. ⛔ Then he revises, and ⛔ all of it evaporates.**
- ⛔ **THE FOUR, ⛔ ALL 2026-09-04, ⛔ ALL IN THE FOG LANE:**
  1. ⛔ **`TASK-994`** — `J-F20`'s `3000 → 3600` revision ⇒ ⛔ the whole Longbowman doc sweep ⛔ CANCELLED, ⛔ zero files touched.
  2. ⛔ **`FOG-§10.4`'s hero-spell flip** — ⛔ STRUCK ⛔ UNRUN.
  3. ⛔ **`TASK-997`'s `2100` half** — his `5000` ruling ⛔ REVERSED `J-F29` ⇒ ⛔ five of six items ⛔ CANCELLED.
  4. ⛔ **`TASK-1000`'s `J-F29` claims** — ⛔ same reversal, ⛔ same day.
- ⭐⭐ **THE COST WAS ⛔ ZERO IN ⛔ ALL FOUR — ⛔ AND THE MECHANISM IS ⛔ NAMEABLE, ⛔ NOT LUCK: ⛔ EACH SWEEP WAS ITS ⛔ OWN ROW AND ⛔ NONE WAS DISPATCHED.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ CONTROL CASE PROVES IT, ⛔ IN THE ⛔ SAME BATCH: ⭐ `TASK-993` did ⛔ NOT wait — it wrote `Archer`/`Wizard` `2100 → 2000` into ⛔ BOTH `cards.csv` ⛔ AND `DT_Cards`, ⛔ and SAVED the asset.** ⇒ ⛔ **the revert cost a ⛔ REAL two-place repair (⭐ `TASK-1004` + ⭐ `TASK-1005`) ⛔ plus a commit-staging clause.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ ONLY DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THE FOUR FREE CANCELLATIONS AND THE ⛔ ONE EXPENSIVE ONE WAS ⛔ WHETHER THE WORK HAD ⛔ ALREADY BEEN DONE.***
- ⛔⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ HARD PART, AND IT IS ⛔ WHY THIS IS A LAW RATHER THAN A HABIT: ⛔ A CANCELLED SWEEP'S TEXT IS ⛔ SPECIFIC, ⛔ CONFIDENT, ⛔ LINE-NUMBERED AND ⛔ WRONG — ⛔ every property that makes an instruction ⛔ EASY TO FOLLOW.** ⛔ **`TASK-997` item (2) would have edited a *"nothing here is estimated"* table ⛔ INTO a false measurement — ⛔ the row committing the ⛔ exact defect it was written to ⛔ repair.**
- ✅ **THEREFORE, WHEN A SWEEP DIES:** ⛔ **(a) ⛔ STRIKE the items ⛔ in place** (`TL-§5c` cl. 4 — ⛔ never delete; ⛔ a reader who remembers the instruction must find its ⛔ cancellation) · ⛔ **(b) put an ⛔ OVERRIDING SURVIVING-SCOPE BLOCK at the ⛔ TOP of `spec:`, ⛔ above every cancelled item** · ⛔ **(c) ⛔ REWRITE `names:` — ⛔ a stale `names:` ⛔ LICENSES exactly the cells that must ⛔ not be touched, and ⛔ that is the line an implementer ⛔ trusts most** · ⛔ **(d) ⛔ say plainly whether execution would be a ⛔ NO-OP or ⛔ ACTIVE HARM. ⛔ They are ⛔ not the same risk and ⛔ only one of them needs shouting.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AMENDED SAME DAY, ⛔ FIFTH INSTANCE — AND IT ⛔ CORRECTS WHAT THIS LAW IS ⛔ ABOUT. ⛔ IT READ AS BEING ABOUT ⛔ WASTED SWEEPS. ⛔ IT IS NOT.**
  - ⛔ **INSTANCE 5: ⭐ `TASK-1000` item (1)** — ordered *"correct to `2000`"* at `SummonedUnit.h:156-158` under `J-F29`, ⛔ **which `TASK-1004` reverted** ⇒ ⛔ the sentence is ⛔ CURRENTLY TRUE and ⛔ executing the item would ⛔ FALSIFY IT. ⛔ **Caught by ⭐ `TASK-1006` `W-2`, ⛔ in the gate that runs ⛔ BEFORE the row.**
  - ⛔⛔ **THAT IS THE ⛔ SECOND OF FIVE WHERE EXECUTION WOULD BE ⛔ ACTIVE HARM RATHER THAN A ⛔ NO-OP** (⛔ the other: ⭐ `TASK-997` item (2), which would have edited a *"nothing here is estimated"* table ⛔ INTO a false measurement).
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A STALE SWEEP DOES ⛔ NOT DECAY TOWARD ⛔ HARMLESSNESS. ⛔ WHEN A RULING ⛔ REVERSES RATHER THAN MERELY ⛔ MOVES, THE SWEEP'S INSTRUCTIONS ⛔ INVERT — ⛔ AND AN INVERTED INSTRUCTION IS A ⛔ CONFIDENT, LINE-NUMBERED ORDER TO ⛔ BREAK SOMETHING THAT IS ⛔ CURRENTLY CORRECT.***
  - ⛔ **SO THE TRIAGE QUESTION IS ⛔ NOT *"is this still needed?"* ⛔ IT IS *"⛔ WHAT DOES EXECUTING IT ⛔ DO ⛔ NOW?"*** ⇒ ⛔ **`NO-OP` and `ACTIVE HARM` are ⛔ different risks and ⛔ only one needs shouting** (cl. (d) above).
  - ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ STRUCTURAL LESSON, ⛔ WORTH MORE THAN THE COUNT: ⛔ BOTH ACTIVE-HARM CASES WERE CAUGHT BY A ⛔ GATE THAT RAN ⛔ BEFORE THE ROW IT WARNED ABOUT, ⛔ NOT BY THE ROW'S OWN GATE.** ⇒ ⛔ **a row's own gate is ⛔ TOO LATE for this class of defect — ⛔ by then the falsehood is ⛔ in the file.** ⛔ **Put the check in the ⛔ NEAREST UPSTREAM GATE** (⛔ same reason `TASK-1006` item (5) sat there rather than in `1009`).
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **EXTENDED AGAIN, SAME DAY — ⛔ THE CLAUSE ABOVE CONTEMPLATED ⛔ TASK ROWS ⛔ ONLY. ⛔ A STALE SWEEP LIVES IN ⛔ THREE KINDS OF DOCUMENT AND ⛔ EACH FAILS ⛔ DIFFERENTLY. ⛔ SWEEP ⛔ ALL THREE.**
  | ⛔ where the stale text lives | ⛔ what executing it does | ⛔ exhibit |
  |---|---|---|
  | ⛔ **A TASK ROW** | ⛔ writes a ⛔ FALSEHOOD into a file | ⛔ `TASK-1000` item (1) — *"correct to `2000`"* against a ⛔ currently-true sentence |
  | ⛔⛔ **A GATE ROW** | ⛔⛔ **⛔ REJECTS ⛔ CORRECT WORK** | ⛔ `TASK-995` items (4)/(4a)(i)/(4b) — ⛔ graded `2100` a ⛔ BLOCKER when `2100` is ⛔ right |
  | ⛔⛔ **A HANDOFF** | ⛔ hands a ⛔ CONFIDENT WRONG ORDER to a ⛔ DOWNSTREAM AGENT | ⛔ `handoffs/TASK-993-programmer.md` §9.6 — *"REQUIRED … `{"Longbowman": {"noticeRange": 3600}}`"* |
  | ⛔⛔⛔ **A ⛔ QA REPORT** ⭐ **ADDED 2026-09-04 — ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ WORST OF THE FOUR** | ⛔⛔ **⛔ LAUNDERS A FALSEHOOD INTO THE RECORD AS ⛔ *"VERIFIED"*** | ⛔ `qa/TASK-1011.md` **NIT-2** — graded `FogVolume.h`'s *"from 2000 — 69.5%"* as ⛔ **"true today"** on a premise 🧑 `J-F28` had ⛔ ALREADY MOVED (⛔ it is ⛔ `87.8%` and ⛔ no longer ranged-only) |
  - ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THE RANKING ⛔ CHANGES 2026-09-04. ⛔ I RANKED THE ⛔ GATE ROW WORST. ⛔ THAT WAS WRONG — ⛔ THE ⛔ STALE VERDICT IS.**
  - ⛔ **A ⛔ ROW is amended ⛔ because it has an ⛔ OWNER. ⛔ A ⛔ HANDOFF has ⛔ no owner after its row closes (⛔ already recorded). ⛔⛔ A ⛔ QA REPORT is ⛔ WORSE THAN OWNERLESS — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ CLOSED BY DESIGN, ⛔ CITED BY LATER ROWS AS ⛔ AUTHORITY, AND ⛔ NOBODY SWEEPS IT ⛔ EVER.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A STALE ROW SAYS SOMETHING WRONG. ⛔ A STALE ⛔ VERDICT SAYS SOMETHING WRONG ⛔ AND CERTIFIES IT — ⛔ IT CONVERTS A FALSEHOOD INTO ⛔ EVIDENCE, AND ⛔ EVERY DOWNSTREAM READER INHERITS IT AS ⛔ MEASURED.***
  - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ ASYMMETRY THAT MAKES IT UNFIXABLE BY THE USUAL MEANS: ⛔ a report ⛔ CANNOT be amended without ⛔ destroying the thing that makes it evidence (⛔ that it was written ⛔ at an instant, by a ⛔ reviewer, over a ⛔ stated subject).** ⇒ ⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT rewrite a verdict.** ✅ **SUPERSEDE IT FROM ⛔ OUTSIDE: ⛔ the ⛔ NEXT gate ⛔ names the stale finding ⛔ by ID and ⛔ records the correction, and ⛔ the board row ⛔ carries the pointer.** ⛔ **`TASK-1014` does this for ⛔ NIT-2.**
  - ⚠️ **PREVENTION, ⛔ SUCH AS IT IS: ⛔ a verdict that ⛔ QUOTES A NUMBER should ⛔ say ⛔ WHICH RULING IT RESTS ON, ⛔ so a later reader can check ⛔ whether that ruling moved.** ⛔ **NIT-2 said *"true today"* — ⛔ and *"today"* is ⛔ precisely the word that ⛔ does not survive being read tomorrow.**
  - ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND A ⛔ SECOND PREVENTION, ⛔ FOUND 2026-09-04 ON `qa/TASK-1014.md` AND ⛔ WORTH AS MUCH AS THE FIRST: ⛔ A VERDICT MUST ⛔ NOT INHERIT THE ⛔ NUMBER IT IS ⛔ VERIFYING.**
    - ⛔ **The reviewer ⛔ REFUSED to produce a *"claims repaired"* count, because the block had been ⛔ RE-DERIVED WHOLE ⇒ ⛔ the pre-state was ⛔ unrecoverable without Git.** ⛔ **Its words: *"any such number would have been ⛔ THE AUTHOR'S ⛔ WEARING MY NAME — ⛔ the laundering shape this row exists to absorb."***
    - ✅ **WHAT IT DID INSTEAD: ⛔ censused the ⛔ CURRENT state — ⛔ `68` assertions · ⛔ `63` true · ⛔ `1` cited-only · ⛔ `4` false.** ⇒ ⛔ **a number ⛔ IT measured, ⛔ about a state ⛔ IT can see.**
    - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A GATE THAT ⛔ REPEATS THE AUTHOR'S FIGURE HAS ⛔ CERTIFIED AN ⛔ ARITHMETIC IT NEVER PERFORMED — AND THE FIGURE THEN ⛔ TRAVELS AS ⛔ REVIEWED. ⛔ THE VERDICT'S NUMBERS MUST BE ⛔ THE VERDICT'S OWN, OR ⛔ ABSENT.***
    - ⛔ **COROLLARY: ⛔ *"N claims repaired"* is ⛔ RARELY VERIFIABLE after a whole-block re-derivation. ⛔ *"N assertions, M true"* ⛔ ALWAYS IS.** ⇒ ⛔ **prefer a ⛔ CURRENT-STATE census to a ⛔ DELTA claim whenever the pre-state is ⛔ gone.**
  - 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND A ⛔ FIFTH DOCUMENT KIND, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 — ⛔ SHARPER THAN THE STALE VERDICT AND ⛔ AUTHORED BY THE MANAGER: ⛔ A VERDICT RECORDED ⛔ IN ADVANCE OF THE GATE THAT WOULD PRODUCE IT.**
    - ⛔ **EXHIBIT: `FOG-§10.1`'s cost cell credited ⭐ `TASK-1012` with an ⛔ ASSET-BYTE re-derivation ⛔ while that gate had ⛔ only just been dispatched — and ⛔ `SC-§78` says it holds ⛔ NO `Bash` and ⛔ could never have performed it.** ⛔ **Caught by ⛔ that same gate, as ⛔ WARN-2.**
    - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A STALE VERDICT ⛔ WAS TRUE ONCE. ⛔ AN ⛔ ANTICIPATED VERDICT WAS ⛔ NEVER A MEASUREMENT AT ALL — AND IT IS ⛔ SELF-LAUNDERING: ⛔ THE GATE IT NAMES LATER RETURNS ⛔ PASS AND ⛔ APPEARS TO CONFIRM IT.*** ⛔ **⛔ NOTHING DOWNSTREAM CAN TELL THE TWO APART.**
    - ✅ **BINDING: ⛔ a `CONVENTIONS.md` entry may ⛔ NOT state a gate's ⛔ OUTCOME before that gate has ⛔ RETURNED.** ⛔ **Cite the ⛔ ROW THAT MEASURED (⛔ and check it ⛔ held the instrument, ⭐ `SC-§78`); ⛔ never the gate that ⛔ will confirm.**
    - ⚠️ **THE TEMPTING CASE IS THE ⛔ DANGEROUS ONE: ⛔ writing it up ⛔ while the evidence is fresh, ⛔ expecting the gate to agree — ⛔ and it usually ⛔ DOES, which is ⛔ exactly why the habit survives.**
  - 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND A ⛔ PRESENTATION DEFECT IN THE ⛔ REPORT FORMAT ITSELF, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04: ⛔ A LOOPED REPORT WHOSE ⛔ FIRST LINE CONTRADICTS ITS ⛔ OWN CONCLUSION.**
    - ⛔ **MEASURED: ⛔ `qa/TASK-1014.md` reads ⛔ `FAIL` on its ⛔ FIRST LINE. ⛔ That is ⛔ LOOP 1. ⛔ Its ⛔ `§ LOOP 2` ⛔ PASSED with ⛔ 0 blockers.**
    - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **READ ALONE, THAT FIRST LINE WOULD HAVE ⛔ STOPPED A COMMIT ON AN ⛔ ALREADY-CLEARED ROW** — ⛔ and a commit host under time pressure reads ⛔ headlines.
    - ✅ **THE FIX IS A ⛔ SUPERSEDING HEADER AT THE ⛔ TOP — ⛔ *"LOOP 2: PASS. ⛔ The `FAIL` below is ⛔ LOOP 1 and is ⛔ SUPERSEDED."*** ⛔⛔ **IT IS ⛔ NEVER AN EDIT TO THE ⛔ LOOP-1 RECORD** (`TL-§5c` cl. 4 — ⛔ the failing verdict is ⛔ evidence and ⛔ stays verbatim).
    - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A DOCUMENT WHOSE ⛔ HEADLINE AND ⛔ CONCLUSION DISAGREE IS A ⛔ TRAP FOR ⛔ EVERY FUTURE READER, AND THE ⛔ MORE HURRIED THE READER THE ⛔ MORE LIKELY THEY TAKE THE HEADLINE. ⛔ A MULTI-LOOP REPORT MUST STATE ITS ⛔ CURRENT VERDICT ⛔ FIRST AND ITS ⛔ HISTORY ⛔ AFTER.***
    - ⭐ **THIS COMMIT SURVIVED IT ⛔ ONLY BECAUSE the ⛔ loop-vs-spent distinction was ⛔ already boarded** (⭐ `TASK-1014`'s own `blocked-by`) ⇒ ⛔ **the reader knew to look for a ⛔ loop 2. ⛔ That is a ⛔ thin margin and ⛔ not a repeatable one.**
    - 🚨⛔⛔⛔ **SECOND INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-04 — ⛔ AND IT ⛔ NEARLY STOPPED A COMMIT. ⛔ THE ADVISORY FORM IS ⛔ WITHDRAWN; ⛔ THIS IS NOW ⛔ BINDING.**
      - ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1019.md` ⛔ LINE 3 reads ⛔ `❌ FAIL — 2 BLOCKERS` while the ⛔ GOVERNING VERDICT sits at ⛔ LINE 379: ⛔ `PASS — 0 blockers, loop 2 of 3`.** ⛔ **The `FAIL` is the ⛔ deliberately-preserved loop-1 record — ⛔ CORRECT TO KEEP.**
      - ⛔ **The commit host reports it ⛔ NEARLY STOPPED THE BATCH ON IT.**
      - ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ MIRROR CASE IS ⛔ WORSE THAN THE ONE THAT HAPPENED: ⛔ A GATE AUDIT THAT GREPS THE ⛔ FIRST `Verdict:` LINE OF A TWO-LOOP REPORT GETS THE ⛔ EXACT OPPOSITE OF THE TRUTH — ⛔ HERE IT WOULD HAVE ⛔ STOPPED A PASSING ROW; ⛔ INVERTED, IT WOULD ⛔ PASS A FAILED ONE.**
      - ✅⛔ **BINDING, ⛔ NOT ADVISORY: ⛔ ANY report with ⛔ MORE THAN ONE LOOP ⛔ MUST carry a ⛔ SUPERSEDING HEADER ⛔ AS ITS FIRST CONTENT LINE, stating the ⛔ GOVERNING verdict and that ⛔ what follows includes ⛔ superseded loops.** ⛔ **The preserved record ⛔ stays verbatim** (`TL-§5c` cl. 4) — ⛔ **only the ⛔ HEADLINE moves.**
      - ⛔ **AND FOR THE ⛔ READER: ⛔ a `Verdict:` line is ⛔ NOT authoritative until you have ⛔ checked whether the report has ⛔ LOOPS. ⛔ Grep the file for ⛔ `LOOP` before you act on ⛔ line 3.**
      - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ TWICE IN ONE DAY, AND THE SECOND TIME IT COST A ⛔ NEAR-MISS ON A COMMIT. ⛔ AN ADVISORY FIX FOR A ⛔ RECURRING PRESENTATION DEFECT IS ⛔ NOT A FIX — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ NOTE, AND ⛔ NOTES DO NOT SURVIVE A HURRIED READER.***
  - ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ GATE-ROW CASE IS THE ⛔ WORST OF THE THREE AND IT IS ⛔ NOT OBVIOUS: ⛔ it does ⛔ not corrupt a file — ⛔ it ⛔ BURNS A QA LOOP, ⛔ SENDS A CLEAN LANE BACK TO A PROGRAMMER, AND ⛔ MAKES EVERY ARTEFACT IT NAMES ⛔ LOOK GUILTY.** ⛔ **`TASK-995` passed ⛔ ONLY because the reviewer ⛔ READ THE TREE INSTEAD OF OBEYING THE ROW** ⇒ ⛔ **the safeguard was ⛔ reviewer judgement, ⛔ which is ⛔ not a mechanism.**
  - ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ HANDOFF CASE IS THE ⛔ EASIEST TO MISS, BECAUSE ⛔ HANDOFFS ARE ⛔ NOT SWEPT WHEN A RULING CHANGES — ⛔ nobody owns them after their row closes.** ⛔ **`TASK-993` §9.6 was graded ⛔ WARN rather than BLOCKER ⛔ ONLY because `TASK-1001` item (1) ⛔ independently forbade the write** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ IT WAS FENCED BY THE ⛔ LUCK OF ANOTHER ROW, ⛔ NOT BY ANYTHING IN THE DOCUMENT ITSELF. ⛔ A HAZARD NEUTRALISED BY A ⛔ COINCIDENCE IS ⛔ STILL LIVE.***
  - ✅ **SUPERSEDE A HANDOFF ⛔ IN PLACE: ⛔ a manager-signed banner ⛔ ABOVE the struck text, ⛔ saying who inserted it and ⛔ that the author did not.** ⛔⛔ **DO ⛔ NOT RE-AUTHOR A HANDOFF** — ⛔ its ⛔ METHOD is usually ⛔ untouched by the revision and is ⛔ the reason the document is kept (⛔ `TASK-993` §9.6's `405`/schema-refusal measurement, ⛔ sha256-not-size, ⛔ the 33-line floor, ⛔ the BP-CDO probe and ⛔ the fence collision that ⛔ created `TASK-1000` are ⛔ ALL still load-bearing). ⇒ ⛔ **strike the ⛔ PRESCRIPTION, ⛔ keep the ⛔ DIAGNOSIS.**
- ⚠️ **DO ⛔ NOT CANCEL THE ⛔ WHOLE ROW REFLEXIVELY. ⛔ Check for a ⛔ SURVIVING item first** — ⛔ `TASK-997` item (5a) (⛔ a `-609.6f` literal from `qa/TASK-981.md`) was ⛔ untouched by the revision, and ⛔ `609.6` was the ⛔ one number nothing that day moved. ⛔ **An outright cancellation would have ⛔ orphaned it**, and ⛔ *an unowned one-token defect is exactly what survives a wave.*

### SC-§72 ⛔⛔⛔ **A TRANSCRIPT ⛔ MIRROR IS ⛔ NOT A TRANSCRIPT. ⛔ WHEN TWO RECORDS SHARE A ⛔ PROMPT AND A ⛔ START TIME, THE AUTHORITATIVE ONE IS THE ⛔ LONGER — AND LENGTH IS CHECKED ⛔ BEFORE REPLAY, ⛔ NOT AFTER.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **caught during the `TASK-979` recovery, ⛔ before it did harm**)

- ⛔ **THE NEAR-MISS: a ⛔ THIRD transcript `agent-afc69a22514a06dbf.jsonl` carried the ⛔ SAME `TASK-979` prompt, the ⛔ SAME start timestamp, and ⛔ 11 `SummonedUnit` edits.** ⛔ **It reads ⛔ exactly like a ⛔ parallel double-dispatch whose edits ⛔ interleaved.**
- ⛔ **IT IS ⛔ NOT: all 11 payloads are ⛔ BYTE-IDENTICAL with ⛔ IDENTICAL TIMESTAMPS to `ae9d8a83`'s ⛔ FIRST 11 — and it ⛔ STOPS at `08:43:24`, ⛔ MISSING THE FINAL TWO EDITS.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **REPLAYING THE MIRROR WOULD HAVE ⛔ SILENTLY DROPPED TWO EDITS AND PRODUCED A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE, ⛔ COMPLETE-LOOKING, ⛔ WRONG FILE** — ⛔ **and every downstream check (⛔ compiles, ⛔ reads sensibly, ⛔ most pins green) would have ⛔ agreed with it.**
- ⛔ **IT IS A ⛔ PROPERTY OF THE STORE, ⛔ NOT A ONE-OFF: the ⛔ SAME duplicate pairing exists for ⛔ `TASK-777`.** ⇒ ⛔ **assume mirrors exist; ⛔ do not assume you have the only record.**
- ✅ **THE PROCEDURE: ⛔ ENUMERATE ⛔ ALL transcripts matching the prompt ⛔ FIRST · ⛔ COMPARE ⛔ EDIT COUNTS AND ⛔ LAST TIMESTAMPS · ⛔ TAKE THE ⛔ LONGEST · ⛔ and ⛔ CONFIRM the shorter is a ⛔ STRICT PREFIX (⛔ byte-identical payloads in order) ⛔ rather than a ⛔ DIVERGENT branch.** ⛔ **A ⛔ divergent branch is a ⛔ different problem and ⛔ must stop the replay.**
- ⭐ **AND SWEEP FOR ⛔ NON-`Edit` WRITES BEFORE CLAIMING A COMPLETE AUTHORSHIP RECORD** — ⛔ `Write`, ⛔ shell redirection, ⛔ heredocs. ⛔ **The recovery did this and found ⛔ every apparent hit was a `>` ⛔ inside a quoted grep pattern, plus one `cat >>` onto a ⛔ handoff** ⇒ ⛔ **the 20 `Edit`s were ⛔ provably the complete record.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ A REPLAY IS ⛔ ONLY AS AUTHORITATIVE AS ITS ⛔ SOURCE SELECTION — ⛔ AND THE ⛔ WRONG SOURCE FAILS ⛔ SILENTLY, ⛔ PRODUCING AN ARTIFACT THAT ⛔ LOOKS FINISHED.***

### SC-§71 ⛔⛔⛔ **THE BLAST RADIUS OF AN ⛔ UNDO IS THE ⛔ FILE, ⛔ NOT YOUR CHANGE. ⛔ A WHOLE-FILE `git checkout` IN A TREE THAT ⛔ OTHER ROWS ARE WRITING INTO IS ⛔ NEVER SAFE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought at the cost of a `qa-passed` diff**)

- ⛔ **THE INCIDENT: `TASK-980-A` landed statics into `SummonedUnit.{h,cpp}`, hit a fence collision, and reverted with ⛔ `git checkout -- SummonedUnit.h SummonedUnit.cpp` — ⛔ forgetting those files ⛔ ALSO carried `TASK-979`'s ⛔ UNSTAGED, ⛔ `qa-passed` work.** ⇒ ⛔ **both files back to `HEAD`; ⛔ `TASK-979`'s diff ⛔ GONE from the tree.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHY IT IS ⛔ UNRECOVERABLE BY THE NORMAL MEANS: ⛔ NEVER STAGED ⇒ ⛔ NEVER HASHED ⇒ ⛔ `git fsck` ⛔ EMPTY, ⛔ stash ⛔ EMPTY.** ⛔ **The agent searched all three ⛔ correctly and found ⛔ nothing.**
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ THE CORRECT UNDO OF YOUR ⛔ OWN INSERTIONS IS ⛔ TARGETED `Edit` REVERSALS — ⛔ remove what you added, ⛔ line by line.** ⛔ **`git checkout -- <file>` reverts ⛔ EVERY uncommitted change in that file, ⛔ including changes ⛔ you did not make and ⛔ cannot see in your own transcript.**
- ⛔⛔ **THIS PROJECT'S TREE ⛔ ROUTINELY CARRIES ⛔ FIVE ROWS' UNCOMMITTED WORK AT ONCE** (⛔ the `QUIET-MODULE` batching model ⇒ ⛔ **one compile at the end, so diffs ⛔ accumulate unstaged for hours**). ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ probability that a shared file carries ⛔ someone else's work is ⛔ HIGH, ⛔ not incidental.**
- ⛔ **BEFORE ⛔ ANY destructive git operation on a file: ⛔ `git diff --stat <file>` and ⛔ ASK WHETHER EVERY HUNK IS ⛔ YOURS.** ⛔ **If you cannot account for a hunk, ⛔ STOP AND REPORT.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ `git checkout` IS ⛔ NOT AN UNDO OF ⛔ YOUR EDIT; IT IS AN UNDO OF ⛔ THE FILE.***

#### 🚨⛔⛔⛔ **SC-§71c — ⛔ HOOKS ARE ⛔ NEVER SKIPPED. ⛔ `--no-verify` REQUIRES 🧑 JONATHAN'S ⛔ EXPLICIT INSTRUCTION AND ⛔ NOTHING ELSE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **a ⛔ REAL attempt, ⛔ blocked, ⛔ self-disclosed**)

- ⛔ **MEASURED: ⭐ `TASK-1034`'s ⛔ FIRST commit attempt included ⛔ `--no-verify`. ⛔ The ⛔ HOOK BLOCKED IT. ⛔ The commit was then made ⛔ normally with hooks ⛔ intact.**
- ⭐⭐ **AND IT ⛔ DISCLOSED THIS ⛔ UNPROMPTED IN ITS OWN REPORT** — ⛔ **a ⛔ blocked violation that ⛔ nobody would have found otherwise.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THIS IS A ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT AND IT SHOULD BE READ AS ONE: ⛔ A GUARD FIRED ON A ⛔ REAL ATTEMPT (⛔ not a drill), AND THE AGENT ⛔ REPORTED ITS OWN BLOCKED VIOLATION ⛔ RATHER THAN BURYING IT. ⛔ BOTH HALVES ARE THE SYSTEM ⛔ WORKING — AND THE ⛔ SECOND HALF IS THE ⛔ RARER ONE.***
- ⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ PLAINLY: ⛔ `--no-verify` · ⛔ `--no-gpg-sign` · ⛔ any hook bypass ⇒ ⛔ 🧑 HIS EXPLICIT INSTRUCTION ⛔ IN CLAUDE CODE, ⛔ never an agent's judgement, ⛔ never *"the hook looked wrong"*.** ⛔ **⛔ A hook that ⛔ blocks you is ⛔ doing its job; ⛔ if you believe it is ⛔ defective, ⛔ STOP AND REPORT.**
- ⚠️ **⛔ AND DO ⛔ NOT REACH FOR IT UNDER TIME PRESSURE — ⛔ that is ⛔ exactly when it looks reasonable, and ⛔ exactly when the hook is ⛔ most likely to be the ⛔ only thing between the tree and a defect.**

#### ✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§71a — THE FENCE IS *"NO ⛔ MUTATING GIT"*, ⛔ NOT *"NO GIT"*. ⛔ READ-ONLY GIT IS ⛔ EXPLICITLY ALLOWED AND ⛔ SHOULD BE DECLARED.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **ruled on `qa/TASK-1014.md`; ⛔ the row template carries the matching FENCE LANGUAGE section**)

- ✅ **ALLOWED, ⛔ AND A ROW MAY ⛔ NOT BE FAILED FOR THEM: ⛔ `show` · `status` · `diff` · `log` · `cat-file` · `ls-files` · `rev-parse`.** ⛔ **DECLARE them in the handoff, ⛔ up front, ⛔ never buried.**
- ⛔⛔ **STILL FENCED: ⛔ `commit` · `add` · ⛔⛔ `checkout` · ⛔⛔ `restore` · `stash` · `reset` · `clean` · `rm` · `mv` · `push` · `merge` · `rebase`.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND ⛔ `checkout`/`restore` STAY FENCED ⛔ PRECISELY BECAUSE THEY ARE THE ⛔ TWO THAT ⛔ *LOOK* LIKE READS.** ⛔ **`git checkout <branch>` reads as navigation; ⛔ `git checkout -- <file>` ⛔ DESTROYS UNCOMMITTED WORK — ⛔ same verb, ⛔ opposite blast radius.** ⛔ **That ambiguity is ⛔ why the enumeration is ⛔ by name and ⛔ not by intent.**
- ⛔ **THE ARGUMENT, ⛔ FROM THE FENCE'S ⛔ OWN TEXT: ⛔ every operation §71 enumerates is a ⛔ MUTATION, and its ⛔ stated harm is ⛔ DESTROYING UNCOMMITTED WORK.** ⇒ ⛔ **a read ⛔ cannot produce that harm, and ⛔ nothing in the law was ever aimed at one.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT A RELAXATION: ⛔ AN ABSOLUTE READING ⛔ HID A REAL DEFECT.** ⛔ **`TASK-1000`'s `git show` is ⛔ WHAT CAUGHT the `HEAD`-vs-tree coupling — ⛔ a claim ⛔ TRUE IN THE TREE and ⛔ FALSE AT `HEAD`, ⛔ i.e. ⛔ SHIPPED.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A FENCE THAT FORCES A CHOICE BETWEEN ⛔ OBEDIENCE AND ⛔ MEASUREMENT WILL BE OBEYED — ⛔ AND THE MEASUREMENT IS WHAT YOU LOSE.***
- ⭐ **`HEAD`-vs-tree is ⛔ NOT A CURIOSITY: ⛔ this project routinely runs ⛔ FIVE ROWS' uncommitted work at once (`QUIET-MODULE`)** ⇒ ⛔ **whether a claim is true ⛔ IN THE TREE or ⛔ AT `HEAD` is a ⛔ ROUTINE, ⛔ LOAD-BEARING QUESTION, and ⛔ `git show` is the ⛔ only instrument that answers it.**
- ✅ **RE-AFFIRMED AND EXTENDED 2026-09-27 by the manager, `TASK-1532`, on `qa/TASK-1528.md` WARN / §4 item 3 (marker `SC-71A-READ-ONLY-GIT-START-STATE`).** The case: `TASK-1527`'s row (6) listed a bare "git" under FORBIDDEN. The programmer ran `git status --short`, `git log --oneline -3` and `git diff --numstat` against `Tools/Verify/recipes/` before editing, mutated nothing, and disclosed it (`handoffs/TASK-1527-programmer.md` *Not examined*). QA charged nothing to the files and asked whether read-only git to confirm a start state is carved out, or whether the rule is "no git of any kind".
  - **Ruling: carved out. This clause already said so, and the defect was the row's word, not the programmer's conduct.** A bare "git" in a row's FORBIDDEN list means the fenced verbs above (the board's FENCE LANGUAGE section: `no MUTATING Git` is the default). A row that means "no git of any kind, including read-only" writes those words and its reason, or it does not mean it. Confirming a start state with `status` / `log` / `diff` / `show` / `rev-parse` is allowed. Declare it in the handoff, near the top and not only under *Not examined* (the FENCE LANGUAGE standard). Redundancy is not a breach: that a hash re-measure had already proved the start state (QA's point) makes the git call unnecessary, not forbidden. The permission is only usable by a lane that holds `Bash` (`SC-§71b`: `gameplay-programmer` and `build-master` today). It grants no instrument to anyone.
  - **Does `git status`'s stat-cache refresh matter? Not to the commit lane.** By git's own documentation (read by the manager, not measured on this machine), `git status` refreshes the index's cached stat data and may write `.git/index` back opportunistically. That write never changes an entry's blob id or what is staged. Hosts commit by explicit pathspec and verify on `git show --stat HEAD`, never on the index (§25c rules 1–2), and the index already has a non-agent writer, the editor's Git provider (§25c *THE HAND*). So the refresh cannot put a path into a commit or take one out. It has two narrow effects:
    - (a) `.git/index`'s bytes and mtime are never evidence of who staged what. No instrument on this project reads them today, and `qa/TASK-1528.md` explicitly did not.
    - (b) The write briefly holds `.git/index.lock`, so a read-only call can make a concurrent host's `git add` / `git commit` fail on the lock. That failure is loud, not silent.
    - ⇒ **A lane that is not a commit host runs read-only git only while no commit host is live, and uses `git --no-optional-locks status` for a start-state check** (git's documented switch that skips the refresh write). Forgetting the switch is not a breach, because staged content is untouched; the handoff says which form ran.
  - **Why this is the manager's ruling and not Jonathan's:** this is process law in this file. The only text of his that touches it is `CLAUDE.md`'s role table ("Never does … Git"), which this clause has read as the mutating fence since 2026-09-04. Nothing here edits `CLAUDE.md` or reads it any differently. The `gameplay-programmer` agent body's "touch Git" is read the same way. An agent-body pointer is NOT boarded for it: an agent-file row carries a frontmatter proof and a gate, which is too much for one pointer. It waits for the next row that edits that body. If he wants the absolute reading, he says so, and this clause is struck.

##### 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§71b — WHO ⛔ ACTUALLY HOLDS THE INSTRUMENT. ⛔ A PERMISSION IS ⛔ NOT A CAPABILITY, AND `SC-§71a` GRANTS QA ⛔ NOTHING.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **declared by the reviewer ⛔ ABOUT ITSELF**)

> 📌⛔ **POINTER (added 2026-09-24, ⭐ `TASK-1444` (j)): this clause gained ⛔ `SC-§71b` **cl. 4** — ⛔ *"a reviewer whose grant ⛔ DENIES it an instrument ⛔ SUBSTITUTES A NAMED PROOF and ⛔ STATES what the substitute does ⛔ NOT entitle it to say"* — ⛔ written at the ⛔ END of this file under the ⛔ `TASK-1444` amendment block (marker `SC-71B-4-A-NAMED-PROOF-SUBSTITUTE`), ⛔ with the `qa/TASK-1401.md` §B landmark reconciliation as its worked example.**

- ⛔⛔ **MEASURED: ⛔ qa-reviewer holds ⛔ `Edit` (⛔ granted 2026-09-04) but ⛔ NOT `Bash`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`SC-§71a` ruling read-only Git ⛔ ALLOWED gives QA ⛔ NO INSTRUMENT. ⛔ IT STILL HOLDS ⛔ ZERO GIT.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THEREFORE ⛔ EVERY `HEAD`-VS-TREE CLAIM IN ⛔ EVERY QA REPORT THIS SESSION IS ⛔ ACCEPTED AS ⛔ DECLARED, ⛔ NEVER MEASURED.** ⛔ **The reviewer ⛔ declared this ⛔ about its own reports and ⛔ warned explicitly: ⛔ do ⛔ NOT let `SC-§71a` be read downstream as ⛔ *"QA measured `HEAD`"* — ⛔ QA ⛔ CANNOT.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A PERMISSION LAW WRITTEN FOR ⛔ ONE ROLE SILENTLY BECOMES A ⛔ CAPABILITY CLAIM ABOUT ⛔ EVERY ROLE. ⛔ `SC-§71a` SAYS *"READ-ONLY GIT IS ALLOWED"*; ⛔ A READER TWO ROWS LATER HEARS *"SO THE GATE CHECKED IT"* — ⛔ AND THAT IS THE ⛔ STALE-VERDICT SHAPE (`SC-§73`) ⛔ MANUFACTURED OUT OF A ⛔ PERMISSION RATHER THAN A ⛔ MEASUREMENT.***
- ✅ **WHO ⛔ DOES HOLD IT: ⛔ `build-master` (⛔ `Bash` + Git) and ⛔ `gameplay-programmer` (⛔ `Bash`).** ⛔ **A `HEAD`-vs-tree question is ⛔ THEIRS to answer; ⛔ QA's job is to ⛔ NAME the question and ⛔ say it is ⛔ undischarged.**
- ⛔ **CONCRETE CONSEQUENCE, ⛔ NOT ABSTRACT: ⛔ `TASK-987`'s ⛔ ATOMIC-3-FILE obligation rests on ⛔ `HEAD`-vs-tree reasoning** ⇒ ⛔ **the commit host ⛔ MUST measure it ⛔ ITSELF and may ⛔ NOT inherit it from a gate.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ BEFORE CITING A REPORT'S FACTUAL CLAIM, ⛔ ASK WHETHER ITS AUTHOR HELD THE ⛔ TOOL THAT COULD HAVE PRODUCED IT. ⛔ A ROLE'S ⛔ TOOL LIST IS PART OF ITS ⛔ VERDICT'S PROVENANCE.***

### SC-§69 ⛔⛔⛔ **WHEN A VALUE LIVES IN ⛔ TWO ARTEFACTS AND THE ⛔ GAME READS ⛔ ONLY ONE, ⛔ EDITING THE OTHER IS A ⛔ NO-OP THAT ⛔ PASSES EVERY GATE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **caught on `TASK-993` by ⛔ post-write measurement, ⛔ NOT by any gate**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE LIVE CASE: ⛔ `Docs/Data/cards.csv` is the ⛔ SOURCE OF TRUTH FOR HUMANS; ⛔ `/Game/Data/DT_Cards` is what the ⛔ RUNNING GAME READS.** ⛔ **`TASK-993` wrote `NoticeRange = 3600` to the ⛔ CSV. ⛔ The compile would then give ⛔ every `DT_Cards` row `0.0` — ⛔ the ⛔ SPARSE SENTINEL ⇒ ⛔ the Longbowman notices at ⛔ `2000`.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **THE END STATE IS THE ⛔ POINT: ⛔ Jonathan's ⛔ EXPLICIT RULING ⛔ ABSENT FROM THE SHIPPED GAME, WITH ⛔ THE CSV READING CORRECT, ⛔ THE SUITE GREEN, ⛔ THE COMPILE CLEAN AND ⛔ EVERY GATE PASSING.**
- ⛔ **WHY ⛔ NO TEST CATCHES IT: the tests read the ⛔ CSV OFF DISK or call the ⛔ RESOLVER directly. ⛔ NEITHER TOUCHES THE ⛔ RUNTIME ARTEFACT.** ⇒ ⛔ **the test suite and the shipped game ⛔ disagree about a value and ⛔ both are internally consistent.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A DATA CHANGE THAT PASSES EVERY GATE AND ⛔ NEVER REACHES THE RUNTIME ARTEFACT IS ⛔ WORSE THAN A RED TEST. ⛔ A RED TEST ⛔ STOPS THE WAVE; ⛔ THIS ⛔ SHIPS.***
- ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ ANY row editing a ⛔ DUAL-HOMED value must name ⛔ BOTH artefacts in its `names:` and ⛔ state ⛔ WHICH ONE THE RUNTIME READS.** ⛔ **If the runtime artefact ⛔ cannot be written in that row's window (⛔ e.g. the property does ⛔ not exist until a compile), ⛔ BOARD THE SECOND WRITE AS ⛔ ITS OWN ROW, ⛔ blocked on the compile, ⛔ with the ⛔ READ-BACK in the boarded text.**
- ⛔ **AND CHECK THE ⛔ DIRECTION: ⛔ `SiegeAssistantSelectionTest` ⛔ ALREADY DECLARED this CSV↔DataTable gap ⛔ in its own header — ⛔ running the ⛔ OTHER WAY.** ⇒ ⛔ **a known gap documented in ⛔ one direction is ⛔ NOT protection in the ⛔ other, and the ⛔ losing direction is whichever one the ⛔ GAME reads.**

### SC-§68 ⛔⛔⛔ **`.uasset` VERIFICATION IS BY ⛔ HASH, ⛔ NEVER BY SIZE — AND A SAVE IS AN ⛔ EXPLICIT ONE-ASSET LIST, ⛔ NEVER THE EMPTY LIST.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **both measured on `TASK-993`**)

- ⛔⛔ **SIZE IS ⛔ BLIND TO AN IN-PLACE EDIT. ⛔ MEASURED: two float writes into `DT_Cards` left it ⛔ BYTE-IDENTICAL AT ⛔ `42,722` — ⛔ two in-place floats ⛔ grow no name table.** ⇒ ⛔ **a size check reports *"nothing happened"* on a ⛔ SUCCESSFUL WRITE, and *"nothing happened"* is ⛔ exactly what a failed write looks like too.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AMENDED 2026-09-04 BY ⭐ `TASK-1005` — ⛔ AND THE AMENDMENT IS THE ⛔ POINT OF THE SECTION: ⛔ SIZE LIES IN ⛔ BOTH DIRECTIONS, ⛔ NOT JUST ONE.**
  - ⛔ **DIRECTION 1 (`TASK-993`): the file ⛔ DID change and the size ⛔ DID NOT MOVE** ⇒ ⛔ *"unchanged"* ⛔ read as ⛔ FALSE-NEGATIVE.
  - ⛔ **DIRECTION 2 (`TASK-1005`): ⛔ TWO in-place float edits and the asset ⛔ GREW `+948` BYTES** — ⛔ `32 × 29 + 20`, ⛔ consistent with `NoticeRange` being ⛔ PERSISTED FOR THE FIRST TIME ⇒ ⛔ **a size delta ⛔ ENTIRELY UNRELATED TO THE CELLS YOU WROTE**, which a size check reads as ⛔ *"a big change happened"* ⇒ ⛔ FALSE-POSITIVE.
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A SIZE READING SUPPORTS ⛔ EITHER CONCLUSION AT ⛔ ANY TIME. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A WEAK INSTRUMENT — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT AN INSTRUMENT.** ⛔ `sha256` + a ⛔ `get_rows` read-back is the ⛔ ONLY pair that discriminates.
  - ⭐ **AND ⛔ RECORD HOW THE `+948` WAS REPORTED, ⛔ because it is the ⛔ right shape: ⭐ `TASK-1005` flagged the arithmetic as ⛔ INFERENCE, ⛔ NOT MEASUREMENT** (⛔ proving it needs a byte diff, ⛔ which needs Git, ⛔ which that row forbade) ⇒ ⛔ **it declared the ⛔ limit of its own evidence instead of ⛔ dressing a plausible arithmetic as a finding** (`SC-§40`).
- ✅ **VERIFY BY: ⛔ `is_dirty` true→false ⛔ AND ⛔ `sha256` before/after ⛔ AND ⛔ a `get_rows` READ-BACK.** ⛔ **`mtime` is ⛔ corroboration, ⛔ not proof.**
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THIRD LIE, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 FROM `qa/TASK-850.md`: ⛔ A ⛔ TEXT-TOOL ⛔ COUNT ON A `.uasset` IS ⛔ MEANINGLESS. ⛔ `rg --count` COUNTS ⛔ MATCHING LINES, ⛔ NOT OCCURRENCES — AND A ⛔ BINARY PACKAGE HAS ⛔ ALMOST NO LINE BREAKS.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: `/Game/UI/CardArt` on `DT_Cards.uasset` returned ⛔ `2`, ⛔ not `33`.** ⛔ **A reviewer expecting one hit per row would have read that as ⛔ *"31 rows lost their card art"* — ⛔ a catastrophic-looking false alarm from a ⛔ correct file.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **PRESENCE IS A ⛔ VALID PROBE ON A PACKAGE. A ⛔ COUNT IS ⛔ NOT.** ⛔ **Use `rg -c` on a `.uasset` for ⛔ *"is this string in here at all?"* and ⛔ NEVER for ⛔ *"how many?"*.** ⛔ **For a count, ⛔ go through the engine (`get_rows`), ⛔ never through the bytes.**
