  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THAT IS THE ⛔ THIRD DISTINCT WAY THIS ⛔ ONE ASSET HAS LIED IN ⛔ ONE DAY — ⛔ SIZE BY ⛔ NOT MOVING (`TASK-993`), ⛔ SIZE BY ⛔ MOVING FOR AN UNRELATED REASON (`TASK-1005`), AND ⛔ NOW ⛔ COUNTS. ⛔ THE PATTERN IS ⛔ NOT *"THIS ASSET IS CURSED"* — IT IS ⛔ *"EVERY TEXT-SHAPED INSTRUMENT POINTED AT A BINARY PACKAGE RETURNS A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE NUMBER THAT MEANS ⛔ SOMETHING ELSE."****
  - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ on a `.uasset`, the ⛔ ONLY trustworthy instruments are ⛔ `sha256` (⛔ did it change?), ⛔ `get_rows` (⛔ what does it say?) and ⛔ `is_dirty` transitions (⛔ was it saved?). ⛔ EVERYTHING ELSE — ⛔ size, ⛔ counts, ⛔ `mtime`, ⛔ line-based greps — is ⛔ CORROBORATION AT BEST AND ⛔ A TRAP AT WORST.**
- ✅⭐⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ CONSTRUCTIVE COMPANION, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-05 FROM ⭐ `TASK-1036` — ⛔ WHAT TO USE ⛔ INSTEAD, ⛔ NOT MERELY WHAT TO DISTRUST: ⛔ ON A `.uasset`, THE ⛔ NAME-TABLE DELTA IS THE ⛔ RIGHT INSTRUMENT AND A ⛔ WHOLE-FILE BYTE DIFF IS ⛔ EXPECTED NOISE.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: a ⛔ ONE-FLAG change produced a ⛔ raw byte diff spanning ⛔ NEARLY THE WHOLE FILE** — ⛔ **because inserting ⛔ 54 bytes ⛔ SHIFTS EVERY HEADER OFFSET and the ⛔ package GUID ⛔ REGENERATES.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **READING THAT SPAN AS ⛔ *"THE FILE CHANGED EVERYWHERE"* IS A ⛔ FALSE POSITIVE ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION — ⛔ the ⛔ NINTH way this project has been misled about a binary, and the ⛔ FIRST where the misleading instrument is a ⛔ genuine diff.**
  - ✅ **THE ⛔ NAME TABLE ⛔ ANSWERS THE ACTUAL QUESTION: ⛔ it gained ⛔ EXACTLY ONE ENTRY — `bEnableVolumetricFog`.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ *"what did this package ⛔ LEARN?"* is ⛔ answerable; ⛔ *"which bytes moved?"* is ⛔ not.**
  - ⭐ **AND IT ⛔ ADJUDICATED ITS OWN FALSE HIT: a ⛔ spurious printable run (`jVU^H`, ⛔ offset 24, ⛔ GUID region) was ruled ⛔ BINARY COINCIDENCE, ⛔ not a name** — ⛔ **a probe that ⛔ knows its own noise floor.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ *"SIZE LIES IN BOTH DIRECTIONS"* SAYS WHAT ⛔ NOT TO TRUST. ⛔ THIS SAYS WHAT ⛔ TO USE — AND A LAW THAT ⛔ ONLY FORBIDS LEAVES THE READER WITH ⛔ NO INSTRUMENT AND ⛔ A DEADLINE.***
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔ **NINTH LIE, ⛔ SAME ROW: ⛔ A ⛔ COMPRESSED-IMAGE BYTE COMPARISON IS ⛔ NOT A PIXEL COMPARISON.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: ⛔ TWO CAPTURES OF ⛔ IDENTICAL CONTENT DIFFERED AT A ⛔ 0.98 BYTE RATIO — ⛔ PURELY FROM ⛔ PNG COMPRESSION.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **WITHOUT THE ⛔ CONTROL IT RAN ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ `0.98` WOULD HAVE READ AS ⛔ *"THE LOOK CHANGED"* — ⛔ and the row would have reported a ⛔ visual regression that ⛔ did not exist.**
  - ✅ **THE CONTROL IS WHAT ⛔ SEPARATED A ⛔ REAL 36× SIGNAL FROM ⛔ MEANINGLESS NOISE** (⛔ luma `132.3964 → 132.0777`, ⛔ −0.24%, against a ⛔ noise floor of `0.0089`).
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A ⛔ CONTROL IS ⛔ NOT A FORMALITY ON AN INSTRUMENT YOU ⛔ HAVE NOT CALIBRATED — ⛔ IT IS THE ⛔ ONLY THING THAT TELLS YOU WHETHER YOUR ⛔ NUMBER HAS A ⛔ UNIT*** (⭐ `SC-§39`, ⭐ `SC-§81`).
- ⭐ **THIS IS ⛔ `§25b` (⛔ LFS: oid-vs-sha256, ⛔ never size) ⛔ FIRING ON A ⛔ SECOND ASSET CLASS.** ⇒ ⛔ **it is ⛔ NOT an LFS rule — it is a ⛔ BINARY-ARTEFACT rule, and it binds ⛔ EVERY `.uasset` verification.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SAVE: ⛔ PASS AN ⛔ EXPLICIT ONE-ASSET LIST.** ⛔ **The ⛔ EMPTY LIST ⛔ FLUSHES ⛔ EVERY DIRTY ASSET IN THE EDITOR** ⇒ ⛔ **with other lanes live in that editor it ⛔ commits ⛔ somebody else's half-finished work, ⛔ silently, ⛔ under your row's name.** ⛔ **`TASK-993` did this correctly and it is ⛔ STANDING PRACTICE from here.**

  - ⛔ **MEASURED: `set_rows` wrote ⛔ `FogClear` and ⛔ read back ⛔ `None`.** ⛔ **⛔ CAUGHT ⛔ ONLY BY THE ⛔ MANDATED `get_rows` READ-BACK.**

  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE ⛔ EDITOR'S REFLECTION DATA IS A ⛔ SILENT THIRD PARTY TO EVERY MCP WRITE. ⛔ AN ASSET WRITE IS VALIDATED AGAINST THE ⛔ LOADED DLL, ⛔ NOT AGAINST YOUR SOURCE — AND ⛔ WHEN THEY DISAGREE THE WRITE ⛔ SUCCEEDS AND THE ⛔ VALUE VANISHES.***
  - ✅ **THE PROBE: ⛔ compare the ⛔ DLL's mtime to the ⛔ source's ⛔ BEFORE writing any ⛔ newly-added field or enum value.** ⛔ **If the DLL is ⛔ older, ⛔ STOP — ⛔ you need a ⛔ compile + ⛔ editor restart** (⭐ `TASK-1020` is the boarded shape for that).

  - ⛔ **MEASURED, ⛔ four ways: ⛔ three `list_toolsets` ⛔ TIMEOUTS · ⛔ an ⛔ EMPTY `curl` BODY · ⛔ the process reporting ⛔ `Responding: True` · ⛔ the log ⛔ FROZEN at *"Engine is initialized"*.**
  - ⛔⛔ **TWO DISTINCT HARMS, ⛔ AND THE SECOND IS THE ⛔ EXPENSIVE ONE:**

  - ⭐ **THE PATTERN ACROSS ALL FIVE, ⛔ NOW UNMISTAKABLE: ⛔ EVERY CHEAP, ⛔ FAST, ⛔ OBVIOUS INSTRUMENT IN THIS STACK RETURNS A ⛔ PLAUSIBLE VALUE THAT MEANS ⛔ SOMETHING ELSE.** ⇒ ⛔ **exit code ⇒ parse the log · size ⇒ `sha256` · count ⇒ `get_rows` · write-return ⇒ read-back · ⛔ port ⇒ ⛔ window title.**

  - ⛔ **ROOT CAUSE, measured against ⛔ FOUR KNOWN-GOOD ROWS: the column ⛔ MARSHALS AS A ⛔ PLAIN STRING, ⛔ but ⛔ `get_schema` ⛔ DOCUMENTS IT AS AN ⛔ OBJECT WITH A `refPath` PROPERTY.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **PASSING THE ⛔ SCHEMA'S OWN DOCUMENTED SHAPE ⛔ SILENTLY YIELDS `None`.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE OTHER FIVE LIE ABOUT A ⛔ RESULT (⛔ exit code · size · count · write-return) OR ABOUT ⛔ LIVENESS (⛔ the port). ⛔ THIS ONE LIES ABOUT THE ⛔ INTERFACE — ⛔ IN THE INTERFACE'S ⛔ OWN DOCUMENTATION.*** ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ CORRECT, ⛔ DILIGENT, ⛔ SCHEMA-FOLLOWING CALL IS THE ⛔ ONE THAT FAILS — AND AN AGENT THAT ⛔ *"CHECKED THE SCHEMA FIRST"* IS ⛔ MORE LIKELY TO HIT IT THAN ONE WHO ⛔ COPIED A WORKING ROW.**
  - ✅⛔ **THE REMEDY IS ⛔ INVERTED FROM THE OTHERS: ⛔ COPY A ⛔ KNOWN-GOOD ROW'S ⛔ MARSHALLED SHAPE. ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT TRUST `get_schema`'s ⛔ TYPE DESCRIPTION FOR ⛔ `UObject`-TYPED CELLS.** ⛔ **Everywhere else in this section the fix is *"use the better instrument"*; ⛔ here it is *"⛔ distrust the documentation and ⛔ imitate the data."*
  - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ CONSEQUENCE, ⛔ WHICH IS WHAT MAKES IT URGENT RATHER THAN CURIOUS: ⛔ `SiegeCardArtRosterTest` walks ⛔ EVERY row with ⛔ NO Spell exemption and requires the cell to resolve as a ⛔ `UTexture2D`.** ⇒ ⛔ **had it shipped, that suite would have gone ⛔ RED on a task ⛔ ALREADY MARKED DONE — ⛔ surfacing at the ⛔ COMMIT HOST'S FIRST EXECUTED SUITE and being ⛔ ATTRIBUTED TO WHATEVER ELSE WAS IN FLIGHT.** ⛔⛔ **CORRECTED 2026-09-04: a `None` fires ⛔ THREE reds (⛔ `:521` · `:601` · `:534`), ⛔ NOT one** ⇒ ⛔ **the misattribution would have looked like a ⛔ BROAD art-pipeline failure, ⛔ not a single stale cell — ⛔ i.e. ⛔ even MORE likely to be blamed on another lane.**
  - ⭐⭐ **SECOND TIME IN ⛔ ONE DAY the ⛔ read-back mandate paid for itself on ⛔ THIS ONE ROW** (⛔ the enum drop was the first). ⇒ ⛔ **`set_rows` has now silently dropped ⛔ TWO DIFFERENT CELLS for ⛔ TWO DIFFERENT REASONS on ⛔ the same task.**
- ✅⭐⭐ **AND A ⛔ POSITIVE EXHIBIT FOR THE PORT CLAUSE, ⛔ 2026-09-04 — ⛔ THE DIAGNOSTIC WORKED ON ITS ⛔ FIRST USE, ⛔ BY A DIFFERENT AGENT, ⛔ WITHIN AN HOUR OF BEING WRITTEN.**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-983` checked for the `Restore Packages` modal ⛔ BY WINDOW ENUMERATION, ⛔ NOT by the port: ⛔ main window ⛔ `enabled=True`, ⛔ which a blocking modal would have ⛔ DISABLED.** ⛔ **No modal was present.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ IT PRODUCED A ⛔ CLEAN NEGATIVE RATHER THAN A ⛔ FALSE ALARM — ⛔ AND THAT IS THE ⛔ STRONGEST EVIDENCE A DIAGNOSTIC ACTUALLY WORKS. ⛔ A CHECK THAT HAS ⛔ ONLY EVER FIRED IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM ONE THAT ⛔ ALWAYS FIRES.***
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SEVENTH LIE, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-04 FROM ⭐ `TASK-987` — ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONLY ONE THAT ⛔ ACTIVELY ACCUSES. ⛔ THE OTHER SIX ⛔ MISLEAD; ⛔ THIS ONE ⛔ REPORTS A DISASTER THAT DID ⛔ NOT HAPPEN, ⛔ AT THE ⛔ WORST POSSIBLE MOMENT.**
  - ⛔ **THE PROBE: `git show HEAD:<path> | sha256sum` ⛔ IMMEDIATELY AFTER THE COMMIT. ⛔ IT REPORTED ⛔ ALL THREE ATOMIC FILES ⛔ DIVERGED.**
  - ⛔ **BOTH HALVES WERE ⛔ INSTRUMENT ERROR: ⛔ `git show` returns the ⛔ LFS ⛔ POINTER TEXT for `DT_Cards.uasset` (⛔ not the blob), and ⛔ LF-NORMALISED bytes for ⛔ CRLF text files.** ⇒ ⛔ **the comparison was ⛔ never against the thing it appeared to be against.**
  - ✅ **CORRECT INSTRUMENTS: ⛔ `git diff HEAD` (⛔ for divergence) and ⛔ THE POINTER'S OWN `oid` (⛔ for LFS content).**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ ITS ⛔ TIMING IS WHAT MAKES IT DANGEROUS: ⛔ IT FIRES ⛔ IMMEDIATELY AFTER A COMMIT, WHEN THE ⛔ OBVIOUS READING IS THAT ⛔ THE COMMIT WENT WRONG — AND THE ⛔ OBVIOUS RESPONSE IS TO ⛔ "FIX" A ⛔ CORRECT COMMIT. ⛔ A FALSE ALARM IS ⛔ CHEAP IN A QUIET MOMENT AND ⛔ EXPENSIVE IN A LOUD ONE.***
  - ⛔ **AND IT ⛔ COMPOUNDS WITH ⭐ `SC-§71`: ⛔ the ⛔ instinctive repair for *"the commit is wrong"* is a ⛔ DESTRUCTIVE git operation ⛔ on a tree that ⛔ still carried other lanes' work.**
- ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND A ⛔ COROLLARY THE SAME ROW BOUGHT — ⛔ A DIAGNOSIS CAN BE ⛔ RIGHT ABOUT THE ⛔ CAUSE AND ⛔ WRONG ABOUT THE ⛔ STATE.**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-983` diagnosed the dropped enum ⛔ CORRECTLY (⛔ a stale DLL's reflection data). ⛔ BUT the row it thought it had written ⛔ NEVER REACHED DISK — ⛔ it lived ⛔ ONLY in the ⛔ killed editor's ⛔ UNSAVED MEMORY.** ⇒ ⛔ **`DT_Cards` on disk has ⛔ 33 rows, ⛔ not 34, and `BrightSun` has ⛔ NO ROW AT ALL.**
  - ✅ **THE AUTHOR'S CONDUCT WAS ⛔ CORRECT THROUGHOUT AND ITS ⛔ EVIDENCE PROVED THE ⛔ RIGHT THING: ⛔ revert-and-never-save was right, and the ⛔ BYTE-IDENTICAL HASH ⛔ correctly proved the disk was ⛔ untouched** — ⛔ **it simply did ⛔ not mean *"the row is there with a wrong enum"*; it meant *"there is ⛔ no row"*.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A CORRECT ROOT CAUSE CAN CARRY A ⛔ WRONG STATE DESCRIPTION, AND ⛔ NOTHING IN THE CAUSE'S OWN EVIDENCE ⛔ EXPOSES IT. ⛔ ONLY THE ⛔ READ-BACK DISTINGUISHES THEM.***
  - ⛔ **PRACTICAL: ⛔ a follow-up row must ⛔ `add_rows` ⛔ BEFORE `set_rows`.** ⛔⛔ **AND A BARE `set_rows` WOULD FAIL ON THE ⛔ MISSING ROW, ⛔ NOT THE ENUM ⇒ ⛔ MISREADING THAT FAILURE WOULD LOOK LIKE ⛔ *"THE COMPILE DIDN'T WORK"* — ⛔ and send someone back to re-run a compile that ⛔ succeeded.**
- ⚠️⛔ **RELATED, ⛔ AND IT IS WHY THE READ-BACK IS ⛔ MANDATORY: ⛔ `set_rows` RETURNS ⛔ `null` ON SUCCESS.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A ⛔ SILENT ACCEPT-AND-DROP IS ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM WORKING.** ⛔ **`TASK-993` got a ⛔ LOUD refusal (`Properties not found in schema`) ⛔ ONLY BY LUCK OF THE SCHEMA GAP — ⛔ do ⛔ not rely on that.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ A WRITE TO A BINARY ASSET IS ⛔ UNVERIFIED UNTIL YOU HAVE ⛔ READ THE VALUE BACK. ⛔ THE RETURN CODE, THE SIZE AND THE TIMESTAMP ⛔ ALL AGREE WITH FAILURE.***

### SC-§67 ⛔⛔⛔ **A DISPATCH'S ⛔ BOILERPLATE FENCES MUST BE ⛔ CHECKED AGAINST THE ROW'S ⛔ OWN ITEMS ⛔ BEFORE SENDING. ⭐ A ⛔ GENERIC PROHIBITION ⛔ SILENTLY OVERRIDES A ⛔ SPECIFIC REQUIREMENT.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought TWICE in ⛔ ONE NIGHT**)

- ⛔⛔ **BOTH INSTANCES, ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE THE ⛔ PATTERN IS ⛔ IDENTICAL AND THE ⛔ COSTS DIFFERED BY 100×:**
  - ⛔ **`TASK-839`:** a dispatch read *"safe half"* as ⛔ *"do not create `AFogVolume`"* ⇒ ⛔⛔ **FOUR OF FIVE ITEMS DIED**, because the row's own items ⛔ REQUIRED that class (⛔ `FOG-§10.1` pins the scalar to it; ⛔ `EditDefaultsOnly` needs a `UCLASS`).
  - ⛔ **`TASK-993`:** a dispatch carried a blanket ⛔ *"no editor, no engine"* that ⛔ CONTRADICTED the row's ⛔ OWN item (4) — the `DT_Cards` `set_rows` ⛔ WRITE. ⇒ ⛔ **the asset shipped ⛔ without the column and ⛔ `TASK-995` item (4) ⛔ could not have passed.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ ASYMMETRY THAT MAKES THIS ⛔ WORSE THAN AN ORDINARY MISTAKE: ⛔ THE ⛔ AGENT CANNOT TELL ⛔ WHICH OF THE TWO INSTRUCTIONS IS ⛔ WRONG.** ⛔ **A dispatch fence and a board item ⛔ both carry authority ⇒ ⛔ the agent must either ⛔ DECLINE (⛔ losing work) or ⛔ ROUTE AROUND (⛔ inventing architecture). ⛔ BOTH outcomes are ⛔ expensive and ⛔ NEITHER is the agent's fault.**
- ✅⭐ **AND BOTH AGENTS ⛔ CHOSE CORRECTLY — ⛔ RECORD IT: ⛔ `839` ⛔ DECLINED rather than inventing a state home on `ASiegeGameState` (⛔ which would have broken ⛔ three laws at once); ⛔ `993` ⛔ MEASURED rather than assuming (⛔ probing MCP and getting ⛔ 405 to a GET ⇒ ⛔ *"the server is UP, this was not an outage"*).** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ WHEN TWO AUTHORITIES CONFLICT, ⛔ DECLARE AND ⛔ STOP. ⛔ NEVER ⛔ SILENTLY PICK ONE, and ⛔ NEVER ⛔ INVENT A THIRD PATH.***
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ THIRD INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-04 — ⛔ WHICH SHOWS THE COST IS ⛔ NOT CONFUSION. ⛔ A SELF-CONTRADICTORY SPEC ⛔ DISGUISES A ⛔ MISSING GATE.**
  - ⛔ **⭐ `TASK-964`'s spec ⛔ CONTRADICTED ITSELF: ⛔ item (3) demanded an ⛔ OBSERVED COMPILED RED, while items ⛔ (5)/(6) fenced ⛔ COMPILING ⛔ OUT OF SCOPE.**
  - ✅ **THE AUTHOR TOOK THE READING THAT ⛔ PRESERVED THE FENCE — ⛔ CORRECTLY — ⛔ declared the shortfall, offered a ⛔ structural substitute, and flipped to ⛔ `ready-for-qa`.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **AND THAT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHY THE ROW ⛔ LOOKED FINISHED WHEN A ⛔ GATE WAS STILL OWED. ⛔ ITS GATE ⛔ NEVER RAN, AND THE ROW ⛔ SHIPPED IN `1a457df` ⛔ UNREVIEWED.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE FIRST TWO INSTANCES COST ⛔ ITEMS (⛔ four died on `TASK-839`; ⛔ a data write on `TASK-993`). ⛔ THIS ONE COST A ⛔ HARD GATE — ⛔ BECAUSE A ROW THAT ⛔ CANNOT SATISFY ITSELF PRODUCES A ⛔ DECLARED SHORTFALL, AND A ⛔ DECLARED SHORTFALL READS AS ⛔ *"HANDLED"* TO EVERYONE EXCEPT THE GATE THAT WAS SUPPOSED TO ADJUDICATE IT.***
  - ✅ **THEREFORE: ⛔ a row carrying a ⛔ DECLARED SHORTFALL is ⛔ NOT closeable ⛔ until its gate has ⛔ ADJUDICATED THAT SHORTFALL ⛔ BY NAME.** ⛔ **The shortfall is ⛔ the gate's subject, ⛔ not a footnote to it.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ FOURTH INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-05 — ⛔ AND IT IS A ⛔ DIFFERENT MECHANISM FROM THE FIRST THREE, ⛔ SO IT GETS ITS OWN CHECK: ⛔ A DISPATCH THAT ⛔ CITES A LAW AND THEN ⛔ CONTRADICTS THAT LAW'S OWN ⛔ *WHO* CLAUSE.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: a dispatch instructed ⛔ build-master to land the ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` re-pin ⛔ itself — ⛔ while ⛔ CITING ⭐ `SC-§82` as governing.** ⛔⛔ **`SC-§82`'s ⛔ WHO clause says the ⛔ MEASURING ROLE ⛔ REPORTS and the ⛔ MANAGER ⛔ LANDS IT; ⛔ `TASK-1036` item (2) says ⛔ *"do NOT edit that file yourself."***
  - ✅ **THE AGENT ⛔ REFUSED, ⛔ ESCALATED, AND ⛔ CITED THREE WRITTEN SOURCES ⛔ RATHER THAN SILENTLY PICKING A SIDE.** ⭐ **⛔ That is `SC-§67` ⛔ CAUGHT BY THE EXECUTOR — ⛔ the shape this section exists to make visible.**
  - ⛔ **AND IT FLAGGED A ⛔ SECOND, ⛔ INDEPENDENT REASON: ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` was ⛔ ALREADY DIRTY from another lane ⇒ ⛔ a concurrent write risked a ⛔ board race.** ⇒ ⭐ **⛔ two reasons, ⛔ measured, ⛔ before acting.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE FIRST THREE INSTANCES WERE A ⛔ GENERIC FENCE OVERRIDING A ⛔ SPECIFIC ITEM. ⛔ THIS ONE IS A ⛔ RESTATEMENT OF A LAW ⛔ CONTRADICTING THE LAW IT RESTATES — AND IT IS ⛔ HARDER TO SEE, BECAUSE THE DISPATCH ⛔ LOOKS MORE AUTHORITATIVE FOR ⛔ HAVING CITED THE SOURCE.***
  - ✅ **CHECK, ⛔ FOR THE SENDER: ⛔ WHEN A DISPATCH ⛔ CITES A LAW ⛔ AND ⛔ GIVES AN INSTRUCTION, ⛔ RE-READ THAT LAW'S ⛔ *WHO* AND ⛔ *WHAT* CLAUSES AND ⛔ CHECK THE INSTRUCTION AGAINST THEM.** ⛔ **⛔ A citation is ⛔ not a summary — ⛔ and ⛔ paraphrasing a law into an instruction is ⛔ exactly where the two diverge.**
  - ⚠️ **⛔ TWO IN ⛔ ONE NIGHT FROM THE ⛔ SAME AUTHOR** (⛔ the first: ⛔ *"add no number"* against a spec ⛔ requiring `5000`/`87.8%`) ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ this is a ⛔ PATTERN, ⛔ not a slip, and ⛔ the fix is ⛔ mechanical: ⛔ cite ⛔ or instruct, and if ⛔ both, ⛔ check them against each other ⛔ before sending.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ FIFTH INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-05 — ⛔ AND THE MECHANISM IS ⛔ NEW AGAIN: ⛔ THE CITED SOURCE ⛔ WENT STALE. ⛔ NOT WRONG WHEN WRITTEN — ⛔ WRONG BY THE TIME IT WAS ⛔ READ.**
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: ⭐ `TASK-1045` cl. (0) ⛔ DELIBERATELY REFUSED to restate the fix (⛔ CORRECT, ⛔ per this very section) and pointed at `qa/TASK-1042.md` ⛔ W-1 as the ⛔ SOLE authority — adding ⛔ *"if the report and this row ever appear to disagree, ⛔ THE REPORT WINS."***
  - ⛔ **THEN ⭐ `TASK-1041` ⛔ LOOP 2 ⛔ RE-BASED EVERY INDEX in that assertion onto a ⛔ COMMENT-FREE PROJECTION.** ⛔ **W-1's printed line slices `UpdateStateBody` — ⛔ the ⛔ RAW body — with ⛔ those indices** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **applying it ⛔ VERBATIM would have ⛔ RE-INTRODUCED A ⛔ MIXED-BASIS DEFECT that `qa/TASK-1047.md` §6.4 had ⛔ just confirmed ⛔ ABSENT, and ⛔ bolted a ⛔ REDUNDANT conjunct over a hole ⛔ loop 2 had ⛔ already sealed.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ A CITATION CARRIES ⛔ NO TIMESTAMP. ⛔ "THE REPORT WINS" IS A RULE ABOUT ⛔ AUTHORITY; ⛔ IT SAYS ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT ⛔ CURRENCY*** — ⛔ **and the ⛔ SAFER a pointer looks, the ⛔ LESS ANYONE RE-READS WHAT IT POINTS AT.**
  - ✅ **CHECK, ⛔ FOR THE SENDER: ⛔ when a row names an ⛔ EXTERNAL ARTEFACT as the ⛔ authoritative form, ⛔ RE-READ THAT ARTEFACT AGAINST THE ⛔ CURRENT FILE at ⛔ dispatch time.** ⛔ **If the ⛔ SUBJECT MOVED after the artefact was written, the pointer is ⛔ DATED ⇒ ⛔ SAY SO ⛔ ON THE ROW, ⛔ BY NAME.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **A ⛔ SIXTH INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-05 — ⛔ AND IT PROVES THE ⛔ POINTER-NOT-RESTATEMENT RULE HOLDS AT ⛔ FILE-HEADER SCALE, ⛔ NOT ONLY AT ⛔ DISPATCH SCALE. ⛔ THE MECHANISM OF SPREAD WAS ⛔ COPY-PASTE.** (⛔ **marker `SC-67-COPIED-PREMISE`** · ⛔ **bought in `qa/TASK-1073.md` ⛔ WARN-2 · ⛔ boarded as ⭐ `TASK-1076`**)
  - ⛔ **MEASURED (⛔ by QA, ⛔ then ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY by the manager, ⛔ which is why it is stated flatly): `Tests/SiegeLadderClimbTest.cpp` declares ⛔ *"this build runs with `ENABLE_NAN_DIAGNOSTIC == 1` (it is ⛔ 0 only in Shipping/Test)"*. ⛔ `Engine/Source/Runtime/Core/Public/Math/UnrealMathUtility.h` defines it ⛔ `1` ⛔ ONLY under ⛔ `UE_BUILD_DEBUG`, ⛔ `0` otherwise** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ `0` in this ⛔ Development editor build. ⛔ The claim is wrong about ⛔ WHICH CONFIGURATIONS, ⛔ not merely imprecise.**
  - ⛔⛔ **⛔ AND IT ⛔ CROSSED A FILE BOUNDARY ⛔ BY BEING ⛔ COPIED: `Tests/SiegeFogVisualTest.cpp`'s new header ⛔ INHERITED THE SENTENCE ⛔ VERBATIM — ⛔ and ⛔ ADDED A ⛔ PROVENANCE CLAIM THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER TRUE: ⛔ *"(`SiegeLadderClimbTest.cpp` ⛔ MEASURED it)"*. ⛔ That file ⛔ ASSERTED it. ⛔ It measured ⛔ nothing.**
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ THE COPY IS ⛔ MORE CONFIDENT THAN THE ORIGINAL, AND ITS ⛔ EXTRA CONFIDENCE IS ⛔ MANUFACTURED PURELY BY THE ⛔ ACT OF COPYING. ⛔ A RESTATEMENT ⛔ ACQUIRES PROVENANCE IT WAS ⛔ NEVER GIVEN — BECAUSE THE ⛔ SECOND AUTHOR REMEMBERS ⛔ READING IT SOMEWHERE AUTHORITATIVE, AND ⛔ WRITES THAT DOWN.***
  - ⛔ **⛔ AND A ⛔ FALSE PROVENANCE IS ⛔ WORSE THAN A FALSE VALUE: ⛔ ⛔ IT TELLS THE NEXT READER THE CHECK HAS ⛔ ALREADY BEEN DONE, ⛔ WHICH IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT STOPS THEM DOING IT.** ⛔ **⛔ The value alone would have invited a re-derivation; ⛔ the citation ⛔ closed the question.**
  - ⛔ **⛔ THE COST WAS ⛔ NOT ZERO EVEN THOUGH THE ERROR WAS ⛔ IN THE SAFE DIRECTION: ⛔ (i) it ⛔ VOIDED a ⛔ PRE-ROUTED REMEDY (`handoffs/TASK-1072-programmer.md` §8.5 pre-authorised ⛔ DELETING TWO REAL NaN ASSERTIONS to cure a ⛔ hazard that ⛔ cannot fire) — ⛔ QA ⛔ overrode it in advance; ⛔ (ii) it is the ⛔ STATED REASON a ⛔ SHIPPED `ContainsNaN()` GUARD has ⛔ NO EXECUTED ROW.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A FALSE PREMISE ⛔ EXCUSING A COVERAGE GAP IS THE ⛔ EXPENSIVE FORM: ⛔ THE COMMENT IS WRONG, THE ⛔ GAP IS REAL, AND THE NEXT READER FINDS THE GAP ⛔ ALREADY ARGUED FOR.***
  - ⚠️ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ ORIGIN FILE NOW ⛔ CONTRADICTS ITSELF: it says constructing a NaN `FVector` ⛔ raises an engine error, ⛔ and it ⛔ CONSTRUCTS ONE ⛔ 2,100 LINES LOWER, ⛔ green, ⛔ inside the executed baseline.** ✅ **⛔ QA ⛔ REPORTED IT AND ⛔ DID NOT SWEEP IT — ⛔ another lane's file. ⛔ That restraint is ⛔ why it became a ⛔ ROW instead of an ⛔ untracked edit.**
  - ✅⭐⭐ **THE RULE, ⛔ WHICH IS THIS SECTION'S ⛔ OWN RULE AIMED ⛔ DOWNWARD: ⛔ ⛔ A COMMENT THAT STATES A ⛔ BUILD-CONFIGURATION, ⛔ ENGINE-MACRO OR ⛔ TOOLCHAIN FACT MUST ⛔ POINT AT ITS ⛔ PRIMARY SOURCE BY ⛔ FILE + ⛔ SYMBOL, ⛔ NEVER RESTATE THE VALUE ALONE, AND ⛔ NEVER CITE A ⛔ SIBLING FILE THAT IS ITSELF A ⛔ RESTATEMENT.** ⛔ **⛔ Symbol, ⛔ not line number** (⛔ a line number ⛔ goes stale; ⛔ a symbol does ⛔ not).
  - ⇒ ⭐ **⛔ WHY THIS BELONGS ⛔ HERE AND NOT IN ITS OWN SECTION: ⛔ instances 1-5 are ⛔ A DISPATCH restating a ⛔ LAW. ⛔ This is ⛔ A FILE restating an ⛔ ENGINE FACT. ⛔ SAME DEFECT, ⛔ ONE ALTITUDE DOWN — and ⛔ the ⛔ COPY is ⛔ HOW IT SPREAD ⛔ BOTH TIMES.**
- 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **A ⛔ SEVENTH INSTANCE, ⛔ 2026-09-05 — ⛔ INSTANCE 3'S ⛔ SELF-CONTRADICTORY SPEC, ⛔ BUT POINTED AT A ⛔ SCOPE FENCE INSTEAD OF AT AN ⛔ ITEM: ⛔ ⛔ A ROW THAT ⛔ ORDERED ITS OWN AGENT TO ⛔ BREACH ITS OWN FENCE.** (⛔ **marker `TASK-1073-CL4-FENCE-FIX`**)
  - ⛔ **MEASURED: ⭐ `TASK-1073` cl. 4 read ⛔ *"Flip ⛔ ONLY ⭐ `TASK-1072`'s `status:` line and write ⛔ ONLY `qa/TASK-1073.md`"* ⛔ AND, ⛔ IN THE ⛔ SAME SENTENCE, ⛔ *"Do ⛔ NOT edit another row."*** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1072` ⛔ IS *"another row."* ⛔ The two halves ⛔ cannot both be obeyed.**
  - ✅⭐ **⛔ THE AGENT ⛔ CAUGHT IT: QA flipped ⛔ ONLY its own row, ⛔ REFUSED the other, and ⛔ ROUTED the conflict (`qa/TASK-1073.md` §7) on ⛔ TWO measured grounds — ⛔ scope, ⛔ and a ⛔ LOCK-FREE BOARD edited by ⛔ live concurrent agents where a ⛔ lost write ⛔ FAILS SILENTLY.** ⛔ **⛔ Its own framing: ⛔ *"a finding I report is ⛔ recoverable; ⛔ an edit I should not have made is ⛔ not."***
  - ⇒ ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ ***⛔ A SCOPE FENCE THAT HOLDS ⛔ ONLY BECAUSE SOMEBODY ⛔ DISOBEYS THE ROW IS ⛔ NOT A FENCE — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ COIN TOSS THAT CAME UP RIGHT. ⛔ THE SAME ROW SENT TO A ⛔ MORE COMPLIANT AGENT PRODUCES THE ⛔ BREACH AND ⛔ NOBODY EVER LEARNS THE CLAUSE WAS WRONG.***
  - ✅ **THE MECHANISM, ⛔ SO IT IS ⛔ NEVER RE-WRITTEN: ⛔ ⛔ A GATE'S WRITE SET IS ⛔ ITS OWN REPORT ⛔ + ⛔ ITS OWN ROW'S `status:` LINE. ⛔ NOTHING ELSE, ⛔ EVER.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ when a verdict ⛔ CHANGES ANOTHER ROW'S STATE, the gate ⛔ NAMES THE ROW AND THE ⛔ TARGET STATUS ⛔ IN ITS REPORT and the ⛔ MANAGER LANDS THE FLIP** — ⛔ **the ⛔ author is finished, the ⛔ reviewer may not, ⛔ and the ⛔ board's owner is ⛔ who is left.**
  - ⚠️⭐ **⛔ AND THE ⛔ AUTHOR OF THE BAD CLAUSE WAS THE ⛔ MANAGER, ⛔ AGAIN** (⛔ see ⭐ `SC-§93`'s ⛔ *"the defect was in a row the manager wrote"*) ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ the ⛔ CHECK is ⛔ mechanical and belongs at ⛔ WRITE time: ⛔ ⛔ after drafting any ⛔ WRITE-SET clause, ⛔ re-read it and ask ⛔ *"is every path in this set ⛔ OWNED BY THE ROLE I AM SENDING IT TO?"***
  - 🚨⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND-ORDER COST, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ EXPENSIVE HALF: the ⛔ SAME ROW demanded the author ⛔ EXHIBIT THE OLD DEFECT — ⛔ a decoy that must read ⛔ RED-BEFORE.** ⛔ **Against the ⛔ MOVED file that decoy ⛔ NO LONGER REPRODUCES** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the spec ⛔ PRESSURES AN AUTHOR TO KEEP ⛔ CONTRIVING UNTIL SOMETHING READS RED.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A SPEC THAT DEMANDS A ⛔ RED-BEFORE MUST BE ⛔ RE-CHECKED ⛔ WHENEVER ITS SUBJECT ⛔ MOVES, ⛔ OR IT BECOMES AN INSTRUCTION TO ⛔ MANUFACTURE EVIDENCE.***
- ⛔ **THE RULE FOR THE ⛔ SENDER: ⛔ before dispatching, ⛔ READ THE ROW'S `names:` AND ITS ⛔ NUMBERED ITEMS, then ⛔ ASK OF EVERY BOILERPLATE FENCE: ⛔ *"does any item ⛔ REQUIRE the thing I am forbidding?"*** ⛔ **`no editor` vs an ⛔ MCP write · `no Git` vs a ⛔ commit item · `comment-only` vs a ⛔ new symbol · `safe half` vs a ⛔ pinned class.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ A FENCE IS ⛔ CHEAP TO WRITE AND ⛔ EXPENSIVE TO OBEY WRONGLY. ⛔ THE COST LANDS ⛔ ENTIRELY ON THE AGENT AND ⛔ ENTIRELY OUT OF THE SENDER'S SIGHT.***

### SC-§66 ⛔⛔⛔ **TWO FIGURES THAT ⛔ SHARE A DERIVATION ⛔ CANNOT CORROBORATE EACH OTHER, ⛔ HOWEVER CLOSELY THEY AGREE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought on `qa/TASK-979.md` WARN-8, where a ⛔ FALSE CORROBORATION was ⛔ relayed twice and ⛔ nearly reached Jonathan**)

- ⛔ **THE CLAIM: the DEFEND disc's ⛔ `83.6%` blind figure *"agrees with"* the grouped lane's ⛔ `84%`, ⛔ *"reached from a completely different operand"*.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **REFUTED.**
- ⛔ **SAME FORMULA, ⛔ SAME NUMERATOR, ⛔ radii ⛔ 1.24% apart:** ⛔ `1 − (2000/5000)² = 84.00%` vs ⛔ `1 − (2000/4937.85)² = 83.59%`. ⇒ ⛔ **ONE OPERAND, ⛔ NOT TWO.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ ARITHMETIC IS ⛔ FINE; THE ⛔ CONFIDENCE CLAIM IS ⛔ INVALID.** ⛔ **The two would agree ⛔ JUST AS TIGHTLY ⛔ IF BOTH DERIVATIONS WERE ⛔ WRONG** — ⛔ **which is ⛔ exactly what corroboration is supposed to ⛔ rule out.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ AGREEMENT BETWEEN TWO NUMBERS IS EVIDENCE ⛔ ONLY IN PROPORTION TO THE ⛔ INDEPENDENCE OF THEIR DERIVATIONS. ⛔ SHARED FORMULA + SHARED INPUT = ⛔ ZERO INDEPENDENCE = ⛔ ZERO EVIDENCE.***
- ⛔ **BEFORE WRITING *"corroborated by"* / *"independently confirms"* / *"agrees with"*: ⛔ NAME THE ⛔ TWO DERIVATIONS AND ⛔ SHOW WHERE THEY ⛔ DIVERGE.** ⛔ **If they diverge ⛔ nowhere, it is a ⛔ RE-STATEMENT, ⛔ not a confirmation — ⛔ say ⛔ that.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND IT IS ⛔ MOST DANGEROUS IN A ⛔ RELAY: this one was ⛔ passed from author → coordinator → manager, ⛔ gaining apparent authority at ⛔ every hop while ⛔ nobody re-derived it.** ⇒ ⛔ **`SC-§40`'s family: ⛔ A CITATION IS NOT A MEASUREMENT — ⛔ and a ⛔ CORROBORATION is a ⛔ citation wearing a measurement's clothes.**

### SC-§65 ⛔⛔⛔ **A ⛔ STALE CLAIM NEED ⛔ NOT CONTAIN THE ⛔ VALUE IT IS STALE ABOUT. ⭐ SEARCH ⛔ CLAIM SHAPES, ⛔ NOT NUMBERS.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought on `TASK-979` BLOCKER-1, where the true count was ⛔ NINE sites — ⛔ relayed as five, ⛔ found as three**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE SITE THAT PROVES IT: `Tests/SiegeFogClampTest.cpp:1047` labelled `AcquireEnemyNearPoint` ⛔ **"UNBOUNDED"** — a label `TASK-979` ⛔ FALSIFIED by giving that path a bound.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE LINE ⛔ CONTAINS ⛔ NO NUMBER AT ALL.** ⛔ **No value-grep — ⛔ for `600`, for `2000`, at ⛔ any threshold, with ⛔ any tolerance — ⛔ could ⛔ EVER have reached it.**
- ⇒ ⛔ **A value census finds claims that ⛔ QUOTE a number. ⛔ It is ⛔ STRUCTURALLY BLIND to claims that ⛔ CHARACTERISE one** — ⛔ *"unbounded"*, *"unclamped"*, *"never"*, *"always"*, *"essentially untouched"*, *"the profile constant"*, *"not a card stat"*.

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§65a — A CORRECT CENSUS ⛔ NARROWS THE GAP; IT DOES ⛔ NOT CLOSE IT. ⛔ NEVER TREAT A CENSUS RESULT AS A ⛔ CEILING.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **measured across FOUR readers of ⛔ ONE doc block**)

- ⛔ **THE PROGRESSION, ⛔ ALL ON `CardRow.h`'s `NoticeRange` BLOCK: ⛔ `3` (⛔ first declarer) ⇒ ⛔ `8` (⛔ `qa/TASK-995.md`) ⇒ ⛔ `15` (⛔ boarded checklist) ⇒ ⛔ **`23`** (⛔ `TASK-1000`, ⛔ which found ⛔ TEN beyond the checklist).**
- ⭐⭐ **AND THE ⛔ CRUCIAL PART: ⛔ THE LAST RISE HAPPENED ⛔ UNDER THE ⛔ CORRECT METHOD.** ⛔ **`TASK-1000` censused ⛔ BY CLAIM SHAPE — ⛔ it did ⛔ NOT walk the enumeration — and ⛔ still found ten more, ⛔ then ⛔ DECLARED ITSELF INCOMPLETE.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THAT IS EVIDENCE ⛔ FOR THE METHOD, ⛔ NOT AGAINST IT. ⛔ A VALUE CENSUS WOULD HAVE FOUND ⛔ THREE AND ⛔ STOPPED CONFIDENTLY. ⛔ A SHAPE CENSUS FINDS ⛔ TWENTY-THREE AND ⛔ SAYS SO. ⛔ THE MEASURE OF A GOOD CENSUS IS ⛔ NOT THAT IT TERMINATES — ⛔ IT IS THAT IT ⛔ KNOWS IT DID NOT.***
- ⛔⛔ **THEREFORE: ⛔ a boarded enumeration is a ⛔ CHECKLIST, ⛔ NEVER A SCOPE, and ⛔ every prose-repair row ⛔ MUST say so in its own text.** ⛔ **⛔ Do ⛔ NOT let the next reader treat ⛔ `23` as a ceiling either.**
- ⛔ **WHY THE GAP ⛔ PERSISTS EVEN UNDER GOOD METHOD: ⛔ the ⛔ DECLARER of a defect is ⛔ SYSTEMATICALLY ITS ⛔ WORST COUNTER — ⛔ they census for the claim they ⛔ ALREADY FOUND** (⛔ the same shape as `SC-§75`(A2)'s ⛔ filtered argument). ⇒ ⛔ **the count rises ⛔ whenever ⛔ FRESH EYES arrive, and ⛔ *"fresh eyes"* is ⛔ the variable, ⛔ not *"effort"*.**
- ✅ **PRACTICAL RULE: ⛔ a prose row ⛔ RE-DERIVES the block ⛔ from scratch against the ⛔ landed diff, ⛔ THEN checks the enumeration as a ⛔ checklist. ⛔ Never the reverse order** — ⛔ reading the checklist first ⛔ anchors the census to it.

#### ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§65b — THE ⛔ LIMIT OF THE SHAPE CENSUS: ⛔ IT IS ⛔ TRIAGE, ⛔ NOT PROOF. ⛔ THE ⛔ TERMINAL INSTRUMENT IS A ⛔ FULL READ OF THE BLOCK.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **the honest counterpart to `SC-§65a`**)

- ⛔⛔ **MEASURED (`qa/TASK-1015.md` WARN-1): a stale sentence carrying ⛔ NO NUMBER ⛔ AND ⛔ NO SHAPE KEYWORD. ⛔ The reviewer's shape-grep returned ⛔ 7 hits across ⛔ 3 files and ⛔ NEVER REACHED IT. ⛔ A ⛔ FULL READ DID.**
- ⛔ **ITS ⛔ ONLY TELL WAS A ⛔ TENSE** — *"…the shape `TASK-839` ⛔ INHERITS is already the correct one"*. ⇒ ⛔ **`inherits` · `will` · `once` · `until` · `then` · `is about to` · `remains` — ⛔ a claim whose ⛔ falseness lives in its ⛔ GRAMMAR, ⛔ not in its ⛔ vocabulary.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE LADDER, ⛔ EACH RUNG A ⛔ STRICT IMPROVEMENT AND ⛔ NONE OF THEM COMPLETE:** ⛔ **(1) VALUE census** — ⛔ blind to claims that ⛔ characterise · ⛔ **(2) SHAPE census** (`SC-§65`) — ⛔ blind to claims that ⛔ neither quote ⛔ nor characterise, ⛔ only ⛔ TENSE · ⛔ **(3) ⛔ FULL READ of the block** — ⛔ the ⛔ terminal instrument.
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ `SC-§65a` RECORDED THAT A SHAPE CENSUS ⛔ KNOWS IT IS NOT DONE. ⛔ THIS CLAUSE NAMES ⛔ WHAT IT SYSTEMATICALLY MISSES — AND THE ANSWER IS ⛔ NOT AN OBSCURE CATEGORY: ⛔ IT IS ⛔ EVERY SENTENCE THAT DESCRIBES A ⛔ FUTURE THAT HAS SINCE ⛔ ARRIVED.***
#### ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **SC-§65c — THE ⛔ SAME DEFECT ⛔ ONE LAYER UP: A CENSUS KEYED ON AN ⛔ INLINE LITERAL ⛔ UNDER-REPORTS, BECAUSE ⛔ REAL CALL SITES ⛔ DO NOT ALL LOOK LIKE ONE.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **`qa/TASK-1014.md` loop 2 — ⛔ the true needle count is `18`, ⛔ NOT `14`**)

- ⛔⛔ **MEASURED: this project's tree-wide needle set was cited as ⛔ `14` by ⛔ every row that used it. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ 18.**
- ⛔ **THE FOUR THAT ⛔ EVERY CENSUS MISSED, ⛔ AND ⛔ WHY EACH ESCAPED:** ⛔ `BreakInvisibility(ESiegeVeilBreakReason::` — ⛔ passed as a ⛔ **VARIABLE** · ⛔ `ApplyVeilMaterial` / `ClearVeilMaterial` — ⛔ passed from an ⛔ **ARRAY** · ⛔ the `MI_Unit_Invisible` path — ⛔ **LINE-WRAPPED**.
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **ALL FOUR SHARE ⛔ ONE PROPERTY: ⛔ NONE IS A ⛔ SINGLE-LINE INLINE `TEXT("…")` AT THE CALL SITE.** ⛔ **A census keyed on that literal reaches ⛔ EXACTLY the 14 and ⛔ NONE of the 4 — ⛔ not by bad luck, ⛔ by construction.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THIS IS `SC-§65`'s ⛔ OWN DEFECT ⛔ WEARING A DIFFERENT COSTUME — ⛔ A VALUE CENSUS IS BLIND TO CLAIMS THAT ⛔ CHARACTERISE; ⛔ A LITERAL CENSUS IS BLIND TO CALL SITES THAT ⛔ INDIRECT. ⛔ THE INSTRUMENT ALWAYS FINDS ⛔ THE SHAPE IT WAS BUILT FOR AND ⛔ REPORTS THE REST AS ⛔ ABSENT.***
- ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ REASON WE KNOW: ⛔ THE REVIEWER ⛔ MISSED THE SAME FOUR IN LOOP 1 ⛔ AND SAID SO.** ⇒ ⛔ **a self-reported instrument failure is ⛔ worth more than a clean report, and it is the ⛔ only mechanism that catches this class at all.**
- ⚠️ **THE ⛔ CONCLUSION SURVIVED (⛔ `0` hits in `CardRow.h` for ⛔ all 18; ⛔ `CountOccurrencesInCode` culls `* ` lines regardless) ⇒ ⛔ WARN, ⛔ not blocker.** ⛔⛔ **BUT ⛔ EVERY ROW THAT EVER CITED *"THE 14 TREE-WIDE NEEDLES"* CITED AN ⛔ INCOMPLETE SET.** ⇒ ⛔ **the number is ⛔ `18`; ⛔ cite it, and ⛔ expect it to be ⛔ low** (⭐ `SC-§65a`).
- ✅ **THE REPAIR: ⛔ census the ⛔ CALLED SYMBOL, ⛔ never the ⛔ ARGUMENT SPELLING** — ⛔ **the function name is the ⛔ KEY (⭐ `SC-§77`); ⛔ an inline literal is a ⛔ COORDINATE in disguise.**

- ✅ **THEREFORE: ⛔ USE THE SHAPE CENSUS TO ⛔ FIND THE BLOCKS, ⛔ THEN ⛔ READ EACH BLOCK ⛔ WHOLE.** ⛔ **A row that reports ⛔ *"N hits adjudicated"* ⛔ WITHOUT reporting ⛔ *"and I read the surrounding block"* has ⛔ run triage and ⛔ called it proof.** ⛔ **`SC-§38a` already says ⛔ `Read` every hit; ⛔ this extends it to ⛔ READ AROUND every hit.**
- ✅⭐⭐ **THE INSTRUMENT THAT WORKS — A ⛔ CLAIM-SHAPE CENSUS, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ TWO STEPS IN THIS ORDER:** ⛔ **(1) ENUMERATE THE ⛔ BEHAVIOURS THE DIFF CHANGED** (⛔ *"this path was unbounded and now has a bound"*, ⛔ *"this value was a class constant and is now data-driven"*) ⛔ **(2) SEARCH THE ⛔ SHAPES THAT ⛔ ASSERT THOSE BEHAVIOURS** — ⛔ **never the values.**
- ⛔ **IT IS A ⛔ STRICT SUPERSET: ⛔ a value census is the ⛔ subset of claim shapes that ⛔ happen to contain a literal.** ⇒ ⛔ **run the claim-shape census and the value census is ⛔ redundant, ⛔ not the other way round.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ THE MOST DANGEROUS STALE CLAIM IS THE ⛔ MOST CONFIDENTLY WORDED ONE, ⛔ BECAUSE ABSOLUTES LIKE "NEVER" AND "UNBOUNDED" ARE WRITTEN ⛔ WITHOUT NUMBERS — ⛔ so the more emphatic the claim, the ⛔ LESS likely a grep finds it when it goes false.***
- ⭐ **SITS BESIDE `SC-§47` (⛔ the stale-claim family) and ⭐ `SC-§61` (⛔ sweep the row, ⛔ grade by destination). ⛔ `SC-§61` says ⛔ WHERE to look; ⛔ this says ⛔ WHAT TO LOOK FOR.**

### SC-§64 ⛔⛔⛔ **ONE GATE MAY ⛔ NOT SPAN TWO ROWS THAT CAN ⛔ FINISH AT DIFFERENT TIMES.** (added 2026-09-04 by the manager; ⛔ **bought on `TASK-985`, where a reviewer examined ⛔ half a gate and wrote to a path ⛔ no gate pointed to**)

- ⛔ **THE SHAPE: the board defined ⛔ ONE gate over `TASK-979` + `TASK-980` → `qa/TASK-985.md`. ⛔ `979` finished ⛔ first, was reviewed ⛔ on its own, and the verdict landed at ⛔ `qa/TASK-979.md`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`qa/TASK-985.md` would then either ⛔ NEVER EXIST or be written as though ⛔ `979` WERE UNREVIEWED, while the ⛔ REAL review sat ⛔ ORPHANED.**

#### 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **SC-§64a — THE RULE, ⛔ HARDENED 2026-09-05 AFTER THE ⛔ SAME TRAP FIRED A ⛔ SECOND TIME ⛔ IN THE SAME BATCH. ⛔ THE ⛔ ADVISORY FORM IS ⛔ WITHDRAWN.**

- ⛔ **SECOND INSTANCE: ⭐ `TASK-1009` gated ⭐ `TASK-1007` ⛔ + ⭐ `TASK-1008`. ⛔ `1007` landed; ⛔ `1008` was ⛔ NEVER WRITTEN — ⛔ no diff, ⛔ not `ready-for-qa` — and the gate was ⛔ DISPATCHED ANYWAY.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A PASS WOULD HAVE SHIPPED `1008` CARRYING A VERDICT ⛔ ISSUED BEFORE ITS DIFF EXISTED.** ⛔ **⛔ THE ⛔ EXACT `TASK-995` DEFECT, ⛔ from the ⛔ opposite direction: ⛔ there a gate ⛔ passed before its subject existed; ⛔ here a gate ⛔ would pass a subject that ⛔ still does not.**
- ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A MULTI-SUBJECT GATE IS A ⛔ SPENT-GATE TRAP ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION: ⛔ ITS `blocked-by` IS SATISFIED WHEN THE ⛔ LAST SUBJECT IS READY, ⛔ BUT ⛔ NOTHING STOPS IT BEING DISPATCHED WHEN THE ⛔ FIRST IS — AND ⛔ NOTHING IN THE ROW RECORDS ⛔ WHICH SUBJECTS IT HAS ACTUALLY SEEN.***
- ⭐ **AND IT IS THE ⛔ SIBLING FORM of *"a gate over a ⛔ DEPENDENT row is ⛔ not a gate over its ⛔ DEPENDENCY"* — ⛔ here it is a gate over ⛔ TWO SIBLINGS, ⛔ discharged by seeing ⛔ ONE.**

##### ✅⚖️ **THE RULING — ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT SIMPLY *"ALWAYS SPLIT"*, BECAUSE ⛔ SPLITTING ALONE ⛔ LOSES SOMETHING REAL.**

1. ⛔⛔ **DEFAULT: ⛔ SPLIT. ⛔ ONE GATE PER SUBJECT.** ⛔ **A gate may carry ⛔ MORE THAN ONE SUBJECT ⛔ ONLY IF THEY LAND IN THE ⛔ SAME ACTION** (⛔ e.g. one row producing two files). ⛔ **⛔ If they can finish at ⛔ different times, ⛔ SPLIT — ⛔ no exceptions, ⛔ no judgement call.**
2. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **AND THE PART THE SPLIT ⛔ WOULD OTHERWISE DESTROY: ⛔ A ⛔ GENUINE CROSS-SUBJECT PROPERTY GETS ITS ⛔ OWN CHECK ON THE ⛔ LATER GATE — ⛔ it is ⛔ NOT a reason to keep a multi-subject gate.**
   - ⛔ **EXHIBIT: *"is there ⛔ EXACTLY ONE derivation of ⛔ has-a-reticle?"* is ⛔ INVISIBLE to ⛔ either subject's own gate and ⛔ only observable ⛔ once BOTH diffs exist.** ⇒ ⛔ **it moved to ⭐ `TASK-1039`, the ⛔ SECOND gate, ⛔ which cites the first rather than re-deriving it.**
   - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE CROSS-CUTTING CHECK IS ⛔ REAL AND IT IS ⛔ NOT AN ARGUMENT FOR A SHARED GATE — ⛔ IT IS AN ARGUMENT FOR PUTTING IT WHERE ⛔ BOTH SUBJECTS EXIST, WHICH IS ⛔ ALWAYS THE LATER ONE.***
3. ⛔ **UNIVERSAL BACKSTOP, ⛔ EVEN FOR A CORRECTLY-SPLIT GATE: ⛔ A VERDICT MUST ⛔ ENUMERATE EACH SUBJECT ⛔ WITH THE INSTANT IT WAS REVIEWED AT, AND ⛔ NAME ANYTHING ⛔ CITED RATHER THAN REVIEWED.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ PARTIAL pass is then ⛔ VISIBLY partial ⛔ in its own headline, ⛔ not inferable only from the board.**
3a. ✅⭐⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 ⛔ PAID OFF ⛔ MEASURABLY, ⛔ WITHIN ONE DAY — ⛔ RECORDED BECAUSE IT LOOKED LIKE ⛔ BOOKKEEPING WHEN IT WAS WRITTEN.** (2026-09-05; ⛔ **`12b8707`'s gate audit**)
   - ⛔ **THE HOST'S GATE AUDIT ⛔ HAD TO ANSWER: ⛔ *"does a verdict cover ⛔ `TASK-1008`?"* ⇒ ⛔ it opened `qa/TASK-1009.md` and found the report ⛔ REFUSING IN ITS OWN WORDS: *"This verdict covers ⛔ `TASK-1007`'s diff ⛔ ONLY."***
   - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE SCOPED REPORT ⛔ ACTIVELY DECLINES TO OVER-CLAIM. ⛔ THE AUDITOR COULD ⛔ SEE THE BOUNDARY INSTEAD OF HAVING TO ⛔ INFER IT FROM THE BOARD — AND ⛔ INFERENCE IS EXACTLY WHERE `TASK-964` SHIPPED UNGATED.***
   - ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ COUNTERFACTUAL IS THE WHOLE POINT: an ⛔ UNSCOPED `qa/TASK-1009.md` would have been ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE from a verdict covering both — ⛔ it would have ⛔ PASSED the audit ⛔ while covering ⛔ half its named subjects, and ⛔ nothing downstream could have detected it.**
   - ⇒ ✅ **THE GENERAL FORM: ⛔ A ⛔ SELF-LIMITING ARTEFACT IS ⛔ CHEAPER THAN ⛔ ANY AMOUNT OF DOWNSTREAM VIGILANCE.** ⛔ **⛔ One sentence written by the reviewer who ⛔ KNOWS the scope ⛔ replaces an ⛔ open-ended investigation by every reader who ⛔ does not.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ this is ⛔ why cl. 3 is ⛔ UNIVERSAL and ⛔ not conditional on the gate being multi-subject.**
   - ⭐ **CORROBORATING, ⛔ SAME AUDIT: ⛔ both verdicts ⛔ POSTDATED the diffs they bless (⛔ the `SC-§73` stale-verdict check, ⛔ run rather than assumed), and the `TASK-1036`/`1037` ⛔ WAIVERS were verified ⛔ BY READING THEIR OWN HANDOFFS — ⛔ NOT taken from the dispatch that cited them** (⭐ `SC-§40` cl. 1: ⛔ **a relay is a citation**).
4. ⛔ **IF A MULTI-SUBJECT GATE IS ⛔ ALREADY IN FLIGHT WHEN THIS IS NOTICED: ⛔ the reviewer ⛔ CONTINUES on the subjects that ⛔ EXIST, ⛔ scopes the verdict ⛔ IN THE HEADLINE, and ⛔ states which subject was ⛔ unwritten at its instant.** ⛔ **⛔ Then the board ⛔ SPLITS the row.** ⭐ **That containment was applied ⛔ correctly on ⭐ `TASK-1009` ⛔ before the ruling existed.**

##### ⛔ **AND THE DISPATCH-SIDE CHECK THAT IS THE ⛔ ONLY ONE THAT CATCHES THIS — ⭐ `SC-§74` CHECK (g):**

- ⛔⛔ **VERIFYING THAT `blocked-by` ⛔ ITEMS ARE SATISFIED IS ⛔ NOT THE SAME QUESTION AS VERIFYING THAT EACH NAMED ⛔ SUBJECT ⛔ EXISTS AS A DIFF.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ ONLY THE SECOND CATCHES A GATE POINTED AT AN ⛔ UNWRITTEN ROW.**
- ✅ **CHECK (g): ⛔ before dispatching ⛔ any gate, ⛔ confirm ⛔ EVERY subject it names has a ⛔ HANDOFF ON DISK.** ⛔ **⛔ Cheap, ⛔ mechanical, ⛔ and it is the check whose absence produced this.**
- ⭐ **THE REVIEWER CAUGHT IT ⛔ ITSELF — *"This file is not `TASK-985`… the `980` half is unexamined"* — ⛔ which is the ⛔ ONLY reason it did not become a ⛔ silent coverage hole.** ⛔ **Do ⛔ not rely on that; ⛔ a gate's topology is the ⛔ MANAGER's to get right.**
- ⛔ **WHY IT IS ⛔ NOT MERELY UNTIDY: ⛔ the two halves get reviewed under ⛔ DIFFERENT PREDICATES, at ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTANTS, by ⛔ DIFFERENT READERS — ⛔ and ⛔ NOTHING ON THE BOARD SAYS SO.** ⛔ **The coverage ledger `SC-§29` requires becomes ⛔ unauditable.**
- ✅ **THE TWO LEGAL SHAPES, ⛔ AND ⛔ PICK ONE ⛔ EXPLICITLY:** ⛔ **(a) SPLIT** into ⛔ per-row gates · ⛔ **(b) COMPOSITE** — ⛔ the gate covers the ⛔ LATER row on its ⛔ own merits and ⛔ CITES the earlier row's verdict ⛔ BY PATH, ⛔ incorporating it ⛔ by reference.
- ⛔⛔ **UNDER (b): ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT RE-REVIEW THE CITED HALF.** ⛔ **That spends a second reviewer on a ⛔ settled diff and risks ⛔ TWO CONTRADICTORY VERDICTS ⛔ ON ONE GATE — ⛔ and a contradiction between two gates is ⛔ far more expensive than the review it saved.**
- ⛔ **IF THE CITED HALF IS ⛔ `qa-failed`, the composite ⛔ CANNOT READ `PASS` until that ⛔ REPAIR IS ⛔ ITSELF VERIFIED — ⛔ and the ledger must name ⛔ WHO verified it and ⛔ WHERE.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ BOARD A GATE PER ⛔ INDEPENDENTLY-COMPLETABLE UNIT OF WORK, ⛔ NOT PER ⛔ THEME. ⛔ THEMES DO NOT FINISH; ⛔ ROWS DO.***

### SC-§62 ⚖️⛔⛔⛔ **WHEN A FENCE MAY BE ⛔ EXCEEDED — THE ⛔ FOUR-CLAUSE TEST. ⛔ MISSING ANY ONE = ⛔ BLOCKER.** (added 2026-09-04 by the manager; ⛔ **ruled on `TASK-981`, where the breach was ⛔ ACCEPTED and the `names:` LIST was found to be the ⛔ DEFECTIVE ARTEFACT**)

- ⛔⛔ **A `names:` fence is ⛔ BINDING. ⛔ It may be exceeded ⛔ ONLY when ⛔ ALL FOUR hold, and the burden is on the ⛔ author:**

| # | ⛔ clause |
|---|---|
| **i** | ⛔ **OBEDIENCE MAKES A SPEC ITEM ⛔ UNSATISFIABLE ⛔ OR BREAKS THE BUILD** (⛔ not *"inconvenient"*, ⛔ not *"tidier"*) |
| **ii** | ⛔ **THE EXTRA FILE IS ⛔ UNOWNED BY ⛔ ANY LIVE ROW** — ⛔ census it, ⛔ do not assume |
| **iii** | ⛔ **THE BREACH IS ⛔ DECLARED ⛔ PRE-REVIEW, ⛔ WITH ITS ⛔ FORCING MEASUREMENT** — ⛔ never discovered by the gate |
| **iv** | ⛔ **AN ⛔ EXPLICIT RULING IS ⛔ REQUESTED** — ⛔ the author does ⛔ not self-absolve |

- ⛔ **THE LIVE CASE:** `TASK-981` item (5) ⛔ retired `FogDensityExponent`; `Tests/SiegeFogTest.cpp` ⛔ referenced it ⇒ ⛔ obeying the fence left a ⛔ NON-COMPILING TREE and would have ⛔ EATEN `TASK-987`'s ⛔ one compile (⛔ **clause i**). ⛔ The file was ⛔ unowned — `TASK-910`'s `names:` covers `SiegeFogClampTest.cpp` + `SiegeFogStatics.h`, ⛔ **not** `SiegeFogTest.cpp`; `838`/`847`/`908` closed (⛔ **clause ii**, ⛔ census ⛔ RE-RUN INDEPENDENTLY BY QA, ⛔ not taken on the author's word). ⛔ Declared pre-review (⛔ **iii**) with a ruling requested (⛔ **iv**). ⇒ ✅ **ACCEPTED.**
- ⭐⭐ **AND QA RULED IT WAS THE ⛔ RIGHT FILE, WHICH IS THE ⛔ SHARPER HALF: `SiegeFogClampTest.cpp`'s own header (`:20-25`) says it ⛔ DELIBERATELY RE-ASSERTS ⛔ NONE of the arithmetic** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ NAMED file contained ⛔ almost none of the rows the change ⛔ invalidated.**
- ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE PRACTICAL LAW FOR WHOEVER WRITES A `names:` LIST: ⛔ LIST THE FILES THE CHANGE'S ⛔ CONSEQUENCES REACH, ⛔ NOT THE FILES THE CHANGE'S ⛔ SUBJECT LIVES IN.*** ⛔ **`TASK-981` retired a ⛔ SYMBOL; the fence listed the symbol's ⛔ HOME and ⛔ not its ⛔ REFERENCES. ⛔ That is a ⛔ MANAGER defect, ⛔ not an author defect.**

### SC-§63 ⛔⛔⛔ **A ⛔ PIN OR ⛔ FENCE WRITTEN AGAINST ⛔ TODAY'S CALL GRAPH BECOMES A ⛔ TRAP THE MOMENT THE FEATURE ACQUIRES ITS ⛔ FIRST LEGITIMATE ⛔ SECOND CONSUMER.** (added 2026-09-04; ⛔ **bought TWICE in one report — `qa/TASK-981.md` W-2 and W-3**)

- ⛔⛔ **A PIN PROTECTS A ⛔ COUNT. ⛔ WHAT IT ⛔ MEANS TO PROTECT IS ⛔ UNAUTHORISED GROWTH.** ⛔ **Those coincide ⛔ only while the authorised count is ⛔ frozen — and it ⛔ never stays frozen.**
- ⛔ **THE ⛔ DEADLOCK SHAPE, ⛔ MEASURED: `Tests/SiegeFogClampTest.cpp:699-714` pins `FSiegeFogStatics::EffectiveVisionRadius(` at ⛔ `2` and its ⛔ OWN FAILURE MESSAGE calls a third *"an ⛔ automatic QA FAIL … ⛔ do NOT fix a red here"* — while ⭐ `TASK-980` item (1) ⛔ PRESCRIBES exactly a third.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE ROW TURNS THE TEST RED AND THE TEST FORBIDS FIXING THE RED. ⛔ A programmer obeying the row and a reviewer obeying the message ⛔ CANNOT BOTH SUCCEED, and ⛔ BOTH ARE FOLLOWING INSTRUCTIONS.**
- ⇒ ⛔ **IT IS A ⛔ ROW-VS-GATE CONTRADICTION, ⛔ NOT A DEFECT IN EITHER ARTEFACT — ⛔ diagnose it as such, or you will *"fix"* a ⛔ correct pin or a ⛔ correct row.**
- ⛔⛔⛔ **RESOLUTION, ⛔ AND ⛔ NEVER THE LAZY ONE: ⛔ DO ⛔ NOT SIMPLY RAISE `N` TO `N+1`.** ⛔ **A bare renumber ⛔ RE-ARMS THE IDENTICAL TRAP for the ⛔ next legitimate consumer.** ⇒ ⛔ **`SC-§60` binds: ⛔ a pin whose ⛔ SUBJECT CHANGED needs its ⛔ ARGUMENT ⛔ RE-DERIVED, ⛔ NOT RE-SIGNED.**
- ✅ **RE-DERIVE TO WHAT IT ⛔ ACTUALLY CARES ABOUT** — here, ⛔ *"every application of the ceiling lives inside a ⛔ NAMED CHOKEPOINT"*, ⛔ not *"there are two of them"*. ⛔ **The re-derived guard must ⛔ STILL GO RED on the original hazard (⛔ a per-site clamp).**
- ⭐ **BOARD PINS SO AN ⛔ AUTHORISED CONSUMER CAN ⛔ PASS ⛔ WITHOUT EDITING THE GUARD'S ⛔ PREMISE.** ⛔ **A guard that must be ⛔ rewritten by every legitimate user is a ⛔ toll booth, ⛔ not a gate.**
- ⛔ **SAME FAMILY, ⛔ SECOND INSTANCE (W-2): a `names:` clause reading *"MUST BE UNTOUCHED: `<file>`"* ⛔ becomes a ⛔ FALSE-BLOCKER the moment a ⛔ SIBLING ROW ⛔ SHIPPING ON THE ⛔ SAME HOST legitimately touches it.** ⇒ ⛔ **fences must name ⛔ WHO may not touch a file, ⛔ not that ⛔ NOBODY may.**

### SC-§61 ⛔⛔⛔ **AN AMENDMENT TO A ⛔ ROW IS ⛔ NOT AN AMENDMENT TO THE ROW'S ⛔ OTHER ITEMS. ⭐ WHEN A FACT IS REFUTED, ⛔ SWEEP EVERY ROW THAT ⛔ CARRIES IT — ⛔ NOT THE INSTANCE YOU WERE SHOWN.** (added 2026-09-04 by the manager; ⛔ **bought at ⛔ zero cost by `qa/TASK-996.md` WARN-2, ⛔ caught with `0` occurrences in `Source/`**)

- ⛔⛔ **THE LIVE CASE, ⛔ RECORDED IN FULL BECAUSE THE ⛔ SEVERITY GRADIENT IS THE LESSON:** the `11.1×` search-area figure was ⛔ measured false. ⛔ **The manager amended `TASK-979` item ⛔ (6)(b) — the instance it had been ⛔ handed — and ⛔ MISSED item ⛔ (2) ⛔ IN THE SAME ROW.**
- ⇒ ⛔⛔⛔ **AND ITEM (2) WAS THE ⛔ DANGEROUS ONE: ⛔ (6)(b) merely ⛔ MISLEADS A HANDOFF; ⛔ (2) ⛔ ORDERED THE FALSE NUMBER ⛔ INTO A ⛔ SHIPPED SOURCE COMMENT ⛔ UNDER `HIGH-§1`** — ⛔ **i.e. into the ⛔ ONE place this project treats as ⛔ AUTHORITATIVE, ⛔ permanently, ⛔ where the next reader takes it as ⛔ fact and ⛔ retunes against it.**
- ⛔ **THE LAW: ⛔ when a fact is refuted, ⛔ `grep` the ⛔ WHOLE BOARD AND ⛔ THE WHOLE LAW FILE for it ⛔ before declaring the amendment done.** ⛔ **A row is ⛔ NOT a unit of correctness — ⛔ it is a ⛔ container, and a refuted fact ⛔ settles in ⛔ several of its items ⛔ independently.**
- ⛔⛔ **GRADE THE INSTANCES ⛔ BY DESTINATION, ⛔ NOT BY COUNT: ⛔ an instance headed for ⛔ SHIPPED SOURCE ⛔ outranks one headed for a ⛔ handoff, which outranks one in a ⛔ gate, which outranks ⛔ prose.** ⛔ **Fix in that order if you cannot fix all at once.**
- ⚖️ ***⛔ THE INSTANCE YOU WERE SHOWN IS ⛔ EVIDENCE THAT THE FACT ⛔ SPREAD — ⛔ NEVER EVIDENCE OF ⛔ HOW FAR.***
- ⭐ **COROLLARY, ⛔ bought the same day: ⛔ a ⛔ CENSUS THAT ⛔ SUBTRACTS A DIRECTORY MUST ⛔ EXAMINE THAT DIRECTORY, ⛔ NOT DROP IT.** ⛔ **`TASK-978` subtracted its `5` `Tests/` occurrences from the count and ⛔ never opened them — and ⛔ that is ⛔ exactly where the `SC-§60` victim was hiding** (`Tests/SiegeFogClampTest.cpp:1063-1068`). ⇒ ⛔ **`EXCLUDED-FROM-CENSUS` is ⛔ PRECISELY WHERE ⛔ STALE ASSERTIONS LIVE, because they are ⛔ invisible to a count that skips them ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION.**

### SC-§60 ⛔⛔⛔ **A TEST THAT ENCODES A ⛔ DEFECT AS ⛔ INTENDED BEHAVIOUR IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO ⛔ EVERY GATE THIS PROJECT OWNS — ⭐⭐ AND WHEN A ROW'S ⛔ POLARITY FLIPS, ITS ⛔ ANTI-FAKE ARGUMENT MUST BE ⛔ RE-DERIVED, ⛔ NEVER RE-SIGNED** (added 2026-09-03 by the manager; ⛔ **proposed by `qa/TASK-943.md` §11, ⛔ bought by `TASK-942`, which ⛔ anticipated all four clauses and ⛔ paid them in advance**)

> ### ⛔⛔ **TWO SHIPPED TESTS ⛔ ASSERTED THE DEFECT AND WERE ⛔ GREEN FOR THE ⛔ ENTIRE TIME 🧑 JONATHAN COULD ⛔ NOT STACK A TOWER.** `SiegeBuildingStackTest` test 10 (*"`AClimbableTower` ⛔ REFUSES the height upgrade"*) and `SiegePlacementTest` test 12 (*"⛔ NEVER yields the blue upgrade state"*). ⛔ **Both correct as written. ⛔ Both green. ⛔ Both wrong about the game.**

> ### ⚖️ ***A PASSING SUITE IS A STATEMENT ABOUT THE ⛔ CODE. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NEVER A STATEMENT ABOUT THE ⛔ DESIGN BEING RIGHT.***

📌 **NUMBERING, ⛔ RECORDED SO NOBODY HUNTS FOR THE WRONG SECTION: `qa/TASK-943.md` proposed this as ⛔ `SC-§57`. ⛔ That number was ⛔ TAKEN THE SAME DAY by the ⛔ count-mismatch law (from `TASK-967`) ⇒ ⛔ legislated ⛔ HERE as ⛔ `SC-§60`. ⛔ A citation of *"`SC-§57` (the polarity law)"* means ⛔ THIS section.**

⛔⛔ **WHY THIS CLASS IS UNIQUELY DANGEROUS — ⛔ THE FOUR NEGATIVES, ⛔ AND ⛔ NOTHING THIS PIPELINE BUILT IN 2026-09-03's WAVE WOULD HAVE FOUND IT:**
- ⛔ **A ⛔ COUNT-BASED check cannot see it** — ⛔ the count is ⛔ RIGHT.
- ⛔ **An ⛔ EXECUTION-BASED check cannot see it** — ⛔ it ⛔ PASSES.
- ⛔ **A ⛔ MUTATION HARNESS cannot see it** — ⛔ it goes ⛔ RED when you break it. ⛔ It is a ⛔ WORKING gate around the ⛔ WRONG DECISION.
- ⛔ **⛔ COVERAGE cannot see it** — ⛔ the line is ⛔ COVERED.
⇒ ⛔⛔ **`SHIP-§9` (⛔ validate a gate against the ⛔ FAILURE it detects) is ⛔ NOT a defence here: the gate ⛔ DOES detect its failure. ⛔ The failure is ⛔ the one it was ⛔ pointed at.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — THE ⛔ CENSUS IS ⛔ MANDATORY AND IT IS ⛔ REPORTED AS A ⛔ NUMBER, ⛔ INCLUDING `0`.** ⛔ **When a defect is fixed, the ⛔ FIXING ROW must ⛔ CENSUS the suite for rows that ⛔ ASSERTED THE OLD BEHAVIOUR** — ⛔ by ⛔ SYMBOL and by ⛔ SUBJECT, ⛔ not by memory — ⛔ **and publish the count.** ⛔ **`TASK-942` did exactly this and found ⛔ `2`.** ⭐⭐ **That census is the ⛔ ONLY INSTRUMENT THAT EXISTS for this class.** ⛔ **A fixing row with ⛔ no such census has ⛔ not looked, and *"I did not see any"* is ⛔ not a `0`.**
2. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 2 — AN INVERTED ROW IS ⛔ KEPT AND ⛔ INVERTED, ⛔ NEVER DELETED**, ⛔ with the ⛔ HISTORY IN ITS ⛔ BANNER. ⇒ ⛔ **the next reader sees the refusal was ⛔ DELIBERATE and ⛔ REVERSED BY A MEASUREMENT, ⛔ rather than never having been there.** ⛔ **A deleted row erases the evidence that the project ⛔ once believed the wrong thing — ⛔ which is precisely the evidence a future reader needs.**
3. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — ⛔ THE ONE WITH TEETH. ⛔ WHEN A ROW'S ⛔ POLARITY FLIPS, ITS ⛔ ANTI-FAKE ARGUMENT MUST BE ⛔ RE-DERIVED, ⛔ NEVER RE-SIGNED.** ⛔ **THE REASON, ⛔ STATED SO IT IS ⛔ NOT MISTAKEN FOR CEREMONY: ⛔ THE OLD RED CONTROL IS ⛔ FREQUENTLY THE VERY FIXTURE THAT ⛔ JUST CHANGED SIDES.**
   ⭐ **WORKED EXAMPLE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT HYPOTHETICAL — `SiegePlacementTest` test 12:** its ⛔ old red control ⛔ **WAS** the climbable tower. ⛔ After the flip, a bare *"it is `Ready`"* would ⛔ **ALSO PASS against a resolver that had ⛔ LOST GATE (5) ENTIRELY.** ⇒ ⛔ **the replacement controls had to be ⛔ NEW ONES** (an own-team building of a ⛔ DIFFERENT card ⇒ `None`, with its ⛔ OWN fixture; an ⛔ UNAFFORDABLE tower ⇒ `Unaffordable`, ⛔ **not** `NotStackable`; an ⛔ ENEMY climbable tower ⇒ `None`). ⛔ **`qa/TASK-943.md` ⛔ RE-DERIVED this rather than accepting it, ⛔ and that is the standard.**
   ⚠️ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND SPECIES, ⛔ FROM THE SAME BATCH: a flip can ⛔ REMOVE A FREE PASS.** `SiegeBuildingStackTest` test 10's ⛔ OLD row asserted `ApplyStackUpgrade() == false` — ⛔ which a fixture ⛔ **LACKING AUTHORITY** would satisfy for ⛔ entirely the wrong reason. ⛔ The ⛔ NEW row demands `true` ⇒ ⛔ **it now ⛔ DEPENDS on the fixture having authority.** ⇒ ⛔ **a polarity flip changes ⛔ WHICH PRECONDITIONS THE ROW SILENTLY RELIES ON. ⛔ Enumerate them again.**
4. ⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — A ROW WHOSE SUBJECT IS A ⛔ DESIGN RULING CARRIES THE ⛔ RULING'S CITATION IN ITS ⛔ BANNER**, so a future reader can tell ⛔ *"the code does X"* from ⛔ *"X is what we ⛔ DECIDED"*. ⛔ **Test 10's banner cites `TASK-941`'s ⛔ MEASUREMENT; test 11's cites ⛔ `STACK-§10`'s ⛔ MEASURED LICENCE. ⛔ Copy that.**
5. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ REVIEWER'S DUTY, ⛔ ADDED BY ME: ⛔ A GATE OVER A ⛔ FIXING ROW MUST ⛔ ADJUDICATE EVERY INVERSION FOR ⛔ VACUITY, ⛔ INDIVIDUALLY, ⛔ AND SAY SO.** ⛔ *"Both inversions correct"* is ⛔ a verdict, ⛔ not a measurement. ⛔ **Name, per inverted row, ⛔ WHAT DISCRIMINATES THE NEW POLARITY** — ⛔ e.g. test 10's `(b)` (⛔ the mesh Z ⛔ actually grew, X and Y ⛔ exactly `1.0` — ⛔ *"'returned true' is ⛔ also what a mutator that ⛔ incremented a counter and ⛔ forgot the transform would report"*). ⛔ **An inversion nobody discriminated is a ⛔ green row asserting ⛔ nothing.**
6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — KINSHIP, ⛔ AND THE ⛔ BOUNDARY.** `SHIP-§9` supplies ⛔ *validate against the failure* (⛔ **and is ⛔ BLIND here — see the four negatives above**) · `SC-§51` cl. 6 supplies the ⛔ anti-vacuity ⛔ TELL · `SC-§50` supplies ⛔ *a wrong rule ships silently and nothing goes red* (⛔ **this is its ⛔ TEST-FILE species; `HELP-§2` mechanism 4 is its ⛔ PLAYER-FACING species**) · `SC-§37` supplies ⛔ measure-the-PROPERTY · `SC-§40` cl. 9 supplies ⛔ re-measure-never-relay. ⇒ 📌 ⛔ **THE FAMILY SENTENCE: ⛔ A GREEN SUITE PROVES THE CODE DOES WHAT THE TESTS SAY. ⛔ IT PROVES ⛔ NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER THE TESTS SAY THE ⛔ RIGHT THING — ⛔ AND ⛔ ONLY A ⛔ HUMAN PLAYTEST OR AN ⛔ EXPLICIT CENSUS EVER CLOSES THAT GAP.**

### TL-§5 ⛔ AN ASSET SWEEP'S **TOTAL** IS A PROPERTY OF ITS **SCOPE** — REPORT THE SCOPE WITH THE COUNT, OR A FUTURE READER SEES DRIFT (added 2026-08-16, from TASK-593)

> ### ⛔ **TWO HONEST SWEEPS OF THE SAME PROJECT, MINUTES APART, RETURNED `7,074` AND `3,229`. NEITHER IS WRONG AND NEITHER IS COMPARABLE TO THE OTHER.**

- ⚠️ **MEASURED: `find_assets("")` searches `/Game` ***plus every project and engine plugin content root***; a `/Game`-scoped sweep returned **3,229 found / 58 `__External*` excluded / 3,171 scanned**, reconciling with the previous task's figure TO THE UNIT.** ⇒ ⛔ **A bare total of `7,074` set beside a recorded `3,229` reads as **3,845 assets of drift** — a STOP condition manufactured entirely by an unstated scope.**
- ⛔ **THE LAW: every asset count is published WITH its scope and its exclusions, in the same sentence** — `"/Game: 3,229 found / 58 __External* excluded / 3,171 scanned"`, ⛔ never a bare number. ✅ **And when comparing against a prior task's figure, RE-RUN AT THAT TASK'S SCOPE rather than explaining the difference** (`SC-§29b`: ⛔ a difference you can only narrate is not a difference you have explained).
- ⚖️ **THE FAMILY THIS BELONGS TO, AND IT IS WHY IT IS LAW RATHER THAN A HANDOFF FOOTNOTE: §23's *"a measurement's BASE is part of the measurement"*.** ⛔ **A count is not a fact on its own — it is a fact about a SET, and the set is the half that gets dropped in transit.** ⚠️ **Same shape as the `X.{h,cpp}` count trap in `(f)` and the stale-figure trap in the relayed-diagnosis law: the number was right when taken and misleading when used.**
- ✅ **The dirtiness sweep this was found in is the good pattern to copy — it was run at BOTH scopes and BOTH reported `dirty_count: 0`, which is a stronger statement than either alone.**

### TL-§5b ⛔⛔⛔ THE **SUITE TOTAL** IS THE SAME TRAP, AND UNDER PARALLEL LANES IT IS **STALE BEFORE THE INK DRIES** — ⛔ **NO SPEC MAY CARRY A SUITE ABSOLUTE AS AN EXPECTATION** (added 2026-09-03, from the `CARDBAR`/`STACK`/`W`/`F` four-lane wave)

> ### ⛔⛔ **MEASURED IN ⛔ ONE HOUR, ⛔ ON ⛔ ONE TREE: `331` → `338` → `344` → `353` → `361` → `370`. ⛔ FILE COUNT `26` → `29`. ⛔ NOT ONE OF THOSE NUMBERS WAS WRONG WHEN IT WAS TAKEN.**

- ⚠️ **`TL-§5` says a count is a fact about a ⛔ SET. ⭐ This clause adds the half that four concurrent lanes expose: ⛔ a count is also a fact about an ⛔ INSTANT — and when ⛔ four agents are landing test files into ⛔ one tree, ⛔ the instant expires in minutes.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND HERE IS THE PROOF THAT NO ABSOLUTE IS TRUSTWORTHY, ⛔ not even a carefully-taken one: ⛔ TWO handoffs written in the ⛔ SAME HOUR report the ⛔ SAME TOTAL `361` against ⛔ DIFFERENT FILE COUNTS (`TASK-827`: 361 across ⛔ 27 files; the board-review census: 361 across ⛔ 28).** ⇒ ⛔ **the disagreement is ⛔ not a miscount by either — it is ⛔ two different instants wearing one number.**
- ✅ **THE LAW, IN THREE PARTS:**
  1. ⛔ **A SPEC ⛔ NEVER STATES A SUITE ABSOLUTE AS A TARGET.** ⛔ Any number written into a task row is ⛔ historical context ⛔ ONLY, ⛔ is ⛔ STALE BY CONSTRUCTION, and ⛔ **must ⛔ never be reconciled *TO*.** ⭐ Where a row must mention one, it is ⛔ labelled *"stale anchor"* ⛔ in the same sentence.
  2. ✅ **THE INSTRUMENT IS A ⛔ FRESH CENSUS TAKEN AT RUN TIME**, ⛔ published ⛔ with its scope and its ⛔ FILE COUNT (`TL-§5`'s rule, applied here): `"370 across 29 files in Siegebound/Tests/*.cpp"`, ⛔ never a bare `370`.
     ⛔⛔⛔ **AND THE PATTERN IS ⛔ `^IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST`, ⛔ SCOPED TO `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/Tests/*.cpp` — ⛔ NEVER A BARE `^IMPLEMENT_`.** ⚠️ **MEASURED 2026-09-03: a bare `^IMPLEMENT_` ⛔ also catches `IMPLEMENT_PRIMARY_GAME_MODULE` in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/GitClaudeUnrealTest.cpp` ⇒ it reports ⛔ `371/30` where the truth is ⛔ `371/29`.** ⭐ **Verified at source: the tree contains ⛔ exactly TWO `^IMPLEMENT_` families — ⛔ that one module macro (⛔ 1 hit, ⛔ outside the Tests scope) and ⛔ `IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST`. ⛔ There is ⛔ no `IMPLEMENT_COMPLEX_AUTOMATION_TEST` in this project.**
     ⛔⛔ **WHY THIS ⛔ OFF-BY-ONE IS ⛔ WORSE THAN IT LOOKS: it lands in the ⛔ FILE COUNT, ⛔ not the test count** ⇒ ⭐ **it reads as *"there is one more test file than I can account for"* — ⛔ i.e. it ⛔ perfectly mimics ⛔ AND ⛔ perfectly masks the ⛔ one condition part 3 exists to detect (⛔ an undeclared file landing, or ⛔ a declared one going missing).** ~~⛔ **Scope ⛔ and pattern, ⛔ both — ⛔ either alone still counts it.**~~ ⛔⛔ **STRUCK 2026-09-03 — ⛔ THAT SENTENCE IS ⛔ FALSE. ⛔ SEE ⛔ 2c.**
  - ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **2c. THE ⛔ MECHANISM ⛔ CORRECTED — ⛔ IT IS A ⛔ SCOPE PROBLEM, ⛔ NOT A ⛔ NEEDLE PROBLEM, AND THE ⛔ MANAGER RELAYED IT ⛔ WRONG IN A ⛔ DOZEN DISPATCHES** (added 2026-09-03, from `qa/TASK-856.md` ⛔ **NIT-6(a)**; ⛔ **the manager names its own part in it**, as at `SC-§40`)
    > ⛔⛔ **MEASURED: over `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/Tests/*.cpp`, the ⛔ BARE `^IMPLEMENT_` needle and the ⛔ SCOPED `^IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST` needle return ⛔ THE SAME NUMBER — ⛔ `428 / 31`, ⛔ IDENTICALLY. ⛔ THE `429 / 32` READING APPEARS ⛔ ONLY WHEN THE ⛔ SCOPE IS WIDENED to `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/**/*.cpp`, ⛔ where the ⛔ 32nd file is `GitClaudeUnrealTest.cpp` carrying `IMPLEMENT_PRIMARY_GAME_MODULE`.**
    - ⛔ **THE TRAP IS ⛔ REAL. ⛔ THE DESCRIPTION OF IT WAS ⛔ WRONG.** ⛔ **It was written up — ⛔ by me — as *"the bare needle is blind"*, and ⛔ relayed in that form into ⛔ roughly a dozen dispatch prompts.** ⇒ ⛔ **Every agent who read it went looking for a ⛔ SECOND NEEDLE and would have ⛔ tested the wrong variable.**
    - ✅ **THE CORRECT RULE, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ SIMPLER THAN THE WRONG ONE: ⛔ THE ⛔ SCOPE IS THE ⛔ LOAD-BEARING HALF.** ⛔ **Pin the scope to `Siegebound/Tests/*.cpp` and the ⛔ module macro is ⛔ OUT OF REACH — ⛔ the pattern then ⛔ cannot matter.** ⛔ **Widen the scope and ⛔ NO pattern discipline saves you** — ⛔ you are now counting ⛔ a different set.
    - ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ WHY THIS CORRECTION IS WORTH A CLAUSE RATHER THAN A ⛔ SILENT EDIT: ⛔ a WRONG EXPLANATION OF A ⛔ REAL HAZARD IS ⛔ MORE DANGEROUS THAN NO EXPLANATION — ⛔ it ⛔ satisfies the reader's curiosity, ⛔ closes the question, and ⛔ points the next investigation at ⛔ the wrong variable.*** ⛔ **A reader who *"fixed the needle"* and ⛔ left the scope wide would have ⛔ believed themselves compliant and been ⛔ counting the module macro the whole time.**
    - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SPECIES: ⛔ this is `SC-§38` cl. 8 (⛔ the snippet is normative) applied to a ⛔ LAW'S OWN PROSE. ⛔ The ⛔ measurement was right; ⛔ the ⛔ sentence explaining it was not — ⛔ and ⛔ the sentence is what ⛔ travels.**
  - ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **2a. THE ⛔ ABSENCE OF A TRAP IS A ⛔ PROPERTY OF A ⛔ TREE, ⛔ NEVER A ⛔ REPEAL** (added 2026-09-03, from the ⛔ bare-`^IMPLEMENT_` trap firing ⛔ AGAIN one day after being measured ⛔ absent)
    > ⛔ **MEASURED: `TASK-814` ran the bare pattern on ⛔ its tree and it agreed with the scoped one ⇒ ⛔ *"the trap did not fire here"*. ⛔ ONE DAY LATER, on `e9df584`'s tree, the ⛔ bare pattern returns `427/32` against the ⛔ correct `426/31`. ⛔ THE TRAP IS ⛔ LIVE AGAIN.**
    - ⛔ **Nothing was ⛔ repealed and ⛔ nobody miscounted. ⛔ The trap's ⛔ visibility depends on ⛔ which files exist in ⛔ which directories at ⛔ which instant** — ⛔ i.e. it is the ⛔ SAME instant-dependence this whole section is about, ⛔ applied to the ⛔ INSTRUMENT instead of the ⛔ COUNT.
    - ⛔⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ A ROW MAY ⛔ NOT CITE A ⛔ PRIOR *"the trap did not fire"* MEASUREMENT AS ⛔ AUTHORITY TO USE THE ⛔ UNSAFE FORM.** ⛔ **The scoped pattern is ⛔ mandatory ⛔ unconditionally; ⛔ a clean bare reading is ⛔ a coincidence that ⛔ expires.** ⚠️ **Several board rows still carry the old measurement as though it ⛔ settled the matter — ⛔ it settles ⛔ one tree at ⛔ one instant and ⛔ nothing else.**
    - ⚖️ ***⛔ GENERALISED, because this is ⛔ NOT about a grep: ⛔ "I checked and the hazard was not present" is a ⛔ MEASUREMENT, ⛔ never a ⛔ GUARANTEE. ⛔ A hazard that is ⛔ currently inert is ⛔ still boarded, ⛔ still guarded and ⛔ still law*** — ⛔ the ⛔ same distinction `SC-§37`/`TASK-838(5c)` draws between ⛔ *"structurally impossible"* and ⛔ *"currently inert by coincidence of data"*, and ⛔ only the first is safety.
  3. ⭐⭐ **THE ⛔ ONLY TRUSTWORTHY FIGURE A TASK PRODUCES IS ITS ⛔ OWN DELTA (`+N`)** — ⛔ what ⛔ IT added. ✅ **Reconciliation is `fresh census == prior census + Σ(declared deltas)`.** ⛔⛔ **AND IF THAT EQUATION ⛔ DOES NOT BALANCE, THAT IS A ⛔ REPORTABLE FINDING — an ⛔ UNDECLARED test file landed — ⛔ NOT a discrepancy to be narrated away** (`SC-§29b`: ⛔ *a difference you can only narrate is not a difference you have explained*).
- ⚖️ **WHY THIS IS LAW AND ⛔ NOT A FOOTNOTE: the failure mode is ⛔ silent and ⛔ inverted.** ⛔ A build-master who ⛔ reconciles *to* a stale spec number will either ⛔ report a healthy tree as an anomaly, or — ⛔ far worse — ⛔ treat a ⛔ genuinely missing test file as ⛔ "the number the spec expected" and ⛔ pass it. ⭐ **The spec's number is the ⛔ one figure in the room that ⛔ cannot have been measured on the tree being gated.**
- ✅⭐⭐ **AND THE LAW ⛔ WORKED ON ITS ⛔ FIRST REAL USE — ⛔ RECORDED 2026-09-03, ⛔ BECAUSE A LAW THAT HAS ⛔ PAID OUT IS WORTH MORE THAN ONE THAT HAS ONLY BEEN WRITTEN.** ⛔ **The equation ⛔ BALANCED EXACTLY, ⛔ on the first fresh census after the wave: ⛔ `376 across 29 files` against git HEAD `bf0cd9e` at `306` ⇒ ⛔ `306 + 70 = 376`, from declared deltas `735` +10 · `807` +5 · `812` +10 · `813` +7 · `815` +5 · `819` +6 · `821` +1 · `827` +8 · `828` +9 · `837` +9.**
  ⭐ **AND IT ⛔ ANSWERED A QUESTION NO ABSOLUTE COULD: ⛔ `TASK-815`'s ⛔ unattributable *"+1"* was ⛔ `TASK-821`'s `SiegeControlsHelpTest.cpp` going ⛔ 13 → 14** — ⛔ **identified by ⛔ RECONCILIATION, ⛔ not by counting harder.** ⚖️ *⛔ The batch had been reporting ⛔ mutually inconsistent absolutes for ⛔ hours; ⛔ the deltas were ⛔ right the whole time. ⭐ **The signal was never missing — ⛔ the wrong instrument was being read.***

### TL-§5c ⛔⛔⛔ A CENSUS RECONCILES **WHAT EXISTS**; ⛔ ONLY AN **EXECUTION** RECONCILES **WHAT WORKS** — ⛔ **`N/N` IS A ⛔ PASS COUNT AND IT MAY ⛔ ONLY BE WRITTEN WHEN THE SUITE ⛔ ACTUALLY RAN** (added 2026-09-02, bought by the LADDER-REGRESSION diagnosis; sibling of `TL-§5b`, ⛔ not a restatement of it)

> ### ⛔⛔ **THE SUITE WENT ⛔ UNEXECUTED FOR ⛔ TWO COMMITS WHILE ⛔ THREE SEPARATE RECORDS CARRIED `306/306` AS A ⛔ PASS. ⛔ A TEST THAT HAD ⛔ NEVER ONCE RUN WAS ⛔ BORN RED INSIDE ONE OF THEM AND ⛔ NOBODY COULD HAVE KNOWN.**

- ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** `handoffs/TASK-805-buildmaster.md` §3 is headed *"✅ THE SUITE — 306"*. ⛔ **Every probe in it counts `IMPLEMENT_` macros ⛔ ON DISK — its own column headers read *"declared"* vs *"on disk"*.** ⛔ **A grep across that whole handoff for `Result={` · `Test Completed` · `RunTests` · `ExecCmds` returns ⛔ ZERO.** ⭐ **The immediately preceding `handoffs/TASK-802-buildmaster.md` (`8910c17`) has ⛔ all of it: `-ExecCmds="Automation RunTests Siegebound;Quit" -nullrhi -unattended`, `Result={Success}` ⛔ 301, `Result={Fail}` ⛔ 0.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`301/301` at `8910c17` is the ⛔ LAST GENUINELY EXECUTED NUMBER THIS PROJECT HAS ⛔ *[⛔ TRUE AS WRITTEN 2026-09-02; ⛔ SUPERSEDED BY LATER EXECUTIONS 2026-09-06 — ⛔ the ⛔ CURRENT ledger is `301 / 301` @ `8910c17` → `496 / 0` @ `c6bb376` (⭐ `TASK-1074`) → **`498 / 0` @ `eeb29c4`** (⭐ `TASK-1104`). ⛔ Annotated rather than rewritten: ⛔ a *"last executed"* claim is ⛔ TRUE AT AN INSTANT and ⛔ every reader must be able to see ⛔ which instant. ⛔ Full correction + the propagation duty: ⭐ `SC-§95` cl. 2.]*. ⛔ `306/306` was ⛔ never measured — the second `306` was ⛔ inferred from the first.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHAT IT COST, AND IT IS ⛔ NOT HYPOTHETICAL:** `bf0cd9e` ⛔ introduced `Siegebound.LadderClimb.ClimbDirectionIsByteIdenticalAndItsThreeOtherReadersStillReadTheLine` ⛔ ALREADY RED (`git log -S"LevelButOffLine = PointOffLine"` puts the failing line's birth in ⛔ that same commit). ⛔ **It sat red through every board row built on top of it.** ⭐ **`TASK-824`'s build is the ⛔ FIRST SUITE EXECUTION SINCE `8910c17`, and it is ⛔ the only reason the row was ever noticed.** ⚖️ *⛔ The paradox that opened the investigation — *"an unchanged test testing unchanged code started failing"* — ⛔ dissolves entirely here: ⛔ nothing started. ⛔ Something finally ⛔ ran.*
- ✅ **THE LAW, IN ⛔ FIVE PARTS** (⛔ the ⛔ FIFTH added 2026-09-02, ⛔ bought by `qa/TASK-865.md` — ⛔ **this law fired ⛔ ONE LEVEL ABOVE where it was aimed**)**:**
  1. ⛔⛔ **A SUITE FIGURE IS WRITTEN `N declared` ⛔ UNLESS IT IS BACKED BY A REAL `Automation RunTests` INVOCATION.** ⛔ **`N/N` is a ⛔ PASS COUNT and it is ⛔ RESERVED: it may be written ⛔ only when a `Result={Success}` / `Result={Fail}` pair from ⛔ that run is in hand.** ⭐ **`376 declared, 375 passing, 1 failing` is an ⛔ honest line; ⛔ `376/376` from a macro count is ⛔ not.**
  2. ⛔ **ANY HANDOFF, COMMIT MESSAGE OR BOARD ROW CLAIMING A SUITE TOTAL ⛔ CITES THE EXECUTION — ⛔ the `ExecCmds` line ⛔ and ⛔ both `Result={}` counts — ⛔ never a macro count.** ⛔ **A census table placed under a `Result: Succeeded` ⛔ BUILD line is ⛔ how this was read as a pass; ⛔ do not put them in the same section without labelling which is which.**
  3. ⛔⛔ **EVERY BUILD-MASTER ROW THAT GATES A ⛔ COMMIT ⛔ MUST EXECUTE THE SUITE. ⛔ A DECLARED COUNT ⛔ CANNOT SATISFY A COMMIT GATE.** ✅ **A census-only build is ⛔ still legitimate** — ⭐ `TASK-824` was ⛔ exactly that and was ⛔ right to be — **but it may ⛔ not report a pass count and it may ⛔ not gate a commit.** ⇒ ⛔ **the two duties are ⛔ separately owed and ⛔ neither substitutes for the other.**
  4. ⛔ **THE RECORDS THAT CARRIED THE WRONG NUMBER ARE ⛔ ANNOTATED, ⛔ NEVER SILENTLY CORRECTED** — the commit message is immutable, and a reader who remembers `306/306` must find its ⛔ resolution rather than ⛔ re-derive it (the same discipline `SLACK.md` uses for its struck-through caveat).
  5. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — ⛔ A ⛔ GATE MAY ⛔ NOT INHERIT AN ⛔ EXIT CODE IT DID ⛔ NOT WATCH; AND WHEN IT ⛔ CANNOT RUN, THE ⛔ DUTY ⛔ TRANSFERS DOWNSTREAM ⛔ BY NAME** (added 2026-09-02; ⛔ bought and ⛔ SELF-DECLARED by `qa/TASK-865.md`, ⛔ which had ⛔ no shell)
     - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** `TASK-865` was handed a task whose evidence line read *"`--check` EXIT 0 · `test_concept_guard.py` 46/46 · 49/49 corpus"*. ⛔ **It had ⛔ NO SHELL — its tool set was ⛔ read/grep/glob/write + Slack — and it said so ⛔ FIRST, ⛔ PLAINLY, ⛔ ABOVE the verdict, in its own ⛔ §0:** ⭐ ***"⛔ Reporting a green I did not watch would be ⛔ this exact defect ⛔ ONE LEVEL UP."***
     - ⭐⭐ **WHY THIS IS ⛔ NOT MERELY THE LAW RESTATED: it was ⛔ written for a ⛔ BUILD-MASTER relaying a ⛔ SUITE COUNT. ⛔ Here it caught a ⛔ GATE about to relay an ⛔ AUTHOR'S EXIT CODE — and a ⛔ GATE is ⛔ precisely the party everyone downstream treats as ⛔ having already checked.** ⇒ ⛔ **`SC-§40`'s two channels (⛔ a QA verdict, ⛔ a manager relay) ⛔ MEET this law's verb here: ⛔ a gate that ⛔ relays an execution is a ⛔ CITATION wearing a ⛔ MEASUREMENT'S CLOTHES.**
     - ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ three parts:**
       ⛔ **(a) A gate that ⛔ CANNOT EXECUTE ⛔ SAYS SO ⛔ BEFORE ITS VERDICT, ⛔ never in a footnote**, and ⛔ splits its report into ⛔ **DECLARED** (⛔ the author's, ⛔ unwatched) vs ⛔ **EXECUTED** (⛔ its own).
       ⛔ **(b) It ⛔ RE-DERIVES everything ⛔ statically checkable ⛔ BY HAND rather than ⛔ relaying it.** ⭐ **`TASK-865` re-derived ⛔ 46 assertions, a ⛔ 49-file corpus (25+24), a ⛔ SINGLE `os.replace` call site ⛔ and its one caller's guard clause, an ⛔ exit-code census across ⛔ TWO sibling tools, and a ⛔ fixture's ⛔ mean luminance from the ⛔ generator's arithmetic. ⛔ EVERY ONE REPRODUCED — ⛔ including the two easiest to fudge.** ⇒ ⛔ **a shell-less gate is ⛔ NOT a weak gate; ⛔ it is a gate with a ⛔ NAMED HOLE, and a named hole is ⛔ fillable.**
       ⛔ **(c) ⛔⛔ THE DUTY ⛔ TRANSFERS ⛔ BY NAME, ⛔ ONTO A ⛔ ROW, ⛔ WITH THE ⛔ COMMANDS AND ⛔ EXPECTED CODES ⛔ WRITTEN OUT.** ⛔ **The ⛔ next party who ⛔ CAN run it ⛔ OWES it, and the ⛔ COMMIT is the ⛔ DEADLINE** — ⛔ a declared green may ⛔ travel through a ⛔ gate, but it may ⛔ NOT travel through a ⛔ COMMIT. ⇒ ⛔ **cl. 3 ⛔ generalised past the C++ suite to ⛔ ANY tool's own test.**
     - ⚠️ **AND THE ⛔ READING TRAP THAT RIDES WITH THE HANDOFF, ⛔ named ⛔ on the row that inherits the duty: ⛔ A HARNESS'S ⛔ VOID CODE IS ⛔ NOT A FAILURE.** ⛔ **`test_concept_guard.py` exiting ⛔ **5** means ⛔ THE CORPUS WAS NOT FOUND (⛔ 25 of the 49 concepts are ⛔ gitignored ⇒ ⛔ a clean checkout sees ⛔ 24) — ⛔ routing that back as a ⛔ guard failure would ⛔ burn a QA loop on a ⛔ machine-local path.** ⭐ **A gate that ⛔ hands off an EXECUTION ⛔ hands off its ⛔ EXIT-CODE TAXONOMY with it.**
     - ⚖️ ***⛔ THE DURABLE SENTENCE: ⛔ THE PIPELINE'S GREENS ARE ⛔ ONLY AS GOOD AS THE ⛔ LAST AGENT WHO ⛔ REFUSED TO PASS ONE ALONG. ⛔ `TL-§5c` EXISTS BECAUSE ⛔ THREE RECORDS DID NOT REFUSE; ⛔ THIS CLAUSE EXISTS BECAUSE ⛔ ONE FINALLY DID.***
- ⚖️ **WHERE THIS SITS AGAINST THE TWO LAWS IT LOOKS LIKE, ⛔ because both were live and ⛔ neither could have caught it:**
  - ⛔ **`TL-§5b` proved the count ⛔ RECONCILES. ⛔ It could not and ⛔ did not prove ⛔ anything RAN.** ⭐ It balanced ⛔ perfectly on its first use (`306 + 70 = 376`) ⛔ while one of those 376 had ⛔ never executed. ⇒ ⛔ **`TL-§5b` is about the ⛔ SET; ⛔ this clause is about the ⛔ VERB.**
  - ⭐ **`SC-§39`'s positive control, applied here: the ⛔ positive control for *"the suite passed"* is a `Result={Fail}` line ⛔ reading `0`.** ⛔ **An ⛔ ABSENT `Result` line is an ⛔ UNREADABLE INSTRUMENT, ⛔ not a green suite** — ⛔ and it lies in the ⛔ reassuring direction, ⛔ exactly like the empty-log-reads-as-a-zero-offset trap.
- ⚠️ **THE FAILURE MODE IS ⛔ SILENT, ⛔ CUMULATIVE AND ⛔ CONFIDENCE-BUILDING:** ⛔ each unexecuted commit ⛔ inherits the previous one's number, so the record gets ⛔ more emphatic while the evidence gets ⛔ older. ⭐ **Two commits was enough to make a born-red test ⛔ look like a regression to ⛔ three agents.**
- ✅⭐ **AND THE ⛔ GOOD PRACTICE THIS LAW ⛔ SHOULD ALSO PROTECT, ⛔ recorded 2026-09-03 from `TASK-811` so it is ⛔ not mistaken for a skipped step: ⛔ A ⛔ NO-OP BUILD IS A ⛔ LEGITIMATE RESULT ⛔ IF IT IS ⛔ REPORTED AS ONE ⛔ WITH ITS BASIS.**
  - ⛔ **`TASK-811`'s compile returned *"Target is up to date"*, ⛔ 0 actions — and it ⛔ SAID SO ⛔ RATHER THAN DRESSING IT UP.** ✅ **It was ⛔ SUFFICIENT ⛔ only because two ⛔ named facts backed it: ⛔ `d287102`'s build had compiled ⛔ all four of its translation units ⛔ INDIVIDUALLY, and ⛔ `find -newer` on the source tree returned ⛔ EMPTY.**
  - ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE TEST: a no-op build is ⛔ REPORTABLE when the row can name ⛔ WHICH EARLIER BUILD COMPILED THE ⛔ SAME BYTES and ⛔ SHOW that nothing changed since. ⛔ Without both, *"up to date"* is ⛔ an unexecuted gate wearing a green shirt** (⛔ this section's own defect, ⛔ one layer down).
  - ⚖️ *⭐ Note what it did ⛔ NOT do: it did ⛔ not report a compile it did not perform, and it did ⛔ not treat the no-op as a reason to ⛔ skip the ⛔ SUITE — which is ⛔ separately owed by cl. 3 and ⛔ was executed.*

### TL-§5d ⛔⛔⛔ A GREEN SUITE MEASURES THE **WORKING TREE** — ⛔ **AN UNTRACKED TEST FILE IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO `HEAD`, AND ⛔ NOTHING WILL EVER GO RED TO SAY SO** (added 2026-09-03, from `TASK-814`'s orphan measurement; sibling of `TL-§5b`/`§5c`, ⛔ not a restatement)

> ### ⛔⛔ **`Tests/SiegeCardHandKeyLabelTest.cpp` WAS ⛔ GREEN IN THE EXECUTED RUN AND ⛔ UNTRACKED IN GIT. ⛔ HAD IT NOT BEEN SWEPT IN, THE SUITE WOULD HAVE GONE ON REPORTING ⛔ 426 FROM A ⛔ WORKING TREE WHILE `HEAD` CARRIED ⛔ 421 — ⛔ FOREVER, ⛔ WITH NOTHING FAILING.**

- ⛔ **THE MECHANISM, ⛔ and it is ⛔ trivial once stated: ⛔ the automation runner compiles and runs ⛔ WHAT IS ON DISK. ⛔ Git tracks ⛔ what has been `add`-ed.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the two sets are ⛔ NOT the same set, and ⛔ EVERY instrument this project trusts — the ⛔ executed `Result={}` pair, the ⛔ fresh `^IMPLEMENT_` census, the ⛔ reconciliation equation — ⛔ measures the ⛔ FIRST one.**
- ⛔⛔ **WHY IT IS ⛔ UNDETECTABLE FROM INSIDE THE SUITE: ⛔ A SUITE ⛔ CANNOT MISS A FILE THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER COMMITTED.** ⛔ **The loss is ⛔ silent at the moment it happens and ⛔ silent forever after — ⛔ the count simply ⛔ steps down at the next clean checkout, on a machine nobody is watching, ⛔ with no diff to blame.**
- ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SECOND-ORDER DAMAGE IS ⛔ WORSE THAN THE LOST TESTS: ⛔ `TL-§5b`'s reconciliation equation would ⛔ CONTINUE TO BALANCE** — ⛔ the working-tree census and the working-tree run ⛔ agree with each other ⛔ perfectly ⇒ ⛔ **two instruments corroborating, ⛔ both blind to `HEAD`.** ⚖️ *⛔ Exactly `SC-§39`'s shape: ⛔ agreement between two tools rules out an ⛔ unreadable instrument, ⛔ never a ⛔ shared blind spot.*
- ✅ **THE LAW, ⛔ THREE PARTS:**
  1. ⛔⛔ **EVERY NEW TEST FILE ⛔ MUST HAVE A ⛔ NAMED COMMIT HOST ⛔ ON THE BOARD ⛔ BEFORE ITS LANE'S GATE PASSES.** ⛔ **A task that declares `+N` and does ⛔ not name the row that ⛔ commits the file has declared ⛔ half a deliverable.** ⛔ **The ⛔ manager owns this: ⛔ a test file with no host is a ⛔ DECOMPOSITION DEFECT, ⛔ not a build-master's oversight.**
  2. ⛔ **`§25b` CLAUSE R'S RECONCILIATION IS THE ⛔ DETECTOR, and it is owed by ⛔ every commit row:** ⛔ `git status --porcelain` against the ⛔ UNION of all pending pathspecs, ⛔ naming every file in ⛔ neither.
  3. ⛔ **A TEST FILE ⛔ TRAVELS WITH THE ⛔ CODE IT COVERS, ⛔ in the ⛔ SAME COMMIT** — ⛔ not because it is tidy, but because ⛔ that rule is what ⛔ produces a host automatically. ⭐ **Two rulings on ⛔ one day rest on it: ⛔ `SiegeCardHandKeyLabelTest` → `TASK-811` (beside `CardHandWidget`) and ⛔ `SiegeAcquisitionFunnelTest` → `TASK-835` (beside `SiegeCombatStatics`).**
- ~~⚠️⛔ **STILL LIVE AS THIS IS WRITTEN: ⛔ `Tests/SiegeAssistantSelectionTest.cpp` is ⛔ in the executed 426 and ⛔ absent from `HEAD`. ⛔ `TASK-906` is the ⛔ only thing between it and a silent loss.**~~
- ⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐ **THAT SENTENCE WAS ⛔ FALSE WHEN IT WAS WRITTEN, AND IT IS ⛔ RETAINED STRUCK RATHER THAN DELETED BECAUSE ⛔ THIS LAW WAS THE ⛔ CHANNEL THAT CARRIED THE ERROR** (corrected 2026-09-03, ⛔ measured by `TASK-919`: `git ls-files --error-unmatch` returns ⛔ **exit 0** ⇒ the file is ⛔ **TRACKED, in `HEAD` since `4a03e03`**; `HEAD` **38** macros vs worktree **39** ⇒ the delta is ⛔ **`+1`**, ⛔ never `+39`).
  - ⛔ **WHERE THE FALSE STATE CAME FROM: ⛔ conflation with `Tests/SiegeFogClampTest.cpp`, which genuinely ⛔ WAS new and untracked at `+8`.** ⛔ **`handoffs/TASK-814-buildmaster.md:68` — the ⛔ last agent in the chain ⛔ WITH Git — recorded `38→39 | +1` and was ⛔ RIGHT.**
  - ⛔⛔ **AND THE DAMAGE PATH IS THE POINT: ⛔ `qa/TASK-889.md` WARN-2 cited ⛔ THIS BULLET (and `TASKBOARD.md:14412`) as *"two dated project records"* to tell the programmer his `git show HEAD:` provenance *"cannot be true"* ⇒ ⛔ he is ⛔ FENCED FROM GIT, so he ⛔ RETRACTED A TRUE CLAIM, and the manager relayed `+39` into ⛔ FOUR dispatches as fact.** ⇒ ⛔ **See ⭐ `SC-§40` cl. 15 — ⛔ the retraction clause this event bought.**
  - ⚖️ ***⛔ A TRACKING STATE IN A LAW IS A ⛔ CITATION, ⛔ EXACTLY AS `SC-§40` cl. 9 SAYS OF A ⛔ COUNT — ⛔ and it is ⛔ WORSE, because ⛔ nobody expects `CONVENTIONS.md` to be the ⛔ stale one.*** ⛔ **This section's own clause 2 (`§25b` cl. R is the detector) is ⛔ unaffected and ⛔ still correct; ⛔ what was wrong was the ⛔ EXAMPLE, ⛔ not the mechanism.** ⭐ **The law's ⛔ substance survives intact: ⛔ `SiegeCardHandKeyLabelTest` and ⛔ `SiegeFogClampTest` were ⛔ both real orphans and ⛔ both were caught by it.**
- ⚖️ **WHERE THIS SITS AGAINST ITS TWO SIBLINGS, ⛔ because all three were live and ⛔ none of the other two could catch it:** ⛔ **`TL-§5b` is about the ⛔ SET** (⛔ does the count reconcile) · ⛔ **`TL-§5c` is about the ⛔ VERB** (⛔ did anything run) · ⛔⛔ **`TL-§5d` is about the ⛔ SUBJECT: ⛔ WHICH TREE DID IT RUN ON, and ⛔ is that the tree we are shipping?**

### TL-§5e ⛔⛔⛔ **A COMMIT HOST IS A ⛔ NAMED DISPATCH, ⛔ NOT A HOPE — A ⛔ BOARD ROW OR THE BUILD-MASTER'S ⛔ OWN ROUTING-RULE-5 DISPATCH RECORDED IN ITS HANDOFF; ⛔ *"THE NEXT COMMIT-GATE BUILD"* IS ⛔ NOT A HOST, AND ⛔ RIDERS CANNOT ⛔ SELF-HOST** (added 2026-09-03, from the ⛔ 13-riders-against-1-blocked-host escalation; ⛔ the ⛔ FOURTH sibling of `TL-§5b`/`§5c`/`§5d`, ⛔ not a restatement of any of them; heading and cl. 1 rewritten 2026-10-04)

> ### ⛔⛔ **`TL-§5d` cl. 1 ⛔ ALREADY REQUIRED A *"NAMED COMMIT HOST ON THE BOARD"*. ⛔ THIRTEEN RIDERS ⛔ SATISFIED IT ⛔ IN LETTER — ⛔ EVERY ONE OF THEM NAMED *"THE NEXT COMMIT-GATE BUILD"*, WHICH IS A ⛔ SENTENCE, ⛔ NOT A ROW. ⛔ THE ⛔ ONE ROW ANYBODY COULD POINT AT (`TASK-843`) WAS ⛔ BLOCKED BEHIND A HUMAN RULING WITH ⛔ NO DEADLINE.**

1. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 1 — WHAT ⛔ COUNTS AS A HOST, ⛔ EXHAUSTIVELY (rewritten 2026-10-04).** A host is a **NAMED DISPATCH of the build-master**, in exactly one of two forms:
   - **(a) A BOARDED HOST ROW** with an ID, assignee `build-master`, whose `blocked-by` is the ⛔ MINIMUM SET that makes it dispatchable — its own compile duties plus the PASS reports of the riders it ⛔ actually takes. A dedicated host row is boarded ⛔ ONLY when the commit carries decisions that need a spec of their own: a `.uproject` change, a law release, a pathspec whose exclusions must be ruled, a cross-lane batch whose roster (cl. 3) and `TAIL-TAKER:` (cl. 7d) must be written down, a docs-only protective commit (`TASK-1322`'s shape).
   - **(b) THE BUILD-MASTER'S OWN 5a / 5c DISPATCH under CLAUDE.md routing rule 5 for the rows it ships**, recorded in that dispatch's `handoffs/TASK-###-buildmaster.md` (named for the lead row it ships) — ⛔ **no row of its own.** The manager names it on the code row at boarding (`HOST: 5c dispatch under routing rule 5, form (b)`), which is what makes it a NAMED dispatch rather than a hope. For cl. 5's count, a code row carrying that line counts as its own unblocked host. For cl. 7d, a form-(b) host carries `TAIL-TAKER:` value (ii) `STANDING` by construction — its tail is the two files every host mints (its own handoff and the board's `status:` flips), and cl. 7a's standing sweep binds the next host without being copied onto a row.
   - **In BOTH forms the commit hash lands on the `status:` line of EVERY row the commit ships, in the same action as the commit** (write-discipline rule 8, `SC-§103`, unchanged) — a form-(b) host that commits and flips only the lead row has left the board asserting that the passengers never shipped.
   - ⛔ **None of the following is a host:** ⛔ *"the next commit-gate build"* · ⛔ *"whichever integration runs first"* · ⛔ **another rider** · ⛔ **a row blocked on a ruling with no date** (`SC-§43` cl. 2(b) already refuses that one — this clause says it also cannot be pointed at) · ⛔ **a dispatch nobody has issued or named**. ⚖️ A host is a NAMED dispatch, not a hope; form (b) is named by the routing rule and by the row it hosts, which is why it needs no row of its own — and why *"someone will commit it"* still is not one.
   _Changelog: 2026-10-04 — cl. 1 rewritten: a host may be the build-master's own routing-rule-5 dispatch recorded in its handoff (form (b)), not only a board row; dedicated host rows are boarded only for commits carrying spec-worthy decisions; the hash still lands on every shipped row's `status:` line; cl. 5's count and cl. 7d's `TAIL-TAKER:` are read for form (b) as stated above, Jonathan's rule-change instruction, orchestrator-implemented._
2. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 2 — ⛔ RIDERS CANNOT SELF-HOST, AND ⛔ THAT IS AN ⛔ ARITHMETIC PROPERTY, ⛔ NOT A STYLE PREFERENCE.** ⛔ **Every rider row carries *"⛔ NO COMPILE OF ITS OWN"*. ⇒ ⛔ `N` riders and ⛔ ZERO hosts commits ⛔ ZERO files, ⛔ however many gates passed.** ⛔ **A fleet of finished, gated diffs with no host is ⛔ exactly `TL-§5d`'s shape one level up: ⛔ work that exists in the tree, is ⛔ invisible to `HEAD`, and goes ⛔ red nowhere.**
   - ⚖️ *⛔ The riders were ⛔ individually correct. ⛔ `TASK-855`'s shape ⛔ worked — it was ⛔ adopted by `TASK-814` and ⛔ closed on `d287102`. ⛔ The defect is ⛔ POPULATION, ⛔ not design: the pattern ⛔ presumes a supply of feature integrations, and ⛔ nobody counted when the supply stopped.*
3. ⛔⛔⭐ **CLAUSE 3 — A HOST'S ⛔ MEMBERSHIP IS MEASURED AT ⛔ DISPATCH; A HOST'S ⛔ BLOCKERS ARE FIXED AT ⛔ BOARDING. ⛔ THEY ARE ⛔ NOT THE SAME LIST AND ⛔ CONFLATING THEM RE-CREATES THE DEFECT.**
   - ⛔ **Board the host against the ⛔ riders that are ⛔ ALREADY gate-passed.** ⛔ **Then give it a ⛔ CLOSED CANDIDATE LIST it may ⛔ additionally adopt ⛔ if — and ⛔ only if — that candidate's ⛔ PASS report is ⛔ in hand at its ⛔ own dispatch instant.**
   - ⛔⛔ **A host may ⛔ NEVER inherit a candidate's blockers.** ⛔ **The moment a host waits for its ⛔ slowest candidate, it has become the thing it was boarded to replace.**
   - ⭐ **This is `SC-§40` cl. 11 applied ⛔ one level up: ⛔ a rider's ⛔ pathspec is written when the ⛔ PLAN is made and consumed when the ⛔ WORK is done — ⛔ so is a host's ⛔ ROSTER.**
4. ⛔⛔ **CLAUSE 4 — GROUP HOSTS BY ⛔ COMPILE CLASS, ⛔ NOT BY FEATURE.** ⛔ **A `Tools/**/*.py` rider moves ⛔ no test count, ⛔ cannot break a build, and ⛔ needs no `QUIET-MODULE` window; a `Source/**` rider needs ⛔ ONE compile and ⛔ ONE executed suite.** ⇒ ⛔ **mixing them makes Python work wait on a UBT window it ⛔ does not need, which is ⛔ how a Python rider ends up hostage to a C++ gate.** ⛔ **Two hosts is ⛔ cheaper than one blocked host.**
5. ✅⭐ **CLAUSE 5 — THE ⛔ DETECTOR, ⛔ MANAGER-OWNED, ⛔ RUN AT ⛔ BOARDING TIME** (⛔ the same instant `§25b` cl. R's boarding-time bullet runs; ⛔ ruled the ⛔ same day, ⛔ for the ⛔ same reason): ⛔ **count the open rider rows against the ⛔ unblocked host rows.** ⛔ **Any ratio of the form `N : 0`, or `N : 1-blocked`, is a ⛔ STOP — ⛔ board the host in the ⛔ SAME SITTING as the rider, ⛔ never later.**
   - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ REASON IT IS A ⛔ COUNT RATHER THAN A JUDGEMENT: ⛔ NO SINGLE RIDER LOOKS WRONG.** ⛔ **Each one names a plausible host, each one is small, each one is gated. ⛔ The defect is ⛔ only visible in ⛔ aggregate** ⇒ ⛔ **an inspection that reads rows ⛔ one at a time ⛔ CANNOT find it, ⛔ and thirteen agents arriving one at a time would each have found ⛔ nothing wrong with their own row.**
