6. ⚖️ **CLAUSE 6 — SCOPE, ⛔ AND WHAT THIS DOES ⛔ NOT REPEAL.** ⛔ **The rider pattern ⛔ STANDS: a one-line test fix ⛔ still does not justify re-opening a UBT window mid-wave** (⛔ `TASK-824` collected ⛔ 15 errors from ⛔ two other tasks' files doing exactly that). ⭐ **What changes is that the ⛔ SAVING is only real if ⛔ somebody boarded the ride.** ⛔ **`TL-§5d` cl. 1 is ⛔ amended, ⛔ not replaced: ⛔ *"a named commit host"* now means ⛔ a host that satisfies ⛔ clause 1 of this section.**

7. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 7 — A BUILD-MASTER HANDOFF ⛔ CANNOT SELF-HOST, SO ⛔ EVERY HOST ROW MINTS ⛔ EXACTLY ONE NEW ORPHAN ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION. ⛔ THE ANSWER IS A ⛔ SWEEPER ROW, ⛔ NOT MORE DILIGENCE** (added 2026-09-03, from `TASK-926` §5(e); ⛔ **measured, ⛔ not theorised** — `TASK-918`'s, `TASK-922`'s and `TASK-926`'s handoffs are ⛔ ALL THREE untracked for ⛔ this reason and ⛔ no other).
   - ⛔ **THE ARITHMETIC, and it is ⛔ not negotiable by care: ⛔ a build-master handoff ⛔ RECORDS ITS OWN COMMIT HASH. ⛔ A file cannot contain the hash of the commit that contains it, and ⛔ amending only moves the hash again.** ⇒ ⛔ **the handoff is ⛔ BORN OUTSIDE its own commit, ⛔ every time, ⛔ for everyone.**
   - ⛔⛔ **THEREFORE ⛔ DO NOT ASK THE NEXT HOST TO TRY HARDER — ⛔ it is ⛔ STRUCTURALLY INCAPABLE. ⛔ Three hosts in one night each did the ⛔ right thing and each ⛔ left one behind.** ⚖️ *⛔ A convention that ⛔ GUARANTEES a leftover needs a ⛔ SWEEPER, ⛔ not a reminder.*
   - ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ MANAGER-OWNED, ⛔ run at the ⛔ SAME boarding instant as cl. 5's count: ⛔ A BATCH OF COMMITS GETS A ⛔ TRAILING DOC-HOST ROW** (`build-master` · ⛔ text only · ⛔ no compile · ⛔ no suite · ⛔ no `QUIET-MODULE` slot · ⛔ no QA gate owed) **that takes the ⛔ PIPELINE DOCUMENTS the batch produced.** ⛔ **It runs ⛔ LAST.**
   - ⛔⛔ **ITS PATHSPEC IS ⛔ DERIVED AT ITS OWN DISPATCH INSTANT — ⛔ THE ⛔ ONE AUTHORIZED EXCEPTION TO `§25b` cl. R's ⛔ HAND-NAMED PATHSPEC, and the ⛔ exception is ⛔ granted ⛔ IN THE ROW, ⛔ never assumed at the terminal.** ⛔ **Reason: a doc list is ⛔ STALE THE MOMENT IT IS TYPED — the ⛔ running lane is ⛔ writing new handoffs while the row is being read.** ⛔ **The ⛔ RESOLVED list is ⛔ PRINTED with a ⛔ COUNT and with ⛔ EVERY EXCLUSION NAMED, ⛔ BEFORE staging.**
   - ⛔ **THE ⛔ STANDING TAIL, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ NOT A DEFECT: the doc-host's ⛔ OWN handoff falls outside its ⛔ own commit by this ⛔ same clause.** ⇒ ⛔ **the class is ⛔ BOUNDED AT ONE, ⛔ not zero, and the ⛔ NEXT doc-host takes it.** ⛔⛔ **A row that tries to ⛔ INCLUDE ITSELF is ⛔ WRONG, ⛔ not thorough.**

   - 🚨✅⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CL. 7d — *"THE ⛔ NEXT DOC-HOST TAKES IT"* ⛔ PRESUMES A ⛔ NEXT DOC-HOST ⛔ EXISTS, AND ⛔ NOTHING ANYWHERE ⛔ GUARANTEED ONE. ⛔ THE TAIL'S ⛔ OWNER IS NOW A ⛔ MANDATORY FIELD ON ⛔ EVERY HOST ROW, ⛔ FILLED AT ⛔ BOARDING.** (added 2026-09-20 by the manager on ⭐ `TASK-1350` · marker `TL-5E-7D-NAME-THE-TAIL-TAKER-AT-BOARDING`)
     - 🚨⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT, ⛔ AND IT IS WHY THIS IS ⛔ STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN BAD LUCK: ⛔ FOUR ⛔ CONSECUTIVE COMMIT HOSTS — ⭐ `TASK-1340` (`d9a98d1`) · ⭐ `TASK-1343` (`4e388d6`) · ⭐ `TASK-1347` (`ce4947d`) · ⭐ `TASK-1351` (`6052dca`) — ⛔ EACH MINTED THE ⛔ SAME TWO-FILE TAIL** (⛔ its own `done` + hash `status:` line, and its own handoff's hash section), ⛔ **and ⛔ EACH ONE NEEDED THE ⛔ NEXT ROW TO ⛔ ALREADY EXIST before its own record could land.** ⛔ **Every one of the four behaved ⛔ correctly.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ FOUR CORRECT HOSTS IN A ROW PRODUCED FOUR TAILS. ⛔ THAT IS A ⛔ PROPERTY OF THE CLAUSE, ⛔ NOT OF THE AGENTS.***
     - ⛔ **AND ⭐ `TASK-1347` PROVED THE ⛔ OBVIOUS FIX IS ⛔ WRONG: it ⛔ REFUSED to invent a second commit to swallow its own ledger, ⛔ and the refusal was ⛔ CORRECT — ⛔ the regress ⛔ DOES NOT TERMINATE, because the ⛔ ledger commit's hash ⛔ cannot be inside itself either.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE REGRESS IS ⛔ NOT SOLVABLE BY ⛔ COMMITTING HARDER. ⛔ It is solvable ⛔ ONLY by ⛔ OWNERSHIP.**
     - ✅🚨 **THE RULE — ⛔ A ⛔ MANDATORY `TAIL-TAKER:` ON ⛔ EVERY COMMIT HOST ROW, ⛔ WRITTEN BY THE ⛔ MANAGER AT ⛔ BOARDING, ⛔ ALONGSIDE cl. 5's COUNT AND cl. 7's SWEEPER RULE.** ⛔ **It takes ⛔ EXACTLY ONE of ⛔ TWO values, and ⛔ *"unstated"* is ⛔ NOT one of them:**
       - ⛔ **(i) A ⛔ BOARDED ROW ID** that will run after this host — ⛔ subject to cl. 1 (⛔ it must be a ⛔ ROW, ⛔ never a sentence) and cl. 7a-vi (⛔ it must be ⛔ DISPATCHABLE; ⛔ a host blocked on an ⛔ UNDATED HUMAN DECISION is ⛔ NOT a valid taker and its tail ⛔ reverts to ⛔ ORPHAN).
       - ⛔ **(ii) THE ⛔ LITERAL `STANDING — next commit host under TL-§5e cl. 7a`** — ⛔ **PERMITTED ⛔ ONLY WHEN the tail is ⛔ BOUNDED and ⛔ EVERY FILE IN IT IS ⛔ NAMED BY PATH ON THE ROW.** ⛔ **This is the value that ⛔ TERMINATES THE REGRESS**, and it is ⛔ honest because cl. 7a's ⛔ STANDING SWEEP already ⛔ binds every future host ⛔ without being copied onto its row ⇒ ⛔ **the taker is ⛔ whoever commits next, ⛔ and there is ⛔ always a next.**
       - 🚨✅⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **(iii) ⛔ THE ⛔ GRANULARITY OF *"EVERY FILE IN IT IS NAMED BY PATH"* — ⛔ RULED 2026-09-20 by the manager on ⭐ `TASK-1353`, ⛔ ON THE ⛔ EXPLICIT ASK OF THE ⛔ FIRST HOST TO CARRY THIS FIELD. ⛔ THE BOUND IS AT ⛔ FILE GRANULARITY. ⛔ ITEMS INSIDE A NAMED FILE ARE ⛔ NOT ENUMERATED.** (marker `TL-5E-7D-III-THE-BOUND-IS-AT-FILE-GRANULARITY`)
         - ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT THAT FORCED THE QUESTION: ⭐ `TASK-1355`'s `TAIL-TAKER:` enumerated ⛔ TWO items; the ⛔ honest tail was ⛔ FOUR items ⛔ ACROSS THE ⛔ SAME TWO FILES — ⛔ because that row was ⛔ separately authorised to flip ⭐ `TASK-1348`'s and ⭐ `TASK-1349`'s `status:` lines, and a flip that ⛔ CITES A COMMIT HASH ⛔ cannot predate it either.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THE FILE BOUND ⛔ HELD. ⛔ THE ITEM ENUMERATION ⛔ DID NOT. ⛔ The host ⛔ noticed, ⛔ reported it, and ⛔ asked — ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly right and is ⛔ why this sub-clause exists rather than a reprimand.**
         - ✅⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ THREE REASONS, ⛔ EACH SUFFICIENT: ⛔ **(1) ⛔ THE TAKER ACTS BY ⛔ PATHSPEC.** ⛔ `git add -- <path>` takes a ⛔ FILE, ⛔ never an item ⇒ ⛔ an item list is ⛔ UNACTIONABLE BY THE ⛔ VERY MECHANISM THE FIELD EXISTS TO DRIVE. ⛔ **(2) ⛔ THE FIELD'S JOB IS TO LET THE NEXT HOST ⛔ DERIVE THE SET AT ⛔ ITS OWN INSTANT** (⭐ `SC-§133` · cl. 7a's standing sweep) — ⛔ a ⛔ FILE list does that; ⛔ an ⛔ ITEM list invites a host to ⛔ OBEY A PREDICTION, ⛔ which is the ⛔ defect ⭐ `SC-§133` was minted against. ⛔ **(3) ⛔ ITEM COUNTS ARE A ⛔ MOVING TARGET** — ⛔ a host authorised elsewhere on its row to flip ⛔ N other rows mints ⛔ N more items ⛔ inside the ⛔ same file, ⛔ through ⛔ no act of its own choosing (⭐ `SC-§138`).**
         - ⇒ ✅ **A `TAIL-TAKER:` USING VALUE (ii) IS ⛔ SATISFIED WHEN ⛔ EVERY FILE IS NAMED BY PATH. ⛔ FLIPS AUTHORISED ⛔ ELSEWHERE ON THE ROW ARE ⛔ COVERED BY THE FILE THAT ⛔ CARRIES THEM AND NEED ⛔ NO SEPARATE ENUMERATION.** 🚨 ⛔ **A HOST THAT MINTS MORE ITEMS INSIDE A ⛔ NAMED FILE IS ⛔ NOT IN BREACH — ⛔ and this sentence exists ⛔ precisely so a future host does ⛔ NOT READ ITS OWN COMPLIANCE AS A BREACH** (⛔ which is a ⛔ real cost: ⛔ an agent that believes it broke a rule ⛔ spends its report defending itself ⛔ instead of reporting).
         - ⛔ **THE ⛔ ANTI-ABUSE LIMB, ⛔ BECAUSE FILE GRANULARITY IS ⛔ COARSER AND COARSER CAN HIDE THINGS: ⛔ NAMING A FILE IS ⛔ NOT A LICENCE TO SWEEP ⛔ UNRELATED DIRT INSIDE IT. ⛔ The taker still ⛔ derives at its own instant, still ⛔ classifies every line, and still ⛔ NAMES anything it finds that the row did ⛔ not predict (cl. 7a).** ⛔ **⛔ AND THE BOUND IS ⛔ STILL A BOUND: ⛔ *"`TASKBOARD.md` and whatever else turns up"* is ⛔ NOT a file list and ⛔ does not satisfy (ii).**
     - 🚨⛔ **WHAT THE FIELD ⛔ ACTUALLY BUYS, ⛔ STATED PLAINLY SO IT IS NOT MISTAKEN FOR BOOKKEEPING: ⛔ IT CONVERTS THE TAIL FROM SOMETHING ⛔ REMEMBERED INTO SOMETHING ⛔ REQUIRED.** ⛔ **A host row with ⛔ NO `TAIL-TAKER:` is ⛔ DEFECTIVE ⛔ AT BOARDING — ⛔ a ⭐ `SC-§100` scope blocker against the ⛔ MANAGER, ⛔ not against the host** — ⛔ and it is ⛔ the manager's defect ⛔ precisely because the manager is the ⛔ only party who can board the successor. ⭐ **This is ⭐ `SC-§50`'s shape applied to the ⛔ LEDGER CLASS: ⛔ a declared gap with ⛔ no row ⛔ SHIPS.**
     - ⛔ **THE ⛔ QUIET-WINDOW HOLE IS ⛔ NOT CLOSED BY THIS AND ⛔ THAT IS SAID RATHER THAN GLOSSED (⛔ cl. 7b's own caveat): ⛔ value (ii) resolves ⛔ only when a host ⛔ RUNS.** ⇒ ⛔ **between waves, the ⛔ protective docs-only row (⭐ `TASK-1322`'s shape) ⛔ REMAINS the gap-filler. ⛔ cl. 7d makes the tail ⛔ OWNED; ⛔ it does not make it ⛔ INSTANT.**
     - ⚖️ ⛔ **AND THE SELF-APPLICATION, ⛔ BECAUSE A CLAUSE THAT EXEMPTS ITSELF IS THE ⛔ DISEASE: ⛔ the ⛔ FIRST ROW TO CARRY THIS FIELD IS ⛔ THE ONE BOARDED IN THE SAME SITTING AS THIS AMENDMENT** (⭐ `TASK-1355`), ⛔ **and it carries value (ii) — ⛔ with both tail files named on its face.**

   - 🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AMENDMENT 2026-09-06 — ⛔ CL. 7a: THE *"EXPECTED ONE-CYCLE LAG"* ABOVE ⛔ ONLY SELF-CORRECTS IF THE ⛔ NEXT HOST HAS A ⛔ STANDING SWEEP DUTY, AND ⛔ UNTIL NOW ⛔ NONE EXISTED. ⛔ THE LAG WAS ⛔ MEASURED AS A ⛔ BACKLOG OF THREE.** (⛔ bought on ⭐ `TASK-1110`; ⛔ **measured across ⛔ four commits, ⛔ not theorised** · marker `TL-5E-STANDING-SWEEP`)
     - ⛔ **THE MEASUREMENT: ⛔ THREE CONSECUTIVE HOSTS — `e37c899` (⭐ `TASK-1085`), `9daa641` (⭐ `TASK-1094`), `eeb29c4` (⭐ `TASK-1104`) — ⛔ EACH STAGED ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT ITS OWN ROW NAMED, which is a ⛔ DEFENSIBLE READING OF ⭐ `§25c` ⛔ EVERY TIME.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ THREE CORRECT HOSTS PRODUCED A ⛔ BACKLOG OF THREE. ⛔ The class repaired itself ⛔ EXACTLY ONCE, at `12160ea`, and ⛔ ONLY because that row's cl. (2) ⛔ NAMED `TASK-1104` ⛔ EXPLICITLY.**
     - 🚨⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ WORST MEMBER PROVES IT IS ⛔ NOT A CARE PROBLEM: ⛔ `handoffs/TASK-1094-buildmaster.md` WAS IN ⛔ NO PATHSPEC AND IN ⛔ NO EXCLUSION LIST ⛔ AT ALL.** ⛔ **It was ⛔ not deferred, ⛔ not declined, ⛔ not named-and-left — ⛔ it was ⛔ INVISIBLE TO EVERY ROW THAT EVER RAN, and ⛔ nothing anywhere would ever have gone red about it.**
     - ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE BUILD-MASTER'S OWN SENTENCE, ⛔ KEPT VERBATIM BECAUSE IT IS THE ⛔ WHOLE CLAUSE: ⛔ "A RULE THAT DEPENDS ON SOMEONE REMEMBERING TO NAME THE FILE IS A ⛔ HABIT, ⛔ NOT A RULE."***
     - ✅🚨 **THE ⛔ STANDING DUTY, ⛔ BINDING ON ⛔ EVERY COMMIT HOST ⛔ WITHOUT BEING WRITTEN INTO ITS ROW (⛔ this is the ⛔ point — ⛔ a duty that has to be ⛔ copied onto a row is the ⛔ habit again): ⛔ BEFORE COMMITTING, ⛔ EVERY HOST RUNS `git status --short --untracked-files=all -- .claude/pipeline/` ⛔ AT ITS OWN DISPATCH INSTANT and ⛔ CLASSIFIES ⛔ EVERY LINE.** ⛔ **⛔ THE DERIVATION ⛔ IS THE PATHSPEC. ⛔ A list written earlier is ⛔ EVIDENCE OF INTENT, ⛔ NEVER THE SET (⭐ `SC-§91`).** ⛔ **⛔ This is the ⛔ SECOND authorized exception to `§25b` cl. R's hand-named pathspec, and — ⛔ unlike the doc-host's — it needs ⛔ NO grant in the row, ⛔ because ⛔ needing a grant is the defect.**
     - ✅🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CL. 7b — `CONVENTIONS.md` IS ⛔ ADOPTED INTO ⛔ EVERY COMMIT HOST'S PATHSPEC, ⛔ ON THE ⛔ SAME NO-GRANT-NEEDED FOOTING AS THE SWEEP ABOVE** (added 2026-09-19 by the manager, ⛔ **on the evidence of ⛔ THREE protective-commit runs of ⭐ `TASK-1322` in ⛔ ONE DAY** · marker `TL-5E-ADOPT-CONVENTIONS`).
       - 🚨⛔ **THE ASYMMETRY THAT WAS THE ⛔ WHOLE BUG, AND ⛔ NOBODY EVER STATED A REASON FOR IT: `TASKBOARD.md` is in ⛔ EVERY host's pathspec ⛔ DESPITE HAVING NOTHING TO DO WITH THAT HOST'S SUBJECT — ⛔ because it is understood as ⛔ PIPELINE BOOKKEEPING, ⛔ not payload. ⛔ `CONVENTIONS.md` IS THE ⛔ SAME CLASS and was ⛔ EXCLUDED FOR NO STATED REASON.**
       - ⛔ **THE MEASURED CONSEQUENCE: the ⛔ MANAGER edits `CONVENTIONS.md` ⛔ constantly (⭐ `SC-§82` ⛔ reserves it to the manager) and ⛔ HOLDS NO GIT.** ⇒ ⛔ **the manager's output was ⛔ STRUCTURALLY ORPHANED — ⛔ not occasionally, ⛔ by construction.** ⛔ Three protective commits in one day (`5c38f37` → `61160e3` → `32bdcd3`) ⛔ is the rate.
       - ✅ ⛔ **THE DUTY: ⛔ every commit host stages `CONVENTIONS.md` ⛔ IF DIRTY, ⛔ alongside `TASKBOARD.md`, ⛔ WITHOUT a grant in its row.** ⛔ **Its ⛔ CONTENT is ⛔ NOT that host's business and is ⛔ NEVER reviewed by it — ⛔ exactly as with the board. ⛔ The host stages it and says it staged it; ⛔ that is all.**
       - ⛔⛔ **WHAT THIS DOES ⛔ NOT DISSOLVE, ⛔ STATED SO THE PROTECTIVE ROW IS NOT RETIRED BY MISTAKE: ⛔ ADOPTION HELPS ⛔ ONLY WHEN A HOST ⛔ RUNS.** ⛔ Between waves there are windows — ⛔ hours or days — with ⛔ NO host in flight, and 🚨 ⛔ **THAT IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHEN THE MANAGER DOES LAW WORK, ⛔ BECAUSE IT IS THE QUIET TIME.** ⇒ ⛔ **a ⛔ protective docs-only row (⭐ `TASK-1322`'s shape) ⛔ REMAINS the gap-filler. ⛔ Adoption makes it ⛔ RARE, ⛔ NOT UNNECESSARY.**
     - ✅🚨⭐⭐⭐ **CL. 7c — THE ⛔ BOUNDARY BETWEEN cl. 7a (⛔ SWEEP THE ORPHANS) AND cl. 7a-v (⛔ LEAVE THE SCHEDULED), ⛔ MEASURED 2026-09-19 ON ⭐ `TASK-1318` — ⛔ AND RECORDED BECAUSE ⛔ THE HOST OVERRODE THE ⛔ ORCHESTRATOR AND WAS ⛔ RIGHT** (marker `TL-5E-ORPHAN-VS-SCHEDULED`).
       - ⛔ **THE EVENT: the orchestrator's ⛔ DISPATCH told the host that `handoffs/TASK-1311-programmer.md` and `qa/TASK-1312-report.md` ⛔ WOULD ride in under cl. 7a, and that this was ⛔ correct and expected.** ⛔ **THE HOST ⛔ REFUSED** and left them ⛔ `HELD-FOR: TASK-1313`, citing ⛔ cl. 7a-v: ⛔ a `handoffs/` / `qa/` file whose ⛔ SUBJECT ROW has a ⛔ **NAMED HOST THAT HAS NOT YET COMMITTED** is ⛔ **SCHEDULED, ⛔ NOT ORPHANED.**
       - ⛔ **THE DECISIVE CORROBORATION, ⛔ AND IT WAS ON THE BOARD IN BLACK AND WHITE THE WHOLE TIME: ⭐ `TASK-1313`'s ⛔ OWN `names:` LINE ⛔ EXPLICITLY STAGES BOTH FILES.**
       - ⇒ ⛔ **taking them would have ⛔ (i) ⛔ STOLEN A LIVE HOST'S ⛔ NAMED PATHSPEC and ⛔ (ii) ⛔ SPLIT ONE SUBJECT ACROSS ⛔ TWO COMMITS — ⛔ the prose in one, the `.cpp` it describes in another.**
       - ✅ ⛔ **THE TEST, ⛔ STATED SO IT IS ⛔ NEVER RE-DERIVED: ⛔ ORPHAN = ⛔ NO ROW NAMES IT. ⛔ SCHEDULED = ⛔ A ROW NAMES IT ⛔ AND THAT ROW HAS ⛔ NOT COMMITTED YET.** ⛔ cl. 7a takes the ⛔ FIRST; ⛔ cl. 7a-v leaves the ⛔ SECOND. ⛔ **A dispatch that ⛔ COLLAPSES THEM is ⛔ WRONG, and the host is ⛔ RIGHT TO REFUSE IT.**
       - ⚖️ ⛔ **AND THE PRECEDENT WORTH MORE THAN THE RULE: ⛔ AN ORCHESTRATOR'S ⛔ DISPATCH PROSE IS ⛔ NOT AUTHORISATION TO BREACH A ⛔ BOARD FENCE.** ⛔ The ⛔ BOARD and the ⛔ LAW outrank the ⛔ dispatch; ⛔ the host checked ⛔ both and ⛔ declined. ⭐ ⛔ **That is ⭐ `SC-§97`'s shape ⛔ one level over — ⛔ *a relayed instruction is ⛔ not a ruling* — and it is ⛔ the second time in one day an agent ⛔ correctly refused an instruction from ⛔ above it** (⛔ the first: ⭐ `TASK-1310` refusing its own acceptance (3)).
       - 🚨✅⭐⭐⭐ **AMENDMENT 2026-09-19, ⛔ SAME DAY, ⛔ RUN 4 — ⛔ cl. 7c IS ⛔ BIDIRECTIONAL, AND AS FIRST WRITTEN IT READ AS A ⛔ ONE-WAY BRAKE. ⛔ THIS SENTENCE IS THE FIX.** ⛔ **cl. 7c was minted when a dispatch was ⛔ LOOSER than the law (⛔ take the scheduled files). ⛔ ON RUN 4 A DISPATCH WAS ⛔ STRICTER than the law — ⛔ *"never `git add`"* — AND THAT ⛔ QUIETLY BREAKS THE TASK.**
         - ⛔ **THE MECHANISM: `git commit -- <paths>` ⛔ REJECTS UNTRACKED PATHS OUTRIGHT** ⇒ ⛔ **any cl. 7a run with `??` candidates ⛔ MUST `git add` them first (⛔ `TL-§5e` (7a-iv) ⛔ says so and is ⛔ right).** 🚨 ⛔ **Had the host obeyed the dispatch literally, the orphan ⛔ COULD NOT HAVE BEEN COMMITTED AT ALL — ⛔ the run would have ⛔ SILENTLY DEGRADED back to the ⛔ retired two-file shape, ⛔ and the orchestrator would have ⛔ REPORTED SUCCESS.**
         - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE HOST'S OWN SENTENCE, ⛔ KEPT VERBATIM BECAUSE IT ⛔ IS THE AMENDMENT: ⛔ "THE BOARD AND THE LAW OUTRANK THE DISPATCH" IS ⛔ NOT ONLY A ⛔ BRAKE — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ SOMETIMES THE ⛔ ACCELERATOR.*** ⛔ **A dispatch that ⛔ FORBIDS what the law ⛔ REQUIRES is ⛔ as much a defect as one that ⛔ permits what the law ⛔ forbids, and it is ⛔ HARDER TO SEE, ⛔ because obeying it ⛔ looks like caution and ⛔ fails silently.**
         - ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ SAFE FORM, ⛔ PRESERVED FROM (7a-iv) BECAUSE THE ⛔ NATURAL REACH IS THE ⛔ DANGEROUS ONE: ⛔ `git add -- <EXPLICIT PATH>`. ⛔ NEVER `-A`. ⛔ NEVER `.`. ⛔ NEVER a bare directory.** ⇒ ⛔ **on a board where ⛔ ten agents write concurrently, ⛔ `-A` is exactly what ⛔ swallows another lane's `Source/`.** ⭐ ⛔ Run 4 staged ⛔ one orphan by ⛔ explicit path and ⛔ verified the index held ⛔ exactly it.
       - 🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **CL. 7a-vi — ⛔ THE STALENESS BOUND ON *"SCHEDULED"*, ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-19: ⛔ *"A HOST EXISTS AND HAS NOT COMMITTED"* IS ⛔ TRUE FOREVER IF THE HOST ⛔ NEVER COMMITS.**
         - ⛔ **THE MEASURED EXPOSURE: ⭐ `TASK-1313` sat parked at `built` across ⛔ ALL FOUR protective runs, on 🧑 an ⛔ UNDATED HUMAN DECISION, with `handoffs/TASK-1311-programmer.md` and `qa/TASK-1312-report.md` ⛔ HELD FOR IT throughout.** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **those two documents are ⛔ DELIBERATELY OUTSIDE ⭐ `TASK-1322`'s trigger, so the ⛔ ONE MECHANISM THAT WOULD PROTECT THEM ⛔ CANNOT REACH THEM ⛔ BY DESIGN.** ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE EXACT STRANDING SHAPE ⭐ `TASK-1322` EXISTS TO PREVENT, ⛔ REPRODUCED ⛔ THROUGH THE CLAUSE THAT EXEMPTS THEM.***
         - ✅ ⛔ **THE BOUND, ⛔ AND IT IS KEYED TO ⛔ DISPATCHABILITY, ⛔ NEVER TO A CLOCK (⛔ a clock threshold is a ⛔ TALLY and would rot — `SC-§104`): ⛔ cl. 7a-v's *"scheduled"* exemption ⛔ REQUIRES THE NAMED HOST TO BE ⛔ DISPATCHABLE. ⛔ A HOST BLOCKED ON AN ⛔ UNDATED HUMAN DECISION IS ⛔ NOT DISPATCHABLE — ⛔ `SC-§43` cl. 2(b) and ⭐ `TL-§5e` cl. 1 ⛔ ALREADY REFUSE IT AS SOMETHING YOU MAY ⛔ POINT AT.** ⇒ ⛔ **its held-for documents ⛔ REVERT TO ⛔ ORPHAN and the ⛔ next sweep ⛔ TAKES THEM.**
         - ⛔ **THE CONTROL, ⛔ SO ONE OBSERVATION IS NOT A MEASUREMENT (⭐ `SC-§39`): ⛔ the revert fires when the host is ⛔ STILL PARKED at a ⛔ SECOND protective run.** ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1313` has been observed parked at ⛔ FOUR ⇒ ⛔ **the condition is ⛔ ALREADY SATISFIED and the ⛔ NEXT run takes both documents.**
         - ⛔ **THE COST, ⛔ NAMED RATHER THAN HIDDEN: ⛔ this ⛔ SPLITS ONE SUBJECT ACROSS TWO COMMITS — ⛔ the prose early, the `.cpp` when ⭐ `TASK-1313` finally runs — ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly what cl. 7a-v protects against.** ✅ ⛔ **IT IS ACCEPTED ⛔ DELIBERATELY, ⛔ ON ⭐ `TASK-1322`'s OWN THESIS: ⛔ PROTECTION BEATS TIDINESS. ⛔ MITIGATION: ⛔ the protective commit's ⛔ MESSAGE NAMES the documents as ⛔ held-for-`TASK-1313` landing ⛔ EARLY FOR PROTECTION, so ⛔ git history carries the link the commit boundary lost.**
         - ⛔ **WHAT THIS DOES ⛔ NOT RELAX: ⛔ a host that is merely ⛔ QUEUED — ⛔ dispatchable, ⛔ waiting its turn, ⛔ blocked only on things the pipeline itself will clear — ⛔ KEEPS its documents. ⛔ cl. 7a-v's default is ⛔ UNCHANGED. ⛔ This bound fires ⛔ ONLY on the ⛔ undated-human-decision case.**
     - ✅⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ CLASSIFIER — ⛔ HOW A HOST TELLS *"ANOTHER LANE'S ⛔ LIVE FILE, ⛔ LEAVE IT"* FROM *"AN ⛔ ORPHAN, ⛔ TAKE IT"*, ⛔ BY ⛔ DERIVATION, ⛔ WITH ⛔ NO LIST:**

     - ⛔ **THE ⛔ DELIVERABLE IS A ⛔ CENSUS, ⛔ NOT A SILENCE: ⛔ the host's handoff prints ⛔ EVERY line the `git status` returned, ⛔ WITH ITS VERDICT AND ⛔ THE TEST THAT PRODUCED IT.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ A candidate that is ⛔ omitted from the census is ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM ONE THAT WAS ⛔ NEVER SEEN — ⛔ which is ⛔ exactly how `TASK-1094-buildmaster.md` survived ⛔ four hosts.**
     - ⚖️ ⛔ **AND THE ⛔ IRONY, ⛔ RECORDED SO IT IS ⛔ NOT LOST: ⛔ the finding that handoffs go unswept ⛔ IS ITSELF WRITTEN IN A HANDOFF THAT IS ⛔ CURRENTLY UNSWEPT** (`handoffs/TASK-1110-buildmaster.md`). ⛔ **⛔ The class ⛔ demonstrated itself ⛔ on the document that ⛔ named it.**

     - 🚨✅⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **AMENDMENT 2026-09-07 — ⛔ CL. 7a HAD ⛔ THREE HOLES AND ⛔ ITS OWN FIRST EXECUTION FOUND ⛔ ALL THREE (⭐ `TASK-1111`); ⛔ CLOSED THE ⛔ SAME DAY.** ⛔ **The run returned a ⛔ TRUE ZERO — 🧑 Jonathan's `e1ba2c4` had ⛔ ALREADY SWEPT the ⛔ ENTIRE cl. (3) floor — and ⛔ a duty that has ⛔ never met an ⛔ EMPTY SET does ⛔ not know what to do with one.** (⛔ marker `TL-5E-7A-ZERO-CENSUS`; ⛔ **measured, ⛔ not theorised**)
       - ✅⭐⭐⭐ **(7a-i) ⛔ THE DUTY'S OUTPUT IS A ⛔ CENSUS. ⛔ A COMMIT IS WHAT A ⛔ NON-EMPTY CENSUS ⛔ PRODUCES — ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT WHAT THE DUTY ⛔ IS.** ⇒ ⛔ **A ⛔ PROVEN ZERO IS A ⛔ COMPLETED DUTY, ⛔ NOT A SKIPPED ONE, and the host ⛔ COMMITS NOTHING.** ⛔⛔ **`--allow-empty` IS ⛔ BANNED IN THIS DUTY: ⛔ an empty commit is a ⛔ FALSE ENTRY IN THE HISTORY, and — ⛔ far worse for a ⛔ STANDING duty — it ⛔ TEACHES EVERY FUTURE HOST THAT THE DUTY ⛔ MUST END IN A COMMIT.** ⚖️ ***⛔ `SC-§95`'s shape ⛔ one lane over: ⛔ A RUN THAT SWEPT ⛔ ZERO FILES MUST ⛔ NOT BE ABLE TO LOOK LIKE A RUN THAT SWEPT ⛔ SOME.*** ⛔ **cl. (6)'s *"commit, then `git show --stat HEAD`"* is ⛔ HEREBY CONDITIONAL ON A ⛔ NON-EMPTY DERIVATION; ⛔ on a zero the host ⛔ pastes the ⛔ HEAD it ⛔ found and ⛔ says whose it is.**
         - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ PRICE OF THE ZERO, ⛔ WHICH IS THE ⛔ WHOLE REASON IT IS ALLOWED: ⛔ IT IS ⛔ REPORTED ⛔ WITH ITS ⛔ POSITIVE CONTROL (⭐ `SC-§96`) — ⛔ an ⛔ UNPROVEN zero is ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A ⛔ DEAD READER.** ⭐ **⭐ `TASK-1111`'s form is the ⛔ WORKED PRECEDENT and ⛔ every future zero copies it: ⛔ `3/3` ⛔ NON-EMPTY index reads (⛔ byte counts printed ⛔ BEFORE any comparison) · ⛔ a ⛔ DELIBERATE cwd-relative ⛔ NEGATIVE control that returned ⛔ SILENCE · ⛔ a ⛔ RE-DERIVATION after its own handoff existed that ⛔ DID see it.**
         - ✅ **THE ⛔ CLASSIFIER ROWS THE TABLE ⛔ LACKED — ⛔ both dispose of a candidate that is ⛔ ABSENT from the derivation, which the ⛔ original table has ⛔ no vocabulary for:**

| ⛔ candidate | ⛔ test, ⛔ run at ⛔ your own instant | ⛔ verdict |
|---|---|---|
| ⛔ a ⛔ FLOOR ITEM (⛔ incl. one your ⛔ OWN ROW NAMED) that is ⛔ **ABSENT from your derivation** | ⛔ `git cat-file -e HEAD:<path>` | ✅ **ALREADY SWEPT ⇒ ⛔ NOT A CANDIDATE.** ⛔ **⛔ NAME IT ⛔ AND THE COMMIT THAT TOOK IT** — ⛔ including 🧑 **a JONATHAN commit: ⛔ he sweeps his own, and ⛔ that ⛔ DISCHARGES the duty ⛔ as completely as your commit would** |
| ⛔ same, but ⛔ **NOT in `HEAD` ⛔ AND NOT ON DISK** | ⛔ file test ⛔ AND `git status` | ⛔ **NEVER MINTED ⇒ ⛔ not a candidate.** ⛔ **⛔ SAY SO — ⛔ an ⛔ UNWRITTEN handoff and a ⛔ SWEPT one are ⛔ DIFFERENT FACTS and a ⛔ silent census ⛔ conflates them** |

       - 🚨✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(7a-ii) ⛔ `--untracked-files=all` ⛔ NEVER SHOWS ⛔ IGNORED FILES ⇒ ⛔ A `.gitignore`-MATCHING FILE UNDER `.claude/pipeline/**` IS ⛔ INVISIBLE TO THE STANDING COMMAND — ⛔ THE ⛔ `TASK-1094` SHAPE (*"in ⛔ NO pathspec and ⛔ NO exclusion list ⛔ AT ALL"*) ⛔ ONE LAYER LOWER AND ⛔ IMMUNE TO THE ⛔ VERY RULE THAT WAS WRITTEN TO CATCH IT.** ⛔ **Probed ⛔ CLEAN on 2026-09-07 — ⛔ and the rule ⛔ MUST NOT REST ON THAT.**
         - ⚖️⭐ **THE RULING — ⛔ BOTH, AND ⛔ NOT AS BELT-AND-BRACES: ⛔ THEY ARE ⛔ DIFFERENT INSTRUMENTS AND ⛔ NEITHER SUBSTITUTES FOR THE OTHER.**
           - ⛔ **(a) THE ⛔ STANDING COMMAND ⛔ GAINS THE FLAG. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOW, ⛔ VERBATIM: `git status --short --untracked-files=all --ignored -- .claude/pipeline/`.** ⛔ **That is the ⛔ DETECTOR; ⛔ it costs ⛔ ONE FLAG and it ⛔ closes the class ⛔ on every run, ⛔ not only on the runs somebody remembers to probe.**
           - ⛔ **(b) ⛔ WHENEVER THE DERIVATION RETURNS ⛔ ZERO LINES, THE ⛔ ON-DISK-vs-TRACKED COUNT UNDER THE PATHSPEC IS ⛔ MANDATORY** (⭐ `TASK-1111`: ⛔ `1285` on disk = ⛔ `1285` tracked, ⛔ via an instrument that ⛔ never calls `git status`). ⛔ **That is the ⛔ CONTROL ⛔ ON the detector — and ⛔ ZERO is ⛔ EXACTLY the output where ⛔ *clean* and ⛔ *broken* ⛔ print the same thing.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **⛔ A DETECTOR ⛔ CANNOT CONTROL ITSELF.**
         - ⛔⛔ **AND THE ⛔ DISPOSITION OF AN ⛔ IGNORED HIT, ⛔ WRITTEN NOW SO THE ⛔ NEW FLAG CANNOT ⛔ CAUSE A ⛔ WRONG COMMIT: ⛔ AN `!!` LINE IS ⛔ NEVER AUTO-TAKEN.** ⛔ **It is a ⛔ REPORTABLE FINDING ⛔ ROUTED TO THE MANAGER ⛔ BY NAME** — ⛔ the question it raises is *"⛔ is the ⛔ IGNORE right, or is the ⛔ FILE an orphan?"*, ⛔ and that is a ⛔ RULING, ⛔ never a staging decision a host makes alone.
       - ✅⚖️⛔⛔ **(7a-iii) ⛔ PRECEDENCE — ⛔ THE TABLE AND THE ROW GAVE ⛔ OPPOSITE ORDERS ON THE ⛔ SAME TWO FILES.** ⛔ **The cl. 7a table's last row excludes `CONVENTIONS.md`/`TASKBOARD.md` as *"⛔ not this duty's business — ⛔ they ride on a row that ⛔ NAMES them"*; ⭐ `TASK-1111`'s own cl. (3) floor ⛔ NAMED THEM.** ⇒ ⛔ **a host reading ⛔ ONLY THE TABLE ⛔ LEAVES them, one reading ⛔ ONLY THE ROW ⛔ TAKES them — ⛔ opposite actions from the ⛔ same law.** (⛔ **Moot at `TASK-1111`'s instant — ⛔ both were already in `HEAD` — ⛔ so it cost ⛔ nothing ⛔ this time.**)
         - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ THE TABLE'S EXCLUSION IS A ⛔ DEFAULT, ⛔ NOT A PROHIBITION. ⛔ AN ⛔ EXPLICIT NAMING IN THE HOST'S ⛔ OWN ROW ⛔ OVERRIDES IT — ⛔ that naming ⛔ IS the *"row that names them"* the table ⛔ defers to.** ⛔⛔ **⛔ SILENCE IN THE ROW ⇒ ⛔ THE TABLE WINS ⇒ ⛔ LEAVE THEM.** ⛔ **Either way the ⛔ disposition is ⛔ PRINTED IN THE CENSUS ⛔ WITH WHICH OF THE TWO APPLIED — ⛔ a host that leaves them ⛔ silently is ⛔ indistinguishable from one that ⛔ never looked.**
       - 🚨✅⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(7a-iv) ⛔ `git commit -- <paths>` ⛔ REJECTS UNTRACKED PATHS ⛔ OUTRIGHT ⇒ ⛔ EVERY cl. 7a RUN WITH `??` CANDIDATES MUST ⛔ `git add` THEM ⛔ FIRST — AND ⛔ HOW IT STAGES THEM IS THE ⛔ WHOLE SAFETY QUESTION.** (added 2026-09-07 by the manager, ⛔ measured on ⭐ `TASK-1131`)
         - ⛔ **THE ERROR ITSELF IS ⛔ SAFE: it ⛔ FAILS LOUDLY and ⛔ commits ⛔ nothing.** ⛔ **The hazard is the ⛔ REACH FOR THE OBVIOUS ESCAPE: ⛔ a host that answers it with ⛔ `git add -A` (or ⛔ `git add .`) has just ⛔ SWALLOWED ANOTHER LANE'S `Source/` — ⛔ on a board where ⛔ ten agents write concurrently and where the ⛔ sweep host is ⛔ BY DESIGN a ⛔ TEXT-ONLY commit.**
         - ✅⛔ **THE RULE, ⛔ ONE SENTENCE: ⛔ STAGE `??` CANDIDATES ⛔ BY EXPLICIT PATH — ⛔ `git add -- <path> [<path> …]` — ⛔ NEVER `-A`, ⛔ NEVER `.`, ⛔ NEVER a bare directory.** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ DERIVED candidate list (⭐ `SC-§77`) stays the ⛔ ONLY thing that enters the index, which is the ⛔ entire premise of a derived pathspec.**
         - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ A LOUD FAILURE IS ⛔ NOT A SAFE FAILURE IF ITS ⛔ MOST NATURAL REMEDY IS ⛔ CATASTROPHIC. ⛔ NAME THE REMEDY IN THE LAW, ⛔ OR THE LAW HAS ⛔ ONLY DOCUMENTED THE TRAP.***
         - ⚠️ **THE ⛔ MANAGER'S HALF, ⛔ BECAUSE THE OVERRIDE IS ⛔ MINE TO WRITE: ⛔ if a doc-host row ⛔ INTENDS the two board files, it ⛔ SAYS SO ⛔ IN THE ROW, ⛔ by name.** ⛔ **⛔ *"The floor is obvious"* is ⛔ precisely how the two readings ⛔ diverge.**
       - ✅⚖️⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(7a-v) ⛔ THE ⛔ SCHEDULED-DOCUMENT EXCEPTION — ⛔ RATIFIED BY THE MANAGER 2026-09-07, ⛔ ON ⭐ `TASK-937`'s OWN JUDGEMENT CALL. ⛔ A `qa-passed` ROW IS ⛔ TERMINAL ⇒ ITS DOCUMENTS ⛔ READ AS ORPHANS ⇒ cl. 7a SAYS ⛔ TAKE THEM. ⛔ THAT IS ⛔ WRONG WHENEVER THE ROW HAS A ⛔ NAMED HOST THAT HAS ⛔ NOT RUN YET.** (⛔ marker `TL-5E-7A-SCHEDULED-NOT-ORPHAN`)
         - ⛔ **THE MEASURED CASE: ⭐ `TASK-937` ⛔ LEFT `handoffs/TASK-1113-programmer.md` and `qa/TASK-1114.md` ⛔ UNSWEPT and ⛔ SAID SO.** ⛔ **By the ⛔ LETTER of cl. 7a they were ⛔ orphans and ⛔ its to take. ⛔ But ⭐ `TASK-1113`'s ⛔ NAMED HOST is ⭐ `TASK-1117`, and `1113`'s ⛔ SOURCE files were ⛔ outside `937`'s scope.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **taking the ⛔ DOCUMENTS ALONE would have ⛔ SPLIT ONE SUBJECT ACROSS TWO COMMITS — ⛔ the ⛔ prose describing a diff in one commit, the ⛔ diff itself in another.**
         - ✅⛔ **THE RULE: ⛔ A `handoffs/` OR `qa/` FILE WHOSE ⛔ SUBJECT ROW CARRIES A ⛔ NAMED HOST THAT HAS ⛔ NOT YET COMMITTED IS ⛔ NOT AN ORPHAN. ⛔ LEAVE IT — AND ⛔ NAME IT IN YOUR HANDOFF AS ⛔ `HELD-FOR: TASK-###`.** ⛔⛔ **THE NAMING IS ⛔ MANDATORY, ⛔ NOT OPTIONAL: ⛔ a ⛔ SILENT LEAVE is ⛔ INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM the ⛔ three-deep backlog cl. 7a was ⛔ written to end** (⛔ the `NAMED-AND-LEFT` discipline, ⛔ applied to a ⛔ deferral instead of an ⛔ exclusion).
         - ⛔ **⛔ THE EXCEPTION IS ⛔ NARROW AND ⛔ HAS EXACTLY ⛔ TWO CONDITIONS, ⛔ BOTH REQUIRED: ⛔ (i) a ⛔ HOST ID EXISTS ON THE BOARD, and ⛔ (ii) that host has ⛔ NOT YET COMMITTED.** ⇒ ⛔ **⛔ a host that has ⛔ ALREADY RUN and ⛔ MISSED the file leaves a ⛔ GENUINE orphan ⇒ ⛔ cl. 7a applies ⛔ in full. ⛔ *"Somebody will probably take it"* is ⛔ NOT a host** (`TL-§5e` cl. 1).
         - ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ cl. 7a's TARGET IS A DOCUMENT ⛔ NO HOST WILL EVER COME FOR. ⛔ A DOCUMENT WITH A ⛔ HOST ON THE BOARD IS ⛔ SCHEDULED, ⛔ NOT ABANDONED — AND SWEEPING IT ⛔ EARLY BUYS ⛔ TIDINESS AT THE PRICE OF ⛔ ATOMICITY, WHICH IS THE ⛔ WRONG TRADE EVERY TIME.***

     - ✅ **THE RULE: ⛔ A DOC-HOST'S HANDOFF ⛔ ENUMERATES ITS TAIL BY ⛔ NAME AND BY ⛔ KIND (⛔ MINTED vs ⛔ ARRIVED), and the ⛔ MANAGER BOARDS THE ⛔ NEXT DOC-HOST ROW ⛔ IN THE SAME SITTING** (`cl. 8`: ⛔ *a finding is homed by ⛔ TASK ID or it is ⛔ not homed*). ⛔⛔ **A *"standing tail"* with ⛔ no row is ⛔ exactly how the class grew to ⛔ 61 in the first place — ⛔ the tail must ⛔ never be the thing that is ⛔ remembered.**
     - ⚖️ *⛔ And the ⛔ ARRIVED case is ⛔ WORSE than the ⛔ MINTED one despite looking milder: ⛔ the minted orphan is ⛔ predictable, ⛔ named in advance and ⛔ always exactly one. ⛔ The arrived orphan is ⛔ discovered ⛔ after the fact, ⛔ its size is ⛔ whatever the night produced, and ⛔ nothing in the row ⛔ anticipates it.*
   - ⭐ **SCOPE, ⛔ so this does not sprawl: the doc-host takes ⛔ `.md` UNDER `.claude/pipeline/handoffs/` AND `.claude/pipeline/qa/` ⛔ ONLY.** ⛔ **`CONVENTIONS.md` + `TASKBOARD.md` are ⛔ EXCLUDED — they are ⛔ concurrently written by the manager and ⛔ historically swept by Jonathan.** ⛔ **Binaries under those directories (⛔ evidence renders) are ⛔ NOT doc-host business: they are ⛔ committed by the row that ⛔ ARGUES FROM them** (`TASK-926`'s standard), ⛔ named-and-left otherwise.

8. ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CLAUSE 8 — ⛔ A FIX DELEGATED TO A HOST THAT IS ⛔ FENCED FROM THE FILE IS ⛔ NOT DELEGATED. ⛔ IT IS DROPPED — AND *"IT COSTS NOTHING, THE HOST WILL FIX IT"* IS THE ⛔ SENTENCE THAT DROPS IT.** (added 2026-09-03, from `qa/TASK-856.md` **W-1** → `TASK-919`'s commit `239ca77`; ⛔ **measured, ⛔ not theorised — the repair ⛔ did not happen and the commit ⛔ shipped a SECOND instance of the same defect**)
   - ⛔⛔ **THE MEASURED EVENT.** `qa/TASK-856.md` W-1 found a ⛔ back-pointer gone false and ruled: *"one-word repair on whichever row next legitimately edits that file — ⚠️ ⛔ **naturally the same host as `TASK-857`**, ⛔ so it costs nothing."* ⇒ ⛔ **`TASK-857`'s host was `TASK-919`, and ⛔ item (9) of ⛔ every build-master row fences it from ⛔ ANY source edit** (⛔ a comment edit is ⛔ still code and owes an `SC-§27` verdict — ⛔ routing rule 6). ⇒ ⛔ **the host was ⛔ STRUCTURALLY INCAPABLE of the repair, ⛔ read the row, ⛔ correctly declined, and ⛔ said so.** ⛔ **The fix simply ⛔ did not exist anywhere as a ⛔ row.**
   - ⛔⛔⭐ **AND THE ⛔ COMPOUNDING, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS A CLAUSE: ⛔ THE SAME COMMIT ⛔ SHIPPED A ⛔ SECOND, ⛔ FRESH INSTANCE OF THE ⛔ IDENTICAL DEFECT** — `SiegeControlsHelpWidget.cpp:1306`, added by ⛔ `TASK-870`'s ⛔ own diff, ⛔ named by ⛔ no gate. ⇒ ⛔⛔ ***THE COMMIT THAT ⛔ MOVED THE REGISTRY-WIDE WALK ⛔ ALSO SHIPPED A ⛔ FRESH POINTER TO ITS ⛔ OLD HOME.***
   - ✅ **THE RULE, ⛔ AND IT IS THE ⛔ MANAGER'S, ⛔ NOT THE GATE'S: ⛔ A FINDING IS ⛔ HOMED BY ⛔ TASK ID OR IT IS ⛔ NOT HOMED.** ⛔ **A QA report may ⛔ NOMINATE a host; ⛔ it may ⛔ NEVER assign one — ⛔ it does not know that row's fences and it is ⛔ not the board.** ⇒ ⛔ **the manager converts ⛔ every *"whoever next touches this"* into a ⛔ ROW WITH AN ID ⛔ IN THE SAME SITTING it is read** (⛔ the same instant `cl. 5`'s rider:host count and `§25b` cl. R's boarding-time bullet run).
   - ⛔⛔ **THE ⛔ TEST TO APPLY, ⛔ ten seconds: ⛔ *"CAN THE NAMED HOST ⛔ ACTUALLY EDIT THIS FILE?"*** ⛔ **A `build-master` row ⛔ CANNOT — ⛔ ever, ⛔ by standing fence. ⛔ A `qa-reviewer` row ⛔ CANNOT.** ⇒ ⛔ **a repair nominated onto either is ⛔ ALREADY DROPPED at the moment it is written, and ⛔ nothing will go red about it** (⛔ `TL-§5d`'s shape applied to a ⛔ FINDING instead of a ⛔ FILE: ⛔ it exists in a report, ⛔ is invisible to the board, ⛔ and ⛔ no instrument measures the gap).
   - ⚖️ ***⛔ "IT COSTS NOTHING" WAS ⛔ TRUE ABOUT THE ⛔ EDIT AND ⛔ FALSE ABOUT THE ⛔ DELEGATION.*** ⛔ **The cheapness of a repair is ⛔ an argument for boarding it ⛔ immediately, ⛔ never an argument for ⛔ not boarding it at all.** ⭐ **Corroboration on the ⛔ same page: `qa/TASK-856.md`'s ⛔ OTHER routed finding — the ⛔ F-1 count at `:492` — got an ⛔ explicit `⚖️ RULING` section, an ⛔ explicit *"the manager boards it"*, and ⛔ STILL sat ⛔ THREE sittings unhomed.** ⇒ ⛔ **even the ⛔ ceremonious form fails if it does not end in an ⛔ ID.**
